### [1] [Entanglement percolation enhanced by the power of choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10119)
*Yanxuan Shao,Adilson E. Motter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"选择幂"(power-of-choice, PoC)协议，通过反转经典爆炸渗流中Achlioptas过程的筛选准则，使纠缠渗流转变提前发生，从而减少建立量子通信网络所需的纠缠资源，且该协议可仅凭局部信息实现而不显著损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信网络理想上依赖最大纠缠态，但纠缠产生与分发过程的缺陷使实际分布式态只能部分纠缠，需借助局域操作与经典通信(LOCC)构造合适的纠缠网络。而已有协议在纠缠资源消耗上不够高效，亟需能降低资源需求、提前触发纠缠渗流的方案。

Method: 将经典爆炸渗流中Achlioptas过程的"选择准则"进行反向设计，提出PoC协议：在纠缠渗流建模框架下，以相反的择优规则引导网络连边(纠缠连接)的形成，使纠缠渗流转变在更低的连边/纠缠密度下发生；并进一步构造仅依赖局域信息的分布式实现方案。

Result: PoC协议使纠缠渗流转变提前出现，因而降低了建立量子通信所需的纠缠资源；与爆炸渗流情形不同，该协议可用局部信息实现，且性能无显著损失。

Conclusion: 通过反转Achlioptas选择准则可有效提前纠缠渗流转变并节约纠缠资源，同时保持局部可实现性，为资源受限的量子通信网络构建提供了实用且可分布执行的协议设计思路。

Abstract: Quantum communication networks are best supported by maximally entangled states, but imperfections in entanglement generation and distribution generally limit the distributed states to being only partially entangled. Therefore, protocols typically consisting of local operations and classical communication are required to create suitably entangled networks. Here, we introduce a power-of-choice (PoC) protocol based on reversing the selection criteria in the Achlioptas process previously considered in classical explosive percolation. The resulting PoC protocol anticipates the entanglement percolation transition, thereby reducing the entanglement resources needed to establish quantum communication. Notably, in contrast to explosive percolation, the PoC protocol admits an implementation using local information with no appreciable loss of performance.

</details>


### [2] [Bayes-Optimal Quantum Computational Sensing via Convex Spectral Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10146)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Alireza Sadeghi,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将二相分类直接表述为传感器测量响应的优化问题，利用GQSP将响应限制为有界三角多项式、贝叶斯误差为其线性泛函，从而构造出一个可精确求解的有限维半定规划，获得全局贝叶斯最优响应，并通过Fejér–Riesz分解与GQSP合成得到物理可实现的相干控制序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子计算感知设计通常通过求解非凸优化来调节量子控制参数，其性能强烈依赖初始化，缺乏全局最优性与可靠性保证。

Method: 以固定查询预算为约束，将二相分类问题直接写在传感器的测量响应层面：GQSP 把响应限制为有界三角多项式，而贝叶斯分类误差是响应的线性泛函，由此得到精确的有限维半定规划；再用 Fejér–Riesz 因子分解与 GQSP 合成把最优响应还原为物理可实现的相干控制序列。该框架还可自然扩展到基于样本的设计与加性相位不确定性情形。

Result: 数值实验表明，与直接对控制角进行训练相比，该方法在精度和优化可靠性上均有提升；噪声感知的重新设计在相位不确定性下进一步降低了误差。

Conclusion: 通过将计算感知的分类任务转化为响应层面的凸优化问题，可获得全局贝叶斯最优且物理可实现的量子传感策略，克服了非凸参数训练的初始化依赖，并能处理采样与相位不确定性等实际情形。

Abstract: Quantum computational sensing performs task-specific inference directly through coherent sensing dynamics, avoiding an intermediate estimation step. Existing designs, however, typically optimize quantum control parameters through nonconvex programs whose performance can depend strongly on initialization. This contribution formulates binary phase classification directly in terms of the sensor's measurement response. For a fixed query budget, generalized quantum signal processing (GQSP) restricts this response to a bounded trigonometric polynomial, while the Bayes classification error is linear in the response. These properties yield an exact finite-dimensional semidefinite program for the globally Bayes-optimal sensor response. Fejér--Riesz factorization and GQSP synthesis then recover a physically realizable control sequence. The same formulation extends naturally to sample-based design and additive phase uncertainty. Numerical tests show improved accuracy and optimization reliability over direct control-angle training; noise-aware redesign also lowers error under phase uncertainty.

</details>


### [3] [Symplectic Reck: In-Situ Learning of Gaussian Quantum Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10206)
*Janet F. Zhong,Renwen Yu,Paul-Alexis Mor,Charles Roques-Carmes,Aviv Karnieli,David A. B. Miller,Shanhui Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向连续变量量子系统的物理学习算法与架构，用"辛Reck网格"在光子芯片上原位训练，以反演未知的无损高斯量子操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程光子电路天然适合物理学习（直接用测量反馈训练硬件参数），但现有自配置光学网络主要针对酉变换。作者希望把这一范式从酉变换推广到连续变量量子硬件所需的辛变换，从而实现对未知无损高斯量子操作的逆操作（如在量子计量/纠错中的误差补偿）。

Method: 1) 理论上证明任意辛矩阵 S∈Sp(2N,R) 可分解为相邻模式上的两模辛门构成的三角阵列，再接单模辛门；2) 据此提出"辛Reck网格"物理架构，推广光神经网络中常用的酉Reck网格；3) 将该网格置于未知高斯酉操作之后，用光学探针与测量逐个"置零"局部响应块进行原位训练；4) 定义局部条件数，刻画单步置零所需压缩量及其对测量误差的敏感度；5) 数值模拟有限光子数（有限采样）训练及对门校准系统误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 给出辛Reck分解的构造性证明与对应网格实现；给出基于局部响应块置零的原位训练协议，并证明其收敛/训练可行；提出局部条件数指标以量化压缩资源与误差敏感度；数值上验证了有限采样下的训练可行性与对系统性门校准误差的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将自配置光学网络从酉变换扩展到辛变换，把物理学习引入连续变量量子光子硬件，为可编程量子光路的自校准与误差补偿提供了理论与架构基础。

Abstract: Programmable photonic circuits are a natural platform for physical learning, where hardware parameters are trained directly from measured feedback. We propose a physical learning algorithm and architecture for continuous-variable quantum systems where a photonic circuit is trained in situ to invert an unknown lossless Gaussian quantum operation. We prove that a generic symplectic matrix $S\in\mathrm{Sp}(2N,\mathbb{R})$ admits a decomposition into a triangular array of two-mode symplectic gates on neighboring modes, followed by single-mode symplectic gates. We then introduce the symplectic Reck mesh, which physically realizes this factorization, generalizing the unitary Reck mesh widely used in optical neural networks. When placed after an unknown Gaussian unitary, the mesh is trained in situ by nulling local response blocks one at a time using optical probes and measurements. We identify a local condition number that quantifies the squeezing required for a nulling step and its sensitivity to measurement error. We numerically demonstrate finite-shot training and robustness to systematic gate-calibration errors. Our work extends self-configuring optical networks from unitary to symplectic transformations, bringing physical learning to continuous-variable quantum photonic hardware.

</details>


### [4] [Can One Hear the Size of a Clock?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10252)
*Markus Baumann,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为随机跃迁（如马尔可夫跳跃、随机游走）系统给出了缺失的"Weyl型计数定理"：若Q为振荡频率与衰减率之比，则d态系统能维持频率-衰减比超过Q的振荡数目少于 2(d-1)/(πQ)，嵌套环状结构可逼近该上界，且实测振荡谱可反推隐藏系统的最小规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Weyl定律统计腔内振动模式数，量子系统d个能级最多可有 d(d-1)/2 个不衰减的振荡频率；但随机在状态间跳跃的系统只在非平衡态下振荡，且跳跃的随机时序会阻尼所有振荡，长期以来缺乏类似的振荡数目计数规律。作者旨在补上这一缺失的计数定理。

Method: 以频率与衰减率之比 Q 作为核心参数，对 d 态随机跃迁系统的非平衡振荡谱进行计数分析，推导可维持的高品质因子（比值超过 Q）振荡数目的上界，并构造嵌套环（nested cycles）类结构考察该上界的可达性。

Result: 得到严格不等式：d 态系统维持的、比值超过 Q 的振荡数目少于 2(d-1)/(πQ)。嵌套环结构可逼近（任意接近）这一上界，表明该界在量级与常数上均为紧的。

Conclusion: 该结果构成随机跃迁系统的Weyl型计数定律，为"频率-衰减"乘积设定了结构性上限；同时提供了逆向推断工具：由可测振荡即可判断隐藏系统至少需要多大。作者未明示的推论是，该界与频率密度、图结构循环秩之间存在深刻联系，可视为热力学/网络谱几何意义的模式计数原理。

Abstract: Weyl's law counts the vibration modes that fit into a cavity, and a quantum system with $d$ levels can oscillate at up to $d(d-1)/2$ frequencies without decay. For systems that jump at random between states, no such count was known: they oscillate only out of equilibrium, and the random timing of their jumps damps every oscillation. We find the missing count: if $Q$ is the ratio of frequency to decay rate, a system of $d$ states sustains fewer than $2(d-1)/(πQ)$ oscillations whose ratio exceeds $Q$. Nested cycles approach this bound, and measured oscillations reveal how large a hidden system must be.

</details>


### [5] [Experimental visual quantum state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10282)
*A. Duplinskiy,M. J. Filipovich,A. I. Lvovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用量子信息论目标训练衍射光学神经网络(DONN)，优化入射光子的量子测量，使计算机视觉任务在光子受限与噪声条件下获得指数级更低的判别误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然与技术中的视觉方案几乎都依赖透镜成像后再由大脑或计算机分析，这种"先测量成像、后计算"的范式在光子受限和高噪声条件下会在测量阶段不可逆地丢失关键信息，因此需要把测量本身纳入优化。

Method: 将DONN与探测器阵列构成的测量作为可学习对象，用AI对入射光子进行量子测量优化；训练目标为量子信息论指标——最大化判别误差随光子累积而衰减的速率（误差指数），并与直接成像及仅针对单光子判别优化的DONN进行对比。

Result: 所得测量的误差较直接成像和单光子优化DONN呈指数级降低；在无噪声设定下验证了优势，并在EMCCD光子计数实验（噪声水平对应每个信号光子10^4个光子）中复现，测量还学会了抑制探测器噪声。

Conclusion: 把量子测量设计与任务目标端到端联合优化，可突破传统成像范式在弱光噪声下的信息瓶颈，并在真实探测器噪声下保持优势。

Abstract: Almost all solutions to vision tasks $\unicode{x2014}$ offered by nature and technology alike $\unicode{x2014}$ rely on capturing an image using a lens or a similar optical instrument, followed by computational analysis by neurons of a brain or a digital computer. This approach is inherently suboptimal in photon-limited and noisy conditions, where critical information is irreversibly lost at the measurement stage. We address this gap by introducing AI-driven optimization of quantum measurements on the incoming photons, learning how to extract the maximum possible information for a given computer vision task from each incoming photon. The trained measurement consists of a diffractive optical neural network (DONN) followed by a detector array. We train the DONN with a quantum information-theoretic objective that maximizes the rate at which the discrimination error decays as photons accumulate. The resulting measurement achieves exponentially lower error than both direct imaging and a DONN optimized for single-photon discrimination. We validate the advantage in a noiseless setting as well as a photon-counting experiment with an electron-multiplying CCD with the noise corresponding to $10^4$ photons per single signal photon, where the measurement also learns to mitigate the detector noise.

</details>


### [6] [Regev's Reduction Reaches Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10283)
*Stephen P. Jordan,Quynh T. Nguyen,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文应用Regev归约结合BPQM量子译码，证明Regev+BPQM可达的最低温度恰等于树状信道的重构阈值温度（即可由1RSB腔方程识别的动力学玻璃转变温度），从而在严格意义上超越已知经典局部采样器的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机：D-正则 max-k-LINSAT 平均情形在低温下变难，需评估量子方法能否在低于经典局部动力学（Metropolis、Glauber）可高效平衡的温度下采样Gibbs分布，并厘清Regev归约在此类自旋玻璃哈密顿量采样问题上的能力极限。

Method: 方法：将Regev基于量子傅里叶变换的格/码问题归约用于经典自旋玻璃哈密顿量的Gibbs采样；用BPQM（Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages）求解归约产生的译码问题；借助Renes关于经典信道与量子信道的对偶性，把量子译码可行性与对偶问题上的经典重构不可行性对应起来；并进行覆盖数百组问题参数的大规模数值实验。

Result: 结果：严格证明Regev+BPQM可达的最低温度恰好等于树状网络上噪声信道的重构阈值温度；该温度与Mézard与Montanari用1RSB腔方程所预测的动力学玻璃转变温度一致；数值实验在数百组参数上与该预测高度吻合，显示经典局部采样器在此温度以下难以高效平衡。

Conclusion: 结论：Regev+BPQM可达到据作者所知尚无匹配经典保证的温度，将量子方法推到经典局部动力学高效Gibbs采样的猜想极限；对偶性给出了"量子可译码当且仅当对偶经典重构不可行"的机制解释。

Abstract: Regev's reduction uses a quantum Fourier transform to carry out reductions between various problems related to lattices and codes. We apply it to the problem of sampling from Gibbs distributions of certain classical spin glass Hamiltonians, which can equivalently be thought of as cost functions for average-case instances of an optimization problem called $D$-regular max-$k$-LINSAT. This problem becomes hard at low temperature. Here, we use a quantum decoding algorithm called Belief Propagation with Quantum Messages (BPQM) to solve the decoding problem arising from Regev's reduction. Our main contribution is a proof that the minimum temperature reachable using Regev+BPQM is exactly equal to the reconstruction threshold temperature for noisy channels transmitting signals on a tree network. This equality can be understood via Renes' duality between classical and quantum channels, which we use to show that quantum decoding is possible exactly when classical reconstruction on the dual problem is impossible. M{é}zard and Montanari identified this reconstruction temperature with a dynamical glass transition predicted by the one-step replica-symmetry breaking (1RSB) cavity equation. It is conjectured that local samplers such as Metropolis Monte Carlo and Glauber dynamics cannot equilibrate efficiently below this reconstruction temperature but may do so above it; our large numerical experiments show close agreement with this prediction across hundreds of different problem parameters. Therefore, our results rigorously show that Regev+BPQM reaches a temperature for which, to our knowledge, there is no matching classical guarantee, and brings Regev+BPQM to the conjectured limit of efficient Gibbs sampling by classical local dynamics.

</details>


### [7] [Practical Ellipse Fitting for Atom Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10289)
*N. Fowler,D. B. Reinhardt,M Meister,C. A. Sackett*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了用于差分原子干涉仪相位提取的多类椭圆拟合方法（统计、代数、几何与贝叶斯方法），并提出一种新的"倒数"几何方法，指出各方法优劣依赖于具体应用场景，并附Python与Matlab代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分原子干涉测量需从两台各自含噪声的干涉仪中提取差分相移，常用手段统称椭圆拟合，但缺乏针对原子干涉参数的多种方法系统对比。

Method: 围绕原子干涉相关的参数与性能指标，对统计法、代数法、几何法和贝叶斯法四类椭圆拟合方法进行对比评估，并推导提出一种新的"倒数"几何方法；补充材料提供各方法的基础Python与Matlab代码。

Result: 给出了各类椭圆拟合方法在精度、稳健性等性能上的比较结果，表明不同方法在偏置、方差、计算开销等方面各有长短，新提出的倒数几何法具备一定优势。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍最优的椭圆拟合方法，方法选择应依据具体使用场景的需求；新"倒数"几何方法可作为有竞争力的备选方案。

Abstract: In differential atom interferometry, it is necessary to extract the differential phase shift between two interferometers that are individually noisy. A variety of techniques can be used for this purpose, under the general label of ellipse fitting. We compare several methods for ellipse fitting, considering parameters and performance relevant to atom interferometry. Statistical, algebraic, geometric and Bayesian methods are discussed, and we describe a novel "reciprocal" geometric method that offers some benefits. Each method has advantages and disadvantages that depend on the use case required. Supplemental material includes basic Python and Matlab code for each method.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文研究了外磁通偏置调控transmon频率时对耦合腔体造成显著损耗的问题，并通过离面磁通软管和片上多级滤波线路等硬件方案，实现了宽带磁通控制与数百微秒的单光子寿命，从而实时调控Rabi模型的失谐与耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中长寿命玻色模式是量子信息处理的重要平台，引入人工原子频率可调性可大幅扩展可达动力学空间；但用外磁通偏置调控transmon频率会给耦合腔带来显著损耗，使单光子寿命降至数十微秒，削弱玻色模式存储与操控多光子量子态的能力。

Method: 探索多种硬件方案以在不牺牲保护的前提下增强微波腔的磁通控制，包括由磁通软管布线的离面远程磁通源，以及具备多级滤波的片上光刻印制线路。

Result: 系统获得宽带磁通控制能力，光子寿命提升至数百微秒，并实现对Rabi模型两个核心参数（失谐与耦合强度）的实时调控，进而支持非线性标定、可调耦合强度的参量共振激活以及数选择性transmon-腔跃迁用于qudit态制备与控制等操作原语。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits hosting long-lived bosonic modes are an excellent platform for emulating the dynamics of light and matter and exploiting them for quantum information processing. The space of accessible dynamics is greatly extended by introducing the frequency tunability of artificial atoms, whereby the interactions with the light field of a microwave cavity can be modified on demand. However, using external flux bias to control the frequency of transmons introduces significant losses to coupled cavities, reducing their single-photon lifetimes to a few tens of $μ$s. These imperfections compromise the usefulness of the bosonic mode, which loses its ability to store and manipulate the quantum information of many-photon states. This thesis investigates the issue and demonstrates how to enhance the flux control of microwave cavities without compromising protection. Several hardware solutions were explored, including off-plane remote flux sources routed by a magnetic hose, and lithographically printed on-chip lines equipped with multiple stages of filtering. These developments equip the systems with broadband flux control and photon lifetimes of several hundred $μ$s. They enable the real-time manipulation of the two central parameters of the Rabi model: detuning and coupling strength. This flexibility translates into operational primitives of interest, such as the calibration of nonlinearities to different interaction regimes, the activation of parametric resonances with adjustable coupling strengths, and the use of number-selective transmon-cavity transitions for qudit state preparation and control. These results lay the groundwork for exploring richer synthetic light-matter systems, controlling cavities with more complex nonlinear circuits, and engineering driven-dissipative systems with applications in quantum computation and sensing.

</details>


### [9] [Adaptive Protocol for Rényi Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10330)

Abstract: The second-order Rényi entropy is one of the most important quantities used for characterizing the entanglement structure of states prepared in quantum many-body experiments. With access to qubit projective measurement data, it can be estimated via a protocol in which repeated measurements are taken after each of a set of random local unitary operations is applied to the quantum state. To obtain accurate values for the second Rényi entropy on shot-limited devices, care must be taken when choosing the number of different unitary settings to apply, and the respective number of projective measurements to take. In this paper, we propose an adaptive protocol for estimating the Rényi entropy that iteratively optimizes the ratio of basis settings to measurements, using a gradient-based approach and starting from an initial small set of measurements. Using synthetic data, we demonstrate that for several prototypical states, our method converges to values for this ratio that are comparable to the optimal values obtained by a brute-force grid search, and it exhibits slower growth of the measurement budget required for a fixed target accuracy than fixed-ratio protocols. Our adaptive protocol therefore promotes efficient estimation of the Rényi entropy in finite-size, shot-limited quantum experiments by making effective use of the available measurement budget.

Abstract: Simulating high dimensional unstructured Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) with quantum processors is a major challenge with significant potential benefit. Existing methods have been demonstrated at small qubit counts, on simplifications of realistic problems. We implement a quantum reservoir network (QRN) for forecasting high-dimensional unstructured PDEs using up to 52 qubits on IBM superconducting hardware. Our method is gradient-free and does not suffer from the challenges of variational quantum algorithms. Instead, the QRN provides a large kernel space, up to 2^52 dimensions and beyond the reach of exact classical simulation, that remains useful under current hardware noise levels. Furthermore, a partial measure-and-reset mechanism enables total circuit runtime for ~24 times the qubit coherence time, so qubit lifetimes do not limit the forecast horizon. Thus, our method enables forecasting realistic unstructured nonlinear PDEs on quantum hardware. Typical difficulties associated with predicting large scale PDEs, such as transpilation, exponential shot requirements, hardware noise, and short qubit lifetimes are mitigated through careful design considerations and a dynamic observable reconstruction method. We validate our approach using a ~34k cell unstructured 2D cylinder flow at Reynolds number 1000 and a challenging nonlinear Gray--Scott reaction--diffusion problem, demonstrating high fidelity prediction on CUDA-Q simulation and on 26 and 52-qubit superconducting quantum processors. Specifically, we show that adding QRN features to an autoencoder linear prediction pipeline reduces prediction error by 21%-43%. Finally, we demonstrate closed-loop autoregressive PDE forecasting on these problems. This work demonstrates, for the first time, quantum circuits with depth beyond qubit lifetimes achieving accurate unstructured mesh forecasting on highly nonlinear PDEs.

Abstract: Given a finite nonlocal game, can the players win with certainty? We prove that the existence of a perfect normal spatial strategy is $Σ^0_2$-complete, even when restricted to games of value one, so it cannot be decided even with an oracle for the halting problem. This is strictly harder than perfect play in the finite-dimensional and commuting-operator models, which are $Σ^0_1$-complete and $Π^0_1$-complete. Within the commuting model, the existence of a perfect normal strategy with a finite or semifinite von Neumann host is $Σ^0_2$-complete, whereas for every fixed type $\mathrm{III}_λ$ subtype it has the same $Π^0_1$-complete language as unrestricted commuting play. A trace thus costs one existential number quantifier: the hardness constructions force every perfect strategy to choose a finite clock period or a finite tree depth. Spatial $Σ^0_2$-completeness also holds when finite entanglement entropy is required, and both forms are formally verified in Lean. For every computable $0<α<1$, perfect spatial play with $\mathrm{Tr}\,h^α<\infty$ for the reduced density $h$ is $Σ^0_2$-complete as well; these conditions form a strict ladder between finite rank and finite entropy. We also prove $Σ^0_2$-completeness for one-shot zero-error communication with normal entanglement, and construct a finite channel with zero-error codes of a given size each of which needs infinite entanglement entropy.

Abstract: Most proposed applications of boson samplers, including photonic machine-learning models, do not use the samples themselves but estimates of output probabilities, marginals or overlaps, obtained to additive precision from a finite number of shots. For product inputs such as single photons, these quantities can already be estimated classically with Gurvits's algorithm and its generalisations. We ask whether entangled inputs change this picture. For any $n$-photon input that is a matrix-product state of bond dimension $χ$, with one photon per site and $L$ modes per site, we give an unbiased estimator of any output amplitude of an $m$-mode interferometer, whose mean-square error after $N$ draws is at most $\|ψ\|^2/N$ whatever the interferometer, the output pattern and the entanglement of the state. Each draw costs $O(nLχ^2+nL^3+mn)$ operations after $O(nLχ^3)$ preprocessing, and an additive error $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$ requires $O(\|ψ\|^2\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ draws, with memory of the order of the input size. The main application is to light emitted sequentially by quantum emitters, such as spin-based sources of photonic cluster states, for which $χ$ is bounded by the dimension of the source's retained quantum memory: one spin gives $χ=2$ at any length, and a memory of $k$ qubits, held in emitters or in photons stored in a delay line, gives $χ\le2^k$, so the cost stays polynomial as long as $k=O(\log n)$. The same sampler also estimates traced-memory probabilities, overlaps and individual marginal probabilities. All guarantees are additive: the estimator gives neither relative estimates of exponentially small probabilities nor a sampler of the output distribution.

</details>

Abstract: The variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) remains a cornerstone hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for quantum simulation, yet its scalability is fundamentally constrained by heavy measurement overheads. While strategies such as variance-preserved shot reduction (VPSR) mitigates this cost, they suffer from convergence instability and optimization stagnation caused by finite-sample bias in limited pilot shots. To overcome this limitation, we introduce delta-corrected shot reduction (DCSR), an analytically enhanced shot allocation strategy that rigorously mitigates finite-sample instability. Utilizing the multivariate delta method, we derive explicit higher-order bias correction terms for the empirical reduction factor. Both theoretical analysis and numerical simulations reveal that incorporating higher-order statistical moments, such as skewness and kurtosis, provides the gradient resolution necessary to bypass noise-induced barren plateaus. DSCR significantly accelerates convergence compared to standard VPSR across diverse noise and resource regimes while matching or decreasing cumulative measurement costs, establishing a mathematically rigorous framework for variance-stabilized shot allocation on near-term hardware.

Abstract: We benchmark the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) as the binary subproblem solver in a two-block alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) for mixed-binary problems against classical solvers under matched budgets. With 23 optimizer evaluations per subproblem, scoring determines the comparison: on its final readout, the optimized QAOA loses to random search at $n=12$ and 16 binary variables (inconclusive at 20; exploratory and post hoc tests). When credited with its best sample, it wins. In a noiseless 12-qubit simulation, transferred-angle depth-2 QAOA finds the exact optimum of 97.3\% of planted-family subproblems, against 50.6\%--67.4\% for the four strongest local heuristics (100\% against 90.4\%--94.4\% crediting the four best bitstrings) and a not significantly different 96.5\% for semidefinite rounding with local search (SDP+LS). Under our evaluation-counting convention, its time to solution there is a factor of three to five below the local heuristics' but grows with a larger exponent; it is ten times that of SDP+LS and, in seconds, 54 times that of tabu search with restarts. For the benchmark class, an unconstrained binary quadratic problem in disguise, ADMM's binary step is a proximal step on the reduced problem: at unit penalty ADMM re-solves it, and for larger penalties exact solutions increasingly stall. On two families that do not reduce to one, less accurate solvers found the problem optimum more often at unit and larger penalties. No family gave QAOA a better ADMM outcome than the best classical solver and penalty. The benchmark includes a correctness gate and a log of its hypothesis tests.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前QAOA硬件验证通常只比较设备的平均代价与模拟值，但这一指标与优化真正关心的目标（找到最优解）并不一致：平均代价可能主要由避开较差比特串决定，而最优解的识别未得到检验。因此需要明确噪声下各统计量保持率的理论关系，并用实验检验平均指标能否代表最优解的质量。

Method: 1）理论部分：在全局去极化噪声模型下，推导平均归一化差距、以及每个比特串集合（含最优解）的底面修正概率（floor-corrected probability），证明它们保留与平均代价相同比例的无噪声相对均匀采样的改进量。2）实验部分：在IBM Heron处理器ibm_marrakesh上运行深度2、角度迁移的QAOA电路，求解交替方向分解（alternating-direction decomposition）产生的二值子问题，分别开启/关闭动态去耦（dynamical decoupling）与twirling。3）在12比特规模下统计平均差距保持率、最优解概率保持率、近优比特串保持率及较差比特串规避保持率；并比较首轮提交中设备匹配噪声模型的预测偏差。4）构建"崎岖植入解"（rugged planted）族，以768-shot分块统计最优解命中率，并与局部启发式比较。

Result: 12比特主族电路保留了29%–46%的平均差距改进，但最优解概率仅保留5%–15%，在每轮运行中至少10个实例里有9个出现该现象。近优比特串的损失与最优解相当，而"避开较差比特串"这一能力保持得最好。首轮提交的设备匹配噪声模型能复现该模式，但将最优解保持率高估了5–8倍。在崎岖植入解族上，设备在768-shot分块中有62%找到最优解（无噪声情形为98%），处于局部启发式的水平范围内。

Conclusion: 平均代价/平均差距与最优解概率在噪声下并非等价指标：平均指标的高保持率主要由规避差解驱动，掩盖了最优解与近优解的大幅衰减。因此QAOA等优化电路的硬件验证应同时报告最优解及近优解集合的保持率，而非仅报告平均代价；设备匹配噪声模型也需谨慎校准，否则会显著高估最优解的可获得性。

Abstract: Hardware executions of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) are often validated by comparing the device's mean cost with a simulation, a check that can miss what matters for optimization. Under global depolarization, the mean normalized gap and the floor-corrected probability of every set of bitstrings, the optimum included, retain the same fraction of their noiseless improvement over uniform sampling. We test this prediction with depth-2, transferred-angle QAOA circuits for binary subproblems of an alternating-direction decomposition on the IBM Heron processor \texttt{ibm\_marrakesh}, with and without dynamical decoupling and twirling. At 12 qubits, the main-family circuits retained 29\% to 46\% of their mean-gap improvement but only 5\% to 15\% of their probability of the optimum, lower on at least nine of ten instances per run. Near-optimal bitstrings were lost about as much as the optimum, whereas the avoidance of poor bitstrings was retained best. In the first submission, a device-matched noise model reproduced the pattern but overestimated the retention of the optimum five- to eightfold. On a rugged planted family, the device found the optimum in 62\% of 768-shot blocks (noiseless: 98\%), within the range of local heuristics. Validations of optimization circuits should report retention of the optimum and of near-optimal sets.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典模型常报告良好的图像任务精度，但很少能厘清量子部分是否真正起作用，因此需要消融与对照实验来检验量子组件的贡献。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical models often report good accuracy on image tasks, but it is rarely clear whether the quantum part is responsible. This paper proposes QCA-DenseNet, a static American Sign Language (ASL) recognition pipeline in which an eight-qubit variational circuit acts as a channel-attention gate on DenseNet121 features. The quantum channel attention (QCA) gate has 17,464 parameters, 7.51 times fewer than a squeeze-and-excitation (SE) block. The pipeline is tested on 1,815 MU HandImages images by holding out one of five signers at a time, and a frozen-backbone ablation uses small parts of the Kaggle ASL Alphabet data. On unseen signers, QCA-DenseNet reaches 94.50% mean accuracy, above SE (93.72%) and within 0.28 points of the best model. A matched classical MLP and a model without attention perform about the same, and no difference is significant. QCA gives a much smaller attention gate without a detectable loss in accuracy, but no quantum advantage.

</details>

*Emilio Semre,Steven Frankel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：在压缩量子优化中，解码非对易可观测量这一步骤会带来结构性限制，且更换初始化（而非加深线路）可以消除该限制；同时用数值实验区分"掩码选择效应"与"能量优化效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩量子优化需将变分态通过解码不可对易可观测量转成经典赋值。作者希望指出：这一解码步骤本身会引入结构性限制，且这类限制即使增加电路深度也无法解除，但改变初始化可以；并强调需区分"解码表达力"、"能量优化"与"经验性能"三个层面。

Method: 对 n=3r 个顶点的连通三次图构造无穷族：固定 Pauli 编码与确定性符号解码器下，证明任意有限深度、任意实角序列的 all-plus QAOA 割不超过 2r。再构造固定深度一角度下的均匀带符号乘积态掩码，期望割 13r/4（加性优势 5n/12），并给出存在最优掩码的无穷子族；第二个构造在弱加权耦合、有界代价-角度域与指定生产编码器排序下保留线性深度一分隔。数值上在 1,699 个随机三次 MaxCut 实例上比较"先选掩码后优化能量"与基线，并在加权 MaxCut、稀疏 Ising、Max-2-XOR、Max-2-SAT 全队列及有限池次序统计中做检验。

Result: 随机三次 MaxCut 上，所选掩码平均比 0.9100，all-plus X 基线 0.7067，r² 个独立优化随机掩码最优值 0.9125。加权 MaxCut、稀疏 Ising、Max-2-XOR、Max-2-SAT 上所选掩码在各规模均优于基线，但小规模落后于池最优、仅在较大 MaxCut 与 Ising 规模显著领先。

Conclusion: 结论区分了"解码表达力"与"优化能量"及"经验性能"；所观察到的优势来自掩码选择而非量子优化本身，作者明确不主张量子计算优势。

Abstract: In compressed quantum optimization, a variational state is converted into a classical assignment by decoding noncommuting observables. We show that this step creates structural restrictions that changing the initialization can remove, even when increasing circuit depth cannot. For an explicit infinite family of connected cubic graphs on $n=3r$ vertices, with a fixed Pauli encoding and deterministic sign decoder, every all-plus QAOA circuit has cut at most $2r$, at every finite depth and every real angle sequence. A uniform signed product-state mask at fixed depth-one angles has expected cut $13r/4$, an additive advantage $5n/12$; an infinite subfamily admits an optimal mask. A second construction retains a linear depth-one separation under weak weighted couplings, a bounded cost-angle domain and a specified production-encoder ordering. Noiseless simulations then test classical mask selection before energy optimization. On 1,699 random cubic MaxCut instances, one selected mask achieves mean ratio $0.9100$, versus $0.7067$ for the all-plus $X$ baseline and $0.9125$ for the best of $r^2$ separately optimized random masks. On complete cohorts of weighted MaxCut, sparse Ising, Max-2-XOR and Max-2-SAT, the selected mask also improves on the baseline at every size, but it trails the pool at small sizes and leads it significantly only at the larger MaxCut and Ising sizes. Finite-pool order statistics separate the effect of selection from that of drawing a random mask. The results distinguish decoded expressivity from optimized energy and empirical performance; they do not establish a quantum computational advantage.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：任何具有经典控制因果序的量子电路，无论调用输入信道多少次（有限次），都无法精确且确定性地模拟量子开关对任意输入信道的作用；该不可能性对量子比特信道即已成立，并可推广到完整 r-switch，从而彻底回答了量子开关的精确确定性电路模拟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关是一种以不定因果序组合输入信道的高阶变换。一个自然且基础的问题是：量子电路能否模拟其作用、若能则需多少次输入信道调用？这直接关系到"对因果序进行量子控制"这一能力是否可被经典控制因果序的电路所复现，即不定因果序过程是否具有超越经典因果结构的计算能力。

Method: 采用严格的理论证明（不可能性证明）。设定模拟模型为"经典控制因果序的量子电路"（即电路的信道调用顺序由经典控制决定，不允许因果序的相干叠加），要求模拟对任意输入信道都精确（exact）且确定性（deterministic）成立，并允许任意有限次调用输入信道；在此框架下证明不存在这样的模拟电路。证明先在量子比特信道上给出，再推广至相干控制 r 个输入信道全部置换的完整 r-switch。

Result: 证明了不存在任何有限次调用的经典控制因果序量子电路能够精确、确定性地模拟量子开关；该不可能性已对量子比特信道成立，并延伸至完整 r-switch。因此，对量子开关的精确确定性电路模拟问题给出了（否定性的）最终答案。

Conclusion: 具有不定因果序的过程，在此设定下一般无法被经典控制因果序的量子电路高效模拟；不定因果序所提供的能力不能被经典因果序电路（即便允许任意有限次调用）以精确确定性方式复现，凸显了因果序量子控制的本质性优势。

Abstract: The quantum switch is a higher-order transformation that combines input quantum channels in an indefinite causal order. Whether quantum circuits can simulate its action, and how many calls to the input channels such a simulation requires, are natural questions about the power of quantum control of causal order. In this work, we prove that no quantum circuit with classically controlled causal order can exactly and deterministically simulate the quantum switch for arbitrary input channels using any finite number of calls. This impossibility holds already for qubit channels and extends to the complete $r$-switch, which coherently controls all permutations of $r$ input channels. These results settle the question of exact deterministic circuit simulation of the quantum switch. They demonstrate that processes with indefinite causal order cannot, in general, be efficiently simulated in this setting even by quantum circuits with classically controlled causal order.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we use a quantum circuit called the Graph Encoding Operator which, applied to the complement $\bar G$ of any graph $G$, yields an oracle for the maximum clique problem suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Analogously, we devise a Hypergraph Encoding Operator which, when applied to a hypergraph $\mathcal H$, yields an oracle for the minimum hitting set suitable for amplification via Grover's algorithm. Interestingly, the latter oracle provides, when measured, an approximation of the number of hitting sets of the hypergraph, thus solving the dualization problem for positive Boolean functions represented in a positive irredundant disjunctive normal form. We give the full construction of both operators, prove their correctness, analyze their complexity, and provide an implementation on a quantum simulator. We discuss the resulting complexity, contrast them with existing quantum computer algorithms based on Grover search and we will show that our algorithms outperform, to the best of our knowledge, all of the previous algorithms on space computational complexity by requiring only $O(N)$ number of qubits with respect to the number of vertices $N$, for representing both graphs and hypergraphs in the encoding operator and, consequently, results in an oracle for both graphs and hypergraphs that uses only $O(N)$ qubits even when the hypergraph contains an exponential (on the number of vertices) number of hyperedges.

Abstract: We demonstrate a novel QKD protocol, named \texttt{Bridge[$d,q$]}, which generalizes the \texttt{SARG04} protocol to $d$ dimensions and $q$ bases. Abstract: Parameterized instantaneous quantum polynomial-time (IQP) circuits generate distributions conjectured to be hard to sample classically. As reinforcement learning (RL) policies, their $2^n$ measurement outcomes are typically mapped to a smaller action space by a classical decoder. This aggregation can mitigate initialization concentration while making the policy classically simulable. We characterize this tradeoff through the decoder's Fourier--Pauli expansion. Under independent uniform initialization, we express policy variance as an exact sum over groups of nonconstant Pauli components with identical active-generator signatures. Each group contributes its squared aggregate coefficient times $2^{-r}$, where $r$ is the binary rank of its active generators. An inverse-polynomial aggregate coefficient with logarithmic rank prevents exponential policy concentration. Sparse Pauli expansions and contiguous action bins permit efficient classical additive estimation of action probabilities. We introduce a majority decoder combining protected low-weight components with exponentially large exact Fourier support, preserving initialization nonconcentration while excluding polynomial-size enumeration of the exact expansion. Numerical experiments corroborate the variance laws. In ten-qubit CartPole experiments, majority achieves higher returns than recursive parity and the tested local readouts, while higher-weight Pauli sectors contribute an increasing relative share of gradient magnitude during training. A complementary 21-qubit block-chain policy outperforms an extensive pool of fully connected classical neural networks in episode efficiency, even though its bounded-width structure permits efficient classical simulation. These results guide decoder design for quantum and quantum-inspired RL, distinguishing policy nonconcentration, learning performance, and classical simulability.

On 500 out-of-sample days two one-sided tests place the trained-versus-frozen dierence inside 0.03 annualised Sharpe (p = 0.0055, minimum detectable eect 0.02): the circuit is equivalent to a frozen one, not merely indistinguishable from it. With the circuit as the head's only input the two are equivalent inside 0.25; against the network with no circuit the design resolves only 0.50. Abstract: We prove variants of the marginal-constrained entropy accumulation theorem (MEAT) that enable finite-size security analysis of quantum key distribution with source and detector correlations. For source correlations, our results allow for the state prepared in a given round to depend on the current setting choice and a finite number of preceding choices. For detector correlations, our results require memory effects to persist for a finite number of rounds after a registered click, and all detectors to be switched off during those rounds. These results can be combined, providing, to the best of our knowledge, the first method to jointly treat correlated source and detector imperfections in quantum key distribution. Moreover, we retain all the benefits of the MEAT, such as (but not limited to) support for on-the-fly announcements and choice of adaptive tradeoff functions. The resulting key rates can be computed via a single-round optimization amenable to existing numerical methods. We demonstrate these results on qubit BB84, and exhibit competitive key rates even in the presence of correlated source and detector imperfections.

Abstract: Variational quantum classifiers can generate highly structured decision boundaries through the Fourier dependence of observable expectation values on circuit parameters. We show that expressivity can come at a sharp statistical cost, by considering a quantum perceptron whose readout is the simplest periodic classifier, namely a square-wave function of the weighted inputs, and determine its typical generalization in the thermodynamic limit using a teacher-student replica-symmetric theory. Above a critical oscillation frequency, increasing the training load induces a first-order transition from weakly to strongly teacher-aligned phases, resulting in a discontinuous improvement in generalization. At fixed load, however, increasing the frequency eventually drives the equilibrium generalization error to chance level, despite perfect classification of all the training examples. Thus, the same periodic structure that enhances random-label storage can delay the onset of learning. Our results expose a quantitative tradeoff between memorization and generalization generated purely by periodic readout, and provide a benchmark for understanding Fourier-rich variational quantum classifiers.

Abstract: A projective measurement of one site in the ground state of a quantum chain disturbs the chain in two ways: learning the outcome shifts distant expectation values at once, through ground-state correlations, and the measurement erases the correlations between the measured site and the rest. Both changes travel outward. We solve this problem exactly for the Kitaev chain, the cluster chain in a transverse field, and the Kitaev chain with power-law couplings, and we find that far from the measured site every quadratic observable responds through a front. Where the front is and how fast it broadens depend only on the quasiparticle dispersion. How strong it is depends on two operators: how strongly the measured one creates the fastest quasiparticles and how strongly the observed one detects them; if either factor vanishes, the front is weaker and decays faster. One measurement therefore launches many fronts, one per observable, with a common position and width but different decay laws. In the Kitaev chain the density and the bond energy, the two terms of its nearest-neighbor Hamiltonian, respond to one density measurement with decay laws that trade places between the topological and the trivial phase; in the cluster chain, measuring a symmetry-odd spin instead of a symmetry-even one changes the critical decay law at a fixed Hamiltonian; and with power-law couplings the width law itself changes. Abstract: Clifford and Clifford's algorithm samples single-photon boson sampling exactly, one output mode at a time, by adding the input photons in a uniformly random order. The excess intensity also provides a lower bound on the accumulated transport contribution beyond the separable limit. We further introduce an environment that responds to emerging order by suppressing local coherence and activating classical transport. The patterns then persist as local entanglement is lost, with both stages arising from a single time-independent Lindblad equation. Applications certify probabilistic and strong contextuality and give the leading shortfall of the distance between the poles of the fuzzy sphere; in a model of monogamous entanglement the pairs that weight-one threads representing operational ER=EPR can occupy at once are exactly the partial matchings.

Abstract: Sklar's theorem in classical probability theory rigorously separates the marginal statistics of a joint probability distribution from its dependence structure data. This means that the local information of the subsystems, discarded table by table, is fully recovered. We call this quantum-state reconstruction from Sklar-type relational data.

Abstract: We establish a microscopic foundation for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in active media. Abstract: The dynamical Casimir effect was first predicted by G. T. Moore in 1969, who considered a real scalar field inside a cavity with a moving mirror in ($1+1$)D, satisfying Dirichlet boundary conditions. Moore wrote the general solution for the field in terms of a function that satisfies a functional equation nowadays known as the Moore equation.In the literature, exact analytical solutions to the Moore equation form a rather small set, restricted to specific classes of laws of motion. Cole and Schieve (1995) developed a geometrical approach that yielded a general formula for the solution of the Moore equation for cavities initially at rest. However, its application has always been done through numerical calculations, and their approach has therefore long been regarded solely as a numerical method. In this work, we show that their method can also be employed as a powerful method for deriving exact analytical solutions, and show the applicability of this approach to deriving analytical solutions to the Moore equation. We obtain original exact analytical solutions for an expanding cavity with a uniformly moving mirror, a contracting cavity with a mirror that approaches the static one asymptotically, and, in addition, we recover the first analytical solution to the Moore equation for a resonant mirror motion, originally obtained by Law in 1994. Moreover, we extend the Cole-Schieve approach, showing that exact solutions can be constructed from any spacetime region where the Moore function has already been determined. We show that this extended geometrical approach allows us to expand the set of known analytical solutions to the Moore equation from the few classes of motion reported in the literature to an infinite set, as it enables the treatment of new classes of laws of motion obtained by combining those presented here with each other as well as with previously known classes of trajectories.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson-Ambainis猜想称每个从{0,1}^n映到[0,1]的d次多项式至少有一个变量的影响度不低于poly(Var(p)/d)。若成立，则可推出量子查询算法在大多数输入上可被经典高效模拟，并在随机预言机模型与非相对化世界中给出BPP与BQP问题的近乎等价。作者希望以清晰的步骤化方式呈现这一结果，降低理解门槛。

Conclusion: 该证明结构可被清晰分解与讲解，为后续研究与教学提供了可读性更强的入口；作者注明证明由David Turturean借助PSI研究系统与非公开模型发现，本文仅为阐述，不打算在会议或期刊发表，且作者未获经济补偿。

Abstract: We give an exposition of a recent proof of the Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture, which states that every degree-$d$ polynomial $p : \{0,1\}^n \to [0,1]$ has a variable of influence at least $\mathrm{poly}(\mathrm{Var}(p)/d)$. This implies that quantum query algorithms for decision problems can be efficiently simulated classically on most inputs. It also yields a near-equivalence between the status of the $\mathsf{BPP}$ vs. $\mathsf{BQP}$ problem in the random oracle and unrelativized worlds.
  We organize the proof around four main steps and discuss how each connects to the literature on the analysis of boolean functions and probabilistic combinatorics. To bring out the key ideas, we do not optimize parameters, and our presentation yields weaker exponents than the original.
  $\textbf{Authors' note.}$ The proof was discovered by David Turturean using a research system developed at Physical Superintelligence (PSI), in combination with a non-public Anthropic model. The proof was then shared with the authors, who worked to understand it and prepare this exposition. Anthropic's public models were used in this process.
  The authors did not receive financial compensation from Anthropic or PSI. This article is not intended for publication at a conference or in a journal.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pauli传播（Pauli propagation）的计算代价依赖于演化算符展开所采用的局部基，但此前缺少对基依赖性的定量刻画，也缺少系统选择良好局部基的实用方法。

Abstract: The cost of Pauli propagation depends on the local basis in which the evolving operator is expanded. We show that its Pauli complexity splits into an occupation part, invariant under local rotations and bounded from below by the operator mass and length distributions, and a flavour part that a suitable basis can remove. We introduce frame-adaptive Pauli propagation (FAPP), which rotates the basis of every qubit, gate by gate, to the principal axes of the operator's local polarisation, exactly and within the memory structure of Pauli propagation. This simple rule achieves most of what the best local frame can, and the polarisation predicts how much any local frame can gain. In the Ising chain in transverse and longitudinal fields, FAPP lowers the truncation error at fixed memory by up to an order of magnitude and the memory by a size-independent factor of three; on the infinite two-dimensional lattice it needs up to seven times fewer strings for a given certified accuracy, and in Clifford circuits doped with T gates two to eight times fewer.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献把器件噪声模型的"家族选择"当作硬件属性，缺乏可在实验前量化的判据，也缺乏统一评估"家族—设计"耦合的框架；作者希望建立可计算、设计敏感的判据。

Method: 把家族选择形式化为假设检验，用闭式散粒噪声下限 $N^*=\lambda_{\mathrm{req}}/(r_b S)^2$（由绝对偏置 $r_b S$ 控制）计算代价；用同一映射读取指纹（固定残余）与 Fisher 信息（排序设计）；在两种平台上穷举 64 种设计放置，比较下限、秩、条件数与最优计数。

Result: 设计盲块处两平台分别为 98.1% 与 48.0%；参考点下限为 $1.1\times10^7$ 与 $6.7\times10^6$ 每分量散粒，$10^5$ 散粒下检验功效仅 0.053（水平 0.05），报告的残余为散粒噪声。64 种设计重建指纹使下限在平台上分别变动 68 倍与 1.7 倍，最精确设计比最低成本设计贵 10 倍。64 种放置均满秩，故为条件数问题：三量子比特时仅放置变化使 rms 变化 2.7 与 3.9 倍，计数最优平坦；五量子比特时同一规则使下限变动 21 倍，判决成本 $10^9$ 散粒；对照真实三量子比特寄存器标定，下限本质是对系统控制的要求。

Conclusion: 家族选择是可预计算的统计判决而非硬件属性，绝对偏置决定散粒噪声下限；设计优化是条件数问题，且家族与设计不可分离；实际瓶颈是系统控制而非散粒数。

Abstract: Family selection in generator-model noise tomography, usually quoted as a property of the hardware, is a hypothesis test whose shot floor is closed form, $N^{*}=λ_{\mathrm{req}}/(r_{\mathrm{b}} S)^{2}$, controlled by the absolute bias $r_{\mathrm{b}} S$ rather than by the relative residual $r_{\mathrm{b}}$, and therefore computable for any device before a single shot is taken. The same computation prices the recovery gates, because the fingerprint that fixes a family's residual and the Fisher information that ranks the designs are read off the same map: there is no design-independent verdict and no family-independent design optimum. Whether a design can move that price is structural, not numerical: a bias confined to the block every design measures identically is design-blind, while one that reaches the code blocks can be repriced by a factor of sixty-eight. The two platforms here are its extremes, at $98.1\%$ and $48.0\%$ in the design-blind block. At the reference point the floor is $1.1\times10^{7}$ and $6.7\times10^{6}$ shots per component for the two families, so at the $10^{5}$ shots a run spends the test has power $0.053$ at a level of $0.05$ and the residual it reports is shot noise. Rebuilding the fingerprint under each of the sixty-four designs moves the floor by $68$ on one platform and $1.7$ on the other, and the most precise design costs ten times more shots for the verdict than the cheapest to test. All sixty-four placements are full rank, so the design problem is one of conditioning: at three gates the rms varies by factors of $2.7$ and $3.9$ with placement alone, while the optimum over counts is flat. At five qubits the same rule moves the floor by $21$, while the verdict itself costs $10^{9}$ shots; checked against the calibration of a real three-qubit register, the floor is a requirement on systematic control before it is a shot count.

</details>


### [53] [Advantage Distillation in Paired-State Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11754)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez,José Mauricio López-Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究配对态回环（Loop-Back）QKD 中"终端轻量化、光站共享"架构下的优势蒸馏（advantage distillation）：用最小连通图上的两条独立公开奇偶校验，从三条两比特记录中筛选一致块，把主导误码项由 e 降为 e²，并给出精确接受概率、残余错误律、保留率及含任意量子边信息的模块化安全定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回环 QKD 允许一个固定光站服务低成本便携终端（终端只需制备偏振脉冲并读取返回探测图样），但这类非对称、探测结果需回传给 Alice 的结构，其误码筛选与安全分析缺少严格刻画；同时需要一种能与后续常规协调（reconciliation）衔接、成本可精确核算的前置筛选手段。

Method: (1) 协议结构：Alice 制备两路偏振编码脉冲，Bob 对每路选择并施加偏振滤波，Alice 由返回探测图样推断 Bob 的选择；(2) 优势蒸馏：基于三组两比特记录的最小连通图（奇偶多边形）做两条独立公开奇偶校验，并证明其"闭合边"冗余——同一顶点集上任意生成树的接受集相同；(3) 解析推导精确接受概率、残余错误规律与保留率，并显式计入丢弃记录与公开信息开销；(4) 给出模块化安全定理，界定在 syndrome 公开与块被接受之后攻击者可获得的额外猜测优势（含任意量子边信息）；(5) 对指定的单光子无源抽头（passive-tap）光学信道进行定量评估，得到正的条件熵界与接受块的精确安全量。

Result: 在独立比特误码模型下，三元记录滤波器将主导错误项从 e 降至 e²；闭合边冗余性使可实现的校验数最小化而接受集不变；给出精确的接受概率、残余错误律和保留率闭式结果；模块化安全定理给出接受块后对抗性（含量子边信息）猜测优势的上界；在单光子被动抽头信道上获得正的条件熵界及精确的接受块安全量，说明该方案在指定信道下仍具安全性余量。

Conclusion: 该奇偶校验型优势蒸馏通过后选择（postselection）检测差异，而非纠正单个错误，定位为常规协调之前的预处理阶段。作者指出两个后续方向：混合滤波—协调联合设计，以及多个便携用户接入共享量子站时的密钥建立。

Abstract: We investigate advantage distillation for paired-state Loop-Back quantum key distribution, with a fixed optical station serving a lightweight portable terminal. Alice prepares two polarization-encoded pulses, Bob selects and applies a polarization filter to each pulse, and Alice infers his selections from the returned detection pattern. The proposed advantage-distillation step selects consistent blocks of three two-bit records using two independent public parity checks on a minimal connected graph. The closing edge of a parity polygon is shown to be redundant: any spanning tree of the same vertices has the same acceptance set. We derive exact acceptance probabilities, residual-error laws, and retention rates. Under independent bit errors, the three-record filter reduces the leading error term from e to e^2, while its cost in discarded records and public information is accounted for explicitly. A modular security theorem bounds the additional guessing advantage available to an adversary after syndrome disclosure and block acceptance, including arbitrary quantum side information. We also evaluate a specified single-photon passive-tap optical channel, obtaining a positive conditional entropy bound and exact accepted-block security quantities. This parity-based advantage-distillation step detects discrepancies by postselection; it does not correct individual errors and is intended to precede conventional reconciliation. The architecture suggests two further directions: hybrid filtering and reconciliation, and key establishment between portable users accessing a shared quantum station.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帽子游戏此前多从组合/经典角度研究，其作为非局部博弈的量子优势是否可精确刻画、能否达到最优并不清楚；作者希望精确计算该博弈的量子值、量子交换值与无信号值，并明确实现最优优势所需的纠缠资源。

Method: 将两人帽子游戏建模为二输入、三输出的非局部博弈；构造基于共享非最大纠缠 qutrit 态的量子策略达到优势；证明最优性时利用博弈在二面体群 D_8 下的对称性，应用其表示论将底层矩阵优化问题约化、显著降低复杂度；并将量子交换值与量子值对照分析。

Result: 得到 ω_q(H_2)=ω_qc(H_2)=(15+√33)/36≈0.576238，即量子交换值与量子值一致；最优量子策略由非最大纠缠 qutrit 态实现；无信号值为 ω_ns(H_2)=2/3。

Conclusion: 该帽子游戏 H_2 的量子优势可被精确确定，且不需最大纠缠即可达到；D_8 对称性加表示论是处理此类矩阵优化的有效工具；由于 ω_qc=ω_q<ω_ns，该博弈不区分量子与量子交换模型，但存在与无信号值之间的间隙。

Abstract: We study the two-player hat game as a two-input, three-output non-local game. We calculate the quantum commuting value and show that it coincides with the quantum one. In particular, we show that \[ω_{\rm q}(H_2)=ω_{\mathrm{qc}}(H_2)=\frac{15+\sqrt{33}}{36}\approx 0.576238.\] We achieve the optimal quantum advantage using a shared non-maximally entangled qutrit state. To establish optimality, we exploit the game's symmetry under the dihedral group $D_8$ and apply its representation theory to substantially reduce the complexity of the underlying matrix problem. We also determine the no-signalling value $ω_{\mathrm{ns}}(H_2)=2/3$.

</details>

Abstract: The resolution of conventional imaging is fundamentally constrained by the diffraction limit. Here we demonstrate that this limit can be overcome using classical thermal light, through the correlation of multiphoton subsystems. By resolving the photon numbers detected in the two arms of a Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometer, the correlation function exhibits a narrowed peak or dip whose width is tunable via the average photon number. Temporal and spatial super-resolution enhancements up to 11.8-fold and 5.1-fold, respectively, are experimentally achieved. As a proof-of-principle experiment, a double-pinhole that is completely unresolved by standard ghost imaging becomes clearly distinguishable via the correlation of two-photon versus zero-photon. The principle of this super-resolution imaging can be interpreted as a combination of classical super-resolution imaging and quantum super-resolution imaging. This work provides a versatile platform for super-resolution imaging and lithography, and opens a new avenue for exploring the boundary between classical and nonclassical phenomena.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code possesses a lattice periodic structure that enables the effective correction of small displacement errors induced by noise in phase space, which is indispensable for quantum computation. The GKP code was also used to construct a long-distance entanglement channel in quantum repeaters and distribute random keys in quantum key distribution (QKD). Using GKP encoding, we propose a quantum secure direct communication (QSDC) protocol that not only transmits the secret message directly, but also helps protect the safety of communication personnel. The exponential attenuation loss of photons can be overcome by the third-generation quantum repeater. Numerical simulation results demonstrate that secure transmission distance exceeding 1000 km is achievable with a 23 dB squeezing level. Furthermore, we establish a cost-based optimization framework to characterize the trade-off among secure communication distance, secrecy capacity, and repeater resources, enabling optimized repeater deployment and providing a systematic design strategy for scalable quantum repeater architectures. The results demonstrate the potential of combining GKP error correction for long-distance QSDC and quantum networks.

</details>


### [57] [Rotational Encoding of Objective Functions for Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11858)
*Sonia Yang,Ali Al-Bayaty,Shanyan Chen,Marek Perkowski*

TL;DR: 提出角旋转计数器（ARC），将可分解为布尔函数加权和的目标函数编码为单量子比特寄存器上累积的量子旋转，用于构建有限自动机/状态机状态分配问题的量子oracle，在26个基准上平均降低量子比特数20.8%、门数89.3%、线路深度92.4%、量子代价90.6%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献多以多量子比特寄存器编码加权布尔函数形式的目标函数，量子比特与门开销大。作者希望用更省资源的编码方式表示此类目标函数，以支撑基于量子搜索的优化（如构建量子oracle）。

Method: ARC将目标函数表示为累加到单个量子比特寄存器上的量子旋转之和，而非多量子比特寄存器；数学上证明这些旋转对所有可能解求和等于目标值，并推导测量概率与目标值之间的关系。随后用加权汉明距离代价函数（二次分配问题的特例）为有限自动机与状态机的状态分配问题构造量子oracle，并在26个基准上与基于布尔函数的oracle按四项代价指标对比。

Result: 在26个基准上，ARC oracle相对布尔型oracle平均减少量子比特数20.8%、门数89.3%、线路深度92.4%、量子代价90.6%，且旋转求和的正确性经数学建立与实验验证。

Conclusion: 旋转编码能在所评估基准上显著降低线路代价，是量子搜索类优化中表示目标函数的有前景方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Angular Rotational Counter (ARC), a novel approach to represent objective functions that can be decomposed as a weighted sum of Boolean functions. ARC encodes the objective as an accumulated sum of quantum rotations onto a single-qubit register instead of a multi-qubit register used in much of the previous literature. We mathematically establish and experimentally validate that these rotations sum to the objective value for all possible solutions and derive the relationship between the resulting measurement probabilities and the objective. This design was applied to construct a quantum oracle for the state assignment problem of finite automata and state machines using a weighted Hamming distance cost function, which is a special case of the quadratic assignment problem. We evaluate this oracle design on 26 benchmarks and compare it with corresponding Boolean-based oracles on four cost metrics. Across these benchmarks, the oracle with ARC produces a mean reduction in the qubit count of 20.8%, gate count of 89.3%, circuit depth of 92.4%, and quantum cost of 90.6%. These results demonstrate substantial reductions in circuit cost for the evaluated benchmarks and establish rotational encoding as a promising approach for representing objective functions in optimization based on quantum search algorithms.

</details>


### [58] [AI-Driven Discovery of Entanglement Witness in Spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11935)
*Jiwei Tian,Mouyang Cheng,Mingda Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 EntNet：基于低阶自旋关联观测量（≤四体）的局域幺正不变表示 + Transformer（RoPE），从可观测量中判别纠缠态与全可分混合态，recall >98% 且误报近零，并能跨体系尺寸与哈密顿量族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠是量子科学的关键资源，但从实验可及的观测量出发检测纠缠仍很困难：常用判据（如部分转置、纠缠熵）需要态层析或高成本测量，难以映射到少量低阶局域关联函数上。

Method: 1) 对一维 spin-1/2、短程哈密顿量体系，构造可测 Pauli 关联子（至四体）的局域幺正不变表示；2) 稀疏筛选 16 个候选不变量特征通道；3) 用带旋转位置编码（RoPE）的 Transformer 学习其空间结构，仅以这些低阶可观测量期望值为输入，输出纠缠/可分的判别。

Result: 在可认证纠缠态上 recall >98%，对可分态误报率近零；跨系统尺寸与不同哈密顿量族（含阻挫与无序自旋模型）迁移时精度损失极小；可解释性上，模型显示成对关联的选择性空间组织，且有限温度下的决策边界与 Jordan-Wigner 准粒子激发间隙相吻合。

Conclusion: EntNet 建立了从局域关联函数数据驱动发现纠缠目击者的可迁移、可物理解释的框架，为实验可行的多体纠缠检测提供新路径；局限在于结论基于一维短程 spin-1/2 体系与低阶关联，向更高维、长程相互作用及真实测量噪声的推广仍有待检验。

Abstract: Entanglement is a crucial resource in quantum science and engineering, but detecting it in many-body states from experimentally accessible observables remains challenging. Here we introduce EntNet, an AI-driven framework to discover compact combinations of spin observables that accurately distinguish entangled from fully separable many-body mixed states in one-dimensional spin-$1/2$ chains governed by short-range Hamiltonians. We construct local-unitary-invariant representations of measurable Pauli correlators up to four-body order, sparsely select 16 candidate invariant feature channels, and learn their spatial structure using a Transformer architecture with rotary positional embeddings. Using only expectation values of these low-order observables, EntNet achieves $>98\%$ recall on certified entangled states with near-zero false-positive rates on separable states. Remarkably, it exhibits transferability across system sizes and distinct Hamiltonian families, including frustrated and disordered spin models, with minimal loss of accuracy. Beyond detection, EntNet provides physical insight into entanglement detection by revealing a selective spatial organization of pair correlations, while its finite-temperature decision boundary tracks the excitation gap of Jordan-Wigner quasiparticles. These results establish a transferable and physically interpretable framework for data-driven discovery of entanglement witnesses from local correlation functions.

</details>


### [59] [Parameterized equivalence checking of quantum circuit generators via weighted automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12049)
*Pavel Chuklanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对量子电路生成器的符号化等价性验证方法：对“阶梯型”程序证明其电路矩阵在阈值以上构成统一矩阵乘积算子（加权自动机），从而将等价性规约为差分自动机的零性判定，并模素数检查，输出反例或带误差界的“相等”结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路由程序（生成器）按量子比特数与比特串批量生成，需要在所有参数取值下验证其与参考实现是否等价。固定规模（固定量子比特数）的测试会漏检随规模变化的错误，因此需要参数化、对任意规模都成立的等价性判定手段。

Method: 1) 由生成器根据量子比特数与比特串构造量子电路；2) 定义六种等价关系，对全部参数取值与参考实现比较；3) 对阶梯型程序，证明存在可计算阈值，阈值以上电路矩阵构成统一矩阵乘积算子（MPO），即维数与量子比特数无关的加权自动机；4) 将等价性问题规约为差分自动机的零性问题，并模若干素数进行检验；5) 输出反例，或给出精确“相等”/带计算误差界的“相等”判定。

Abstract: Whether gravity must be quantum is often approached by asking whether two masses can become entangled through their mutual gravitational interaction. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 补全仅以常数因子量子比特开销保持 LDPC 性质；对量子比特码，gauging 并 un-gauging 距离 d 的母码可实现电路故障距离 Ω(d)、轮数 O(d) 的逻辑测量；并以两个环面码 gauge 成 D(D₄) 量子双模型再还原为例，给出显式电路，其电路故障距离等于母码距离。

Conclusion: 补全机制为使用对易校验的容错逻辑测量提供了统一框架，可应用于格点手术制备魔态等场景；其核心价值在于把 gauging 的非对易性障碍转化为可控的、常数开销的构造，并给出与测量轮数无关的严格容错界。

Abstract: Gauging turns a logical operator into a product of local checks, providing a route to logical measurement and lattice surgery. These checks can fail to commute outside the code space, so measuring one can randomize the outcome of another. Adding one completion qubit per noncommuting pair preserves the gauged code space and its distance while keeping the checks Clifford. Completion also preserves the low-density parity-check property with a constant-factor qubit overhead. We generalize completion to qudits and establish bounds on the Clifford-hierarchy level of the completed checks in prime dimensions. Global commutation ensures that a fault-free round fixes the syndrome until the next fault and allows us to bound fault propagation independently of the number of measurement rounds. For qubit codes, we show that gauging and ungauging a distance-$d$ parent code implements a logical measurement with circuit fault distance $Ω(d)$ in $O(d)$ rounds. We demonstrate the construction by gauging two toric codes into the $D(D_4)$ quantum double and back, with an explicit circuit whose circuit fault distance equals the parent code distance. We focus on procedures to characterize the links and evaluate their suitability for QKD. Abstract: Superconducting qubits operate within a well-defined temperature range, making local temperature monitoring important. Hence, a narrow sweep becomes locally indistinguishable from a change in offset or contrast. We illustrate this principle with a strain-swept two-level defect in a Josephson junction. For a reported defect with tunneling energy $Δ_{0}/h=\SI{1.3}{\giga\hertz}$, swept along one branch of its hyperbola to \SI{2.69}{\giga\hertz} (an asymmetry excursion of \SI{2.36}{\giga\hertz}, or \SI{8}{\volt} of piezo voltage), the analysis projects a single-defect fit precision of \SI{0.58}{\percent} at \SI{20}{\milli\kelvin}. The resulting response enables temperature estimation without prior calibration of the readout offset and contrast, while the same sweep overdetermines the Boltzmann form and identifies significant deviations from thermality. These results establish intrinsic junction defects as a resource for calibration-free local thermometry and highlight identifiability as a key design criterion for primary temperature sensing.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the gauge dependence of quantum precision in ultrastrongly coupled light--matter systems. Abstract: Ergotropy is a key quantity of a quantum battery that measures the maximum extractable energy. When the extraction process is not local, a faithful transfer of ergotropy across distance is necessary. In this work, we investigate such transfer by using spin chains with weakened coupling at the boundaries as mediator. Weakening the coupling at the two boundaries of the chain gives rise to localized edge states allowing high fidelity transfer within given time duration. We found that, for long spin chains, the transferred ergotropy alternates between even and odd number of spins. We explain this parity effect by investigating the chain spectral structure. Furthermore, we consider both the nearest-neighbor and long-range interactions between spin. In both model of interactions, weakening the boundary couplings improves the maximum transferred ergotropy for long spin chain, compared with in the case of conventional uniform chain. Although the long-range interaction reduces the transferred ergotropy, the transfer time is reduced, too, in particular for longer chain. In addition to the total transferred ergotropy, we also investigate the effect of initial states of the sender spin to the transport of the coherent and incoherent components of the total ergotropy, and the average transport efficiency.

Abstract: In the distributed quantum Merlin-Arthur ($\mathsf{dQMA}$) model introduced by Fraigniaud, Le Gall, Nishimura, and Paz (ITCS 2021), a network of quantum computers verifies a global property with the aid of a quantum proof supplied by an untrusted prover. While the nodes are computationally unbounded, the challenge is to limit proof size and the communication required for verification. In this paper, we consider the model of Hasegawa, Kundu, and Nishimura (PODC 2024), where the proof must be unentangled across nodes, and define its extension to $k$ unentangled provers, denoted by $\mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$. In this setting, we investigate the proof size per node and the communication costs of reducing the number of provers.
  Our first result reduces the number of provers from $k$ to two, doubling the proof size without additional communication. Moreover, with four additional rounds, we can reduce the number of provers from $k$ to one, using at most three times the original local proof size plus a logarithmic term in the number of nodes. Finally, we establish amplification for perfectly complete protocols. In this regime, a single prover suffices with a constant-factor overhead in our reductions, yielding $\mathsf{dQMAS} = \mathsf{dQMAS}(k)$.

</details>

</details>


### [75] [No Exponential Quantum Advantage for Decoded Quantum Interferometry with Local Quantum Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12718)
*Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Enrico Fontana,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Ruslan Shaydulin,Shreehari Sureshbabu,Jacob Watkins*

TL;DR: 本文研究解码量子干涉（DQI）在随机 D-正则 k-稀疏实例上的解码阈值，指出其对应统计物理中的相变：局域解码器阈值对应树重建阈值，最优解码器对应凝聚阈值，从而给出 DQI 性能的紧上界。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI 将稀疏线性约束优化及 Gibbs 采样归约为从量子态解码线性码，但对局域解码器（如 BPQM）与最优解码器的能力边界缺乏严格刻画，尤其缺少对量子优势可能来源的界定。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) reduces optimization of sparse linear constraint problems, and sampling from their Gibbs measures, to decoding a linear code from a quantum state. We study DQI on random $D$-regular $k$-sparse instances with $k\ge3$, for which local decoders such as belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) are commonly used. Such decoders estimate a typical codeword symbol using only the qudits within logarithmic distance of it. Our main insight is that the thresholds of the decoding problem induced by DQI correspond to phase transitions of the Gibbs measure that are well studied in statistical physics: tree reconstruction for local decoders and, for Max-$k$-XORSAT, condensation for the optimal decoder. This insight allows us to prove two main results. First, we prove an information-theoretic obstruction: no local decoder can recover the codeword with high probability above the tree reconstruction threshold. Conversely, we show that a decoder based on BPQM reaches this threshold. Since the cavity method predicts that local Markov chains equilibrate rapidly up to the same threshold, we expect DQI with local decoders to give no exponential speedup for sampling and no advantage in approximate optimization with amplitude functions that depend only on the number of violated constraints. Second, we consider the optimal decoder (the pretty-good measurement, for which no efficient implementation is known) and show that it succeeds exactly below the condensation threshold. The condensation threshold thus provides a tight upper bound on the performance of DQI with these amplitude functions, providing a strict refinement of the limit that Jordan et al.\ derived from the Holevo bound. Our results point to efficient nonlocal decoders that succeed between the two thresholds as the only viable path to a quantum advantage with DQI on these instances.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究在 sandwiched Rényi 散度约束下量子态不精确时可观测量期望的最大偏差，对所有阶 α∈[1/2,∞] 给出闭式的一维对偶刻画，并证明"经典化定理"使经典概率的不等式（Hoeffding、Bernstein、Markov–Krein）可直接迁移到量子情形。

Motivation: 实际中量子态往往仅近似已知，需要量化这种不确定性对可观测量期望估计的影响；现有量子风险度量缺乏统一的、覆盖所有 Rényi 阶且可对接经典集中不等式的框架，尤其缺乏对非交换性所带来效应的精确刻画。

Method: 以参考态的 sandwiched Rényi 散度为"预算"，求最坏情形期望；利用凸对偶将其化为关于 sandwiched Schatten 范数的一维优化，得到从 α=1/2 的保真度风险、α=1 的熵风险到 α=∞ 的量子条件风险价值的统一谱系；核心是经典化定理：借助 Heinävaara 的迹联合谱测度构造谱区间上的概率测度 ω_α，证明量子问题与该经典问题等价，α=1 时由 Stahl 定理给出 ω_α。

Result: 量子最坏情形期望等于经典问题在测度 ω_α 下的最优值，因此经典 Hoeffding、Bernstein、Markov–Krein 界均适用，并导出带 Bernstein 型精化的 Pinsker 型不等式；ω_α 仅在 α=1/2 时为 Born 测度，α>1/2 时非交换性使 ω_α 的方差严格低于 Born 方差，从而收紧所有方差敏感界，形成"量子通缩"效应。

Conclusion: 该工作给出统一的对偶与经典化框架，将量子态不确定性下的期望偏差问题完全归约为经典概率问题，明确揭示了非交换性相对于 Born 近似的严格增益（方差通缩），为量子集中不等式与风险敏感估计提供精确工具。

Abstract: If a quantum state is known only approximately, how much can the expectation of an observable change? We answer with the worst-case expectation over all states within a sandwiched Rényi divergence budget of a reference state, for every order $α\in [1/2,\infty]$. Convex duality gives this value as a one-dimensional optimization over a sandwiched Schatten norm, running from a fidelity risk at $α= 1/2$, through the entropic risk at $α= 1$, to a quantum conditional value-at-risk at $α= \infty$. The main result is a classicalization theorem: the quantum problem coincides with the classical one for a probability measure $ω_α$ on the spectral interval of the observable, constructed from Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure and given by Stahl's theorem at $α= 1$. The Hoeffding, Bernstein and Markov--Krein bounds of classical probability thereby apply to the quantum problem, and yield Pinsker-type inequalities with Bernstein-type refinements. The measure $ω_α$ is the Born measure only at $α= 1/2$; for $α> 1/2$, noncommutativity of the observable with the state strictly lowers its variance below the Born variance, and with it every variance-sensitive bound: a quantum deflation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的量子—经典分离主要针对 $\mathsf{BQP}$（有界错误），而更严格的 $\mathsf{EQP}$（零错误、确定性接受/拒绝）能否超越 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 尚不清楚。作者希望把分离强化到精确量子计算，并同时对抗 $\mathsf{IP}^O$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}^O$ 两类经典复杂度类。

Result: 得到一个递归谕示 $O$，使得 $\mathsf{EQP}^O \not\subseteq \mathsf{IP}^O \cup \mathsf{PH}^O$；存在三次查询、多项式时间的精确量子算法以概率 1 区分两种承诺情形，而经典 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 机器无法区分。

Conclusion: 精确量子计算相对 $\mathsf{IP}$ 与 $\mathsf{PH}$ 具有谕示分离，说明零错误量子计算的优势不依赖于有界错误假设；"修复方法"为在保持不可区分性的同时实现精确相关取值提供了通用技术手段。

Abstract: We construct a recursive oracle $O$ and a unary language $L\in\mathsf{EQP}^O\setminus(\mathsf{IP}^O\cup\mathsf{PH}^O)$, strengthening the $\mathsf{BQP}$ versus $\mathsf{IP}$ oracle separation of Bouland et al. to exact quantum computation. The separation is based on a variant of the Forrelation problem in which the normalized correlation of one Boolean function with the Fourier transform of another is promised to be exactly $+1/2$ or $-1/2$. The main technical contribution is a repair method that constructs distributions supported on these exact levels while preserving the indistinguishability needed for the $\mathsf{IP}$ and $\mathsf{PH}$ lower bounds. A uniform polynomial-time quantum algorithm distinguishes the two cases with certainty using only three oracle queries.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的信息可被可持续地提取并复用，但多种观察者共享量子关联时，不同策略（并行 vs 顺序）能否产生相同统计、量子优势能否被持续共享尚不清楚，需要厘清二者的关系与界限。

Method: 对比两类实验可行的共享方案：并行共享（借助对初始量子系统的不完美克隆）与顺序共享（观察者依次相互作用）。通过统计可实现性分析刻画二者的等价性，并构造涉及两个解码测量的通信任务以证明并行共享量子优势的不可能性；进而将分析推广到三解码测量情形，考察3-bit RAC与3-bit parity-oblivious RAC。

Result: (1) 仅两个观察者时，并行策略产生的任意统计均可由顺序策略复现；(2) 观察者多于两个时，存在并行可得而顺序不可复现的统计；(3) 对一类含两个解码测量的通信任务，并行共享量子优势不可能，从而在顺序/并行策略的量子优势可持续性之间建立任意大差距；(4) 3-bit RAC中并行共享量子优势不可能，但在3-bit parity-oblivious RAC中可行，最多两个观察者可通过测量初始量子系统的两个不完美克隆同时获得量子优势。

Conclusion: 并行与顺序共享在少数观察者下统计等价，但在多观察者及量子优势层面存在本质差异；解码测量数量与任务结构决定量子优势能否被并行持续共享，结果对量子通信网络与分布式量子信息处理具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Information can be extracted from a quantum system in a sustainable manner, allowing the system to be reused for further information extraction through subsequent interactions. We investigate the possibility of consensus between two experimentally feasible methods, namely, parallel and sequential sharing, that enable multiple observers to extract information encoded in a quantum system. We show that any statistics generated by a parallel sharing strategy can be reproduced by a sequential strategy when only two observers are involved. However, when more than two observers participate in sharing quantum correlations, certain statistics achievable through parallel strategies cannot be reproduced by sequential strategies. Moreover, we demonstrate that sharing quantum advantage through a parallel strategy is impossible for a class of communication tasks involving two decoding measurements, thereby establishing an arbitrarily large gap between the sustainability of quantum advantage under sequential and parallel strategies. This naturally raises the question of whether this impossibility extends to communication tasks involving more than two decoding measurements. We show that, while parallel sharing of quantum advantage is impossible in a 3-bit random access code (RAC), it becomes possible in a 3-bit parity-oblivious RAC, where at most two observers can simultaneously obtain a quantum advantage by performing measurements on two imperfect clones of the initially prepared quantum system. Our findings have potential applications in the development of quantum communication networks and distributed quantum information processing.

</details>


### [79] [Succinct Infinite-Horizon Lyapunov and LQR Value Problems Are BQP-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12757)
*Mohamed Serry,Jun Liu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性系统分析中的无穷时域输出能量（Lyapunov方程）与最优值函数（代数Riccati方程）是基本量，但当系统矩阵稀疏且以电路简洁表示（输入长度为维度的对数量级）时，其计算复杂性此前不清楚；作者希望刻画这类简洁输入下的量子/经典可解性边界。

Method: 对耗散自治系统与坐标向量初态定义简洁Lyapunov值问题SLV，并推广为简洁LQR值问题SLQRV；通过将任意BQP问题多项式时间归约到SLV证明其BQP-hard；再构造在简洁输入长度多项式时间内运行的量子算法求解更一般的SLQRV。

Result: SLV为BQP-hard；SLQRV存在多项式时间量子算法；因SLV是SLQRV特例，二者均为BQP-complete。故除非BPP=BQP，不存在能在简洁输入长度多项式时间内求解全部promise实例的经典随机算法。

Conclusion: 稀疏、电路简洁表示的Lyapunov与Riccati值估计问题精确刻画了BQP类，构成"量子优势"型的完全性问题，为控制与系统分析中的简洁表示计算提供了复杂性理论下界与量子算法上界。

Abstract: Fundamental tasks in linear systems analysis and control require evaluating infinite-horizon output energies for autonomous systems and optimal value functions for controlled systems. These quantities are characterized by Lyapunov and algebraic Riccati equations, respectively. In this work, we study promise problems for their evaluation when the system matrices are sparse and represented succinctly by Boolean circuits specifying their nonzero entries, with input length polynomial in the logarithm of the system dimension. For dissipative autonomous systems with coordinate-vector initial states, we define the succinct Lyapunov value problem (SLV). Its extension to linear-quadratic optimal control is the succinct LQR value problem (SLQRV). We prove that SLV is $\BQP$-hard: every problem in bounded-error quantum polynomial time ($\BQP$) reduces to it in polynomial time. We then establish that the more general SLQRV problem admits a quantum algorithm running in time polynomial in the succinct input length. Since SLV is a special case of SLQRV, both problems are $\BQP$-complete. Consequently, unless bounded-error probabilistic polynomial time coincides with $\BQP$, no classical randomized algorithm can solve either problem on all promised instances in time polynomial in the succinct input length.

</details>


### [80] [An efficient classical algorithm for OTOCs in chaotic quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12773)
*Yunchao Liu,Yihui Quek,Shreya Vardhan,Tianci Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文利用量子混沌的退相位机制，提出并实现了一个多项式时间的经典算法，可在典型一维随机量子电路中估计四点OTOC^(1)与八点OTOC^(2)，在电路间涨落较大的敏感区域实现平均情形下的经典可计算性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计单实例随机量子电路中的OTOC被认为是可验证量子优势的候选，且已被证明对张量网络、蒙特卡洛等常规经典算法困难。作者希望检验这种"经典困难性"是否普遍成立，尤其是OTOC对电路实现涨落敏感的区域。

Method: 借鉴量子混沌洞见，将OTOC组织为快速收敛的级数：首项仅包含复制希尔伯特空间中置换自由度内的历史贡献，次阶项来自偏离该置换子空间的"激发"。混沌导致的退相位效应压制这些次阶项，从而给出多项式时间经典算法，并配合理论与数值证据验证该压制。

Result: 理论上与数值上均给出次阶项被强烈压制的有力证据；实际实现该算法可高精度估计一维电路中的OTOC^(1)与OTOC^(2)，且与Google近期二维实验相比，在更大电路深度下获得更高信噪比。

Conclusion: 混沌并非经典模拟不可逾越的障碍，反而可被用作退相位资源实现平均情形下的经典可计算性；但将算法推广到更高维、以及厘清二维OTOC中量子优势的地位仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Estimating out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) in individual instances of random quantum circuits has been proposed as a candidate for verifiable quantum advantage, and demonstrated to be hard for conventional classical algorithms such as tensor networks and Monte Carlo. Using insights from quantum chaos, we develop and implement a polynomial-time classical algorithm for the four-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and the eight-point ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in typical instances of one-dimensional (1D) quantum circuits. Our results demonstrate average-case classical tractability of estimating OTOCs in the sensitive regime where circuit-to-circuit fluctuations are large. Our algorithm organizes the OTOC into a rapidly converging series, where the leading term includes contributions from histories within permutation degrees of freedom in the replicated Hilbert space, and subleading terms arise from excursions outside this space. Although chaos is often viewed as an insurmountable barrier to classical simulation, our algorithm exploits a form of dephasing caused by chaos, which suppresses the subleading terms. We provide strong theoretical and numerical evidence to support this suppression. Further, we demonstrate that practical implementations of our algorithm estimate ${\rm OTOC}^{(1)}$ and ${\rm OTOC}^{(2)}$ in 1D circuits with high accuracy, achieving higher signal-to-noise ratio at larger circuit depths compared to recent 2D experiments by Google. Extending our algorithm to higher dimensions, and resolving the status of quantum advantage in 2D OTOCs, remain open problems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在所提出的里德堡-XXZ平台上成功实现了上述多种双比特门及连续门族，表明单一物理资源加单比特控制即可覆盖完整双比特门集合。

Conclusion: 该架构使中性原子平台可原生实现任意双比特门，支持在指令集间灵活切换，从而降低量子算法实现中的电路深度与编译开销。

Abstract: We propose a unified architecture for two-qubit gates in neutral-atom quantum processors which allows the implementation of the complete set of two-qubit gates. Our approach rests on a single resource that unlocks the entire gate set: an XXZ interaction with tunable anisotropy, combined only with single-qubit operations. We introduce a Rydberg-atom realization of the tunable XXZ interaction and combine it with optimally controlled global microwave pulses to implement the B, QFT, iSWAP, and $\sqrt{\mathrm{iSWAP}}$ gates, as well as a continuous gate family interpolating from CZ to iSWAP via the B gate. This work allows the implementation of any two-qubit gate natively in neutral-atom platforms, enabling flexible switching between instruction sets to reduce circuit depth and compilation overhead in quantum algorithm implementation.

</details>


### [82] [Self-Energy Construction for Chain Representations of Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12782)

  <summary>Details</summary>
