### [123] [Direct product geometries in $n$-dimensional linear, quadratic, and conformal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12483)
*Petr Jizba,Tereza Lehečková*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究直积时空（含非正常数曲率二维子空间）在线性引力、一般纯二次曲率引力及共形引力高维推广中的精确解结构，对解空间进行分类，并分析其与理论类型、曲率耦合常数的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直积时空为超越广义相对论的引力理论提供了获得精确解的可行途径，但此前对允许哪些子空间几何、以及这些几何如何依赖具体理论（尤其是二次曲率耦合）缺乏系统认识，故需对解空间做完整分类。

Method: 考察 $n$ 维 $(m_1+m_2)$、$(2+m)$、$(2+\ldots+2)$ 型直积时空，允许二维子空间具非正常数曲率；在线性引力、一般纯二次曲率引力及共形引力高维推广中求解场方程，按理论与曲率耦合对解空间分类，并讨论与线性引力解的共形关系、广义 Petrov 型及电磁源相容性。

Result: 含非正常数曲率二维部分的度规由曲率剖面的代数条件支配，或（二次引力某一特殊类）由微分方程支配；仅含常数曲率 $m$ 维子空间的度规满足纯代数关系。特别地，对所考虑形式的共形引力，当 $n>4$ 时直积扇区中不存在标准静态黑洞几何。四维与六维情形被详加分析，并尽可能给出说明结构的显式解析解。

Conclusion: 直积扇区的可容许几何强烈依赖引力理论与曲率耦合：非正常数曲率二维部分导致代数或微分约束，常数曲率子空间仅需代数约束；共形引力高维直积扇区缺乏静态黑洞解，构成其显著特征。

Abstract: Direct-product spacetimes offer a tractable route to exact solutions in gravity theories beyond general relativity. We study $n$-dimensional direct-product spacetimes of $(m_1+m_2)$, $(2+m)$, and $(2+\ldots+2)$ type in linear gravity, general pure quadratic-curvature gravity, and one of the recently proposed higher-dimensional extensions of conformal gravity, allowing the two-dimensional subspaces to have nonconstant curvature. We classify the resulting solution spaces and determine how the allowed geometries depend on the gravitational theory and, in quadratic gravity, on the curvature couplings. Metrics containing two-dimensional part(s) of nonconstant curvature are governed either by algebraic conditions on a curvature profile or, in one special class of quadratic gravity, by a differential equation. Relations governing metrics with only constant-curvature $m$-dimensional subspaces are purely algebraic. We further examine conformal relations to linear-gravity solutions, generalized Petrov types, and compatibility with electromagnetic sources. Particularly noteworthy is that standard static black-hole geometries are absent from the direct-product sector of the considered form of conformal gravity for $n>4$. Four- and six-dimensional cases are analyzed in detail, and explicit analytic solutions illustrating the resulting structure are presented whenever possible.

</details>


### [124] [Gravity Without Absolutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12489)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 作者以牛顿引力理论为案例，论证还原论框架下（绝对时空+牛顿第二定律）描述N体宇宙会引入无穷多任意解，而莱布尼茨充足理由律（PSR）可消除这种任意性并给出意想不到的性质；若进一步用PSR确定宇宙粒子数则只能为无穷，进而提出以"关联"替代"因果"的整体论图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近半个世纪物理学缺乏重大进展，主流范式（物理学与宇宙学）仍有未解难题，作者怀疑还原论已难以为继，主张整体论可能成为新出路。选择牛顿引力是因为它本体论上最简单且历史意义重大，是检验这一可能性的理想理论。

Method: 概念/哲学分析结合理论推演：将牛顿第二定律视为理论核心，考察其在绝对时空的还原论框架内用于描述N体宇宙（而非其中的子系统）时的解空间；引入莱布尼茨充足理由律（PSR）作为约束条件，消除无穷多任意解，并进一步用PSR推断宇宙粒子数的可能取值。文中还提出用"关联"取代"因果"的解释方案。

Result: 在还原论框架下用牛顿第二定律描述N体宇宙会产生无穷多任意解；PSR可消除这一无穷任意性，并改变N体宇宙的预测性质，使其全部呈现"意想不到的有趣"；PSR进一步暗示宇宙粒子数只能是无穷。在此类宇宙中，"关联"可替代"因果"，同时仍呈现出每个状态仿佛由过去的动力学演化产生的表象，而真实解释可能完全蕴含于当前状态之中。

Conclusion: 作者承认将这些想法发展为可行理论仍需大量工作，但确信该工作推进了整体论（holism）的论证。

Abstract: By formulating his theory of universal gravitation in absolute space and time, Newton demonstrated the power of reductionism; it has been behind every major advance in physics since then. However, the lack of significant advances over the last half-century, together with unresolved problems in the dominant paradigms of physics and cosmology, suggest that holism may need to replace reductionism as the way forward. The ontological simplicity and historical significance of Newtonian gravity makes it the ideal theory for examining this possibility. Taking Newton's second law of motion to be the core of his theory, we show that its use within the reductionist framework of absolute space and time introduces an infinity of arbitrary solutions if used to describe $N$-body universes rather than subsystems within them. We show that Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason (PSR) eliminates the infinite arbitrariness and transforms the predicted nature of $N$-body universes; they are all shown to be unexpectedly interesting. An even greater change is enforced if one asks what principle could determine the number of particles in the universe. The PSR suggests it can only be infinite. We suggest that, in such a universe, correlation could replace causation while still giving the appearance that each state has been created by dynamical evolution from the past, whereas its actual explanation may lie entirely within the present state. While much work remains to develop these ideas into a viable theory, we are confident that it advances the case for holism.

</details>


### [125] [Thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black holes from background subtraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12683)
*Chang-Xu Yan,Zili Mou,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对浸没在Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的带电旋转黑洞，利用新建立的积分条件形式并结合渐近平直带电旋转黑洞的规范选择作为初始条件，唯一地固定了规范，进而用背景减除法（以Minkowski时空为参考）得到质量与角动量，发现黑洞热力学第一定律自动满足，CR关系与Smarr公式同时成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电旋转黑洞浸没在Bertotti-Robinson磁场中时表现出非渐近平直性，导致时间生成元的规范选择与视界电势存在不确定性，从而使质量、角动量等守恒量的定义出现歧义。作者希望消除这一规范模糊性，给出自洽的热力学量定义。

Method: 采用新建立的积分条件形式，并以渐近平直带电旋转黑洞的规范选择作为初始条件，从而唯一确定规范；在此基础上，用背景减除法（以Minkowski时空为参考时空）计算质量和角动量。

Result: 规范可被唯一固定；由此得到的质量与角动量使黑洞热力学第一定律自动满足，并且CR关系与Smarr公式均被遵守。

Conclusion: 在非渐近平直的Bertotti-Robinson磁场背景下，通过积分条件加初始规范条件可以消除规范歧义，背景减除法给出的守恒量与黑洞热力学定律及Smarr型关系完全自洽，为这类背景下黑洞热力学的定义提供了自洽框架。

Abstract: The non-asymptotic flatness of charged rotating black holes immersed in the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field causes the ambiguity in the gauge choice for the time generator and the horizon electric potential. By using the newly established form of the integrability condition together with the gauge choice for the asymptotically flat charged rotating black holes as the initial condition, we have found that the gauge can be uniquely fixed. With this fixed gauge, we further obtain the mass and angular momentum by the background subtraction method with the Minkowski spacetime as the reference spacetime. As a result, not only is the first law of black hole thermodynamics automatically satisfied, but also both the CR relation and the Smarr formula are obeyed.

</details>


### [126] [Numerical-Relativity Particle-in-Cell Simulations: Self-Gravitating Collisionless Matter in Dynamical Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12831)
*Jiaxi Wu,Elias R. Most*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了三维数值相对论粒子网格（NRPIC）框架，在AthenaK上求解Einstein–Vlasov系统，将无碰撞物质的完全动理学描述与Z4c时空演化耦合，采用Boris型推进器与守恒应力-能量沉积，并在自适应网格上验证了从球对称尘埃坍缩、长椭球焦散成洞、相对论星团非线性迁移到双过密区坍缩—双黑洞并合—残余吸积的全过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力坍缩与不稳定性、大尺度分离下的黑洞动力学、极端黑洞形成等问题缺乏解析手段，必须依赖全非线性数值模拟；而已有的相对论动理学方法（如相对论等离子体PIC）尚未推广到广义相对论引力体系，缺乏在三维中自洽求解Einstein–Vlasov系统的成熟数值工具。

Method: 构建NRPIC算法：物质用粒子（Vlasov/无碰撞）动理学表示，时空用Z4c演化方程在moving puncture规范下推进；借助"等效电动力学表述"把测地线受力改写为洛伦兹力形式，从而复用相对论等离子体PIC的Boris型粒子推进器；在自适应细化网格上以守恒方式沉积应力-能量张量作为引力源；整个框架实现在性能可移植的AthenaK代码之上。

Result: (1) 与球对称尘埃坍缩的解析/半解析结果对比完成耦合演化验证；(2) 追踪长椭球坍缩经无碰撞焦散形成黑洞；(3) 研究相对论星团的稳定性与非线性迁移；(4) 模拟共同无碰撞包层内两个过密区的坍缩，完整跟踪黑洞形成、双星旋近与并合，以及并合后残余的黑洞吸积。

Conclusion: 该工作首次（在所述框架下）提供了可用于广义相对论引力的全动理学三维NRPIC工具，证明将相对论等离子体PIC技术经等效电动力学改写后能有效求解Einstein–Vlasov系统；结果展示其在研究数值相对论中的引力不稳定性、无碰撞坍缩与黑洞双星形成方面的潜力，为强场无碰撞引力动力学开辟了新的数值途径。

Abstract: Gravitational collapse and instability, black-hole dynamics in systems with large scale separations, and questions surrounding extremal black hole formation require full nonlinear studies using accurate and adapted numerical tools. To this end, we present a new algorithm to study collisionless relativistic systems using a fully kinetic description of matter coupled to the evolving spacetime. In detail, we present a numerical-relativity particle-in-cell (NRPIC) framework for solving the Einstein--Vlasov system in three spatial dimensions. Implemented on top of the performance-portable \texttt{AthenaK} code, the method couples particle dynamics to Z4c spacetime evolution in moving puncture gauge through conservative stress-energy deposition on adaptively refined meshes. Our implementation makes use of advances in the computational modeling of relativistic plasmas using modern PIC models, now made available to general-relativistic gravity via an equivalent electrodynamic formulation. We introduce a Boris-type particle pusher that exploits the electrodynamic (Lorentz force) form of the geodesic force. We validate the coupled evolution against spherical dust collapse, follow prolate collapse through collisionless caustics into black-hole formation, and investigate the stability and nonlinear migration of relativistic clusters. Highlighting its potential to study gravitational instability in NR, we simulate the collapse of two overdensities within a common collisionless envelope, following black-hole formation, binary inspiral and merger, and subsequent mass accretion onto the remnant.

</details>


### [127] [Scalar quasinormal modes of the exact spinning Morris--Thorne wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12838)
*Mark Sukaiti,Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究精确旋转Morris-Thorne虫洞上无质量标量场的准正规模，用二维Chebyshev配点法求解完整双端几何上的二次特征值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转虫洞背景的准正规模谱尚未被系统求解，且双端几何两端渐近静止系相互旋转，使频率谱的对称性与超辐射约束需重新刻画。

Method: 在完整双端几何上提取轴行为与两端出射相位后紧致化径向坐标，采用二维Chebyshev配点构造二次特征值问题；用独立积分验证低阶模，并以Kerr谱作为求解器的基准。

Result: 固定m时频率关于Re ω̂=3πjm/2镜像对称；多极族仅依赖mj，三个角向Chebyshev多项式精确表示L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2；守恒通量将非阈值实频限制在超辐射窗口0<ω/(mΩ_-)<1；m=1,2,3、j≤19的采样扇区中所有收敛频率均为阻尼且落在窗口外；大mj时频率趋近窗口上沿，偏移正比于(mj)^{-1/2}。

Conclusion: 谱的解析约束与数值结果一致，但完整稳定性判定仍需对未解析的标量谱及耦合度规-物质系统做进一步微扰分析。

Abstract: We study the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a minimally coupled massless scalar field on the exact spinning Morris-Thorne wormhole. The problem is posed on the complete two-ended geometry, whose asymptotic rest frames rotate relative to each other. After extracting the axis behaviour and the outgoing phases at both infinities, we compactify the radial coordinate and construct a quadratic eigenvalue problem by two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation. The analytic background yields exact constraints on the spectrum. At fixed azimuthal number $m$, the frequencies $\widehatω=r_0ω$ are mirror symmetric about $\operatorname{Re}\widehatω=3πjm/2$, where $r_0$ is the throat radius and $j=J/r_0^2$ is the dimensionless angular momentum. Each multipole family depends on $m$ and $j$ only through $mj$ and three angular Chebyshev polynomials represent exactly the multipoles $L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2$. A conserved flux confines nonthreshold real quasinormal frequencies to the superradiant window $0<ω/(mΩ_-)<1$, where $Ω_-=3πJ/r_0^3$. For the sampled sectors $m=1,2,3$ and spins up to $j=19$, every converged frequency is damped and lies outside this window. Independent integration verifies the low-lying modes, while Kerr spectra provide a separate benchmark for the two-dimensional solver. A limiting radial equation explains why the computed mode frequencies approach the upper edge of the superradiant window with frequency offsets proportional to $(mj)^{-1/2}$ at large $mj$. A complete stability assessment requires further analysis of the unresolved scalar spectrum and of perturbations of the coupled metric-matter system.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond $Λ$CDM: Probing Structure Growth and Observational Viability of Generalized ${f\,(Q , \mathcal{L}_{m})}$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12839)
*Raj Kumar Das,Sivasish Paul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究 $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)=-Q^\mu+\alpha\mathcal{L}_m^{\nu}+\beta$ 这一加性最小耦合的广义幂律模型，通过新参数化导出 $H(z)$，并联合背景与微扰数据（含 $f\sigma_8$）做约束与贝叶斯证据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m)$ 研究多集中于背景层面宇宙学拟合，缺乏对非守恒物质连续性方程下结构形成（微扰层面）的检验，本文旨在补齐这一环节并评估额外自由度的统计必要性。

Method: 采用加性最小耦合的广义幂律形式，以新参数化得到哈勃函数；$\nu\neq1$ 时物质连续性方程不守恒，可解释为有效粒子创生。用 OHD、DESI DR2 BAO 与 Pantheon+/Union3/DES-SN5YR 三组 SNe Ia 限制参数；在非守恒条件下推导微扰场方程、欧拉方程与连续性方程，联合 $f\sigma_8$ 数据分析结构增长，并以 $\Lambda$CDM 为参照计算贝叶斯证据。

Result: 背景数据在若干情形下支持该模型，其中一种实现获得中等程度支持；但引入 $f\sigma_8$ 后，约束驱动 $\nu\to1$，附加自由度被决定性地排除。

Conclusion: 背景几何数据不足以区分该扩展与 $\Lambda$CDM 的等效性，增长类数据对检验此类非最小耦合扩展具有决定性作用；模型的粒子创生机制未被观测支持。

Abstract: $f(Q, \mathcal{L}_m)$ gravity is a well-known extension of $f(Q)$ gravity, obtained through non-minimal or additive minimal coupling in the action's Lagrangian density. Background-level cosmography within this framework, along with comparisons to $Λ$CDM, has been the focus of prior studies. The present work considers a generalized power-law form of the additive minimal coupling, $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m) = -Q^μ+ α\mathcal{L}_m^{\,ν} + β$, from which the Hubble function $H(z)$ is derived through a novel parametrization. When $ν\neq 1$, the matter continuity equation is rendered non-conserved, a feature that can be interpreted as an effective particle-creation mechanism. Constraints on the model parameters are obtained from Observational Hubble Data, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and three SNe Ia compilations (Pantheon+, Union3, DES-SN5YR), allowing their cosmographic effects to be examined. Beyond the background level, the perturbed field, Euler, and continuity equations are derived under non-conservation, and their impact on structure growth is studied through a joint analysis incorporating the $fσ_8$ probe. Bayesian evidence, computed relative to $Λ$CDM, shows that the model is favoured by background data in several cases, with moderate support for one realisation, whereas the inclusion of $fσ_8$ drives $ν\to1$ and decisively disfavours the additional freedom, highlighting the importance of growth data in testing such extensions.

</details>


### [129] [Mapping of Spacetimes with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12857)
*Estuti Shukla,David Radice,Rossella Gamba,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的神经网络方法，从数值度规数据中学习坐标映射，将模拟坐标映射到指定目标规范，并在Schwarzschild/Kerr解析几何与移动穿刺数值黑洞演化中验证，进而构造曲率扰动并用Teukolsky方程演化获得与全非线性模拟高度吻合的波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论中的规范自由度使模拟坐标的物理解释困难，也妨碍同一时空不同描述之间的比较。作者希望在不预设坐标变换函数形式的前提下，自动建立数值时空与解析背景之间的规范对应关系。

Method: 训练神经网络学习坐标映射：以变换后源度规与目标度规的匹配作为损失，不假设变换的解析形式。先在解析Schwarzschild与Kerr度规上验证（可恢复到目标时空等距变换意义下的已知变换）；再应用于移动穿刺坐标下的黑洞数值演化，包括非旋转与旋转穿刺情形。利用学到的映射把数值度规识别为Kerr背景，构造曲率扰动并用Teukolsky方程演化。

Result: 在解析情形下能恢复已知坐标变换（至等距变换）。对非旋转黑洞，学到的映射将含时数值度规联系到内落Eddington-Finkelstein坐标下的静态Schwarzschild度规；对旋转穿刺，尽管数值时空偏离Kerr，仍得到近似的Kerr背景识别。由此构造并演化的Teukolsky波形与全非线性模拟提取的波形高度一致。

Conclusion: 学习得到的坐标映射能够把数值时空与解析背景联系起来，显示出在跨代码验证（cross-code validation）与黑洞微扰理论方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Gauge freedom in numerical relativity complicates the interpretation of simulation coordinates and the comparison of different descriptions of the same spacetime. We present a data-driven neural network method for learning coordinate maps from numerical metric data to a prescribed target gauge. The network is trained by matching the transformed source metric to the target metric, without assuming a functional form for the coordinate transformation. We first validate the method on analytical Schwarzschild and Kerr geometries, recovering known transformations up to the isometries of the target spacetime. We then apply it to numerical black hole evolutions in moving-puncture coordinates. For a non-spinning black hole, the learned map relates the time-dependent numerical metric to a stationary Schwarzschild metric in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. For a spinning puncture, the method provides an approximate identification with a Kerr background despite departures from Kerr in the numerical spacetime. We use this identification to construct curvature perturbations and evolve them with the Teukolsky equation, obtaining waveforms that closely match those extracted from the full nonlinear simulation. These results demonstrate that learned coordinate maps can relate numerical spacetimes to analytical backgrounds, with potential applications to cross-code validation and black hole perturbation theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Process then Retrieve: Investigating the Origins of the Modality Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09897)
*Arpita Shanbhag,Julia Tran,Dhruv Mandala,Ayda Sultan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究视觉语言模型（VLM）中"模态鸿沟"的成因，提出嵌入层上文本token与视觉token的信息密度差异是导致该不平衡的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态鸿沟（modality gap）的起源仍是VLM研究的重大挑战，作者希望厘清嵌入层上不同模态token语义密度差异是否驱动了模型早期层的处理偏向。

Method: 从信息密度角度进行机理分析：比较文本token与视觉token在嵌入层的语义密度；并通过将视觉token位置替换为语义最近的文本token对应物，观察早期层注意力分布的变化。

Result: 文本token进入模型时语义密度显著低于视觉token，导致早期层优先处理文本表示、再与视觉信息融合；将视觉位置替换为语义最近的文本token后，早期层对这些位置的注意力明显上升。

Conclusion: 信息密度差异是模态鸿沟的重要来源，为其缓解提供了基础，并为改进VLM架构中的多模态推理提供启示。

Abstract: Understanding the origins of the modality gap in vision language models remains a significant challenge. This work investigates whether differences in information density between modality specific tokens at the embedding layer contribute to this imbalance. We show that text tokens enter the model with much lower semantic density than visual tokens, driving early layers to focus on processing text representations before combining them with vision. Through mechanistic analysis, we demonstrate that replacing vision token positions with their semantically closest text token counterparts elicits increased attention to those positions in early layers. These findings provide a foundation for addressing the modality gap and provide insight to improve multimodal reasoning in VLM architectures.

</details>


### [131] [Universal Approximation by Equivariant Message-Passing Graph Neural Networks with a Bilinear Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10772)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明双线性读出等变消息传递网络（BREMP）具有通用逼近性，并由此将结论推广到生产使用的 DPA4 原子间势模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变图神经网络原子间势（如 DPA 系列）在实际中广泛使用，但缺乏严格的理论保证：现有工作未刻画"层数与滤波度数"受限条件下的表达力边界。因此需要一个可作为参考架构的模型，并给出其通用逼近性的完整证明。

Method: 以 BREMP 为参考架构——其层由等变卷积与仿射映射复合，读出层由等变特征乘积构造不变量。核心是构造单层特征，使其在同类原子置换意义下唯一确定局部环境，所用球谐滤波的度数不超过"距中心最远同一距离处邻居数量的最大值"；迭代该构造可恢复与层数相同跳数内可达的环境，层截断处仍用相同的度数上界。读出层在紧集上一致逼近特征的任意连续不变函数。证明中还假设在容许类内，消息传递在统一的层数内可覆盖每个物理环境的全部邻居。

Result: 得到原子能量在给定物理范围内的通用逼近结论，且所需层截断更短；并证明 DPA4 通过逼近证明中构造的 BREMP 层并实现双线性读出而继承该结果。

Conclusion: BREMP 是一类可实现通用逼近的等变消息传递架构，其表达力仅需与邻居几何复杂度相匹配的滤波度数以及受统一跳数约束的层数即可保证；该理论保证了实际部署的 DPA4 模型的逼近能力。

Abstract: We prove universal approximation for the bilinear-readout equivariant message-passing network (BREMP), a reference architecture for equivariant graph neural network interatomic potentials. Its layers combine equivariant convolutions with affine maps, and its readout forms invariants from products of the equivariant features. We construct a one-layer feature that determines the local environment up to permutations of atoms of the same type. The construction uses spherical harmonic filters of degree no larger than the largest number of neighbors at a common distance from the center. Iterating the construction recovers the environment reachable in as many hops as there are layers, using the same bound on filter degrees at the layer cutoff. The readout approximates every continuous invariant function of the features uniformly on compact sets. Together these give universal approximation of atomic energies at a prescribed physical range using a shorter layer cutoff. We assume that, throughout the admissible class, message passing reaches all neighbors in each physical environment within a common number of layers. Finally, DPA4, an interatomic potential architecture in production use, inherits this result by approximating the BREMP layers constructed in the proof and realizing the bilinear readout.

</details>


### [132] [MAGE: Towards Generalizable Multi-timescale EEG Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09928)
*Tuan-Kiet Doan,Van-Chien Nguyen,Trung-Hieu Tran,Quang-Hung Pham,Ngoc-Son Vu,Huy Phan,Phi Le Nguyen,Duc-Han Le,Nikola Simidjievski,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGE是一个面向EEG的多时间尺度自适应基础模型，通过多时间尺度注意力与可训练聚合模块动态捕捉跨时间尺度神经动态，在七个数据集上以更少参数超越现有EEG基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EEG基础模型的方法多依赖固定的时间尺度，难以刻画EEG中天然存在、跨越多个时间尺度的神经动态特征，从而限制了表征能力。

Method: 提出MAGE基础模型，包含两个机制：(i) 新颖的多时间尺度注意力机制，可学习跨多个时间尺度的表征；(ii) 可训练聚合模块，动态识别并适配输入信号中最具信息量的时间尺度。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，MAGE在参数量显著更少的情况下持续优于现有EEG基础模型；结果亦支持不同下游任务受益于不同时间尺度这一假设。

Conclusion: 多时间尺度自适应设计有效提升了EEG基础模型的性能与参数效率，并揭示时间尺度选择与下游任务相关，源码将公开。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) data plays an important role in understanding human brain activity, with various major applications. While current approaches, which largely build on EEG foundation models, have demonstrated strong performance across datasets and tasks, they typically rely on a fixed temporal scale. This design choice limits their ability to capture characteristics of neural dynamic behavior, often spanning across multiple timescales, which are naturally captured in EEG recordings. To address this limitation, we propose MAGE (\textbf{Multi-timescale Adaptive Generalization for EEG}), a foundation model that dynamically adapts to capture and integrate information across different timescales. Specifically, MAGE combines two key mechanisms: (i) a novel multi-timescale attention mechanism, capable of learning representations across multiple temporal scales and (ii) a trainable aggregation module, that dynamically identifies and adapts to the most informative timescale of the input signal. Experimental results across seven datasets demonstrate that MAGE consistently outperforms existing EEG foundation models while using substantially fewer parameters. Moreover, our results support the hypothesis that different downstream tasks benefit from distinct temporal scales. All source code will be released.

</details>


### [133] [Causal Discovery Bench: Can LLMs Autonomously Conduct Effective Experiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09934)
*Zhuoyang Liu,Ning Peng,Ming Shen,Zijun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Causal-Discovery-Bench 可验证沙箱，用可配置结构方程模型与资源约束评测 LLM 智能体的序贯实验设计与因果发现能力；在 3,802 个回合、5 家厂商 13 个模型、40 项指标上的结果显示智能体的失败 94.5% 源于实验设计而非推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体被越来越多用于自主研究，需要不仅能对既有证据推理，还要设计信息量大的实验、分配资源、分析序贯观测并判断何时停止并下结论；但现有基准对这些能力测量不足，尤其缺乏去除领域语义线索、可验证的因果发现评测环境。

Method: 构建 Causal-Discovery-Bench：一个可验证沙箱，内含可配置的结构方程模型与资源约束，用于评测无领域语义线索下的序贯实验设计与因果发现；在 3,802 个回合、5 家厂商 13 个模型上，沿 7 大类 40 项指标分析智能体行为，并结合动作轨迹归因与消融实验。

Result: 智能体表现呈现四级难度层级：单次实验设计、因果关系的序贯探索、兼顾成本与统计功效的资源感知实验、冲突证据的自主消解。在"对已整备证据做因果推断"与"通过自主实验进行因果发现"之间存在显著性能差距；轨迹归因显示 94.5% 的错误来自实验设计而非推断，消融实验进一步印证。仅放宽资源约束并不能可靠解决序贯发现失败；基础统计工具可显著提升多模型家族表现，而高级工具在模型缺乏底层前置能力时收益有限。

Conclusion: 智能体自主因果发现的瓶颈在于实验设计与序贯决策，而非统计推断；提升应优先强化基础统计与实验设计能力，工具增强需与模型底层能力匹配，单纯放松资源约束无效。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents get actively adopted for autonomous research, they must not only reason over existing evidence, but also design informative experiments, allocate resources, analyze sequential observations, and decide when to stop and make conclusions. Yet these capabilities remain poorly measured. We introduce Causal-Discovery-Bench (https://github.com/mshen1019/causal-discovery-bench), a verifiable sandbox with configurable structural equation models and resource constraints to evaluate sequential experimental design and causal discovery without domain-semantic cues. Across 3,802 episodes with 13 models from 5 vendors, we analyze agent behavior along 40 metrics in seven families. Agents' performance exhibits a four-level difficulty hierarchy spanning single-experiment design, sequential exploration of causal relationships, resource-aware experimentation balancing cost and statistical power, and autonomous resolution of conflicting evidence. We identify a substantial performance gap between causal inference from correctly curated evidence and navigating causal discovery through autonomous experimentation: Analyzing agents' action trajectories attributes 94.5% of their mistakes to experimental design rather than to inference, which is further consolidated with ablation studies. Relaxing resource constraints alone does not reliably resolve failures in sequential discovery. In contrast, basic statistical tools substantially improve performance across multiple model families, while advanced tools provide little additional benefit when models lack prerequisite lower-level capabilities.

</details>


### [134] [Fresh Enough to Decide: Age of Intelligence for Cost-Aware Model Synchronization in Dynamic IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09935)
*Hui Wang,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour,Jacob Chakareski,Ming Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"智能年龄"(AoInt)指标，将其定义为模型版本相关、客户端特异、以动作效用失真度量的信息年龄，并据此把模型同步建模为决策后悔与刷新成本的权衡，进而设计两种同步策略并给出路径级理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限IoT设备缓存学习模型以支持实时决策，但频繁更新会带来带宽、能耗与服务开销。由于对决策有影响的模型差异未必随经过时间增长，传统基于时间的信息年龄(AoI)不能准确刻画模型"过期"的决策价值。

Method: 定义AoInt为模型版本相对、客户端特异、以动作效用失真度量的量；将同步问题形式化为陈旧决策后悔与刷新成本的权衡。提出KL-AoIntSync，利用客户端特异的无标签探针累积输出分布变化；提出Param-AoIntSync，使用服务器广播的标量参数变化量进行度量。

Result: 在固定客户端分布、动作集与效用函数的条件下，建立路径级(pathwise)保证。数值结果显示，经校准的Param-AoIntSync相对Instant-Parameters策略将联合同步成本降低6.15%，且性能与KL-AoIntSync相当。

Conclusion: AoInt为模型同步提供了面向决策效用的新度量视角；轻量的参数感知同步(仅需广播标量)在显著降低同步成本的同时可逼近分布感知方法的效果，适合资源受限IoT场景。

Abstract: Resource-constrained IoT devices cache learned models for real-time decisions, but frequent updates incur substantial bandwidth, energy, and service overhead. Because decision-relevant model differences need not grow with elapsed time, we define Age of Intelligence (AoInt) as a model-version-relative, client-specific, and action utility distortion, and formulate synchronization as a tradeoff between stale-decision regret and refresh cost. Furthermore, we develop Kullback-Leibler-aware AoInt Synchronization (KL-AoIntSync), which accumulates output-distribution changes using client-specific unlabeled probes, whereas Parameter-aware AoInt Synchronization (Param-AoIntSync) uses a server-broadcast scalar parameter change. Then, we establish pathwise guarantees with fixed client distributions, action sets, and utilities. The numerical results show that calibrated Param-AoIntSync reduces joint synchronization cost by 6.15% relative to an Instant-Parameters policy while performing comparably to KL-AoIntSync.

</details>


### [135] [Topological Deep Learning Challenge 2026: Bridging the Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10046)
*Guillermo Bernárdez,Lev Telyatnikov,Mathilde Papillon,Marco Montagna,Louisa Cornelis,Louis Van Langendonck,Antonio Purificato,Bruno Neri,David Leko,Donatella Genovese,Eleonora Grassucci,Jan-Willem Van Looy,Johan Mathe,Lucia Testa,Luka Benić,Melanie Weber,Olga Zaghen,Pavlo Vasylenko,Raffael Theiler,Scott Mahan,Gaurav Khanal,Valentina Sánchez,Dario Loi,Gabriele Onorato,Prasanna Devadiga,Taejin Paik,Anier Velasco Sotomayor,Jonas Müller,Aarav Gupta,Riyan Patel,Grold Otieno Mboya,Pushparaj Devadiga,Adam Gohain,Dominik Filipiak,Michael Banf,Alireza Mohammadian,Amirreza Akbari,Gabriel Chaviano Díaz,Marcos Chávez Mesa,Ankit Grover,Rémi Bourgerie,Alessio Comparini,Ankit Kumar,Mohammad Yusuf,Devesh Kaushal,Francesco Orsi,Ghazal Mostofi,Kolya Lettl,Salwa Khattami,Gabriel Franco,Yelizaveta Falkouskaya,Jorge Luiz Franco,Kajal Singla,Fernando David Espinosa Iñiguez,Raj Patil,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,Luigi Petti,Chenguang Xu,Stefano Blando,Christine Zeh,Lennart Bastian,Kaden Zheng,Vihan Tiwari,Sud Athreya,Hisataka Kajita,Hugo Micheron,Rémi Devaux,Antoine Jardin,Tegan Emerson,Olga Fink,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍2026年拓扑深度学习挑战赛（TAG-DS会议），首次在统一评测流程下并排比较GNN与TNN，基于GraphUniverse合成基准，在社区发现与三角形计数任务上分析两类模型的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往TDL挑战赛主要扩展建模与数据生态，缺乏GNN与TNN在同一评测pipeline下的直接、可比对评估，因此需要搭建统一基准以厘清两类模型的适用边界。

Method: 在TopoBench中实现SOTA架构，Track 1为GNN、Track 2为TNN；使用GraphUniverse生成可独立控制同质性、平均度与度分布重尾程度的共享合成基准；首次引入公开排行榜并对提交结果统计分析。

Result: 34支队伍共提交68份合格作品，覆盖图、单纯复形、胞腔复形、超图与组合域。差异高度依赖任务：社区发现上平均精度差距较小；三角形计数上Track 1平均误差为Track 2的1.85倍，且若干结构感知GNN甚至优于Track 2均值。

Conclusion: GNN与TNN的相对优劣并非全局性，而取决于任务的结构需求；公开排行榜与可控合成基准为TDL的公平比较与后续研究提供了可复现的评估框架。

Abstract: This paper describes the 2026 edition of the Topological Deep Learning Challenge: Bridging the Gap, hosted at the second Topology, Algebra, and Geometry in Data Science (TAG-DS) Conference. Where previous editions grew the modelling and data ecosystem of Topological Deep Learning (TDL), this edition set out to place Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Topological Neural Networks (TNNs) side by side under a single evaluation pipeline. Participants implemented recent state-of-the-art architectures in TopoBench, with Track 1 covering GNNs and Track 2 covering TNNs. They evaluated these implementations on shared synthetic benchmarks generated with GraphUniverse, which independently controls homophily, average degree and degree-distribution heaviness. The challenge yielded 68 qualifying submissions from 34 teams, spanning the graph, simplicial, cellular, hypergraph and combinatorial domains. For the first time, submissions were also tracked on a public leaderboard, which we summarize and analyze here. Our analysis finds that the differences between the submitted tracks are strongly task-dependent: the mean accuracy gap is modest on community detection, while on triangle counting the Track 1 mean error is 1.85 times the Track 2 mean. Several structurally informed GNNs also outperform the Track 2 mean on triangle counting.

</details>


### [136] [Selected-Risk Calibration for Feasibility at Returned Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10050)
*Raghav Thind,Youran Sun,Ling Liang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Selected-Risk Calibration (SRC) 框架，针对"代理约束可行但真实约束不可行"的问题，以优化器返回决策为单位校准学习约束，给出有限样本下的选择风险（返回决策不可行概率）保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理约束（surrogate constraint）可行并不保证真实约束可行；而现有工具（split conformal 的随机输入覆盖保证、反馈协变量偏移下的似然比重加权）都不能控制"所选决策的不可行概率"这一真正关心的量，前者只覆盖随机输入，后者在学习约束在返回决策处起作用时失效。

Method: SRC 以给定目标分布下优化器返回的决策为校准对象，对学习约束进行校准：在高维目标集上按"选择风险"（返回决策不可行的概率）筛选并认证 margin，使得以至少 1−δ 的概率，所有在留出目标上认证的 margin 的选择风险 ≤ γ。作者还给出理论分析，说明 split conformal 的输入覆盖界不约束选择风险、似然比重加权在约束活跃处不适用，并与基线进行等成本对比实验。

Result: 实验中，覆盖约90%随机输入的 conformal margin，对返回决策的覆盖率可低至17%；而 SRC 认证的每个 margin 在未见目标上的违约率均低于目标值；在等成本条件下，62 组比较中有57组 SRC 的修正方案不可行解数量少于或等于基线。

Conclusion: 决策中心（而非输入中心）的校准是控制选择风险的关键；SRC 适用于任何"代理模型选择决策、且实例可行性可验证"的场景，是通用且统计有效的框架。

Abstract: When an optimization problem uses a surrogate constraint, a decision that is feasible for the surrogate need not be feasible for the true constraint. We propose Selected-Risk Calibration (SRC), a framework that calibrates a learned constraint against the optimizer's returned decisions for objectives drawn from a given distribution. SRC guarantees that, with probability at least $1-δ$, every margin it certifies on held-out objectives has selected risk, the probability that the returned decision is infeasible, of at most $γ$. We also show that the random-input coverage guarantee of split conformal prediction does not bound the selected risk, and that likelihood-ratio reweighting for feedback covariate shift does not apply when the learned constraint is active at the returned decision. Our experiments show that a conformal margin covering about $90\%$ of random inputs covered as few as $17\%$ of the returned decisions, and every certified margin had a violation rate below its target on unseen objectives. Moreover, at equal cost, SRC's correction had fewer or equal infeasible solutions to the baselines in 57 of 62 comparisons. The SRC framework applies to any setting in which a surrogate model chooses a decision whose feasibility can be verified for a sample of instances.

</details>


### [137] [All roads lead to Markov kernel networks: A representation theorem for discretization-stable learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10051)
*S David Mis,Trevor Teolis,Ivan Dokmanić,Matti Lassas,Maarten V. de Hoop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"一致离散化稳定性"的定量刻画，并证明其算子均可精确表示为"稳定马尔可夫核网络"，从而统一刻画 transformer、FNO、Flowers 等架构的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子学习需在输入离散化变化时保持稳定（离散化不变性），但原有概念缺乏定量刻画，也缺少对现有成功架构（transformer、FNO、Flowers）为何具备该性质的统一理论解释与系统化设计原则。

Method: 将输入函数及其离散化统一表示为概率测度，用测度间 Wasserstein 距离给出输出变化的 Hölder 界，定义"一致离散化稳定性"；进而提出稳定马尔可夫核网络这一抽象架构，并用正则化变分问题的解来刻画其读核（read kernel）。

Result: 证明表示定理：所有一致离散化稳定算子都可精确写成稳定马尔可夫核网络。恰当选择代价函数与正则项时，其核可复原 transformer、FNO、Flowers 及其混合架构的核，并获得一致离散化稳定性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 表示定理给出离散化稳定算子学习的设计空间：新架构设计归结为选择参数化马尔可夫核网络的哪一部分及如何参数化；变分形式则提供系统的架构设计方法与稳定性充分条件。

Abstract: Operator learning seeks to approximate function-to-function maps in a way that remains stable when the input discretization changes, a property sometimes called "discretization invariance". We consider a quantitative refinement of this idea, called $\textit{uniform discretization stability}$, which builds on a common representation of input functions and their discretizations as probability measures and provides a Hölder bound relating output changes to the Wasserstein distance between these measures. Our main result is a representation theorem showing all uniformly discretization stable operators can be written exactly as a $\textit{stable Markov kernel network}$---an abstract architecture that is a joint generalization of transformers, Fourier neural operators (FNO), Flowers, and other successful learned operators. Our representation theorem characterizes a design space for discretization-stable operator learning; designing new architectures becomes a question of which parts of a Markov kernel network to parameterize, and how. We further show that the read kernels of these networks can be realized as solutions of regularized variational problems, with particular choices of costs and regularizers recovering the kernels of transformers, FNO, Flowers, their hybrids, and beyond. This variational formulation provides a systematic approach to architecture design, together with sufficient conditions for uniform discretization stability.

</details>


### [138] [fNIRSAtlas: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Functional Near-Infrared Spectroscopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10082)
*Kalle Mäkelä,Tuukka Ruotsalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: fNIRSAtlas：目前最大的fNIRS分类开放基准，含19个公开数据集、23个分类任务，提供全自动处理流水线与标准化跨被试/被试内评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fNIRS在脑机接口与认知状态解码方面潜力大，但机器学习进展受限于数据集规模小、彼此孤立以及评测协议不一致，缺少可复现、可累积的对比平台。

Method: 构建统一基准：整合19个公开数据集的23个分类任务，覆盖运动执行、运动想象、心算、工作记忆、情绪识别等范式；提供从原始数据到评测的全自动流水线；定义标准化跨被试与被试内分类任务；并在其上系统评测来自fNIRS、相关神经影像及时间序列文献的多种基线模型。

Result: 不同任务间性能差异显著；在有限数据上验证过的方法（尤其是深度学习方法）往往无法泛化到原始评测设定之外。

Conclusion: fNIRSAtlas为fNIRS机器学习提供了可扩展、开放、可复现的基准，有助于推动更稳健且可累积的研究进展。

Abstract: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) is a promising modality for brain-computer interfaces and cognitive state decoding, but progress in machine learning for fNIRS has been limited by small, isolated datasets and inconsistent evaluation protocols. We introduce fNIRSAtlas, the largest open benchmark for fNIRS classification to date, comprising 23 classification tasks from 19 public data collections, spanning diverse experimental paradigms, including motor execution, motor imagery, mental arithmetic, working memory, and emotion recognition. fNIRSAtlas provides a fully automated pipeline from raw data to evaluation and defines standardized benchmark tasks for both cross-subject and within-subject classification, ensuring reproducible comparison of methods. We evaluate a variety of baseline models from fNIRS and related neuroimaging and time-series machine learning literature on the benchmark. Our results demonstrate that performance varies substantially across tasks and that methods validated on limited data -- especially deep learning approaches -- often fail to generalize beyond their original evaluation. fNIRSAtlas serves as an extensible, open, and reproducible benchmark to support more robust and cumulative progress in machine learning for fNIRS data. Our code and data can be found at https://fnirsatlas.github.io.

</details>


### [139] [Transferring local rib responses to new component geometries in sheet forming with a graph-based Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10086)
*Yingxue Zhao,Haoran Li,Haosu Zhou,Tobias Pfaff,Nan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基线条件残差代理模型 R-BiLoGo，将局部特征效应与全局几何解耦：先在单一全局几何上学习加强筋效应，再通过迁移学习推广到其他几何，仅需少量目标仿真即可达到或超过从头训练的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钣金件由全局几何（如圆角半径、成形深度）与附加局部特征共同组成，二者都会影响材料流动。若代理模型学习所有组合需大量训练数据；但同一类局部特征常被用于多种全局几何上，因此存在"部分解耦"以减少仿真需求的可能。

Method: 针对热冲压转角件侧壁加强筋，构建基线条件残差代理模型 R-BiLoGo：输入为基线响应与加强筋引入的几何扰动，输出为相对基线的位移与减薄变化量。在源几何上训练后，通过迁移学习转移到目标几何。用四种无参数几何描述子及4个转角子类的受控变化，分析全局几何对迁移效果的影响。对比模型为层级图神经网络模型与基于 Transolver 的模型。

Result: R-BiLoGo 在源几何及迁移后的3个目标几何中的2个上误差低于两个对比模型。在22个目标几何中，20次目标仿真的迁移学习对约半数目标达到与80次从头训练相当或更低的误差；在同样80次仿真下，迁移学习使95%的目标误差下降。迁移收益随源-目标表面距离增大、曲率变化增大以及目标比源更浅而下降；这些趋势在其他转角类型中也存在，但幅度不同。

Conclusion: 局部特征与全局几何的部分解耦结合迁移学习可显著降低仿真数据需求，残差式建模有效；迁移收益可由几何描述子（表面距离、曲率变化、深度关系）预判，但趋势幅度依赖转角类型。

Abstract: Sheet metal components often consist of a global geometry, such as the corner radii and forming depth of a corner component, and local features added to it. During forming, both affect material flow. A surrogate learning all their combinations may need large training data. However, the same local feature type is often used on many global geometries. This study therefore partly decouples local features from the global geometry. The local-feature effect is first learned on one global geometry, needing fewer simulations than learning all combinations, then transferred to other global geometries via transfer learning, which can need fewer target simulations than training from scratch. A baseline-conditioned residual surrogate, R-BiLoGo, is proposed for ribs on the side walls of hot-stamped corner components. Its inputs are the baseline response and the geometric perturbation introduced by the ribs. Its outputs are the changes in displacement and thinning from the baseline. R-BiLoGo gave lower errors than a hierarchical graph-based model and a Transolver-based model on the source geometry, and on two of three selected target geometries after transfer. Across the 22 target geometries, transfer learning with 20 target simulations gave errors similar to or lower than training from scratch with 80 target simulations for about half of the targets. With the same 80 target simulations, transfer learning reduced errors for 95% of targets. The effect of the global geometry on transfer was studied with four parameter-free geometric descriptors and controlled variations across 4 corner subclasses. The benefit of transfer decreased with increasing surface distance and high-curvature change between source and target, and for targets shallower than the source. These trends were also seen in other corner types with a different magnitude.

</details>


### [140] [Coordinated Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization for Efficient LLM-based Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10096)
*Weizhong Huang,Jinchao Zhang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMixKV 是首个在共享内存预算下对同时驻留的多个智能体 KV 缓存进行联合混合精度分配的量化框架，通过在线敏感度估计与联合位宽分配实现高精度压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统依靠协作解决复杂任务，但多个智能体同时驻留的 KV 缓存不断增长，形成内存瓶颈。若独立地对单一模型套用量化策略，会忽略各智能体对精度需求的不同，可能损害整体推理性能。

Method: 提出协同混合精度 KV 缓存量化（CoMixKV），结合在线敏感度估计与共享预算下的智能体—层联合位宽分配。估计器基于局部注意力误差上界，利用近期 query、key、value 推导敏感度，无需校准数据、试量化或额外前向。共享控制器将敏感度分数与剩余可量化容量及字节级存储开销结合，以最小化总估计误差；当智能体启停或出现新观测时刷新分配。所选位宽仅作用于新量化的块，近期状态保持原生精度，已量化块保持不变。

Result: 在四个基准与三个 LLM 主干、平均 2/3/4 bit 设置下，CoMixKV 相较所评估的量化基线取得较强准确率。Gemma-4-31B-it 平均 3-bit 下达到 68.35% 平均准确率，超出最强量化基线 0.59%；Qwen3-32B 上峰值 KV 缓存内存降低 32.08%，相较 FullKV 获得 1.38 倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 跨智能体、跨层的协调式混合精度分配能在严格内存预算下兼顾精度与效率，优于独立单模型量化策略，并显著降低多智能体系统的 KV 缓存开销。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems solve complex tasks through collaboration, but their growing, simultaneously resident key-value (KV) caches create a memory bottleneck. Applying single-model quantization policies independently ignores differences in agents' precision needs, potentially degrading collective reasoning. We introduce Coordinated Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization (CoMixKV), which combines online sensitivity estimation with joint agent-layer bit-width allocation under a shared memory budget. To our knowledge, CoMixKV is the first mixed-precision KV-cache quantization framework to jointly allocate precision across simultaneously resident agent caches under such a budget. The estimator derives sensitivity from a local attention-error bound using recent queries, keys, and values, without calibration data, trial quantization, or additional model passes. A shared controller combines these scores with remaining quantizable capacity and byte-level storage costs to minimize total estimated error. Allocations are refreshed as agents start or finish and new observations become available. Selected widths apply only to newly quantized blocks; recent states retain native precision and previously quantized blocks remain unchanged. Across four benchmarks and three LLM backbones at average 2-bit, 3-bit, and 4-bit settings, CoMixKV achieves strong accuracy against the evaluated quantization baselines. At the average 3-bit setting on Gemma-4-31B-it, it achieves 68.35% average accuracy, exceeding the strongest quantized baseline by 0.59%. On Qwen3-32B, it reduces peak KV-cache memory by 32.08% and achieves a 1.38x inference speedup over FullKV.

</details>


### [141] [TrialJev: Open-Weight Single Pass Eligibility Scoring for Patient Trial Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10145)
*Omar Ibrahim,Grant Hamblin,Benjamin Haibe-Kains*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrialJev：基于LoRA适配Qwen与指针头的单次前向推理合格性打分器，用于临床试验筛选的首轮优先级排序，在无需自回归解码的前提下达到约81%准确率，速度比生成式判决快2.45–2.70倍，并可在单卡本地运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验筛选需评估大量患者—试验配对以优先选出需详细资格审查的候选，且常受患者记录不可外传的共享限制。现有LLM判决生成方式需自回归解码，推理慢且可能需调用外部API。

Method: 提出TrialJev：采用LoRA适配的Qwen模型加指针头（pointer head），对自然语言答案选项在单次前向传播中直接打分，不做自回归解码。在TREC Clinical Trials 2021上训练，在TREC 2022的50名未见合成患者的6,975个合格/排除配对上评估；与同数据微调生成判决的LLM对比，并做batch-1匹配计时、统一开发集拟合的决策校正，以及A/B-logit消融。

Result: 准确率80.97%（Qwen3.5-27B）与81.84%（Qwen3.8-27B），优于生成式微调LLM的79.87%与79.93%；batch-1下判决生成耗时是TrialJev单次推理的2.45–2.70倍（9B–27B）。经统一决策校正后两模型达80.99%与81.88%，与专有Jev API的81.28%相当（配对置信区间含零）。27B单对约178 ms，单GPU本地运行，患者文本不出本地。消融显示效率优势来自免解码而非指针头本身。

Conclusion: TrialJev定位于候选试验的首轮优先级排序，补充而非替代可解释模型与临床审查。局限为仅用合成患者、计时依赖具体实现，临床用途未经验证；适配器、指针头与模型卡已开源于Hugging Face。

Abstract: Clinical trial screening requires assessing large numbers of patient-trial pairs to prioritize candidates for detailed eligibility review, often under restrictions on sharing patient records. We introduce TrialJev, an open-weight eligibility scorer that uses LoRA-adapted Qwen models and a pointer head to score natural-language answer options in a single forward pass, without autoregressive decoding. Trained on TREC Clinical Trials 2021 and evaluated on 6,975 eligible-or-excluded pairs from 50 unseen TREC 2022 synthetic patients, TrialJev achieves 80.97% (Qwen3.5-27B) and 81.84% (Qwen3.8-27B) accuracy, compared with 79.87% and 79.93% for LLMs fine-tuned on the same data to generate verdicts. In matched batch-1 measurements, verdict generation takes 2.45-2.70 times as long as the one-pass inference of TrialJev at 9B-27B. Under a common development-fitted decision correction, the two 27B TrialJev models reach 80.99% and 81.88% accuracy, compared with 81.28% for the proprietary hosted Jev API; paired confidence intervals include zero. TrialJev runs locally on a single GPU, requiring approximately 178 ms per pair at 27B, without transmitting patient text to an external model API. An A/B-logit ablation shows that avoiding decoding, rather than the pointer head alone, accounts for the measured efficiency advantage. TrialJev is intended for first-pass prioritization of candidate trials, complementing rather than replacing explanatory models and clinical review. Evaluation is limited to synthetic patients and implementation-specific timing, and clinical use has not been validated. Adapters, pointer heads, and model cards are released on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Across Heterogeneous Feature Spaces with Group Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10153)
*Xenia Konti,Arjun Tschand,Chuan Hong,Nicoleta J. Economou-Zavlanos,Michael M. Zavlanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向异构特征空间的group robust learning框架，以"worst-group excess loss"（组损失与其可达最优的差距）替代最坏组损失作为优化目标，并用组专属编码器映射到共享隐空间、以可学习高斯锚点按结局聚类，实现组间知识迁移；在表格与影像医学数据（含完全不相交特征场景）上最坏组损失最多降79%、excess loss最多降95%，参数最多少18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上患者接受的检查组合不同，导致特征空间异构；现有方法按最高损失加权患者，但高损失可能仅源于特征信息量不足而非模型欠拟合，且当特征空间完全不相交时退化为各组建独立模型、无法迁移知识。

Method: 将"组"定义为共享同一特征空间的患者集合；不直接优化最坏组损失，而优化最坏组的excess loss（该组损失与其可达最优估计之差）；为促成组间协作，各组使用专属编码器映射至共享隐空间，并在其中引入可学习高斯锚点按结局对患者进行聚类。

Result: 在表格与影像医学数据集（包括特征完全不相交的设定）上，当各组最优损失不同或单组无法训练出好模型时，优于现有SOTA；最坏组损失最多降低79%，excess loss最多降低95%，参数量最多减少18倍。

Conclusion: 以excess loss衡量组的"可改进空间"比原始损失更能识别真正欠拟合的组，避免被无信息特征主导；共享隐空间+高斯锚点可在特征不相交时仍实现跨组知识迁移，并显著降低参数开销。

Abstract: In clinical applications, patients undergo different combinations of tests, and machine learning models must therefore ensure accurate predictions across heterogeneous feature spaces. Current approaches upweight the patients with the highest loss, without accounting for the limited predictive information in their features preventing their loss from going any lower. Moreover, when patients' feature spaces are fully disjoint, current methods reduce to training independent models, preventing knowledge transfer across them. We propose a framework for group robust learning across heterogeneous feature spaces, where groups are patients sharing a feature space. Since a high loss may simply reflect uninformative features, we instead optimize for worst-group excess loss, the gap between a group's loss and an estimate of its achievable optimum. To allow collaboration between groups, we use group-specific encoders mapping into a shared latent space in which learnable Gaussian anchors cluster patients by outcome. On tabular and imaging medical datasets, including fully disjoint feature settings, our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods when groups differ in their optimal losses or cannot train a good model on their own, reducing worst-group loss by up to 79% and excess loss by up to 95% with up to 18$\times$ fewer parameters.

</details>


### [143] [Projected Neural Additive Models as Universal Approximators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10186)
*Nhon N. Phan,Qiang Du,John D. Clayton,WaiChing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文为投影神经可加模型（PNAM）建立首个理论基础：证明其在保留可解释性的同时具备万能逼近能力，并给出最优逼近率，配合结构化稀疏与符号回归恢复可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经可加模型（NAM）可解释但缺乏表达能力，因其刚性可加结构无法刻画跨变量交互（连 χ1χ2 都无法逼近）；而 MLP 表达力强却缺乏透明性。作者希望弥合二者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在 NAM 中引入可学习的线性变换 T 得到 PNAM，以捕捉跨变量交互；理论上证明其万能逼近性并刻画精确表示多项式所需的最少投影数，同时推导 C^s 函数的逼近率 O(M^{-s/(N-1)})。为从投影输入恢复可解释性，引入结构化稀疏正则与事后符号回归，实现全局输入排序、参数剪枝及学习基到紧凑解析式的转换。

Result: 理论上：标准 NAM 的交叉导数恒为零，无法逼近最简单的交互项 χ1χ2；PNAM 为万能逼近器，且所需的投影数逼近率对岭函数逼近而言是最优的。实验上：在纽结不变量、MNIST 与相场断裂力学任务中，PNAM 的精度匹配或优于同类架构，同时能发现显著特征，并给出参数量少几个数量级的符号模型。

Conclusion: PNAM 在理论上统一了可解释性与表达力，在实践中兼具高精度、特征发现能力与极简符号表达，为可解释机器学习提供了有理论保证的新范式。

Abstract: Neural additive models (NAMs) offer interpretability but lack expressivity due to their rigid additive structure, whereas multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) achieve universal approximation at the cost of transparency. We establish the first theoretical foundation for projected neural additive models (PNAMs), which augment NAMs with a learnable linear transformation $\boldsymbol{T}$ to capture cross-variable interactions. While standard NAMs cannot approximate even the simplest interaction term $χ_1 χ_2$ because their cross-derivatives are identically zero, we prove that PNAMs are universal approximators and characterize the minimum number of projections required for exact polynomial representation. Furthermore, we derive an approximation rate of $\mathcal{O}(M^{-s / (N - 1)})$ for $\mathcal{C}^s$ functions (i.e., functions with $s$ continuous derivatives), where $M$ denotes the number of projections and $N$ the input dimension; this rate is known to be optimal for ridge function approximation. To recover interpretability from the projected inputs, we introduce structured sparsity regularizers and post-hoc symbolic regression techniques that enable global input ranking, parameter pruning, and the conversion of learned bases into compact analytical expressions. Experiments on knot theory invariants, MNIST, and phase field fracture mechanics demonstrate that PNAMs match or surpass similar architectures in accuracy while discovering salient features and yielding symbolic models with orders of magnitude fewer parameters.

</details>


### [144] [Fast Accurate Quantum Monte Carlo without Metropolis Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10187)
*Reuben Cohn-Gordon,Gabriel Pescia,Sumner N. Hearth,Jakob Robnik,Uroš Seljak,Peter Lunts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"未校正"哈密顿蒙特卡洛（unadjusted HMC）引入量子蒙特卡洛领域，在自旋-费米子量子相变模型上验证其相较标准Metropolis-Hastings校正HMC具有更高的每次梯度调用的有效样本量，同时保持结果精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统物理量可写成概率分布的期望，可用MCMC（即QMC）估计；其中以哈密顿动力学设计马尔可夫核的HMC在量子应用中广泛使用。通常HMC核须用Metropolis-Hastings准则"校正"，以保证估计量在样本无穷时收敛到真值。但近期计算统计学研究表明，未校正HMC可显著更高效，其渐近偏差相对于有限样本方差引起的误差可忽略。作者希望把这一思路迁移到QMC场景，检验其在高维复杂量子模型上的可行性。

Method: 选取一个具有挑战性的高维"自旋-费米子"量子相变模型作为测试平台，将未校正HMC核适配到QMC设定中，并与采用Metropolis-Hastings校正的标准HMC进行比较，评价指标为每次梯度调用的有效样本量（ESS per gradient call），同时考察结果精度（渐近偏差是否可忽略）。

Result: 在自旋-费米子量子相变模型上，未校正HMC在每次梯度调用的有效样本量上优于校正HMC，并且结果仍保持准确，即其引入的渐近偏差相对于有限样本方差误差足够小。

Conclusion: 未校正HMC可有效迁移至量子蒙特卡洛，是比MH校正HMC更高效的采样方案；在保持精度的前提下提升采样效率，为凝聚态与高能物理中量子系统期望值的估计提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: In quantum systems, physical quantities of interest can be expressed as expectation values of a probability distribution, which allows their estimation by Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods. This approach, an instance of Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC), plays a central role in obtaining predictions of system properties relevant to condensed matter and high energy physics. The use of Hamiltonian dynamics to design a Markov kernel, known as Hybrid or Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC), is widespread in quantum applications. In this context, it is standard to use an HMC kernel that is ``adjusted'' with the Metropolis-Hastings (MH) criterion, so that estimates of expectations converge to their true value in the limit of infinite sample size. However, recent work in computational statistics suggests that an \emph{unadjusted} HMC kernel can be significantly more efficient, while safely maintaining an asymptotic bias which is small relative to the error arising from the variance of the finite sample size. We adapt this approach to the QMC setting, focusing on a challenging and high-dimensional \emph{spin-fermion} model of a quantum phase transition. We find that unadjusted methods outperform adjusted HMC in terms of effective sample size per gradient call, while retaining accurate results.

</details>


### [145] [BiMuon: A Geometry-Aware Stochastic Bilevel Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10188)
*Zhen Qin,Zhiyao Zhang,Yingbin Liang,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BiMuon：首个结合隐式超梯度估计与Muon式正交化矩阵更新的随机双层优化方法，具O(ε^{-4})复杂度保证，并给出分块对角近似与步长分配规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化方法将矩阵梯度展平为向量处理，忽略了现代神经网络中普遍的矩阵结构；同时下层问题的曲率会导致上层几何出现显著谱不平衡。

Method: 将双层优化中的隐式超梯度（hypergradient）估计与上层Muon式正交化更新相结合，直接以矩阵参数进行优化；进一步针对多矩阵参数模型引入分块对角矩阵近似，并基于上层进展与分块下层敏感性推导相对步长分配规则。

Result: 理论上给出O(ε^{-4})的迭代复杂度，与基于矩阵参数化的Muon类方法最优已知速率持平；在表示学习与分块双层优化任务上提供了实证支持。

Conclusion: BiMuon表明在上层采用矩阵感知的正交化更新可有效缓解展平向量化与下层曲率引起的谱不平衡，兼具理论保证与并行计算效率，适用于大规模多矩阵参数模型的随机双层优化。

Abstract: Stochastic bilevel optimization provides a general framework for many machine learning applications, such as hyperparameter learning, data reweighting, language model alignment, and actor-critic reinforcement learning. However, most existing bilevel optimization methods treat matrix-valued gradients as flattened vectors and perform optimization in the resulting vectorized space, ignoring the matrix structures common in modern neural networks. Moreover, the lower-level (LL) problem curvature can induce significant spectral imbalance in the upper-level (UL) geometry. Such limitations of existing bilevel optimization methods motivate us to develop BiMuon, a geometry-aware stochastic bilevel optimization method that integrates implicit hypergradient estimation in bilevel optimization with Muon-style orthogonalized updates at the UL. To our knowledge, BiMuon is the first stochastic BLO method with Muon-style orthogonalized matrix updates and an $O(ε^{-4})$ iteration-complexity guarantee, matching the best-known rates achieved by Muon-type methods based on matrix parameterizations. To improve computational and parallel efficiency for models with multiple matrix parameters, we further study BiMuon for blockwise diagonal matrix approximation and derive a relative stepsize allocation rule based on UL progress and blockwise LL sensitivity. We evaluate BiMuon on representation learning and blockwise bilevel optimization, providing empirical support for our methods.

</details>


### [146] [BO-Arena: An Evolving Benchmark for High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10198)
*Colin Doumont,Thomas Christie,Henry Moss,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑高维贝叶斯优化（HDBO）领域基准测试实践的可靠性，指出存在基线选择不当与关键方法实现错误等问题，并据此提出统一参考实现包 BO-Arena，以及一个在观测数据较多时可与当前最优方法竞争的更简单算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDBO 领域新方法层出不穷，但"哪个方法才是当前最优"这一问题难以回答。作者认为该领域的基准测试实践系统性失效：已发表工作往往未与最强基线对比，甚至包含关键方法的错误实现；而缺乏共享参考实现使得此类错误在同行评审中难以被发现。

Method: 提出 BO-Arena——一个持续维护、可扩展的软件包，提供当前最优算法的规范化（canonical）实现，支持研究者开发新方法并严格地进行基准测试。基于 BO-Arena 的实验分析进一步提炼出真正驱动 HDBO 性能的因素，并据此设计了一个新算法；该算法不依赖近年来方法中使用的复杂 GP 近似。

Result: 借助 BO-Arena 的系统评测，作者发现 HDBO 的性能驱动因素比此前认为的更为简单；由此提出的新算法在观测数量较多的问题上可与当前最优方法竞争，且无需复杂的高斯过程近似。

Conclusion: 作者对当前证据是否足以支撑"更复杂方法优于简单高斯过程基线"这一主张提出质疑，呼吁以共享参考实现和规范化基准测试来保障 HDBO 研究的可比性与可信度。

Abstract: High-dimensional Bayesian optimisation (HDBO) is an increasingly crowded field with new methods proposed at every major conference, making it ever harder to answer a deceptively simple question: "Which method is currently state-of-the-art?" In this paper, we question the efficacy of benchmarking practices in the field, and find them to be systematically ineffective: multiple published works fail to benchmark against the strongest available baselines, or even include incorrect implementations of key methods. Without a shared reference implementation, such errors are difficult to detect during peer review. We therefore introduce BO-Arena, an actively maintained and extensible package providing canonical implementations of state-of-the-art algorithms, which allows researchers to develop new methods and benchmark them rigorously. Using BO-Arena, we find that what currently drives performance in HDBO is even simpler than previously thought. We use these insights to propose a new algorithm which is competitive with the state of the art on problems with many observations, without resorting to the complex GP approximations used in recent methods. Finally, we question whether there is currently sufficient evidence that any more complex method outperforms a simple Gaussian process baseline for HDBO.

</details>


### [147] [Semiprime Factorization with Discrete Diffusion and Beam Search: Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10202)
*Brendan O Donoghue,Nenad Tomasev,Volodymyr Mnih,Timothy Nguyen,Dalin Guo,Arun Nair,Kenneth Yang,Alexandre Frechette,Jasper Xian,Jean Tarbouriech,Pavel Tyletski,Ingrid von Glehn,Johannes Bausch,Demis Hassabis,Raia Hadsell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用离散去噪扩散训练 Transformer，并通过带引导的测试时束搜索解码，直接从数据中学习半素数因式分解，成功分解 68 位整数（此前最优为 56 位），且无需数论先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索学习式方法在半素数因式分解上能达到的极限，检验不依赖数论先验、纯数据驱动的生成模型能否学习并恢复素数因子，并厘清规模律规律及其对密码安全的意义。

Method: 以离散去噪扩散目标训练 Transformer；推理时用引导式测试时束搜索（guided test-time beam search）解码因子候选；扩散模型充当生成先验，配合精确验证或格基后处理完成最终分解；并推导近似质量与训练数据量、模型规模、测试时计算之间的经验规模律。

Result: 成功分解 68 位半素数，较此前 56 位的最优结果显著提升；发现与语言任务不同，问题规模（比特长度）是规模行为的主要驱动因素，在固定测试精度下所需计算随比特长度指数增长；效率低于经典算法。

Conclusion: 学习式扩散模型可从纯数据中学习因式分解并扩展前沿，但计算随比特长度指数增长，分解密码相关规模整数所需算力仍不可行，因此不构成对现有密码安全的威胁。

Abstract: We demonstrate that transformer architectures trained with discrete denoising diffusion and decoded via guided test-time beam search can learn to recover the prime factors of semiprime integers directly from data. Our approach establishes a new frontier for learning-based semiprime factorization, successfully factoring 68-bit integers-a substantial advance over the prior 56-bit state of the art. While computationally less efficient than classical factoring algorithms, our model requires no domain-specific number-theoretic priors and learns purely from training examples. In practice, the diffusion model acts as a generative prior, identifying promising factor candidates where exact verification or lattice-based post-processing can complete the factorization. Furthermore, we derive empirical scaling laws relating approximation quality to training data volume, model size, and test-time computation. In contrast to language, problem size is the key driver of scaling behavior. Achieving a fixed accuracy on a held-out test set requires compute that scales exponentially with bit length. Consequently, our findings pose no threat to security, as the projected compute cost required to factorize cryptographically relevant integers remains prohibitive.

</details>


### [148] [DisMUSE: An Efficient Distributed Singular-Spectrum-Based Optimization Approach with Theoretical Convergence Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10212)
*Zhen Qin,Zhiyao Zhang,Yingbin Liang,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布式奇异谱优化框架 DisMUSE，采用 Muon 式精确正交化动量作为谱更新，结合递归方差缩减动量估计器，在数据中心与联邦学习场景下达到 O((KT)^{-1/3}) 的收敛率；并进一步分析有限 Newton-Schulz 迭代与低秩动量传输两种扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奇异谱的优化器（SSB）虽能保持梯度矩阵结构、实现几何感知优化，但大多为集中式，难以用于大规模并行的分布式学习基础设施；分布式 SSB 优化尚处早期，收敛保证与通信效率仍是开放问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以 Muon 风格的精确正交化动量作为谱更新步骤，构建 DisMUSE 框架，并集成递归方差缩减的动量估计器；在此基础上研究两种扩展：(1) 使用有限步 Newton-Schulz (NS) 迭代近似正交化的变体，在统一归一化支撑谱条件下分析其收敛性；(2) 基于低秩动量传输的通信高效变体，量化其引入的误差。

Result: DisMUSE 达到 O((KT)^{-1/3}) 的收敛率（K 为节点数，T 为每节点本地迭代数），与方差缩减非凸分布式优化的已知最优速率相匹配；有限 NS 迭代版本在统一归一化支撑谱条件下保持相同收敛阶（仅差一个乘性因子）；低秩传输变体给出了引入误差对平稳性界的影响刻画，明确了精度与通信开销之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地为分布式奇异谱优化提供了收敛保证与通信效率分析，统一覆盖数据中心与联邦场景，并通过 NS 近似和低秩动量传输的误差分析展示了实际部署中精度—通信权衡的可控性。

Abstract: In recent years, singular-spectrum-based optimizers have attracted increasing attention due to their ability to preserve the matrix structure of neural network gradients, thereby enabling geometry-aware optimization. However, most singular-spectrum-based (SSB) approaches remain centralized, limiting their use in distributed learning infrastructures that exploit massive parallelism. Distributed SSB optimization is still at an early stage, with open questions about convergence guarantees and communication efficiency. To fill this gap, we propose the Distributed Momentum-based Spectral Estimator (DisMUSE), an efficient distributed SSB optimization framework for both data-center and federated settings. Using Muon-style exact orthogonalized momentum as the spectral update, DisMUSE integrates a recursive variance-reduced momentum estimator and achieves an $O((KT)^{-1/3})$ convergence rate, where $K$ and $T$ denote the number of nodes and local iterations per node, respectively. This rate matches the best-known rates for variance-reduced nonconvex distributed optimization. Building on this variance-reduced framework, we study two extensions. First, our DisMUSE method with finite Newton-Schulz (NS) iterations preserves the same convergence order under a uniform normalized support-spectrum condition, up to a multiplicative factor. Second, we develop a communication-efficient variant based on low-rank (LR) momentum transmission and quantify the error it introduces to the stationarity bound, characterizing the trade-off between accuracy and communication cost.

</details>


### [149] [Rethinking How to Leverage Cheap Physics for Data-Efficient Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10217)
*Shuo Zhang,Senwei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PI-Init：先用物理信息损失在无标签输入上预训练神经算子，再在标签数据上监督微调，从而以廉价物理信息提升标签效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督训练神经算子需昂贵的数值解标签，而PDE输入与方程本身是廉价物理信息。作者先检验"用物理信息挑选待标注输入"（主动学习式采样），实验发现相对随机采样无稳定优势，故转向利用廉价物理信息改进训练初始化。

Method: PI-Init：在无标签输入上用物理信息损失（方程残差等）预训练神经算子，保留所得参数作为监督微调的初始化；并给出线性化分析，说明预训练通过减小有限标签无法纠正的初始化误差来提升下游精度。

Result: 在多种PDE基准与多种神经算子架构上，预测精度、分布外泛化与标签效率均有提升；同等标签预算下误差最高降低62.7%，少用90%标签即可达到相当或更优精度。

Conclusion: 廉价物理信息的最佳用途在于改进初始化而非选择标注样本；PI-Init简单有效、架构无关，可显著提升神经算子的标签效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from PDE inputs, such as initial conditions and coefficient fields, to the corresponding solutions. Once trained, they can rapidly predict solutions for new inputs. However, obtaining input-solution pairs for supervised training often requires expensive numerical simulations, whereas PDE inputs and governing equations provide inexpensive physical information. This raises a question: how can cheap physical information reduce the need for labeled solutions? A natural approach is to use physics to select inputs for labeling, but our acquisition experiments show no consistent advantage over random sampling. This observation motivates exploring an alternative: using cheap physics to improve neural operator initialization before supervised training. We propose Physics-Informed Initialization (PI-Init), a simple yet effective method that pretrains neural operators on unlabeled inputs using physics-informed losses and retains the learned parameters for supervised fine-tuning on the labeled dataset. A linearized analysis shows that pretraining can improve downstream prediction accuracy by reducing initialization errors that limited labeled data cannot effectively correct. Experiments across diverse PDE benchmarks and neural operator architectures demonstrate improvements in predictive accuracy, out-of-distribution generalization, and label efficiency. Representative results show error reductions of up to 62.7% at the same label budget and comparable or better accuracy with 90% fewer labels, relative to supervised-only training.

</details>


### [150] [Lower-Level Agnostic Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10223)
*Peiwen Qiu,Prashant Khanduri,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究下层级（LL）不可见（LL-agnostic）的双层优化问题，提出 LeGo-BiO：一种坐标式伪超梯度方法，用有限差分估计缺失的 LL 响应 Jacobian；在 LL 误差界/Polyak-Łojasiewicz 条件下分别给出确定性 O(T^{-1}) 与随机 O(T^{-1/2}) 收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化算法普遍假设下层级目标可评估、可微分或可展开（unrolling），但当 LL 响应来自黑盒求解器、模拟器、对手或学习得到的预测器时，该假设失效。因此需要一种仅能访问近似 LL 解、无法查询 LL 目标/梯度/Hessian/Jacobian 及生成过程的算法。

Method: 提出 LeGo-BiO——坐标式伪超梯度方法：对每个上层坐标构造受 1-sparse 更新约束的决策向量，利用 \bar{y}(·) 的有限差分逐坐标估计 ∇_x \bar{y}(x)，从而隔离出上层梯度计算所需的对应 Jacobian 列；相较全参数响应差分的方向投影局限，以及针对整个上层梯度的免导数/零阶近似，该方法保留了关于上层变量的一阶梯度信息，提升上层梯度评估精度。

Result: 在确定性设置下得到 O(T^{-1}) 收敛率；在随机设置下，当 LL 近似误差几何递减时得到 O(T^{-1/2}) 收敛率。理论保证基于 LL 误差界/Polyak-Łojasiewicz 型条件，无需 LL 强凸性。实验表明，即使对 LL 目标完全不可知，LeGo-BiO 仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LeGo-BiO 为 LL 不可知场景提供了一种可行且具理论保证的双层优化方案：以一阶信息保留加坐标式 Jacobian 估计替代对 LL 的访问，并在弱于强凸性的条件下获得收敛保证。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a fundamental framework for machine learning problems, where an upper-level (UL) objective depends on the solution of a nested lower-level (LL) problem. Existing bilevel algorithms typically assume that the LL objective is available for evaluation, differentiation, or unrolling. However, this assumption can fail when the LL response is produced by a black-box solver, simulator, adversary, or learned predictor. In this paper, we study LL agnostic bilevel optimization, where the UL learner has access only to an approximate LL solution $\bar{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x})$ and cannot query the LL objective, its gradients, Hessian/Jacobian information, or the procedure that generates the LL response. We propose LeGo-BiO, a coordinate-wise pseudo-hypergradient method that estimates the missing Jacobian $\nabla _{\mathbf{x}}\bar{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x})$ using finite differences of $\bar{\mathbf{y}}(\cdot)$. For each UL coordinate, LeGo-BiO constructs a decision vector subject to 1-sparse updates, thereby isolating the corresponding Jacobian column needed in the UL gradient computation. This avoids the directional-projection limitation of full-parameter response differences and, unlike derivative-free/zeroth-order approximations of the entire UL gradient, preserves the available first-order gradient information with respect to the UL variables for more accurate UL gradient evaluation. We establish convergence guarantees for LeGo-BiO in both deterministic and stochastic settings under an LL error-bound/Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type condition, without requiring LL strong convexity. Our bounds yield an $O(T^{-1})$ rate in the deterministic setting and an $O(T^{-1/2})$ rate in the stochastic setting when the LL approximation error decreases geometrically. Experiments demonstrate competitive performance of LeGo-BiO despite being agnostic to the LL objective.

</details>


### [151] [RAD-TFM: Robust and Domain-Adapted Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10230)
*Matthew Peroni,Franck Le,Vadim Sheinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAD-TFM（Robust and Domain-Adapted Tabular Foundation Models），一种模型无关的对抗式持续训练框架：将合成数据生成器分布的参数化视为对抗方，通过最优性间隙（optimality gap）度量使生成器聚焦于模型难以处理的数据集，并引入真实领域数据对齐约束以保证生成数据现实性，从而仅用极少额外合成数据提升表格基础模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）可完全依赖合成数据预训练，因此设计能诱导理想模型性质的数据生成器至关重要。既有工作主要致力于构造高质量先验以提升整体预训练效果，但忽略了"针对性强化模型薄弱环节"这一角度。作者希望从分布鲁棒性视角出发，让生成器主动强调对当前模型具有挑战性的数据集。

Method: 1）参数化生成器分布，将其视为对抗方，在训练中自适应调整以强调困难数据集；2）形式化引入最优性间隙（optimality gap）度量，用于刻画生成分布下模型性能与最优性能之间的差距并作为对抗目标；3）提出与给定领域真实数据对齐的方法，将对抗方约束在"真实数据"可行域内，避免生成不切实际的分布；4）以上算法构成 RAD-TFM 流水线，与具体模型无关，可用于对已有 TFM 的对抗式持续训练。

Result: 应用于 TabPFN V2 分类器时，RAD-TFM 在 6 个多样化表格基准上均取得提升，平均归一化 AUC 相比最强基线最高提升 10.8 个百分点，且仅额外使用不足 10 万个合成训练数据集（少于原始预训练数据的 0.1%）。将同一流程应用于 TabICL V2，也在三大基准上取得性能提升，表明框架具有通用性。

Conclusion: 结果说明，仅依赖合成数据对 TFM 进行有针对性的对抗式持续训练是一条有前景的新方向；RAD-TFM 以极低的数据成本实现跨模型、跨基准的稳健增益，验证了将生成器分布参数化与分布鲁棒性、领域对齐相结合的有效性。

Abstract: The development of tabular foundation models (TFMs) has accelerated in recent years, outperforming traditional ML methods on many tabular benchmarks. A key finding is that TFMs can be pretrained entirely on synthetic datasets, opening opportunities to design data generators that encourage desirable model properties. Prior work has mainly focused on crafting high-quality priors over generators to improve overall pretraining performance. Our key insight is that parameterizing the generator distribution enables an adversarial, distributional robustness perspective: during training, we can adapt the generator to emphasize datasets that are particularly challenging for the model, which we formalize by introducing an optimality gap measure. Further, we develop a method to align the synthetic data generation with real-world datasets from a given domain, constraining the adversary to generate realistic data. Together, these algorithms comprise the Robust and Domain-Adapted Tabular Foundation Models (RAD-TFM) pipeline, a model-agnostic adversarial training framework. Applied to the TabPFN V2 classifier, RAD-TFM improves performance across 6 diverse tabular benchmarks, with up to a 10.8 percentage-point improvement in mean normalized AUC over the strongest baseline, using fewer than 100k additional synthetic training datasets, less than 0.1% of the original pretraining data. Applying the same pipeline to TabICL V2 also improves performance across three major benchmarks, demonstrating the generality of the framework. These results highlight a promising new direction for targeted adversarial continued training of TFMs using synthetic data alone.

</details>


### [152] [Implicit Bias in State Space Models and Linear Autoregressive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10237)
*Gal Katzhendler,Gilad Yehudai,Gal Vardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究线性SSM与线性自回归序列模型中"共享参数、步数样本相关"所导致的隐式偏置，证明梯度下降方向收敛到"有效次数最大间隔问题"的KKT点，并揭示长序列偏置机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性SSM与线性自回归模型在样本相关的步数上复用同一组参数，不同样本因此通过不同的预测器施加约束，但所有预测器共享权重。这种非齐次性与参数共享如何改变经典（齐次）隐式偏置结论尚不清楚，尤其缺少对长序列/多步自回归所带来偏置机制的一阶刻画。

Method: 构建一个更一般的模型：多个共享参数的非齐次多项式预测器的分类问题。在标准插值假设与方向收敛假设之外，引入依赖轨迹的"有效次数"条件；在此框架下分析指数损失上梯度下降的极限行为，推导其方向极限所满足的KKT条件。

Result: 在所述假设下，梯度下降（指数损失）沿方向收敛到一个"有效次数最大间隔问题"的KKT点。该问题中，只有有效次数最小的样本构成硬间隔约束；间隔增长更快的样本渐近不活跃（仅保留可行性要求）。由此给出线性SSM与线性自回归模型中隐式偏置的首个一阶刻画。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了一种长度偏置机制：更长的输入序列或额外的自回归步会提高样本的有效次数，而有效次数最小的样本主导极限分类器。结果说明非齐次性与参数共享如何实质性改变经典齐次隐式偏置结论。

Abstract: Linear State Space Models (SSMs) and linear autoregressive sequence models reuse the same parameters across a sample-dependent number of steps. As a result, different examples impose constraints through different predictors, even though all predictors share the same weights. We study this phenomenon through a general model of multiple non-homogeneous polynomial predictors with shared parameters, in a classification setting. Under standard interpolation and directional-convergence assumptions, together with a trajectory-dependent effective-degree condition, we show that gradient descent on the exponential loss converges in direction to a KKT point of an effective-degree max-margin problem. In this problem, only examples with minimal effective degree impose hard margin constraints; examples with faster-growing margins are asymptotically inactive except for feasibility.
  Our result provides a first-order characterization of the implicit bias in linear SSMs and linear autoregressive models. It exposes a mechanism of length bias: a longer input sequence or additional autoregressive steps may increase the example's effective degree, and the examples with the smallest effective degree dominate the limiting classifier. Our result highlights how non-homogeneity and parameter sharing alter classical homogeneous implicit-bias results.

</details>


### [153] [PACE: Keeping the Right Tokens in the KV Cache Is Not Enough for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10240)
*Suman Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出现有 KV 缓存驱逐方法只保证"需要的 token 在缓存中"并不够，因为长距离 rotary 位置会导致这些 token 无法被检索；提出 PACE，将"规划感知保留"与"有界位置重编号"结合，在 100K–2M token 会话中显著优于 H2O/SnapKV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM agent 的会话可达百万 token，KV cache 远超单卡容量，系统必须只保留少量 token。现有驱逐方法（如 H2O、SnapKV、planner-guided 策略）的共同假设是"只要后续步骤需要的 token 仍在缓存里就能用"，但作者用实验表明该假设不成立：在 Qwen3-8B、100K token 代码会话、2,048 token 预算下，保留率高达 0.996 的策略仍随"定义—使用"间距从 2K 增至 90K 使准确率由 93% 降至 64%。问题在于所需 token 与 query 的 rotary 位置距离过大导致不可检索，这是被忽视的失效来源。

Method: 提出 PACE（Planner-Aware Context Engine），包含两个互补组件：(1) plan-aware retention——由 agent 的 planner 告知缓存未来步骤将读取哪些上下文，从而指导驱逐决策；(2) bounded positions——对保留下来的 token 重新编号（renumbering），使其与 query 的 rotary 距离在任何会话长度下都保持在 8K 以内。评估采用预注册（pre-registered）的 fresh tasks 隐藏单元测试。

Result: 同预算下 100K token 会话中，PACE 通过 97% 隐藏单元测试，H2O 为 42%，SnapKV 为 3%；消融显示两个组件缺一不可，单独使用各为 63%（组合显著优于任一组件，p < 10^-4）。可扩展性：单张 24 GB GPU 上 1M 与 2M token 会话（定义分别早于使用 0.9M、1.8M token），PACE 通过 99% 与 100% 检查并召回全部会话事实，而无 foresight 的有界位置方案从 33% 降至 13%。PACE 仅占 <0.7 GB KV 状态，全量缓存则需 147–295 GB。重编号是精确的、无可测困惑度损失，仅增加 9% wall time。在 Mistral-7B（即便不驱逐其代码能力上限也只有 40%）上，PACE 保持在该上限，而仅用 retention 会低于该上限。

Conclusion: KV 驱逐的正确目标不应止于"保留所需 token"，还需控制其相对 query 的 rotary 位置距离；将 planner 前瞻与有界位置重编号结合，可在极小 KV 预算下支撑百万级 token 的 agent 会话，且开销可忽略。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate sessions of up to millions of tokens, far more key-value (KV) cache than one GPU holds, so serving systems keep a small budget of tokens and evict the rest. Eviction methods differ in which tokens they keep but share one goal: that the tokens a later step needs are still cached. We show this is not enough. In 100K-token coding sessions on Qwen3-8B with a 2,048-token budget, a planner-guided policy that keeps over 99% of the needed tokens resident still fails late steps increasingly often: its accuracy falls from 93% to 64% as the gap between a definition and its use grows from 2K to 90K tokens, at residency 0.996. The needed tokens are resident but unretrievable: what grows is their rotary-position distance to the query. We present PACE (Planner-Aware Context Engine), a KV-cache engine that couples plan-aware retention, in which the agent's planner tells the cache which context future steps will read, with bounded positions, which renumber kept tokens so their rotary distance to the query stays below 8K at any session length. In a pre-registered evaluation on fresh tasks, PACE passes 97% of hidden unit tests at 100K tokens, against 42% for H2O and 3% for SnapKV under the same budget, and needs both components: either alone reaches 63% (the combination beats each, p < 10^-4). The result holds with length: in 1M- and 2M-token sessions on one 24 GB GPU, with definitions written 0.9M and 1.8M tokens before use, PACE passes 99% and 100% of checks and recalls every session fact, while bounded positions without foresight falls from 33% to 13%; PACE holds under 0.7 GB of KV state where the full cache would need 147-295 GB. Renumbering is exact, costs no measurable perplexity, and adds 9% wall time. On Mistral-7B, whose coding ability caps it at 40% even with nothing evicted, PACE stays at that ceiling while retention alone falls below it.

</details>


### [154] [Can Time Series Foundation Models Serve as Surrogates for Power Systems Dynamic Simulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10247)
*Sandy Miguel,Bruno Paes Leao,Cheng Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究用预训练时间序列基础模型(TSFM)作为高保真动态仿真的高效代理模型，结合上下文学习、检索增强参考样本选择与物理信息正则化，在9节点电力系统数据上验证，微调后误差更低、成本更低，相比原物理仿真加速563倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真动态仿真对科学与工程应用至关重要，但其计算开销限制了大规模场景筛选，因此需要计算高效的替代（代理）模型。

Method: 考察将预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）用作代理模型；采用上下文学习与后训练策略，包括检索增强的参考样本选择以及物理信息正则化；以领域内代表性的传统AI模型作为基线进行基准对比；实验使用开源TSFM与仿真9节点电力系统数据。

Result: 检索增强提升了上下文学习性能；微调（尤其加入物理信息正则化）优于传统AI模型；微调后的TSFM误差更低、计算成本更低，且相对原始物理仿真实现563倍加速。

Conclusion: 预训练时间序列基础模型可作为高保真动态仿真的高效代理，检索增强与物理信息正则化有效提升性能，在精度与效率上均优于传统AI方法，具备支撑大规模场景筛选的潜力。

Abstract: High-fidelity dynamic simulation is essential for scientific and engineering applications, but its computational cost limits large-scale scenario screening. We investigate the use of pretrained time-series foundation models (TSFMs) as computationally efficient surrogates to address this limitation. In-context learning and post-training strategies, including retrieval-augmented reference sample selection and physics-informed regularization, are considered in the investigation. Results are benchmarked against a traditional AI model representative of the state-of-the-art in the domain. Experiments using an open-source TSFM and data from a simulated 9-bus power system indicate promising results. Retrieval augmentation improves in-context learning performance, and fine-tuning leads to superior results compared to the traditional AI model, especially when including physics-informed regularization. The fine-tuned TSFMs achieve lower errors and improved computational costs compared to the traditional AI model, and a 563$\times$ speedup compared to the original physics-based simulation.

</details>


### [155] [FedLoVA: Value-Only Aggregation for Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10248)
*Ensieh Khazaei,Baturalp Buyukates,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedLoVA 提出在联邦 LoRA 微调中仅在本地训练 query 与 value 投影的适配器，而只将 value 投影的 LoRA 适配器上传至服务器共享，从而在保持或提升性能的同时显著降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦 LoRA 微调对 transformer 层的 query 和 value 投影不加区分地同时应用 LoRA，忽略了二者的差异化作用。作者观察到 value 投影的收敛速度明显快于 query 投影，因此存在通过差异化共享策略压缩通信的空间。

Method: 首先实证分析 query 与 value 投影在联邦 LoRA 微调中的收敛行为差异；基于该发现设计 FedLoVA 框架：本地同时训练 query 和 value 投影上的 LoRA 适配器，但聚合/通信阶段仅将 value 投影的适配器与服务器共享，query 投影适配器保留在本地。

Result: 在自然语言理解、多语种情感分析和生成任务上的实验表明，与现有联邦 LLM 微调基线相比，FedLoVA 将通信成本最高降低 7.8×，同时取得相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: query 与 value 投影在联邦 LoRA 微调中贡献不对称，采用 "本地全训练、仅共享 value" 的非对称策略可有效兼顾通信效率与模型性能，为联邦 PEFT 的通信优化提供了简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a popular parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method for fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs), especially in federated learning, due to its strong performance and communication efficiency. In practice, LoRA is applied to the query and value projections of transformer layers without considering the distinct roles of these components. In this work, we analyze the impact of these projections in federated fine-tuning of LLMs using LoRA and observe that the value projection converges significantly faster than the query projection. Motivated by this finding, we propose FedLoVA, a federated fine-tuning framework that trains LoRA adapters on both query and value projections locally while sharing only the value projection with the server. This approach reduces communication overhead while improving convergence efficiency. Experiments on natural language understanding, multilingual sentiment analysis, and generation tasks show that FedLoVA reduces the communication cost by up to $7.8\times$ compared to existing federated LLM fine-tuning baselines while achieving comparable or better performance. The code is available at: https://github.com/EnsiehKhazaei/FedLoVA.

</details>


### [156] [TORNADO: Adaptive Latent Stochastic Transport for Calibrated Probabilistic PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10251)
*Jorge Mifsut Benet,Armand Kassaï Koupaï,Ramon Daniel Regueiro-Espino,Nicolas Baskiotis,Patrick Gallinari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TORNADO：一种将下一步预测形式化为VAE编码器后验间随机传输的潜空间概率预测框架，学习状态依赖扩散的SDE，使预测不确定性适应局部潜动力学，从而在少步推理下改善校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE概率神经代理模型可用于集合预报，但在不确定性量化时（尤其计算受限、少步推理场景）常出现校准不良。现有生成方法多采用固定的全局随机性，无法让预测不确定性随局部动力学状态自适应。

Method: 将下一步预测建模为VAE编码器后验之间的随机传输；学习具有状态依赖扩散系数的SDE（而非全局预设随机性）；利用高斯插值导出的解析条件端点目标进行免模拟训练；附加能量距离正则项改善分布对齐。

Result: 在不确定性校准上优于随机插值、扩散模型、流匹配等代表性生成基线，同时保持可比的预测精度。

Conclusion: 将随机性变为依赖潜状态而非全局预设，是提升实际神经PDE预测校准的有效机制。

Abstract: Probabilistic neural surrogates for PDEs enable ensemble forecasting, but often exhibit miscalibration when used for uncertainty quantification, especially in compute-constrained, few-step inference regimes. Existing generative approaches typically rely on fixed global stochasticity, which cannot adapt predictive uncertainty to the local dynamical state. We propose TORNADO, a latent probabilistic forecasting framework that formulates next-step prediction as stochastic transport between VAE encoder posteriors. Unlike existing approaches with globally prescribed stochasticity, TORNADO learns a SDE with state-dependent diffusion, enabling the predictive uncertainty to adapt to the local latent dynamics. The model is trained using analytic conditional endpoint targets derived from a Gaussian interpolant, enabling simulation-free learning. An additional energy distance regularization term improves distributional alignment. Empirically, TORNADO improves uncertainty calibration over representative generative baselines, including stochastic interpolants, diffusion models, and flow matching, while maintaining comparable predictive accuracy. Our results show that adapting stochasticity to the latent state, rather than prescribing it globally, is an effective mechanism for improving calibration in practical neural PDE forecasting settings. Project page: https://tornado-sde.github.io.

</details>


### [157] [Match the Geometry Before the Surrogate: Extreme Low-Budget Optimization in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10267)
*Michal Průšek,Adam Novozámský,Filip Šroubek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在仅30次评估的低预算分子优化中，SELFIES-VAE隐空间候选分布集中于一窄球壳，将候选生成约束到该球壳（on-sphere scaffold）并与采集最大化器形式产生显著交互，效果依最大化方式而异。


<details>
