  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实湿实验轮次通常只允许几十次评估，而标准分子优化基准常允许数百甚至上千次，此低预算情形缺乏研究。作者希望厘清：在隐空间中，收益究竟来自代理模型，还是来自候选支撑集（support）与采集最大化器的选择。

Method: 采用高斯先验的SELFIES分子VAE，在GuacaMol目标上以N=30次评估进行受控研究。观察到参考分子集编码均值集中于窄球壳，而许多高维优化器在箱式或箱式信任域内提出候选。提出简单约束"on-sphere scaffold"将候选限制在该球壳，并设计两个极简优化器：GeoWalk（无代理、球壳上测地局部搜索）与GeoCMA（随机子空间协方差学习）。另做因子实验：单一GP在候选支撑（球壳/箱）与采集最大化器（有限候选集/连续优化器L-BFGS-B）上交叉，并在LassoBench上验证。

Result: 30次评估后，GeoWalk与球壳上的GP代理未检出差异（GP在预算早期更领先）；GeoCMA在分子基准上未优于GeoWalk，在LassoBench上平均损失低于GeoWalk，但全维CMA-ES在多数任务上更强。因子实验显示：使用有限候选集最大化采集时球壳候选优于箱式候选，而用L-BFGS-B连续最大化时该优势消失。

Conclusion: 结果来自单一高斯先验VAE基准的受控研究，并不构成对VAE用于分子优化的推荐。核心实践启示是：在将增益归因于代理模型之前，应先控制候选支撑集与采集最大化器这两项因素。

Abstract: Low-budget molecular optimization is often framed as Bayesian optimization in a learned latent space, but standard benchmarks typically allow hundreds or thousands of evaluations, whereas a realistic wet-lab round may allow only a few dozen. We revisit this regime using a SELFIES molecular variational autoencoder (VAE) with a Gaussian prior and GuacaMol objectives at N = 30 evaluations. The main finding is not a new surrogate model, but an interaction in candidate generation. The encoder means of a reference set of molecules concentrate near a narrow spherical shell, while many high-dimensional optimizers propose candidates from boxes or box-shaped trust regions. We study a simple constraint, the on-sphere scaffold, that restricts candidate generation to this shell. We then introduce two minimal optimizers to probe what remains useful once the support is fixed. GeoWalk performs geodesic local search on the shell without a surrogate. After 30 evaluations, it shows no detected difference from a Gaussian-process (GP) surrogate on the shell, which is ahead earlier in the budget. GeoCMA adds covariance learning in a random subspace. It does not improve on GeoWalk in the molecular benchmark. On LassoBench it has a lower mean loss than GeoWalk, but full-dimensional CMA-ES remains stronger on most tasks. A factorial experiment with a single GP crosses the candidate support (shell or box) with the acquisition maximizer (a finite candidate set or a continuous optimizer). In this benchmark, shell candidates outperformed box candidates when acquisition was maximized over a candidate set, but not with continuous maximization by L-BFGS-B. These results come from a controlled study of one Gaussian-prior VAE benchmark and do not recommend VAEs for molecular optimization. The practical lesson is to control the candidate support and the acquisition maximizer before attributing a gain to the surrogate.

</details>


### [158] [Transit-Oriented Land Use Planning Using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10281)
*Bo Yang,Yuebing Liang,Zhan Zhao,Fangyi Ding,Yunhan Zheng,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRL4TOD，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，可自动优化轨道站点周边地块的土地用途与容积率（FAR），在满足TOD原则的同时兼顾改造成本，为规划者提供可调权重的决策支持方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TOD规划多依赖启发式方法，难以高效识别定量优化的公交导向土地利用模式；同时需兼顾密度、多样性与交通运能利用等多目标，并考虑开发可行性与改造成本。

Method: 构建DRL4TOD框架：用图神经网络（GNN）刻画地块、路网与地铁站点之间的空间依赖关系；再以DRL优化策略为选定地块分配土地用途类型与FAR；多目标联合优化土地利用密度、多样性与公交运力利用，并最小化改造成本；通过调整目标权重实现优先级切换。在上海两个真实站点区域进行验证，与规则方法和遗传算法对比。

Result: 在上海两个站点区域的实验中，DRL4TOD一致优于规则方法与遗传算法，获得更优的TOD效果且改造成本更低；通过调整目标权重可灵活改变优先级（如财务可行性受限时更强调成本最小化）。

Conclusion: DRL4TOD可作为规划者指定优先级下生成协调的土地用途与FAR配置的决策支持工具，而非给出单一固定设计方案；其为政策制定者提供地块级、约束感知的替代方案，使多目标权衡显式化，支持关于开发强度与可行性的针对性决策。

Abstract: Transit-Oriented Development (TOD) is a key strategy for advancing sustainable urban development by promoting high-density, mixed-use land development within walking distance of public transit stations. However, traditional TOD planning often relies on heuristic methods, making it difficult to efficiently identify quantitatively optimized transit-oriented land use patterns. This paper presents DRL4TOD, a deep reinforcement learning framework that automatically optimizes land use types and floor area ratios (FARs) around transit stations in alignment with TOD principles. DRL4TOD incorporates a graph neural network to capture spatial interdependencies among land parcels, road networks, and metro stations, and uses a DRL-based optimization strategy to assign land-use types and FARs to selected parcels. It jointly optimizes multiple objectives, including land use density, land use diversity, and transit capacity utilization, while minimizing reconstruction costs to account for development feasibility. Applied to two real-world station areas in Shanghai, DRL4TOD consistently outperforms rule-based approaches and genetic algorithms, achieving more effective TOD outcomes with lower reconstruction costs. By adjusting objective weights, the framework enables flexible prioritization, such as placing greater emphasis on cost minimization when financial feasibility is a concern. These results demonstrate DRL4TOD's potential as a decision-support tool for generating coordinated land-use and FAR configurations under planner-specified priorities, rather than prescribing a single fixed design. For policymakers, the framework provides parcel-level, constraint-aware alternatives that make trade-offs among competing objectives explicit and support targeted decisions about development intensity and feasibility.

</details>


### [159] [A Clinical Evaluation of Conformal Prediction for Chest Radiograph Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10288)
*Nikolaos Kontogeorgis,Pascal Krezer,Klaus-Rudolf Kladny,Arne Estler,Christian F. Baumgartner,Lisa Koch,Saif Afat,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLARITY——一套临床可接受性标准及基于放射科医师标注的评测框架，用于检验放射报告生成中的风险控制方法；实验表明常用的自动化"可接受性代理指标"与真实临床可接受性基本脱钩，难以检出具有临床后果的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型虽提升了放射报告自动生成的效率，但幻觉问题突出，需要具备临床意义的风险控制手段。保形预测（CP）虽能提供统计保证，却依赖未经校准的自动抽取器，且未考虑错误在临床上的重要性差异——即无法区分"有临床后果的错误"与"无关紧要的偏差"。

Method: 提出CLARITY（clinical admissibility criteria）框架，显式界定放射报告的临床可接受性，将具有临床意义的错误与轻微偏差区分开来；为避免依赖自动评审器，收集放射科医师依据CLARITY对MLLM生成报告所作的标注；以专家标注为金标准，对多种CP策略及自动评测指标进行基准测试。

Result: 基准测试显示：常用的可接受性代理指标（自动抽取器与自动评测指标）系统性地无法检出临床相关错误；自动化可接受性判断与真实临床可接受性在很大程度上相互解耦。

Conclusion: 当前基于自动判断的保形预测与评测流程缺乏临床有效性保障；需要以临床可接受性为准则、由专家标注校准的风险控制与评测体系，才能为放射报告生成提供真正有临床意义的可靠性保证。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have improved automated radiology report generation, they remain prone to hallucinations, necessitating clinically meaningful risk-control methods. Conformal prediction (CP) provides statistical guarantees, yet existing CP approaches rely on uncalibrated automated extractors and fail to account for the clinical relevance of errors. We introduce clinical admissibility criteria (CLARITY), a framework for evaluating radiology reports that explicitly distinguishes clinically consequential errors from minor deviations. To eliminate reliance on automatic judges, we collected radiologist annotations of MLLM-generated reports using CLARITY. By benchmarking CP strategies and automatic evaluation metrics against these expert annotations, we demonstrate that commonly used admissibility proxies systematically fail to detect clinically relevant errors. Our findings reveal that automated admissibility judgments are largely decoupled from true clinical admissibility.

</details>


### [160] [Plug-and-Play Verifier for Decoupling Process Reward in Stabilizing Long-Horizon Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10305)
*Mingwei Xu,Jieyuan Pei,Binghao Tu,Hao Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可插拔验证器（plug-and-play verifier）框架，通过对推理轨迹中的中间产物进行显式过程验证并生成有界稠密过程奖励，与稀疏结果奖励经两阶段后训练结合，从而稳定长程推理；HotpotQA 上 Qwen3.5-4B 准确率 60.18%，优于 GRPO 的 57.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理需要在连续推理步与工具交互中保持有效进展。现有两类RL范式均有缺陷：actor-critic RL依赖可学习critic网络估计步级优势，代价高且估计不稳；GRPO等critic-free方法仅用稀疏结果奖励的组内变体，缺乏步级信号。二者都将"部分有用的失败轨迹"处理为隐式信息或与完全无产出的轨迹不加区分，随推理长度增长难以维持一致的推理轨迹。

Method: 1）可插拔验证器框架：通过统一接口从智能体运行轨迹中抽取中间推理产物（artifacts），交由领域特定验证器评估，再将验证分数聚合为有界（bounded）过程奖励，实现过程验证的解耦与模块化替换。2）两阶段后训练调度：先利用稠密验证过程奖励，再与稀疏结果奖励结合，形成稠密-稀疏混合奖励训练流程。3）验证器可即插即用，作为"最后一公里"的自我反思与自我一致性机制进一步稳定各推理步。

Result: 在 HotpotQA 上以 Qwen3.5-4B 评测，准确率达 60.18%，超过标准 GRPO 基线的 57.73%（约 +2.45 个百分点）；并可提升推理步骤的稳定性，表现为末段自我反思与自我一致性增强。

Conclusion: 可插拔验证器为长程推理提供了一种模块化方案，能学到更稳定、更可靠的推理行为；其核心价值在于以低耦合方式引入稠密过程监督，弥补纯结果奖励对失败轨迹中部分有效信息的忽视。局限在于仅在单一数据集与单一模型规模上验证，验证器质量与领域覆盖度对最终效果的影响有待进一步考察。

Abstract: Stabilizing long-horizon reasoning requires intelligent agents to sustain useful progress across successive reasoning steps and tool interactions. Such agents are typically trained with reinforcement learning (RL), where actor-critic RL uses a learnable critic network to estimate step advantages, while critic-free methods such as GRPO eliminate critic models and rely mostly on the group variants of sparse outcome-only rewards. Both reward types leave partially useful failed trajectories either implicit or indistinguishable from entirely unproductive ones, and as reasoning horizons grow, it becomes difficult to maintain consistent reasoning trajectories. In this work, we propose a \textit{plug-and-play verifier} framework for stabilizing long-horizon reasoning through explicit verification of intermediate reasoning artifacts. Specifically, the framework decouples process verification through a unified interface that extracts intermediate reasoning artifacts from agent running trajectories, evaluates them using domain-specific verifiers, and aggregates the verification scores as bounded process reward. Then, we combine our dense verified process rewards and the sparse outcome rewards through a two-stage post-training schedule. We showed that with plug-and-play verifiers, we further stabilize the reasoning steps as last-mile self-reflection and self-consistency. Typical evaluation on HotpotQA using Qwen3.5-4B achieves a superior accuracy (60.18\%), outperforming the standard GRPO baseline (57.73\%). Our results suggest that plug-and-play verifiers provide a modular approach to learn more stable and reliable reasoning behaviors over long horizons.

</details>


### [161] [How Much Is Left Matters: False Positives and False Negatives in Partial-Prompt Contamination Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10307)
*Abhishek Anand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统审计了"部分提示补全"这一基准污染检测方法，发现其标志结果强烈依赖于剩余文本长度，在真实负样本上产生大量假阳性，且对上游蒸馏污染不敏感；据此提出长度分层报告、≤60%前缀、风格匹配的后截止期对照与跨家族校准等改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分提示补全（partial-prompt completion）被广泛用于检测基准污染：给定题目前半部分，检查模型能否复现后半部分。但该测试的有效性本身缺乏审计——其判定可能受剩余文本长度、阈值设定等因素干扰，且可能无法捕捉上游污染（如从污染基座蒸馏的模型）。作者希望量化该测试的假阳性与漏检情况，并给出更可靠的使用规范。

Method: 在7个开源权重模型上，使用MATH-500以及作为真实负样本的AIME 2025/2026（后者晚于所有模型训练截止期）进行审计。按"剩余词数"分层比较标志率；在不同前缀比例（40%、60%、80%）下评估ROUGE-L≥0.75阈值与精确匹配的假阳性率；考察从Qwen2.5-Math蒸馏模型的"模型特异性召回"以检验上游污染检测能力；并用跨家族模型校准来分离模型特定信号。

Result: 记忆标志强烈依赖剩余文本量：每个模型中，剩余≤8词的题目被标记的频率是剩余≥25词题目的数倍（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct为31.5% vs. 5.5%），且一半到四分之三的ROUGE类标志来自短剩余文本。在真实负样本上，60%前缀下ROUGE-L≥0.75阈值标记高达6.7%的题目；80%前缀下即使精确匹配也标记8.6%（合并模型；在AIME 2026上为6.2%），而40–60%前缀下为0%。该测试还漏检上游污染：从Qwen2.5-Math蒸馏的模型几乎无模型特异性召回。以其他家族模型作校准可消除大部分标志，并在所有前缀长度上分离出一致的Qwen2.5特异性信号。

Conclusion: 部分提示补全测试存在严重的长度依赖假阳性和上游污染漏检问题，其结果不宜按原始形式直接解读。建议：(1) 按剩余文本长度分层报告；(2) 前缀比例不超过60%；(3) 使用风格匹配的、训练截止期之后的对照题集；(4) 采用跨家族模型校准以提取模型特异性信号。

Abstract: Partial-prompt completion, where a model receives the first part of a benchmark problem and is checked for whether it reproduces the rest, is a widely used test for benchmark contamination. We audit this test on seven open-weight models using MATH-500 and recent AIME 2025/2026 problems, which serve as true negatives. Memorization flags depend strongly on how much text remains: for every model, problems with at most 8 leftover words are flagged several times more often than problems with at least 25 (31.5% vs. 5.5% for Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), and half to three-quarters of all ROUGE-based flags come from short leftovers. On true negatives, a ROUGE-L >= 0.75 threshold flags up to 6.7% of problems at a 60% prefix, and at an 80% prefix even exact match flags 8.6% (pooled over models; 6.2% on AIME 2026, which postdates every model), against 0% at 40-60%. The test also misses contamination upstream: models distilled from Qwen2.5-Math, whose base is heavily flagged in prior work, show almost no model-specific recall. Calibrating each model against models from other families removes most flags while isolating a consistent Qwen2.5-specific signal at every prefix length. We recommend length-stratified reporting, prefixes of at most 60%, style-matched post-cutoff controls, and cross-family calibration.

</details>


### [162] [Multi-objective drift-aware MLOps agent for model retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10313)
*Kumuda Valli,Anli Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DARE（Drift-Aware Retraining Engine）——一个离线、事件驱动、可确定性回放的再训练框架，将重训练建模为序贯决策问题，并用LinUCB策略在延迟标签下兼顾性能与运维开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署ML模型面临数据非平稳与标签延迟，而现有再训练策略多依赖固定调度或脆弱的漂移阈值，且忽略运维成本，未将其视为序贯决策问题。

Method: DARE通过显式schema与确定性回放，将数据摄取、监控、策略决策、训练与日志解耦为事件驱动流程；采用LinUCB上下文赌博机策略做再训练决策，并与固定间隔、阈值持续（threshold-persistence）、以及动作率匹配的固定调度等启发式基线对比。

Result: 在Airline数据集上，锁定后的LinUCB策略获得更高的平均F1，同时显著降低干预频率；基于运行时校准（累计更新墙钟时间）的评估显示该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 保守的序贯再训练策略在延迟反馈下可在提升性能的同时减少不必要的运维开销；DARE提供了可复现、模块化的工程化再训练评估范式。

Abstract: Deployed ML models face non-stationary data and delayed labels, yet most retraining strategies rely on fixed schedules or brittle drift thresholds that ignore operational cost. In this setting, retraining is a sequential decision problem rather than a one-time threshold choice. We present DARE (Drift-Aware Retraining Engine), an offline, event-driven retraining framework that separates ingestion, monitoring, policy decisions, training, and logging through explicit schemas and deterministic replay. We compare DARE against practical heuristic retraining baselines, including fixed-interval, threshold-persistence, and action-rate-matched fixed scheduling policies. On Airline, the locked LinUCB policy achieves a higher mean F1 while substantially reducing intervention frequency relative to heuristic and action-rate-matched baselines. Additional runtime-calibrated evaluation shows that these gains persist when measured using the cumulative update wall-clock time. These results suggest that conservative sequential retraining policies can improve performance while reducing unnecessary operational overhead under delayed feedback.

</details>


### [163] [Update Slope Is Bounded by Prior Calibration: A Structural Bound and Its Contingent Empirical Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10325)
*Ben Jenkins,Mihaela Cardei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别出LLM预测器"更新斜率"指标的两个混杂因素：结局感知的证据构建会大幅夸大响应度（约4倍），且更新斜率作为变化分数存在依赖先验的结构性上限；建议采用盲于结局的证据阶梯、条件于先验校准，并报告余量归一化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常用"证据越丰富、预测越准"的更新斜率来衡量LLM预测器的更新能力，但该指标可能受到证据构建方式与先验校准差异的污染，导致结论失真。

Method: 在相同模型与问题上对比"结局感知"与"分辨率盲"两种证据阶梯所测得的斜率；从理论上分析更新斜率作为变化分数的结构上限（可行域）；在Manifold、ForecastBench、ACLED三个数据池上检验斜率与先验校准的经验耦合是否依赖于余量利用率（headroom utilization），并考察中间更新与最终Brier分数的关联。

Result: 结局感知证据使斜率约为盲法证据下的4倍；斜率存在依赖先验的结构上限，仅定义可行域，斜率与先验校准是否耦合取决于余量利用率，且在三个池间存在差异；条件于先验校准后，中间更新与最终Brier分数的关联约提升三倍（盲法与结局感知构建下均成立）；问题身份解释的方差显著多于模型身份。

Conclusion: 评估LLM预测器时应构建盲于结局的证据阶梯、条件于先验校准，并在报告原始斜率的同时给出余量归一化指标，以避免将先验校准差异误读为更新行为差异。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM forecasters increasingly measure how forecasts improve as richer evidence is supplied. We identify two confounds in this update-slope metric. First, outcome-aware evidence construction substantially inflates measured responsiveness: on the same models and questions, slopes are roughly four times larger than with resolution-blinded evidence ladders. Second, update slope is a change score with a prior-dependent structural ceiling, so raw comparisons can reflect differences in starting calibration rather than updating behavior. The ceiling defines only a feasible region; whether slope and prior calibration are empirically coupled depends on headroom utilization and varies across our Manifold, ForecastBench, and ACLED pools. Despite this variability, conditioning on prior calibration roughly triples the association between intermediate updating and final Brier score under both blinded and outcome-aware construction. Question identity also explains substantially more variance than model identity across the evaluated pools. We recommend building evidence ladders blind to outcomes, conditioning on prior calibration, and reporting headroom-normalized measures alongside raw slopes.

</details>


### [164] [Reproducible LoRA Inference Through Deterministic Split-K Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10334)
*Dhayaneshwar Balusamy,Ankur Mohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 vLLM 中 Split-K 内核使用浮点原子加法合并部分结果所导致的数值非确定性，并提出确定性 Triton Split-K 实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 适配模型在重复评测、审计与决策中需要可复现性，但用于并行化低秩 LoRA 乘法的 Split-K 内核因浮点原子加法合并部分和而引入非确定性，威胁结果可重复。

Method: 在 GSM8K 数学推理、MBPP 代码生成，以及 Civil Comments 上 Jev 风格结构化毒性决策三类任务上，对 vLLM 中原子 Split-K 的非确定性进行测量；并进行受控追踪以分析误差传播路径；同时提出保留并行性的确定性 Triton Split-K=8 实现。

Result: 同输入重放下，Atomic Split-K=8 在 97.61% 的比较中产生不同的 FP32 shrink 输出，而确定性版本未观察到变化；重复原子推理在 17.30%（GSM8K）与 14.96%（MBPP）比较中改变生成结果，有时影响任务正确性；结构化决策概率在 81.47% 比较中变化并出现决策翻转。追踪显示 shrink 输出差异经 KV 缓存累积传播，最终改变 token 决策与答案。确定性实现对非拆分归约取得 16.8–21.1% 延迟加速，相比 Atomic Split-K=8 仅增加 1.02–2.62% 延迟。

Conclusion: 浮点原子加法的 Split-K 归约会实质性地破坏 LoRA 模型推理的可复现性并影响下游决策；所提确定性 Triton Split-K 在保持并行加速的同时消除了可观测的非确定性，是低成本的可复现性修复方案。

Abstract: Reproducibility is important for LoRA-adapted language models used in repeated evaluation, auditing, and decision-making. However, Split-K kernels used to introduce parallelism in low-rank LoRA multiplications can have the unintended consequence of numerical nondeterminism when partial results are combined using floating-point atomic additions. We investigate this non-determinism in vLLM across GSM8K mathematical reasoning, MBPP code generation, and Jev-style structured toxicity decisions on Civil Comments and introduce a deterministic Triton Split-K implementation. Under identical-input replay, Atomic Split-K=8 produces different FP32 shrink outputs in 97.61% of comparisons, while our Deterministic Split-K=8 shows no observed variation. Repeated atomic inference changes generated outputs in 17.30% of GSM8K and 14.96% of MBPP comparisons, sometimes altering task correctness. Structured-decision probabilities vary in 81.47% of atomic comparisons, with decision flips. Controlled tracing demonstrates how shrink-output differences propagate into the KV cache and how accumulated cache differences can change token decisions and final answers. Our deterministic Triton Split-K=8 implementation preserves parallelism, achieving 16.8-21.1% latency-based speedup over unsplit reduction with only 1.02-2.62% additional latency relative to Atomic Split-K=8.

</details>


### [165] [Two-Sided Conformal Evidence Sets for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10335)
*Ben Jenkins,Mihaela Cardei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双侧共形证据集框架CES，为"支持"与"反驳"分别返回具有有限样本覆盖保证的证据集合，并指出证据覆盖校准与判定可靠性是两种不同性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG校准式接地方法只考虑支持性证据，是单向的，无法同时给出"支持"和"反驳"两侧的可靠证据集合。

Method: 构建Conformal Evidence Sets（CES）双侧共形框架：在可交换性假设下，支持集与反驳集各自达到至少1−α的有限样本包含覆盖，并用Bonferroni联合得到至少1−2α的联合覆盖；在VitaminC对比基准的带标注候选池上评估，并进行FEVER分布偏移、评分器替换、难负例检索、跨数据集迁移及端到端试点等压力测试。

Result: 在α∈{0.05,0.10,0.15,0.20,0.30}下覆盖紧贴名义值，支持侧最大偏差3.0个百分点、反驳侧5.4个百分点，且无需测试集调参即达到与测试调优固定阈值启发式几乎相同的覆盖—集合大小前沿；FEVER分布偏移下双侧均欠覆盖，最多达12.7个百分点，支持侧分组分层重校准可显著缩小差距。

Conclusion: 当可交换性成立且检索到相关证据时，即使评分器不完美，证据包含仍保持校准，而支持/反驳判定质量直接随评分器质量变化；因此校准的证据覆盖与可靠判定应分开报告。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can surface passages that support a claim, contradict it, or fail to address it, yet existing calibrated grounding methods are typically one-sided and model only supporting evidence. We introduce Conformal Evidence Sets (CES), a two-sided framework that returns separate support and refutation sets with finite-sample inclusion coverage of at least $1-α$ on each side under exchangeability; a Bonferroni union yields joint coverage of at least $1-2α$. On labeled candidate pools from the contrastive VitaminC benchmark, CES closely tracks nominal coverage across $α\!\in\!\{0.05,0.10,0.15,0.20,0.30\}$, with maximum deviations of $3.0$ percentage points for support and $5.4$ for refutation, and reaches essentially the same empirical coverage--size frontier as a test-tuned fixed-threshold heuristic without any test exposure. Stress tests mark the boundary of that guarantee: under FEVER distribution shift both sides under-cover, by up to $12.7$ percentage points, and support-side group-stratified recalibration substantially reduces the support gap. Scorer swaps, hard-negative retrieval, cross-dataset transfer, and a live end-to-end pilot expose a clear separation: when exchangeability holds and relevant evidence is retrieved, evidence inclusion remains calibrated even with an imperfect scorer, whereas the support/refute verdict tracks scorer quality directly. Calibrated evidence coverage and reliable verdicts are therefore distinct properties and should be reported separately.

</details>


### [166] [On the universal structure in deterministic generative diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10338)
*Defang Chen,Zhenyu Zhou,Can Wang,Zeyu Zheng,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示扩散模型的确定性采样轨迹具有与数据模态和模型实现无关的普适几何结构，并可用基于最优线性去噪器的可精确求解模型加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成建模已成为跨领域的高维结构化数据模拟通用框架，但对其生成机制（尤其是采样轨迹的组织方式）缺乏原理性理解，制约了训练与推理方法的系统改进。

Method: 从确定性采样轨迹的几何性质入手进行经验刻画，识别出跨数据模态与不同扩散实现共享的不变结构；进而构建以最优线性去噪器（optimal linear denoiser）为核心的可精确求解（exactly solvable）理论模型，对观测到的几何结构进行解析解释。

Result: 实验/分析表明确定性采样轨迹呈现统一的几何结构，且该结构在很大程度上可由线性去噪器解析模型复现与解释。

Conclusion: 扩散模型确定性采样的行为具有可解析的普适规律，线性去噪器模型可作为理解其生成机制的理论基线，为原理化的训练与推理设计提供依据。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative modeling has emerged as a general computational framework for simulating high-dimensional, structured data across various domains. Understanding its generative mechanisms is paramount for advancing model training and inference in a principled way. Here, we demonstrate that deterministic sampling trajectories share a universal geometric structure invariant to data modalities and diffusion model implementations. This structure can be largely explained by an exactly solvable model based on the optimal linear denoiser.

</details>


### [167] [TripletAD: Weakly Supervised Anomaly Detection in Embedding Space with Rare Anomalies and Scarce Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10352)
*Sumit Wadhwa,Souvik Nath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TripletAD：面向极少已确认异常的弱监督异常检测，采用三元组边际嵌入+双软惩罚线性评分头，在稀有异常/稀疏标签场景优于DevNet、PReNet、DeepSAD与GBDT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际问题中仅有少量确认异常，且未复核数据不能假定为正常；因此采用"精选标签"协议（未确认异常从训练中移除而非重标为正常），需要一种能让少量标签贯穿全训练集、不被大量正常样本稀释的弱监督检测器。

Method: 提出TripletAD：学习三元组边际嵌入，并用两个软惩罚校准线性评分头——对已标注异常的部分交叉熵（partial cross-entropy），以及分离异常/正常样本平均得分的相对对比合页损失（relative-contrast hinge）。每个挖掘的三元组都含一个已标注异常，从而实现标签复用。

Result: 在24个ADBench数据集（预训练文本/图像/语音嵌入）上，异常稀有条件下优于DevNet、PReNet、DeepSAD及梯度提升树：1%异常率下平均AUCPR居六法之首，较DevNet高20%；自然稀有的Speech数据集AUCPR翻倍（0.394 vs 0.196）；MVTec-AD上仅1/4或1/2异常被标注时最佳。70个稀有/稀疏标签设置汇总平均排名2.21/6，Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm校正）均显著优于各基线；异常较频繁时与基线持平。无需逐数据集调参，对损失权重与训练长度不敏感；原始表格特征上GBDT仍更优。

Conclusion: 三元组+部分交叉熵+相对对比合页的弱监督设计能有效利用极少确认异常并避免标签稀释，在稀有异常与稀疏标签下稳健领先；适用范围以预训练嵌入为主，原始表格特征仍建议GBDT。

Abstract: Many practical anomaly detection problems come with only a handful of confirmed anomalies, and the unreviewed remainder of the data cannot be trusted to be normal. We study this setting under a curated-label protocol, in which unconfirmed anomalies are removed from training rather than relabelled as normal, and propose TripletAD, a weakly supervised detector that learns a triplet-margin embedding and calibrates a linear scoring head with two soft penalties: a partial cross-entropy on labelled anomalies and a relative-contrast hinge that separates the mean scores of anomalous and normal samples. Because every mined triplet contains a labelled anomaly, the few labels are reused across the entire training set instead of being diluted by it. On 24 ADBench datasets whose inputs are pre-trained embeddings (text, image and speech vectors), TripletAD outperforms established state-of-the-art weakly supervised detectors (DevNet, PReNet and DeepSAD) and gradient-boosted trees when anomalies are rare. At a 1% anomaly rate it has the highest mean AUCPR of six methods, 20% above DevNet; on the naturally rare Speech dataset it doubles the best baseline (0.394 vs. 0.196 AUCPR); and on MVTec-AD it is best when only a quarter or half of the anomalies are labelled. Pooled over 70 rare- or sparse-label settings, it has the best average rank (2.21 of 6) and outperforms each baseline under Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. When anomalies are more frequent it performs on par with these methods. The method needs no per-dataset tuning and is insensitive to its loss weights and training length; on raw tabular features, gradient-boosted trees remain the stronger choice.

</details>


### [168] [Bias, Measurement Error, and Double-Dipping: When Can GNN Convolutions Help Brain Connectome Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10364)
*Tommaso Castellani,Jiaqi Li,Muriah D. Wheelock,Rezwana Rosen Razzaque,Helmut Laufs,Hong Chen,Claire Donnat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将脑连接组预测形式化为变量含误差（EIV）问题，在线性渐近设定下把图卷积效应分解为平滑偏差与方差缩减、图估计误差、以及双重使用（double-dipping）效应三种机制，并在潜社区模型下刻画出一个狭窄的有利区间，解释了连接组GNN常不优于图无关基线的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN被广泛用于fMRI预测，经验功能连接矩阵既作特征又用于构图；但近期研究与作者自身实验均显示这类方法常打不过更简单的图无关（graph-agnostic）基线。作者希望从统计角度解释这一失败，而非仅停留在经验观察。

Method: 将连接组预测建模为errors-in-variables问题：真实连接组是潜变量，观测协方差矩阵是有限时长时间序列的噪声估计。在线性渐近框架下，把图卷积的作用分解为三部分：(1) 平滑带来的偏差与方差缩减；(2) 图估计误差；(3) 当同一噪声矩阵同时决定图与特征时产生的double-dipping效应。并采用适配神经科学的潜社区模型（latent community model）进行理论分析；辅以仿真验证分解，并以真实数据实验检验。

Result: 仿真验证了上述三机制分解的正确性；理论分析识别出一个由脑社区结构、预测信号强度、图构造方式与记录时长共同决定的狭窄有利区间，消息传递在其中才可能提升预测；真实数据实验表明现有连接组GNN流程往往落在该区间之外。

Conclusion: 本文为"图卷积何时有助于脑连接组预测、为何常常无益"提供了首个统计解释框架，指出GNN的收益依赖于一个受限的参数条件，可作为今后设计连接组GNN与评估其实用性的理论依据。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are an important tool for fMRI prediction, where empirical functional connectivity matrices serve both as features and for graph construction. However, recent studies and our own experiments show that these methods often fail to outperform simpler graph-agnostic baselines. We propose a statistical explanation for this failure by formulating connectome prediction as an errors-in-variables (EIV) problem: the true connectome is latent, while the observed covariance matrix is a noisy finite-time-series estimate. In a linear asymptotic setting, we decompose the effect of graph convolution into three mechanisms: smoothing-induced bias and variance reduction, graph estimation error, and a double-dipping effect arising when the same noisy matrix determines both graph and features. Under a latent community model adapted to neuroscience, we identify a narrow favorable regime, set by the brain community structure, the predictive signal, the graph construction and the recording length, in which message passing can improve prediction. Simulations validate this decomposition, while real-data experiments illustrate that connectome GNN pipelines often fall outside this regime. Our results provide a first step towards explaining when graph convolutions can help brain connectome prediction, and why they often do not.

</details>


### [169] [CHARM: Certified Homology-Aware Reverse-scale Mapping via Diffusion-Guided Quotient Towers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10375)
*Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CHARM 方法，将二维可视坐标与可认证的同调记录及样本级定位结合，在保证拓扑正确性的同时实现高效降维，并在 22 个数据集上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用降维方法只强调邻域或距离保持，仅凭平面坐标无法证明高阶结构的同调类型及样本级支撑；而直接计算同调证据代价高昂，因为单纯复形规模随维度急剧增长。

Method: CHARM 由四部分组成：（1）扩散坐标引导覆盖提案；（2）安全收缩与 Cover Quotient Tower（覆盖商塔）缩小结构计算的定义域，其中安全收缩保持输入 flag 复形的同伦型，商塔与粗图跳距 Rips 滤流呈乘性 3-交错；（3）精确代数计算认证并追踪同调代表元；（4）反向尺度布局将代表元支撑放入共享坐标。理论部分给出可验证的欧氏与粗图 Rips 滤流间加性距离畸变界，及正尺度下复合乘性保证。

Result: 在固定配置下覆盖 22 个数据集、41,637 个样本，CHARM 在非合成数据的全部 15 项样本加权指标中均位列前二，并在四项欧氏持久图距离上取得最小值；全基准共提供 957 条可追溯支撑记录。球形壳实验中，一条经认证的 H2 记录在二维视图中定位出 1,578 个原始样本。

Conclusion: CHARM 将几何探索与同调类型及位置的显式证据相结合，使降维结果具备可验证的拓扑可信度与可追溯性。

Abstract: Widely used dimensionality reduction methods prioritize neighborhood or distance 1 preservation, yet planar coordinates alone cannot certify the homological type or 2 sample-level support of higher-order structures. Obtaining this evidence directly 3 is costly because simplicial complexes grow rapidly with dimension. We propose 4 Certified Homology-Aware Reverse-scale Mapping (CHARM), which combines 5 two-dimensional coordinates with certified homology records and sample-level 6 localization. Diffusion coordinates guide cover proposals; safe contractions and a 7 Cover Quotient Tower reduce the domain of structural computation; exact algebra 8 certifies and tracks homology representatives; and reverse-scale layout places their 9 supports in shared coordinates. Safe contraction preserves the homotopy type of 10 the input flag complex. The quotient tower is multiplicatively 3-interleaved with 11 the coarse-graph hop-distance Rips filtration. Verifiable distance-distortion bounds 12 give an additive guarantee between the original Euclidean and coarse-graph Rips 13 filtrations, and composite multiplicative guarantees for the tower on positive scales. 14 Across 22 datasets and 41,637 samples under a fixed configuration, CHARM 15 ranks among the top two on all 15 sample-weighted metrics for nonsynthetic data, 16 including the smallest values on all four Euclidean persistence-diagram distances. 17 It also provides 957 traceable support records across the full benchmark. On a 18 spherical shell, one certified H2 record identifies 1,578 original samples in the 19 two-dimensional view. CHARM thus combines geometric exploration with explicit 20 evidence of homological type and location.

</details>


### [170] [Quantitative Assessment of Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10380)
*Sergio Rincón,Gabriel González,Nicolás Andrade,Rafael Velásquez,Sebastián Ojeda,Juanita Puentes,Nicolás Aparicio,Paula Cárdenas,Pablo Arbeláez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPAL-Bench基准，将CSP与SICSP分开评估，采用无阈值几何与物理指标并进行多晶型感知评测；结果表明无方法全面占优，几何性能与物理合理性弱相关，并发布SE(3)等变流匹配基线OPAL-CSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSP评测框架存在三大问题：依赖阈值敏感且不可比的指标；偏重几何精度而忽视物理合理性；未考虑多晶型（同一组分可结晶为多种稳定结构）。同时SICSP方法在推理时接收晶体学模板，约束了搜索空间，本质上是不同任务，却常与标准CSP基线混在一起比较。

Method: 构建OPAL-Bench：将CSP与SICSP作为两个独立任务评估；在4个数据集上评测7种CSP方法与2种SICSP方法；采用无阈值（threshold-free）的几何距离指标与物理距离指标，并在多晶型感知协议下统一评估。另发布OPAL-CSP——面向CSP赛道的SE(3)等变流匹配基线。

Result: 没有任何方法在所有指标上占优；几何性能与物理合理性之间相关性不强。OPAL-CSP在几何指标上具竞争力，在物理指标上位居前列。

Conclusion: 通过在多晶型感知协议下统一几何与物理指标，OPAL-Bench 能够对CSP生成质量进行更全面的评估。

Abstract: Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) aims to predict a material's 3D structure from its chemical composition, with applications from energy storage to biomedical devices. Despite its importance, existing evaluation frameworks suffer from key limitations: they rely on threshold-sensitive or incomparable metrics, focus predominantly on geometric performance while overlooking physical plausibility, and fail to account for polymorphism, where a chemical composition can crystallize into multiple stable structures. In parallel, recent Symmetry-Informed CSP (SICSP) methods often report results alongside standard CSP baselines while receiving crystallographic templates at inference, which constrain the search space and make SICSP a fundamentally different task. To address these limitations, we introduce an Orientation-invariant, Polymorph-Aware, Large Benchmark (OPAL-Bench) that evaluates CSP and SICSP as separate tasks. We evaluate 7 CSP and 2 SICSP methods under OPAL-Bench across 4 datasets using threshold-free geometric and physical distance metrics. Our results show that no method dominates across all metrics, and that geometric performance is not strongly correlated with physical plausibility. We also release OPAL-CSP, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching baseline for the CSP track that performs competitively on geometric metrics and ranks among the best on physical ones. By unifying geometric and physical metrics under a polymorph-aware protocol, OPAL-Bench enables a more comprehensive assessment of generative quality in CSP.

</details>


### [171] [Pruning DNA Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10386)
*Faias Satter,Shaker Islam,Aminul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequence Pruning (SP)，一种针对DNA语言模型预训练的序列级动态剪枝框架，利用前向传播得到的每序列预训练准确率移除模型已易预测的基因组序列，最多减少96%数据处理量且基本保持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据剪枝已被证明能通过移除学习信号贡献小的样本降低训练成本，但在DNA语言模型预训练中尚未被研究；基因组语料高度冗余，因此存在显著的效率提升空间。

Method: SP是一种动态剪枝框架：在预训练过程中，直接由模型前向传播计算每条序列的预训练准确率，无需单独的评分模型或对语料的额外遍历，随序列变"易预测"而将其移除。同时在两个语料、三类模型家族、MLM与CLM两种目标共五种预训练设置上评估，下游使用五个基因组基准；并支持困惑度、EL2N等替代判据。

Result: 数据处理量最多减少96%且基本保持下游性能；中等节省比例下性能较全数据预训练提升至多1.2 MCC点；最高节省时仅用基线4%的GPU小时且性能损失很小；在多数比较中优于InfoBatch与BPAS；准确率判据在同节省比例下下游效果最佳。

Conclusion: 基因组预训练无需反复处理全部可用序列，序列级动态剪枝可显著提升其数据与计算效率；代码已开源。

Abstract: Data pruning can substantially reduce training cost by removing examples that contribute little additional learning signal, but it has not been studied for DNA language-model pretraining. We introduce Sequence Pruning (SP), a dynamic pruning framework that removes genomic sequences as they become easy for the model to predict. SP uses per-sequence pretraining accuracy computed directly from the model's forward pass, requiring no separate scoring model or additional pass over the corpus. We evaluate SP across five pretraining settings spanning two corpora, three model families, and both MLM and CLM objectives, with downstream evaluation on five genomic benchmarks. SP reduces pretraining data processing by up to 96% while largely preserving downstream performance. At moderate savings, pruning improves performance by up to 1.2 MCC points over full-data pretraining; at the highest savings, pretraining uses only 4% of the baseline GPU hours with little performance loss. SP also outperforms the dynamic pruning methods InfoBatch and BPAS across most comparisons. Beyond accuracy, our framework supports alternative criteria such as perplexity and EL2N, although accuracy yields the strongest downstream performance at matched savings. These results show that genomic pretraining does not require repeatedly processing all available sequences and that dynamic sequence-level pruning can substantially improve its data and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/FaiasPromit/pruning-dna-sequence.

</details>


### [172] [From Task Affinities to Optimal Allocations: Probabilistic Graphical Modelling for Supervised Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10389)
*Prateek Chanda,Saral Sureka,Krishnateja Killamsetty,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TASKMIXPGM，用基于能量的马尔可夫随机场对 SFT 任务进行连续混合权重学习：节点为一元势（任务效用），边为基于单任务模型预测分布间行为散度的二元势（任务关系），从而在固定预算下得到兼顾效用、覆盖与冗余的任务混合，并给出弱子模性下的离散选择近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构任务的监督微调计算开销高，实践中需在固定训练预算下挑选 SFT 任务子集（即最优任务混合）。常用启发式（均匀采样、按规模比例采样）忽略任务间交互，导致迁移效果差、预算浪费在冗余任务上。

Method: 将每个 SFT 任务视为马尔可夫随机场中的节点：一元势刻画任务效用，二元势通过单任务微调模型预测分布间的行为散度建模任务间关系；对能量目标优化得到连续任务混合权重，平衡效用、覆盖与冗余。进一步证明在预算约束下，该任务集合上的诱导集合函数具有弱子模性，从而为离散任务选择提供近似保证。

Result: 给出一个固定、与基准无关的任务混合方案，在多种模型族与不同训练预算下均优于启发式混合；同时揭示任务交互中的可解释结构，识别出互补性与冗余性，为有效 SFT 混合提供依据。

Conclusion: TASKMIXPGM 将任务混合选择建模为带交互的能量模型，兼具理论保证（弱子模近似）与实用价值（固定可复用的混合配方、跨模型族与预算的稳定增益），并能解释任务间的互补与冗余关系。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning over a large pool of heterogeneous tasks is often computationally expensive, leading practitioners to train language models on a fixed training budget of carefully chosen SFT tasks. Thus, achieving strong downstream performance hinges on selecting an effective subset of tasks, or equivalently, identifying an optimal task mixture under a fixed budget constraint. In practice, supervised finetuning mixtures are often determined using simple heuristics (e.g., uniform or size-proportional sampling) that ignore task interactions, which can hurt transfer and waste budget on redundant task sources. We introduce TASKMIXPGM, a framework for learning continuous task mixtures through energy-based modelling over SFT tasks. Each task forms a node in a Markov random field, with unary potentials capturing task utility and pairwise potentials modelling inter-task relationships via behavioral divergences between predictive distributions of single-task fine-tuned models. Optimizing this objective yields task mixtures that balance utility, coverage, and redundancy. We further show that the induced set function defined over the set of such tasks is weakly submodular under budget constraints, enabling approximation guarantees for discrete task selection. Empirically, we provide a fixed, benchmark-independent mixture that improves over heuristic mixing across multiple model families and different training budgets. TASKMIXPGM also uncovers interpretable structure in task interactions, revealing complementarities and redundancies that inform effective SFT mixtures.

</details>


### [173] [Finite Time Analysis of Risk-Sensitive RL via Noisy Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10393)
*Waqar Mirza,Yashaswini Murthy,Laixi Shi,Eric Mazumdar,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究基于噪声归一化幂迭代对正算子主特征对进行估计，在Hilbert射影度量下给出有限时间随机逼近保证，并将其用于指数效用风险敏感平均代价强化学习的策略评估与控制，获得$\tilde{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从随机观测估计正算子的主特征对等价于带噪的归一化幂迭代；指数效用下的风险敏感平均代价RL中Bellman方程是乘性的，值函数由非线性特征值问题刻画而非加性不动点，传统的加性TD/Q学习框架难以直接适用。

Method: 在Hilbert射影度量下分析每步"采样算子估计+重归一化"的噪声幂迭代；将策略评估与控制统一为非线性特征值问题，构造风险敏感TD学习与Q学习算法，仅依赖单条Markov轨迹样本；在显式正性、混合性与线性函数逼近假设下推导非渐近收敛率。

Result: 证明所恢复特征向量与特征值的有限时间误差界，样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}(\epsilon^{-2})$（含问题相关常数）；结果同时覆盖表格型与线性函数逼近情形，并借助指数效用与KL鲁棒控制的对偶性，给出KL鲁棒平均代价RL的有限时间保证。

Conclusion: Hilbert射影度量是处理乘性Perron--Frobenius型特征问题的自然工具；噪声幂迭代的有限时间分析为风险敏感及KL鲁棒平均代价RL提供了统一的可证明高效算法框架。

Abstract: Estimating the principal eigenpair of a positive operator from stochastic evaluations gives rise to a noisy form of normalized power iteration: each step applies a sampled operator estimate and then renormalizes. We establish finite-time stochastic-approximation guarantees for this procedure, working in Hilbert's projective metric, which is well-suited to the multiplicative geometry of Perron--Frobenius eigenproblems. Our main application is risk-sensitive average-cost reinforcement learning (RL) with exponential utility. In this setting the Bellman equations are multiplicative, and value functions are characterized by nonlinear eigenvalue problems rather than additive fixed points. We cast both policy evaluation and control in this framework, obtaining risk-sensitive TD- and Q-learning algorithms that learn from a single Markovian trajectory. Under explicit positivity, mixing, and linear function approximation assumptions, we prove finite-time bounds on the recovered eigenvector and eigenvalue with $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity up to problem-dependent constants. The results cover both tabular and linear function approximation regimes, and through the duality between exponential utility and KL-robust control yield finite-time guarantees for KL-robust average-cost RL as well.

</details>


### [174] [Regularization Amplifies Subliminal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10397)
*Lawrence Feng,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Gaurav R. Ghosal,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究正则化能否限制隐藏偏见的"隐性迁移"，发现结论恰好相反：正则化（低秩适配、显式惩罚、增加数据）反而放大隐性偏见的迁移强度，并提出"正则化诱导的简单性偏置促进隐性影响"的解释及最优迁移正则化强度依赖任务复杂度的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，隐藏偏见可通过与偏见无明显语义关系的数据进行迁移。作者由此提出核心问题：正则化通过约束模型适配，能否抑制这类"看似无关输入"上的行为变化？这一假设在直觉上合理，但此前缺乏系统检验。

Method: 在多个隐性影响设定下系统对比实验：阈下学习（subliminal learning）、幻影迁移（phantom transfer）、logit-linear selection；操控正则化形式，包括完全微调 vs. 低秩适配（LoRA）、显式惩罚、以及增加数据量这一隐式正则化手段，观察隐藏偏见迁移强度的变化。

Result: 与假设相反：完全微调下阈下学习通常不出现，而低秩适配或显式惩罚可诱导出强迁移；幻影迁移与logit-linear selection 呈现相似趋势；增加数据（隐式正则化）同样增强隐性影响。

Conclusion: 正则化并不能抑制隐性影响，反而放大其迁移，原因可能在于正则化诱导的简单性偏置（simplicity bias）有利于隐性影响的形成；并进一步假设存在使隐性迁移最大化的正则化强度，且该强度取决于任务复杂度。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that hidden biases can be transferred through data with no apparent semantic relationship to those biases. Can regularization limit this covert influence? By constraining adaptation, regularization might be expected to suppress behavioral changes on seemingly unrelated inputs. We systematically study this hypothesis in subliminal learning, as well as in related settings of covert influence, including phantom transfer and logit-linear selection. We find the opposite: regularization amplifies the transfer of hidden biases. In particular, subliminal learning is typically absent under full fine-tuning, but low-rank adaptation or explicit penalties can induce strong transfer. Phantom transfer and logit-linear selection show similar trends. Adding more data, an implicit form of regularization, also strengthens covert influence. Together, these findings suggest that the simplicity biases induced by regularization may facilitate covert influence. We further hypothesize that the level of regularization that maximizes covert transfer depends on task complexity.

</details>


### [175] [Sharp Logarithmic-Product Bounds for List Replicability of Sign Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10408)
*Guangjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 证明任意含m个二元概念、n个域点的非空类其list replicability不超过805·ln(2m)·ln(2n)，从而解决Blondal等的对数乘积猜想；构造Hanner矩阵给出同阶下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定list replicability对概念类规模(m、n)的最优增长率，即对数乘积猜想是否成立，并厘清其与等变指数等几何量的关系。

Method: 在立方体各面构造嵌套无环集；用临界点估计给出高连通区域，加权的胞腔附着保持跨面相容性，再由立方载体定理转化为精确有限覆盖与学习规则。追加一个常坐标使list replicability化为精确几何量"face width"；并发展熵倾斜与双边Gibbs构造、适配结构化矩阵的组合上界。

Result: 得到上界805ln(2m)ln(2n)；Hanner矩阵给出方形及不等对数纵横比情形下同阶下界；图定向类满足精确乘积公式；存在list replicability为4k+1而等变指数为3k的显式类，故两者加性间隙无界；给出经典族的精确值并研究Paley矩阵的指数。

Conclusion: list replicability的对数乘积猜想被证实，量级为Θ(ln m·ln n)；face width为精确刻画，等变指数与list replicability的加性差距可任意大。

Abstract: List replicability measures how many accurate hypotheses suffice to contain the output of a learning rule with arbitrarily high confidence. We prove that every nonempty class of $m$ binary concepts on $n$ domain points has list replicability at most $805\ln(2m)\ln(2n)$. This resolves the logarithmic-product conjecture of Blondal, Hatami, Hatami, Lalov, and Tretiak. Hanner matrices give lower bounds of the same order, for square matrices and for explicit families with unequal logarithmic aspect ratios. The upper bound is obtained by constructing nested acyclic sets in the faces of a cube. A critical-point estimate supplies highly connected regions, weighted cell attachments keep the sets compatible across faces, and a cubical carrier theorem converts them into accurate finite covers and learning rules. Appending one constant coordinate turns list replicability into an exact geometric quantity, the face width, and graph-orientation classes satisfy exact product formulas. In particular, there are explicit classes with list replicability $4k+1$ and equivariant index $3k$, so the additive gap between the two is unbounded. We also develop entropy-tilting and two-sided Gibbs constructions, give combinatorial upper bounds adapted to structured matrices, compute exact values on classical families, and study the index of Paley matrices.

</details>


### [176] [Applied bifidelity material response model construction and uncertainty quantification with a sampling hypernetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10429)
*Reese E. Jones,Cosmin Safta,Dan Bolintineau,Craig M. Hamel,Gianluca Geraci,Sharlotte LB Kramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双层保真度（bifidelity）Langevin 采样超网络框架，通过后验分布学习构建概率神经响应模型，实现高保真数据稀缺时的高精度预测与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真数据稀缺而低保真数据相对充足，多保真建模是兼顾预测精度与不确定性量化的实用途径；但现有方法多为确定性拟合，难以在数据受限下给出可靠的预测不确定性。

Method: 采用双保真Langevin采样超网络：学习模型参数的后验分布而非单一确定性拟合；对低/高保真响应构建灵活的共享隐变量数据表示，实现两保真度间的参数共享与迁移学习。方法改进包括：促进稳定性的漂移模型（drift model）、分阶段与条件式训练策略、Langevin轨迹的自适应初始化，以及更好利用保真度间相关性的训练流程。

Result: 上述增强改善了收敛行为，减少了获得代表性后验集成所需的采样开销，并在数据受限情形下给出更可靠的预测不确定性；整体提升了预测性能与不确定性刻画质量，工作流程更稳定。方法在实验数据、高保真仿真数据及合成示例数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架更有效地利用现有多保真数据，同时通过所学后验结构保持可解释性，为构建不确定性感知代理模型提供了更稳定的流程。

Abstract: Multifidelity modeling offers a practical route to accurate prediction and uncertainty quantification when high-fidelity data are scarce but lower-fidelity data are comparatively abundant. This work develops a bifidelity Langevin sampling hypernetwork framework that constructs probabilistic neu- ral response models by learning a posterior distribution over model parameters rather than a single deterministic fit. The method combines a flexible shared-latent data representation for low- and high-fidelity responses, enabling parameter sharing and transfer learning between the fidelities. We introduce several methodological enhancements to improve the robustness and efficiency of this ap- proach, including stability-promoting drift models, staged and conditional training strategies, adap- tive initialization of Langevin trajectories, and training procedures designed to better exploit corre- lations between fidelity levels. These additions improve convergence behavior, reduce the sampling effort required to obtain representative posterior ensembles, and provide more reliable predictive uncertainty in data-limited regimes. The resulting framework makes more effective use of available multifidelity data while preserving interpretability through the learned posterior structure, yielding improved predictive performance, better uncertainty characterization, and a more stable workflow for constructing uncertainty-aware surrogate models. The method is demonstrated on experimental, high-fidelity simulation and illustrative synthetic data.

</details>


### [177] [HexIt: Learning Hexahedral Block Decomposition with Expert Iteration on Combinatorial Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10433)
*Arjun Narayanan,Per-Olof Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将多面体分解为少数高质量六面体块的问题建模为三维广义映射上的序列决策问题，用卷积策略选择插入操作，并通过模仿学习加专家迭代求解，在倒角实体上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多面体实体分解为少量形状良好的六面体块是网格生成领域的长期开放难题，传统方法难以在组合搜索空间中自动生成高质量分解。

Method: 把分解过程表述为三维广义映射（generalised map）上的序列决策：智能体通过三种插入操作逐步分裂边、面与体，直到每个单元均为六面体且满足质量阈值；策略网络对映射的对合（involution）结构做卷积以选择每次插入。先从构造式分解中克隆策略，再用专家迭代改进。

Result: 成功分解通常需上百次决策，均匀随机策略完全无法找到解。在与克隆数据无共享边界拓扑的留出倒角实体上，最终策略20次采样的期望求解率为94.4%；专家迭代将大型倒角实体上 best-of-20 求解率由0.203提升至0.355。在同等计算量下，PPO、GRPO与VinePPO更新均使克隆策略性能下降。

Conclusion: 基于广义映射结构的策略学习与专家迭代可有效解决长序列六面体块分解问题，而常规强化学习更新反而有害，提示该任务更适合模仿学习与专家迭代范式。

Abstract: Decomposing a polyhedral solid into a few well-shaped hexahedral blocks is a long-standing open problem in mesh generation. We cast it as a sequential decision problem on a 3-dimensional generalised map: an agent splits edges, faces and volumes with three insert operations until every cell is a hexahedron and the mesh passes a quality threshold, and a policy that convolves over the map's involutions chooses each insert. A successful decomposition takes over a hundred decisions, and a uniformly random policy never finds one. We clone a policy from constructed decompositions and improve it by expert iteration. On held-out chamfered solids that share no boundary topology with the cloning data, the final policy solves an expected $94.4\%$ in $20$ samples, and expert iteration raises the best-of-20 solve rate on large chamfered solids from $0.203$ to $0.355$. At matched compute, PPO, GRPO and VinePPO updates all make the cloned policy worse.

</details>


### [178] [Bayesian Low-Rank Posteriors for Scalable Membership Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10455)
*Khanh Nguyen,Antonio Emanuele Cinà,Andrey Barsky,Alejandra Reinares,Fabio Roli,Dimosthenis Karatzas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以LoRA子空间上的贝叶斯后验（SWAG）替代显式影子模型集成的成员推断攻击，显著降低大规模视觉语言模型的攻击成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强成员推断攻击依赖参考（影子）模型集合来估计目标条件下的得分分布，但对现代大规模视觉语言模型训练此类模型集成代价过高，难以实用。

Method: 仅在低秩适配（LoRA）子空间内建模参数不确定性：从单次训练过程中构造后验分布，并采样得到一组多样化的"虚拟参考模型"；具体采用随机权重平均高斯（SWAG）实现该后验近似，从而以远低于传统影子模型集成的成本估计目标条件得分分布。

Result: 在三个经下游适配的VLM上、两个隐私敏感的视觉问答任务（Document VQA、Medical VQA）中进行评估，并引入受控合成数据集以隔离预训练知识的影响；所提攻击优于标准基于参考模型的攻击，且仅需一次参考模型微调运行。

Conclusion: 以LoRA子空间贝叶斯近似取代影子模型集成，可在保持攻击强度的同时大幅提升效率，使成员推断攻击对大规模VLM更具可行性。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) aim to determine whether a sample was used during the training of a target model. Strong MIAs rely on reference (or shadow) models to estimate target-conditional score distributions, but training such ensembles is computationally expensive for modern large-scale Vision--Language Models (VLMs). In this work, we propose a scalable alternative that replaces explicit reference-model training with a Bayesian approximation over low-rank adaptation parameters. Specifically, we model uncertainty only within the LoRA subspace and construct a posterior from a single training run, from which we sample a diverse set of \emph{virtual reference models}. We instantiate this approach using Stochastic Weight Averaging Gaussian (SWAG), enabling efficient approximation of target-conditional score distributions at a fraction of the cost of conventional shadow-model ensembles. We evaluate the resulting attack on three downstream-adapted VLMs across two privacy-sensitive visual question answering tasks, namely Document VQA and Medical VQA. To isolate the effect of pretraining knowledge, we additionally introduce a controlled synthetic dataset. Our approach outperforms standard reference-based attacks while requiring only a single reference-model fine-tuning run, making membership inference more practical for large VLMs.

</details>


### [179] [The Fast Tree Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10466)
*Paul Burchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出快速树变换（Fast Tree Transform），一种面向任意图几何的局部聚合变换，统一泛化了Hadamard频率变换与圆周上的Haar小波变换；其纯频率部分近似对角化图Laplacian，可作为几何优化问题的预条件子，完整变换对图信号近似冗余但能稀疏表示含边缘与纹理的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变换（Hadamard频率变换、Haar小波变换）各自只适用于特定几何，缺乏一种既能在任意图上定义、又能同时兼顾频率对角化性质与边缘/纹理稀疏表示的局部聚合变换，也难以直接用作几何优化中的预条件子。

Method: 构造Fast Tree Transform：在任意图几何上定义局部聚合（locally-aggregated）变换，使Hadamard频率变换与圆周Haar小波变换成为其特例；其中纯频率部分被设计为近似对角化图Laplacian，从而充当几何优化的预条件子；完整变换则对图信号给出仅轻微冗余的多尺度表示。

Result: 纯频率部分能近似对角化图Laplacian，在几何优化问题中作为预条件子有效；完整变换是图的近似冗余（仅轻微过完备）表示，可对同时包含边缘与纹理的信号实现稀疏表示。

Conclusion: 该变换在一个统一框架下整合了频率变换与小波变换的优势，兼具预条件化与多尺度稀疏表示能力，适用于任意图几何上的信号处理与几何优化任务。

Abstract: The Fast Tree Transform is a locally-aggregated transform defined for arbitrary graph geometries that generalizes both the Hadamard frequency transform and the Haar wavelet transform on the circle. The pure frequency part of the transform approximately diagonalizes the graph Laplacian; this fact makes it useful as a preconditioner in geometric optimization problems. The full transform provides an only slightly-redundant representation of signals on the graph that allows sparse representation of signals that include both edges and textures.

</details>


### [180] [Dynamic State Estimation from Sparse PMUs via Physics-Informed Graph Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10468)
*Huy Hoang Le,Haoguang Wang,Christian Moya,Marcos Netto,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息图神经算子，从少数发电机母线的稀疏间歇量测中，单次前向推理估计全网（含未量测）机组的转子角与转速轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统动态状态估计需要估计全部机组状态，但相量测量单元部署稀疏且量测间歇；传统卡尔曼平滑与移动时域估计依赖逐场景优化、代价高，且往往需要状态标签或精确模型参数。

Method: 图注意力编码器处理量测，摇摆方程以硬约束或带可学习修正项（吸收模型误差）的软约束形式嵌入；仅用仿真量测训练，不需要状态标签；多PMU场景下采用掩码训练（把一个PMU从输入中隐藏但保留在损失中）。

Result: 在四个输电系统上，该算子是不做逐场景优化、也不用状态标签的最准确估计器；仅在最小系统且模型误差较大时被调参卡尔曼平滑器超越。用仿真状态训练的网络在两个最小系统上更准，但在118节点系统上不如本方法；后者还比硬约束移动时域估计更快更准，在模型误差下以极小代价匹配或超过基于优化的基准。

Conclusion: 物理约束与图神经算子结合可实现免标签、单次前向的全网动态状态估计，对模型误差鲁棒、计算廉价，且在详细机组模型（参数仅通过电压相量隐式体现）下依然有效。

Abstract: This paper proposes a physics-informed graph neural operator for power system dynamic state estimation. From a sparse, intermittent measurement window at a few generator buses, the operator estimates the rotor-angle and speed trajectories of all machines, unmeasured ones included, in one forward pass. A graph-attention encoder processes the measurements, and the swing equations are imposed as a hard constraint or as a soft one with a learned correction that absorbs model errors. It is trained on simulated measurements, without state labels. With several phasor measurement units, masked training hides one from the input but keeps it in the loss. On four transmission systems, simulations show that the operator is the most accurate estimator that neither optimizes per scenario nor uses state labels, except on the smallest system at large model errors, where tuned Kalman smoothers are better. Networks trained on simulated states are more accurate on the two smallest systems but not on the 118-bus system, where the operator is faster and more accurate than hard-constrained moving-horizon estimation. Under model errors, it matches or outperforms an optimization-based benchmark at a fraction of its cost. The results hold for a detailed machine model with exact parameters seen only through voltage phasors.

</details>


### [181] [Learning a Unified Cross-Model Semantic Dictionary via Gated Bottleneck Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10503)
*Jinchi Zhu,Tie Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门控瓶颈稀疏自编码器（GB-SAE），将跨模型统一可解释性建模为表示分解问题，同时学习一个共享字典与多个模型私有字典，实现可复用的统一概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同基础模型虽共享语义结构，但因架构、预训练目标与协议不同而表示各异，阻碍统一可解释性与跨模型特征复用；现有方法仅在潜坐标层面做对齐，概念本身仍是模型专属，无法共享或复用。

Method: GB-SAE：在稀疏自编码器框架中引入门控瓶颈，同时学习（1）统一的共享字典，编码跨模型公共语义结构、可被所有参与模型乃至未见模型复用；（2）多个模型私有字典，由残差概念构成，刻画模型特有偏置。

Result: 在ViT、SigLIP、DINOv2、ConvNeXt等不同基础模型上评估，GB-SAE在重建能力、统一且忠实的可解释性、以及对未见模型和下游任务的复用性与泛化性方面均表现优越。

Conclusion: 通过表示分解而非仅潜坐标对齐，GB-SAE首次使概念本身成为跨模型可共享、可复用的统一单元，为跨模型可解释性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on similar data distributions tend to learn overlapping semantic structure, but their representations are distinct due to different model architecture, pretraining objectives and protocols. This prevents unified interpretability and cross-model feature reuse. Existing approaches extract interpretable concepts from individual models and align these concepts in a shared coordinate system using cross-reconstruction objectives, but the alignment only applies to latent coordinates while the concepts themselves remain model-specific and cannot be shared or reused. This paper introduces the Gated Bottleneck Sparse Autoencoder (GB-SAE), which casts unified cross-model interpretability as a representation factorization problem and solves it by simultaneously learning a unified shared dictionary and multiple private model-specific dictionaries via a gated bottleneck sparse autoencoder framework. The shared dictionary encodes the cross-model common semantic structure in unified concepts that are reusable across all participating models (and beyond), while each private dictionary comprises residual concepts that capture model-specific biases. We evaluate GB-SAE with distinct foundation models including ViT, SigLIP, DINOv2, and ConvNeXt. GB-SAE demonstrates superior reconstruction capability, unified and faithful interpretability, strong reusability and generalization to unseen models and downstream tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/trustAIoT/GB-SAE_NeurIPS.

</details>


### [182] [CALFA-KV: Joint Calibration and Fisher-Guided Anchors for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10511)
*Xiancai Tian,Leichao Zhang,Shenhui Zhang,Yi Shan,Hailong Li,Feng Lin,Xiaotian Yu,Aijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALFA-KV：将量化器校准与选择性高精度存储结合，离线联合拟合 key center、径向归一化参数与 K/V 码本并估计通道级 Fisher 敏感度，在线据此加权 KV 状态重建误差以挑选锚点，在同等锚点预算下优于所有名义 1-bit 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1-bit KV cache 压缩需要降低重建误差，单纯量化误差较大；作者希望把"量化器校准"与"选择性高精度存储"两种手段结合，用对下游损失敏感的程度来指导哪些 KV 状态值得保留高精度。

Method: 离线阶段：联合拟合 key center、径向归一化（radial normalization）参数以及 K/V 向量码本，使重建结果匹配冻结模型投影后的注意力输出；同时用参考的下一 token 交叉熵损失平方梯度估计通道级 Fisher 型敏感度。在线阶段：以敏感度对各 KV 状态（某层某 KV 头中一个 token 的 key-value 对）的平方重建误差加权打分，在每层每 KV 头的预算约束下保留得分最高者以高精度存储。理论上在精确恢复条件下，给出基于分数的锚点选择相对于同预算最优解的 excess risk 上界（风险定义为损失一阶变化的条件均方）。

Result: 在每层每 KV 头额外 56 个 FP16 锚点预算下，CALFA-KV (d8b8) 在三个模型、七个基准上的平均分数均优于所有被评估的名义 1-bit 基线；同预算下 CALFA-KV (d8b10) 名义索引码率为每通道 1.25 bit，与 BF16 的差距缩小到 0.90–2.01 分。

Conclusion: 校准化码本拟合、Fisher 敏感度引导的锚点选择与分层分头预算三者结合，可在极低码率（约 1–1.25 bit/通道）下显著逼近 BF16 性能，并具备可证明的选择风险界。

Abstract: 1-bit key-value (KV) cache compression can combine quantizer calibration with selective high-precision storage to reduce reconstruction error. We propose Calibration and Fisher-guided Anchors for KV cache compression (CALFA-KV). Offline, we jointly fit a key center, radial normalization parameters, and K/V vector codebooks to match the frozen model's projected attention output. We also estimate channel-wise Fisher-type sensitivities from squared gradients of a reference next-token cross-entropy loss. Online, we use these sensitivities to weight the squared reconstruction errors of each KV state. Here, a KV state is one token's key-value pair within one layer and KV head. We retain the highest-scoring states at high precision under a per-layer, per-KV-head budget. Under exact restoration, we bound the excess risk of score-based anchor selection relative to the optimum at the same anchor budget, where risk is the conditional mean square of the first-order loss change. With a budget of 56 additional FP16 anchors per layer and KV head, CALFA-KV (d8b8) outperforms all evaluated nominal 1-bit baselines on the mean score across seven benchmarks for each of the three models. At the same anchor budget, CALFA-KV (d8b10) uses a nominal index rate of 1.25 bits per channel and narrows the gap to BF16 to 0.90--2.01 points on the same metric.

</details>


### [183] [FADE: Fractional Anomalous Dynamics Extrapolation for Training-Free Diffusion Transformers Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10514)
*Jinlong Yang,Lizilin,Jinke Wu,Yao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的DiT加速框架FADE，用反常扩散建模+Mittag-Leffler分数阶算子外推DiT特征演化，并结合离线profile构建多维查找表实现在线自适应参数检索，在激进跳步下保持SOTA保真度且延迟开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformer迭代采样计算开销大；现有特征缓存方法基于整数阶算子做复用或预测，难以适应DiT隐轨迹的非平稳、高曲率动态，易产生误差累积。

Method: 观察到DiT特征演化呈现反常扩散特性及一定时空异质性；据此用反常扩散刻画特征演化，采用Mittag-Leffler分数阶算子进行特征外推；利用该类动态的跨样本稳定性，离线profiling构建多维查找表，在线推理时以近乎零开销完成时空自适应参数检索；整体为training-free框架。

Result: 在多种架构与模态上的广泛评测表明达到SOTA加速效果，在激进跳步区间下仍保持优异的生成保真度，且延迟开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 分数阶（反常扩散）建模比整数阶算子更契合DiT潜变量轨迹，配合LUT检索可实现高效、免训练、即插即用的DiT推理加速。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have established themselves as the preeminent paradigm for high-fidelity generative modeling across various modalities, yet their practical deployment is hindered by the severe computational overhead of iterative sampling. While recent feature-caching methods mitigate this latency through feature reuse or forecasting based on integer-order operators, they remain limited in adapting to the non-stationary and highly curved dynamics of DiT latent trajectories. In this work, we reveal that DiT feature evolution exhibits anomalous diffusion characteristics and a certain degree of spatiotemporal heterogeneity, rendering static, integer-order forecasting operators inadequate and prone to error accumulation. To address this, we propose Fractional Anomalous Dynamics Extrapolation (FADE), a unified framework for training-free diffusion transformers acceleration. FADE models DiT feature evolution via anomalous diffusion and employs a Mittag--Leffler fractional operator to better adapt to high-curvature and non-linear latent trajectories of DiTs. Leveraging the empirical observation that these complex dynamics maintain cross-sample stability, we further construct a Look-Up Table with multiple dimensions through offline profiling. This enables negligible LUT-retrieval overhead, spatiotemporally adaptive parameter retrieval during online inference. Extensive evaluations across diverse architectures and modalities demonstrate that FADE achieves state-of-the-art acceleration, preserving exceptional generation fidelity under aggressive skip intervals with negligible latency overhead. Our project page is available at https://youngkinlon.github.io/fade-project-page/.

</details>


### [184] [Intra-Option Fitted Q-Evaluation: Evaluating Hierarchical Policies from Non-Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10543)
*Yunfu Deng,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IO-FQE，在增广的状态-选项空间中做拟合Q评估，可在缺少选项标注的数据上无偏评估分层策略，并在长时程连续控制任务上显著优于选项级重要性采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层策略（高层选选项、选项内产生原始动作直至终止）的离线策略评估（OPE）研究不足；现有选项级逐步重要性采样需选项标注轨迹且方差随时程增长，而把FQE简单扩展到分层策略会引入有偏估计。

Method: 提出Intra-Option FQE（IO-FQE）：在增广的状态-选项空间中估计价值函数，利用选项内部（intra-option）的Bellman备份，从而无需数据中的选项标注即可评估分层策略；并给出两种连续动作下的具体实例化。

Result: 在长时程分层连续控制任务（AntMaze导航、OGBench Puzzle操作）上，IO-FQE消除了朴素FQE扩展的偏差，且均方误差显著低于选项级重要性采样。

Conclusion: 在增广状态-选项空间做拟合Q评估是分层策略OPE的有效途径，兼具免选项标注、低偏差与低方差优势。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the expected return of a target policy from previously collected data without additional environment interaction. While OPE methods for flat Markovian policies are well studied, little work has addressed evaluating hierarchical policies in which a high-level policy selects temporally extended options that generate primitive actions until a termination condition is met. The primary existing approach applies per-decision importance sampling at the option level, but requires option-annotated trajectories and exhibits variance that still grows with the horizon. For non-hierarchical policies, fitted Q-evaluation (FQE) typically achieves lower error than importance sampling by leveraging the classic Bellman equation for policy evaluation; however, a naive extension of FQE to hierarchical policies results in a biased policy value estimate. In this paper, we introduce Intra-Option Fitted Q-Evaluation (IO-FQE), which estimates a value function in the augmented state-option space; this approach enables OPE of hierarchical policies even when we lack annotations of what option was run in the data. We develop two continuous-action instantiations of IO-FQE and show on long-horizon hierarchical continuous control tasks (AntMaze navigation and OGBench Puzzle manipulation) that IO-FQE eliminates the bias incurred by a naive application of FQE to hierarchical policies and substantially lowers mean squared-error compared to option-level importance sampling.

</details>


### [185] [Dual-Granularity Learning for Regression with Continuous Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10550)
*Hui Guo,Boyu Wang,Grace Y. Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAIN双粒度框架，用细粒度残差学习与粗粒度分箱对比正则，结合直方图桥接，统一应对回归任务中的异质标签噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带噪标签学习在分类中研究充分，但回归场景探索不足。回归中标签噪声幅度在样本间异质：小到中等偏差需对标签值敏感以做实例级修正，大偏差（标注损坏/离群）则使精确标签不可靠，需转而利用样本间序结构。两类需求相互冲突，要求方法同时捕获全局结构与样本级偏差。

Method: 双粒度框架。(1) 细粒度：由鲁棒损失推导出显式残差学习机制，引入实例特定辅助变量，将标签噪声与预测信号解耦；(2) 粗粒度：基于分箱的对比正则，强制形成与标签邻近性一致、保序的特征结构，处理局部残差修正无法覆盖的噪声；(3) 直方图桥接机制实现双向信息流：粗粒度结构引导为残差学习提供有序特征空间锚点，细粒度残差修正弥补结构正则无法解决的实例级偏差。

Result: 实验表明，在多种噪声条件下，GRAIN一致提升鲁棒性与泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度残差学习与粗粒度保序对比正则的双向耦合，可统一处理回归中异质幅度的标签噪声，实现稳健且泛化的带噪回归。

Abstract: Learning with noisy labels has been extensively studied for classification, yet its regression counterpart remains relatively underexplored. Unlike classification, regression involves continuous-valued targets where label noise exhibits heterogeneous magnitudes across instances, which poses conflicting requirements on the model: correcting small-to-moderate deviations requires sensitivity to label values for instance-level refinement, whereas large deviations caused by corrupted or outlying annotations render exact label values unreliable, necessitating reduced reliance on exact labels and instead leveraging ordinal structure among samples. These requirements call for methods that capture both global structure and sample-specific deviations in a unified manner. To this end, we propose GRAIN, a dual-granularity framework for robust noisy regression. At the fine-grained level, we derive an explicit residual learning mechanism from a robust loss formulation, introducing instance-specific auxiliary variables to decouple label noise from predictive signals. At the coarse-grained level, we introduce a binning-based contrastive regularization that enforces an order-preserving feature structure aligned with label proximity, addressing noise beyond the capacity of local residual refinement. The two levels are coupled via a histogram-based bridging mechanism that enables bidirectional information flow: coarse-grained structural guidance establishes a well-ordered feature space that anchors residual learning, while fine-grained residual refinement corrects instance-level deviations that structural regularization alone cannot resolve. Experimental results show that GRAIN consistently improves robustness and generalization under diverse noise conditions.

</details>


### [186] [Meta-Reinforcement Learning with Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10553)
*Jake Grigsby,Siddhant Agarwal,Yu Lei,Leonidas Varveropoulos,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出行为基础模型（BFM）可在线推断任务，因而与元强化学习（meta-RL）构成固定环境中在线奖励适应的两条互补路径；作者提出四大挑战并给出混合方法 MetaBFM，其在分布内恢复监督式 meta-RL 的性能，同时泛化到训练集之外的奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: meta-RL 需要多样化训练环境与奖励函数，但测试时可能出现的所有目标难以预先设想；BFM 从无奖励数据学习策略并支持零样本 RL（ZSRL），近期工作表明 BFM 的任务推断可在线进行而无需任务特定先验数据。作者据此把 BFM 与 meta-RL 视为固定环境下在线奖励适应的两种替代方案，并希望把 BFM 扩展到新环境等更广场景以与 meta-RL 全面竞争。

Method: 提炼出四项挑战：环境识别、在线数据采集、测试时探索、混合奖励监督学习，逐一研究后提出 MetaBFM 混合方法：训练阶段结合监督式奖励跟随与无监督学习；测试阶段可在探索、利用与 ZSRL 式奖励推断之间插值切换。

Result: MetaBFM 能够复现监督式 meta-RL 在分布内的性能，并泛化到训练集之外的奖励函数。

Conclusion: BFM 与 meta-RL 是互补的研究方向，二者优势结合可使自适应智能体从一组固定的监督目标中学到更多样化的行为。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning (meta-RL) trains agents to adapt to novel tasks from test-time experience, but requires diverse training environments and reward functions. While environments are becoming cheaper to generate, it remains difficult to anticipate every objective an agent may be asked to pursue at test time. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) learn policies from reward-free data and support zero-shot RL (ZSRL): adaptation to new objectives at test time using a large reward-labeled dataset. Recent work has shown that BFM task inference can instead be performed online without prior task-specific data. We note that this capability positions BFMs and meta-RL as alternative approaches for online adaptation to new reward functions in a fixed environment. We then ask whether we can extend BFMs to compete with meta-RL more broadly, including in novel environments, and identify four challenges: environment identification, online data collection, test-time exploration, and learning from mixed reward supervision. We study each individually, then propose MetaBFM, a hybrid method that addresses all four. During training, it combines supervised reward-following with unsupervised learning; at test time, it can interpolate between exploration, exploitation, and ZSRL-style reward inference. We demonstrate that MetaBFM can recover the in-distribution performance of supervised meta-RL while generalizing to reward functions outside the training set. Our work suggests that BFMs and meta-RL are complementary research directions whose combined strengths allow adaptive agents to learn more diverse behaviors from a fixed set of supervised objectives.

</details>


### [187] [Confronting Uncertainty in Mechanism-AI Coupled Dynamical Systems via Optimal Transport and Information Geometry: Part I. Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10563)
*Pengfei Song,Yanni Xiao,Shuangshuang Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从最优传输与信息几何视角建立机制-AI耦合动力系统中不确定性量化的统一数学框架，将UQ表述为黎曼度量概率密度空间上能量泛函的极小化，统一描述Wasserstein与Fisher-Rao等度量下的梯度流，并给出抽象伴随敏感性分析数值解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制-AI耦合动力系统广泛用于复杂生物系统研究，但噪声、稀疏与部分观测数据带来的不确定性限制其可靠性；现有贝叶斯推断方法缺乏统一的理论与几何刻画，难以在共同计算预算下比较。

Method: 在配备黎曼度量的概率密度空间上，将UQ问题表述为能量泛函（经由微分方程评估损失）的极小化问题；给出不同度量（Wasserstein、Fisher-Rao等）下能量泛函梯度流的统一描述；基于抽象伴随敏感性分析提出求解这些梯度流的数值方法。

Result: 该框架将一系列面向动力系统的贝叶斯推断方法纳入统一表述；数值实验在共同计算预算下比较了不同方法的后验逼近与预测不确定性表现。

Conclusion: 研究为动力系统建模及异质性演化研究提供了几何视角，并为机制-AI耦合系统的不确定性量化提供了统一的理论与计算基础。

Abstract: Mechanism-AI coupled dynamical systems are increasingly used to study complex biological systems. However, uncertainty associated with noisy, sparse, and partially observed data limits their reliability. This paper presents a systematic mathematical formulation for treating these uncertainties from the perspective of optimal transport and information geometry. We formulate the uncertainty quantification problem as the minimization of an energy functional on a probability density space equipped with a Riemannian metric, with the loss evaluated through a differential equation, and give a unified description of the gradient flows of the energy functional under specific metrics, such as the Wasserstein and Fisher-Rao metrics. Furthermore, we present corresponding numerical methods for solving these gradient flows using abstract adjoint sensitivity analysis. The formulation connects a range of Bayesian inference methods for dynamical systems; numerical experiments compare posterior approximation and predictive uncertainty under a common computational budget. Moreover, it also provides a geometric perspective on dynamical modeling and the study of heterogeneity evolution.

</details>


### [188] [Predictive Modelling of Solid Waste Generation through Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10579)
*Mitra Tithi Dey,Punyasha Chatterjee,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于斯里兰卡2012–2018年真实城市固体废弃物数据，对比SARIMAX、Prophet、LSTM、GRU四种时间序列模型，并进行节假日、工作日—周末、季节性、温度湿度等因素分析，结果显示LSTM与SARIMAX整体预测性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSW预测研究存在局限：缺乏下级行政单元的细粒度数据、数据集规模小、对影响废弃物产生的关键因素（节假日、周末、季节性、温度、湿度变化）考虑不足或完全未考虑。可靠的MSW产生量预测对可持续城市管理、资源规划、收运调度优化及降低环境影响至关重要。

Method: 使用斯里兰卡2012–2018年真实MSW数据，对四种常用时间序列预测模型——SARIMAX、Prophet、LSTM、GRU——进行综合对比分析；并开展因素层面的细致分析，考察节假日、工作日与周末差异、季节模式、温度与湿度对废弃物产生行为的影响；采用MAE、MAPE、RMSE及决定系数R²评估模型性能。

Result: 节假日与周末废弃物产生量较低；夏季与冬季产生量较高，季风季与季风后相对较低；温度与湿度同废弃物产生量之间不存在直接的线性关系，但其季节性变化与不同季节废弃物产生量的变化相关联。模型评估中，LSTM与SARIMAX取得最佳整体预测性能。

Conclusion: 结合外生因素与季节性刻画的方法能够更准确预测MSW产生量；LSTM与SARIMAX为本研究数据条件下最适用的预测模型，可为城市废弃物管理规划与收运调度提供依据，并弥补以往研究在因素考量和数据粒度上的不足。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of Municipal Solid Waste (MSW) production is pivotal for sustainable urban management, as it enables efficient resource planning, optimized collection schedules, and reduced environmental impacts. Existing works on MSW forecasting have some limitations, such as unavailability of granular data from lower administrative units, small datasets, limited or sometimes no consideration of important factors that can affect MSW generation, including holidays, weekends, seasonality, changes in temperature or humidity.
  This work presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of four popularly used time series forecasting models, namely Seasonal Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average with Exogenous Regressors (SARIMAX), Prophet, Long Short Term Memory (LSTM) and Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), using a real-world MSW dataset from Sri Lanka spanning 2012-2018. A detailed factor-wise analysis is conducted to examine waste generation behaviour considering holidays, weekday-weekend variations, seasonal patterns, temperature and humidity. The results show lower waste generation during holidays and weekends, higher generation during Summer and Winter, and comparatively lower generation during Monsoon and Post-Monsoon. Temperature and humidity do not exhibit a direct linear relationship with waste generation, although their seasonal variations are associated with the changes in waste generation across different seasons. All model performances are evaluated using Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), and the coefficient of determination (R2). Among the evaluated models, LSTM and SARIMAX achieve the best overall forecasting performance.

</details>


### [189] [Detent: Batch-Invariant LLM Serving and RL Rollouts at Near-Stock Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10611)
*Peixu Xin,Runchuan Feng,Jiazhen Guo,Kan Wu,Ming Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Detent：将内核选择建模为可执行程序，仅固定其中少量批次相关取值并使工作负载调优，从而在 vLLM/SGLang 多 GPU 部署上实现批次不变（batch-invariant）推理，延迟代价远低于确定性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现评测、可验证推理与 on-policy RL 要求同一请求在不同批次组合下输出完全相同的比特。现有引擎的 kernel 选择依赖批次，且数学等价的两个 kernel 舍入方式可能不同；而引擎的确定性模式通过每个算子只保留一个保守 kernel 消除选择，导致显著延迟损失。

Method: 观察到许多快速 kernel 本身即批次不变，只需固定 kernel 选择所读取的少数批次相关值。Detent 将 kernel 选择表达为引擎执行的程序，自动找出该程序中必须固定的最少取值集合，并在部署前针对具体负载调优这些取值。

Result: 在 vLLM 与 SGLang 上对 Llama-3.1-70B、Qwen3-30B-A3B 多 GPU 部署中实现批次不变；按负载调优后每输出 token 延迟为 Stock 的 1.04–1.17x（确定性模式为 1.67–10.3x），SGLang 中 GSM8K 准确率与确定性模式持平；覆盖多引擎、多 GPU 代际、TP 度、稠密与 MoE 模型。带种子 RL rollout 在不同批次与 cache 切分下可逐比特复现，训练端在 Detent kernel 与取值上逐比特匹配 rollout logprob，rollout 时间为 Stock 的 1.03x；BF16 GRPO 保持稳定而 Stock 训练崩溃。

Conclusion: 批次不变性无需以“每算子单一保守 kernel”为代价：只需固定 kernel 选择中少数批次相关取值并调优，即可在近原生延迟下获得比特级可复现性，支撑可复现评测、可验证推理与稳定 RL 训练。

Abstract: Reproducible evaluation, verifiable inference and on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) need an LLM engine to give a request the same output bits whichever requests share its batch. Batch-invariant kernels exist, but engines still select each kernel and its parameters from the batch. Two kernels that compute the same sum can round it differently. The engines' deterministic modes remove the choice by keeping one conservative kernel per operator and pay for it in latency. Many fast kernels are batch-invariant by themselves, so it is enough to fix the few batch-dependent values that kernel selection reads. Detent expresses kernel selection as a program that the engine executes. It finds the fewest values in this program that must be fixed and tunes them for the workload before deployment. On multi-GPU deployments of Llama-3.1-70B and Qwen3-30B-A3B in vLLM and SGLang, Detent is batch-invariant. Tuned per load, it serves at 1.04-1.17x the latency per output token of Stock (each engine with its standard settings), against 1.67-10.3x for the deterministic modes. In SGLang it matches the GSM8K accuracy of SGLang's deterministic mode. Detent covers multiple deployment combinations, including multiple engines, GPU generations, tensor-parallel degrees, and dense and MoE models. Seeded RL rollouts of models up to Llama-3.1-70B replay bit for bit under other batches and cache splits. A trainer on Detent's kernels and values matches every rollout logprob bit for bit at 1.03x Stock's rollout time per token. BF16 GRPO then stays stable where Stock's run collapses.

</details>


### [190] [Clinical Evidence-Guided Residual Learning for Medication Recommendation with Dynamic DDI Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10613)
*Zesheng Li,Chengchang Pan,Honggang Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EviDrug——一种临床证据引导的残差学习框架，用于药物推荐，在三个数据集上以更低参数与运行开销超越十二个已发表基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物推荐需同时结合患者特异性的治疗证据与处方内部的交互结构。核心难点在于判定哪些证据支持每一个药物，以及如何在保持预测质量的前提下恰当地正则化药物间交互。

Method: 1）临床记忆骨干：由自训练的访视感知编码器、训练病例记忆库以及一个药物集合交互模块构成，生成患者特异性的处方表征；2）门控精炼头：将骨干状态与临床关联、以病史为条件的药物身份表示结合，通过有界logit修正进行细化；3）DDI反馈机制：利用训练查询预测自适应调整正则化强度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、NWICU与SICdb三个数据集上，EviDrug在AP、F1与Jaccard指标上均优于十二个已发表方法基线，AP分别较最强对比方法提升2.26、2.06与1.71个百分点；且参数规模与运行时间成本低于MetaDrug。配对对照实验显示DDI指标下降而预测质量保持稳定。

Conclusion: EviDrug通过证据引导的残差学习与自适应DDI正则化，在保持甚至提升预测性能的同时降低药物交互风险，并具备更优的效率—效果权衡。

Abstract: Medication recommendation must combine patient-specific treatment evidence with the interaction structure of a prescription. The central challenge is to determine which evidence supports each medication and how strongly to regularize interactions while preserving predictive quality. We propose EviDrug, a clinical evidence-guided residual learning framework. Its clinical-memory backbone integrates a self-trained visit-aware encoder, training-case memory and one medication-set interaction block to establish a patient-specific prescription. A gated refinement head combines the backbone states with clinical associations and history-conditioned medication identities through bounded logit corrections. Drug-drug interaction (DDI) feedback adapts regularization strength using training-query predictions. Across MIMIC-IV, NWICU and SICdb, EviDrug leads twelve published-method baselines in AP, F1 and Jaccard, improving AP by 2.26, 2.06 and 1.71 percentage points over the strongest comparator. These gains come at lower parameter and runtime costs than MetaDrug. Matched controls demonstrate DDI reductions with stable predictive quality. Code is available at https://github.com/LIZESHENG13/EviDrug.

</details>


### [191] [What Do Collusion Probes Read? Three Pitfalls in Evaluating Multi-Agent Collusion Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10620)
*Mainul Hasan Mohsin,Mohammad Shahidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活探针看似能近乎完美地监控大模型智能体间的秘密串通，但实验表明它们主要检测"智能体被指示串通"而非"决定串通"；通过分离指令与决策的新基准发现，现有检测器在指令去除后AUROC由1.00降至0.61，而基于智能体自身选择的探针仍可检测真实决策（无指令AUROC 0.73），并提出通用测试协议与在线监控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究宣称激活探针是近乎完美的串通监控器，但这类结果可能混杂了"收到串通指令"这一信号，无法区分智能体是否真正做出串通决策。缺少把指令与决策分离的基准，也缺乏对评测陷阱的识别，导致报告的性能被系统性高估。

Method: 构建新基准：2,500个委员会情境与1,476个代码评审情境，引入"收到串通指令但拒绝执行"的智能体，从而将指令与决策解耦；在此基础上评测已发表检测器与探针，并进行消融（移除指令信息）。同时识别两类评测陷阱：投票后才打分的泄漏式规则、以及公开辩论中缺乏串通者发言的情形；进一步训练基于智能体自身选择的探针，并测试目标被微调进权重的场景（预注册）。最后将对照实验规范化为可复用协议，并把扫描统计量适配为适用于任意规模群体的在线监控器。

Result: 已发表检测器难以区分串通者与拒从者；最佳探针主要依赖读取指令，移除指令后智能体AUROC从1.00跌至0.61。两类陷阱使结果虚高：仅在投票后打分的规则可达AUROC 0.990但投票前毫无发现；57%的尝试中公开场合无串通者为目标辩护。基于智能体自身选择的探针可在无指令下读出决策（AUROC 0.73）；对目标被微调进权重的串通者，探针AUROC达0.995（预注册）。

Conclusion: 激活探针的高分主要来自对"指令"而非"决策"的识别，评测需严格区分二者并排除后验打分类泄漏。真实串通决策仍可被基于选择的探针检出，尤其在权重层面植入目标时几乎完全可测。作者将对照条件转化为通用监控测试协议，并提供可扩展至任意规模群体的在线扫描统计监控器。

Abstract: Activation probes look like near-perfect monitors for secret collusion among large language model (LLM) agents. We show that they mostly detect that an agent was told to collude, not that it decided to. Our benchmark (2,500 committee and 1,476 code-review episodes) adds agents who receive the collusion instruction and refuse it, separating instruction from decision. Published detectors barely tell colluders from these refusers, and our best probe separates them mostly by reading the instruction: without it, agent AUROC falls from 1.00 to 0.61. Two further pitfalls inflate reported results: scored after the vote, a rule that only reads the votes reaches AUROC 0.990 but finds nothing before it; and in 57% of attempts no colluder argues for the target in public. The decision is still there: a probe trained on agents' own choices reads it (AUROC 0.73 without the instruction), and probes find colluders whose goal was fine-tuned into the weights (AUROC 0.995; pre-registered). We turn the controls into a protocol for testing any collusion monitor, and adapt scan statistics into an online monitor for groups of any size.

</details>


### [192] [CONSTRAINER: Promptable Graph-Structured Optimization via Constraint Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10627)
*Zeeshan Memon,Mingke Tian,Xinyuan Song,Hongwei Jin,Kibaek Kim,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CONSTRAINER：将约束规范解析为算子图并用 GNN 编码为不变约束嵌入，以此条件化共享骨干模型，实现跨图结构优化任务的可复用求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图结构优化的神经方法多在固定问题形式上训练，难以跨相关变体复用；近期多任务共享骨干的工作主要共享实例级表示、隐式处理可行性约束，忽略了约束集本身在任务间的高度重叠这一可共享结构来源。

Method: 以约束为结构化输入：将约束规范解析为算子图（operator graph），用图神经网络编码得到不变的约束嵌入（constraint embedding），再以该嵌入条件化一个共享骨干网络进行解的预测。

Result: 在电力网络优化与混合整数线性规划（MILP）任务上评估，相比基线方法取得更优的解最优性，并支持高效的迁移学习与泛化。

Conclusion: 显式建模并以约束嵌入条件化共享骨干，是可跨问题变体复用的有效范式，为约束优化中的"约束级"共享表示提供了可行路径。
