Abstract: Neural methods for graph-structured optimization have achieved strong performance within individual problem classes, but are typically trained for fixed formulations, limiting reuse across related problem variants. Recent progress in foundation models has motivated shared neural backbones trained across multiple graph-structured optimization tasks; however, existing approaches primarily share instance-level representations while treating feasibility constraints implicitly. A key source of shared structure across constrained optimization problems is the constraint set itself, which often exhibits substantial overlap across tasks. We propose CONSTRAINER, a constraint-conditioned framework for graph-structured optimization that treats constraints as structured inputs. Constraint specifications are parsed into operator graphs and encoded using a graph neural network to produce invariant constraint embeddings, which condition a shared backbone model for solution prediction. We evaluate our approach on power grid optimization and mixed-integer linear programming problems, where it shows improved solution optimality and enables efficient transfer learning and generalization compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [193] [Coverage-Verified Sparse Attention: Closed-Loop Quality Control for Long-Context LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10634)
*Yan-Cheng Li,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CVSA 将稀疏注意力内核中被选中 token 上的 softmax 质量占比（coverage）作为可验证信号，构建免训练的闭环"先验证后纠正"解码流程，在高覆盖率草稿被接受、覆盖率不足的头单独回退纠正，在长上下文下兼顾质量与约 6 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力加速长上下文解码时是开环的：内核一旦提交选择，就没有信号说明是否保留了足够的注意力质量，也没有失败后的恢复路径，这使质量损失不可控、不可诊断。

Method: 作者指出所需信号已存在于内核内部：选中 token 上的 softmax 质量占比（coverage）与单步输出误差满足精确代数等式。据此把 coverage 同时用作接受判据与恢复时的插值权重，构成 training-free 的闭环 verify-then-correct 解码：接受高覆盖率的 draft，仅对不达标的注意力头做纠正；不引入采样、草稿模型或投机解码基础设施。并辅以阈值扫描、预算与调度消融来确定部署工作点。

Result: 阈值扫描范围内质量波动仅 0.14 分；LongBench 上 7B 与 70B 距稠密注意力 1.13 分以内，70B 上统计等价；RULER 上损失 0.8 分；128K 上下文下实测解码步中位加速 6.21×。

Conclusion: 用内核内生的 coverage 信号即可把开环稀疏注意力转为闭环可验证解码，在不需额外模型与投机解码基础设施的前提下，以极小质量代价换取长上下文的大幅解码加速，且对阈值不敏感、易于部署。

Abstract: Sparse attention accelerates long-context decoding by reading a subset of the KV cache, but existing methods are open-loop: the kernel commits to its selection with no signal indicating whether enough attention mass was preserved and no recovery path when it was not. The needed signal already exists inside the kernel. The fraction of softmax mass on the selected tokens, which we call coverage, satisfies an exact algebraic equality with per-step output error and serves a dual role as the acceptance criterion and the interpolation weight for recovery. Coverage-Verified Sparse Attention (CVSA) turns coverage into a training-free, closed-loop verify-then-correct decode loop that accepts high-coverage drafts and recovers only the heads that fall short, with no sampling, no draft model, and no speculative-decoding infrastructure. Quality varies by only $0.14$ points across the threshold sweep; budget and scheduling ablations characterize the deployed operating point. On LongBench at 7B and 70B, CVSA comes within $1.13$ points of dense attention, statistically matching it at 70B, and it loses $0.8$ points on RULER; measured median decode-step speedup reaches $6.21\times$ at 128K context.

</details>


### [194] [MACaQ: Fast, Model Agnostic Calibrated Quantization for Low-Precision Weights and Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10649)
*Lorenz K. Muller,Philippe Bich,Chiara Boretti,Hyun-Min Chang,Jiawei Zhuang,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACaQ：一种层独立的MXFP4 W4A4训练后量化方法，在线做逐通道缩放与块Hadamard变换、并将逆变换吸收进权重，配合Hessian感知的Flip-Refinement贪心码本微调，实现低开销、可流式执行的量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重-激活量化方法依赖昂贵的优化过程，且需要把基变换跨层传播，与具体架构强耦合，实施复杂、难以通用。

Method: 每个线性层独立处理：激活端在线施加逐通道scale与前向块Hadamard变换，对应逆变换吸收进权重；变换保持在线从而消除跨层依赖，支持一次只驻留一层内存的流式量化。针对round-to-nearest的不足，引入Flip-Refinement——基于Hessian的坐标下降，按贪心策略将单个FP4码移动到相邻可表示值。

Result: 在vLLM中Qwen3-32B上端到端额外开销仅2.3%（相对纯MXFP4）；困惑度低于DuQuant++与MR-GPTQ，且在Llama-3-8B上以约1/11的量化时间达到二者最优困惑度；无需架构特定修改即用于图像扩散模型；64分钟、仅32.5GB显存完成122B参数模型量化。

Conclusion: 层局部、在线变换的设计在保持精度竞争力与低推理开销的同时，带来良好的通用性与显著的内存/时间效率，适用于大模型与跨模态架构。

Abstract: Weight-activation quantization can reduce the memory and compute cost of neural-network inference, but recent methods often require expensive optimization and architecture-specific propagation of basis transforms across layers. We introduce MACaQ, an MXFP4 W4A4 post-training quantization method that treats every linear layer independently. MACaQ applies a per-channel scale and a block-Hadamard transform online to each layer's activations, while absorbing the corresponding inverse transforms into its weights. Retaining these transforms online avoids cross-layer dependencies and enables a simple streaming implementation of the quantization process, in which only one layer must reside in memory at a time. To improve over round-to-nearest quantization, we introduce Flip-Refinement, a Hessian-aware coordinate-descent procedure that greedily moves individual FP4 codes to adjacent representable values. Despite executing the activation transforms at inference time, MACaQ adds only 2.3% end-to-end overhead over pure MXFP4 on Qwen3-32B in vLLM. MACaQ reaches lower perplexity than DuQuant++ and MR-GPTQ, and already matches their best perplexity on Llama-3-8B in 11 times less quantization time. We further apply MACaQ without architecture-specific modifications to an image diffusion model, illustrating the generality of its layer-local design and show quantization of a 122B parameter model in 64 minutes, using only 32.5GB GPU RAM.

</details>


### [195] [STABLE: A Continual Learning Optimizer with Adaptive Drift Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10655)
*Xiaoyan Li,Lanpei Li,Massimo Coppola,Vincenzo Lomonaco,Antonio Carta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种优化器层面的持续学习方案 STABLE，通过原始-对偶自适应漂移控制抑制灾难性遗忘，在无需重放缓冲区、架构改动或正则项的情况下兼顾稳定性与可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖重放缓冲区、网络架构修改或额外正则项来缓解灾难性遗忘。作者认为遗忘本质上是学习动力学不稳定的结果，因此更适合在优化器层面解决；难点在于学习动力学具有时间依赖性（如稳定性间隙现象）。

Method: 首先针对距离型分类器形式化证明：在局部得分稳定性条件下，参数漂移有界可保证间隔侵蚀有界。基于该结论提出 STABLE——一种带自适应漂移控制的原始-对偶优化器：当漂移预算被违反时增大对偶变量，否则减小，从而以预算形式约束参数漂移。

Result: 在长时程单模态任务流与基于 CLIP 的多模态任务流上，用 STABLE 训练的普通 PEFT 基线达到或超过专用持续学习方法；多模态场景下还保持了零样本迁移能力，匹配或优于专门的多模态持续学习方法。

Conclusion: 遗忘可通过优化器层的漂移控制有效缓解；STABLE 在不大幅牺牲可塑性的前提下实现高稳定性，说明优化器级方案是持续学习的一种简洁且通用的替代路径。

Abstract: Continual learning suffers from catastrophic forgetting, leading to the loss of past knowledge and unstable adaptation across task streams. Existing approaches typically address this issue through replay buffers, architectural changes, or additional regularization terms. We argue that forgetting is a consequence of unstable learning dynamics, which may benefit from an optimizer-level solution. The main challenge is that learning dynamics are time-dependent, as demonstrated by empirical phenomena such as the stability gap. First, we formally prove that, for distance-based classifiers, bounded parameter drift guarantees bounded margin erosion under a local score-stability condition. Based on this insight, we propose STABLE, a primal-dual optimizer with adaptive drift control. STABLE enforces a drift budget by increasing the dual variable when the budget is violated and decreasing it otherwise. This results in high stability without penalizing plasticity. Experiments on long-horizon unimodal streams and CLIP-based multimodal streams show that vanilla PEFT baselines trained with STABLE match or outperform specialized continual learning methods. In the multimodal setting, STABLE further preserves zero-shot transfer, matching or exceeding dedicated multimodal continual learning methods.

</details>


### [196] [Causal Heteroscedastic Structure Learning From Incomplete Temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10668)
*Abdellah Rahmani,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cheesefill 是一个基于最优传输的框架，用于在缺失值与异方差噪声并存的多变量时间序列中联合学习因果结构，通过条件归一化流与随机校正映射联合优化，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列因果结构发现在金融、神经科学等领域很重要，但存在三个障碍：缺失值导致"先填补再发现"流程扭曲分布并产生伪因果图；瞬时效应识别困难且缺失使填补与图恢复纠缠；异方差噪声方差同时受瞬时与滞后原因驱动，违反标准方法假设。现有方法无法同时解决这些问题，且消融实验表明顺序处理而非联合处理会显著降低性能。

Method: 提出 Cheesefill：用条件归一化流参数化因果机制，并联合学习一个随机校正映射，将朴素填补精化为与推断因果机制一致的轨迹；理论上证明在随机校正映射上优化等价于 Kantorovich 问题的条件核重构，由此得到可处理的神经参数化目标。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，跨多种缺失机制下 Cheesefill 一致优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输统一框架联合处理缺失值、瞬时因果与异方差噪声，是可行且优于分步策略的方案。

Abstract: Discovering causal structures in multivariate time series (MTS) is critical in domains such as finance and neuroscience, yet remains challenging in practice due to three distinct obstacles. First, MTS are routinely corrupted by missing values, where naive impute-then-discover pipelines distort the underlying data distribution and yield spurious causal graphs. Second, identifying instantaneous effects is difficult even on complete data, and missingness further entangles imputation with graph recovery. Third, real-world data frequently exhibits heteroscedastic noise with variance driven by both instantaneous and lagged causes, violating the core assumptions of standard discovery methods. Existing approaches fail to address these challenges concurrently, typically presuming complete data, homoscedastic noise, or strictly time-lagged relationships. Crucially, we show through ablation studies that resolving these issues sequentially, rather than jointly, leads to a substantial degradation in performance. To overcome this, we introduce Cheesefill, an optimal transport framework for causal structure learning under missingness and heteroscedastic noise. Cheesefill parameterizes causal mechanisms using conditional normalizing flows and jointly learns a stochastic correction map that refines naive imputations into trajectories consistent with the inferred causal mechanisms. Theoretically, we prove that optimizing over stochastic correction maps is equivalent to a conditional kernel reformulation of the Kantorovich problem, from which our neural parameterization yields a tractable objective. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that Cheesefill consistently outperforms existing baselines across diverse missingness regimes.

</details>


### [197] [Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning using Behavior Regularisation and Latent Feasibility Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10677)
*Mahesh Keswani,Parth Bhardwaj,Ankur Kumar,Raunak P. Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCAL，将行为正则化策略梯度引入CVAE演员，并依据可行性估计对目标函数分块优化，同时推理时按状态可行性动态调整隐变量先验尺度，在DSRL基准上全部任务满足约束且回报具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全离线RL需要保证逐状态安全，通常用可达性分析刻画可行域；但常见的AWR策略提取依赖指数化权重，会放大量价值估计误差，而行为正则化策略梯度直接利用价值函数的动作梯度，往往更有效地利用学到的critic。

Method: 提出Feasibility-Optimized Conditional Actor Learning（FOCAL）：把行为正则化策略梯度嵌入CVAE演员网络；采用按可行性划分的一阶目标——在估计可行的转移上提升回报，在估计不可行的转移上降低约束违反；推理阶段依据状态的可行性估计动态调整隐变量先验的尺度，以调节动作采样的保守程度。理论上对比AWR与FOCAL的策略更新方差随优势/价值估计误差的变化。

Result: 理论上证明AWR的策略更新方差随优势误差指数增长，而FOCAL的方差随价值估计误差方差线性增长；在DSRL基准的全部评测任务上FOCAL均满足约束，回报与基线相当或更高。

Conclusion: 通过将可行性分区的一阶策略梯度与CVAE条件演员、以及推理期可行性自适应先验尺度相结合，FOCAL在缓解指数化误差放大的同时实现了安全且高性能的离线策略学习。

Abstract: Safe offline reinforcement learning (RL) seeks to learn policies that maximize return while satisfying safety constraints, using a static dataset. To characterize state-wise safety, reachability analysis identifies the feasible region, defined as the set of states from which constraints can be satisfied at every future step. Policies are commonly extracted from learned value functions with advantage-weighted regression (AWR). However, AWR relies on exponentiated weights that amplify value estimation errors, whereas behavior-regularized policy gradient methods directly utilize the action gradient of the value function and often make more effective use of learned critics. We propose Feasibility-Optimized Conditional Actor Learning (FOCAL), a method that incorporates behavior-regularized policy gradients into a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) actor. FOCAL optimizes this policy using a first-order objective partitioned by feasibility, updating the actor to increase return on transitions estimated to be feasible and to reduce constraint violations on transitions estimated to be infeasible. During inference, FOCAL dynamically adjusts the scale of the latent prior according to the estimated feasibility of the state to adjust the conservatism of action sampling. Theoretically, we show that for AWR, the policy update variance depends exponentially on the advantage error, whereas for FOCAL it scales linearly with the variance of the value estimation error. Across the DSRL benchmark, FOCAL satisfies the constraints on all evaluated tasks while achieving competitive or higher returns compared to baselines.

</details>


### [198] [A Geometric Theory for Nonequilibrium Transformer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10691)
*Aythami Bethencourt de León,Darryl D. Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文基于统计物理建立变分-几何理论，刻画固定参数下Transformer注意力与token表示的联合演化：为注意力引入自由能，用Fisher–Rao梯度流描述注意力权重演化，并证明其收敛到softmax唯一极小值；将表示的Hamilton动力学与注意力弛豫耦合，得到能量耗散律，并证明注意力流及三类耦合系统的全局适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer注意力与token表示的动态演化缺乏统一的物理/几何理论解释；固定模型参数下，注意力的收敛性与表示-注意力耦合系统的适定性缺少严格分析。作者希望用统计物理的变分原理与信息几何（Fisher–Rao流）给出统一刻画。

Method: 1) 为注意力定义自由能，将注意力权重演化建模为Fisher–Rao梯度流；2) 在表示固定时证明softmax矩阵为唯一自由能极小元且任意正注意力轨道收敛于它；3) 对给定的时变表示，通过logit变量变换将注意力动力学线性化，得到相对瞬时softmax的跟踪界与收敛到固定矩阵的充分条件；4) 将表示的Hamilton动力学（含动量）与同一能量驱动的注意力弛豫耦合，得到能量耗散律；5) 构造两种引入注意力value映射的Hamilton量变体；6) 证明注意力流及三类耦合系统的全局适定性。

Result: softmax是唯一自由能最小者，所有正注意力轨迹收敛于它；logit变量下注意力动力学线性化，给出对瞬时softmax的跟踪界与收敛到固定矩阵的充分条件；耦合系统满足能量耗散律；含value映射的两种Hamilton量变体可行；注意力流与三类耦合系统全局适定。

Conclusion: 该文为Transformer内部动力学提供了严格的变分-几何与统计物理框架，统一解释了softmax的极小性、注意力的线性化跟踪行为及表示-注意力耦合的能量耗散结构，并给出全局适定性保证，为后续动力学分析与模型设计提供理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a variational and geometric theory based on statistical physics for the joint evolution of Transformer attention and token representations at fixed model parameters. To this end, we introduce a free energy for the attention and model the evolution of the attention weights by its Fisher--Rao gradient flow. At fixed token representations, we prove that the softmax matrix is the unique free-energy minimiser and that every positive attention trajectory converges to it. For prescribed time-dependent representations, we show that the attention dynamics becomes linear after a change to logit variables. This result yields tracking bounds relative to the instantaneous softmax and sufficient conditions for attention to converge to a fixed matrix. Hamiltonian dynamics for the representations and their momenta are then coupled to the attention relaxation driven by the same energy. This coupling results in an energy-dissipation law. We also develop two alternative Hamiltonians incorporating the attention value map. We prove global well-posedness of the attention flow and all three coupled systems.

</details>


### [199] [Spectral Bias Reduction in Local Gated ReLU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10700)
*Victorita Dolean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究浅层 ReLU 模型中的谱偏差问题，提出"门控局部 ReLU 字典"（ReLU 特征乘以 hat 单位分解权重），从 Gram 矩阵角度统一分析 L² 回归与 H¹ 能量的学习率谱。结果显示门控能将 L² 情形下频率相关学习率的离散度从 1.9×10⁵ 降至 4.5；但 H¹ 刚度矩阵的符号在零频消失，门控无法消除 Θ(N²) 条件数并在频域上反转序，需配合基于同一内部模板的预条件子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度法训练的神经网络先学低频后学高频，这种谱偏差已被广泛记录，但哪些架构选择能缓解、哪些不能仍不清楚。作者希望在同一理论框架下统一刻画架构（门控局部化）与损失形式（L² 与 H¹）对频率相关学习率的影响。

Method: 针对参数线性的浅层 ReLU 模型，每个模式的学习率由 Gram 矩阵特征值决定；对一般对称双线性形式 a(·,·) 统一分析，L² 内积对应最小二乘回归、H¹ 能量形式对应变分/物理信息损失。提出门控局部 ReLU 字典：每个 ReLU 特征乘以 hat 单位分解权重（类似单位分解有限元与重叠 Schwarz 方法）。证明所得 Gram 矩阵对任意局部双线性形式均为块带状，内部为块 Toeplitz，其矩阵值符号决定谱。

Result: L² 回归情形：符号有正下界，条件数比与窗口数无关，匹配模型规模下频率相关学习率的离散度从 1.9×10⁵ 降到 4.5。H¹ 刚度矩阵情形：符号在零频处消失，门控仍保留 Θ(N²) 条件数，并反转频率序而非消除偏差；需用同一内部模板构造预条件子才能修复谱偏差。1D 回归与 Poisson 问题实验验证了这种二分性；当隐藏层参数也被训练时，回归效果依然保持。

Conclusion: 门控局部化能显著缓解 L² 回归中的谱偏差，但对 H¹ 型损失无效甚至会反转频率序，说明仅靠架构不足，必须结合相应预条件。该框架为区分"架构可解决"与"需算法配合"的谱偏差来源提供了统一判据。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient-based methods learn the low-frequency content of a target function faster than its high-frequency content. This spectral bias is well documented, but it is much less clear which architectural choices mitigate it and which cannot. We study the question for shallow ReLU models linear in their trainable parameters, where each mode is learned at a rate given by a Gram-matrix eigenvalue, and for a general symmetric bilinear form $a(\cdot,\cdot)$, so that least-squares regression ($a=$ the $L^2$ inner product) and variational or physics-informed losses ($a=$ the $H^1$ energy form) are two instances of one analysis.
  Within this framework we propose gated local ReLU dictionaries, obtained by multiplying each ReLU feature by a hat partition-of-unity weight, in the spirit of partition-of-unity finite elements and overlapping Schwarz methods. The resulting Gram matrices are block-banded for any local bilinear form, with a block-Toeplitz interior whose matrix-valued symbol governs the spectrum.
  The two forms then behave very differently. For $L^2$ regression, the symbol is bounded away from zero, the ratio is uniform in the number of windows, and the measured spread of frequency-dependent learning rates falls from $1.9\times10^{5}$ to $4.5$ at matched model size. For the $H^1$ stiffness matrix, the symbol vanishes at zero frequency: gating leaves the $Θ(N^2)$ conditioning intact and inverts the frequency ordering instead of removing the bias. A preconditioner built from the same interior stencil will fix the spectral bias, showing that architecture alone is not enough. Experiments on $1$D regression and Poisson problems confirm the dichotomy, and the regression effect persists when the hidden-layer parameters are trained as well.

</details>


### [200] [AbductionBench: Benchmarking Abductive Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10705)
*Moein Salimi,Amir Qeysarbeigi,Babak Hosseini Mohtasham,Faraz Doagoye Tehrani,Mahdi Naieni,Hanieh Sartipi,Ali Nouri,Amin Aghakasiri,Reyhane Khayatzade Mahani,Mahdi Jafari Siavoshani,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架 AbductionBench，将溯因推理拆解为生成解释、判别假设、修订假设、选择下一步证据等能力，在 27 个数据集（10 个领域）上评测 8 个 LLM，并区分最终答案评估与可观测推理轨迹/交互历史分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溯因推理能力是多方面的，但现有评测分散在任务形式与评分流程各异的孤立基准上，导致"模型擅长溯因"的结论可能只反映能力的某一狭窄侧面，缺乏统一可比且兼具过程视角的评估框架。

Method: 构建基于分类学（taxonomy）的统一评测框架 AbductionBench，并实例化到 27 个数据集、10 个领域；评测 4 个模型家族的 8 个 LLM，任务统一为单项选择；将最终答案评估与对推理轨迹、交互历史的分析分离，把轨迹视为可观测输出而非内部计算的忠实反映，分析维度包括证据使用、假设比较、不确定性、矛盾处理与答案承诺。

Result: Jev 在证据较短的解释选择任务上与小型 LLM 持平或更优，但在长案例上明显落后，说明候选判别与长证据整合对系统的要求不同；交互任务中，被判定为相关的动作比例随步数增加而下降，且首个问题相关与最终答案正确相关联；在被动任务中，模型很少随新证据到达而修订初始假设。

Conclusion: 溯因推理评估应同时考察最终答案与可观测的证据使用、假设修订和信息获取过程；AbductionBench 提供统一框架以揭示单答案评测所掩盖的能力差异。

Abstract: Abductive reasoning is the inference of plausible explanations for observed evidence. Evaluating this capability requires more than a single task: a system may need to generate an explanation, discriminate among competing hypotheses, revise its hypotheses as evidence arrives, or decide what evidence to seek next. Yet these capabilities are typically evaluated through isolated benchmarks with different task formulations and scoring procedures, so claims that a model is ``good at abduction'' may reflect only a narrow aspect of this capability. We introduce AbductionBench, a unified framework whose taxonomy characterizes abductive tasks. We instantiate this framework on 27 datasets spanning ten domains and evaluate eight LLMs from four model families evaluated on single-choice selection tasks. Beyond harmonizing heterogeneous tasks, AbductionBench separates final-answer evaluation from analysis of the observable reasoning traces and interaction histories produced alongside those answers. We analyze written traces as observable model outputs rather than faithful representations of internal computation, characterizing evidence use, hypothesis comparison, uncertainty, contradiction handling, and answer commitment. Jev matches or exceeds small LLMs when selecting among explanations over compact evidence but falls substantially behind on longer cases, suggesting that candidate discrimination and extended evidence integration place different demands on a system. In interactive tasks, the proportion of actions judged relevant declines at later steps, and a relevant first question is associated with a correct final answer; in the passive task, models rarely revise their initial hypothesis as new evidence arrives. Together, these findings highlight the value of evaluating abductive reasoning through both final answers and observable evidence use, hypothesis revision, and information gathering.

</details>


### [201] [FedSGN: Federated Sparse Grassmann Newton Learning for IoT Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10718)
*Jingjing Liu,Xiansen Ju,Xianchao Xiu,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦稀疏Grassmann牛顿学习法（FedSGN），用ℓ2,0范数约束消除特征冗余，用于IoT异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦PCA方法虽能在不共享原始数据的前提下学习正常样本子空间，但存在特征冗余问题，会降低异常检测性能。

Method: 在全局投影矩阵上施加ℓ2,0范数约束以消除冗余，并引入正交性惩罚以保持内在结构；设计增广拉格朗日求解器，结合半光滑牛顿方案，实现客户端并行更新与Grassmann投影以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在获得良好整体检测性能的同时降低了通信开销；源代码已公开。

Conclusion: FedSGN通过稀疏约束与Grassmann优化的联邦框架，有效缓解特征冗余，兼顾检测性能与通信效率，适用于IoT异常检测场景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in securing the Internet of things (IoT). Although existing federated principal component analysis (PCA) methods can learn the subspace of normal samples without sharing raw data, they often suffer from feature redundancy that may degrade detection performance. To address this limitation, we propose a novel and effective federated sparse Grassmann Newton (FedSGN) learning method for IoT anomaly detection. Technically, our FedSGN leverages the $\ell_{2,0}$-norm constraint on the global projection matrix to eliminate redundancy and introduces an orthogonality penalty to preserve the intrinsic structure. Moreover, we develop an augmented Lagrangian solver integrated with a semismooth Newton scheme to enable parallel client updates and Grassmann projection for robustness. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves favorable overall detection performance while reducing communication overhead. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/FedSGN

</details>


### [202] [Breaking Precision Walls in First-, Second-, and Zeroth-Order Optimizers: One Constant, Set in Advance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10730)
*Lngel Kyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在幂律信号衰减下，优化器的平均损失会掩盖弱方向恢复失败的问题；本文用"精度墙"刻画最弱方向在固定预算内恢复所需的衰减临界点，并给出用单一常数设定该墙的规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均损失无法暴露弱方向拟合失败，需要量化"最强与最弱方向条件差异"下的可优化极限，并理解 Adam、Shampoo 嫁接、零阶 Adam（ε 参数）等优化器中决定该极限的来源。

Method: 定义精度墙：最弱方向在固定预算内收敛到 5% 误差所对应的最陡信号衰减率。通过低秩矩阵感知与二次问题分析：证明某常数一旦主导分母，Adam 与嫁接 Shampoo 退化为步长 η/c 的动量下降并由此设定墙；在 Shampoo 中将嫁接常数与阻尼匹配；零阶 Adam 中由均方谱模型推导 ε*，并与 ε 对比。用真实曲率/谱在新任务形状上设定规则，并与同步长默认、余弦调度、按预算重调步长等基线比较。

Result: 按规则设定常数使精度墙相对同步长默认提升：两个感知形状上 Adam 提升 1.96、1.15，嫁接 Shampoo 提升 1.59、1.74；两个 Hadamard 旋转二次问题上零阶 Adam 提升 1.12、0.73（更长预算），且所有随机种子均提升；同步长动量可达到这些墙。余弦调度默认在某一感知形状更高、在另一形状新种子上持平、在规则常数下更低。按预算重调默认步长在旋转二次与总体逻辑回归上追平，但在两个对角二次上 1 query/step 失败（16 时追平），并在 digits 逻辑回归（全批与 mini-batch）上超过规则。一阶逻辑回归上规则未能提升墙，同步长默认 Adam 更高。

Conclusion: 精度墙为"弱方向恢复"提供了可预测的量化指标；由真实曲率/谱推导的常数可前瞻性地抬升墙并给出可预测的增幅，但存在边界（一阶逻辑回归、小样本对角二次、梯度噪声主导的情形）。

Abstract: Under power-law signal decay across directions, an optimizer can fit strong directions and miss weak ones while average loss hides it. A precision wall measures the steepest decay where the weakest direction recovers within 5% in a fixed budget. On low-rank matrix sensing and quadratics one constant sets the wall: once it dominates the denominator, Adam or grafted Shampoo is momentum descent at step $η/c$, setting the wall; in Shampoo, matching grafting's constant to damping restores the wall after three hypotheses failed. In zeroth-order Adam, $\varepsilon$ acts against $\varepsilon^\ast$ from a mean-square spectral model; below $\varepsilon^\ast$ error hits a noise floor. Set from true curvature or spectrum on new shapes, the rule raised the wall above the same-step default by a forecast amount: on two sensing shapes by $1.96, 1.15$ (Adam) and $1.59, 1.74$ (grafted Shampoo), and on two Hadamard-rotated quadratics by $1.12, 0.73$ (zeroth-order Adam, longer budget), rising on all seeds; fixed-step momentum at that step reaches those walls. A cosine-scheduled default (small constant giving weak modes large steps) is higher on one sensing shape, matched on new seeds of the other, and lower with the rule's constant. A default step retuned per budget catches up on rotated quadratics and population logistic regression, fails at 1 query/step on two diagonal quadratics (catching up at 16), and exceeds the rule on digits logistic regression (full-batch and minibatches, where gradient noise sets the $η= 0.01, 0.1$ default walls). On logistic regression the rule did not raise the first-order wall: same-step default Adam was higher (its wall on 4 new shapes at the forecast stability limit), while in zeroth order on the population loss setting $\varepsilon$ raised the wall by $0.98, 0.88$.

</details>


### [203] [Spectrogram-Based CNN Autoencoder for One-Class Anomaly Detection in Machining Vibration Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10733)
*Sebastian Zeleny,Christian Gawron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于卷积自编码器的一类异常检测框架，将三轴振动信号转为时频谱三通道表示，仅用正常数据训练，以重建误差检测异常行程，并引入分段垂直误差（SVE）指标提升对颤振等短时失稳的敏感性；约1万次拉削行程实验显示正常与异常状态清晰分离，并在公开车削数据集上验证了初步可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业加工过程的振动状态监测面临异常样本稀缺、类别极度不平衡的问题，且颤振等短时失稳在整体重建误差中易被稀释；需要一种仅依赖正常数据、可扩展且对短时不稳定敏感的一类异常检测方案。

Method: 采集工业拉削过程中的三轴振动信号，转换为时频谱并组合为紧凑的三通道表示，以便在多元时间序列上进行卷积特征学习；构建卷积自编码器，仅在正常过程数据上训练，依据重建误差判别异常行程；用有标签验证集校准阈值，保留独立测试集做最终评估；并提出基于局部残差的分段垂直误差（SVE）指标以增强对短时过程失稳的检测敏感性。

Result: 在约10,000次拉削行程上，正常与异常工况在强类别不平衡条件下呈现清晰分离；处理流程在外部公开车削数据集上的概念验证表明其在原始拉削场景之外具有初步适用性。

Conclusion: 基于时频谱的自编码器模型可为工业振动监测中的一类异常检测提供实用且可扩展的基础；SVE类局部残差指标有助于捕捉短时失稳，且方法具备向其他加工场景迁移的潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a one-class anomaly detection framework for vibration-based condition monitoring in machining processes using a convolutional neural network autoencoder. Three-axis vibration signals acquired during industrial broaching are transformed into time--frequency spectrograms and combined into a compact three-channel representation, enabling convolutional feature learning on multivariate time-series data. The autoencoder is trained exclusively on normal process data and detects anomalous strokes based on reconstruction errors. Labeled validation data are used for threshold calibration and an independent test set is reserved for final performance evaluation. To improve sensitivity to short-lived process instabilities such as chatter, a localized residual-based metric termed Segmented Vertical Error (SVE) is introduced. Experimental results on approximately 10{,}000 broaching strokes show a clear separation between normal and anomalous process conditions under strong class imbalance. The processing pipeline is additionally evaluated on an external public turning dataset as a proof of concept, indicating preliminary applicability beyond the original broaching setup. The results suggest that spectrogram-based autoencoder models can provide a practical and scalable basis for one-class anomaly detection in industrial vibration monitoring.

</details>


### [204] [AERO: Adaptive Ensemble-Disagreement Routing for Oracle Feedback in Sample-Efficient Online RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10750)
*Shrey Singhal,Nannan Huang,Haytham M. Fayek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AERO：基于Dyna式模型化RLHF的完全在线算法，用集成认知奖励模型（ERM）的投票熵加自适应阈值路由，仅在信息量最大的上下文查询oracle，其余用ERM合成偏好标签，在降低oracle预算的同时取得有竞争力的对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中偏好标签获取成本是主要瓶颈。现有主动探索方法虽提升样本效率，但对所有上下文均匀地查询oracle，忽略了部分上下文奖励估计已足够可靠、可自行合成标签，导致标注预算浪费。

Method: 构建Dyna风格的模型化在线强化学习架构；训练集成式Epistemic Reward Model（ERM），同时充当探索用的奖励后验与偏好反馈的生成模型；以集成投票熵（Query-by-Committee式分歧度量）结合自适应阈值机制，判定各上下文是请求oracle反馈还是使用ERM合成标签，从而把昂贵标注集中于高信息量上下文。

Result: 在多种模型家族与规模上的实验显示，AERO的对齐性能与在线DPO及强主动探索基线高度相当，同时显著减少所需的oracle查询预算。

Conclusion: 将"是否查询oracle"视为可自适应的路由决策，并用集成不确定性指导，可在不牺牲对齐质量的前提下提升偏好标注效率；模型化合成标签与主动查询互补，是降低RLHF标注成本的有效途径。

Abstract: Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences via Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has driven substantial improvements across a wide range of tasks, but the cost of acquiring preference labels remains the dominant bottleneck in the alignment pipeline. While many recent alignment methods improve sample efficiency through active exploration over candidate responses, they typically query the oracle uniformly across contexts, even for contexts where the learned reward estimates are already reliable enough to provide synthetic labels. We propose Adaptive Ensemble-disagreement Routing for Oracle feedback (AERO), a fully online RLHF algorithm built on a Dyna-style model-based RL architecture, in which an ensemble-based Epistemic Reward Model (ERM) serves both as a reward posterior for exploration and as a learned preference-feedback model for synthetic labeling. AERO uses ensemble vote entropy, a Query-by-Committee disagreement measure, combined with an adaptive thresholding mechanism to decide which contexts require oracle feedback and which can instead receive ERM-based synthetic preference labels, concentrating expensive labels where they are most informative. Empirical results across different model families and scales show that AERO achieves highly competitive alignment performance while reducing the required oracle-query budget compared to online DPO and strong active-exploration baselines.

</details>


### [205] [Constrained Look-ahead Guidance for Interference-Aware Flow Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10751)
*Doudou Zhang,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需反演的、以轨迹为中心的前瞻式（one-step look-ahead）流模型图像编辑框架，通过评估候选更新对未来编辑/保真奖励的影响来修正当前更新，在显式保真约束下实现更好的编辑-保真权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型的生成能力强，但受控编辑困难：需在生成轨迹上平衡源图像保真与编辑遵从度。现有方法依赖反演或启发式引导，易产生误差累积且难以协调两个目标，梯度干扰明显。

Method: 从轨迹中心视角构建无反转框架。引入一步前瞻机制：对候选更新，借助去噪估计其对编辑奖励与保真奖励的未来影响，并据此调整当前更新；每一步在显式保真约束下施加奖励驱动的修正，使轨迹演化稳定连贯。前瞻设计通过预判两目标在未来状态的交互，缓解其梯度干扰。

Result: 实验表明，与现有强基线相比，该方法实现更强的语义编辑效果，同时更好地保持源图像内容。

Conclusion: 以轨迹为中心的前瞻式奖励修正可在不依赖反演与启发式引导的前提下有效改善编辑质量与保真度的权衡，为流/扩散模型的可控编辑提供了一条更稳定、更具原则性的路径。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models enable powerful generative modeling, but controlled editing remains challenging due to the need to balance source fidelity and edit compliance along the generative trajectory. We propose a principled, inversion-free framework for flow-based image editing from a trajectory centric perspective. Rather than relying on inversion or heuristic guidance, we introduce a one-step look-ahead mechanism that evaluates the effect of candidate updates on both edit and fidelity rewards estimated via denoising, and adjusts the current update based on this evaluation. At each step, our method applies reward-driven corrections guided by the look-ahead evaluation under an explicit fidelity constraint, resulting in stable and coherent trajectory evolution. This look-ahead design mitigates gradient interference between edit and fidelity objectives by anticipating their interaction in future states, leading to improved trade-offs. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves stronger semantic edits while better preserving source content compared to existing strong baselines.

</details>


### [206] [ConwayNet: Interpretability, Activation Coherence, and Noise Resilience via a Game-of-Life-Inspired Lateral Inhibition in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10760)
*Yair Cohen,Scott M. Gilmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于连续"生命游戏"规则的侧抑制机制，将元胞自动机的局部可编程动力学嵌入神经网络，规则参数固定不学习但通过其值与导数塑造训练，从而提升激活一致性、鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络依赖堆叠非线性实现通用逼近，但因此对噪声敏感且难以进行机制层面的解释；相反，以Conway生命游戏为代表的元胞自动机凭局部更新规则即可产生可解释甚至可编程的动力学。作者希望在这两个世界之间架起桥梁。

Method: 设计一种由连续生命游戏规则导出的侧抑制：依据层输入同时生成抑制性与兴奋性响应，并以状态条件方式将其混合进激活图。该机制可微、与架构无关；其调节是"施加"而非"学习"的——规则参数固定不参与优化，但规则取值与导数在全网络训练中持续塑造梯度。方法被接入全连接、卷积、宽残差与图卷积网络。

Result: 激活图在空间、深度与随机种子上变得连贯：粗结构贯穿深度保持，细粒度细节在每层更新。隐单元共激活图偏离Erdős–Rényi随机图，呈现分布于图像上的空间图特征，其巨型连通分量呈阶梯式增长。在标签噪声下，由近乎相同初始化出发的网络在测试图像上的分歧减少约三十倍。显著图聚焦于目标而非背景，对训练中未见的像素噪声的鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: 将元胞自动机式固定局部规则作为侧抑制先验注入网络，可在不增加可学习参数的前提下带来激活结构规整、预测一致性、可解释性与噪声鲁棒性等多重收益，说明"施加而非学习"的调节是一种有效且有前景的设计范式。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve universal function approximation by stacking interconnected nonlinearities, but as a consequence they are sensitive to noise and resist mechanistic interpretation. In contrast, the local update rules of cellular automata, such as Conway's Game of Life, give rise to not merely interpretable but programmable dynamics. We explore a bridge between these worlds: a lateral inhibition in neural networks based on continuous Game-of-Life rules that derives inhibitory and excitatory responses from the layer input and blends them into the activation map in a state-conditioned way. The mechanism is differentiable and architecture-agnostic, and its regulation is imposed rather than learned: the rule's parameters are fixed and are not optimised, while the rule's values and derivatives shape training throughout the network. Wired into fully connected, convolutional, wide-residual and graph convolutional networks, it gives rise to several properties. Activation maps become coherent across space, depth and seeds, with coarse structure persisting through depth while fine detail is renewed at every layer. The co-activation graph of the hidden units departs from an Erdős--Rényi random graph: it is laid out on the image and its giant component grows in steps, as in a spatial graph. Networks started from nearly identical initialisations, under label noise, disagree on thirty times fewer test images. Saliency maps rest on the object rather than the background, and resilience to pixel noise not seen in training is substantially improved.

</details>


### [207] [EEGFaceSem: An EEG Benchmark with Paired Generative Latents for Semantic Visual Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10781)
*Jun Ma,Michiel M. Spapé,Keith M. Davis,Tuukka Ruotsalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEGFaceSem：大规模EEG-合成人脸数据集，30名被试、67,200条锁时EEG记录，配生成潜向量，支持语义级单试次分类与脑监督图像编辑/生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究视觉语义与神经响应的工作依赖固定图像集合，缺乏对细粒度视觉特征变化的可控性，难以分离特征变化及其与感知的关系。

Method: 用生成模型合成高质量真实感人脸图像并作为刺激，提供每个刺激的生成潜向量；被试在快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP）范式中注意特定语义特征（如微笑、发色），同步采集EEG；构建单试次语义分类基准（单被试、跨被试、被试自适应）及跨被试投票聚合；并展示两项应用：在潜空间中推导语义方向用于脑监督图像编辑，以及以脑解码标签conditioning生成模型实现脑监督图像生成。

Result: 数据集含30名被试共67,200条锁时EEG记录，并给出多设定下的单试次语义分类基准与跨被试投票结果，验证了潜空间-神经表征连续映射及脑监督编辑/生成的可行性。

Conclusion: 该数据集为语义级视觉显著性检测的BCI及脑监督生成建模提供基础，数据与代码已在OSF与HuggingFace公开。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used to measure brain activity for brain-computer interface applications for its non-invasive nature and high temporal resolution. However, progress in modeling the neural responses to visual semantics has been limited by a reliance on fixed image collections that lack the fine-grained control needed to isolate fine-grained changes in visual features and their relation to perception. In this work, we present EEGFaceSem, a large-scale dataset pairing high-quality EEG with photorealistic face images synthesized from a generative model. We provide the generative latent vector for each stimulus, enabling research into the continuous mapping between a visual latent space and neural representations. Each participant's brain responses were recorded while they attended to specific semantic features (e.g., smiling, hair color) in a rapid serial visual presentation paradigm. The dataset contains EEG signals from 30 participants, totaling 67,200 time-locked EEG recordings. We present benchmarks for single-trial semantic classification under single-subject, cross-subject, and subject-adapted settings, as well as for cross-subject voting that aggregates per-stimulus predictions across participants. We further demonstrate the dataset's unique utility with two complementary applications: deriving semantic directions in the latent space for brain-supervised image editing, and conditioning a generative model on brain-decoded labels for brain-supervised image generation. Our dataset paves the way for advancing brain-computer interfaces (BCI) for semantic-level visual saliency detection and brain-supervised generative modeling. Our dataset and code are openly released at OSF (https://osf.io/2guk3/) and at HuggingFace https://huggingface.co/datasets/yefllower/EEGFaceSem.

</details>


### [208] [Proximal Preference Optimization: A Unified Framework for Stable and Reward-Free Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10785)
*Qisai Liu,Zhanhong Jiang,Zahid Hasan,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"近端偏好优化"统一框架，将DPO的隐式奖励与PPO的信任域稳定性结合，衍生P²O与PKTO两个算法，理论上有KL界、策略改进下界与收敛保证，实验中在三个偏好基准上匹配或超越DPO/KTO且数据集重复使用时更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO式隐式奖励方法无需奖励模型、实现简单，但缺乏PPO的信任域约束，在数据集重复多轮使用时易出现策略漂移与不稳定；希望在保持无奖励模型优势的同时引入近端/信任域稳定性。

Method: 构建统一近端偏好优化框架：P²O在DPO偏好间隔中直接引入裁剪的重要性比率，得到无奖励模型、支持安全数据复用且有界策略散度与单调偏好改进保证的算法；PKTO进一步以KTO的前景理论效用间隔替换DPO间隔，加入随策略漂移自适应收紧有效信任域的自调节基线，以及处理不平衡/非对称偏好数据的非对称加权；并给出KL信任域界、策略改进下界与多轮近端更新下的收敛性统一分析。

Result: 在多个大模型、三个偏好基准上，P²O与PKTO的对齐质量达到或超过DPO与KTO，并在数据集重复复用条件下保持更好的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 近端信任域机制可作为通用组件统一DPO类与PPO类目标，在免奖励模型的对齐中同时获得理论可证的安全性与实证稳定性，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce a proximal preference optimization framework that unifies the implicit reward formulation of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) with the trust-region stability of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Our framework yields two complementary algorithms. P$^2$O (Proximal Preference Optimization) introduces a clipped importance ratio directly into the DPO preference margin, producing a reward-model-free algorithm that supports safe dataset reuse, bounded policy divergence, and monotonic preference improvement guarantees. PKTO (Proximal Kahneman-Tversky Optimization) extends P$^2$O by replacing the DPO margin with KTO's prospect-theoretic utility margins, adding a self-regulating adaptive baseline that tightens the effective trust region as the policy drifts, and an asymmetric weighting that handles imbalanced or asymmetric preference datasets. We provide a unified theoretical analysis, including Kullback Leibler (KL) trust-region bounds, a policy-improvement lower bound, and convergence guarantees under multi-epoch proximal updates. Empirically, P$^2$O and PKTO match or exceed DPO and KTO in alignment quality while maintaining better stability under repeated dataset reuse across multiple LLMs on three preference benchmarks. The code base is available here.

</details>


### [209] [Generating Diverse and Verified Synthetic Clinical Notes with Labels by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10789)
*Matthias Lehmann,Madeleine Werthebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPARCLING：基于大语言模型的流水线，合成带字符级精确PHI标注的德语出院小结，用于构建去标识化基准并提升模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床领域受严格隐私法规约束，去标识化是开展NLP等数据利用的前提；但现有方法难以判断去标识化模型是否真正适用，缺乏既有挑战性、多样性、临床合理性又保证标签正确性的基准，且公开数据稀缺。

Method: SPARCLING流水线结合三部分：结构化属性采样、模板生成、红队测试（red teaming），由LLM生成合成出院小结，并附带字符级完全对齐（character-exact）的PHI跨度标注。

Result: 实验表明合成数据在内容多样性上优于现有公开数据集；作为基准可揭示德语临床去标识器的失效模式（下游效用）；仅用合成数据训练可得到比仅用稀缺公开数据训练更强的去标识器。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的合成数据生成可作为临床去标识化评估的高质量、多样化且标签可靠的基准来源，并能有效补充稀缺真实数据以提升模型性能；代码已开源。

Abstract: Under strict privacy regulations in the clinical domain, de-identification is necessary to enable the use of clinical data such as for natural language processing. However, we currently lack sophisticated ways to tell which de-identification models actually are suitable. Specifically, we need benchmarks that are challenging, diverse, and clinically plausible, all while ensuring label correctness. To this end, we present SPARCLING, an LLM-based pipeline to generate synthetic discharge letters with character-exact PHI span annotations. Our pipeline combines structured attribute sampling, template generation, and red teaming to obtain diverse PHI-labeled discharge letters. In our experiments, we demonstrate this superior content diversity over existing public datasets. Further, we show SPARCLING's downstream utility as a benchmark to uncover failure modes of de-identifiers in the German clinical domain. Finally, our experiments show that training on our synthetic data can yield more capable de-identifiers than training on the scarce public data alone. Our code is available at https://github.com/Matt00n/phi_discharge_letter_generator.

</details>


### [210] [Zeroth-Order Riemannian Optimization on Fixed-Rank Manifolds for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10792)
*Xinjue Wang,Xiuheng Wang,Yejun Zhang,Sergiy A. Vorobyov,Esa Ollila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在固定秩矩阵流形上约束加性更新、保持预训练权重冻结的零阶黎曼优化方法ZO-RGD及其平滑变体ZO-RGD-S，用于大模型内存高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩零阶优化方法存在两类问题：一是用欧氏因子参数化加性更新，表示不唯一；二是把每个有限差分方向限制为低秩，无法保证学习到的更新在微调全程保持秩约束。因此希望在流形几何上直接约束加性更新为固定秩。

Method: 将每个加性更新块约束在固定秩矩阵流形上，冻结预训练权重；在该几何上构造黎曼零阶梯度估计器：把随机矩阵投影到切空间，并做归一化以校正投影带来的尺度失配；据此提出零阶黎曼梯度下降ZO-RGD及平滑版本ZO-RGD-S。

Result: 理论分析表明：环境空间有限差分与其回缩（retraction）对应量一致；归一化估计器在期望意义下可恢复黎曼梯度；ZO-RGD在轨迹正则条件下收敛。在LLM微调任务上，所提算法在不同模型族与任务上可与强前向-only基线竞争，并在若干设置中取得提升。

Conclusion: 通过流形几何直接施加固定秩约束，配合归一化的切空间零阶估计器，可在不存储激活的前提下实现有效且内存友好的LLM微调。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization estimates descent directions from function values alone, which removes the activation storage required by backpropagation and makes it attractive for memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing low-rank ZO methods either parameterize the additive update through Euclidean factors with a non-unique representation, or restrict each finite-difference direction to be low-rank. In this work, we propose to constrain each additive update block to a fixed-rank matrix manifold while keeping the pretrained weights frozen, so the learned update remains rank-constrained throughout fine-tuning. On this geometry, we build a Riemannian ZO gradient estimator that projects a random matrix onto the tangent space, normalizes it to correct the scale mismatch induced by projection, and further propose Zeroth-order Riemannian Gradient Descent (ZO-RGD) and its smoothed variant, ZO-RGD-S. We show that the ambient finite difference matches its retracted counterpart, the normalized estimator recovers the Riemannian gradient in expectation, and ZO-RGD converges under trajectory-regularity conditions. Experiments on LLM fine-tuning tasks show that the proposed algorithms are competitive with strong forward-only baselines across model families and tasks, with gains in several settings.

</details>


### [211] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Internal Computation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10822)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应内部计算（AIC），一种能学习"何时停止细化潜在表示并行动"的迭代策略；通过联合训练动作选择与停止决策、期望深度惩罚和中间价值辅助监督，在最多8步细化内平均仅用3.3步，控制与机器人任务上单决策延迟降低31–44%，并在多数任务上不劣于最佳固定深度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式神经策略对每个决策都投入相同计算量，但不同状态从额外计算中获益程度并不相同。作者先证明：当只有部分状态能从进一步细化中获益时，在相同期望深度下，理想的状态依赖分配器优于任何状态无关（固定调度）的停止策略——这为"自适应停止"提供了理论动机。

Method: AIC 在迭代细化框架中联合训练动作选择与停止决策：用期望深度惩罚（expected-depth penalty）为计算量定价，并以中间价值预测（intermediate value predictions）提供辅助监督；每个决策最多允许8个细化步。

Result: 在12个基准任务上平均执行3.3步；在4个控制与机器人任务上相比固定8步策略降低单决策延迟31–44%；平均回报在7个任务上超过最佳固定深度策略、在4个任务上略逊；消融显示：PickCube与AntMaze上回报增益主要来自自适应停止（且这些任务中执行了更多细化步），RareEvent上主要来自价值监督。

Conclusion: 状态依赖的计算分配是可行且有效的：学习到的停止机制在显著降低平均计算量与延迟的同时保持或提升性能，且停止与价值监督在不同任务中贡献互补，说明"何时停止"与"如何细化"应分别设计与评估。

Abstract: Iterative neural policies usually spend the same computation on every decision, although the benefit of additional processing varies across states. We introduce Adaptive Internal Computation (AIC), an iterative policy that learns when to stop refining its latent representation and act. In AIC, we train action selection and stopping jointly, an expected-depth penalty values computation and intermediate value predictions provide auxiliary supervision. We show that when only some states benefit from further refinement, an ideal state-dependent allocator outperforms every state-independent stopping schedule at the same expected depth. With at most eight refinement steps per decision, AIC executes 3.3 steps on average across twelve benchmark tasks and lowers per-decision latency by 31--44% relative to a fixed eight-step policy on four control and robotics tasks. Its mean reward exceeds the best fixed-depth policy on seven tasks and trails it on four. Ablating adaptive stopping and value supervision separately shows that stopping accounts for most of the reward gain on PickCube and AntMaze, where it also executes more refinement steps and that supervision accounts for most of it on RareEvent.

</details>


### [212] [Learning Biological Hierarchies in Single-Cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10838)
*Xiangyu Guo,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将细胞本体（cell ontology）层级结构嵌入表示学习的生物信息学基础模型 scOntoFM，借助LCA距离的离线三元组采样与层级本体学习，同时保留局部邻域并塑造反映本体结构的全局嵌入几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞基础模型普遍忽略细胞本体中编码的细胞类型谱系（taxonomic/lineage）关系，导致嵌入空间未能反映细胞类型的层级组织。

Method: scOntoFM 将层级式细胞本体嵌入表示学习：利用本体图中的最低共同祖先（LCA）距离，结合高效的离线三元组采样与层级细胞本体学习，联合保持局部邻域并塑造符合本体结构的全局嵌入几何。

Result: 在多样化的细胞层级与基因层级基准上性能一致提升，零样本（zero-shot）场景增益尤为显著；同时在批次整合与生物结构保持之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 将本体知识注入表示学习是有前景的方向，为现有嵌入评估框架提供互补视角，并支持可扩展的生物发现。

Abstract: Cell types are organized by taxonomic relationships that define lineages in the so-called cell ontology. However, most existing foundation models typically ignore cell-type lineages encoded in this cell ontology. We introduce a biologically informed foundation model called scOntoFM, which embeds hierarchical cell ontology into representation learning. Using Lowest Common Ancestor (LCA) distances from the ontology graph, our framework pairs efficient offline triplet sampling with hierarchical cell-ontology learning. This approach jointly preserves local neighborhoods and shapes the global embedding geometry to reflect the ontology structure. Across diverse cell- and gene-level benchmarks, the model consistently improves performance, with especially strong gains in zero-shot settings. Our model also balances batch integration with biological structure preservation, providing complementary perspectives to existing embedding evaluation frameworks and supporting scalable biological discovery.

</details>


### [213] [Representing Tensors as Compositions for Efficient Computation on Sparse and Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10855)
*Valérian Rey,Praneth Paruchuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种张量代数形式化框架：将张量视为从索引到值的函数，把逐元素运算、重索引、归约、堆叠统一表示为函数复合，从而将张量计算引擎的中间表示建立在"索引函数代数"之上，实现不求值即可重写表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代张量框架中，每种张量类型（稠密、稀疏各形态、不规则等）与每种运算组合都要单独实现，代码量随"运算数 m × 张量类型数 n"平方增长，维护困难且难以覆盖稀疏 × 稀疏 einsum 等组合。

Method: 定义张量为索引集到值的函数。逐元素运算=值上函数复合；重索引（reshape、转置）=索引上函数复合；归约=索引函数（把单索引映射为多索引）与归约算子（sum、prod、max）的复合；再结合张量堆叠，可表达稠密、锯齿、任意稀疏模式、矩阵分解以及惰性（lazy）版本运算，包括任意张量间的 einsum。利用复合之间的恒等式可在不求值情况下做表达式重写。

Result: 通过复合恒等式重写，例如将稀疏矩阵乘积改写为仅在非零元素间计算，把稀疏问题转换为稠密问题；理论上将代码库复杂度从 O(mn²) 降至 O(m + k²)，其中 k 为索引函数类型的数量。作者提供了该代数的张量计算框架草案实现。

Conclusion: 以该代数为中间表示，可将研究重点从"在张量上定义运算"转向"在索引函数上定义代数"，显著降低引擎实现复杂度，并天然支持惰性求值与跨张量类型的统一优化。

Abstract: We propose a new mathematical formalism in which all kinds of tensors and of operations on those tensors are represented as simple function compositions. Tensors are functions from indices to values. An element-wise operation (e.g. an activation function) is a composition with a function applied to values; re-indexing (e.g. reshape, transposition) is a composition with a function applied to indices; and a reduction is a composition with both an index function, mapping each index to several indices, and a reduction operation (e.g. product, sum, max). Combined with tensor stacking, these compositions can represent most interesting kinds of tensors (e.g. dense, jagged, all kinds of sparsity patterns, matrix factorizations), as well as lazy versions of almost every operation available in modern tensor computation frameworks, including einsums between tensors of any kind. Identities between compositions can then be used to rewrite such expressions into equivalent ones, without evaluating them. For instance, the product of two sparse matrices can be rewritten so that only products between non-zero values are computed, turning a sparse problem into a dense one. Using this algebra as the intermediate representation of a tensor computation engine would shift the focus from defining operations on tensors (e.g. einsum between sparse tensors) to defining an algebra on index functions. This should reduce the codebase complexity from $\mathcal{O}(mn^2)$, with $m$ the number of binary operations between tensors and $n$ the number of tensor types, to $\mathcal{O}(m + k^2)$, with $k$ the number of types of index functions. A draft implementation of a tensor computation framework based on this algebra is publicly available.

</details>


### [214] [Self-contained Data Cleaning for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10856)
*Jordan F. Masakuna,Ahmed Yacine,Djeff K. Nkashama,Arian Soltani,Marc Frappier,Pierre M. Tardif,Froduald Kabanza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自包含的预处理框架，利用自编码器早期训练阶段样本与其重建之间的余弦相似度进行动态分析，无需已知污染比例即可识别并"硬清除"训练集中的异常污染，从而在数据层面提升下游检测模型的安全性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测常假设训练集无异常，但实际数据常含恶意或异常污染，严重削弱下游模型可靠性与安全性；现有抗污染方法跨架构泛化差，且依赖先验的污染比例，而该信息在真实安全场景中难以获得。

Method: 构建自包含预处理框架：在自编码器训练早期，对每个样本与其重建的余弦相似度进行动态分析，将样本映射到动态空间，使异常区域自然呈现低密度；据此识别异常候选并执行"硬清除"（从训练池中移除全部候选），在数据层面清洗训练集。

Result: 在8种不同模型与10个安全相关数据集上评估，最高实现100%污染削减，F1平均提升11.2%，分类准确率平均提升7.3%。

Conclusion: 基于数据层面的清洗使安全性与可靠性提升可无缝泛化至多种下游异常检测架构，且无需先验污染比例，适用于真实安全运维场景。

Abstract: Training datasets for unsupervised anomaly detection are often assumed to be anomaly-free, but in practice, they frequently contain malicious or anomalous contamination. This contamination severely degrades the dependability and security of downstream detection models. While some contamination-robust methods exist, they rarely generalize across different model architectures and typically rely on a priori knowledge of the contamination ratio\textemdash information that is rarely available in real-world security operations. In this paper, we propose a novel, self-contained preprocessing framework for anomaly detection that identifies and removes training set contamination without relying on known contamination ratios. Our approach performs a dynamic analysis of the cosine similarity between each data point and its reconstruction during the early training stages of an auto-encoder, mapping points into a dynamic space where anomalous regions naturally exhibit lower density. By executing ``hard cleaning''\textemdash removing all identified anomalous candidates from the training pool\textemdash we systematically improve data quality. Because this cleaning occurs at the data level, the security and reliability improvements generalize seamlessly across various downstream anomaly detection architectures. Evaluating our framework across 8 distinct models and 10 security-relevant datasets, our approach achieves up to 100\% contamination reduction, yielding average improvements of 11.2\% in F1 score and 7.3\% in classification accuracy.

</details>


### [215] [Structurally Separated DAG Learning with Multi-Scale Normalized Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10860)
*Shuai Feng,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMNC 多层缩放闭包 logits 监督的连续 DAG 学习：长度无关下界、多项式梯度因子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续 DAG 学习通常只用加权邻接矩阵的标量无环约束，路径证据在被聚合前被标量化，长环上的乘积式衰减使循环信号弱化。作者希望在聚合前显式表示多跳结构关系。

Method: 引入矩阵值闭包 logits 作为补充监督层，与数据拟合、稀疏项及 DAGMA 式障碍项共同训练。SMNC 在对数空间反复平方构造结构门，并按路径长度预算归一化各尺度；对角元用于逐节点环监督，非对角元提供成对可达性线索。线性模型采用有界结构—幅度分解，非线性模型用诱导门构造。

Result: 强环条件下归一化 logits 具有长度无关下界；在唯一活跃环假设下，最终尺度硬读出给出 log-gate 梯度的多项式因子，而非指数乘积衰减。在无提示、未标准化高斯线性与非线性 ER 基准上，默认超参数与缩减预算下，稠密图优于 DAGMA，稀疏图接近。

Conclusion: 将路径证据以矩阵值闭包 logits 形式在聚合前暴露并提供监督，可缓解长环衰减，为连续 DAG 学习提供互补的结构正则化视角。

Abstract: Continuous DAG learning commonly enforces acyclicity through scalar functions of a weighted adjacency that aggregate evidence from weighted closed walks. We add a complementary layer: matrix-valued closure logits---explicit multi-hop structural relation scores for each ordered pair---supervised alongside data fit, sparsity, and a DAGMA-style barrier on the weighted adjacency. The distinction is not scalarization of the overall objective but how path evidence is represented and exposed before aggregation. SMNC (scale-normalized multi-scale closure) builds these scores on a structural gate by repeated squaring in log space and divides each scale by its path-length budget, counteracting product-style attenuation along long cycles; diagonal entries support node-wise cycle supervision and off-diagonals optional pairwise reachability cues. In the linear model, a bounded structure--magnitude factorization ties strong effective edges to strong gates; a nonlinear induced gate offers another construction with the same intent. For sufficiently strong cycles, normalized logits admit a length-independent lower bound; under a unique-active-cycle assumption, the hard final-scale readout yields a polynomial log-gate gradient factor rather than exponential product-style decay. On cue-free, unstandardized Gaussian linear and nonlinear ER benchmarks, these models improve over DAGMA, run with default hyperparameters at a reduced budget, on denser graphs and are close to it on sparse ones.

</details>


### [216] [SWXAI: A Unified Multimodal Explainability Library for Space Weather Forecasting and Heliophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10861)
*Benedict Antonio Mervyn,Anli Ji,Manolis K. Georgoulis,Berkay Aydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源库 SWXAI，统一空间天气预报与太阳物理中图像、时间序列、表格数据的可解释AI（XAI）工作流，提供跨模型的可扩展接口与持久化解释对象，并以可复现示例、测试和文档支撑异构科学机器学习管线的可比较、可复用解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间天气预报与太阳物理研究日益依赖处理异构输入（多视角多波段太阳图像、栅格、多变量时间序列、空间标注、事件级表格数据）的机器学习模型，但现有可解释性工作流分散于不同库、模型接口与输出格式之间，导致复用性与可复现性受限。

Method: 构建开源库 SWXAI：为常用视觉、时间序列与表格学习模型提供统一且可扩展的公共接口，集成输入校验、模型/解释器适配机制与持久化解释对象；同时提供领域化示例、可复现 notebook、测试与文档，支持用户检查输出、替换模型或解释器，并扩展到新的太阳物理数据集；最后与各后端直接实现进行基准对比。

Result: 基准测试表明，相比直接调用后端实现，SWXAI 显著减少了用户生成解释所需的代码量，且未带来实际运行时开销。

Conclusion: SWXAI 降低了模态相关的工程开销，支持在异构科学机器学习管线中获得可比较、可复用的解释结果，可作为空间天气与太阳物理 XAI 实践的统一基础设施。

Abstract: Space weather forecasting and heliophysics increasingly rely on machine learning models that process heterogeneous inputs such as multi-viewpoint multi-wavelength solar images and rasters, multivariate time series, spatial annotations, and tabular event-level data. However, explainability workflows remain fragmented across libraries, model interfaces, and output formats, limiting reuse and reproducibility. We present SWXAI, an open-source library that unifies explainable AI (XAI) workflows for image, time-series, and tabular data in space weather forecasting and heliophysics. SWXAI provides a common, extensible interface across commonly used vision, time-series, and tabular learning models, with validation, adaptation, and persistent explanation objects. It also includes domain-grounded examples, reproducible notebooks, tests, and documentation, enabling users to inspect outputs, swap models or explainers, and extend workflows to new heliophysics datasets. SWXAI reduces modality-specific engineering overhead and supports comparable, reusable explanations across heterogeneous scientific machine learning pipelines. A benchmark against direct backend implementations shows that SWXAI substantially reduces the code users write to produce an explanation, with no practical runtime cost.

</details>


### [217] [Stochastic Port-Hamiltonian Gaussian Processes for Learning from Noisy Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10864)
*Thribhuvan Rapolu,Luke McLennan,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LieSPHGP：一个在Lie群/Lie代数上表示刚体运动、带端口Hamilton结构与稳定性鲁棒性的随机高斯过程框架，用Matérn核GP分别建模质量、势能、耗散与控制项并加独立扩散项表示随机激励，结合指数映射更新保证旋转约束与正定阻尼，配合IMEX积分器实现无条件稳定，在含噪刚体基准上取得更高精度与长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学建模困难在于观测稀疏含噪、真实动力学演化于非线性构型流形上并受环境耗散与随机扰动影响。标准神经动力学模型虽能拟合短期轨迹，但常违背物理结构、长时域误差累积，且所用参数化（如欧拉角）会引入万向节锁等奇异性。作者因此希望同时兼顾几何一致表示、物理结构保持与噪声鲁棒性。

Method: LieSPHGP在Lie群与Lie代数上表示运动，避免奇异参数化并实现坐标一致的优化；将动力学建模为端口Hamilton系统，学习得到的质量、势能、耗散与控制分量保持各自物理角色，另设独立扩散项表示随机强迫；每个分量用Matérn核高斯过程建模以刻画噪声观测带来的不确定性；用Lie群指数映射更新以保持旋转约束，采用正定阻尼保证确定性耗散非负；最后使用隐显（IMEX）积分器，对冻结的线性阻尼子系统提供无条件稳定性。

Result: 在多个含噪刚体动力学基准上的实验表明，与神经动力学基线相比，该方法预测精度更高、长时域稳定性更强，且对流形约束的满足程度更好。

Conclusion: 将Lie群几何表示、端口Hamilton物理结构、GP不确定性量化与结构保持积分统一起来，可获得对噪声鲁棒、尊重刚体几何、同时刻画内在运动与环境耗散的预测动力学模型；代码、数据与交互结果已公开。

Abstract: Learning predictive models for dynamics is difficult because observations are often sparse and noisy, while the underlying dynamics evolve on nonlinear configuration manifolds and are affected by environmental dissipation and stochastic disturbances. Standard neural dynamics models can fit short-term trajectories but often violate physical structure, accumulate error over long horizons, or rely on parameterizations that introduce singularities such as gimbal lock. We introduce LieSPHGP, a stochastic Gaussian-process framework for learning rigid body dynamics with port-Hamiltonian structure and stability-aware robustness. LieSPHGP represents motion on Lie groups and Lie algebras, enabling coordinate-consistent optimization without singular parameterizations. Learned mass, potential energy, dissipation, and control components retain physical roles and a separate diffusion term represents stochastic forcing. Each component is modeled by Matérn kernel Gaussian processes which capture uncertainty induced by noisy observations. To improve physical validity, Lie group exponential map updates preserve rotational constraints, while positive-definite damping ensures nonnegative deterministic energy dissipation. The implicit-explicit integrator additionally provides unconditional stability for the frozen linear damping subsystem. The resulting model learns predictive dynamics that are robust to noise, respect rigid body geometry, and capture both intrinsic motion and environmental dissipation. Experiments on various noisy rigid body benchmarks show improved prediction accuracy, stronger long-horizon stability, and better satisfaction of manifold constraints compared with neural dynamics baselines. Code, data and interactive results are available on the project website: https://thribhuvanrapolu.github.io/liesphgp/

</details>


### [218] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Deleting Weights in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10866)
*Jack Lu,Zhenbang Yang,Mike Lasby,Tejas Pote,Yani Ioannou,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Deletion Tuning：仅将 Transformer 块门控投影中不到 0.05% 的权重置零（学习二值掩码），即可在 RLVR、SFT、on-policy 蒸馏三种后训练范式上达到全参数微调（FFT）的效果，同时参数开销极小、灾难性遗忘显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"预训练 LLM 需要改变多少才能获得新能力"这一核心问题；同时针对后训练中 FFT（checkpoint 巨大、遗忘严重）与 LoRA/稀疏微调（效果或稳定性不足）的两难，寻求一种极小改动但等效于 FFT 的方案。

Method: Deletion Tuning：在 Transformer 块的门控投影上学一个二值掩码，只做权重置零（删除），不引入任何其他修改或新增参数；并从几何角度用 stable rank、谱漂移（spectrum drift）、主权重重叠度（principal-weight overlap）分析 RL 下权重变化的几何特性，与 LoRA、随机稀疏微调对比。

Result: 三种后训练范式下均匹配 FFT 的规模化效果；训练得到的掩码比对应 FFT checkpoint 小数百至数千倍；RL 设置下先前任务准确率几乎保持在基座水平，而 FFT、LoRA、随机稀疏微调最多下降 5.4 分；三项几何指标上 Deletion Tuning 均最接近 FFT。

Conclusion: 极稀疏的结构化"删除"足以复现 FFT 的能力增益，说明新能力的获得只需极少权重的功能性改变；该方法兼具存储高效与抗遗忘优势，并可作为理解后训练权重变化几何的切入点。

Abstract: How little of a pretrained large language model (LLM) has to change for it to acquire new capabilities? We find that zeroing fewer than 0.05% of its weights, with no other modification, matches full fine-tuning (FFT) at scale across three LLM post-training regimes: reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, supervised fine-tuning, and on-policy distillation. Our method, Deletion Tuning, learns a binary mask over the gate projection of transformer blocks. Two practical advantages follow: the trained mask is hundreds to thousands of times smaller than the corresponding FFT checkpoint, and Deletion Tuning forgets far less, which addresses a central challenge in continual learning. Under reinforcement learning, its prior-task accuracy stays near the base level while FFT, LoRA, and random sparse fine-tuning lose up to 5.4 points. To understand why masking alone suffices, we analyze the geometry of the learned weight changes under reinforcement learning using stable rank, spectrum drift, and principal-weight overlap. Compared with LoRA and random sparse fine-tuning, Deletion Tuning is closest to FFT across the three measures.

</details>


### [219] [Online Imitation Learning for Stabilizing Vlasov--Poisson Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10870)
*Xiaofan Xia,Qin Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究部分可观下Vlasov–Poisson等离子体系统的镇定控制，从理论上区分在线与离线模仿学习，并提出DAgger式算法在宏观测量下学习镇定控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体不稳定性控制是核聚变控制的核心难题；实际中等离子体状态仅部分可观（仅有宏观测量），而专家控制器基于全观测。因此需要研究在全观测专家示范、部分可观测执行条件下的模仿学习可行性。

Method: 建立理论分析：证明行为克隆（离线模仿学习）即使只有很小的误差，也会随时间指数放大并导致不稳定性误差累积；反之，在线模仿学习的损失可多项式地控制 rollout 轨迹的稳定性。基于此提出DAgger风格的在线模仿学习算法，在部分可观测条件下学习镇定控制器。

Result: 在一维Vlasov–Poisson系统仿真中，所提算法相比行为克隆能在更长的时间尺度上有效镇定等离子体不稳定性。

Conclusion: 在线与离线模仿学习在稳定性控制上存在本质分离：离线行为克隆的误差呈指数级复合放大，而在线性模仿学习可实现多项式级稳定性保证；在线DAgger式方法在部分可观条件下更适用于等离子体镇定控制。

Abstract: We study the problem of controlling instability in the Vlasov--Poisson plasma systems, a fundamental challenge for nuclear fusion control. Motivated by the partial observability of plasma states in practice, we investigate imitation learning algorithms that learn from a fully-observable expert controller, but operate under macroscopic measurements. We theoretically establish a separation between online and offline imitation learning --- even a small behavior cloning error leads to error compounding that exponentially amplifies instability over time. By way of contrast, the online imitation learning loss can polynomially control the stability of rollout trajectories. We then propose a DAgger-style algorithm that learns the stabilizing controller under partial observability. Simulation results on a 1D Vlasov--Poisson system demonstrate that our algorithm can effectively stabilize plasma instabilities over longer time horizons than behavior cloning.

</details>


### [220] [BRAT: Bounded Residual-Aligned Training for Stable Multi-Pass LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10873)
*Xunlan Zhou,Yuzhe Zhao,Xuhong Huang,Feng Chen,Xuanlin Chen,Lang Feng,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM后训练中rollout多轮复用导致的不稳定问题，指出其根源是对采样组的过拟合，并提出BRAT（有界残差对齐训练），用线性衰减、在信任域边界归零的token级梯度权重加组中心奖励的极值归一化，实现零均值有界优势，在四轮复用下避免崩溃、抑制策略漂移并提升准确率、降低种子方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为摊销生成成本，RL后训练常将每批采样rollout复用多次梯度更新。但现有多轮复用下的目标都存在缺陷：无裁剪更新随策略偏离采样策略而崩溃；加负基线虽避免崩溃却会强化所有采样响应（含劣质样本）；PPO式比率裁剪会突然截断梯度。作者进一步追问"复用为何脆弱"，通过测量小rollout组估计的更新与同提示留出rollout上KL正则目标的Held-out对齐度进行诊断。

Method: 1）诊断实验：度量in-sample与held-out rollout上更新与KL正则目标的对齐度，跨越三个基座模型比较。2）BRAT方法：动机为向采样策略的有界重加权做近端（proximal）更新；对每个token赋予随概率比线性递减、在信任域边界降为零的梯度权重；将组中心化奖励按其最大幅值归一化，使优势零均值且有界，无需额外超参数。

Result: 对齐度从in-sample到held-out rollout下降近一个数量级（三个基座模型一致），说明重复多轮passes主要是对采样组的过拟合。在四次复用passes下，BRAT避免训练崩溃、限制策略漂移，并在多数基准上取得更高的平均准确率与更低的种子方差。

Conclusion: 多轮复用失效的机制在于对采样组的过拟合而非单纯梯度尺度问题；通过有界、边界归零的比率权重与零均值有界优势设计，可在几乎不增加复杂度（无额外超参数）的前提下稳健支持多轮复用，兼顾训练稳定性与性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language model post-training often reuses each batch of sampled rollouts for several gradient passes to amortize the cost of generation. Under such multi-pass reuse, existing objectives fail in different ways: unclipped updates collapse as the policy drifts away from the sampling policy, adding a negative baseline avoids collapse but reinforces every sampled response, and PPO-style ratio clipping cuts off gradients abruptly. We first ask why reuse is fragile. Measuring how well an update estimated from a small rollout group aligns with the KL-regularized target on held-out rollouts of the same prompts, we find that this alignment drops by nearly an order of magnitude from in-sample to held-out rollouts across three base models, suggesting that repeated passes largely overfit the sampled group. Motivated by this finding, we propose Bounded Residual-Aligned Training (BRAT), motivated by a proximal update toward a bounded reweighting of the sampling policy. BRAT assigns each token a gradient weight that decreases linearly with its probability ratio and vanishes at a trust-region boundary, and normalizes group-centered rewards by their maximum magnitude, keeping advantages zero-mean and bounded without extra hyperparameters. With four reuse passes, BRAT avoids training collapse, limits policy drift, and achieves higher average accuracy with lower seed variance on most benchmarks.

</details>


### [221] [Robust and Efficient Continual Model Merging via Global Singular Subspace Separation and Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10881)
*Juncheol Shin,Byeongho Yu,Semin Kim,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GS3R（全局奇异子空间分离与恢复）方法，通过紧凑的"恢复状态"修正持续模型合并中的奇异几何畸变，并结合 PRF 降低合并时的内存开销，在视觉、NLP 和多模态基准上取得精度-效率的良好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续模型合并需在不访问历史模型与数据的情况下增量整合任务专属模型，但反复合并会使过去任务的分量相互纠缠、扭曲其奇异几何结构，导致难以在任务间做均衡分配（rank/参数预算分配）。

Method: GS3R 为合并更新附加一个紧凑的恢复状态（restoration state）：每次合并时(1)恢复被保留的奇异几何结构；(2)分离各任务的子空间；(3)在全局范围内跨任务公平地重新分配可用秩（rank）。另引入 Pre-merge Restoration and Flushing（PRF）以减少合并阶段的内存开销。

Result: 在视觉、自然语言处理和多模态基准实验中，GS3R 展现出较强的精度-效率权衡表现（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 基于奇异值/子空间视角的恢复与全局 rank 重分配，可有效缓解持续合并中的几何畸变与任务间不均衡问题，且 PRF 使方法在内存受限场景下仍具实用性。

Abstract: Continual model merging incrementally integrates task-specific models without revisiting past models or data. However, repeated merging entangles past task components and distorts their singular geometry, making balanced task-wise allocation increasingly difficult. We propose Global Singular Subspace Separation and Restoration (GS3R), which augments the merged update with a compact restoration state. At each merge, GS3R restores the retained singular geometry, separates task-wise subspaces, and globally reallocates the available rank across tasks fairly. To reduce merging-time memory overhead, we further introduce Pre-merge Restoration and Flushing (PRF). Experiments on vision, NLP, and multimodal benchmarks show that GS3R provides a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [222] [Robust Satisficing Ensemble: Scalable Model Aggregation Under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10895)
*Ahmet Faruk Cetinkaya,Enes Agirman,Cem Tekin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒满足集成（RSE），一种可扩展的概率聚合框架，通过与二次风险代理结合的鲁棒满足目标解析推导内层对手，实现高效权重更新，并在多种分布偏移上优于 GroupDRO、IRM、SRM 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准集成方法虽能通过缓解单模型随机性提升鲁棒性，但难以泛化到训练分布之外，系统在分布外（OOD）数据上依然脆弱。作者希望构建一种既能大规模扩展、又有理论保证的模型聚合机制，以最小化模型组合对分布变化的脆弱性。

Method: 提出 Robust Satisficing Ensemble（RSE）：将鲁棒满足（robust satisficing）目标与二次风险代理相结合，解析求出内层对手（analytical inner adversary），从而避免标准 minimax 优化的计算瓶颈，把优化复杂度与样本量解耦，可扩展至大规模数据。理论上利用 Lagrange-Bürmann 展开证明脆弱性近似具有几何衰减的截断误差，并给出 RSE 的平稳性保证。

Result: 在覆盖多种分布偏移的评测中——对抗性标签偏移（SST-5、TREC）、自然子群与时间偏移（CivilComments、HuffPost）——RSE 一致优于 GroupDRO、IRM、SRM 等强鲁棒基线。

Conclusion: RSE 是一种实用且具备理论基础的模型聚合保障机制，可在动态环境中实现可靠的模型组合；其解析内层对手设计兼顾了效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Standard ensemble methods can improve robustness by mitigating individual model stochasticity, but they frequently fail to generalize beyond the training distribution, leaving systems vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We introduce the Robust Satisficing Ensemble (RSE), a scalable probabilistic aggregation framework designed to secure model combinations by minimizing their fragility against distributional changes. By bridging a robust satisficing objective with a quadratic risk surrogate, RSE derives an analytical inner adversary. This fundamental reformulation enables highly efficient weight updates without the computational bottleneck of standard minimax optimization, crucially decoupling optimization complexity from sample size to scale effectively to large datasets. In particular, we prove a geometrically decaying truncation error for the fragility approximation via Lagrange-Burmann expansion and establish stationarity guarantees for RSE. We evaluate RSE across a comprehensive spectrum of distribution shifts, including adversarial label shift (SST-5, TREC), natural subpopulation and temporal shifts (CivilComments, HuffPost). Our results demonstrate that RSE consistently outperforms strong robust baselines, including GroupDRO, IRM, and SRM. These findings establish RSE as a practical, theoretically grounded safeguard for reliable model aggregation in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [223] [Optimal Byzantine-resilient Federated Learning with User-level Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10898)
*Ming Xiang,Stratis Ioannidis,Edmund Yeh,Carlee Joe-Wong,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对异构客户端下、同时具备拜占庭容错与用户级差分隐私（user-level DP）的联邦学习，建立了信息论下界，并提出中心化DP算法ByzULDP（客户端-服务器双层动量），达到强凸与非凸目标的阶最优收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署FL需同时抵御恶意客户端（拜占庭故障）并保护数据隐私（user-level DP 保护整个客户端贡献）。尽管已有大量工作，拜占庭容错FL在user-level DP下的极小极大最优收敛界仍未解决；且朴素地将非线性鲁棒聚合与中心DP噪声结合不足以收敛到有界邻域。

Method: 建立异构客户端下任意拜占庭容错ERM在user-level DP下的信息论下界；提出ByzULDP：中心DP算法，客户端与服务器双层动量。客户端动量降低鲁棒聚合中的随机梯度方差；服务器动量利用user-level DP中隐私敏感度依赖客户端更新构造方式这一特性，驱动收敛。

Result: ByzULDP 达到阶最优收敛界：强凸目标误差项以最优 O(1/T) 衰减，非凸目标以 O(1/√T) 衰减；并在真实数据集、不同隐私预算下的数值实验验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了拜占庭容错FL在user-level DP下最优收敛界的空白，表明双层动量（尤其服务器动量）是协调鲁棒聚合、隐私噪声与收敛的关键设计。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of federated learning (FL) requires resilience against adversarial clients and data privacy. Byzantine fault captures the worst-case behavior of adversarial clients, while differential privacy (DP) provides statistical guarantees against privacy loss. In particular, user-level DP guards a client's entire contribution and provides more realistic protection against information leakage. Despite intensive efforts, the minimax-optimal convergence bound of Byzantine-resilient FL with user-level DP remains open. We establish an information-theoretic lower bound for any Byzantine-resilient empirical risk minimization problem under user-level DP and heterogeneous clients. We propose ByzULDP, a central DP algorithm featuring a two-level momentum across the client and server. Client-side momentum mitigates stochastic gradient variance during robust aggregation. Unlike the fault-free case, naively combining non-linear Byzantine-resilient aggregation with central DP noise can be insufficient to guarantee convergence to a bounded neighborhood of a stationary point. Our key contribution is a server-side momentum that leverages a property specific to user-level DP, where privacy sensitivity depends on how client updates are constructed, to empower convergence. ByzULDP achieves the order-optimal convergence bound for strongly-convex objectives, with vanishing error terms decaying at the optimal $O(1/T)$ and $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rates for strongly-convex and non-convex objectives, respectively. We corroborate our analysis with numerical experiments on real-world datasets across diverse privacy budgets.

</details>


### [224] [Prescriptive Learning of Linear Policies with Feasibility Guarantees for Binary Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10923)
*Farzaneh Pourahmadi,Trine Krogh Boomsma,Pierre Pinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向二元决策的规范性学习（prescriptive learning）框架，联合优化线性决策策略与训练场景下的可行指派，并扩展为基于Wasserstein模糊集的分布鲁棒机会约束形式，在机组组合与人员配置案例中显著优于预测式学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将机器学习用于加速/替代混合整数求解器的方法属于预测式学习：以标注的最优决策为标签训练模型，缺乏对反事实结果的认识，也无法保证决策可行性；在分布漂移下，基于历史数据训练的策略可能系统性地给出不可行决策。

Method: 构建规范性学习框架：不预测最优标签，而是在所有训练场景上联合优化线性决策策略与可行指派，使可行替代方案及其运行性能在训练中直接被考虑；进一步引入Wasserstein模糊集，构造分布鲁棒机会约束形式，在模糊集内最坏分布下以给定概率保证可行性，并实现鲁棒性与解质量的可调权衡。

Result: 在机组组合（unit commitment）与人员配置（staff allocation）两个案例上，于与训练分布一致或不一致的测试分布下进行样本外实验：鲁棒模型在分布一致时更保守；在分布漂移下，相比经典预测式学习，显著减少未满足需求，总成本最多降低76.5%。

Conclusion: 将可行性直接嵌入学习过程并结合分布鲁棒机会约束，可有效克服预测式学习在可行性保证与分布漂移下的缺陷，为不确定环境下的二元决策提供兼具理论保证与实用性能的方法。

Abstract: Binary decision-making under uncertainty arises across a wide range of applications, including energy systems scheduling, transportation, and workforce planning. While machine learning has been increasingly applied to accelerate or replace mixed-integer solvers in such settings, existing approaches rely on predictive learning, training models to reproduce optimal decisions from labeled data, which lacks knowledge of counterfactual outcomes and provides no guarantees that the resulting decisions will be feasible. This limitation is further compounded under distributional shift, where policies trained on historical data may produce systematically infeasible decisions. We propose a prescriptive learning framework for binary decision-making that addresses both limitations. Rather than predicting optimal labels, the framework jointly optimizes over linear decision policies and feasible assignments across all training scenarios, allowing feasible alternatives and their operational performance to be considered directly during training. We further extend the framework to a distributionally robust chance-constrained formulation using a Wasserstein ambiguity set, which enforces feasibility with a prescribed probability under the worst-case distribution in the ambiguity set and enables a tunable trade-off between robustness and solution quality. We demonstrate the framework on two case studies, namely a unit commitment problem and a staff allocation problem. Out-of-sample experiments under test distributions that either match or differ from the training distribution show that the robust model is more conservative when the two distributions coincide, while under distributional shift it substantially reduces unmet demand and lowers total cost by up to 76.5% relative to classical predictive learning.

</details>


### [225] [CoupledFlow: One-Step Neural Operators for Coupled Multi-Physics PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10945)
*Trong Khiem Tran,Minh Long Bui,Phi Le Nguyen,Mrinal Sen,Sanjay Srinivasan,Trong Nghia Hoang,Jana Doppa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 COUPLED FLOW，一种面向多物理场耦合 PDE 系统的神经算子学习框架，通过结构约束的 flow matching 学习各过程特定的平均速度场，实现快速准确的一步预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法主要针对单一物理场设置，难以刻画多个物理过程之间的交叉耦合与共同演化，而直接数值模拟耦合 PDE 代价高昂。

Method: 采用结构约束的 flow matching 算法学习以完整多物理状态为条件的各过程平均速度场；推导多物理平均流一致性恒等式以刻画跨过程交互并定义结构约束的平均流匹配损失；基于后验多输出高斯过程采样相关条件插值样本，以获得比端点条件线性插值更丰富的跨过程相关性。

Result: 理论上证明预测分布与真实 PDE 解分布之间的 Wasserstein-2 距离可由结构约束 flow matching 损失及所学光流的光滑性上界控制；在多个耦合 PDE 基准上一致优于现有最先进神经算子与生成式 PDE 求解器。

Conclusion: COUPLED FLOW 为多物理耦合 PDE 提供了兼具效率与精度的一步预测方案，并通过理论与实验验证了跨过程相关建模的有效性。

Abstract: This paper introduces COUPLED FLOW, a new neural operator learning framework for multi-physics PDE systems that captures the co-evolution of multiple physical processes by modeling their cross-process interactions. Unlike existing neural operator methods that primarily target single-physics settings, COUPLED FLOW employs a novel structure-constrained flow matching algorithm to learn process- specific mean velocity fields conditioned on the full multi-physics state. This enables fast and accurate one-step prediction of coupled PDE systems that would otherwise require expensive numerical simulations. A key challenge in training COUPLED FLOW however lies in sampling structure-constrained conditional flow interpolants that capture cross-process correlations and serve as training examples for flow matching. To address this challenge, we derive a multi-physics mean-flow consistency identity that characterizes cross-process interactions and defines the structure-constrained mean-flow matching loss. To provide informative training samples for flow matching with this loss, we develop a principled method for sampling correlated conditional interpolants based on posterior multi-output Gaus- sian processes, which capture richer cross-process correlations than the commonly used endpoint-conditioned linear interpolants. We also establish a theoretical re- sult showing that the Wasserstein-2 distance between the predicted and oracle PDE solution distributions is upper-bounded in terms of the structure-constrained flow matching loss and the smoothness of the learned flow. Experiments across diverse coupled PDE benchmarks demonstrate that COUPLED FLOW consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art neural operators and generative PDE solvers.

</details>


### [226] [Interpretable Cyber-Physical Attack Detection in Smart Grids: A Leakage-Controlled Multi-Testbed Evaluation of Fusion, Robustness, and Observability Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10950)
*Muhammad Ridwanul Hoque,Sarah Sharif,Reza Saeed Kandezy,Yaser Banad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文围绕可解释的时间窗融合随机森林检测器，在多个测试床上进行严格评估，发现传统"黑箱+打乱数据"验证过于乐观，并揭示时间退化、对抗攻击与语义操纵等系统性脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息物理智能电网检测器常被当作黑箱，仅在干净的、时间打乱的数据集上验证，导致性能被高估、结论过度乐观；需要更贴近现实的、多测试床的评估框架。

Method: 构建多测试床评估体系，核心为可解释的时间窗融合随机森林；在协议冲突时采用悲观（更差）结果作为结论。评估包括：公开配对信息物理数据集上的滚动原点时间验证与无拓扑GCN对照；自适应/自适应联合特征空间攻击、对抗训练与随机平滑、IEEE 14-Bus物理约束进化策略规避；真实IEC-104流量及可观测性分类；AC加权最小二乘状态估计测试床上的预测辅助时间检验与速率不变物理流形检验；IEEE 118-Bus端到端评估。

Result: (i) 打乱交叉验证偏乐观：滚动原点评估下macro-F1由0.96降至0.88±0.06，轻量GCN在该划分上具竞争力。(ii) 自适应与自适应联合特征空间攻击分别将macro-F1降至0.67与0.34，对抗训练与随机平滑可部分恢复，IEEE 14-Bus上规避模式一致。(iii) 真实IEC-104流量中网络视图对结构性攻击召回0.87–0.98，但对语义值操纵召回仅0.11–0.35；IEEE 14-Bus可观测性分类显示隐蔽虚假数据注入可击败全部三种观测视图。(iv) AC WLS状态估计测试床上，预测辅助时间检验被慢斜坡规避，而物理流形检验在5%虚警目标下持续保持检测；IEEE 118-Bus端到端证实该排序但强度有限：流形检验在0.05度/步下全检出，0.02度/步为0.48，时间检验仅0.14。

Conclusion: 打乱验证系统性高估检测器性能；现实时间序、对抗操纵与语义攻击会造成显著退化；单一视图难以覆盖全部威胁，需采用速率不变物理流形等更具鲁棒性的检验，并以多测试床、悲观准则进行端到端评估。代码与基准全部开源。

Abstract: Cyber-physical smart-grid detectors are typically treated as black boxes and validated exclusively on clean, temporally shuffled datasets, which risk overly optimistic performance claims. This work presents a multi-testbed evaluation study built around an interpretable, time-windowed fusion Random Forest, adopting the pessimistic result whenever evaluation protocols disagree. (i) On a public paired cyber-physical dataset, shuffled cross-validation is optimistic: macro-F1 falls from 0.96 to 0.88 +/- 0.06 under rolling-origin temporal evaluation. A topology-free graph convolutional network (GCN) serving as a control confirms that this lightweight model is competitive on these splits. (ii) The adaptive and adaptive-joint feature-space attacks, which are infeasible as realistic grid manipulations, degrade macro-F1 to 0.67 and 0.34, respectively. Adversarial training and randomized smoothing partially restore performance, and a physics-constrained evolution-strategy evasion on the IEEE 14-Bus testbed shows the same vulnerability pattern. (iii) On real IEC-104 traffic, the cyber view reliably catches structural attacks (recall of 0.87 to 0.98) but largely fails on semantic value manipulation (recall of 0.11 to 0.35). An observability taxonomy on the IEEE 14-Bus system reveals that stealthy false data injection defeats all three observation views. (iv) On a full AC weighted least-squares state-estimation testbed, a forecasting-aided temporal test is evaded by slow ramps precisely where a rate-invariant physics-manifold test sustains detection at a common 5% false-alarm target.
