A full end-to-end evaluation on IEEE 118-Bus confirms the ordering but bounds its strength: the manifold test detects every trial down to 0.05 degree/step and 0.48 at 0.02 degree/step, against 0.14 for the temporal test. All code and benchmarks are released.

</details>


### [227] [JUBILEE: A Benchmark Environment for Off-Policy Evaluation in Debt Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10972)
*Diego Silva Prado,Tiago Amaral Penteado de Jesus,Bruno Caldas de Souza,Cristiano Bevitori Maffia De Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JUBILEE——一个基于催收(债务催收)模拟器的基准环境，用于在存在隐藏混杂因素的情形下研究离策略评估(OPE)的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催收场景中未被记录的因素会同时影响处置动作与回收结果，导致OPE估计产生偏差；现有研究缺乏可在受控机制下复现此类失败模式的可重复基准环境。

Method: 构建催收模拟器生成含竞争风险、稀有回收事件与隐藏混杂的百万级贷款面板；在两种合成结果机制与190组参数网格下开展80次运行的表格型与梯度提升估计器对比；另做190组参数网格实验，并用独立策略仿真提供带蒙特卡洛不确定性的参考值（合成奖励分数与模拟现金流）；同时考察随机化决策下的估计表现。

Result: 隐藏因素影响动作分配时，多种表格型方法估计值高度一致，却系统性低估实地探访政策得分，提升模型亦存在该误差；表格型双重稳健区间在无隐藏分配依赖时通常覆盖参考值，而在存在依赖时大多失效。随机化决策估计未出现同等量级误差，但精度有限使政策选择不确定。政策价值定义影响决策后果：实地探访政策在分数口径下排名末位、在模拟现金流口径下排名首位；在配对研究中，现金流口径下基于日志的估计器在几乎所有运行中均漏判该最优候选。

Conclusion: JUBILEE为在特定催收机制下可复现地研究OPE上述失效模式提供了基准支持，并揭示隐藏混杂、政策价值定义与估计器一致性之间的关键交互。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value a candidate decision policy would achieve, using only data logged by the policy already in production. In debt collections, unrecorded factors can influence both actions and outcomes. This paper presents JUBILEE, a benchmark environment built around a debt-collections simulator that generates million-loan panels with competing risks, rare recovery events, and hidden factors driving both outcomes and assignment. The evaluation includes an 80-run comparison of tabular and gradient-boosted estimators under two synthetic outcome mechanisms and a 190-run parameter grid. Independent policy simulations provide reference values with estimated Monte Carlo uncertainty for a synthetic reward score and for simulated cash. With hidden factors influencing action assignment, several tabular methods agree closely while underestimating the field-visit policy's score, an error that also appears with boosted models. Intervals from tabular doubly robust estimation usually contain the reference without hidden assignment dependence but mostly miss it when that dependence is present. Estimates from randomized decisions do not show the same large error, although their limited precision leaves policy choice uncertain. The decision consequences depend on how policy value is defined. The field-visit policy ranks last under the score and first under simulated cash, which excludes channel costs. Under cash, log-based estimators miss this best candidate in nearly every run with hidden assignment dependence in the paired study. JUBILEE supports reproducible study of these failure modes under specified collections mechanisms.

</details>


### [228] [An Analysis of Reinforcement Learning with Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10981)
*Francisco S. Melo,Sean P. Meyn,M. Isabel Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无限状态空间中Q函数的逼近计算，将Tsitsiklis与Van Roy关于TD学习收敛性的分析推广到随机控制场景，给出Q学习结合函数逼近时以概率1收敛的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛性理论主要针对TD学习（策略评估），而Q学习作为随机控制下的最优控制问题求解方法，其在函数逼近下的收敛性缺乏系统分析，需要将已有分析框架扩展至控制设定。

Method: 分析Q学习的多个变体与函数逼近相结合时的收敛性质，沿用Tsitsiklis与Van Roy (1996)针对TD学习的分析框架（基于随机逼近理论），推导近似方法以概率1收敛的充分条件。

Result: 明确了若干Q学习近似变体在满足特定条件时以概率1收敛，并简要讨论这些结果的普适性，同时与相关工作进行对比。

Conclusion: 函数逼近下的Q学习收敛性可由TD学习分析框架自然推广得到；所给条件界定了其适用范围，为无限状态空间最优Q函数的近似求解提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We address the problem of computing the optimal Q-function in Markov decision problems with infinite state-space. We analyze the convergence properties of several variations of Q-learning when combined with function approximation, extending the analysis of TD-learning in Tsitsiklis and Van Roy (1996) to stochastic control settings. We identify conditions under which such approximate methods converge with probability 1. We conclude with a brief discussion on the general applicability of our results and compare them with several related works.

</details>


### [229] [Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10987)
*Haoran Xu,Pragya Srivastava,Xinjie Liu,Siddhant Agarwal,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HQL通过"仅当数据集动作被critic估值不低于BC策略best-of-N采样动作时才做多步扩展，否则回退单步目标"的自适应规则，在无需动作密度的前提下平衡单步与多步回报，实现长horizon离线Q学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据下学习准确Q函数随任务horizon增长而变难：单步TD每步误差沿horizon累积，TD损失很小时早期状态也可能被严重错误估值；多步TD虽缩短误差链，但行为策略次优导致每跟随一个动作就引入偏差，且不存在适用于整条轨迹的固定步数。

Method: 提出Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning (HQL)：将目标沿数据集动作扩展，当且仅当critic对该动作的估值不低于从BC策略采样best-of-N动作的估值；否则退回单步TD目标。该规则被论证为一种无需动作密度的、有原则的离策略修正，因而兼容flow与diffusion等表达性策略。

Result: 在combination-lock任务上跨多种horizon避免了单步与多步TD各自的失败；在长horizon的OGBench与OCBench任务上优于已有horizon缩减方法。

Conclusion: 自适应的单步/多步回报平衡是长horizon离线价值学习的有效途径，且不依赖动作密度估计，可与表达性生成式策略结合。

Abstract: Learning accurate off-policy state-action value functions (Q-functions) from offline datasets becomes harder as the task horizon grows. One-step temporal-difference (TD) learning bootstraps after every transition, so small per-step errors accumulate along the horizon, and early states can be badly misvalued even when the TD loss is small. Multi-step TD shortens this chain by following the dataset trajectory for several steps; however, since the behavior policy is often suboptimal, every followed action adds a bias, and no fixed number of steps suits a whole trajectory. In this paper, we propose Horizon-Scalable Q-Learning (HQL), a simple method that enables accurate long-horizon value learning by automatically balancing between one-step and multi-step returns. HQL extends a target through a dataset action only when the critic values it at least as highly as the best-of-N actions sampled from the BC policy, and otherwise falls back to a one-step target. We show that this rule is a principled off-policy correction that needs no action densities, which makes HQL compatible with expressive flow and diffusion policies. Experiments show that HQL avoids the failures of both one-step and multi-step TD on a combination-lock task across horizons, and outperforms prior horizon-reduction methods on long-horizon OGBench and OCBench tasks.

</details>


### [230] [Hydro-Dataset: A Multi-Modal Open-Source Pipeline for Nordic Hydrological Research and Foundation Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10992)
*Getnet Demil,Md Moinul Islam,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布 Hydro-Dataset v1.1：一个可复现的开源流水线，将瑞典 SMHI 的 582 个气象观测站、9 个水文冬季（2015/16–2023/24）数据统一为 CF-1.9 规范的 NetCDF 产品，含约 180 万条带标注的站点-日记录，衍生出人工雪水当量等六个积雪参数，为水文基础模型训练提供站点锚定的多参数积雪基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北欧寒温带（boreal）区域虽有 SMHI 与 FMI 世界级积雪监测，但缺乏以站点观测为锚、多参数、可直接用于机器学习基准评测的积雪数据集，现有再分析产品（如 ERA5-Land）精度不足，难以作为训练标签来源。

Method: 构建开源可复现的数据处理流水线：整合 582 个 SMHI 观测站、9 个水文冬季数据，按 CF-1.9 标准输出为 NetCDF 格式；从原始观测派生六类积雪参数（雪水当量 SWE、积雪物候、融雪速率、度日融雪因子、降雨致融雪（ROS）标记、冻融循环）；并用 ERA5-Land 进行交叉验证。

Result: 数据集包含约 1.8 million 条标注站点-日；与 ERA5-Land 交叉验证得 Pearson R = 0.78、偏差 bias = −262 mm，表明再分析产品存在系统性低估。

Conclusion: 站点锚定的观测型 SWE 相较再分析数据是更可靠的机器学习训练基准；该数据集可作为积雪/水文基础模型的标准 benchmark（多参数标签 + 可复现流水线），弥补北欧寒温带领域基准数据集的空白。

Abstract: Despite world-class snow monitoring by the Swedish Meteorological and Hydrological Institute (SMHI) and Finnish Meteorological Institute (FMI), no station-anchored, multi-parameter snow benchmark dataset exists for the Nordic boreal domain. We present Hydro-Dataset v1.1, a reproducible open-source pipeline that harmonizes 582 SMHI meteorological observation stations across nine hydrological winters (2015/16-2023/24) into a CF-1.9 NetCDF product with 1.8~million annotated station-days. Six derived snow parameters, such as Snow Water Equivalent (SWE), snow phenology, melt rates, degree-day melt factor, rain-on-snow flags, and freeze-thaw cycles, supply benchmark-ready labels for hydrological foundation model training. ERA5-Land cross-validation (Pearson R=0.78, bias=-262mm) confirms observation-anchored SWE provides a more reliable benchmark than reanalysis for machine learning training.

</details>


### [231] [DPDP: Diffusion Policy-Steered Diffusion Planner for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10993)
*Ruyi An,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPDP 在扩散规划器的执行（逆动力学）步骤把行为先验替换为价值优化的扩散策略，从而同时获得规划器的远视性与策略的价值感知，并通过免训练采样器与偏好目标两种方式实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略（受 critic 引导）价值感知强但短视，不规划要访问的状态；扩散规划器远视但执行时仅依赖行为克隆，缺乏价值意识。两者优势恰好互补，因此需要一种机制在规划的"执行一步"处融合二者。

Method: 将规划器的逆动力学执行器视为动作上的后验：环境转移似然约束动作与计划下一状态一致，行为先验决定其在离线数据下的权重。用价值优化的扩散策略替换该行为先验，保留计划转移似然的同时偏向有利于实现计划的高价值动作。目标分布的采样有两条路线：(1) 免训练采样器，在匹配的动作噪声水平上组合策略与逆动力学得分并做 Langevin 修正（需共享噪声调度与近似一致的终端先验）；(2) 偏好目标，将迁移摊销进单个扩散执行器。

Result: 在 D4RL 上，DPDP 相比其基座规划器有稳定提升；在 locomotion 任务上与反应式扩散策略竞争力相当，在长时程导航任务上取得领先。

Conclusion: 在执行步融合"价值感知策略"与"远视规划"是一种有效且互补的组合方式，既能保持计划可行性又能提升动作价值，且可通过免训练采样或偏好蒸馏两种方式落地，长时程任务收益尤为明显。

Abstract: In offline reinforcement learning, diffusion models fill two roles with complementary strengths. Typically guided by a critic, a diffusion policy is value-aware and favors high-return, behavior-supported actions, but it is short-sighted: it never lays out the states it intends to visit. A diffusion planner is far-sighted, synthesizing such a long-horizon trajectory, but it is only behavior-driven at execution, realizing each planned transition using only offline behavior.Each is strong exactly where the other is weak. We introduce DPDP to bring them together at the planner's execution step, where a planned transition becomes an action. Reading the planner's inverse-dynamics executor as a posterior over actions: the environment's transition likelihood relates an action to the planned next state, while the behavioral prior determines its weight under the offline data. Replacing this prior with a value-optimized diffusion policy preserves the planned transition likelihood and favors valuable actions that can serve the plan. We target the resulting clean-action distribution in two ways. A training-free sampler composes policy and inverse-dynamics scores at matched action noise levels and applies Langevin correction; this route uses a shared action noise schedule and an approximately common terminal prior. A preference objective instead amortizes the transfer into a single diffusion executor. Across D4RL, DPDP improves the planner it builds on, staying competitive with reactive diffusion policies on locomotion and leading on long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [232] [Toward a theory of Evaluability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10996)
*Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了在测试样本可能已被用于训练（即不假设测试集留出）的情形下的“可评估性”概念，并系统研究黑盒模型评估在何种条件下可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大基础模型的训练数据规模巨大，传统评估所依赖的“测试集与训练集不相交”假设难以成立，因此在测试样本可能见过的情况下，评估准确性、安全性、校准与公平性等指标是否仍然可行，成为一个需要理论刻画的问题。

Method: 按模型访问权限分层研究：首先分析仅观测模型在测试集上输出的自然设定，完整刻画可精确评估的分布类，并给出样本复杂度的紧上界与下界；随后引入可在额外输入上查询模型的评估算法，将其与程序测试中的自纠正器（self-corrector）建立联系。

Result: 在不加查询的黑盒设定下，可评估性成立的充要条件是数据分布接近小支撑（small support）；少量查询访问能够对自然分布与概念类显著增强评估器的能力，并给出相应的样本复杂度紧界与联系结论。

Conclusion: 黑盒评估在测试数据可能参与训练时并非普遍可行，其可行性受分布支撑结构严格约束；引入有限查询可突破这一限制，为数据污染下的模型评估提供了理论框架与访问权限的权衡刻画。

Abstract: Machine learning models, particularly large foundation models, are increasingly evaluated along multiple axes that are relevant for their usage. In addition to accuracy measures appropriate for the specific task of interest, measures capturing safety, calibration and fairness properties are often of interest. The classical approach to these problems assumes that the test set has been held out from the training process, an assumption that is increasingly harder to justify for large foundation models. In this work, we formalize a notion of evaluability without this assumption, and ask if it is feasible to evaluate black-box models in settings where our test samples may have been used during training.
  We study this problem under different settings, capturing different levels of access to the model. We first study the natural setting, where the evaluator only looks at the output of the model on the test set. We fully characterize the class of distributions where accurate evaluation is possible, and show tight upper and lower bounds on the sample complexity of evaluation. Our results show that black-box evaluation in this setup is feasible if and only if the distribution is close to being small support. We then consider evaluation algorithms that can query the model on additional inputs and demonstrate connections to self-correctors studied in program testing. Using these, we show that a small amount of query access can increase the power of the evaluator for natural distributions and concept classes.

</details>


### [233] [Differentiable Agent-Based Modeling for Multi-Echelon Supply Chains: Gradient-Based Optimization of Heterogeneous Policies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10999)
*Bulent Soykan,Murtadha Alsayegh,Sean Mondesire,Ghaith Rabadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可微分的智能体模型（Differentiable ABM），通过可微张量运算实现随机动力学下的端到端梯度传播，用于多级库存管理的大规模异构智能体同时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球供应链依赖数千个异构智能体交互，优化极为困难：传统优化为可解性常假设同质性，而强化学习在高维状态空间下样本效率低，难以扩展到大规模异构策略优化。

Method: 构建可微分智能体仿真框架，以可微张量操作实现随机动态，使梯度可直接通过仿真过程反向传播；结合梯度敏感性分析生成因果热力图，从计算图中识别提前期延迟结构。

Result: 无监督条件下可复现Newsvendor理论最优；在数秒内同时优化10,000个异构智能体策略，成本较标量基线降低53%；因果热力图准确还原固有提前期时滞。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了仿真与优化之间的鸿沟，支持高效训练可自我校正的数字孪生，为大规模异构供应链的个性化策略优化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Global supply chains rely on the interactions of thousands of heterogeneous agents, posing significant challenges for optimization. While traditional optimization often assumes homogeneity to ensure tractability, Reinforcement Learning struggles with sample inefficiency when scaling to high-dimensional state spaces. We propose a Differentiable Agent-Based Model for multi-echelon inventory management. By implementing stochastic dynamics via differentiable tensor operations, we enable direct gradient propagation through the simulation. We validate the framework by recovering theoretical Newsvendor optima without supervision. Crucially, we demonstrate ``Mass Personalization'' by optimizing 10,000 distinct agent policies simultaneously in seconds, achieving a 53% cost reduction compared to scalar baselines. Furthermore, we utilize gradient sensitivity analysis to generate causal heatmaps, accurately recovering inherent lead-time delays from the computation graph. This approach bridges the gap between simulation and optimization, enabling the efficient training of self-correcting Digital Twins.

</details>


### [234] [Elastic Representations via Hyperbolic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11001)
*Arjun Ramesh Kaushik,Rudrasis Chakraborty,Nalini Ratha,Venu Govindaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HERL框架，通过轻量适配器与双曲几何实现无需重训基础编码器的维度自适应嵌入（压缩与扩展）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定维度嵌入无法适应不同下游资源与任务约束；MRL需昂贵端到端重训且不支持事后扩展；CSR虽降低训练开销但维度高4倍；且此前工作均仅支持压缩。

Method: 利用双曲几何的指数容量，学习从编码器空间到双曲空间的非线性映射：训练简单MLP先对表示降采样，再投影到Lorentzian流形，学习符合Lorentz几何的自适应表示；模型无关，适用于监督与无监督场景。

Result: 在图像与文本模态的大量实验中，HERL在广泛嵌入尺寸范围内保持下游性能，维度间插值平滑，且计算成本仅为原有方案的一小部分。

Conclusion: HERL为维度自适应嵌入（压缩与扩展）提供了高效、模型无关的通用框架，兼具性能保持与低计算开销。

Abstract: Learned representations (or embeddings) are the foundation of modern machine learning systems, yet they are typically trained as fixed-size embeddings, without accounting for varying downstream resource or task constraints. Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) addresses this limitation by learning nested representations, but requires costly end-to-end retraining and does not support post hoc expansion, while Contrastive Sparse Representations (CSR) reduce training overhead via lightweight adaptors but operate at a 4x larger dimensionality. Additionally, all prior works restrict their framework towards compression only. In this work, we introduce Hyperbolic Elastic Representation Learning (HERL), a framework for learning dimension-adaptive embeddings (compression and expansion) through a lightweight adaptor without retraining the base encoder. Our key idea is to exploit the exponential capacity of hyperbolic geometry by learning a non-linear mapping from the encoder space to hyperbolic space. We train a simple MLP to downsample the representations before projecting them onto a Lorentzian and learn adaptive representations that respect the Lorentzian geometry. Our approach is model-agnostic and effective in both supervised and unsupervised settings. Through extensive experiments across image and text modalities, we show that HERL preserves downstream performance across a wide range of embedding sizes, exhibits smooth interpolation between dimensions, and achieves these benefits at a fraction of the computational cost. Code: https://github.com/arjunrkaushik/herl.

</details>


### [235] [GradReason: LLMs as Meta-Learners that Replace Training Epochs with Training-Data-Free Weight Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11005)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Abbas Yazdinejad,Hadis Karimipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GradReason 让 LLM 代理每 10 个 epoch 读取权重快照、梯度与验证指标，生成 Python 程序来把部分 epoch 替换为"虚拟 epoch"（不计算梯度、不读训练图像）；在 CIFAR 上以 32.8% 更少的数据 epoch 达到仅低 0.31 个百分点的测试精度，但节省主要源于"权重平均"这一被 LLM 自行发现的更新形式，固定规则同样可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准训练配方固定遍历数据若干轮，而后期 epoch 往往只带来小批量噪声，边际收益低。作者希望检验：LLM 能否依据一次运行的权重/梯度历史，用无梯度的更新替代部分 epoch，从而减少数据与算力开销。

Method: 构建 GradReason 代理：每 10 个 epoch 调用一次 LLM（Claude Opus 5.5），在受限沙箱中分析近期权重快照、epoch 平均梯度与验证指标，输出计算新权重的 Python 程序及"data/virtual"标注计划；虚拟 epoch 仅在通过 5000 张留出验证图像的纯前向检查后保留。在 CIFAR-10/100、三种架构上共 120 次运行，并与固定规则基线（外推、平均）对比。

Result: 相对 80-epoch 配方，数据 epoch 减少 32.8%，测试精度仅降 0.31 个百分点（95% CI −0.72 至 +0.09；ResNet-18 上降 0.94 点），处于原配方的数据-精度前沿上。LLM 未经提示即持续采用"近期权重快照平均"，该选择带来主要收益：相同验收检查下的固定外推规则仅省 5.4%，加入平均后省 25.0%。单次 Opus 运行成本 0.68 美元，但此规模下代理延迟超过其节省的 GPU 时间。

Conclusion: LLM 的价值在于在一次运行中"发现"有效的无梯度更新形式，一旦发现，固定规则即可等效实现；此时用 LLM 代理并不划算。该框架提供了可复用的"代理探索—规则固化"范式，但其收益依赖于发现的更新是否可被简单规则化。

Abstract: Standard training recipes make a fixed number of passes over the training data, even when an epoch mostly adds mini-batch noise. We study whether a large language model (LLM) that reads the weight and gradient history of a run can replace some of these epochs with updates that compute no gradient and read no training image. In GradReason, an LLM agent is called every ten epochs. In a restricted sandbox, it analyses recent weight snapshots, epoch-mean gradients, and validation metrics, then submits a Python program that computes new weights and a plan marking each of the next ten epochs as data or virtual. A virtual epoch is kept only if it passes a forward-only check on 5,000 held-out validation images. In 120 runs on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with three architectures, Claude Opus 5.5 used 32.8% fewer data epochs than the 80-epoch recipe at 0.31 percentage points lower test accuracy (95% CI -0.72 to +0.09; 0.94 points on ResNet-18), on the recipe's own frontier of data against accuracy. Unprompted, Opus averaged recent weight snapshots in every run. This choice produced the savings: a fixed rule with the same acceptance check but extrapolations from prior work saved 5.4% of the data epochs, and the same rule with averaging added saved 25.0%. The LLM's contribution is to find a working update within a single run; once found, a fixed rule implements it equally well. An Opus run cost 0.68 USD, but at this scale the agent's latency exceeded the GPU time it saved.

</details>


### [236] [PIA: Agentic Physics-Informed Runtime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11009)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Abbas Yazdinejad,Hadis Karimipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息智能体（PIA）：由大语言模型智能体自动编写以物理残差形式表达的检测器，并经评测工作台依据正常运行数据迭代优化，在BATADAL攻击基准上F1达0.878，远超最强基线0.504，且可对每次告警定位被攻击部件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统的运行时异常检测存在两难：数据驱动的黑箱检测器只学正常数据分布，难以发现使各信号均处于正常范围之内的隐蔽操纵；物理模型手工推导的方法则需要针对每套装置投入专家成本，难以规模化。

Method: 让LLM智能体直接生成检测器本体，即以一组物理残差（泵特性、储罐质量平衡、控制器逻辑等）表达的解析式；候选检测器在部署前通过评测工作台，用正常工况数据反复验证与精炼。进行消融实验：分别考察仅依据装置描述写残差（不看数据）的智能体，以及仅看到匿名化信号（不看物理描述）的智能体的表现。

Result: 在BATADAL供水管网攻击基准上，PIA（Claude Opus 5.5）测试集F1为0.878，六种统计与深度基线中最强者仅0.504；仍可检测出经重放与偏移传感器读数伪装的攻击；对全部被检出的测试攻击均能正确归因到被攻击部件。消融显示：仅凭装置描述写残差会损失最多0.28 F1，而仅见匿名化信号的智能体可恢复大部分物理结构。

Conclusion: 所得检测器紧凑、人类可读、毫秒级运行，并能给出每个告警对应的部件名称。关键结论是"用数据检验假设"这一环节起决定作用：数据反馈比物理先验描述更关键，而物理归纳能力甚至可从纯匿名信号中被隐式恢复。

Abstract: Runtime anomaly detectors for cyber-physical systems are either learned as black boxes from normal data, which miss stealthy manipulations that keep every signal inside its normal range, or derived by hand from physical models, which requires expert effort for every plant. We introduce physicsinformed agents (PIA), in which a large language model agent writes the detector itself as a set of physical residuals, such as pump characteristics, tank mass balances, and controller logic, and refines every candidate against normal operating data through an evaluation workbench before deployment. The result is a compact, human-readable detector that runs in milliseconds and names the component behind each alarm. On the BATADAL water distribution attack benchmark, PIA with Claude Opus 5.5 reaches an F1 of 0.878 on the test attacks against 0.504 for the strongest of six statistical and deep baselines, keeps detecting attacks concealed by replayed and offset sensor readings, and attributes every detected test attack to the attacked component. Ablations show that testing hypotheses against data is decisive: agents that write residuals from the plant description alone lose up to 0.28 F1, whereas agents that see only anonymized signals recover most of the physical structure.

</details>


### [237] [Learning Actionable Information Landscapes for Multimodal Active Sensing in Hawkmoths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11011)
*Abdelrahman Sharafeldin,Yaqing Wang,Simon Sponberg,Hannah Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态预测编码框架，从视觉与机械感觉学习"动作条件下的不确定性削减地图"，并据此构建不确定性门控的字典序探索算法，解释天蛾花朵交互行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物需整合多模态感觉信息来指导探索，其内部应维持"何种动作可降低不确定性"的估计；但以往研究局限于单模态感觉框架，不清楚这类动作条件化信息地图如何从多模态经验中习得。

Method: 构建多模态预测编码框架，在模拟环境中训练变分生成模型，从视觉与机械感觉观测中推断动作空间上的模态特异性信息景观；再用该地图解释天蛾花朵追踪与蜜腺搜索行为数据，并提出基于感知不确定性在"信息获取"与"奖励利用"间切换的不确定性门控字典序算法。

Result: 模型恢复出的信息景观与天蛾若干行为特征一致（非零跟踪偏移、沿视觉图案探测、几何依赖的探索）；自然轨迹倾向分布于高不确定性削减区域；蜜腺搜索中，不确定性门控相对仅利用、ε-greedy、固定时间及不确定性校准的非字典序基线提升学习效果，并具更好泛化性与对感觉、曲率变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将生成式建模习得的信息景观与生物感知及具身探索联系起来，为多模态主动感知提供统一解释与可迁移的计算机制。

Abstract: Animals integrate information across sensory modalities to guide exploration, suggesting that they maintain internal estimates of where actions are expected to reduce uncertainty. However, how such action-conditioned information maps are learned from multimodal sensory experience remains unclear, as previous studies have been largely confined to unimodal sensing frameworks. We introduce a multimodal predictive-coding framework for learning uncertainty-reduction maps from visual and mechanosensory observations in hawkmoth flower interaction tasks. A variational generative model trained on simulated environments recovers modality-specific information landscapes over the action space, capturing how visual patterns, surface geometry, and mechanosensory cues shape the value of exploratory actions. These learned maps are consistent with several features of hawkmoth behavior, including nonzero tracking offsets, probing along visual patterns, and geometry-dependent exploration. Applying the model to behavioral data from flower tracking and nectary search, we find that natural trajectories tend to occupy regions of high uncertainty reduction in these learned maps. Building on these observations, we develop an uncertainty-gated lexicographic algorithm that switches between information gathering and reward exploitation based on perceptual uncertainty. In nectary search, uncertainty gating improves learning relative to exploit-only, $ε$-greedy, fixed-time, and uncertainty-calibrated non-lexicographic controls, with better generalization and robustness to sensory and curvature shifts. These results link information landscapes learned through generative modeling to biological sensing and embodied exploration.

</details>


### [238] [ProtLink: Sequence and Multispecies Graph Context for Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11017)
*Xingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProtLink——结合冻结ESM-2表征与蛋白互作拓扑、跨物种同源组连接的序列条件图学习框架，用于人类PPI预测，并系统比较序列模型、GraphSAGE、同构图Transformer与异构图Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭序列预测蛋白-蛋白相互作用（PPI）困难，而已有互作网络可提供有价值的生物学上下文；需明确进化图上下文的价值、深度依赖性与局限。

Method: 将冻结的ESM-2表征作为节点特征，构建含人类PPI边与各物种间正交群（orthogroup）介导连接的异构图；比较序列模型、GraphSAGE、同构图Transformer与HGT；在不同负采样策略与三随机种子控制实验下评估，并区分留出边预测与留出蛋白泛化。

Result: 随机负采样下，三层多物种HGT+ESM-2 650M达AP 0.980（同构图Transformer为0.951）；二跳偏好负采样下降至0.878。三种子实验中三层全图HGT AP为0.9701±0.0022，优于仅正交群模型（0.9435±0.0094）；两层对比显示非人PPI边增益有限，说明需更深消息传递。对训练中未出现互作的人类蛋白，多物种HGT AP为0.8335±0.0094，序列对模型仅0.6482±0.0052。

Conclusion: 进化图上下文能显著提升PPI预测，但收益依赖于消息传递深度，且留出边预测与留出蛋白泛化存在本质差异；同时揭示了该方法的性能边界。

Abstract: Predicting protein-protein interactions (PPIs) from sequence alone is difficult, while known interaction networks may provide informative biological context. We introduce ProtLink, a sequence-conditioned graph-learning framework that combines frozen ESM-2 representations with protein interaction topology and orthogroup-mediated connections between species. We compare sequence-only models, GraphSAGE, a homogeneous graph transformer, and a heterogeneous graph transformer (HGT) for human PPI prediction. In an initial edge-level comparison, three-layer multispecies HGT with ESM-2 650M features achieves an average precision (AP) of 0.980 under random negative sampling, compared with 0.951 for the homogeneous transformer; its AP decreases to 0.878 with two-hop-preferred negatives. In controlled three-seed experiments, three-layer full-graph HGT obtains an AP of $0.9701\pm0.0022$, exceeding an orthogroup-only model ($0.9435\pm0.0094$). The corresponding two-layer comparison shows little additional benefit from non-human PPI edges, consistent with the greater message-passing depth needed to transmit their information. For human proteins excluded from training interactions, multispecies HGT achieves $0.8335\pm0.0094$ AP versus $0.6482\pm0.0052$ for a sequence-only pair model. These results identify the value, depth dependence, and limits of evolutionary graph context while distinguishing prediction of held-out edges from generalization to held-out proteins.

</details>


### [239] [Unified Factored Representations Resist Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11020)
*Joel Lehman,Kenneth O. Stanley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"表征遗忘假说"：具有统一因子化表征（UFR）的网络无需修改微调算法或架构即可抵抗灾难性遗忘，因为灾难性遗忘主要源于"碎裂纠缠表征"（FER）导致的全局破坏性更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难性遗忘通常被归因于微调算法或架构缺陷；本文试图揭示一个被忽视的因素——网络内部表征的质量（统一因子化 vs. 碎裂纠缠），并检验其与遗忘程度之间的因果关系。

Method: 在1D回归任务中手工构造UFR网络，与SGD从零训练的网络对比；在图像回归任务中使用已知具备UFR的网络；在两个域中对网络进行反复、UFR兼容的变体微调，测量遗忘量；进一步用蒸馏UFR表征的训练损失把UFR表征注入新网络，并配合多项对照实验以隔离表征这一因素。

Result: 在两个设定中，当反复在UFR兼容的变体上微调时，UFR网络的遗忘显著少于FER网络；将UFR表征蒸馏进新网络的损失函数也能降低遗忘；附加对照实验支持表征是造成差异的关键因素。

Conclusion: 表征质量是灾难性遗忘的一个被低估的成因；维持这种质量可能需要关注特定网络"准备学习"何种模式，而非仅调整微调算法或架构。

Abstract: This paper explores an unexpected connection between the quality of a neural network's (NN's) internal representation and its vulnerability to catastrophic forgetting. The representational forgetting hypothesis is that networks with unified, factored representation (UFR) can resist catastrophic forgetting without any modification to the underlying fine-tuning algorithm or network architecture. The key insight is that catastrophic forgetting may largely result from fractured entangled representation (FER): fine-tuning on only part of the input domain results in disruptive changes for other input patterns, because of how individual features are globally entangled. In contrast, if a gradient update is compatible with how a UFR network factors its representation, such an update may result in coordinated change across the input domain, alleviating catastrophic forgetting. Experiments in two domains provide evidence for this hypothesis: (1) an illustrative 1D regression task, in which UFR networks constructed by hand are compared against networks trained from scratch with SGD, and (2) an image-regression task with NNs previously shown to exhibit UFR. In both settings, UFR networks forget substantially less than their FER counterparts when repeatedly fine-tuned on UFR-compatible variations. Further experiments show that a training loss that distills UFR representations into fresh NNs reduces forgetting; along with several additional controls, these results help isolate representation as the critical factor. Together these results suggest that representational quality is an underappreciated contributor to catastrophic forgetting, and that the encouragement and maintenance of such quality may require attending to what patterns a particular NN is ready to learn.

</details>


### [240] [Where Should Models Run? A Collaborative Inference Framework for Embodied Intelligence in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11023)
*Wanlu Sun,Zhenhui Yuan,Xiang Ji,Yi Han,Qing Li,Gan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文综述具身智能中的协同推理，并提出以边缘为中心的框架，通过仿真与WiFi/5G实测给出ResNet-101、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、OpenVLA-7B在Jetson端设备与AGX Thor边缘服务器间的最小时延切分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能需实时感知、推理与控制，但LLM/VLA等大模型难以直接部署在算力与能耗受限的机器人平台上，因此需要在端—边—云之间切分模型进行协同推理；而最优切分点随无线网络状况、模型结构与隐私要求变化，仍是开放问题。

Method: 综述协同推理研究现状；提出以边缘为中心的框架，由边缘侧协调模型切分与执行；在WiFi与5G网络下开展仿真与真实实验；针对ResNet-101、Qwen2.5-VL-7B与OpenVLA-7B，在与NVIDIA Jetson Nano/AGX Xavier端设备及AGX Thor边缘服务器的协同推理中，在不同无线条件下确定最小时延切分点。

Result: 实验表明最优切分策略随网络条件与模型结构而变化；论文给出了上述三个模型在不同无线条件下的时延最小化切分点。

Conclusion: 协同推理是解决具身智能大模型部署约束的有前景方案，边缘协调的切分框架在实践中可行；文章最后讨论了6G赋能的具身智能系统中协同推理的未来方向。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence systems rely on fast and reliable inference to enable real-time perception, reasoning, and control. Recent advances in large-scale artificial intelligence (AI) models, including Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have significantly enhanced the capabilities of embodied agents in complex environments. However, deploying these models remains challenging due to the limited computational resources and energy budgets of robotic platforms. A fundamental question is where inference should be executed. Collaborative inference, which partitions models across end devices, edge servers, and the cloud, has emerged as a promising solution. Yet determining the optimal partitioning point remains an open problem, as it depends on wireless network conditions, model structure, and privacy requirements. This article reviews the state of the art in collaborative inference for embodied intelligence and proposes an edge-centric framework in which the edge coordinates model partitioning and execution. Through both simulation and real-world experiments over WiFi and 5G networks, we demonstrate that the optimal partitioning strategy varies with network conditions and model structure. We further identify latency-minimizing partition points for ResNet-101, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and OpenVLA-7B in collaborative inference between NVIDIA Jetson Nano or AGX Xavier devices and an AGX Thor edge server under varying wireless conditions. Finally, we discuss the future directions for collaborative inference in 6G-empowered embodied intelligence systems.

</details>


### [241] [Phase-Conditioned Reward Shaping for Token-Efficient LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11024)
*Zhe Chen,Xiaolin Zhou,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出阶段条件奖励塑形（PCRS）框架，用阶段识别（隐状态分类器PCRS-H或冻结文本分类器PCRS-T）区分中间步骤功能，再据此调整长度惩罚。默认文本分类器配置在六个数学与科学推理基准上平均缩短响应长度3.13%，Pass@1约62.07%持平，Oracle Pass@16由86.08%升至87.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展推理有助于语言模型解决复杂问题，但长回答中既有有效计算也有冗余校验；传统长度惩罚只压短长度，不区分各中间步骤的作用，可能误伤有用推理。

Method: PCRS将阶段识别与训练期奖励分配结合：PCRS-H使用隐状态分类器，PCRS-T使用冻结文本分类器。主实现中，文本分类器对原生chunk打上重叠的"推理/执行/修订"标签，结合精确重复检测来调整已有的token级惩罚；比较完整配置于六个数学与科学推理基准，并考察四种奖励权重设置。

Result: 相对token级长度惩罚基线，默认文本分类器配置平均响应长度降低3.13%；采样Pass@1两者均约62.07%，Oracle Pass@16由86.08%提升至87.67%。

Conclusion: 阶段感知的惩罚分配可在不增加推理时分类开销的前提下减少输出冗余，并改善多次采样的覆盖能力；但长度缩减幅度有限，单一权重设置下收益较温和。

Abstract: Extended reasoning can help language models solve complex problems, but long responses often contain both useful computations and redundant checks. Conventional length penalties encourage shorter responses without distinguishing the functions of intermediate steps. We propose Phase-Conditioned Reward Shaping (PCRS), a framework that combines phase identification with training-time reward allocation. We investigate two classification routes: PCRS-H with a hidden-state classifier and PCRS-T with a frozen text classifier. Our main text classifier implementation assigns overlapping reasoning, execution, and revision labels to native chunks, then uses these labels and exact repetition to adjust existing token penalties. We compare the completed configurations on six mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks and examine four reward-weight settings with the text classifier. Compared with a baseline using token-level length penalties, the default text classifier configuration reduces mean response length by 3.13%; sampled Pass@1 is approximately 62.07% for both methods, while oracle Pass@16 rises from 86.08% to 87.67%. These results show how phase-informed penalty allocation can reduce output volume and improve multi-attempt coverage without inference-time classification.

</details>


### [242] [Hybrid Product Search Without the Copy: Lexical, Semantic and Filtered Retrieval Served Directly from a Data Lake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11031)
*Raghvendra Yadav,Xiang Fu,Chinmay Soman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将搜索应用直接托管在数据湖上的方案：以 Iceberg 表作为唯一记录系统，扩展 Apache Pinot 实现 BM25 相关性打分、全语料词项统计与 SQL 内 RRF 融合检索，消除词法引擎与向量数据库的目录副本与同步管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商目录数据已存于湖仓（Iceberg + Parquet + 对象存储），但搜索仍运行在目录副本上：词法引擎与向量数据库各自维护摄入管道、Schema、陈旧窗口与成本，导致存储冗余、重建耗时与一致性运维负担。作者希望验证搜索能否直接承载于数据湖之上。

Method: 在已支持原地查询 Iceberg 表的实时 OLAP 引擎 Apache Pinot 上扩展三点：(1) 基于 Lucene 文本索引的 BM25 相关性打分；(2) 全语料词项统计，使跨文件分片分数可比；(3) 以纯 SQL 实现 reciprocal rank fusion（RRF）混合检索。同时支持由流式摄入的实时表经 broker 合并查询，实现湖表与实时数据的统一检索。

Result: 在 Amazon ESCI 基准（120 万美国商品、22,458 条测试查询）上，湖内托管混合搜索达到与精确检索相当的 nDCG@10（0.361）。湖表文件注册后 149 秒即可查询，湖提交 3.8 秒内可搜索，接近直接索引进搜索引擎。在按品牌排序的 2.77 亿商品湖表（0.8 TB）上，单台 16 vCPU 节点品牌限定向量、BM25 与客户端融合混合搜索 p50 为 6.5–9 ms；持续承载 1,100 次向量/秒与 635 次 BM25/秒，p99 低于 180 ms；提交可搜索延迟 4.3 秒。

Conclusion: 搜索可直接构建于湖仓表之上，仅新增索引而无目录副本、无同步管道、无第二套 Schema，从而降低存储开销、缩短重建时间并减少副本一致性运维工作，且检索质量与延迟不劣于专用搜索系统。

Abstract: E-commerce catalogs increasingly live in lakehouse tables: Apache Iceberg metadata over Parquet files in object storage. Product search, however, still runs on copies of that catalog, in a lexical engine and a vector database, each with its own ingestion pipeline, schema, staleness window and bill. We show that search applications can be hosted directly on the data lake, with the lakehouse table as the single system of record. We extend Apache Pinot, a real-time OLAP engine that already serves Iceberg tables in place, with BM25 relevance scoring over its Lucene text index, corpus-wide term statistics so that scores are comparable across files, and reciprocal rank fusion in plain SQL; a real-time table fed by a stream can sit behind the same table, merged by the broker. On the Amazon ESCI benchmark (1.2M US products, 22,458 test queries), hybrid search served from the lake matches exact retrieval (nDCG@10 .361). The lake table is queryable 149 s after its files are registered, and a lake commit is searchable in 3.8 s, close to indexing directly into a search engine. On a brand-sorted lake of 277M products (0.8 TB), one 16-vCPU node answers brand-scoped vector, BM25 and client-fused hybrid searches in 6.5-9 ms (p50), sustains 1,100 vector and 635 BM25 searches per second at under 180 ms p99, and makes a commit searchable in 4.3 s. Search adds only its indexes: no copy of the catalog, no sync pipeline and no second schema, which cuts storage, rebuild time and the operational work of keeping copies consistent.

</details>


### [243] [Finite-Sample and Communication-Efficient Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11032)
*Mohammadhossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带通信约束的有向无环图多智能体学习：指出Kearns等(SODA 2026)有限样本证明的漏洞并给出新论证，同时提出用k维线性草图替代m维完整消息的共享草图协议，实现统计误差由样本量m控制、压缩误差由草图维度k控制的分离式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SODA 2026的Kearns等人在有限样本协议下，每条边传输智能体在共享样本上的完整预测向量（m维），通信代价随样本量线性增长，难以扩展；且其有限样本分析存在证明缺口。因此需要（1）修补/重建有限样本下的总体风险保证，（2）在压缩通信的前提下保持统计性能，探索除行子采样之外的其他通信—统计权衡。

Method: 1) 在确定性M-coverage条件下沿路径重新分析完整消息协议，采用与Kearns等不同的论证弥补有限样本证明缺陷；2) 提出shared-sketch协议，每个智能体发送预测向量的k维线性草图（k≪m）；3) 通过归约为压缩样本上的最小二乘问题并结合oblivious subspace embedding（OSE）引理进行分析；4) 针对高斯草图给出显式高概率总体风险界。

Result: 得到将统计项与通信项分离的界：统计误差仍由原始样本量m决定，压缩引入的额外误差由草图维度k控制；对高斯草图给出了显式的高概率总体风险上界，表明行子采样并非网络化信息聚合中唯一的通信—统计权衡方式。

Conclusion: 线性草图压缩可在保持m阶统计精度的同时显著降低边通信量，为DAG中带通信约束的分布式学习提供了新的协议与理论工具；同时修正了已有有限样本分析中的错误。

Abstract: Building on SODA 2026 work Kearns et al. (2026), we study learning in directed acyclic graphs in which each agent observes only a subset of the input coordinates and may use the predictions of its parents as additional features. In the finite-sample protocol, the message sent along each edge is the agent's prediction vector on a shared sample, so the communication cost is linear in the sample size $m$. In this paper we study this model under communication constraints.
  First, we revisit the finite-sample analysis of the full-message protocol. We identify a gap in the finite-sample proof of Kearns et al. (2026) and develop a different argument that establishes a finite-sample population-risk guarantee along paths satisfying deterministic $M$-coverage.
  Second, we introduce a shared-sketch protocol in which each agent sends a $k$-dimensional linear sketch of its prediction vector instead of the full $m$-dimensional message, where $k\ll m$. We analyze this protocol through a reduction to least squares on the compressed sample together with an oblivious subspace embedding argument. The resulting bound separates statistics from communication: the statistical term is still governed by the original sample size $m$, while the additional error due to compression is controlled by the sketch dimension $k$. For Gaussian sketches, we obtain an explicit high-probability population-risk bound, showing that row subsampling is not the only possible communication-statistics tradeoff for networked information aggregation.

</details>


### [244] [Prune to Probe: Causal Decision-Stability Probes for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11034)
*Yufei Qi,Chonghe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prune-to-Probe，一种推理时控制器：在模型给出答案后，暂时抑制对“答后继续生成”有贡献的晚期 MLP 坐标，以其停止响应相对随机干预的差异作为答案稳定性信号，决定是否停止推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常在给出答案后继续生成，但过早停止又可能中断有用的验证或修正。作者希望用模型内部计算的定向干预提供一个可靠的“何时停止推理”的信号。

Method: 识别对答后继续生成有因果贡献、但排除对答案完成重要的晚期 MLP 坐标。答案陈述后，临时抑制这些坐标，并测量相对于匹配随机干预的停止响应。干预仅作探针：模型要么返回已有答案，要么从未被修改的缓存继续生成，不永久修改模型。

Result: MATH 上减少 2.60% 的推理 token 前向，保留全部自然正确的轨迹并修复 6 个错误；MMLU-Pro 上取得 1.145% 的汇总节省且未观察到损害。进一步分析显示该干预捕捉的是决策稳定性而非正确性（AUROC 0.502）。

Conclusion: 因果激活探针可用于高效的推理时控制，无需永久修改模型；但其信号对应稳定性而非正确性，适用性受此限制。

Abstract: Reasoning models often continue generating after producing an answer, yet stopping prematurely may interrupt useful verification or revision. In this work, we investigate whether targeted interventions in a model's internal computation can provide a reliable signal for when to stop reasoning. We introduce Prune-to-Probe, an inference-time controller that uses causal activation interventions to probe the stability of a model's current answer. Specifically, we identify late-MLP coordinates that contribute to post-answer continuation while excluding those important for answer completion. Once an answer is stated, Prune-to-Probe temporarily suppresses the selected coordinates and measures the resulting stopping response relative to matched random interventions. Crucially, these interventions serve only as probes: the model either returns its existing answer or resumes generation from an untouched cache. On MATH, Prune-to-Probe reduces reasoning-token forwards by 2.60%, preserving all naturally correct trajectories while repairing six errors. On MMLU-Pro, it achieves 1.145% pooled savings with no observed harm. Further analysis shows that the intervention captures decision stability rather than correctness (AUROC 0.502). These results demonstrate the potential of causal activation probes for efficient inference-time control without permanent model modification.

</details>


### [245] [Target Recoverability under Selective Labels: A Failure Mode in ECG-Based Acute Coronary Syndrome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11063)
*Alexander Schubert,Anton Håkansson,Ziad Obermeyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示"选择性标签+不可见病例"复合偏差机制：当诊断包含模型输入无法察觉的病例（如心电图不可见的ACS亚型）时，模型会在被检测人群中"学到"看似可预测、但在未检测人群中失效甚至反号的关系；并主张用可推断的目标标签与目标部署人群评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于选择性标签（selective labels）偏差的研究多聚焦于"只有被临床医生选择检测的患者才被观察"这一问题，但较少讨论另一难点：当诊断标签本身包含了模型输入无法检测的病例时会发生什么。由于检测决策依赖模型不可见的信息，这些"不可见病例"在被检测人群中可能呈现出表面可预测性，从而产生在未检测人群中失效、甚至方向反转的关系。作者因此希望形式化该机制并在真实临床任务中验证。

Method: 1）理论/模拟：形式化"检测选择性×输入不可检测性"耦合导致的偏差机制，并通过仿真考察检测选择性增强、不可检测病例比例上升对偏差的影响，同时比较仅用可检测病例训练的模型。2）实证：以心电图（ECG）预测急性冠脉综合征（ACS）为例，ACS 需有创检查确诊且其亚型在 ECG 上的可见度不同；以血运重建为 ACS 代理标签，微调三个 ECG 基础模型，分别在被导管检查人群与未被检测人群中评估；并对比基于 ECG 可见亚型训练的模型与基于不稳定型心绞痛（可无 ECG 改变）训练的模型。

Result: 仿真显示该偏差随检测选择性提高和不可检测病例增多而加剧，而仅在可检测病例上训练的模型仍具信息量。实证中，三个微调 ECG 基础模型在导管检查人群中表现良好（AUROC 0.740–0.770），但在未检测人群的 30 天主要不良心血管事件上低于随机水平（AUROC 0.381–0.478）。基于 ECG 可见亚型训练的模型保持判别力（AUROC 0.680–0.709），而基于不稳定型心绞痛训练的模型与风险呈反向关系（AUROC 0.310）。

Conclusion: 在选择性标签条件下，有效学习不仅取决于数据量与模型容量，更要求：预测目标可由模型输入推断，且评估应在真实部署人群（常超出被检测人群）而非仅在已检测亚群中进行。否则模型可能学到仅在检测人群中成立、在目标人群中失效甚至反向的伪关系。

Abstract: Medical machine learning models often learn from diagnoses observed only in patients clinicians chose to test. The biases of such selective labels are well known, but less understood is what happens when a diagnosis includes cases the model's inputs cannot detect. Because testing decisions draw on information the model does not see, these cases can appear predictable among tested patients, producing relationships that fail, and can even reverse, among the untested. We formalize this mechanism and show in simulations that it worsens as testing becomes more selective or undetectable cases more common, while models trained only on detectable cases remain informative. We empirically study electrocardiogram (ECG)-based prediction of acute coronary syndrome (ACS), which is confirmed only by invasive testing and whose subtypes differ in how clearly they appear on the ECG. Three ECG foundation models fine-tuned on revascularization, our ACS proxy, performed well among catheterized patients (AUROC 0.740-0.770) but worse than chance for 30-day major adverse cardiovascular events among untested patients (AUROC 0.381-0.478). Models trained on ECG-visible subtypes retained discrimination (AUROC 0.680-0.709), whereas a model trained on unstable angina, which can occur without ECG changes, was inversely related to risk (AUROC 0.310). Under selective labels, effective learning therefore requires prediction targets that can be inferred from the model's inputs and evaluation in the intended deployment population, which often extends beyond the patients who were tested.

</details>


### [246] [Discrete Diffusion Playground: A 2D Benchmark for Discrete Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11068)
*Justin Shao,Sihyun Park,David Ryan Koes,Maria Chikina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Discrete Diffusion Playground"诊断性基准，将已知2D连续分布经精确离散双射编为二值token序列，从而可在解码后直接与真实分布比对，系统检验离散扩散模型是否真正学到了目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散是类别生成建模的重要框架，但现有评测协议多依赖代理指标，难以揭示模型是否真正学到了目标分布；需要一种能直接对照已知真值分布的严格诊断手段。

Method: 构建2D连续分布到二值token序列的精确离散化双射，形成"离散序列训练—解码回2D—对照真值"的评测设置；基准涵盖平滑高斯混合与带尖锐边界、不连通支撑、坐标依赖结构的倾斜棋盘分布；提供精确网格散度、Wasserstein距离、密度可视化与非参数两样本检验。并在该平台对比掩码扩散与自回归基线，附加反转/交错token顺序的对照实验。

Result: 掩码扩散能学到有意义的自适应揭示顺序，但在平滑目标上稳定性不及自回归基线，在倾斜棋盘任务上基本失败；自回归模型能保持目标几何结构。反转与交错顺序的对照实验表明，自回归的优势不能仅由有利的比特顺序解释。

Conclusion: Discrete Diffusion Playground 提供了一个简单但严苛的基准，可用于压力测试离散生成模型，并推动能检测真实分布失配而非代理性能的评测指标发展。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion is a promising framework for categorical generative modeling, but current evaluation protocols often fail to reveal whether models have actually learned the target distribution. We introduce the Discrete Diffusion Playground, a diagnostic benchmark that converts known 2D continuous distributions into binary token sequences through an exact discretized bijection. This creates a rare setting in which models are trained on discrete sequences, but generated samples can be decoded back into 2D and compared directly against a known ground-truth distribution. The benchmark spans smooth Gaussian mixtures and tilted checkerboards with sharp boundaries, disconnected support, and coordinate-dependent structure, allowing it to expose failures that are invisible in standard proxy metrics. It supports exact grid divergences, Wasserstein distances, density visualizations, and nonparametric two-sample tests, providing both quantitative evaluation and interpretable failure diagnosis. Using this testbed, we show that masked diffusion models can learn meaningful adaptive reveal orders but remain less stable than autoregressive baselines on smooth targets and largely fail on tilted checkerboards, where autoregressive models preserve the target geometry. Control experiments with reversed and interleaved token orderings show that autoregressive performance is not explained by favorable bit order alone. The Playground provides a simple but stringent benchmark for stress-testing discrete generative models and for developing evaluation metrics that detect real distributional mismatch rather than proxy performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/shaojus/DiscreteDiffusionPlayground .

</details>


### [247] [System Prompts Affect Response Bias More Than Discriminability in LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11074)
*Mohan W. Gupta,Sandy Tanwisuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出安全评测不应局限于"合规—拒答"权衡，而应考察"提示条件化的任务选择"。通过交叉8类安全任务系统提示与3种清晰度、固定基准条目、评测13个模型，用信号检测论分离辨别力与反应标准，发现系统提示主要改变拒答倾向而非辨别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评测多聚焦合规—拒答权衡（拒绝不安全请求、不拒绝安全请求），但忽视了同一请求在不同系统提示下会引发不同行为的"提示条件化任务选择"现象；且难以判断提示带来的改善究竟源于更强的安全/不安全辨别力，还是仅仅更倾向拒答。

Method: 采用受控实验设计：将8类安全任务系统提示族与3种清晰度水平交叉，覆盖HarmBench、XSTest、IHEval三个基准，保持基准条目固定；评测13个模型，并引入信号检测论（SDT）将表现分解为辨别力（discriminability）与反应标准（response criterion）两个成分；此外对拒答之外的行为进行六类行为策略的归类分析。

Result: 模型间辨别力存在显著差异；但系统提示主要移动拒答意愿（反应标准），对辨别力影响较小且不一致。提高提示清晰度使模型更倾向拒答，却未可靠提升其区分安全与不安全请求的能力。提示族还会在六类行为策略（含澄清请求、安全帮助等）之间转移响应分布。

Conclusion: 系统提示主要改变模型对安全相关请求的"如何回应"，而非一致地提升其"能否区分"安全与不安全请求的能力；因此安全评测与提示设计应区分辨别力与反应标准，并纳入拒答之外的多维行为策略。

Abstract: Large language model safety evaluations often focus on a compliance-refusal tradeoff: models should refuse unsafe requests without refusing safe ones. We argue that this misses a broader form of prompt-conditioned task selection, where the same request can elicit different behaviors depending on the system prompt. We investigate this using a controlled evaluation crossing eight safety-task prompt families with three clarity levels across HarmBench, XSTest, and IHEval. Holding benchmark items fixed, we evaluate 13 models and use signal detection theory to distinguish two possibilities: whether models become better at telling safe from unsafe requests (discriminability), or simply more willing to refuse (response criterion). We find substantial differences in discriminability across models, but system prompts primarily shift the willingness to refuse, with smaller and less consistent effects on discriminability. Increasing prompt clarity makes models more willing to refuse without reliably improving their ability to distinguish safe from unsafe requests. Beyond refusal, prompt families also shift responses among six distinct behavioral policies, including clarification and safe help. Together, these findings show that system prompts primarily change how models respond to safety-relevant requests rather than consistently improving their ability to distinguish safe from unsafe ones.

</details>


### [248] [When Calibration Data Matter in Sub-4-Bit Language Model Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11079)
*Yehong Jiang,Yen-Kuang Chen,Xinmin Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出 FragCal-DC：在两比特扫描中识别全精度准确率保留率低的“任务家族”，用其训练文档补充通用文本作为校准数据，并在六种稠密模型（1.7B–8B）、十个任务及 70B 规模上系统评估六种数据依赖量化方法，发现任务信息驱动的校准主要在最低功能位宽（两比特）带来下游准确率恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特训练后量化（PTQ）能降低语言模型内存占用，但会显著损害下游任务准确率；现有校准通常只用通用文本，而“任务相关数据能否恢复准确率、哪类量化器受益”尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 提出 FragCal-DC：先做两比特扫描，找出那些在全精度下准确率保留率低的任务家族，再用这些家族的训练文档补充通用文本构成校准集；采用二至四比特的位宽扫描界定“功能位宽区间”，并用四随机种子校准比较，在六种稠密模型（1.7B–8B）、十个任务上评估六种数据依赖量化方法（含 SignRound V2、OmniQuant、GPTQ、AWQ 等），并扩展到 70B 参数规模，辅以任务级对照实验与重构误差分析。

Result: 在各自最低功能位宽下，迭代式 SignRound V2 与 OmniQuant 在两比特获益，单次 GPTQ 与 AWQ 在三比特仅有小幅平均响应；两比特时 SignRound V2 在六模型十任务平均准确率上较通用文本校准提升 1.83 个百分点；70B 规模下 GPTQ 与 SignRound V2 在两比特均提升（通用校准基线为功能性的）。两比特 SignRound V2 的任务级对照显示恢复集中于被纳入校准的任务家族，而错误标注答案仍保留约 90% 的平均准确率增益；重构误差变化与准确率增益不一致。

Conclusion: 任务信息驱动的校准可作为低比特量化中保留下游准确率的设计原则；恢复效果取决于量化器的迭代/数据依赖程度与位宽区间，且不能简单用重构误差来预测准确率收益。

Abstract: Low-bit post-training quantization reduces language-model memory but can sharply degrade downstream task accuracy. Calibration usually relies on generic text, but when task-informed data recover accuracy and which quantizers benefit remain poorly understood. We introduce FragCal-DC, which supplements generic text with training documents from task families with low retention of full-precision accuracy in a two-bit scan. We use a two- to four-bit sweep to locate functional regimes and four-seed calibration comparisons to evaluate six data-dependent methods across six dense models (1.7B-8B) and ten tasks. At their lowest tested functional bit widths, iterative SignRound V2 and OmniQuant improve at two bits, whereas single-pass GPTQ and AWQ show small mean responses at three bits. At two bits, SignRound V2 gains 1.83 percentage points in mean ten-task accuracy over generic-text calibration across six models. At 70 billion parameters, GPTQ and SignRound V2 both improve at two bits, with functional generic-calibration baselines. For two-bit SignRound V2, task-level controls show that recovery concentrates on families included in calibration, while incorrect answer designations retain about 90% of the mean accuracy gain. Changes in reconstruction error do not consistently track accuracy gains. These findings support task-informed calibration as a design principle for preserving downstream accuracy in low-bit quantization.

</details>


### [249] [A Hierarchical Tokenization Framework for Voxel-Level fMRI Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11081)
*Karanpartap Singh,Yue Zhao,Mohammad H. Abbasi,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HBAR 是一个分层 tokenization 框架，将体素级静息态 fMRI 组织为"子体素组—脑区—大规模功能网络"的多尺度层级，实现最高约 415 倍压缩并保留细粒度空间信息，性能优于等码率扁平 tokenizer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规脑区划分（parcellation）会损失细粒度空间信息，而扁平 tokenization 缺乏多尺度结构先验；作者希望系统考察结构化先验在脑表征学习中的作用，并提出一种兼顾高压缩率与细粒度空间信息的紧凑表征方法。

Method: 提出 HBAR：对体素级 rs-fMRI 做分层 tokenization，层级为子体素组（sub-parcel voxel groups）→ 脑区（parcels）→ 大规模功能网络。支持三种层级构建方式：固定图谱驱动、完全学习、软先验引导；并以自回归方式训练，配合人口学变量读出（demographic readouts）评估表征质量。

Result: 在 HCP-YA 上，约 415 倍压缩时 HBAR 的脑区与网络相关性高于等码率（rate-matched）扁平 tokenizer；在 52 倍较低压缩下，打乱解剖分组使脑区相关性从 0.920 降至 0.886；自回归训练下的人口学读出显示层级结构带来潜在收益。

Conclusion: HBAR 结合子体素级输入、紧凑离散 token 与多尺度脑结构先验，为高维神经影像数据的表征学习提供了实用框架；结果也表明结构化解剖先验对表征质量有实质贡献。

Abstract: We introduce HBAR (Hierarchical Brain Activity Representation), a hierarchical tokenization framework for learning compact, multi-scale representations of neuroimaging data. HBAR organizes voxel-level resting-state fMRI into a hierarchy spanning sub-parcel voxel groups, parcels, and large-scale functional networks, enabling up to 415x compression while preserving fine-grained spatial information beyond conventional parcellation. The framework supports fixed atlas-based, fully learned, and softly prior-guided hierarchies, allowing us to systematically study the role of structured priors in brain representation learning. On HCP-YA, at approximately 415x compression, HBAR achieves higher parcel and network correlations than rate-matched flat tokenizers, while at a lower 52x compression rate, shuffling the anatomical grouping reduces parcel correlation from 0.920 to 0.886. Demographic readouts with autoregressive training also reveal potential gains from the hierarchical structure. By combining sub-parcel inputs, compact discrete tokens, and multiscale brain structure, HBAR offers a practical framework for representation learning in high-dimensional neuroimaging data.

</details>


### [250] [Uncertainty-Guided Reward Labeling for Reinforcement Learning under Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11091)
*Renhao Zhang,Shreyas Chaudhari,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"保留标签"型有限反馈强化学习：查询到的奖励可存入奖励模型，提出SURE方法，用后继特征选择既高不确定性又与高价值状态可达相关样本的奖励，从而以极少标注达到接近全反馈性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLLF中仅能标注离线数据集中很小一部分样本的奖励，核心问题是选哪些样本标注才能学到高性能策略。已有工作把问题形式化为奖励选择问题，但将下游策略学习视为黑箱，且假设查询到的奖励不用于训练奖励模型（不保留标签）。本文关注更实际的"保留标签"设定：查询的奖励可存储并用于拟合奖励模型，从而需要重新刻画标注预算应如何用于降低奖励模型不确定性。

Method: 先给出学习策略次优性的界，分解为两部分误差：固定离线数据集上离线RL产生的误差，以及奖励模型不确定性带来的误差。由于奖励选择无法改变离线数据集，标注预算只能用于降低奖励不确定性。据此提出SURE（Successor-Guided Uncertainty REduction）：利用后继特征（successor features）选择既具有高不确定性、又位于"可到达高价值状态"的状态上的奖励样本。方法由该界诱导的设计目标形式化推导而来，并刻画了其精确的单步边际增益。

Result: 在多个领域、多种离线RL骨干网络上，SURE用少量奖励标签即达到接近全反馈的性能。

Conclusion: 将奖励选择与奖励模型训练统一到保留标签设定下，并以次优性界为设计依据，SURE为反馈高效的强化学习提供了一种有效方法。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning from limited feedback (RLLF), only a small fraction of an offline dataset can be labeled with rewards, and the central question is which samples should be labeled to learn a high-performing policy from the resulting partially labeled dataset. Prior work formalized this as a reward-selection problem by focusing on the selection stage while treating downstream policy learning as a black box, in a regime where queried rewards are not retained for reward-model training. We instead study the retained-label setting, where queried rewards can be stored and used to fit a reward model before policy learning. We bound the suboptimality of the learned policy in terms of two sources of error: one arising from offline RL on a fixed, offline dataset, and the other from uncertainty in the reward model. Since reward selection cannot change the offline dataset, the limited labeling budget must be used to strategically reduce reward uncertainty. Motivated by RLLF's observation that useful rewards tend to keep the agent on high-return trajectories, we introduce Successor-Guided Uncertainty REduction (SURE), a novel method that uses successor features to select rewards that are both uncertain and associated with states from which high-valued states are reachable. We formally derive SURE from a bound-induced design objective and characterize its exact one-step marginal gain. Empirically, SURE reaches near full-feedback performance with few reward labels across a variety of domains and offline-RL backbones, yielding an effective method for feedback-efficient reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [251] [Task presentation and execution paths shape LLM mathematical performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11102)
*Min Huang,Mingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过配对对照实验，把数学推理表现拆分为提交要求、草稿复用与候选答案可得性三个因素，发现失败提交中的生成文本仍有条件性价值：草稿与候选答案均显著提升答对率，但二者作用呈次可加性，且受保留哪段文本影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的数学表现不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于它如何接收信息、如何利用生成的文本以及如何提交答案；这三者此前常被混在一起评估，缺乏可分离的因果性分析。作者希望明确"提交格式要求"与"失败尝试中残留的生成文本"各自带来多少收益、收益是否可叠加。

Method: 采用配对比较（paired comparisons）设计，将提交要求、草稿复用（draft reuse）与候选答案可用性（candidate availability）相互分离。在65道开发题上对比两种指令：一种允许继续推导，另一种要求单行提交；随后在固定提交协议下，针对198道初次提交失败的问题，分别提供原题、草稿、候选答案、二者兼有；并比较保留尾部摘要与头部摘要的效果，最后在第二个补全模型及"初次提交可解析"的队列上做重复与对照。

Result: 允许继续推导的指令在390次短输出调用中产生0个有效最终答案，而单行提交指令产出390个格式合法输出（其中389个可解析）。在固定提交协议下，198道失败题的正确率分别为：仅题目9.09%、加草稿33.84%、加候选46.46%、二者兼有59.60%；控制候选时草稿增益13.13个百分点，控制草稿时候选增益25.76个百分点，经Holm校正后均显著，且准确率尺度上呈次可加性。保留尾部摘录比保留相近平均长度的头部摘录高10.10个百分点（Holm校正p=0.027）。第二个补全模型在固定材料上呈现相同方向。在初次提交可解析的队列中，草稿存在时候选答案未增加任何正确答案，草稿缺失时增加27个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成文本具有条件性价值：一次失败的提交仍可能留下有用材料，但其贡献取决于同时还提供了什么、以及保留文本的哪一部分。评测与提示设计须将提交格式、草稿复用与候选信息分别控制，否则会混淆测量结果。

Abstract: Mathematical performance depends on how a language model receives information, uses generated text and submits an answer. We study these dependencies through paired comparisons that separate submission requirements, draft reuse and candidate availability. On 65 development problems, an instruction permitting continued derivation produced no valid final answers in 390 short-output calls; a single-line submission instruction produced 390 format-valid outputs, including 389 parseable answers. Under the fixed submission protocol, 198 problems with failed initial submissions were answered correctly in 9.09p of cases with the question alone, 33.84p with its draft, 46.46p with candidates, and 59.60p with both. Drafts added 13.13 percentage points with candidates fixed, and candidates added 25.76 points with drafts fixed; both effects were significant after Holm correction and were subadditive on the accuracy scale. Retaining a trailing excerpt outperformed retaining a leading excerpt of similar average length by 10.10 points Holm-adjusted p 0.027. A second completion model showed the same directions of input effects on the fixed materials. In a cohort with parseable initial submissions, candidates added no correct answers when the previous draft was supplied, but added 27 points when it was absent. These results establish a conditional value of generated text: a failed submission can leave useful material, whose contribution depends on what else is supplied and what is retained.

</details>


### [252] [Jointly Optimizing Memory, Computing and Offloading for Energy-Efficient DNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11106)
*Yunchu Han,Zhaojun Nan,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文面向边缘智能系统中的DNN推理任务，提出一种联合优化内存、计算与卸载的能效方案。将带截止时间约束的能耗最小化问题建模为MINLP，利用卸载策略固定下的问题分解、几何规划与凸优化求解，并以坐标下降算法联合优化全部变量，实现近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘智能系统多通过计算卸载与DVFS提升能效，但DNN推理等应用复杂度提升使内存资源需求显著增加，内存已成为同时影响时延与能耗的关键瓶颈，而现有方案通常忽略内存频率这一维度。

Method: 将截止时间约束下的能耗最小化问题形式化为混合整数非线性规划（MINLP），核心难点在于卸载决策与资源优化之间的耦合。在给定卸载策略下将原问题分解为两个子问题：本地子问题转化为等价的几何规划（GP）问题，求得最优内存频率与计算频率；边缘子问题用凸优化求解；进而设计高效的坐标下降（CD）算法联合优化所有变量。

Result: 仿真表明所提算法性能接近最优，与最优策略的差距至多9.3%；相比未做内存频率优化的基准方案，设备能耗最多降低16.1%。

Conclusion: 联合考虑内存频率、计算频率与卸载决策可有效缓解内存瓶颈、提升DNN推理能效，所提分解加坐标下降的求解框架在保证近最优性的同时具有良好计算效率。

Abstract: In edge intelligence systems, computation offloading and dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) are widely used to improve energy efficiency. However, as applications like deep neural network (DNN) inference evolve in complexity, their demands for memory resources have progressively increased, establishing memory as a critical bottleneck that impacts both latency and energy consumption. In this paper, we propose a joint optimization scheme integrating memory, computing and offloading for energy-efficient DNN inference. We formulate the energy minimization problem under deadline constraints as a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, where the main challenge arises from the coupling between offloading and resource optimization. To address this issue, we decompose the original problem into two subproblems under a fixed offloading policy. The local subproblem is transformed into an equivalent geometric programming (GP) problem to derive the optimal memory frequency and computing frequency, while the edge subproblem is solved via convex optimization. An efficient coordinate descent (CD) algorithm is further developed to jointly optimize all variables. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed algorithm achieves near-optimal performance with the gap from the optimal policy being at most 9.3%, and reduces the energy consumption of devices by up to 16.1% compared with benchmarks without memory frequency optimization.

</details>


### [253] [Compositional Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Stage LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11114)
*Yuxia Sun,Jiahuang Wang,Haoting Liu,Zhiwei Huang,Tingrui Pei,Saiqin Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CompCRC框架，为预先指定的多阶段LLM级联在错误风险与推理成本之间提供可认证的平衡：将接受错误的风险分解为各阶段的加性贡献，并把局部预算与系统级保证挂钩，在条件可交换下给出有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段LLM级联能平衡准确率与推理成本，但可靠部署还需要控制错误风险并识别风险在各阶段的来源；现有的全局风险控制方法无法给出阶段级的风险归因与预算分配依据。

Method: 构造并冻结一组候选的预算分配与接受阈值，在独立数据上选择其中一个候选，再在另行留出的数据上认证其各阶段风险贡献；在条件可交换性假设下，对完整部署流程的边际期望接受错误风险给出有限样本上界不超过全局风险预算α。

Result: 在α=0.45下，CompCRC三个阶段在MMLU、GSM8K、HellaSwag上均通过局部检验；与同为系统级认证的Global-CRC相比，观测接受错误风险降低1.33–2.00个百分点，最终准确率提升0.67–2.00个百分点，HellaSwag上加权推理成本降低30.37%；基于成本的预算分配使单位成本准确率比均匀分配提升10.6%–46.7%。

Conclusion: CompCRC在保持系统级风险保证的同时实现了更优的风险-准确率-成本权衡，且阶段级统计量可作为诊断工具定位未通过局部检验的阶段。

Abstract: Multi-stage large language model (LLM) cascades balance accuracy and inference cost, but reliable deployment also requires controlling error risk and identifying its sources across stages. We propose Compositional Conformal Risk Control (CompCRC), a risk-control framework for pre-specified multi-stage LLM cascades. We decompose the risk of accepting incorrect answers into additive stage-wise contributions over the full query population, linking local risk budgets to a system-level guarantee. CompCRC constructs and freezes candidate budget allocations and acceptance thresholds, selects one candidate on independent data, and certifies its stage-wise risk contributions on separate held-out data. Under conditional exchangeability, we establish a finite-sample guarantee: the marginal expected accepted-error risk of the complete deployment procedure does not exceed the global risk budget. At the reported comparison budget of $α=0.45$, all three stages of CompCRC pass their local checks on MMLU, GSM8K, and HellaSwag. Compared with Global-CRC, which passes system-level risk certification at the same budget, CompCRC reduces observed accepted-error risk by 1.33--2.00 percentage points, improves final accuracy by 0.67--2.00 percentage points, and reduces weighted inference cost by 30.37\% on HellaSwag. The selected cost-based budget allocation also improves accuracy per unit cost by 10.6\%--46.7\% over uniform allocation. Further experiments show that stage-wise statistics identify stages that fail local certification checks, providing diagnostic information alongside system-level risk control.

</details>


### [254] [When gene summaries reveal the answers: Gene Ontology circularity in gene embedding benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11118)
*Jiawei Wu,Huishu Wang,Junlin Chen,Hongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示基因文本嵌入评测存在"标注泄漏"：Alliance of Genome Resources 生成的 GO 注释摘要被写入 NCBI Gene 并进入 GenePT 输入，而 GO 注释本身正是评测标签，导致 GenePT 等嵌入模型的性能被高估（1–10 篇文献基因的 AUROC 下降 0.078）。作者提出 GeneProv 以在匹配文本上公平比较编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因文本嵌入（如 GenePT）常用基因摘要作为输入，并以 GO 注释衍生的功能预测任务作为评测基准。但部分摘要本身由 GO 注释自动生成，因而评测标签可能已"泄漏"进输入文本，使性能估计失真。作者希望量化这种泄漏造成的性能高估，并建立更公平的编码器比较框架。

Method: 统计 Alliance of Genome Resources 将 GO 注释转为句子后覆盖的基因比例；对同一语料分别在有/无生成摘要两个版本上训练嵌入，并重新拟合功能预测探针（线性探针与多层感知机），比较 AUROC 变化（bootstrap 区间）；辅以 TF-IDF 编码器、仅用实验注释评测、用 UniProt 功能描述替换生成摘要等对照；在小鼠与酵母中做类似分析；提出 GeneProv 用于匹配文本下的编码器比较。

Result: 生成摘要覆盖 19,256 个人类蛋白编码基因中的 6,172 个（32.1%），占拥有 1–10 篇关联文献基因的 58%。去除摘要后，1–10 篇文献基因的 per-gene AUROC 在线性探针下下降 0.078（95% bootstrap 区间 0.072–0.084），MLP 下下降 0.033（注：所用历史 GO 打分器存在并列处理缺陷，原预测不可重打分）。TF-IDF 编码器同样受益于摘要，仅用实验注释评测时亦如此；用 UniProt 功能描述替换生成摘要可恢复大部分损失性能，尽管逐字重叠很少（且 UniProt 本身也策划了大量评测标签）。小鼠与酵母中观察到对描述来源的类似依赖。

Conclusion: 基因文本嵌入基准中存在由摘要生成方式导致的标签泄漏，使 GenePT 类模型性能被显著高估，且该问题跨物种、跨编码器、跨评测终点普遍存在。GeneProv 提供匹配文本的对照协议，用于考察表征排序在 GO 召回、非 GO 终点与共必需性（co-essentiality）任务间如何变化。

Abstract: A gene summary can contain the very annotations used to evaluate an embedding of its text. The Alliance of Genome Resources converts Gene Ontology (GO) annotations into sentences, which NCBI Gene displays and GenePT includes in its input. These summaries covered 6,172 of 19,256 human protein-coding genes (32.1%), including 58% of genes with one to ten linked publications. We embedded the same corpus with and without the generated summaries and refitted a function-prediction probe on each version. Among genes with one to ten publications, removal reduced the reported per-gene area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) by 0.078 with a linear probe (95% bootstrap interval 0.072-0.084) and by 0.033 with a multilayer perceptron. These estimates use a historical GO scorer that mishandled ties. The original predictions are unavailable for rescoring. A simple TF-IDF encoder also benefited from the summaries, as did models evaluated only on experimental annotations. Replacing generated summaries with UniProt function descriptions recovered much of the lost performance despite little verbatim overlap. UniProt also curates many of the evaluation labels. In mouse and yeast, we found a similar dependence on the source of the description. We introduce GeneProv to compare encoders on matched text and to examine how representation rankings change across GO recovery, non-GO endpoints and co-essentiality.

</details>


### [255] [DISCOVER: Online Variance-Guided Data Discovery for Budgeted Multimodal GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11145)
*Peyman Gholami,Shivam Chandhok,Saber Malekmohammadi,Shayan Shams,Robert Xiao,Leonid Sigal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISCOVER 是一个面向预算受限多模态 GRPO 的在线数据发现框架，通过语义聚类、奖励迁移与动态效用估计，在仅使用 10% 唯一样本的严格预算下显著提升视觉语言模型的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（如 GRPO）在数据与算力上效率低下，大量 rollout 与标注预算被消耗在几乎不提供学习信号的提示上。因此需要一种能在训练过程中动态识别“对当前策略最有价值”提示的数据选择机制。

Method: 方法分四步：(1) 将多模态样本池按语义聚类，查询少量密度多样（density-diverse）的代表样本以获得初始奖励观测；(2) 训练中通过稀疏奖励反馈的迁移，估计未标注样本的效用；(3) 因样本效用随策略演化而变化，采用近期加权（recency-weighted）的 rollout 观测动态更新效用估计；(4) 以兼顾奖励方差与难度的策略条件分数（policy-conditioned score）仅挑选高效用提示进行标注，预算耗尽后转为基于效用的回放（utility-based replay）。

Result: 在 ViRL39K 与 VLAA-Thinking 数据集、Qwen3-VL-2B 模型上，DISCOVER 在 10% 唯一样本预算下显著提升多模态数学推理表现，优于预算对齐的随机选择、难例采样、同等训练步数预算下的全池 GRPO 以及在线选择基线；消融实验验证了语义覆盖、在线奖励迁移与稳定发现节奏的重要性。

Conclusion: DISCOVER 为数据高效的多模态强化学习后训练提供了一个实用框架，证明在严格标注预算下，结合语义覆盖与策略感知的在线效用估计能够显著优于传统均匀或启发式采样策略。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning have made verifiable rewards a central post-training paradigm for improving multimodal reasoning. However, methods such as GRPO remain data- and compute-inefficient, spending rollout and annotation budget on prompts that provide little learning signal. We propose DISCOVER, an online data-discovery framework for budgeted multimodal GRPO that enables vision-language models to dynamically identify prompts that are most useful for the current policy. DISCOVER first organizes the multimodal sample pool into semantic clusters and queries a small set of density-diverse representative samples to obtain initial reward observations. During training, it estimates the utility of unannotated samples by transferring sparse reward feedback from queried samples. Since sample utility changes as the policy evolves, DISCOVER dynamically updates these estimates with recency-weighted rollout observations. It then selects only high-utility prompts for annotation using a policy-conditioned score that accounts for reward variance and difficulty. Once the discovery budget is exhausted, DISCOVER continues with utility-based replay. Experiments on ViRL39K and VLAA-Thinking with Qwen3-VL-2B show that DISCOVER substantially improves multimodal mathematical reasoning under a strict 10% unique-sample budget, outperforming budget-matched random selection, hard-example sampling, full-pool GRPO under the same training-step budget, and online selection baselines. Ablations further validate the importance of semantic coverage, online reward transfer, and stable discovery cadence, establishing DISCOVER as a practical framework for data-efficient multimodal RL post-training.

</details>


### [256] [ZarrFM: A Canonical Tensor Storage Architecture for EEG Foundation-Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11149)
*Dheeraj Chintapalli,Sunkalp Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ZarrFM，一种面向执行的脑电（EEG）语料存储表示，作为 BIDS 的补充，将语料存为单一 Zarr v3 层级，在大规模 EEG 预训练场景下兼顾紧凑、可互操作与高效读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCI 统计模型需要大规模、异构的 EEG 语料，但现有数据集在采样率、通道布局、窗口长度与存储格式上不一致，带来大量预处理与 I/O 开销；BIDS 侧重描述而非执行效率。

Method: ZarrFM 以单个 Zarr v3 层级存储语料，含带索引的元数据、规范化通道空间、显式掩码、哈希稳定的受试者划分、经校验的事件表与块对齐分片。在真实 PhysioNet 记录与合成语料上，与 EDF、FIF、NPY、HDF5、Parquet 对比评测。

Result: 200 条记录下，两级索引将元数据查询从 601 次降至 14 次；保留原生信号编码使存储由 EDF 的 42.4 MB/小时降至 22.7 MB/小时；块对齐采样达 3,131 窗口/秒，比 Parquet 快至 11 倍；读取在不同 Zarr 实现间位级一致，并发注入故障的追加可无数据丢失恢复。

Conclusion: ZarrFM 可为大规模 EEG 预训练提供紧凑、可互操作且执行高效的存储层。

Abstract: Statistical models for brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) increasingly require large, heterogeneous EEG corpora, yet existing datasets vary in sampling rates, channel layouts, window lengths, and storage formats, creating substantial preprocessing and I/O overhead. We propose ZarrFM, an execution-oriented representation that complements BIDS by storing a corpus as a single Zarr v3 hierarchy with indexed metadata, canonical channel spaces, explicit masks, hash-stable subject splits, verified event tables, and block-aligned sharding. On real PhysioNet recordings and synthetic corpora, we compare ZarrFM with EDF, FIF, NPY, HDF5, and Parquet. Across 200 recordings, its two-level index reduces metadata queries from 601 requests to 14. Preserving native signal codes reduces storage from 42.4 MB/hour in EDF to 22.7 MB/hour, while block-aligned sampling reaches 3,131 windows/s, up to 11 times faster than Parquet. Reads remain bit-identical across independent Zarr implementations, and concurrent failure-injected appends recover without data loss. These results show that ZarrFM can provide a compact, interoperable, and execution-efficient storage layer for large-scale EEG pretraining.

</details>


### [257] [Full Fine-Tuning is not the Problem: Why Adam Fails and SGD Succeeds in Few-Shot CLIP Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11152)
*Nairouz Mrabah,Ismail Ben Ayed,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 少样本CLIP适配的失败主因是优化器诱导的不可靠预条件（尤其Adam的二阶矩低估），而非稠密全微调本身；纯SGD全微调在11个数据集上优于代表性PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内普遍将少样本CLIP适配视为参数化问题，认为全微调必然过拟合，因而偏好PEFT。本文指出该结论混淆了参数化与优化，需要重新审视失败根源。

Method: 在11个数据集、3种CLIP骨干、5个shot设置下系统对比稠密全微调（SGD/Adam/AdamW）与代表性PEFT、Shampoo、SOAP；通过诊断性干预（将Adam首步更新预算对齐SGD、分子侧对齐）、插值探针与曲率探针定位失效机制，并给出理论刻画少样本估计误差被放大为不安全更新的过程。

Result: 在报告协议下，SGD全微调优于PEFT，而Adam/AdamW崩溃；崩溃源于少样本支持集二阶矩在初始化时即系统性低估全数据统计量，经逆平方根归一化放大为过大自适应步长；对齐首步更新预算可消除大部分崩溃，分子侧对齐无解释力，Shampoo/SOAP亦失效。插值探针显示低损失连通，方向曲率良好。

Conclusion: 少样本CLIP适配的主要失败源是优化器诱导的预条件不可靠，而非稠密全微调本身；SGD因规避不可靠预条件并贴近预训练CLIP而稳健。应以优化器设计而非仅参数化选择作为改进重点。

Abstract: Few-shot CLIP adaptation is often treated as a parameterization problem. Full fine-tuning is assumed to overfit, so parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods are preferred. In this paper, we show that this conclusion conflates parameterization with optimization. Across 11 datasets, 3 CLIP backbones, and 5 shot levels, dense full fine-tuning with plain SGD outperforms representative PEFT methods under the reported evaluation protocol, whereas Adam/AdamW collapses. We trace this collapse to unreliable support-set preconditioning. The few-shot second moment systematically underestimates the corresponding full-data statistic, already at initialization. Adam's inverse-square-root normalization converts this error into an oversized adaptive update. Matching Adam's first-step update budget to SGD resolves most of the collapse, whereas numerator-side alignment does not explain the failure. Shampoo and SOAP also fail, so richer preconditioning alone does not resolve the problem. Theory formalizes how few-shot estimation error is amplified into unsafe updates. SGD succeeds because it avoids unreliable preconditioners and stays close to pretrained CLIP. Interpolation probes show low-loss connections along tested directions, and curvature probes show favorable directional curvature. These results identify optimizer-induced preconditioning, not dense full fine-tuning itself, as the main source of failure in few-shot CLIP adaptation.

</details>


### [258] [The Race Is Decided Early! A Kernel Law Predicts Lock-In in Majority-Vote Test-Time RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11165)
*Jaeho Hwang,Sehong Park,Daejin Choi,Uiwon Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究用多数投票奖励进行测试时强化学习（TTRL）时，语言模型对每个问题会"锁定"到某一答案（难题上常为错误答案）这一现象，证明锁定结果在训练早期由采样噪声驱动的一场"竞赛"决定，并给出各结果概率的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTRL 用多数投票作为奖励，在无标注问题上训练语言模型，但每个问题在训练中会锁定到单一答案，且只有训练结束后才知道锁定了哪个（难题上往往是错误答案）。这种"锁定"现象缺乏理论刻画，无法事先预判，因此作者希望揭示锁定发生的机制并预测其结果。

Method: 作者将锁定过程建模为起始阶段接近的答案之间由采样噪声驱动的短程竞争：(1) 在答案各自拥有独立 logit 的情形下，用正规尾概率刻画次席答案获胜的概率，其尺度由显式噪声常数决定；(2) 在网络情形下，答案 logit 依赖共享参数，通过参数核（kernel），落后答案的更新会改变两个领先者之间的差距，即"溢出效应"（spillover），从而移动各领先者获胜区域的边界；(3) 给出闭式公式，由 kernel probe 与起始 logits 计算被移动后的概率，无需拟合常数。在 LoRA 实验中用两个家族三个模型验证：训练前、核冻结在初始值的预测，与训练后拟合的 logistic 模型对比。

Result: 训练前（核冻结于初值）做出的预测，比用训练结果重新拟合的 logistic 模型更好地预测每个问题最终锁定的答案，且不使用任何拟合常数；在训练前检查确认两个领先者始终高于其他答案的那些运行中，闭式公式与预测吻合；该结果同时解释了为何忽略溢出效应的规律需要被大幅放大的步长。

Conclusion: TTRL 的答案锁定结果在训练极早期即被决定，可由起始 logits 与参数核的探测在训练前定量预测；共享参数导致的溢出效应是刻画锁定边界的关键，忽略它会使既有规律失效并需要人为放大步长。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning with majority-vote rewards trains a language model on unlabeled questions by rewarding the samples that agree with its most frequent answer. During training, each question locks into one answer, on hard questions often a wrong one, but which answer it locks into is known only after training. In this paper, we show that this outcome is decided early, in a short race driven by sampling noise between the answers that are close at the start, and we derive the probability of each outcome. With a separate logit for each answer, the runner-up wins with a normal tail probability of the initial gap, scaled by an explicit noise constant. In a network, however, the answer logits depend on shared parameters, and through the kernel of these parameters the updates of the trailing answers also change the gap of the two leaders. This spillover shifts the boundary between the starts won by each leader, and a closed form computes the shifted probability from a kernel probe and the start logits. In LoRA experiments with three models from two families, predictions made before training with the kernel frozen at its start value forecast which answer each question locks into better than a logistic model refitted on the outcomes, although they use no fitted constants. The closed form matches these predictions on the runs in which a check before training finds that the two leaders stay above the other answers, and it explains why a law that ignores the spillover needs a strongly inflated step.

</details>


### [259] [ClusLoad: Cluster-Guided Model Sharing for Scalable Distribution Transformer Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11170)
*Fangzhou Lin,Guoshun He,Yi Wang,S. Supraja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出趋势聚类预测框架：用T-Rep学习变压器负荷序列的紧凑时序表示，GMM聚类后每簇用代表变压器训练PatchTST模型，兼顾精度与部署可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模配电变压器设备级负荷预测面临显著的跨设备异质性，且维护设备专属模型部署负担重。现有方法或依赖难以扩展到大规模变压器群体的单设备模型，或采用全局模型降低部署复杂度但忽视设备间异质负荷模式，精度与可扩展性难以兼顾。

Method: 构建趋势聚类预测框架：采用T-Rep（基于时间嵌入的时序表示学习）学习变压器负荷序列的紧凑时序表示；以高斯混合模型（GMM）对表示进行聚类；在每簇内采样代表变压器训练簇专属PatchTST模型，同时保留设备级预测。基于近千台配电变压器真实15分钟运行数据评估。

Result: 相对直接PatchTST基线，MAE降低约20%；高负荷预警准确率达95.66%；仅需7个簇专属预测模型。

Conclusion: 基于行为的设备组织方式在预测精度、部署可扩展性与面向风险的运行支持之间实现了有效平衡，适用于配电变压器管理。

Abstract: Large-scale device-level load forecasting for distribution transformers is challenged by substantial cross-device heterogeneity and the deployment burden of maintaining device-specific forecasting models. Existing approaches typically rely either on device-specific models, which are difficult to scale to large transformer populations, or on global models, which reduce deployment complexity but may overlook heterogeneous load patterns across devices. To address this trade-off, this study develops a trend-clustered forecasting framework that enables model sharing among transformers with similar temporal behaviors. Representation Learning for Time Series using Time-Embeddings (T-Rep) is employed to learn compact temporal representations of transformer load series, which are subsequently grouped using a Gaussian mixture model (GMM). Representative transformers are sampled within each cluster to train cluster-specific Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) models while retaining device-level predictions. The framework is evaluated using real-world 15-min operational data from nearly one thousand distribution transformers. The proposed framework reduces mean absolute error (MAE) by approximately 20% relative to the direct PatchTST baseline, achieves a high-load warning accuracy of 95.66%, while requiring only seven cluster-specific forecasting models. These results demonstrate that behavior-based device organization provides an effective balance between forecasting accuracy, deployment scalability, and risk-oriented operational support for distribution transformer management.

</details>


### [260] [TEAM: Topology-Aware, Semantic-Sensitive, General Transformer for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11189)
*Yushuo Li,Xunkai Li,Zhengyu Wu,Ronghua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TEAM框架，以"节点-模态token"为基本交互单元构建并编码多模态属性图的图上下文，在多项图学习与跨模态生成指标上取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态属性图（MAG）方法以节点级状态和拓扑邻域组织传播，导致细粒度模态贡献被掩盖：有限邻域内可能包含语义弱证据，而有效上下文未必局限于该邻域。

Method: TEAM将局部拓扑展开为Modality Encoding Tree，进行树锚定的语义证据获取，在组织局部结构上下文的同时把可访问证据空间扩展到Tree之外；再引入Modality-Source Context Transformer（MSCT），编码选中的节点-模态token并协调模态-来源组间交互，文本与视觉属性保持为独立交互单元直至读出。

Result: 在所评估基线中，TEAM在所有图学习指标、全部三项G2Text指标与两项G2Image指标上均取得最高均值；在Toys数据集上，Accuracy与Macro-F1分别领先最强基线3.37和2.93个百分点。

Conclusion: 以模态为中心、延迟整节点特征坍缩的建模范式可有效提升多模态属性图的表征与跨模态生成性能。

Abstract: Multimodal-Attributed Graphs (MAGs) organize heterogeneous data in graphs whose nodes are characterized by modalities such as text and images rather than traditional unimodal attributes. However, many representative MAG methods organize propagation around node-level states and topology-defined neighborhoods, which can obscure fine-grained modality contributions: a bounded neighborhood may contain semantically weak evidence, while compatible context need not be confined to it. To address both limitations, we propose TEAM, a modality-centric framework that constructs and encodes graph context with node-modality tokens rather than treating each node as a single interaction unit. Concretely, TEAM unfolds the local topology into a Modality Encoding Tree and performs tree-anchored semantic evidence acquisition, organizing local structural context while expanding the accessible evidence space outside the constructed local Tree. We further introduce the Modality-Source Context Transformer (MSCT), which encodes the selected node-modality tokens and coordinates interactions among modality-source groups.
