By retaining textual and visual attributes as separate interaction units until readout, it supports fine-grained cross-node cross-modal exchange without first collapsing them into whole-node features. Across the evaluated baselines, TEAM attains the highest reported mean on all graph-learning metrics, all three G2Text metrics, and both G2Image metrics. On Toys, it surpasses the strongest baseline by 3.37 and 2.93 percentage points in Accuracy and Macro-F1, respectively.

</details>


### [261] [Not Yet in the Profile: Uncertainty-Aware Dynamic User Profiling by Learning from Future Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11204)
*Jieyong Kim,Neil Shah,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的自适应画像管理器 UAPM，用 Profile、Buffer、Rulebook 三部分分离"已确认的用户特征"与"尚未确认的新行为解读"，并以特权在线策略蒸馏 POPD-DS 训练，在三个网页个性化任务上同输入预算下最高超越最佳基线 5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页个性化中，动态用户画像需随行为与偏好变化更新，但新行为往往具有歧义性，且同一行为的解读因人而异；若直接把歧义证据写入画像，会污染紧凑表示、降低个性化效果。

Method: UAPM 将画像拆为三模块：Profile 存有支撑的一般性与情境性特征，Buffer 暂存歧义证据待后续决策，Rulebook 记录用户特有的解读与更新规则。训练采用 POPD-DS 两阶段：第一阶段 Dual Specialization，把强模型蒸馏为仅用当前信息的 Student 与可获取未来信息的 Teacher；第二阶段 POPD，仅固定 Teacher 接收未来交互，在任务奖励之外引导 Student 自身的管理决策；部署时 Student 不访问未来信息。

Result: 在三个网页个性化任务上，在相同下游输入预算下 UAPM 优于多个基线，相较最佳同预算基线最高提升 5%。

Conclusion: 将歧义证据与已确认结论分离、并借助用户特定规则与"训练期特权信息蒸馏"来训练画像管理策略，是提升动态用户画像质量的有效途径。

Abstract: On the Web, user profiling builds compact user representations from interaction histories for efficient personalization, and dynamic user profiling updates them as users' behavior and preferences change. However, new user behavior can be ambiguous, and its interpretation differs across users. To address this, we introduce Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Profile Manager (UAPM), which separates the claims in a user profile from interpretations of new behavior that are not yet confirmed, through a Profile, a Buffer, and a Rulebook. UAPM records user-specific interpretation and update rules in the Rulebook, keeps ambiguous evidence in the Buffer outside the Profile as evidence for later decisions, and writes supported general and situational characteristics to the Profile. We train UAPM with Privileged On-Policy Distillation with Dual Specialization (POPD-DS), which learns from recorded future interactions during training without access to them at deployment. Its first stage, Dual Specialization, distills a stronger model into a current-only Student and a future-informed model that becomes the Teacher. In the second stage, privileged on-policy distillation (POPD), only the fixed Teacher receives future interactions and guides the Student's own management decisions alongside task rewards. Experiments on three Web personalization tasks show that UAPM outperforms multiple baselines under the same downstream input budget, improving on the best same-budget baseline by up to 5%.

</details>


### [262] [scBIG++: Learning Across Genes and Cells to Predict Single-Cell Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11217)
*Jiafa Ruan,Zican Qiao,Zongxin Yang,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: scBIG++是一个全基因扰动预测框架，通过将表达自适应基因模块与群体条件生成相结合来建模细胞异质性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动预测研究多只关注高变基因，限制了可用于下游分析的表达变化范围；同时同一扰动下不同细胞的表达响应存在异质性，表现为协同基因程序以不同强度激活，而现有方法难以兼顾共享基因结构与细胞间变异。

Method: 提出三部分设计：(1) Gene-Relation Clustering将基因组织为公共窗口，并在窗口内用Dynamic SoftSub学习依赖表达的子模块隶属关系，以在容纳细胞变异的同时保留共享基因结构；(2) Set-GCAE在该共享组织基础上，跨对照细胞交换对应基因群信息，将个体表征置于其源群体背景中；(3) 以细胞表征初始化隐空间流，并由其池化摘要与扰动表征共同条件化响应生成，解码得到覆盖全部测定蛋白编码基因的表达谱。

Result: 在六个遗传与化学扰动设置（涉及6,539--19,451个基因）上实验，在未见靶点与组合、跨细胞系迁移及留出化合物条件下，均表现出有竞争力的扰动效应恢复与全基因表达重建能力。

Conclusion: 以全基因建模替代仅高变基因的范式，结合共享基因模块组织与群体条件生成，可有效刻画细胞异质性扰动响应并实现全基因表达预测；代码已开源。

Abstract: Predicting single-cell transcriptional responses to genetic and chemical interventions supports gene-function discovery and regulatory analysis. Existing studies often focus only on highly variable genes, restricting the expression changes available for downstream analysis. Cells exposed to the same intervention can also show heterogeneous expression responses, with coordinated gene programs active to different degrees. We propose scBIG++, a full-gene perturbation prediction framework that models this heterogeneity by connecting expression-adaptive gene modules with population-conditioned generation. To preserve shared gene structure while accommodating cellular variation, Gene-Relation Clustering organizes genes into common windows, within which Dynamic SoftSub learns expression-dependent submodule memberships. This shared organization enables Set-GCAE to exchange information about corresponding gene groups across control cells, placing individual representations in their source-population context. The cell representations initialize a latent flow, while their pooled summary and the perturbation representation condition response generation. Generated states are decoded into expression profiles covering all assayed protein-coding genes retained after preprocessing, supporting comprehensive analysis of perturbation-induced expression changes. Experiments across six genetic and chemical settings spanning 6,539--19,451 genes demonstrate competitive perturbation-effect recovery and full-gene expression reconstruction under unseen targets and combinations, cross-cell-line transfer, and held-out compounds. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/ttruan2426-dot/scBIG.

</details>


### [263] [EbbFlow: PINN-Inspired Progressive Curriculum for MeanFlow Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11220)
*Mengfan Yuan,Xunheng Kuang,Jianbin Chen,Lei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将采样时间坐标视为配点，用TSA调度对角/区间监督，EbbFlow在不改变模型与推理的前提下提升少步生成FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow用区间平均速度实现少步生成，但其固定的时间采样器在整个训练过程中混合对角（边界）监督与内部（差分一致性）监督，二者作用机制未被区分，缺乏可控的训练调度设计。

Method: 把时间坐标解释为配点：对角样本提供随机边界目标，有限区间提供差分一致性目标；对角比例决定一致性成分出现频率，区间长度通过显式因子进入梯度。基于此提出时间监督分配（TSA）：先进行对角监督，再引入内部样本并扩大最大区间，采用左锚定区间律保持全程覆盖。逐样本损失、目标、骨干网络与推理流程均不变。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256类别条件、无CFG、DiT-B/2、单种子、400k更新的匹配设置下，EbbFlow在单次网络评估下50k样本FID为39.65、两次评估ODE采样为36.02；MeanFlow分别为44.82与39.86。梯度探针显示训练中期成分几何存在差异；消融支持分配调度与左锚定区间，但固定全时域变体在单次评估下更优。

Conclusion: 时间采样是一种有效的训练控制手段，其收益依赖推理协议；监督分配应据推理评估次数权衡设计。

Abstract: MeanFlow learns interval-average velocity for few-step generation, but its fixed temporal sampler mixes diagonal and interior supervision throughout training. We interpret temporal coordinates as collocation points: diagonal samples provide a stochastic boundary target, whereas finite intervals supply a differential-consistency target. The diagonal fraction controls how often the consistency component appears, while interval length enters its gradient through an explicit factor. Motivated by this distinction, EbbFlow uses Temporal Supervision Allocation (TSA) to schedule the diagonal fraction and interval geometry. It starts with diagonal supervision, then admits interior samples and expands their maximum interval; a left-anchored interval law preserves coverage across time. The per-sample loss, target, backbone, and inference procedure remain unchanged. In matched class-conditional ImageNet $256\times 256$ runs without classifier-free guidance (DiT-B/2, one training seed per method, 400k updates), EbbFlow achieves 50k-sample FID of 39.65 at one network evaluation and 36.02 at two-evaluation ODE sampling, versus 44.82 and 39.86 for MeanFlow. Gradient probes show a mid-training difference in component geometry. Ablations support allocation scheduling and left-anchored intervals, although a fixed full-horizon variant performs better at one evaluation. These results suggest that temporal sampling is a useful training control whose benefits depend on the inference protocol.

</details>


### [264] [SGA-Sparse: Training Native SwiGLU Gates for Activation-Sparse and Low-Bit LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11223)
*Ruokai Yin,Steven Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SGA-Sparse，通过将预训练 SwiGLU 模型中已有的 gate 投影直接训练为 top-k 路由器，实现固定预算的激活稀疏化，1B–8B LLaMA 在 75–85% FFN 通道稀疏下仅损失 0.2–2.7pp 零样本精度，并在等效位宽指标下确立新的精度-流量帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单用户 LLM 解码中激活稀疏可减少 FFN 权重带宽，但需在取权重前尽早识别活跃通道。预训练 SwiGLU 模型上，用原生 gate 做免训练路由在高稀疏度下明显劣于用 up 激活路由（即 gate 与 up 的路由优劣顺序与直觉相反）；作者发现这一顺序在稀疏感知训练后会反转，由此引出对原生 gate 进行训练的思路。

Method: 1) 将预训练 SwiGLU 的既有 gate 投影直接作为 top-k 路由器进行端到端训练，不引入额外路由参数，保留原 SwiGLU 激活形式，实现固定预算（per-token 均匀）通道稀疏；2) 全参数端到端训练约 30B tokens；3) 针对训练后量化导致的退化，研究两种 QAT 方案：continued-QAT（延续式）与 joint-QAT（联合式）；4) 将稀疏带来的权重流量下降折算为等效位宽，与稠密低位宽量化在统一流量指标下比较。

Result: LLaMA 1B–8B 达成 75–85% 均匀 per-token FFN 通道稀疏，平均零样本精度较原稠密检查点仅差 0.2–2.7pp。continued-QAT 在 4-bit 权重量化下基本恢复性能；joint-QAT 在更激进的 sub-4-bit 目标下更优。等效位宽口径下，2.5-bit-equivalent 模型持平稠密 3-bit QAT，1.5-bit-equivalent 模型接近稠密 2-bit 模型，并平均领先稠密三值模型 1.3pp，形成新的精度-流量帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 原生 gate 训练是结合高 FFN 稀疏与低位宽权重的可行路径：免训练阶段 gate 路由劣于 up 路由的排序在稀疏感知训练后反转，说明该劣势源于未训练而非结构缺陷；配合适配的 QAT 方案，激活稀疏在等效权重流量指标下优于稠密低位宽量化。

Abstract: Activation sparsity can reduce FFN weight traffic during single-user LLM decoding, but active channels must be identified early enough to avoid fetching unused weights. In pretrained SwiGLU models, training-free routing with the native gate performs substantially worse than routing with the up activation at high sparsity. We show that this ordering reverses after sparsity-aware training. Based on this insight, we introduce SGA-Sparse, which converts pretrained SwiGLU LLMs into fixed-budget activation-sparse models by training the existing gate projection directly as a top-k router. SGA-Sparse updates all model parameters end-to-end while preserving the original SwiGLU activation and introducing no auxiliary router parameters. After approximately 30B training tokens, LLaMA models from 1B to 8B achieve uniform per-token 75-85% FFN channel sparsity while remaining within 0.2-2.7 pp of their original dense checkpoints in average zero-shot accuracy. Because post-training quantization degrades sparsity-trained checkpoints, we further study two complementary quantization-aware training (QAT) recipes: continued-QAT largely recovers performance at 4-bit weight quantization, while joint-QAT performs better for the more aggressive sub-4-bit target. To compare activation sparsity with dense low-bit quantization, we translate the sparsity-induced reduction in weight traffic into an equivalent bit-width. Under this unified weight-traffic metric, our 2.5- and 1.5-bit-equivalent models establish a new accuracy-traffic Pareto frontier: the 2.5-bit-equivalent models match dense 3-bit QAT accuracy, while the 1.5-bit-equivalent models approach dense 2-bit models and outperform dense ternary models by 1.3 pp on average. These results establish native-gate training as a practical approach for combining high FFN sparsity with low-bit weights.

</details>


### [265] [Do Feature Interactions Break Explainability? Provable Explanations for Any-Order Neural Additive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11224)
*Idan Refaeli,Yizhak Yisrael Elboher,Guy Katz,Shahaf Bassan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带受限特征交互的神经网络中可证明最小充分解释的计算问题，证明即使常数阶交互也使问题Σ₂^P-难，并给出两个可处理设定下的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验解释方法多为启发式、缺乏可证明保证；现有"可证明最小充分特征子集"问题对一般网络不可解，而可解的NAMs排除了特征交互、表达能力受限。作者希望弥合一般网络与纯加性模型之间的空白。

Method: 在允许受限（任意阶但受约束）特征交互的网络族上形式化最小充分解释问题：先用复杂性理论证明常数阶交互下的Σ₂^P-难性；再识别两类可处理设定——(1) 交互结构稀疏，(2) 更强的交互感知充分性定义；并为二者设计在任意阶交互下仍保持加性模型效率的算法。

Result: 理论上给出Σ₂^P-难性下界并刻画两类可处理条件；实验表明所得解释比标准算法更小、求解更快，且所用模型精度显著高于标准NAMs。

Conclusion: 扩大了具有高效可证明解释的网络类，并厘清了特征交互在决定解释计算复杂性中的根本作用。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanation methods for neural networks are often heuristic and lack provable guarantees. A common alternative to such methods that was studied in recent years is to compute a cardinally-minimal subset of input features that is provably sufficient to determine the model's prediction. For general neural networks, however, finding such explanations is computationally intractable. In contrast, recent work showed that this problem becomes more tractable for a particular family of neural networks, namely neural additive models (NAMs), though standard NAMs are highly restrictive because they exclude feature interactions altogether. In this work, we study the computation of provably minimal and sufficient explanations for neural networks with restricted feature interactions, bridging the gap between fully general neural networks and purely additive models. We first prove that even with constant-order interactions, the problem is $Σ_2^P$-hard, implying that worst-case exponential complexity is unavoidable. We then identify two provably tractable settings: one based on sparsity in the interaction structure and another based on a stronger interaction-aware notion of sufficiency. Under either of these settings, we develop algorithms that compute provable explanations for neural networks with any-order interactions while retaining much of the computational efficiency of additive models. Empirically, we show that these explanations are significantly smaller and faster to obtain than those produced by standard algorithms, while being derived from models that achieve much higher accuracy than standard NAMs. Overall, our results help expand the class of neural networks that admit efficient provable explanations, while clarifying the fundamental role of feature interactions in shaping their complexity.

</details>


### [266] [MCSA: Multi-Cutoff Spectral Aggregation in SO(3) Equivariant GNNs for Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11226)
*Junfu Tan,Mengfan Wu,Yu Zhu,Yu Shi,Yifang Qin,Chang Liu,Bonan Zhu,Ziheng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从径向谱角度分析SO(3)等变消息传递，指出截断半径决定径向相互作用的特征空间频率尺度，并提出即插即用的多截断谱聚合模块（MCSA），以低开销融合多尺度径向核，提升能量、力、应力及声子等性质预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于等变图神经网络的机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）虽取得显著进展，但如何在单一模型中准确刻画跨空间尺度的原子相互作用仍是难点。作者希望厘清截断半径在径向表征中的物理/谱学含义，并据此提升多尺度几何表征能力。

Method: 首先分析SO(3)等变消息传递的径向谱性质，论证截断半径控制径向相互作用的特征空间频率尺度；随后提出MCSA（Multi-Cutoff Spectral Aggregation）模块，将不同截断半径的径向核进行聚合以丰富多尺度几何表征。实现上利用径向核的紧支撑性，在角向张量积运算之前融合依赖于截断的径向权重，从而避免冗余角向计算，同时保持旋转等变性。

Result: 在多个代表性EGNN骨干网络上，MCSA一致提升能量、力与应力预测精度，并改善高阶性质（声子色散、晶格热导率）的准确性；在具有不同相互作用尺度的层状材料上，验证了基于物理动机选择截断半径的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 截断半径并非单纯的工程超参，而是决定径向谱尺度的重要物理量；以此为基础的多截断谱聚合是一种高效、通用、可即插即用的多尺度表征增强方案，可在保持等变性的同时显著提升MLIPs的精度与泛化能力。

Abstract: Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs) based on Equivariant Graph Neural Networks (EGNNs) have achieved remarkable progress in atomistic modeling. However, accurately representing atomic interactions across different spatial scales remains challenging. In this work, we investigate the radial spectral properties of SO(3)-equivariant message passing and establish that the cutoff radius controls the characteristic spatial-frequency scale of radial interactions. Based on this analysis, we propose Multi-Cutoff Spectral Aggregation (MCSA), a plug-and-play module that aggregates radial kernels with different cutoff radii to enrich multi-scale geometric representations. To achieve efficient implementation, we exploit the compact support of radial kernels to fuse cutoff-dependent radial weights before angular tensor-product operations, avoiding redundant angular computations while preserving rotational equivariance. Extensive experiments across representative EGNN backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in energy, force, and stress prediction, as well as enhanced accuracy in higher-order properties, including phonon dispersion and lattice thermal conductivity. Further evaluations on layered materials with distinct interaction scales demonstrate the effectiveness of physically motivated cutoff selection. Code is available at: https://github.com/JF-Tan/MCSA.

</details>


### [267] [TLRS-DPO: Token-Level Risk Scoring for Better Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11232)
*Suxin Ji,Hungtao Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TLRS-DPO，在标准 DPO 上引入token级风险评分与鲁棒补偿项，实现细粒度、全离线、基于参考模型的偏好对齐，提升安全相关表现且几乎不损有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 的序列级目标无法精确定位并抑制使一个整体可接受回复变得不安全或不可取的局部模式，因此需要在不引入在线采样与额外奖励模型的前提下实现token级行为控制。

Method: 三项设计：(1) token级鲁棒补偿项，将拒绝样本上的 policy-reference 对数比转换为局部风险分数；(2) 课程式调度，训练中逐步增强补偿强度；(3) 风险自适应筛选机制，仅对高风险 token 施加惩罚而非整条序列。整体保持完全离线、reference-based，可作为标准 DPO 训练器的即插即用改造。

Result: 实验表明，相较强 DPO 基线，TLRS-DPO 提升了对齐质量与安全相关行为，同时对有用性的影响极小。

Conclusion: 通过桥接序列级偏好拟合与token级行为控制，TLRS-DPO 以低实现成本实现更精细的对齐控制，验证了细粒度风险加权在离线偏好优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a simple and widely used alternative to RLHF for aligning large language models (LLMs), but its sequence-level objective cannot precisely locate or suppress the local patterns that make an otherwise acceptable response unsafe or undesirable. We propose TLRS-DPO (Token-Level Risk Scoring for Better Direct Preference Optimization), which augments DPO with fine-grained token-level control while remaining fully offline and reference-based. TLRS-DPO introduces a token-level robust compensation term that converts the policy-reference log-ratio on rejected responses into localized risk scores, a curriculum-style schedule that gradually increases the strength of this compensation during training, and a risk-adaptive screening mechanism that selectively penalizes only high-risk tokens instead of entire sequences. This design bridges sequence-level preference fitting and token-level behavior control, and can be implemented as a drop-in modification to standard DPO trainers. Empirically, TLRS-DPO improves alignment quality and safety-relevant behavior with minimal impact on helpfulness compared to strong DPO baselines.

</details>


### [268] [FRESCO: A Novel Consistency Control for Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11238)
*Dongyeop Lee,Namhoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRESCO 将异步流水线并行中的不一致梯度问题建模为约束优化：在激活子空间上"平凡作用"（保持前向等价）的前提下，寻找最小的参数更新修正，从而兼顾异步训练的吞吐与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步流水线并行可加速分布式训练，但梯度计算不一致导致训练不稳定。作者指出其根本来源：误差信号与"前向时刻的特征"耦合，而不只依赖于参数状态。因此只要参数演化保持前向等价性，就是可接受的——这一洞察为重新定义"一致性"提供了空间。

Method: 把异步更新形式化为带约束的优化问题：约束更新对激活子空间的作用为零（trivially on the activation subspace），目标是最小化对原始异步更新的修改量。由此得到一种"最小修正"的更新规则，替代直接使用过期梯度。

Result: 在 LLM 预训练中一致优于代表性异步基线，且在深流水线与高不一致程度下优势最明显：流水线深度 32 时，达到最强基线相同 loss 所需迭代数少 43%、墙钟时间少 30%，内存开销低 8~921 倍。

Conclusion: FRESCO 突破现有异步流水线并行的稳定性瓶颈，为高利用率流水线并行训练提供了新基础，为大模型在异构、资源受限系统上的可扩展训练提供路径。

Abstract: Asynchronous pipeline parallelism can accelerate distributed model training, but suffers from instability caused by inconsistent gradient computations. We identify a fundamental source of this inconsistency: error signals are coupled to forward-time features, not just parameter states. This means that parameters may evolve as long as they preserve forward-equivalence. Leveraging this insight, we introduce FRESCO, which frames asynchronous updates as a constrained optimization problem: it finds the minimal update modification that acts trivially on the activation subspace. We demonstrate that FRESCO consistently outperforms representative asynchronous baselines in LLM pre-training, with the gains most pronounced under deep pipelines and high inconsistency levels: at pipeline depth 32, it reaches the same loss as the strongest baseline with 43% fewer iterations and 30% less wall-clock time, while requiring 8~921$\times$ less memory overhead. Moving beyond the current limitations, FRESCO establishes a new foundation for high-utilization pipeline parallel training, providing a scalable path for large-model development on diverse, resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [269] [ALETHEIA: A Multi-Frequency Eddy Current Pulsed Thermography Dataset for Neural Operator Learning in Nondestructive Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11252)
*Changbin Sun,Jie Xiao,Xiaotian Chen,Yuankai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Aletheia，首个面向无损检测（NDT）的 3D 神经求解器基准数据集，模拟导电固体中涡流致热与瞬态热传导，包含 4700+ 高分辨率样本、10 个激励频率（1–100 kHz），支持正向温度预测与由表面红外测温反演内部热源/缺陷，并附真实裂纹钢轨红外热像数据用于校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实约束下（稀疏、含噪边界观测）的时空 PDE 神经求解器学习仍是科学机器学习的核心难题，逆问题尤甚；而 NDT 场景缺乏统一、高保真、含真实数据的 3D 基准，导致方法间难以可比评估。

Method: 构建基于热方程的涡流感应加热瞬态仿真数据集：10 个激励频率（1–100 kHz），样本提供随时间演化的体热源场与温度场，网格含规则与不规则两类；定义四个规范化任务（正向温度演化预测、由表面红外测量反演内部热源/缺陷等），并引入真实裂纹钢轨红外热像数据用于标定与对比；以多种神经算子（neural operators）进行基线评测。

Result: 数据集含 4,700+ 高分辨率 3D 样本，覆盖多频多时刻的体热源与温度场；在规则/不规则网格的四类任务上给出多种神经算子的基准结果，并提供真实实验数据作为参照。数据集与代码已开源。

Conclusion: Aletheia 为真实 NDT 条件下的神经 PDE 求解器提供统一评测平台，推动可靠的数据驱动逆建模研究；其多频、3D、含真实数据的设计弥补了现有基准在稀疏含噪边界观测与逆问题评估上的不足。

Abstract: Learning neural solvers for spatiotemporal partial differential equations (PDEs) under real-world constraints remains a key challenge in scientific machine learning, especially for inverse tasks with sparse and noisy boundary observations. We present the Aletheia dataset, the first 3D benchmark for learning data-driven solvers in the context of nondestructive testing (NDT). The dataset simulates eddy-current-induced heating in conductive solids and models the resulting transient heat propagation governed by the heat equation. Aletheia contains over 4,700 high-resolution samples across 10 excitation frequencies (1-100,kHz), each providing volumetric heat source and temperature fields over time. It supports both forward prediction of temperature evolution and inverse reconstruction of internal heat sources or defects from surface infrared measurements. Real infrared thermography data from cracked rail specimens are included for calibration and comparison. We define four canonical tasks on both regular and irregular grids and benchmark them using various neural operators. Aletheia establishes a unified platform for evaluating neural PDE solvers under realistic NDT conditions, enabling progress in reliable, data-driven inverse modeling. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/sherlock-0327/ALETHEIA.

</details>


### [270] [Primitives and Paths: Predicting What Post-Training Can Do from What the Base Model Already Has](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11260)
*Anqi Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个"可后训练性"的二维状态空间框架，认为"RL是教会新能力还是放大已有能力"这一问题取决于基座模型所处的能力状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献结论相互冲突：pass@k研究表明RL只是对潜在行为重新加权，而受控组合实验显示存在超出放大的增益。作者认为该争论缺少对基座模型能力状态这一前置条件的界定，因而无法给出统一答案。

Method: 提出两个维度的能力状态刻画：一是"有效完整解支撑"（在可行探索预算下能否采样到成功轨迹），二是"原子基元覆盖"（剥离组合负担后能否执行各构成技能）。二者组合划分出三种状态区制：放大区制、组合受限区制、习得受限区制，各自对应不同的经验特征与干预方式。作者给出前瞻性测量协议，并在三个小规模语言模型上以合成组合任务进行验证。

Result: 仅具备基元掌握并不能保证RL学会组合任务。可可靠执行全部基元的检查点，若训练中从未采样到完整解，则RL无法带来改进；而少量建立非零支撑的组合监督即可使RL获得显著收益。

Conclusion: RL的效果应相对于基座模型的能力状态来评估；框架将"中期训练"重新定位为"后训练的准备阶段"，其作用是推动模型进入RL能够有效发挥作用的状态区制。

Abstract: Does reinforcement learning teach language models new capabilities, or merely amplify ones acquired during pre-training? The literature offers evidence for both: pass@$k$ studies suggest RL reweights latent behaviours while some controlled composition studies demonstrate gains beyond simple amplification. We argue that this question has no answer independent of the capability state of the base model, and propose a two-dimensional state space for post-trainability. The first axis, \emph{effective full-solution support}, measures whether successful trajectories can be sampled under a feasible exploration budget. The second axis, \emph{atomic primitive coverage}, measures whether the constituent skills of a solution can be executed when the burden of composition is removed. Together, these dimensions motivate three regimes -- amplification, composition-limited, and acquisition-limited -- each predicting a distinct empirical signature and calling for a distinct intervention. We propose a prospective measurement protocol and investigate the framework using synthetic composition tasks across three small language models. We find that primitive mastery alone does not guarantee that RL can learn a composition. Checkpoints that execute every primitive reliably can fail to improve when complete solutions are never sampled during training, whereas modest composition supervision that establishes nonzero support enables substantial RL gains. The framework reframes mid-training as preparation for post-training: its role is to move models into states from which RL can operate effectively.

</details>


### [271] [Chatter Attack: Resource Consumption Attack for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11261)
*Yibo Miao,Yichuan Cao,Xiao-Shan Gao,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Chatter Attack——通过优化对抗提示诱发预设短路目标短语（自我怀疑）并持续放大，从而绕开搜索空间爆炸，使LLM输出长度激增，并在黑盒场景引入贝叶斯提示优化（BPO）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本高，恶意用户可诱导超长输出以抬高延迟与运营开销；已有资源消耗攻击（强制重复、压低EOS token logit）在自回归采样生成下搜索空间随长度指数增长，微小偏差或不稳定轨迹会使优化失效，攻击效果与稳定性受限

Method: 1) 优化对抗提示，使其诱导预设的短目标短语，避免搜索空间爆炸但仍触发长输出；2) 以短"怀疑诱导短语"为目标触发self-doubt，并用推理注意力损失（inference attention loss）与全局熵损失（global entropy loss）在整个生成过程中维持该状态；3) 黑盒设定下提出贝叶斯提示优化BPO，通过构造离散token核全局建模目标函数，利用先验信息高效探索整个搜索空间

Result: 在多模型、多数据集上优于基线，最大响应长度提升至31.5倍，揭示真实LLM推理服务的实际资源耗尽风险

Conclusion: 短目标短语+状态维持的优化范式可稳定、有效地实施资源消耗攻击，BPO可迁移至黑盒；表明LLM推理服务存在现实的资源耗尽威胁，需引起防御关注

Abstract: LLMs are powerful but incur substantial inference cost, which can be exploited by malicious users to induce overly long outputs, increasing latency and operational expense. Prior resource consumption attacks either coerce repetition or suppress the end-of-sequence token by lowering its logit value. However, with autoregressive, sampling-based generation, the search space grows exponentially with length and small deviations or unstable trajectories can derail optimization, limiting attack effectiveness and stability. To address this issue, we introduce Chatter Attack, a novel class of resource consumption attacks that optimize adversarial prompts to induce predefined short target phrases, thereby avoiding search-space explosion while still eliciting long responses. Specifically, Chatter Attack triggers self-doubt using short doubt-inducing phrases as the target, and sustains this state throughout generation via inference attention loss and global entropy loss. To extend to black-box settings, we further propose a novel Bayesian Prompt Optimization (BPO) method, which models the objective function globally by constructing a discrete token kernel, efficiently exploring the entire search space using prior information. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets show that our method outperforms baselines, with maximum response length increases to 31.5$\times$, and revealing practical resource-depletion risks for real-world LLM inference services.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Node Attributes: A Controlled Evaluation and Ablation of Local Interaction Statistics for Ethereum Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11263)
*Thanh Dat Tran,Duy Loi Duong,Duy Luu Hoang,Minh Huy Tran,Vo Minh Thu Le,Nhat Tung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将12个局部交互统计特征（UFE）附加到HEIG-Phish的14个源特征上，在6种模型家族中进行严格的配对对比实验，证明UFE能稳定提升钓鱼账户检测的Macro-F1（+1.0~9.1个百分点）并降低假阳性率，但增益主要来自单一入向转账边求和特征，且仅在单一静态直推基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以太坊钓鱼账户检测方法通常作为完整流水线（在特征、架构、训练协议上各不相同）进行评估，导致"交互信息"本身的贡献难以被单独隔离。作者希望在不改变数据划分、模型配置、随机种子、决策阈值与采样邻域的条件下，干净地度量局部交互特征的独立作用，并检验特征增强基线是否应与图模型并列报告。

Method: 提出无标签特征扩展UFE（Unlabeled Feature Extension）：基于每个账户的流入/流出调用边与转账边计算12个局部交互统计量，不读取类别标签；将其追加到HEIG-Phish的14个源特征上。在2,763个有标签外部账户（EOA）的20个预定分层划分上，对逻辑回归、随机森林、LightGBM、图注意力网络、GraphSAGE、Meta-IFD六种模型构建Baseline/Plus成对变体，仅改变输入特征块。另做精确边随机森林消融（10个预定划分）识别各UFE列的边际贡献，并用Nadeau-Bengio校正的95%置信区间评估显著性。

Result: 六种模型上UFE使测试Macro-F1提升1.0~9.1个百分点，每一对模型在全部20个划分上均提升，所有Nadeau-Bengio校正95%置信区间下界均大于0，六种模型的假阳性率均下降。消融实验显示增益大部分可归因于"入向转账边求和"（incoming transfer-edge sum）这一属性；一旦该特征存在，其余11列UFE的增量贡献未能确立。

Conclusion: 结果支持在报告图模型时同时报告特征增强基线，说明局部交互统计具有很强的判别力且可稳定叠加。但证据仅限于单一静态、直推式基准的重划分稳定性，尚未证明时间维度或跨数据集泛化能力；且UFE的有效性高度集中于单一聚合特征，提示后续研究需关注特征冗余性与时序/跨域验证。

Abstract: Methods for detecting phishing accounts on Ethereum are usually evaluated as complete pipelines that differ in features, architecture, and training protocol, which makes the contribution of interaction information hard to isolate. This paper evaluates the Unlabeled Feature Extension (UFE), a block of 12 local interaction statistics computed from each account's incoming and outgoing call and transfer edges without reading class labels. UFE is appended to the 14 source features of HEIG-Phish and tested with six model families: Logistic Regression, Random Forest, LightGBM, Graph Attention Network, GraphSAGE, and Meta-IFD. Within each pair of Baseline and Plus variants, the data partition, model configuration, random seed, decision threshold, and sampled neighborhoods are identical; only the input feature block changes. Across 20 predetermined stratified partitions of the same 2,763 labeled externally owned accounts, UFE increased test Macro-F1 by 1.0 to 9.1 percentage points across the six models. Every pair improved on all 20 partitions, all Nadeau-Bengio corrected 95% confidence intervals lie above zero, and the false-positive rate decreased for all six models. An exact-edge Random Forest ablation over 10 predetermined partitions associates most of the gain with the incoming transfer-edge sum attribute; once this feature is present, an incremental contribution of the remaining 11 UFE columns was not established. The results support reporting feature-augmented baselines alongside graph models, but they show stability under re-partitioning of one static, transductive benchmark rather than temporal or cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [273] [How Efficient Is Your Agent? Online Predictive Bounds under Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11279)
*Roy Maor Lotan,Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PROBE 方法，在有限预算下为在线 AI 智能体任务构建校准的「成功时间」上预测界，通过逆概率加权反馈与偏差校正实现在线校准与预算自适应分配，并给出有限时域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评估 AI 智能体时，任务顺序到达，且需在运行任务前预测其 time-to-success（直至成功所需的交互次数）的上界。但预算有限导致部分运行被提前终止，其 time-to-success 被右删失而不可观测，预测界的正确性也无从判断，因此需要在预算约束下仍能给出可靠（覆盖率达标）且紧致的预测界。

Method: 提出 PROBE（Predictive Bounds for Online Budget Efficiency）：在线构造校准的上预测界，利用逆概率加权（IPW）反馈进行在线校准，并加入一种在保持无偏性的同时最小化覆盖缺口的校正项；同时将有限预算自适应地分配给各任务，以降低预期覆盖缺口。

Result: 理论上证明 PROBE 在任意有限时域下均提供覆盖保证且满足预算约束；在软件工程、检索增强生成（RAG）与指令遵循三类任务上的实验表明，相比校准基线，PROBE 的覆盖更接近目标水平，且预测界更紧致。

Conclusion: 在存在右删失与预算约束的在线智能体评估中，PROBE 兼顾覆盖有效性、界紧致性与预算合规性，优于已有校准基线。

Abstract: We evaluate AI agents in an online setting where tasks arrive sequentially and, before running each task, we construct an upper predictive bound on its \emph{time-to-success}---the number of interactions until the task is completed successfully. Under a limited budget, some runs are stopped early, so their time-to-success is unobserved (right-censored), and the correctness of the bound may be unknown. We introduce \emph{\textbf{PR}edictive Bounds for \textbf{O}nline \textbf{B}udget \textbf{E}fficiency} (\texttt{PROBE}), a method for constructing calibrated upper predictive bounds while adaptively allocating a limited budget across agent tasks to reduce the anticipated coverage deficit. Our method calibrates the bounds online using inverse-probability-weighted feedback together with a correction designed to minimize coverage deficit while remaining unbiased. We prove that \texttt{PROBE} provides a coverage guarantee for any finite time horizon while satisfying the budget constraint. Experiments on software engineering, retrieval-augmented generation, and instruction following show that \texttt{PROBE} attains a coverage rate closer to the target level with tighter predictive bounds than a calibrated baseline.

</details>


### [274] [A Unified Framework for License Plate Identification and Character-Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11289)
*Saurabh Kumar,Dewant Katare,Prakriti Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出共享CSP-Darknet与PANet特征编码器的统一车牌检测与字符识别框架，在Jetson Orin Nano上以INT8 TensorRT部署实现147 FPS、6.8 ms端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车牌识别流程通常为“检测—裁剪—再识别”两阶段，需要图像空间裁剪与第二个识别骨干网络，计算与内存开销大，难以在低功耗边缘硬件上高效运行；因此需要一种端到端统一、轻量化的检测与识别一体化框架。

Method: 采用共享CSP-Darknet + PANet特征编码器；检测端为无锚框（anchor-free）定位头，识别端为基于RoIAlign的序列识别分支，直接在对齐后的特征图上进行字符解码，避免图像空间车牌裁剪与独立识别骨干。训练数据为约37,000张人工核验的AI辅助数据集，覆盖全美50州车牌版式及光照、视角、天气、模糊、遮挡、版式等变化。模型转为INT8 TensorRT引擎，在8 GB内存的NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上评测。

Result: INT8模型mAP50为97.8%，全序列识别准确率90.7%；达147 FPS、6.8 ms端到端延迟，推理内存315 MB，平均功耗9.4 W。相比YOLOv8s+CRNN基线，吞吐量提升7.1倍，推理内存分配减少77.8%。

Conclusion: 共享特征编码器加RoIAlign序列识别的统一架构配合INT8量化部署，可在低功耗边缘设备上同时获得高精度、高吞吐与低内存/低功耗的车牌检测与识别性能，显著优于两阶段基线方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified framework for license plate detection and character recognition on low-power edge hardware. The proposed network uses a shared CSP-Darknet and PANet feature encoder with an anchor-free localization head and a RoIAlign-based sequence-recognition branch. Character decoding is performed directly on aligned feature maps, avoiding image-space plate cropping and a second recognition backbone. The model is trained on an AI-assisted dataset of approximately 37,000 manually verified images covering license plate layouts from all 50 United States. The dataset includes variations in illumination, viewpoint, weather, blur, occlusion, and plate design. The framework is converted to an INT8 TensorRT engine and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano with 8 GB memory. The INT8 model shows an mAP50 of 97.8% and a full-sequence accuracy of 90.7%. It records 147 FPS at 6.8 ms end-to-end latency, with 315 MB inference memory and 9.4 W average power consumption. Relative to the YOLOv8s+CRNN baseline, the proposed deployment provides a 7.1x throughput increase and a 77.8% reduction in allocated inference memory

</details>


### [275] [Shape-Adaptive Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11313)
*Zheng Zhai,Zhuan Liang,Sujun Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交替极大似然估计方法 AMLE-SQMF，将二次流形拟合与噪声分布联合估计，用广义高斯分布形状参数自适应确定拟合损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二次流形拟合方法采用预设噪声模型或固定损失函数，难以适应变化的噪声特性。

Method: 对子空间约束二次矩阵分解的残差建模为广义高斯分布（GGD），其形状参数控制尾部行为并决定拟合损失；利用尺度参数的闭式极大似然估计消去尺度变量，得到轮廓负对数似然；据此设计交替优化算法，迭代更新流形参数与噪声形状参数，在统一似然框架内实现自适应损失选择。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据集上的实验表明，AMLE-SQMF 在二次流形拟合中有效，并对多种噪声分布具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 无需预先指定噪声特性即可自适应选择损失函数，为二次流形拟合提供了灵活且稳健的似然框架。

Abstract: Quadratic manifold fitting provides a flexible framework for modeling nonlinear low-dimensional structures embedded in high-dimensional spaces. However, existing methods typically employ prescribed noise models or fixed loss functions, limiting their adaptability to varying noise characteristics. To address this limitation, we propose an alternating maximum likelihood estimation method for subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization, termed AMLE-SQMF, which jointly estimates the quadratic manifold and the underlying noise distribution. Specifically, we model the residuals using a generalized Gaussian distribution (GGD), whose shape parameter controls the tail behavior and determines the associated fitting loss. By exploiting the closed-form maximum likelihood estimator of the scale parameter, we eliminate the scale variable and derive a profile negative log-likelihood. Based on this formulation, we develop an alternating optimization algorithm that iteratively updates the manifold parameters and estimates the noise shape parameter. This enables adaptive loss selection within a unified likelihood-based framework, without requiring prior specification of the noise characteristics. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AMLE-SQMF in quadratic manifold fitting and its robustness to diverse noise distributions.

</details>


### [276] [PROLA: Principal-Orthogonal Low-rank Adaptation for Predictive Spatiotemporal Weather Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11318)
*Minseo Yoon,Minseong Bae,Sojin Lee,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"预测式时空天气降尺度"(PSWD)任务，并给出一个基于共享预训练骨干、固定数值脚手架与轻量残差头的两阶段框架；核心方法是PROLA低秩适配，在固定低秩预算下将主方向与正交补分别分配，并用梯度信号对秩-1优化器更新重缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率天气预报对刻画局地大气结构很重要，但粗分辨率全球预报难以分辨这些结构。大型预训练天气骨干提升了全球预报能力，但将其改造为"由近期粗状态生成未来高分辨率场"既困难又代价高：现有低秩适配方法通常把容量集中在预训练权重的主方向上，与需要引入新时空细节的降尺度需求不匹配。

Method: 将问题形式化为PSWD（同时提升空间与时间分辨率并预测未来高分辨率轨迹）。框架把预测分解为空间阶段与时间阶段，复用共享预训练骨干、固定数值脚手架（numerical scaffolds）和轻量残差头。骨干适配采用PROLA：在固定低秩预算下按比例分配至预训练权重的主方向与正交补子空间；并对秩-1优化器更新按梯度信号进行重缩放，在不增加可训练秩的前提下改善适配。

Result: 在PSWD设定下，PROLA优于代表性低秩适配基线；在多个数据集与任务上的进一步实验表明其增益可推广到天气降尺度之外的场景。

Conclusion: PROLA为预训练天气骨干的时空降尺度适配提供了一种参数高效的方案，其"主方向-正交补"预算分配与梯度重缩放机制具有跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: High-resolution weather prediction is important for resolving local atmospheric patterns missed by coarse global forecasts. Large pretrained weather backbones have improved global forecasting, but adapting them to produce future high-resolution fields from recent coarse states remains challenging and costly. We formulate this setting as Predictive Spatiotemporal Weather Downscaling (PSWD), a task that jointly refines spatial and temporal resolution while predicting future high-resolution trajectories. To this end, we propose a framework that decomposes prediction into spatial and temporal stages using a shared pretrained backbone, fixed numerical scaffolds, and lightweight residual heads. For backbone adaptation, we introduce PROLA (PRincipal-Orthogonal Low-rank Adaptation), which allocates a fixed low-rank budget between pretrained weights' principal directions and their orthogonal complement. PROLA further rescales rank-1 optimizer updates using gradient signals, improving adaptation without increasing trainable rank. Experiments in the PSWD setting show that PROLA outperforms representative low-rank adaptation baselines. Further experiments across diverse datasets and tasks support the broader applicability of PROLA, showing that its benefits extend beyond the primary weather downscaling setting.

</details>


### [277] [When Sensors Fail: Coverage Auditing with Incomplete Sensor Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11324)
*Chung-I Huang,Wen-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"测量记录审计"框架，用于在传感器中断条件下评估预测区间覆盖率的统计证据，将发布时中断时长、原始目标可用性与时间一致统计检验联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器中断会让预测区间覆盖率的不足被表面上可接受的边际性能所掩盖，同时未来读数缺失导致部分已发布预测无法打分，现有评估无法区分真实覆盖不足与不可评分造成的假象。

Method: 审计保留完整预测键；在每个测量时刻将所有传感器与重叠预测步长分批处理；将同时覆盖计数与已评分计数的界限投影到累积条件期望覆盖率比值上；并明确区分该目标与经验覆盖率、中断条件总体覆盖率。

Result: 交通与空气质量数据显示汇总覆盖率与长中断覆盖率存在差距；METR-LA中仅7.6%的长间隔发布预测具有原始观测目标；30项主要序贯中断状态审计中26项结论不确定，另4项缺乏已评分支持。

Conclusion: 该简要报告给出核心构造与部分汇总证据，表明中断感知的覆盖率审计对预测区间可信度评估不可或缺；完整实验补充材料未包含在内。

Abstract: Sensor outages can conceal prediction-interval coverage deficits behind acceptable marginal performance, while unavailable future readings leave some issued forecasts unscorable. We present a measurement-record audit that links issue-time outage duration, original target availability, and time-uniform statistical evidence. The audit retains complete forecast keys, batches all sensors and overlapping horizons at each measurement tick, and projects simultaneous covered-count and scored-count bounds onto a cumulative conditional expected coverage ratio. This target is distinguished from empirical coverage and outage-conditional population coverage. Selected traffic and air-quality results illustrate the gap between pooled and long-outage coverage; in METR-LA, only 7.6% of issued long-gap forecasts have originally observed targets. Of 30 primary sequential outage-state audits, 26 are inconclusive and four lack scored support. This concise presentation gives the core construction and selected aggregate evidence without the full experimental supplement.

</details>


### [278] [When Credit Turns Against Tools: Transition-Aware Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11330)
*Xiangyu Lu,Zhanke Zhou,Jiazhe Ning,Chentao Cao,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析智能体强化学习（Rubric/可验证奖励）训练中推理能力与工具调用能力下降的机制，并提出Transition-Aware Credit Assignment (TACA)方法加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮工具交互的可验证奖励强化学习（agentic RLVR）中，模型训练期间推理性能与工具调用能力会出现退化，作者希望厘清其成因并加以抑制。

Method: 作者将退化归因于三种耦合机制：工具token不平衡（错误轨迹每轮工具token更多导致整体工具信用为负）、聚合放大（token均值聚合使更长错误轨迹在策略更新中获得更大权重）、失败驱动重试（执行错误后重复调用工具延长轨迹，将负信用扩散至恢复尝试）。据此提出TACA，聚焦"推理→工具调用"的过渡环节，同时监督调用发起与生成：1) Advantage Projection，在每个prompt组内通过最小均匀平移，使工具调用token的平均优势非负；2) Decision Contrast，对比同一prompt下工具辅助回答与直接回答的奖励以指导工具使用决策；3) Supervised Replay，利用先前采样的正确轨迹中的调用来强化工具调用生成。

Result: 在多个模型与任务上，TACA提升性能并在训练中维持更可靠的工具交互；六个推理基准上，相比MT-GRPO，Qwen2.5-1.5B平均准确率提升4.61个百分点，Qwen2.5-3B提升3.61个百分点。

Conclusion: 工具信用抑制是agentic RLVR退化的核心症结，针对推理到工具调用过渡环节的信用分配（TACA）可有效缓解该问题并稳定训练，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards trains language models through multi-turn tool interactions, yet their reasoning performance and tool-calling ability can decline during training. We trace this degradation to three coupled mechanisms: (1) tool-token imbalance produces negative overall tool credit when incorrect trajectories contain more tool tokens per turn than correct ones; (2) aggregation amplification can further strengthen this negative signal as token-mean aggregation gives longer incorrect trajectories greater weight in policy updates; and (3) failure-driven retries prolong trajectories as models tend to call tools again after execution errors, extending potential negative credit to recovery attempts. To counter this suppression, we propose Transition-Aware Credit Assignment (TACA), which targets the transition from reasoning to tool use through supervision of both call initiation and generation. Specifically, TACA uses Advantage Projection to enforce a nonnegative mean advantage on tool-call tokens within each prompt group through a minimal uniform shift. Additionally, TACA guides tool-use decisions with Decision Contrast, comparing rewards for tool-assisted and direct answers to the same prompt. Through Supervised Replay, it reinforces tool-call generation using calls from previously sampled correct trajectories. Across models and tasks, TACA improves performance and sustains more reliable tool interaction during training, raising mean accuracy over MT-GRPO on six reasoning benchmarks by 4.61 percentage points for Qwen2.5-1.5B and 3.61 for Qwen2.5-3B. Our code is available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/TACA.

</details>


### [279] [Where Should a Gate Look? Input vs. State Gating in Diagonal State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11336)
*Riccardo Zattra,Giacomo Baggio,Leonardo De Marchi,Augusto Ferrante,Francesco Ticozzi,Marco Del Prà,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究在对角状态空间模型（SSM）中引入状态反馈门控（gate 依赖循环状态）能否提升表达能力，并与输入门控对比。理论证明标量情形下状态门控可解任意词表规模与序列长度的 induction-head 任务，而输入门控在状态转移矩阵稳定性假设下于某个有限实例上失败；实验显示高维模型中状态门控在 induction-head 上占优，输入门控在 selective copying 上更优，二者结合性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代状态空间模型因线性（常为对角）动力学而高效，但经典 RNN 允许非线性状态间交互，当记忆更新需依赖过去上下文时更具优势。作者由此提出核心问题：通过一个简单门来调节记忆更新的"状态反馈"机制，能否增强对角 SSM 的表达能力？并希望厘清门控信号来源（输入、循环状态或二者）各自的贡献。

Method: 对比三类门控：仅依赖当前输入的门控、仅依赖循环状态的门控、以及两者兼有的门控。理论上在标量模型设定下进行严格的表达能力分离分析（给出状态门控的可解性构造与输入门控的失败证明），并在高维模型上开展 induction-head 与 selective copying 任务的实证对比。

Result: 标量模型下：状态门控可解决任意词表规模与序列长度的 induction-head 任务；输入门控在状态转移矩阵的标准稳定性假设下于某个有限实例上必然失败，形成严格分离。高维实验中：状态门控在 induction-head 上保持优势，输入门控在 selective copying 上表现更好，说明两种门控能力互补。

Conclusion: 即便形式简单、保持对角结构的状态反馈门控，也能解锁输入控制模型所不具备的能力；输入门控与状态门控各有互补优势，二者结合可获得最佳整体性能。这为在保持效率的同时设计更具表达力的 SSM 架构提供了依据。

Abstract: Modern state-space models owe much of their computational efficiency to their linear, often diagonal, dynamics. Classical recurrent neural networks instead allow nonlinear state-to-state interactions, which can be beneficial when memory updates need to depend on the past context. In this paper, we ask whether state feedback through a simple gate regulating memory updates can enhance the expressivity of diagonal state-space models. To investigate this question, we compare gates that depend on the current input, the recurrent state, or both. We establish a strict expressivity separation: for scalar models, state gating solves induction-head tasks for arbitrary vocabulary size and sequence length, whereas input gating fails on a finite instance under standard stability assumptions on the state transition matrix. Experiments show that state gating retains its advantage on induction-head tasks in higher-dimensional models, whereas input gating performs better on selective copying tasks. Together, our results show that even a simple, diagonal form of state-feedback gating can unlock capabilities unavailable to input-controlled models, while input and state gating offer complementary strengths and achieve the strongest overall performance when combined.

</details>


### [280] [OPSRL-SSP: Optimistic Posterior Sampling for Stochastic Shortest Path with Minimax-Optimal Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11337)
*Yiming Xu,Xian Wei,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OPSRL-SSP，一种面向随机最短路径（SSP）问题的乐观后验采样算法，取得匹配极小极大下界的遗憾界 ĤO(B⋆√(SAK)+B⋆S²A)，并推广到零代价 SSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于 SSP 的后验采样方法虽经验表现良好，但遗憾保证未达到极小极大最优阶（下界 Ω(B⋆√(SAK))），Jafarnia-Jahromi 等 [19] 提出的开放问题亟待解决。

Method: 算法采用分 epoch（阶段）运行，后验采样数量使用随时间变化的对数调度；通过引入一个赋予乐观边界值的伪状态来增广转移模型，从而诱导乐观性；并将分析借助统一的代价扰动论证推广到一般零代价 SSP。

Result: 获得高概率遗憾界 ĤO(B⋆√(SAK)+B⋆S²A)，其中 B⋆ 为最优策略期望代价上界，S、A 分别为状态数与动作数，K 为回合数；主导项与极小极大下界 Ω(B⋆√(SAK)) 相匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法在未知转移的 SSP 在线强化学习中首次使后验采样的遗憾达到极小极大最优阶，解决了 Jafarnia-Jahromi 等提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study online reinforcement learning for the stochastic shortest path (SSP) problem with unknown transition dynamics. Although existing posterior-sampling methods perform well empirically on SSPs, their regret guarantees do not achieve the minimax-optimal order. We address this limitation by proposing OPSRL-SSP, an optimistic posterior-sampling algorithm for the SSP setting. The algorithm operates in epochs and uses a logarithmic, time-dependent schedule for the number of posterior samples. To induce optimism, we augment the transition model with a pseudo-state assigned an optimistic boundary value. We establish a high-probability regret bound of $\tilde{O}(B_\star \sqrt{SAK} + B_\star S^2 A)$, where $B_\star$ is an upper bound on the expected cost of the optimal policy, $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, respectively, and $K$ is the number of episodes. In addition, we extend the analysis to general zero-cost SSPs using a universal cost-perturbation argument. The leading term matches the minimax lower bound $Ω(B_\star \sqrt{SAK})$, thereby addressing the open problem raised by Jafarnia-Jahromi et al. [19] for posterior sampling in the SSP setting.

</details>


### [281] [Agent Self-Improvement with Frozen Weights: The Added Value and Limits of Failure Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11342)
*Arkid Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 测试固定权重的90亿模型：基于失败轨迹写出的"心智笔记"在单轮客服决策上比对照组提升4.0个百分点，但其中约60%来自"试错保留更优笔记"这一搜索过程本身；完整对话场景与工具使用任务未显示可证实增益，盲审还判定240个工具使用测试项中146项有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验AI智能体的"harness"（模型外围的笔记/上下文机制）能否真正从智能体的失败轨迹中学习，而非仅由见过或未见过失败信息的匹配式搜索带来表观提升。

Method: 使用权重固定的开源9B模型作为执行体，由更大的教师模型撰写笔记；设置对照——一组笔记基于智能体错误轨迹，另一组在相同流程中隐藏错误信息；在生成的客服决策任务上比较准确率。将同一方法迁移到完整客服对话与工具使用任务，并对"单一经验"笔记做严格筛选；最后对工具使用测试项进行盲审质检。

Result: 单轮客服决策上，基于失败轨迹的笔记较对照提升4.0个百分点，但纯搜索过程（尝试并保留更高分笔记）即贡献约60%的总提升；完整客服对话中无可见改进。严格筛选中仅2条客服经验入选（尚待新数据验证），1条未借助失败信息写成的工具使用经验在新任务上无可证实增益；一个无任何流程的"中性笔记"得分高于学到的经验（属探索性结果）。盲审判定240个工具使用测试项中146项存在缺陷。

Conclusion: "从失败中学习"的效应存在但在小效应量下脆弱：大部分增益可归因于搜索而非信息利用，且难以在更真实/更复杂的任务与更严格评测下复现；评测数据本身的质量问题也会混淆结论。

Abstract: We evaluate whether an AI agent's harness, the notes around its model, can learn from the agent's failure traces beyond a matched search that never sees them. It can, but less than it looks. We tested an open 9-billion-parameter model with fixed weights; a larger teacher model wrote its notes. On generated customer-service decisions, where both note writers were told the target rules, notes based on the agent's mistakes improved accuracy by 4.0 percentage points over notes from the same process with the mistakes withheld. That process alone, trying and keeping better-scoring notes, reached about 60% of the total improvement. In full customer-service conversations the same notes showed no demonstrated improvement. Separately, a strict screen of single lessons admitted two customer-service lessons, still awaiting tests on new data, and one tool-use lesson, written without seeing the agent's mistakes, which showed no demonstrated gain on new tool-use tasks. A neutral note with no procedure scored higher than the learned lessons (exploratory). A blind audit judged 146 of 240 tool-use test items defective.

</details>


### [282] [Spatial Harness: Grounding Spatial Reasoning in Executable World States with Physics Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11344)
*Chentao Cao,Zhanke Zhou,Sangni Duan,Bo Han,Hang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Spatial Harness 框架，将场景表示为显式、可执行的世界状态，用物理引擎反馈为视觉语言模型的空间推理提供基础。在 MindCube 上把 Qwen3.5-27B 从 52.70% 提升到 71.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型做空间推理要完成两步：从部分视角构建场景理解，再想象视点或运动变化后的结果，而这两步都在模型内部隐式完成。场景理解错了答案就错；即使理解正确，也不保证想象的变换满足空间约束。作者因此希望用显式、可执行的世界状态为空间推理提供物理引擎反馈。

Method: 提出 Spatial Harness：1）Perceiver 把从问题中抽取的场景约束与专门的视觉估计结合起来，把物体和相机放到统一坐标系；2）得到的状态实例化到物理引擎中；3）Reasoner 在引擎中执行视点变化，用计算出的测量值和渲染视图引导推理。在 MindCube、SAT、MMSI-Bench、SpatialRGPT-Bench 上，用开源和闭源权重的 VLM 评测。

Result: MindCube 上 Qwen3.5-27B 从 52.70% 提升到 71.14%，比 pySpatial 高 7.52 个百分点。在给出相同标注的物体和相机位姿时，达到 98.25%，而模型内部推理为 71.81%。

Conclusion: 把空间推理显式化为可执行的物理引擎世界状态，并配合测量反馈与渲染视图，能显著优于依赖模型内部隐式推理的方法；位姿准确时性能上限远高于纯模型内部推理，说明瓶颈主要在显式场景 grounding 而非推理本身。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires understanding how objects and observers are arranged in a scene and how their relations change with viewpoint or movement. Vision-language models (VLMs) typically perform both steps through model-internal reasoning, forming an implicit scene interpretation from partial views and imagining the consequences of spatial transformations. A misread scene can lead to a wrong answer, while even a correct interpretation does not ensure that imagined transformations satisfy spatial constraints. We introduce Spatial Harness, which represents scenes as explicit and executable world states to ground spatial reasoning in physics-engine feedback. A Perceiver integrates scene constraints extracted from the question with specialized visual estimates to place objects and cameras in a shared coordinate frame. The resulting state is instantiated in a physics engine, where a Reasoner executes viewpoint changes and uses computed measurements and rendered views to guide reasoning. We evaluate on MindCube, SAT, MMSI-Bench, and SpatialRGPT-Bench with open- and closed-weight VLMs. On MindCube, Spatial Harness improves Qwen3.5-27B from 52.70% to 71.14%, surpassing pySpatial by 7.52 percentage points. Given identical annotated object and camera poses, it reaches 98.25% versus 71.81% for model-internal reasoning. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/tmlr-group/Spatial-Harness

</details>


### [283] [MG-Cache: Multi-Granularity Caching for Accelerating Diffusion Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11365)
*Zesen Chen,Youxuan Xu,Shigang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MG-Cache：一种免训练的多粒度缓存方法，在时间步与Transformer层两个粒度上协同复用缓存以加速DiT高分辨率生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT在高分辨率图像生成时推理成本高；现有扩散缓存策略无法准确刻画不同计算模块的重要性差异，也缺少时间步与层粒度间的缓存协同，导致误差累积难以控制。

Method: 两阶段缓存策略：(1) 离线时间步profiling分析，识别可复用缓存的冗余时间步；(2) 自适应逐层缓存，结合输入稳定性与各Transformer层重要性，选择性缓存稳定的Attention与MLP子模块。方法完全免训练。

Result: 在主流开源DiT模型上验证有效：在保持与未缓存输出高度一致的同时显著加速，HunyuanVideo上最高达4.18倍加速。

Conclusion: 多粒度缓存可有效利用DiT在时间步与层维度上的计算冗余，实现免训练推理加速且保真度较高。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformer (DiT) models, while having become a powerful backbone for generative synthesis, suffer from high inference cost on generating high-resolution images. Diffusion caching has emerged as a promising acceleration technique to reduce inference cost. However, existing caching strategies fail to accurately identify the varying importance of different computational modules and rarely coordinate cache reusing across the timestep and layer granularities, making it difficult to control error accumulation. To address these limitations, we propose multi-granularity caching (MG-Cache) for DiTs, a novel and fully training-free approach to exploit computational redundancy both on timesteps and Transformer layers. Our core innovation contains a two-stage caching strategy: an offline timestep profiling analysis that identifies redundant timesteps for cache reuse, followed by an adaptive layer-wise caching strategy that combines input stability with importance of each Transformer layer to selectively cache stable Attention and MLP submodules. Extensive experiments on mainstream open-source DiT models validate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving significant acceleration while maintaining high fidelity to the uncached output, with up to a 4.18x acceleration on HunyuanVideo.

</details>


### [284] [D3Tree: Learning to Prune Block-Diffusion Draft Trees for Efficient Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11371)
*Hong Nie,Zhangchi Gao,Jun Yu,Bo Wang,Jianqing Sun,Shengping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D3Tree 用一个小型树大小预测头学习块扩散草稿树中各节点是否值得验证，从而按请求与解码轮自适应地产生祖先封闭的验证子树，并在高并发下按批级节点预算调度，在 72 个负载设置中 70 个取得最快速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散 drafter 一次前向可生成整块 token，基于逐位置边际构造的草稿树能提升投机解码的期望接受长度。但每个树节点在目标验证中占用一行 query，并发服务时低产出分支会挤占其他请求的验证算力。现有树方法按草稿概率在固定或按成本选定的每请求预算下选节点，等价于总是保留 drafter 排名的一个前缀，无法判断哪些节点真正值得验证。

Method: 提出 D3Tree：1) 树大小预测头仅 2H+2 参数，输入草稿状态、候选嵌入与草稿对数概率，预测各候选的条件存活概率；2) 沿父链对各分数连乘并阈值化，得到祖先封闭（ancestor-closed）的子树，其规模随请求和解码轮自适应；3) 在冻结 drafter 上以全候选树的条件下标签训练该头，可选联合模式还引导 drafter 偏向被保留的参考路径；4) 服务时轻负载验证整棵树，重负载按预测存活率分配批级节点预算并对齐目标模型矩阵乘 tile；5) 保留树共享一次祖先掩码的目标前向，构造、剪枝与调度在 GPU 上执行并与主机重叠。

Result: 在六个视觉语言验证器、17 个文本与多模态基准上，D3Tree 在 72 个负载设置中的 70 个最快；单请求相对自回归解码加速 3.2–4.0x，64 并发请求下 1.3–1.8x，而固定预算树在后者已低于自回归速度；在时变到达轨迹下，每个到达阶段的平均端到端延迟均最低。

Conclusion: 验证应被视为可学习的资源分配问题而非草稿排名的前缀截断；极轻量的存活预测头即可实现请求自适应、负载感知的树规模控制，兼顾轻载加速与重载下的调度友好性。

Abstract: Block-diffusion drafters propose a whole block of tokens in one forward pass, and draft trees built from their position-wise marginals increase the expected accepted length of speculative decoding. Every tree node occupies a query row in target verification, so under concurrent serving low-yield branches compete with other requests for verification capacity. Existing tree methods select verified nodes by draft probability under a fixed or cost-selected per-request budget, and thus always retain a prefix of the drafter's ranking. We present D3Tree, which learns which nodes of a block-diffusion draft tree are worth verifying. A tree-size head with 2H+2 parameters predicts each candidate's conditional survival from the draft state, the candidate embedding, and its draft log-probability. Thresholding the product of these scores along parent chains yields an ancestor-closed subtree whose size adapts to each request and decoding round. The head is trained on a frozen drafter with conditional labels on the full candidate tree; an optional joint mode also steers the drafter toward the retained reference path. At serving time, D3Tree verifies whole trees under light load and, under heavy load, allocates one batch-wide node budget by predicted survival, sized to the target's matrix-multiplication tiles. Retained trees share one ancestor-masked target pass, while construction, pruning, and scheduling run on the GPU and overlap with the host. Across six vision-language verifiers and 17 text and multimodal benchmarks, D3Tree is fastest in 70 of 72 load settings, with 3.2-4.0x speedup over autoregressive decoding for one request and 1.3-1.8x at 64 concurrent requests, where fixed-budget trees fall below autoregressive speed. Under a time-varying arrival trace, it has the lowest mean end-to-end latency in every arrival phase.

</details>


### [285] [LLM-based Multi-Criteria Deep Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Efficient 5G UPF Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11372)
*Abdelrahman Soliman,Ahmed Refaey Hussein,Lei Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型（LLMs）翻译运营商意图并结合NWDAF异常告警的深度强化学习（DRL）UPF选择方法，采用动态多目标奖励函数，并在真实5G仿真平台上验证，性能优于启发式基线和固定DRL约16.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有5G网络中UPF（用户面功能）选择研究多采用局限性较强的方法，难以兼顾网络效率与资源管理，缺乏面向动态、意图驱动场景的有效方案，且在真实环境中验证不足。

Method: 构建基于DRL的UPF选择框架，其奖励函数为动态多目标形式；利用LLM将网络运营商意图转化为可执行的决策信号，并融合NWDAF的异常告警信息作为状态输入；在真实5G仿真平台上进行实验验证，与启发式基线和固定奖励DRL方法对比。

Result: 在真实实验环境中，所提动态DRL方案在时延、能耗和流量分布之间实现了有效平衡，整体性能较启发式基线和固定DRL提升约16.89%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明基于LLM意图解析与NWDAF告警驱动的动态多目标DRL可用于实际场景的UPF选择，具备良好的现实适用性与意图驱动自适应能力。

Abstract: User plane function (UPF) selection in 5G networks is an active area of research, with various limited approaches proposed to enhance network efficiency and manage resources effectively. In this paper, we address this gap in the related literature by proposing a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) UPF selection method that utilizes a dynamic, multi-objective reward function and validating it on a real-world 5G emulation platform. The proposed method is based on the large language models' (LLMs) translation of network operator intents and the network data analytics function (NWDAF) abnormality alerts. The experimental results validated in the real-world experimental setup demonstrate that our dynamic DRL solution achieves an effective balance between latency, power consumption, and traffic distribution, outperforming the heuristic baselines and fixed DRL by 16.89%. These findings highlight the real-world applicability of our approach for dynamic, intent-driven UPF selection.

</details>


### [286] [Self-Supervised Collapse Prevention with Score-Based Entropy Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11379)
*Adarsh Kappiyath,Silpa Vadakkeeveetil Sreelatha,Xiatian Zhu,Yann LeCun,Lu Yin,Amir Bar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于熵的正则化框架 EBReg，利用潜空间 score 估计正则化自监督训练中的潜分布，防止表示坍缩，并在测试时用同一 score 模型精炼受扰表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的 score-based 目标在生成建模中有效，但其在自监督表示学习中的潜力尚未充分探索；自监督方法面临表示坍缩风险，需要一种保持多样性的正则信号。

Method: 联合学习编码器与潜空间 score 模型，由互信息下界导出目标：视图对齐 + 平滑边缘熵最大化，避免显式密度估计即可优化边缘熵；学到的 score 模型在测试时用于将受扰表示推向训练潜分布（score-guided test-time adaptation）。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 上 ViT-L 线性探测达 75.57%；跨多种架构，score 引导的测试时自适应在 ImageNet-C 上带来约 5% 的平均相对精度提升。

Conclusion: EBReg 将 score 估计引入自监督学习，可有效防坍缩并获得有竞争力的表示，同一 score 模型兼具正则化与测试时鲁棒性提升的双重作用。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated the effectiveness of score-based objectives for generative modeling, yet their potential for self-supervised representation learning remains underexplored. We introduce Entropy-Based Regularization (EBReg), a framework that uses latent score estimation to regularize the latent distribution during self-supervised training. EBReg jointly learns an encoder and a latent score model, and utilizes the learned score to provide a diversity-preserving signal that prevents representational collapse. Motivated by a mutual-information lower bound, we formulate the objective as view alignment with smoothed marginal entropy maximization and optimize the marginal entropy without explicit density evaluation. The same score model can additionally be used at test time to refine corrupted representations toward the training latent distribution. Empirically, EBReg learns competitive representations on ImageNet-1K, achieving 75.57% linear-probe accuracy with ViT-L. Across diverse architectures, score-guided test-time adaptation further improves robustness on ImageNet-C, yielding an average relative accuracy gain of approximately 5%.

</details>


### [287] [The comparison of density estimation for transfer learning in Banach, Hilbert, Euclidean spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11382)
*Maryam Alsadat Kazemian,Azam Bastanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Banach流形迁移学习（BMTL），将特征嵌入测地流形并用L1范数密度正则化（等价于压缩总变差距离）替代L2/RKHS对称散度，在多个DA基准上取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域自适应方法（域核密度匹配、子空间对齐）默认采用希尔伯特几何，用欧氏空间或RKHS上的对称L2散度度量分布差异。L2对偏差取平方，会放大尾部误差、过度强调不代表真实分布漂移的密度离群点。

Method: BMTL的两个耦合设计：(1) 特征嵌入测地流形而非平坦欧氏空间中的点；(2) 密度匹配正则项采用L1 Banach范数，依据Scheffé定理，其最小化等价于压缩域间总变差（TV）距离——一个有界度量，对样本空间各区域均匀加权。

Result: 在MNIST-USPS、COIL20、Office+Caltech10上稳定提升；L1变体在每个任务上均优于同构的L2消融，如USPS->MNIST上92.44% vs 89.80%；在12个Office+Caltech迁移任务上平均超过最强基线DMDA +3.68%。

Conclusion: 论文以欧氏、希尔伯特与Banach三种几何的显式对比定位其贡献，表明非希尔伯特（L1/TV）几何为域自适应提供了更稳健的分布匹配范式。

Abstract: Domain adaptation or DA methods are indispensable when target labels or computational resources are scarce, yet the domain kernel density and subspace alignment approaches completely assume a Hilbertian geometry.
  They measure distributional discrepancy with symmetric L2 divergences over Euclidean feature spaces or reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS). This preprint summarizes the manuscript Non-Hilbertian Domain Adaptation. Total Variation Alignment via L1 Density Regularization on Feature Manifolds, which proposes Banach Manifold Transfer Learning or BMTL. BMTL makes two coupled design decisions. The first one is that features are embedded on a geodesic manifold rather than treated as points of a flat Euclidean space, and the second one is that the density matching regularizer is formulated in an L1 Banach norm, whose minimization is equivalent, by Scheffe's theorem, to contracting the Total Variation or TV distance between domain densities, a bounded metric that weights all regions of the sample space uniformly. The L2 formulation, by contrast, squares deviations and therefore amplifies errors in the tails, over emphasizing density outliers that do not reflect meaningful distributional shift. Experiments on MNIST-USPS, COIL20, and Office+Caltech10 show consistent gains; the L1 variant beats an otherwise identical L2 ablation on every task, for example 92.44% vs. 89.80% on USPS->MNIST and surpasses the strongest baseline DMDA by +3.68% on average over twelve Office+Caltech transfers. This document situates the contribution with an explicit comparison of Euclidean, Hilbert, and Banach geometries.

</details>


### [288] [Orthogonal Updates for the Win: Towards Accelerated Adaptive Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11387)
*Zhiwei Zhai,Xinyu Wang,Wenjing Yan,Lei Ding,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于正交更新的极小极大优化框架，并发展自适应步长方法 AdaSGDA 及其方差缩减版本 AdaSGDA-VR，实现"参数无关"的同时达到 O(T^{-1/4}) 与 O(T^{-1/3}) 收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单循环随机极小极大方法在原始-对偶紧耦合动态下易震荡、不稳定，性能对步长极为敏感，需从根本上提升稳定性并摆脱问题相关的调参。

Method: 1）构建面向极小极大优化的正交更新框架，沿正交化方向更新以降低更新各向异性；2）针对正交化改变优化几何、原始-对偶步长协调更困难的问题，提出 AdaSGDA，用累积梯度范数驱动的自适应步长规则自动平衡耦合的原始-对偶进度；3）进一步提出 AdaSGDA-VR，引入精心设计的方差缩减机制。

Result: 在非凸-强凹设定下证明 AdaSGDA 无需问题相关调参即可达到当前最优的 O(T^{-1/4}) 收敛率；AdaSGDA-VR 借助方差缩减达到更快的 O(T^{-1/3}) 收敛率；大量实验显示与现有极小极大优化方法相比具竞争力。

Conclusion: 正交更新可有效缓解紧耦合原始-对偶动态的不稳定性，配合梯度范数驱动的自适应步长可实现有效参数无关且理论最优的单循环极小极大优化，方差缩减进一步带来更优复杂度。

Abstract: Single-loop minimax methods are appealing for modern machine learning, but standard stochastic gradient updates can be unstable and oscillatory under tightly coupled primal-dual dynamics, making performance notoriously sensitive to stepsizes. To fundamentally improve stability, we develop an orthogonal update framework tailored to minimax optimization, which applies updates along orthogonalized directions to reduce update anisotropy. However, orthogonalization reshapes the optimization geometry and makes coordinating primal-dual stepsizes even more delicate. To overcome this challenge, we develop AdaSGDA, which equips orthogonal updates with an adaptive stepsize rule driven by accumulated gradient norms. This mechanism automatically balances coupled primal-dual progress under orthogonalization, yielding an effectively parameter-free method for nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax optimization. We prove that AdaSGDA avoids problem-dependent tuning while attaining the state-of-the-art $\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-1/4}\right)$ convergence rate. We further propose AdaSGDA-VR, which incorporates a carefully designed variance reduction scheme and achieves the faster $\mathcal{O}\left(T^{-1/3}\right)$ rate. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our methods deliver competitive performance compared to existing minimax optimization methods.

</details>


### [289] [Decomposing Earth Embeddings with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11398)
*Vitus Benson,Fanny Yang,Markus Reichstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将地球观测基础模型的高维嵌入分解为稀疏、近似单义的过完备字典特征，并设计针对性评测协议（同稀疏度下的重建、与参考概念的对齐、空间尺度），在AlphaEarth与Tessera嵌入上验证SAE的重建与可解释性优势，同时指出其下游应用尚不成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测基础模型产出的米级全球嵌入被广泛用于农业、生态、水文等领域，但这些嵌入不透明：单个维度纠缠多个互不相关的物理概念，导致难以解释，也难以评估其鲁棒性。现有做法中，用LLM评判特征解释的方式无法迁移到卫星像元上，且需考虑空间自相关，因此缺乏适配的评估手段。

Method: 1）将 SAE（含BatchTopK、Matryoshka等变体）用于把地球嵌入分解为过完备字典上的稀疏、近似单义特征；2）提出定制评测协议，从三方面对任意SAE打分：同稀疏度下的重建质量、与参考概念组的对齐程度、特征方差的空间尺度分布，并与多个基线和“解码器为冻结随机字典”的SAE对比；3）在AlphaEarth与Tessera嵌入上实验；4）在两个应用上检验稀疏码：发现土地覆盖子概念、通过适用域估计检测分布外像元。

Result: 在同稀疏度下，SAE对AlphaEarth和Tessera的重建优于所有基线；其特征与参考概念的对齐远少于原始维度（AlphaEarth上88%的特征至多与20个概念组中的1个对齐，多数不对齐任何概念组，而原始维度中位数对齐12个）；特征方差更多分布在细空间尺度（BatchTopK与Matryoshka在640 m斑块内为23%–32%，对照为9%）。冻结随机解码器的SAE同样具备这两种性质，说明二者源于稀疏性；但只有学习到的字典能准确重建嵌入——AlphaEarth上训练SAE的未解释方差少八倍。两个应用中稀疏特征均显示出可用潜力，但可靠性尚不足以实用。

Conclusion: SAE为地球观测嵌入提供了可检视的稀疏分解路径，是迈向特征可解释的地球观测模型的一步；但稀疏性本身即可带来单义性与细尺度特性，准确重建依赖学习字典，下游应用仍需提升可靠性。

Abstract: Earth observation foundation models produce global metre-resolution embeddings that underpin work across agriculture, ecology, hydrology, and beyond. Yet these embeddings are opaque: each dimension entangles unrelated physical concepts, which makes them difficult to interpret and their robustness hard to assess. Here, we propose sparse autoencoders (SAEs) as a principled way to decompose Earth embeddings into sparse, approximately monosemantic features over an overcomplete dictionary. Because LLM judges of interpretations do not transfer to satellite pixels, and spatial autocorrelation matters, we introduce a tailored evaluation protocol. It scores any SAE on reconstruction at matched sparsity, alignment with reference concepts, and spatial scale, each against baselines that include an SAE whose decoder is a frozen random dictionary. On this protocol, SAEs reconstruct AlphaEarth and Tessera embeddings better than every baseline at matched sparsity. Their features align with far fewer reference concepts than raw embedding dimensions (on AlphaEarth, 88% align with at most one of 20 concept groups, most with none, against a median of 12 per raw dimension). They also carry more of their variance at fine spatial scales (23 to 32% within a 640 m patch for BatchTopK and Matryoshka, against 9%). An SAE with a frozen random decoder shares both properties, which therefore follow from sparsity, whereas only a learned dictionary reconstructs the embedding accurately: on AlphaEarth, trained SAEs leave eight times less variance unexplained. We test the sparse code on two applications, discovering land-cover sub-concepts and detecting out-of-distribution pixels by area-of-applicability estimation. Both indicate how sparse features could be used, but neither is yet reliable enough for practical use. Together, these results are a step toward Earth observation models whose features can be inspected.

</details>


### [290] [Computationally Efficient Offline Policy Optimization with All-Policy Linear Realizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11423)
*Zhijie Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限时域MDP中离线策略优化：所有确定性马尔可夫策略的动作价值函数在已知特征下线性、系数范数不超过B。在单条未知行为策略的完整轨迹和全策略占用覆盖假设下，给出样本依赖为Õ(ε^-2)、计算复杂度关于log(2+B)多项式的算法，每层仅做一次凸回归，并给出两动作下界证明精度指数2是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有离线策略优化理论多依赖强假设（如对全部策略的可实现性、经验协方差近似、大样本下的计算可行性等），在仅有确定性策略可实现、系数规模B任意大、初始状态分布任意且需精确计算的情形下，如何同时获得最优样本复杂度与多项式（关于log B）计算复杂度尚不清楚。

Method: 算法以单条一致的马尔可夫witness策略的值为停止回报目标，每层仅执行一次凸回归；采用共享参考设计（shared reference design）与有界预测约束，将快速裁剪回归误差转化为原始线性预测的逐点置信；用截断杠杆（truncated leverage）控制性能损失而无需在B^-2尺度上近似经验协方差；将置信半径向上取整以保持witness精确性，从而在有理数输入下得到多项式时间比特实现。保证仅要求对确定性策略的可实现性与覆盖，适用于任意固定初始状态分布；另给出协方差覆盖变体，具备一致近似可实现性与关于B的多项式依赖。

Result: 得到Õ(ε^-2)的样本复杂度与关于log(2+B)多项式的计算复杂度；两动作实例的下界表明精度指数2不可改进，说明样本复杂度在指数意义下紧。

Conclusion: 该工作在全策略占用覆盖下，仅凭确定性策略可实现性即实现近最优的离线策略优化：样本复杂度Õ(ε^-2)、对log B多项式计算、每层单次凸回归，并给出匹配的两动作下界；同时提供协方差覆盖变体，扩展了线性可实现设定下离线RL的可解性边界。

Abstract: We study offline policy optimization in finite-horizon Markov decision processes where every deterministic Markov policy has an action-value function linear in known features, with coefficient norm at most $B$. From independent complete trajectories of one unknown Markov behavior policy, and under all-policy occupancy coverage, we give an algorithm with $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ sample dependence and computational complexity polynomial in $\log(2+B)$. The algorithm performs exactly one convex regression per layer. Its stopped-return targets are the values of a single consistent Markov witness policy. A shared reference design and bounded prediction constraints convert fast clipped-regression error into pointwise confidence for the raw linear predictions. Truncated leverage controls the performance loss without requiring empirical covariance approximation at scale $B^{-2}$. Rounding confidence radii upward preserves the exact witness and yields a polynomial-time bit implementation for rational numerical inputs. The guarantees allow any fixed initial-state distribution; realizability and coverage need only be checked for deterministic policies. We also establish a covariance-coverage variant with uniform approximate realizability and polynomial dependence on $B$. A two-action lower bound shows that the accuracy exponent $2$ is necessary.

</details>


### [291] [CHANNELS4WORLD: An Environment-Scale Dataset for Wireless Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11428)
*Joonkyu Jang,Hyung-Joo Moon,Kwang Soon Kim,Seong-Lyun Kim,Jakob Hoydis,Robert W. Heath,,Chan-Byoung Chae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Channels4World：以场景为中心的10万室外RF场景数据集及程序化生成方法，每场景含32个基站位置、16384个UE位置与3.5/7 GHz对齐多径及交互记录，共524.3亿BS-UE对、1048.6亿频点评估、29.14 TB；揭示环境广度可独立于场景内观测密度变化，并能驱动无线基础模型的跨环境泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线基础模型需跨任务、配置与部署环境复用表示，预训练要求环境多样性（environmental breadth）；但现有无线数据集往往站点少而采样密集（few sites sampled densely），缺乏场景层面的广度，且难以把"环境广度"与"场景内观测密度"解耦控制与评估。

Method: 构建场景中心式（scene-centered）数据集与程序化生成流程：每个场景将生成环境与RF材质赋值，绑定32个BS位置、16384个UE位置，并在3.5 GHz与7 GHz上给出对齐的变长多径及有序交互记录；以场景身份统一几何、部署、信道与交互的时间/空间对齐；提供多尺度预定义子集与组件化访问，采用确定性90K/10K场景级划分（两频段与全部场景组件同置）；分析覆盖三种生成剖面下的形态学、RF材质与收发机部署，并进行受控传播与路径保留（path retention）分析及等回合数（equal-episode）对照训练实验。

Result: 语料规模：10万场景、5.243e10个物理BS-UE对、1.0486e11次频点特定评估、29.14 TB打包数据。受控传播分析显示衍射显著影响保留链路的可用性与多径结构；路径保留策略在大幅降低存储的同时，对已表征的聚合功率损失很小。匹配训练曝光下，基于更广场景集合训练的模型在留出场景上表现更好，但等回合控制表明仅靠场景数量无法解释该增益。

Conclusion: 通过使环境广度显式且可控，Channels4World为无线基础模型在部署环境间泛化的开发与评测提供数据与方法基础；结果提示环境多样性的收益并非单纯来自场景计数，需结合场景结构与观测密度设计训练策略。

Abstract: Wireless foundation models learn reusable representations across tasks, configurations, and deployment environments. Their pretraining requires environmental breadth, yet wireless datasets often sample few sites densely. We introduce Channels4World, a scene-centered dataset of 100,000 distinct outdoor RF scenes and a procedural method for generating them. Each scene links a generated environment and RF material assignments to 32 base-station (BS) positions, 16,384 UE positions, and aligned variable-length multipath and ordered interaction records at 3.5 and 7~GHz. The corpus comprises 52.43 billion physical BS-UE pairs, 104.86 billion frequency-specific evaluations, and a 29.14-TB packaged payload. Scene identity aligns geometry, placements, channels, and interactions across frequencies, allowing environmental breadth and within-scene observation density to vary independently. The public release offers predefined subsets at multiple scales and separate access to corpus components. A deterministic 90K/10K scene-level train-test split keeps both frequencies and all scene components together. We characterize morphology, RF materials, and transceiver placement across three generation profiles. Controlled propagation and path-retention analyses show that diffraction materially affects retained-link availability and multipath structure, while the retention policy reduces storage substantially with little loss in represented aggregate power. Under matched training exposure, models trained on broader scene collections perform better on held-out scenes, although an equal-episode control shows that scene count alone cannot explain the gain. By making environmental breadth explicit and controllable, Channels4World supports the development and evaluation of wireless foundation models for generalization across deployment environments.

</details>


### [292] [Learning to Rank Propagators in Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11447)
*Matija Lojović,Mladen Nikolić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出从静态问题特征中学习实例相关的传播器优先级，将任务形式化为排序学习问题，用自注意力模型联合打分并用Plackett-Luce排序损失训练，在Choco求解器上验证可显著提升求解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传播器执行顺序对约束规划求解器性能影响显著，但现有传播器优先级通常由固定的人工设计启发式决定，缺乏针对具体实例的自适应能力。

Method: 将传播器优先级分配建模为learning-to-rank问题：由于优先级优劣只能通过求解器运行时间间接体现、单个传播器的理想优先级不可直接观测，作者基于随机化优先级配置下的求解运行时间构造成对偏好，训练自注意力模型对实例中所有传播器联合打分，并采用Plackett-Luce排序目标优化；预测排名在搜索开始前映射为Choco求解器的离散优先级等级，不修改其传播与搜索流程。

Result: 在XCSP3实例上验证：传播器排序对求解时间影响显著。在按问题类别筛选出的698个最难测试实例上，相比Choco默认优先级，学习优先级使求解实例数从634提升至654，中位运行时间降低27.93%，PAR1聚合罚时降低12.65%；提升幅度随问题类别而异，且简单的"学习/默认优先级"选择策略可进一步增加求解实例数。

Conclusion: 学习实例相关的传播器优先级能够有效提升约束规划求解器性能，尤其在困难实例上效果显著，表明数据驱动的传播器调度是改进CP求解性能的可行方向。

Abstract: The order in which propagators are executed can substantially affect the performance of constraint programming solvers, yet propagator priorities are typically determined by fixed, manually designed heuristics. In this work, we explore learning instance-dependent propagator priorities from static problem features. We formulate the task as a learning-to-rank problem, addressing the challenge that the quality of a priority assignment can be measured through solver runtime, while the desired priorities of individual propagators are not directly observable. A self-attention-based model jointly scores the propagators present in an instance and is trained using pairwise preferences derived from solver runtimes measured under randomized propagator priority assignments, with a Plackett-Luce-based ranking objective. Predicted rankings are mapped to the discrete priority levels of the Choco solver before search begins, without modifying its propagation or search procedures. We evaluate the approach on XCSP3 instances and demonstrate that propagator ordering can have a substantial impact on solving time. On a test set of 698 instances, picked out as most difficult by problem class, the learned priorities increase the number of solved instances from 634 to 654, reduce median solver runtime by 27.93%, and reduce aggregate penalized runtime (PAR1) by 12.65% compared with Choco's default priorities. We further observe that improvements vary across problem classes and show that a simple policy selecting between learned and default priorities further increases the number of solved instances. These results demonstrate the potential of learning instance-dependent propagator priorities to improve constraint programming performance, particularly on difficult instances.

</details>


### [293] [How Much Training-Inference Mismatch Can On-Policy Distillation Tolerate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11450)
*Cong Yu,Ziting Wang,Songlin Jiang,Sitong Zhang,Chengliang Chai,Youhe Jiang,Eiko Yoneki,Tuo Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究on-policy distillation中的训练-推理失配，提出可测量的“失配预算”，并给出准入检查与FP8+异步rollout的实用配方，实现至少1.93× KV-cache容量与1.62×训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD在自身生成响应上训练，但为降低成本采用量化推理与异步rollout，二者都造成rollout引擎与训练模型间的失配。该失配可使RL不稳定，但其对OPD的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 将各类失配源统一到同一尺度度量：在三组师生对上考察量化，并在其中一组上测试权重陈旧与注入噪声；对比OPD与RL在rollout配置下的稳定性；分析层面敏感性与前缀偏移；提出无需完整训练即可用校准预算筛选rollout配置的轻量准入检查。

Result: OPD可容忍显著失配而训练质量无明显损失，超出后性能下降，呈现经验失配预算；失配代价与量化引入的失配均依赖模型，部分层对低精度尤其敏感；OPD在会使RL失稳的配置下仍稳定；权重噪声下失配会改变生成前缀，逐token校正无法弥补。模型专属FP8配方提供至少1.93×于BF16的KV-cache token容量，FP8+异步rollout比同步BF16训练快1.62×达到同等质量。

Conclusion: OPD对训练-推理失配具有可量化的容忍度，可被系统性地利用：通过预算化度量与准入检查筛选配置，并结合模型专属FP8量化与异步rollout在保持质量的同时提升吞吐与KV-cache容量。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student language model on its own responses, guided by a fixed teacher. Generating these responses is expensive, so systems use quantized inference and asynchronous rollouts to reduce the cost. Both, however, introduce training-inference mismatch between the rollout engine and the model being trained. Such mismatch can destabilize reinforcement learning (RL), but its effects on OPD remain unclear. This paper investigates how much mismatch OPD tolerates, where its effects arise, and how training systems can use this tolerance. Measuring all mismatch sources on one scale, we examine quantization across three student-teacher pairs, with additional tests of weight staleness and injected noise on one pair. OPD tolerates substantial mismatch without meaningful loss of training quality but degrades at higher levels, revealing an empirical mismatch budget. Both the quality cost of mismatch and the mismatch introduced by quantization depend on the model, with some layers particularly sensitive to reduced precision. In controlled comparisons, OPD remains stable under rollout configurations that destabilize RL methods. Under weight noise, mismatch also shifts the prefixes reached during generation, introducing errors that per-token corrections cannot address. To put these findings into practice, we introduce a lightweight admission check that screens rollout configurations against a calibrated budget without requiring a full training run for each candidate. Model-specific FP8 recipes preserve training quality while providing capacity for at least $1.93\times$ as many KV-cache tokens as BF16. Combining FP8 with asynchronous rollouts reaches the same training quality $1.62\times$ sooner than synchronous BF16 training. Code is available at https://github.com/benyucong/train-inference-mismatch-opd

</details>


### [294] [MOMENT-DP: Momentum Aware Evaluation of Correlated Noise for Differentially Private Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11457)
*Sunny Gupta,Raja Viswanadha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOMENT-DP 框架，研究优化器动量状态如何影响时间相关隐私噪声在差分隐私优化中的传播与效用，并发现仅改变动量即可导致机制排序反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间相关噪声能显著提升差分隐私优化的效用，但现有机制设计与评估通常只利用工作负载结构、学习率调度或局部曲率等部分信息。作者追问：持久化的优化器状态（如动量）是否提供额外信息，从而改变隐私有效的时间相关噪声机制之间的相对效用。

Method: 提出 MOMENT-DP，一个感知优化器状态的框架，刻画时间相关隐私噪声如何经基于动量的优化过程传播。对局部二次目标，用"优化器传递算子"将隐私导致的最终损失表示为联合刻画曲率、学习率调度、动量与噪声时间协方差的表达式；NoiseCurve 的曲率感知二次准则被精确还原为零动量、常数学习率的特例。

Result: 在曲率、调度、隐私校准与候选机制均固定时，仅改变动量即可引发真实的机制排序反转；该效应在逐样本裁剪的私有逻辑回归（非严格二次动态）中依然存在，而在所研究的神经网络情形中尚未定论。

Conclusion: 优化器状态是被低估且与决策相关的评估维度，在评估时间相关隐私噪声时应显式纳入建模。

Abstract: Temporally correlated noise can substantially improve the utility of differentially private optimization, yet candidate mechanisms are commonly designed or evaluated using only partial information about the optimization process, such as workload structure, learning-rate schedules, or local curvature. We study whether persistent optimizer state provides additional information that can change the relative utility of privacy-valid correlated-noise mechanisms.
  We introduce MOMENT-DP, an optimizer-state-aware framework that models how temporally correlated privacy noise propagates through momentum-based optimization. For locally quadratic objectives, MOMENT-DP expresses privacy-induced final loss through an optimizer transfer operator that jointly captures curvature, learning-rate scheduling, momentum, and temporal noise covariance. NoiseCurve's curvature-aware quadratic criterion is recovered exactly as the zero-momentum, constant-learning-rate special case.
