  Changing only momentum while holding curvature, schedule, privacy calibration, and candidate mechanisms fixed induces genuine mechanism-ranking reversals. The effect persists outside exact quadratic dynamics in per-example-clipped private logistic regression, while remaining unresolved in the neural regime we study. These results establish optimizer state as an under-modeled, decision-relevant axis in evaluating temporally correlated privacy noise.

</details>


### [295] [One-Shot Hierarchical Federated Clustering for Distributed Non-IID Biomedical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11481)
*Shenghong Cai,Yiqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的一次性（one-shot）层次联邦聚类框架，通过客户端到服务器的单向原型级通信实现跨机构数据分布探索与聚合，并用多粒度竞争学习机制缓解一次性传输带来的信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机构生物医学数据分析中，完整疾病画像分散在不同数据孤岛，且本地数据分布存在不同粒度甚至复杂的嵌套结构。层次聚类（HC）虽能刻画此类拓扑，但其递归过程计算开销大，且易造成隐私泄露，难以直接用于联邦场景。

Method: 客户端进行本地分布探索，服务器进行分布聚合，仅通过客户端向服务器的一次性、原型级单向通信完成；为弥补一次性原型传输导致的信息损失，在服务器端设计多粒度竞争学习机制，对聚类小簇（clusterlets）进行融合，以在受限单向通信下最大化全局分布拓扑的保留与挖掘。

Result: 实验表明，跨客户端的复杂聚类分布可被高效探索；在公开数据集上与多种先进方法对比，所提框架在稳健聚类性能、隐私保护与计算效率三者之间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 一次性原型级单向通信结合服务器端多粒度竞争学习，可有效替代传统递归层次聚类，在保证隐私与低计算开销的同时实现对复杂嵌套分布的联邦层次聚类。

Abstract: In multi-institutional biomedical data analysis, comprehensive disease profiles are often fragmented across different data silos. Furthermore, local data distributions typically exhibit varying levels of granularity or even complex nested structures. Although Hierarchical Clustering (HC) is a powerful tool for exploring such data topologies, its cumbersome recursive process incurs prohibitive computational overhead and renders it highly susceptible to privacy leakage. This paper, therefore, introduces an efficient one-shot hierarchical FC framework that performs client-end (institutions) distribution exploration and server-end distribution aggregation through one-way prototype-level communication from clients to the server. Although the designed one-shot prototype transmission guarantees minimal computational overhead and mitigates privacy risks, it inevitably incurs information loss. To counteract this, a multi-granular competitive learning mechanism is proposed on the server to fuse the clusterlets, effectively maximizing the retention and mining of global distribution topologies under limited, one-way communication constraints. It turns out that the complex cluster distributions across clients can be efficiently explored, and extensive experiments comparing state-of-the-art methods on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of the proposed framework in balancing robust clustering, privacy preservation, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [296] [Learning through Capacity Awareness to Minimize Hospital Readmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11493)
*Lydia T. Liu,Kara Schechtman,Pengyi Shi,Angela Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究出院决策与再入院风险预测模型反复重训练之间的反馈回路，指出容量感知的"discharge-up-to"策略能自然产生多样化的住院时长观测，从而在无需显式公平约束下获得无偏风险估计并缓解偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测式算法被广泛用于指导出院决策，但因历史数据不足或既往出院决策的影响，这类模型会对弱势患者产生删失偏倚（censoring bias）。当有偏的再入院风险预测器用于出院决策时，决策生成的新训练数据在重训练中会延续甚至放大同类偏倚，形成恶性循环。作者希望在不额外引入公平性约束的前提下打破这一循环。

Method: 将用于出院决策的排队论马尔可夫决策过程（queueing-based MDP）扩展为包含周期性重训练的再入院风险预测器。核心思想是：成本最优的"容量感知"（capacity-aware）运营决策策略会自然使患者结局被观测到的住院时长多样化，从而保证再入院风险的一致可估。作者证明在模型良设定（well-specification）下，采用 discharge-up-to 策略的重复重训练收敛到无偏风险估计。验证手段包括合成实验，以及基于合作医院患者级数据校准的仿真。

Result: 常用的"风险阈值"（threshold-on-risk）策略可能持续保持偏倚；而 discharge-up-to 策略相较现状决策策略将学习误差降低 24%–25%。估计精度的提升同时缓解了部分形式的偏倚，在调整人群间基线风险差异后，将再入院成本差距缩小 38%–63%。

Conclusion: 容量感知的决策策略通过缓解删失偏倚，可同时改善预测性能与公平性，说明运营层面的策略设计与统计学习之间存在正向耦合，为打破"决策—数据—模型"偏倚循环提供了可操作的途径。

Abstract: Predictive algorithms are increasingly used to guide hospital discharge decisions, yet these models can exhibit censoring bias for vulnerable patients due to insufficient historical data or past discharge decisions. When biased readmission risk predictors are used to inform discharges, the training data generated by discharge decisions may perpetuate similar biases when predictors are re-trained, leading to a vicious cycle. We extend a queueing-based Markov decision process for hospital discharge to incorporate periodically retrained readmission risk predictors. Our key insight is that cost-optimal, "capacity-aware" operational decision policies can naturally diversify the lengths of stay at which patient outcomes are observed, enabling consistent estimation of readmission risk without explicit fairness constraints. We prove that repeated retraining under discharge-up-to policies converges to unbiased risk estimates under well-specification. In synthetic experiments and calibrated simulations based on patient-level data from a partner hospital, we find that where commonly used threshold-on-risk policies can remain persistently biased, discharge-up-to policies reduce learning error over status quo decision policies (such as thresholding on risk) by 24%-25%. These improvements to estimation also mitigate some forms of bias, reducing readmission cost disparities (adjusted for baseline risk differences between demographic groups) by 38%-63%. Our results demonstrate how capacity-aware decision policies can improve both predictive performance and fairness by addressing censoring bias.

</details>


### [297] [Regret without Optimal-Arm Feedback under Reward-Dependent Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11494)
*Zhong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究奖励依赖（可无限）且回报不含轮次信息的随机赌博机延迟问题，提出平衡历史乐观（BHO）算法，给出下界与有限K时的Ω(G log K)最坏情形界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有延迟赌博机理论多假设延迟与臂无关或已知轮次归属，而实际中延迟由奖励触发、甚至无限，且反馈省略来源轮次，导致"等待"无法归因，需要新的乐观机制与平衡探索设计。

Method: BHO：对每个c∈[0,1)，以延迟项由次优臂最大分位数（水平1-cΔ_i）控制，无需最优臂报告；引入"挂起触发的平衡"（pending-triggered balance）来保持加权截断界；并用间隙加权比较与实验验证。

Result: 下界显示对数统计区间内存在平方倒数松弛代价，c=1时minimax regret线性；K增长时任意学习器最坏 regret 为Ω(G log K)，G=max_i Δ_i h_i；pending-triggered balance 在c<1保留加权截断界，确定性奖励、唯一最优、固定有界延迟下达到历史贪心探索系数。

Conclusion: 奖励依赖、无轮次归属的延迟可被间隙加权截断刻画；平衡探索是必要代价，共享等待的收益依间隙结构而定。

Abstract: We study stochastic bandits with reward-dependent delays, possibly infinite, whose reports omit the originating round. For each $c\in[0,1)$, Balanced Historical Optimism (BHO) has a delay term controlled by the largest suboptimal-arm quantile at level $1-cΔ_i$, without requiring optimal-arm reports. Lower bounds give an inverse-square slack cost in the logarithmic statistical regime and linear minimax regret at $c=1$. A family with growing $K$ forces worst-case regret $Ω(G\log K)$ for every learner, where $G=\max_iΔ_i h_i$ is the largest gap-weighted suboptimal cutoff. Pending-triggered balance preserves BHO's weighted-cutoff bound for $c<1$ and attains the historical-greedy exploration coefficient with deterministic rewards, a unique optimum and fixed bounded delays. Gap-weighted comparisons and experiments show when shared waiting helps and what balanced exploration costs.

</details>


### [298] [JoMoHa: Joint Model--Harness Optimization with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11499)
*Xudong Wu,Guangchen Lan,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JoMoHa框架，用强化学习联合训练可执行的harness生成策略与任务执行策略，使模型权重与harness协同演化，在ALFWorld、WebShop、DBBench上取得最高成功率（平均70.3%，ALFWorld达97.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的性能同时取决于模型权重与harness，单独优化任一方都会受另一方制约，难以获得整体提升；作者分析朴素交替训练后发现模型与harness会逐渐变得不兼容，干扰任务执行并可能损害模型训练，导致性能下降。

Method: JoMoHa通过强化学习联合训练两个策略：一个是可执行harness的生成策略，另一个是任务执行策略，从而实现模型与harness的协同演化。

Result: JoMoHa优于所评估的仅优化权重和仅优化harness的基线，在ALFWorld、WebShop和DBBench上取得所评估方法中的最高成功率，平均70.3%，ALFWorld上达到97.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从自身生成的轨迹中同时学习任务执行与运行时改进，JoMoHa实现了递归自我改进（RSI），表明模型与harness需要联合优化而非交替优化。

Abstract: The performance of language agents depends jointly on model weights and the harness. Optimizing either alone can be constrained by the other component, limiting overall performance gains. Our analysis of naive alternating training reveals progressive incompatibility between the model and harness, which increasingly interferes with task execution and may also undermine model training, leading to performance degradation. To coordinate the evolution of models and harnesses, we introduce JoMoHa, to our knowledge the first framework to jointly train an executable harness generation policy and a task execution policy through reinforcement learning. JoMoHa outperforms the evaluated weight-only and harness-only baselines and achieves the highest success rates among the evaluated methods on ALFWorld, WebShop, and DBBench, averaging 70.3% and reaching 97.0% on ALFWorld. By learning both task execution and runtime improvement from its own generated trajectories, JoMoHa performs recursive self-improvement (RSI).

</details>


### [299] [Data-informed Adaptation of Foundation Models for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11504)
*Chunwei Ma,Russell D Wolfinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoE-Learner，一种混合因果专家框架：将数据集在多种假设特定视角下嵌入，并用变差函数（variogram）作为无需反事实数据的路由器，判断各专家（对应不同因果估计策略的假设）在该数据上是否适用。在 IHDP、ACIC、Lalonde 基准上取得最佳平均排名，并把因果假设的验证从先验固定选择转变为可由数据几何直接检验的准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断的根本难题是无法观测反事实结果，导致训练目标（结果、倾向得分）与推断目标（CATE、ATE）之间存在固有鸿沟。传统做法依赖预先设定假设、选择识别策略与估计器；近期基于 PFN 的因果基础模型（CFM）虽通过大规模合成先验数据预训练简化了流程，但摊销化降低了透明性——难以判断模型对特定数据集隐含依赖了哪些假设。真实数据生成过程（DGP）与结构因果模型（SCM）高度多样，先验上难以知道哪种因果推断策略与给定数据集相容。因此作者聚焦"观测数据集与方法特定假设的相容性"这一问题。

Method: 作者发现数据嵌入的几何特征——特别是变差函数（variogram）——可作为"某因果估计策略是否合适"的有效信号。据此构建 MoE-Learner：一个混合因果专家框架，在多个假设特定视角下对数据集进行嵌入，并以变差函数作为无需反事实的路由器（counterfactual-free router），评估每个专家的有效性；随后按路由权重整合各专家结果。

Result: 在 IHDP、ACIC、Lalonde 基准数据集上，MoE-Learner 取得最佳平均排名（best average rank），优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法并非消除因果假设，而是将其验证从固定的先验选择转变为可直接从数据几何检验的判据。与（因果）基础模型正交，本文构建的是从数据特征到因果估计策略的桥梁，而非再提出一个新的 CFM。

Abstract: Due to the fundamental problem of causal inference -- the impossibility of observing counterfactual outcomes -- there is always a gap between the training goal (outcome, propensity) and the inference target (CATE, ATE). Traditional causal effect estimation revolves around setting assumptions, selecting identification strategies, and choosing causal estimators, while more recently, Causal Foundation Models (CFMs), built on Prior-Data-Fitted Networks (PFNs), ease this process by pretraining on massive synthetic prior datasets. Despite their success, such amortization makes causal inference less transparent, obscuring which assumptions the model implicitly relies on for a given dataset. The high diversity of real-world data-generating processes (DGPs) and Structural Causal Models (SCMs) makes it hard to know, a priori, which causal inference strategy is compatible with a given dataset. In this work, we focus on this compatibility between an observational dataset and method-specific assumptions, and find that geometric characteristics of data embeddings -- specifically, the variogram -- provide a useful signal of whether a given causal estimation strategy is appropriate. We develop MoE-Learner, a mixture-of-causal-experts framework that embeds a dataset under multiple assumption-specific views and uses the variogram as a counterfactual-free router to assess each expert's validity. Rather than eliminating causal assumptions, this shifts their validation from a fixed, a priori choice to a criterion that can be checked directly from data geometry. MoE-Learner achieves the best average rank on benchmark datasets (IHDP, ACIC, Lalonde). Being orthogonal to (causal) foundation models, our method builds a bridge from data characteristics to causal estimation strategy rather than proposing another CFM.

</details>


### [300] [ConvLUT: Rethinking Convolutional Architectures for Ultra-Low-Latency LUT-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11507)
*Marta Andronic,Olivia Weng,Danial Zuberi,Ryan Kastner,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConvLUT 提出一族可完全展开、可映射为查找表（LUT）的卷积架构，将 LUT 神经网络从 MLP 拓扑扩展到结构化视觉与音频任务，在 CIFAR-10、VWW、KWS 上以低于约 50 ns 的推理延迟取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LUT 的神经网络能在 FPGA 上实现超低延迟推理，但现有工作基本局限于 MLP 式拓扑，结构化视觉与音频任务（如卷积网络承载的任务）尚未被充分探索。因此作者希望把 LUT 映射范式推广到卷积架构，并在 MLPerf Tiny 类基准上验证。

Method: 提出 ConvLUT 架构族，包含基于层次化 LUT 树的标准卷积模型与遵循 MobileNet 设计的深度可分离模型；核心技术为四项：(1) 统一 LUT 卷积框架，在显式扇入约束下支持标准、深度可分离与逐点卷积；(2) LUT 树结构以缓解 k²c 感受野维度瓶颈；(3) 通道划分的 LUT 池化层替代传统空间池化；(4) 面向硬件的迭代剪枝策略以保证大规模训练稳定收敛。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、Visual Wake Words 与 Keyword Spotting 上，ConvLUT 取得有竞争力的精度，全展开推理延迟低于约 50 ns；CIFAR-10 上相较非逻辑基实现至少降低 8844× 延迟，并在同任务上比现有 MLP 式 LUT 模型测试精度至少高 20 个百分点。

Conclusion: LUT 映射网络可成功扩展到卷积与深度可分离拓扑，在保持精度的同时获得纳秒级推理延迟，证明"逻辑基/全展开"路线对结构化视觉与音频任务是可行且高效的，为 FPGA 上的超低延迟边缘推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Lookup-table (LUT)-based neural networks (NNs) offer a compelling path to ultra-low-latency inference on FPGAs. However, existing LUT-based neural networks have largely been restricted to MLP-style topologies, leaving structured vision and audio tasks underexplored. We present ConvLUT, a family of fully unrolled LUT-mappable convolutional architectures targeting the MLPerf Tiny benchmarks. It is comprised of a standard convolutional model built around a hierarchical tree of LUTs and a depthwise separable model following the MobileNet design philosophy. These architectures are enabled by four key contributions: a unified LUT-based convolutional framework supporting standard, depthwise, and pointwise convolutions under explicit fan-in constraints; a LUT-tree that addresses the $k^2c$ receptive field dimensionality bottleneck; a channel-partitioned LUT pooling layer that replaces conventional spatial pooling; and a hardware-aware iterative pruning strategy for stable convergence at scale. Evaluated on CIFAR-10, Visual Wake Words, and Keyword Spotting, ConvLUT achieves competitive accuracy with fully unrolled inference latencies below $\sim 50$ ns. On CIFAR-10 it reaches at least a $8844\times$ latency reduction over non-logic-based prior implementations, and outperforms existing MLP-style LUT-based models on the same task by at least $20$ pp in test accuracy.

</details>


### [301] [Detecting Common Golfing Faults Using AI and Video-Derived Biomechanical Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11515)
*Oliver Martin,Jeff Clark,Nawid Keshtmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一套无需标记与标定的视频分析流程，从普通手机视频中检测高尔夫挥杆动作缺陷（over-the-top、early-extension），用浅层提升树分类教练可测量的生物力学量，ROC-AUC分别达0.975与0.911，且决策可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室外运动员技术评估仍依赖肉眼观察，主观且缺乏量化；端到端深度学习方法缺乏可解释性，难以与教练的诊断逻辑对应。因此需要一种无标记、免标定、可解释且可在消费级设备上运行的自动技术评估方案。

Method: 不做端到端学习，而是先跟踪人体姿态与杆头，并在以身体为中心的坐标系中表达；提取教练实际会测量的生物力学量（如杆头相对挥杆平面的高度），在挥杆下杆的已知相位上计算特征，再用浅层提升树（boosted trees）做分类。在160个OTT样本与143个EE样本的公开数据上做交叉验证。

Result: OTT检测交叉验证ROC-AUC为0.975，EE为0.911；模型最依赖的信号与教练诊断这些缺陷时所检查的信号一致；每个决策可追溯到具体信号与挥杆时刻；普通笔记本上数秒内处理完手机视频。

Conclusion: 基于生物力学特征+浅层模型的流程可实现无标记、免标定、可解释的挥杆缺陷检测，具备做成面向各类水平球手App的可行性，并可推广到其他以生物力学参考评判技术的运动项目。

Abstract: Understanding how an athlete moves can improve both technique and safety, yet outside the laboratory technique is still assessed by eye. We present a pipeline that detects named golf swing faults from ordinary video, without markers or calibration. Instead of learning end to end, it tracks body pose and the clubhead in a body-centered frame, and classifies the biomechanical quantities a coach would measure, such as the height of the clubhead above the swing plane, with shallow boosted trees. We demonstrate the approach on two common faults, coming over-the-top (OTT) and early-extension (EE). On 160 swings and 143 swings from public sources it detects OTT and EE with a cross-validated ROC-AUC of 0.975 and 0.911 respectively. Because every feature is a biomechanical quantity a coach already uses, measured at a known phase of the downswing, each decision can be traced to the signals and the moments of the swing that drove it. The signals the model relies on most are the same ones a coach checks when diagnosing these faults. The pipeline processes phone video on a consumer laptop in seconds, so it could feasibly be developed into an app for golfers of all abilities and extended to other sports in which technique is judged against a biomechanical reference.

</details>


### [302] [Hydra: Towards Transferable Multi-Task Learning on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11520)
*Kiarash Shamsi,Farimah Poursafaei,Tran Gia Bao Ngo,Reihaneh Rabbany,Baris Coskunuzer,Guillaume Rabusseau,Michael M. Bronstein,Shenyang Huang,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hydra 架构，将时序 GNN 的局部连通性特征与谱学习模块的全局连通性模式相结合，在多任务目标下联合学习，实现时序图上的多任务图属性预测与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的演化网络可建模为时序图（TG），需捕捉时序动态以预测未来图属性。但现有方法主要面向单任务预测，其跨任务泛化与向未见网络的迁移能力尚不清楚，时序图多任务图属性预测仍是开放挑战。

Method: Hydra 架构：集成时序 GNN 提取的局部连通性特征与谱学习模块（spectral learning module）捕获的全局连通性模式，在多任务目标下联合学习局部与全局信息。

Result: 多任务分类中 AUC 相较最强基线取得 8.9% 相对提升；多任务回归中三项任务均有竞争力，两项任务最优，MAE 相对最强基线提升 8.2%；训练时间较时序迁移模型减少 22%。

Conclusion: 首次系统性地证明时序图上的多任务可迁移学习是有效的；Hydra 稳定取得最优排名，表明时序图多任务训练是通向可适应时序图基础模型的有前景方向。

Abstract: Real-world evolving networks are naturally modeled as temporal graphs (TGs), where capturing temporal dynamics is essential for predicting future graph properties that support downstream decision-making. Existing temporal graph methods have been developed primarily for single-task prediction, and little is known about their generalization across tasks or transfer to unseen networks. This leaves the challenge of multi-task graph property prediction in TGs largely open. We address this challenge by introducing Hydra, a novel architecture that integrates local connectivity features from temporal GNNs with a spectral learning module that captures global connectivity patterns. This design enables joint learning of local and global information under a multi-task objective. In multi-task classification, Hydra achieves an 8.9% relative gain in AUC over the strongest competitor. In multi-task regression, Hydra achieves competitive results in all three tasks, while obtaining the best results in two tasks with a 8.2% relative gain in MAE compared to the strongest baseline. Moreover, Hydra delivers these gains with a 22% reduction in training time compared to temporal transfer models. These results provide the first systematic evidence that multi-task transferable learning on temporal graphs is effective. By delivering consistent top-ranked performance, Hydra highlights multi-task training on temporal graphs as a promising direction toward adaptable foundation models for temporal graphs.

</details>


### [303] [Boundary Mass in Feature Space as a Label-Budget Diagnostic for Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11525)
*Siming Zhang,Zhehui Shen,Shijie Chen,Yansen Yu,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"有效边界复杂度"作为冻结表示+线性探针的标签预算诊断量:计数边界薄带附近的目标样本质量并按类别平衡归一化,理论上证明光滑表示映射下密度变化与切向面变化在一阶相消,仅边界法向伸缩起主导作用,并给出基于缓存特征的估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低标签迁移的核心问题是:表示看起来有用之后,学习下游边界还需要多少标签。现有可迁移性与探针质量分数(全局对齐、似然、间隔、校准)只刻画全局诊断,无法反映稀缺标签代价最高的模糊区域,因此需要补充"边界附近目标质量"这一诊断。

Method: 1) 定义有效边界复杂度:度量学习到的边界周围薄带内的样本质量,并以类别平衡归一化,同时计入稠密模糊区域与预测类别不平衡;2) 理论分析:在光滑表示映射下证明密度变化与切向表面变化在一阶上相互抵消,主导因子为边界法向的拉伸/压缩;3) 估计器:在缓存特征上于一个划分上拟合探针,再对独立留出特征统计其边界附近计数;4) 进一步构造可微代理以降低测得复杂度;5) 在受控变换、真实冻结特征、CIFAR-100/Tiny-ImageNet 上验证,含五分类 OvR 接口骨干排序与三种采样策略的采集实验。

Result: 受控变换下预测规律在74个映射上成立,相对误差中位数0.38%;真实冻结特征上法向变化同时改变测得的边界质量与标签需求,而切向对照保持一阶系数不变;可微代理在同等 top-1 效用下将测得复杂度降低16–36%;五路实验中 OvR 接口骨干排序 Spearman 相关达0.872,采集实验中初始边界质量与实际标签预算在三种采样策略下相关性为0.779–0.830。

Conclusion: 边界质量可作为近并列表示的二级诊断指标,能预测低标签迁移中的实际标签需求,并为表示选择提供与全局对齐类指标互补的信号。

Abstract: Low-label transfer is often decided by a practical question: after a representation looks useful, how many labels are still needed to learn the downstream boundary? Transferability and probe-quality scores characterize global alignment, likelihood, margins, and calibration. Boundary-near target mass complements these diagnostics by measuring the ambiguous regions where scarce labels are most costly. We propose effective boundary complexity, a label-budget diagnostic for frozen representations and linear probes. It measures mass in a thin strip around the learned boundary and normalizes it by class balance, so dense ambiguous regions and predicted-class imbalance are both counted. We prove that, under a smooth representation map, density change and tangential surface change cancel at first order, leaving boundary-normal stretch or compression as the governing factor. This yields a cached-feature estimator that fits a probe on one split and counts independent held-out features near its boundary. Experiments support the diagnostic at three levels. Controlled transformations match the predicted law across 74 maps with 0.38% median relative error. On real frozen features, normal-direction changes move both measured boundary mass and label demand, while tangent controls preserve the first-order coefficient. A differentiable surrogate reduces measured complexity by 16-36% at matched top-1 utility. Finally, CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet experiments support boundary mass as a second-stage diagnostic for near-tied representations. Five-way experiments show strong OvR-interface backbone-ranking agreement (Spearman 0.872), while acquisition experiments show high correlations between initial boundary mass and realized label budgets across three sampling policies (0.779-0.830). Code is available at https://github.com/qqaqq-code/boundary_mass.

</details>


### [304] [A Stability Analysis of AdamW: Unstable Equilibria and Non-Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11526)
*Baiyu Su,Lizhang Chen,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从动力系统视角分析AdamW：其不动点等价于一个带约束与正则项的优化问题的驻点，但并非都稳定；稳定性敏感依赖于曲率、权重衰减与动量参数，即便一维也可能出现持续振荡；给出高维连续时间局部稳定的充分条件，并提出保持不动点的收敛性修正算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW在深度学习中极为常用，但其行为（究竟对应什么优化问题、何时收敛）仍缺乏理解；作者希望厘清AdamW隐含的优化目标及其收敛条件，并据此启发更可靠的优化器设计。

Method: 以动力系统理论为分析框架：先刻画AdamW的隐式不动点目标（证明其不动点与某个带约束、带正则化优化问题的驻点重合）；再做稳定性分析，考察不动点对曲率、权重衰减、动量参数的敏感性，包括一维情形下的振荡现象；随后推广到高维，推导连续时间动力学的局部稳定性充分条件；最后设计一种保持原不动点的收敛性改进变体。

Result: AdamW的不动点虽对应某约束正则优化问题的驻点，但其中部分在该动力学下不稳定；稳定性对曲率、权重衰减、动量参数高度敏感；一维情形即可出现平衡点不稳定与持续振荡而非收敛；高维情形给出局部稳定的充分条件；所提出的修正算法保持不动点并具备收敛性。

Conclusion: 研究明确了AdamW对应的隐式优化问题、其收敛可被期望的条件，以及其动力学特性如何启发更可靠的优化器设计。

Abstract: AdamW is ubiquitous in deep learning, yet its behavior remains poorly understood. We analyze its dynamics through the lens of dynamical systems and show that AdamW admits an implicit fixed-point objective: its fixed points coincide with the stationary points of a constrained and regularized optimization problem. However, not all of these fixed points are stable under AdamW's dynamics, and stability depends sensitively on curvature, weight decay, and momentum parameters. Even in one-dimensional settings, equilibria may be unstable, and trajectories can exhibit persistent oscillations rather than convergence to them. We further extend the analysis to higher dimensions, deriving sufficient conditions for local stability of the continuous-time dynamics, and propose a convergent modification that preserves AdamW's fixed points. These results clarify what optimization problem AdamW is associated with, when its convergence can be expected, and how its dynamics could inspire more reliable optimizers.

</details>


### [305] [Data Manipulation Attacks Against Machine Learning Integrated Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11529)
*Soyon Choi,Scott Alfeld,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作进展论文研究由多个ML模型组合而成的信息物理系统(CPS)中的对抗攻击问题,提出将攻击者建模为影响状态机的攻击策略(状态到攻击的映射),并给出威胁模型与计算最优攻击策略的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有大量工作表明单个ML算法可被操纵,但真实系统中多个ML模型(视觉、规划等)组合完成自动决策。攻击者如何将"欺骗单个学习器"的能力组合起来攻击整个组合系统尚不清晰。

Method: 在CPS或其他基于状态机的AI组合系统上,将数据操纵型攻击者形式化为攻击策略(状态到攻击的映射),建立基本威胁模型,并提出计算最优攻击策略的框架与方法。

Result: 给出攻击策略形式化框架、基本威胁模型及最优攻击策略计算方法;论文为工作进展性质,尚属规划与初步结果。

Conclusion: 旨在引发关于组合学习安全性的讨论并促成新合作,后续将扩展方法。

Abstract: There is a well-established body of work demonstrating that individual machine learning (ML) algorithms can be manipulated or deceived. However, in many real-world systems, multiple ML algorithms are composed to enable automated decision-making, particularly in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS). Often such systems use a composition of vision, planning, and other ML models to affect their internal state and ultimate take actions in the physical world. While there is a huge literature on the (in)security of ML algorithms, it is not immediately clear how an attacker might leverage their ability to trick individual learners when attacking the composition as a whole. We develop methods for creating an attack policy (a mapping from state to attack) for a data-manipulating attacker affecting a CPS or other state-machine-based AI compositions. We describe our framework, a basic threat model and corresponding methods for computing an optimal attack policy, as well as planned extensions. The goal of this work-in-progress paper is to stimulate conversation and forge new collaborations regarding the security of compositional learning.

</details>


### [306] [Probing Transformer Representations Layer by Layer, Module by Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11531)
*Ambroise Odonnat,Theo Gnassounou,Laetitia Chapel,Romain Tavenard,Ievgen Redko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对视觉基础模型(VFM)与大型语言模型(LLM)逐层、逐模块进行超过144,000次线性探测，发现层与模块应联合选择，并提出简单固定策略：视觉用最后一层FFN激活(Act@100)、语言用60%深度处的第二个归一化层(LN2@60)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征探测研究多将每个transformer block视为原子单元、仅沿深度维度分析，且普遍采用最后一层残差流作为默认选择，但已有工作表明中间层可能更优。作者认为模块维度被忽视，因此希望联合考察"层×模块"的选择空间。

Method: 对VFM和LLM执行超过144,000次线性探测评估，逐层、逐模块（残差流、FFN激活、归一化层输出等）分解表征；比较"最佳层-模块对"与"固定残差流下的最佳层"的增益；据此验证两种简单固定策略：视觉的Act@100与语言的LN2@60。

Result: 最佳层-模块对的增益显著大于固定残差流下的最佳层；模块性能随层变化明显，说明有用表征无法仅由深度刻画。固定策略在多数评估中优于探测基线，分别恢复视觉42%、语言86%的最佳层-模块对增益；VFM的增益随预训练与下游数据间的迁移难度增大而提高。

Conclusion: 探测基础模型时应联合考虑层与模块，而非仅沿深度选择；简单的固定层-模块策略可作为有效的默认探测选择，且其收益与迁移难度正相关。

Abstract: Which representation should be probed in a transformer-based foundation model? Recent work shows that the standard practice of probing the final layer is often suboptimal, as intermediate layers can provide better features. Yet, representations are still largely studied along depth alone, treating each transformer block as an atomic unit. In this work, we analyze transformer representations layer by layer, module by module. Through more than 144,000 linear probing evaluations of vision foundation models (VFMs) and large language models (LLMs), we show that layer and module choices should be considered jointly: the best layer-module pair yields substantially larger gains over the final-layer residual stream than the best layer with the residual stream fixed. Moreover, module performance can vary substantially across layers, showing that useful representations cannot be characterized by depth alone. Building on these findings, we evaluate simple fixed probing strategies: the final-layer feedforward activation (Act@100) for vision and the second normalization layer at 60% depth (LN2@60) for language. These strategies improve over the probing baseline in a majority of evaluations and recover, on average, 42% and 86% of the gains from probing the best layer-module pair for vision and language, respectively. For VFMs, these gains increase with the transfer difficulty between pretraining and downstream data.

</details>


### [307] [UTOPI: Efficient Egocentric Long-Video Understanding in AR via User-Guided Token Pre-Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11562)
*Ziqi Wang,Su Chen,Qiance Tang,Jieyu Lin,Ziyun Li,Barbara De Salvo,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 UTOPI，一个面向 AR 设备自我中心长视频的即插即用预压缩模块，利用用户运动信号估计帧间重叠进行视角平移不变的剪枝，并借助眼动追踪识别注视点以优先保留前景内容，在多个基准和骨干网络上实现最高 95% 压缩且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解受到关注，AR 设备成为有吸引力的部署平台，但自我中心视频存在严重时间冗余，给资源受限的 AR 硬件带来显著内存与计算负担。

Method: UTOPI 作为预压缩模块：第一，利用用户运动信号估计帧间重叠，提出视角平移不变（viewpoint-shift-invariant）的剪枝算法；第二，利用眼动追踪传感器识别用户注视点，将受关注的前景内容与背景分离，并通过微调的神经网络调整机制优先保留前景。

Result: 在多个基准与模型骨干上，UTOPI 在保持下游性能的同时实现最高 95% 的压缩率。

Conclusion: 面向 AR 自我中心长视频，结合运动信号与眼动追踪的即插即用预压缩可有效缓解时间冗余带来的资源压力，兼具高压缩率与性能保持能力。

Abstract: As long-video understanding gains increasing attention, augmented reality (AR) devices are emerging as a compelling deployment platform. However, egocentric videos exhibit significant temporal redundancy, imposing substantial memory and computational burdens on resource-constrained AR hardware. To address this challenge, we introduce UTOPI, a plug-and-play pre-compression module designed specifically for egocentric long videos on AR platforms. First, we leverage user motion signals to estimate inter-frame overlap and propose a viewpoint-shift-invariant pruning algorithm. Second, we exploit eye-tracking sensors to identify user fixations, enabling the separation of attended foreground content from background and its preferential retention via a fine-tuned neural network-based adjustment mechanism. Across multiple benchmarks and model backbones, UTOPI achieves up to 95% compression while preserving performance. Code and project page are available at https://sai-lab-nyu.github.io/utopi/.

</details>


### [308] [Actions Should EARN Their Credit: Test-Time Adaptation and Route Consolidation for Frozen LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11565)
*Bian Sun,Kevin Zhai,Mubarak Shah,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EARN方法，利用历史经验对LLM智能体的动作进行条件化信用分配与动作重放，在固定交互预算下提升并保持最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可通过重复尝试的反馈改进决策而无需更新参数，但存在两个问题：正向动作信用会持续偏向那些反复取得相同分数的动作，即便有更高分可达；在固定交互预算下，末期探索新动作反而可能降低性能。

Method: EARN利用过往经验引导进一步进展，并通过条件化复用有效动作序列保持先前性能。具体地：降低那些在早期回合已反复达到的分数所对应的动作信用，对达到更高分数的动作片段赋予更大信用，这些信用用于调整LLM所提动作的概率；在最后若干回合，若当前上下文与记录上下文匹配且动作仍可用，则直接重放已存储的动作，跳过LLM候选生成。

Result: 以交互预算末段（最后四个回合的平均分数）为评价指标，在Jericho、ScienceWorld和Crafter上，EARN在33个任务—预算设置中的31个（含并列）取得所评估方法中的最佳成绩；在Jericho各预算下，最后四回合中动作重放平均跳过78.9%–86.3%的候选生成调用。

Conclusion: 结合差异化信用分配与条件化动作重放的EARN能有效避免重复低分行为的固化与末期探索的性能损失，在有限交互预算下显著提升并稳定最终表现，同时大幅减少推理调用开销。

Abstract: LLM agents can use feedback from repeated attempts to improve their decisions without updating model parameters. However, positive action credits can continue to favor actions that repeatedly achieve the same score, even when higher scores are achievable. Under a fixed interaction budget, trying new actions in the final attempts can also lower performance. We introduce EARN, a method that uses past experience to guide further progress and retain earlier performance through conditional reuse of effective action sequences. EARN reduces action credits for reaching scores repeatedly attained in earlier episodes and assigns larger credits to action segments that reach higher scores. These credits adjust the probabilities of LLM-proposed actions. During the final episodes, EARN directly replays stored actions when the current context matches the recorded context and the actions remain available, bypassing LLM candidate generation. We evaluate performance at the end of the interaction budget using the average score over the final four episodes. Across Jericho, ScienceWorld, and Crafter, EARN achieves the best score on this metric in 31 of 33 task--budget settings among the evaluated methods, including ties. Across the evaluated Jericho budgets, replay bypasses 78.9--86.3\% of candidate-generation calls in the final four episodes, averaged across games.

</details>


### [309] [Building heterogeneous multistep workflows in automated microscopy: reward design and knowledge transfer for autonomous structure property discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11609)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预算约束下的自主显微镜多步"结构-性质"工作流框架，控制器不仅决定在哪测，还决定测什么信息、用什么扫描速率、何时重标定，并在真实EELS数据上通过两组实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主显微镜要从"快速采集"转化为"科学进展"，需让控制器同时决策测量位置、信息类型、扫描速率与重标定时机；而现有工作缺少统一处理预算、动作成本、仪器观测模型与实验前知识迁移的框架。

Method: 构建预算约束的多步结构-性质工作流框架：融合显式科学奖励、动作成本、仪器观测模型、实验前知识向在线实验的迁移，以及独立于控制器的评估。迁移知识包括预测器精度与计算成本、加权模型奖励的经验学习成熟度函数、训练控制器前测得的漂移特性。基于真实电子能量损失谱（EELS）数据集开展两组互补实验：其一控制器在成像/谱学、图像尺寸、扫描速度间选择；其二存在仪器漂移时，控制器额外决定何时重标定。

Result: 两组实验表明：响应发现与结构-性质学习偏好不同的代理模型和采集策略；简单采集规则是强而实用的基线；独立误差分析揭示模型不确定性在何处可靠、何处不可靠；漂移实验中显式配准显著提升定位精度，并量化其对谱学通量的代价。

Conclusion: 该框架提供可复现的设计、比较与审计自主实验的方法，并明确部署所需显微镜能力：可独立执行的动作、已表征的扫描与探测器响应、带时间戳的配准遥测、完整动作记录。

Abstract: Autonomous microscopy can turn fast acquisition into scientific progress when it decides not only where to measure, but also what information to acquire, at what scan rate, and when to recalibrate the instrument. We develop a budget constrained framework for such multistep structure-property workflows. It combines explicit scientific rewards, action costs, instrument observation models, transfer of pre experiment knowledge into the online campaign, and evaluation kept independent of the controller. The transferred knowledge includes predictor accuracy and computational cost, empirical learner maturity functions that weight model-derived rewards, and drift characteristics established before controller training. Using a real electron energy-loss spectroscopy dataset, we run two complementary campaigns. In the first, the controller chooses between imaging and spectroscopy, image size, and scan speed. In the second, the instrument drifts and the controller also decides when to recalibrate. The campaigns show that response discovery and structure-property learning favor different surrogate models and acquisition strategies. They also show that simple acquisition rules form strong, practical baselines. Independent error analysis reveals where model uncertainty is and is not a reliable learning signal. In the drift campaign, explicit registration greatly improves positional accuracy, and the framework measures what this costs in spectroscopy throughput. Together, these results provide a reproducible way to design, compare, and audit autonomous experiments, and they define what microscopes must provide for deployment: independently executable actions, characterized scan and detector responses, timestamped registration telemetry, and complete action records.

</details>


### [310] [Daily Emergency Call Volume Forecasting using Univariate and Multivariate TimeGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11613)
*Feriel Fass,Djemel Ziou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估时间序列基础模型TimeGPT在每日急救呼叫量预测中的表现，发现其大规模预训练优势并未转化为更高的预测精度，传统精简模型（SARIMAX、PR、NBR）仍更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验预训练时间序列基础模型（TimeGPT）能否在急救呼叫量这一实际应急管理场景中超越基于本地数据训练的传统精简方法。

Method: 使用2101条真实每日急救呼叫观测数据，设置单变量与多变量（含时间、气象、人口、社会经济变量）两种情境，并分别进行一步超前与七天多步超前预测。采用按时间顺序的训练/验证/测试划分以避免信息泄漏，通过网格搜索与早停选择超参数；以多种误差指标评估，并与SARIMAX、泊松回归、负二项回归、XGBoost、MLP、LSTM对比。

Result: 单变量情境下SARIMAX整体表现最佳；多变量情境下负二项回归与泊松回归表现最佳。TimeGPT未取得最优精度。

Conclusion: TimeGPT的大规模预训练并不必然带来该任务的预测精度优势，仅依赖可用数据训练的“精简”方法在此应用中仍更具实用价值。

Abstract: This study investigates the potential of TimeGPT, a pretrained foundation model for timeseries, for forecasting daily emergency call volumes. Real-world emergency call data, comprising 2,101 daily observations, are used to evaluate both univariate and multivariate forecasting settings under one-step-ahead and seven-day multi-step-ahead scenarios. The multivariate forecasting incorporates temporal, meteorological, demographic, and socioeconomic variables. A chronological training, validation, and test split is adopted to prevent information leakage, while hyperparameters are selected using the validation set through grid search and early stopping when applicable. Forecasting performance is evaluated using multiple error metrics. Its forecasting performance is compared with several established forecasting approaches, including Seasonal Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average with Exogenous Regressors (SARIMAX), Poisson regression (PR), Negative Binomial regression (NBR), Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Multilayer Perceptron (MLP), and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM). The results show that SARIMAX achieves the best overall performance in the univariate setting. In the multivariate setting, NBR and PR provide best performance. These findings indicate that the large-scale pretraining of TimeGPT do not necessarily translate into superior forecasting accuracy for emergency call volumes. Frugal methods trained solely on the available data remain better for this application.

</details>


### [311] [GenISys: Integrated Ship Systems Design Using Subsystem-Conditional Tabular Diffussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11622)
*Noah J. Bagazinski,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GenISys框架与同时条件去噪扩散概率模型（SCDDPM），用于早期散货船集成系统生成；在5000海里航程、低成本目标下，55.70%生成请求满足工程约束并达到低成本目标，优于整体式与串行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶设计需协调船体几何、结构、推进与运营等耦合子系统，在满足工程约束的同时平衡性能与成本；已有生成式AI多用于部件级设计，难以处理子系统间相互作用，因此需要面向集成系统的生成方法。

Method: 构建含217,999个候选设计（其中16,247个满足所建工程约束）的数据集；提出同时条件去噪扩散概率模型（SCDDPM），在生成过程中让各子系统模型相互响应、联合生成；与整体式整船生成、串行子系统生成在每种架构5次独立训练运行下对比。

Result: 在5000海里航程、降低单位货物运输成本的研究中，SCDDPM有55.70%的生成请求得到满足约束且达到统一低成本目标的设计，整体式模型为51.10%，串行模型为47.54%，分别高出9.0%与17.2%，且估计运输成本相近。

Conclusion: 结果表明生成式AI可协调多子系统，生成可行且高性能的集成系统；同时条件联合生成优于整体式与串行式策略，为早期船舶集成设计提供有效途径。

Abstract: Ship design requires coordinating coupled subsystems to satisfy engineering constraints and balance competing performance and cost objectives. Generative artificial intelligence has supported component design, but integrated ship generation must also account for interactions among hull geometry, structure, propulsion, and operation. This paper presents GenISys, a framework for generating integrated systems, demonstrated through early-stage bulk-carrier design. Its dataset contains 217,999 proposed designs, including 16,247 that satisfy the formulated engineering constraints. The proposed simultaneous conditional denoising diffusion probabilistic model (SCDDPM) generates subsystems together, allowing each subsystem model to respond to changes elsewhere during generation. Experiments compare this approach with monolithic whole-ship generation and sequential subsystem generation across five independent training runs per architecture. In a study targeting lower cost per tonne of cargo transported, designs were generated for a required voyage range of 5,000 nautical miles. SCDDPM produced designs satisfying the formulated engineering constraints and meeting a common low-cost target in 55.70% of generation requests, compared with 51.10% for the monolithic model and 47.54% for the sequential model. These correspond to 9.0% and 17.2% more qualifying designs, respectively, while maintaining similar estimated transport costs. These results show how generative artificial intelligence can coordinate subsystems to generate feasible, high-performing integrated systems.

</details>


### [312] [A Trust Region Approach for Learning Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11649)
*Takeshi Koshizuka,Denis Blessing,Max Zimmer,Sebastian Pokutta,Lorenz Richter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种信赖域版本的IPF算法，约束相邻迭代的KL散度固定，用于仅已知未归一化目标密度时的Schrödinger Bridge数值近似，并证明其等价于自适应步长的镜像下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在只有未归一化目标密度、无法获得目标分布样本的设定下，常用IPF及其变体因底层控制问题病态、最优解远离初始化而出现严重数值不稳定。

Method: 构造信赖域式IPF：每次更新时约束新迭代与当前迭代保持固定KL散度，从而限制更新幅度；并给出其作为自适应步长镜像下降的理论解释。

Result: 该约束显著提升数值稳定性，并在多个具有挑战性的采样任务上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 固定KL散度的信赖域IPF为未归一化密度下的Schrödinger Bridge计算提供了稳定且具优化视角的实用方案。

Abstract: We study the numerical approximation of Schrödinger Bridges in the setting where samples from the target distribution are unavailable and only an unnormalized target density is given. In this regime, the widely used iterative proportional fitting (IPF) procedure (and its variants) often exhibits severe numerical instabilities, stemming from ill-conditioned underlying control problems and from the fact that the optimal solution may lie far from the initialization. To address these issues, we propose a trust region variant of IPF that constrains successive updates to remain at a fixed Kullback-Leibler divergence from the current iterate. This restriction significantly improves numerical stability. Moreover, we show that the resulting method admits an interpretation as a mirror descent scheme with an adaptively chosen step size. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on several challenging sampling tasks.

</details>


### [313] [Means vs. Foundation Models for Emergency Call Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11656)
*Feriel Fass,Djemel Ziou,Marie-Flavie Auclair-Fortier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较广义均值（Hölder均值HM与Lehmer均值LM）与微调TimeGPT在1–7天日度急救呼叫量预测上的表现，发现二者在短期（1、3天）MAE略优于TimeGPT，长期（7天）TimeGPT略优，但Diebold-Mariano检验显示各预测步长下均无统计显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需大规模参数的简单广义均值方法能否在日度急救呼叫量预测中达到与TimeGPT等基础模型相当的预测性能，从而为资源受限场景提供轻量替代方案。

Method: 构建Hölder均值（HM）与Lehmer均值（LM）两类广义均值预测器，与微调后的TimeGPT在1至7天预测步长上对比；在真实急救呼叫数据集上使用多种误差指标评估，并采用Diebold-Mariano检验判断差异的统计显著性。

Result: HM与LM在各步长上预测性能几乎一致；短期上二者优于TimeGPT，1天与3天MAE分别约降低1.24%与2.48%；7天步长时TimeGPT误差更低，MAE约降低1.98%；但DM检验表明各步长下方法间差异均不具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 广义均值可提供与高能耗基础模型相竞争的预测性能，说明在短期急救呼叫量预测中简单统计方法足以匹敌复杂基础模型；但差异不显著，结论需在更多数据集与步长上进一步验证。

Abstract: This study investigates whether generalized means can achieve forecasting performance comparable to that of TimeGPT for daily emergency call volume prediction. Specifically, two families of generalized means, the Hölder means (HM) and the Lehmer means (LM), are evaluated against fine-tuned TimeGPT across forecasting horizons ranging from one to seven days. Forecasting performance is assessed on a real-world emergency call dataset using multiple error metrics, and Diebold-Mariano test. Experimental results show that HM and LM achieve nearly identical predictive performance across all forecasting horizons. For short-term forecasting, both generalized means outperform TimeGPT, with reductions of mean absolute error (MAE) approximately 1.24\% and 2.48\% at horizons of one and three days, respectively. Conversely, TimeGPT achieves a lower error at the seven-day horizon, with an descresing of MAE by 1.98\%. However, Diebold-Mariano tests reveal no statistically significant differences between the methods at the evaluated horizons. These findings suggest that generalized means can provide competitive forecasting performance compared with a energyvore foundation model.

</details>


### [314] [A Theoretical Reduction From Membership Inference to Reconstruction Attacks Against Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11680)
*Amnon Levi,Yuval Dagan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立了成员推断到重构攻击的一般归约，并证明任何在随机凸优化中达到低超额风险的学习算法都存在多项式时间重构攻击，能恢复 Ω(n) 个训练点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重构攻击可从训练好的模型中恢复训练数据，但此前缺乏与成员推断之间的一般性理论联系，尤其不清楚在随机凸优化（SCO）等标准学习设定中重构攻击的可行性边界。

Method: 提出对抗模型：对手构造一个难实例（来自参数化数据分布族）并获得生成该实例所用的参数。核心是一个适用于任意学习算法的一般归约：若存在召回率至少 q(n)、假阳性率 O(δ²/n²) 的成员推断测试，则可构造重构算法。对 SCO，通过将支撑集划分为多项式多个类别、每类含指数多元素的"结构化分布"，使重构按类别而非全支撑迭代，从而在指数大域上实现计算高效；再结合已有的 SCO 成员推断结果。

Result: 归约保证：重构以 1−O(δ) 的概率只输出训练点，并在期望上恢复 Ω(n·q(n)) 个训练点。对 SCO，任何在所有分布上实现低超额风险的学习算法，都允许一个多项式时间重构攻击恢复 Ω(n) 个训练点，即使底层分布支撑集指数级大。

Conclusion: 成员推断在理论上蕴含重构攻击，这一等价关系可推广到 SCO 等广泛实用场景；低超额风险与计算高效的重构攻击不可兼得，对隐私保护机器学习具有重要的不可能性含义。

Abstract: We study reconstruction attacks, which recover training data from a learned model. In our adversarial model, the adversary designs a hard instance, drawn from a parameterized family of data distributions, and is given the parameter used to generate it. Our main contribution is a general reduction showing that, in this model, membership inference (determining whether a given datapoint was used for training) implies reconstruction: for a training set of size $n$, a membership test with recall at least $q(n)$ and false-positive rate $O(δ^2/n^2)$ yields a reconstruction algorithm that, with probability $1 - O(δ)$, outputs only training points, and recovers $Ω(n \cdot q(n))$ of them in expectation. This reduction applies to any learning algorithm. For stochastic convex optimization (SCO), we make the reduction computationally efficient over exponentially large domains via structured distributions whose support is partitioned into polynomially many categories of exponentially many elements each, so that reconstruction iterates over categories rather than the full support. Combining this with an existing membership inference result for SCO, we prove that any learning algorithm achieving low excess risk over all distributions admits a polynomial-time reconstruction attack recovering $Ω(n)$ training points, even when the underlying distribution has exponentially large support.

</details>


### [315] [Resolvent Ellipsoid for Minty Set Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11691)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Gabriele Farina,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究紧凸集上带集值算子的 Minty 集包含问题，提出“分离原则”：固定尺度约束预解（resolvent）预言机的每次响应要么给出 ε-SVI 证书，要么给出 Minty 集的严格分离法向量；结合椭球法得到 RESOLVENT-ELLIPSOID 算法，以 poly(d, log(1/ε)) 次黑箱调用求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Minty 集包含（单调包含）在固定尺度 resolvent 黑箱模型下缺乏多项式（关于 d 与 log(1/ε)）的黑箱复杂度保证；已有方法多依赖一阶预言机或强假设，本文希望在该更弱的预解预言机模型下建立几何化的复杂度理论。

Method: 采用几何视角，在固定尺度约束预解预言机模型中工作：在锚点 a 处查询返回预解点 x⁺ 与 Yosida 向量 g_η(a)=(a−x⁺)/η。证明每次预言机响应构成尖锐二分：要么返回点给出 ε-SVI 证书，要么 Yosida 向量定义 Minty 集的严格分离法向。将此分离原理与椭球法结合，设计 RESOLVENT-ELLIPSOID 算法，迭代地产生有效几何割以收缩可行域。

Result: 证明每次预解查询即可获得“证书或割平面”的二选一结论；RESOLVENT-ELLIPSOID 在 O(poly(d, log(1/ε))) 次黑箱预言机调用内计算 ε-SVI。对单调包含，据作者所知这是固定尺度 resolvent 模型下首个 poly(d, log(1/ε)) 黑箱保证。

Conclusion: 预解预言机的单次查询天然携带分离几何信息，使椭球法可直接作用于 Minty 集包含，从而在固定尺度 resolvent 模型下获得首个关于维度与精度的多项式黑箱复杂度结果；该分离原则为预解型黑箱算法提供了统一的几何分析框架。

Abstract: We study Minty set inclusion on a compact convex set $\mathcal{X} \subseteq \mathbb{R}^d$ with a set-valued operator $A : \mathcal{X} \rightrightarrows \mathbb{R}^d$. Under the Minty promise, the goal is to compute $x \in \mathcal{X}$ and $u \in A(x)$ such that $\sup_{y \in \mathcal{X}} \langle u, x - y \rangle \le \varepsilon$. Our approach is geometric and we work in the fixed-scale constrained-resolvent oracle model, where a query at anchor $a$ is assumed to return a resolvent point $x^+$ together with the Yosida vector $g_η(a) = (a - x^+)/η$. We prove that each oracle response yields a sharp dichotomy: either the returned point provides an $\varepsilon$-SVI certificate, or the Yosida vector defines a strict separating normal for the Minty set. Thus, a single black-box resolvent query supplies either a certified candidate solution or a valid geometric cut. Combining this separation principle with the ellipsoid method, we obtain RESOLVENT-ELLIPSOID, an algorithm that computes an $\varepsilon$-SVI in $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\operatorname{poly}(d, \log(1/\varepsilon))\right)$ black-box oracle calls to the fixed-scale constrained resolvent. For monotone inclusions, our algorithm gives, to our knowledge, the first $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\mathrm{poly}(d, \log(1/\varepsilon))\right)$ black-box guarantee in the fixed-scale resolvent model.

</details>


### [316] [Direct GPU Energy Measurements of Batch-Level Sharing in Mixture-of-Experts and Recurrent Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11696)
*Rafael Pina,Varuna De Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过直接GPU能耗测量，检验批内行能否跨batch与跨时间共享计算以获得实测节能。结论：循环策略上暂不可行，MoE语言模型上可行且粒度决定收益，并提出的persistence-aware OEA以更少新增专家实现批量感知裁剪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效神经网络的能耗论断多由操作计数（FLOPs等）间接推断，缺乏实测依据；作者希望直接测量GPU能耗，并统一检验一类问题：批内各行能否共享计算并带来实测节能。

Method: 选取两个批处理特性迥异的工作负载进行直接GPU能耗测量：（1）GPU向量化多智能体强化学习中的循环策略；（2）MoE语言模型。对二者均检验跨batch与跨时间的计算共享可能性，包括共享激活模式、冻结策略掩码、训练所得掩码与测试时steering；并在Qwen3-30B-A3B上实现batch-aware routing（KL约束下减少专家权重读取），另提出单参数的persistence-aware OEA规则控制每步新增专家数。

Result: 循环策略：共享激活模式存在但极少持续一步以上，冻结策略掩码失效，训练所得掩码仅因等价于窄GRU而奏效，能耗为小型稠密策略的11倍；测试时steering在两个负载上均无额外收益。MoE：Qwen3-30B-A3B上batch-aware routing在KL 0.05内少读25–31%专家权重，500条基准上变化在噪声范围内，融合前向下每token节能12–18%。persistence-aware OEA在batch 32–64时较原生路由每步新增专家少约40%，但能耗未测。

Conclusion: 批内计算共享的节能效果高度依赖负载形态：循环策略不适用，MoE适用且粒度是关键；实操能耗节约为12–18%。结果为单一GPU类型的初步（pilot）测量，作者明确陈述其局限，persistence-aware OEA的能耗仍待测量。

Abstract: Energy claims for efficient neural networks are often inferred from operation counts. We report direct GPU energy measurements on two workloads that batch very differently, recurrent policies in GPU-vectorised multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) and mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models, and ask one question of both: can the rows of a batch be made to share computation, across the batch and over time, at a measured saving? On recurrent policies the answer so far is no. Shared activity patterns exist but rarely last one step, masking a frozen policy fails, and trained-in masks succeed only because a fixed mask is equivalent to a narrow GRU, at 11x the energy of a small dense policy in our implementation. Test-time steering adds nothing beyond re-picking on either workload. On MoE models the answer is yes, and granularity decides how much: on Qwen3-30B-A3B, batch-aware routing reads 25-31% fewer expert weights within KL 0.05, with benchmark changes within noise at 500 items, and uses 12-18% less energy per token on a fused forward. We also introduce persistence-aware OEA, a one-parameter rule that keeps the batch-aware cut with about 40% fewer new experts per step than native routing at batch 32-64, though its energy has not yet been measured. These are pilot measurements on one GPU type, and we state their limits.

</details>


### [317] [Scalable Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11704)
*Peiyu Li,Pouya Hosseinzadeh,Soukaina Filali Boubrahimi,Shah Muhammad Hamdi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种策略学习框架，用于时间序列反事实解释，将其建模为单步上下文赌博机问题，在预训练自编码器潜空间中训练actor-critic智能体，实现单次前向传播直接生成反事实样本，避免逐实例优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列反事实解释面临复杂时间依赖与逐实例优化计算低效两大挑战，现有方法需对每个新输入进行迭代优化，难以扩展。

Method: 将反事实生成建模为单步上下文赌博机（contextual bandit）策略学习问题；在预训练自编码器的潜空间中训练actor-critic智能体，学习捕获全局结构规律的可迁移策略；推理时通过单次前向传播直接生成反事实，无需逐实例优化。

Result: 在13个UCR基准数据集上，框架在多个异常检测指标下均取得高有效性（validity）与强合理性（plausibility）。

Conclusion: 通过将反事实生成与逐实例优化解耦，该方法为时间序列反事实解释提供了高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Generating counterfactual (CF) explanations for time series is challenging due to complex temporal dependencies and the computational inefficiency of traditional instance-specific optimization. We propose a policy learning framework for counterfactual explanation in time series, trained as a one-step contextual-bandit policy-learning problem. By training an actor-critic agent in the latent space of a pre-trained autoencoder, our method learns a transferable policy that captures global structural regularities. Unlike existing methods that rely on iterative optimization for each new input, the learned policy enables direct generation of valid and plausible counterfactuals through a single forward pass, avoiding per-instance optimization at inference time. Experiments on 13 UCR benchmark datasets show that our framework achieves high validity and strong plausibility across multiple anomaly detection metrics. Overall, by decoupling counterfactual generation from per-instance optimization, the proposed method provides an efficient and scalable alternative to existing approaches for time series counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [318] [Variational Approach to Optimal IPS Estimator for Multi-logger Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11712)
*Joon Suk Huh,Junghoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多日志策略情境下的上下文赌博机离线策略评估（OPE）。作者通过变分法推导出在无偏约束下使方差最小的样本相关权重，得到闭式最优 IPS 估计量，并进一步给出双重稳健（DR）扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多日志策略下，平衡 IPS 与加权 IPS 均无法稳定优于对方（Agarwal et al., 2017），现有方法存在效率不确定性的困境，需要一个在无偏前提下达到最优方差的多日志 IPS 估计量。

Method: 将问题表述为：在无偏性约束下最小化方差，用变分法求解样本相关权重的闭式解，得到广义加权 IPS 类内的渐近最优估计量；该估计量无需奖励模型，仅需 K×K 矩阵与 K 维矩向量（K 为日志策略数）。再引入条件奖励估计量构造 DR 形式，并与 Kallus et al. (2021) 的半参数有效 DR 估计量进行理论与实验比较。

Result: 所提 IPS 估计量无偏且在广义加权 IPS 类中渐近方差最优，并量化了相对半参数 oracle 的效率损失；基准数据集实验显示其总体优于现有多日志 IPS 方法。DR 扩展在奖励模型一致时与 Kallus 等人的估计量具有相同渐近方差，且在某些奖励误设情形下仍保持半参数有效性；实验中通常取得更低的估计误差。

Conclusion: 样本相关最优权重可化解多日志 OPE 中平衡 IPS 与加权 IPS 的取舍困境；所提估计量仅依赖低维矩信息即可逼近半参数效率，其 DR 扩展在理论与实证上均具竞争力，是现有多日志 OPE 方法的有效替代。

Abstract: We study off-policy evaluation (OPE) in contextual bandits with data collected from multiple logging policies. Inverse propensity scoring (IPS) is a standard approach to OPE and extends naturally to the multi-logger setting. However, as highlighted by Agarwal et al. [2017], neither balanced nor weighted IPS consistently outperforms the other in this setting. We resolve this dilemma by deriving an optimal IPS estimator with sample-dependent weights that minimize variance subject to unbiasedness. Using a variational calculus approach, we obtain closed-form optimal weights, yielding an estimator that is unbiased and achieves asymptotically optimal variance within a generalized weighted-IPS estimator class. Instead of a reward model, the estimator needs only a $K\times K$ matrix and a $K$-vector of moments, where $K$ is the number of logging policies, and we quantify how much efficiency this costs relative to the semiparametric oracle. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that our estimator generally improves on existing multi-logger IPS methods. We also extend our estimator to a doubly robust form by incorporating a conditional reward estimator. We compare the resulting DR extension with the semiparametrically efficient DR estimator of Kallus et al. [2021], both theoretically and empirically. We show that the two estimators achieve the same asymptotic variance when the conditional reward estimator is consistent, while our estimator can retain semiparametric efficiency under certain forms of reward misspecification. Empirically, the proposed DR estimator often achieves lower estimation error than the existing DR baseline.

</details>


### [319] [Autonomous Continual Learning and Knowledge Transfer via Cortex-Inspired Neural Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11717)
*Kevin L. McKee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Functional Task Networks（FTN），一种受哺乳动物新皮层特性启发的持续学习方法，用稀疏二值路由掩码在推理时基于小样本支持集配置，形成重叠子网络专家库以缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络存在灾难性遗忘：在新功能（概念漂移）或当前功能的非重叠域（域漂移）上重新训练时会完全覆盖已有解，且要求每批数据大致代表完整分布，代价高且不实用。作者希望借鉴新皮层机制实现持续学习与知识迁移。

Method: FTN被建模为分布式随机专家混合体：推理时利用少量输入-输出对（支持集）计算稀疏二值路由掩码。训练期间对路由器进行逐批次重配置，并结合"掩码重叠过滤的回放"（mask-overlap-filtered replay），从而塑造出由重叠、部分训练的子网络构成的基。无关任务经过基本分离的组装体，相关任务在共享组件上重叠。

Result: 在块序列式训练的持续学习与知识迁移任务上，FTN的解保持能力接近不相交掩码隔离法，并在同架构对比方法中取得最低的更新前"领先损失"（head-start loss）。学习到的掩码重叠几何结构能追踪已知的概念共享结构。实验显示：在相关概念漂移下，即使仅为保持也需要支持集掩码重配置；在域漂移下，仅靠分布式库加回放即可实现保持，无需配置器。前向迁移（领先优势）与后向迁移也由支持集掩码配置所贡献。

Conclusion: FTN通过稀疏二值掩码路由与重叠子网络专家库，在无需求每批数据代表完整分布的前提下实现了有效的持续学习与知识迁移；掩码重叠几何可反映概念共享结构，且掩码重配置对概念漂移下的保持及前/后向迁移至关重要，而域漂移下的保持主要依赖于分布式库与回放机制。

Abstract: Unlike the brain, artificial neural networks (ANNs) suffer from "catastrophic forgetting": complete overwriting of solutions when re-training on new functions ("concept shift") or on a disjoint domain of the current function ("domain shift"). ANNs require each batch of data to be roughly representative of the complete distribution, a typically expensive and impractical requirement. In this paper, we introduce Functional Task Networks (FTNs), a method of continual learning and knowledge transfer modeled after several properties of the mammalian neocortex. FTN is formulated as a distributed, stochastic mixture of experts in which a sparse, binary routing mask is computed at inference time using a small support sample of input-output pairs. Per-batch reconfiguration of the router during training, together with mask-overlap-filtered replay, shapes a basis of overlapping, partially trained subnetworks. Unrelated tasks route through mostly separate assemblies, related tasks overlap on shared components. On block-sequentially trained continual learning and knowledge transfer tasks FTN retains solutions nearly as well as disjoint-mask isolation and attains the lowest pre-update head-start loss among matched-architecture comparators. The learned mask-overlap geometry tracks the known concept-sharing structure where available. We show experimentally that under correlated concept shift, support-set mask reconfiguration is necessary even for retention. Under domain shift, the distributed bank with replay is sufficient to handle retention without the configurer. Forward transfer, or head-start, and backward transfer, are also contributed by support-set mask configuration.

</details>


### [320] [The Dark Matter of Clinical Data: An Identification Limit on Disentangling Physiology from Care Process in electronic health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11726)
*Mohammad Shahin,Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn,Dimitrios Proios,David Restrepo,Vishala Mishra,Boya Zhang,Aadarsh Sivaraman,Sparsh Roy,Benet Fité Abril,Tina Yen-Ting Chen,Hyunjung Gloria Kwak,Andre Kurepa Waschka,Vinayak Mathur,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EHR表示将患者生理状态与决定测量对象的临床过程纠缠在一起；作者形式化数据生成过程，证明"值/开单"通道的信息划分不可识别、跨站点不变性不构成识别策略，并给出恢复识别的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从EHR学到的表示同时编码了患者生理与"测什么、何时测、测谁"的临床决策过程。既往研究试图通过拆分实验室"数值"与"开单"通道的信息，或通过检验表示-结果关系的跨站点不变性来"消除"这种纠缠，但这两条路线的可识别性缺乏理论分析。

Method: 构建含潜在生理、开单意图、仅在被测时存在的实验室值、学习到的表示和临床环境的形式化数据生成过程；分析单数据集下"值/开单"通道的偏信息分解（PID），并构造观测等价模型检验跨站点不变性假设的可证伪性与可识别性。

Result: (1) 单数据集下值/开单通道的信息划分不可识别：当开单依赖分析物时，未测患者的分布不可恢复；用哨兵码表示未定义记录值时，该码携带开单指示变量的全部熵，从而对所有满足Williams-Beer公理且对单射重编码不变的冗余度量固定PID。(2) 表示条件结果的跨站点不变性是"生物不变性+排除性+表示充分性"的联合可证伪推论，而非识别策略——可构造观测等价模型，无论不变性检验通过与否都对生物不变性给出相反结论；二值生理下，当病例构成不同、表示有损且生理影响结果时必然出现该歧义；算例显示生物机制不变时仍存在9.6个百分点的跨站点差距。(3) 对每位患者都做检测可移除开单门控；被测检测可恢复识别：当某项检测精确汇总结果相关生理，或在病例构成不同的站点具备两项误差独立的检测时（二值生理），生物不变性可识别。

Conclusion: EHR表示中的生理-观测过程纠缠在单数据集和跨站点设计下都不能靠信息分解或不变性检验消除；跨站点分析应被报告为对某一明确联合假设的检验，而非识别策略。

Abstract: Representations learned from electronic health record (EHR) data encode patient physiology together with the clinical process that decides what is measured, when, and for whom. We formalize the data-generating process with latent physiology, ordering intent, laboratory values that exist only when measured, a learned representation, and clinical environments, and analyze two strategies for undoing this entanglement. First, no division of the information carried by laboratory "value" and "ordering" channels is identified from a single dataset: when ordering depends on the analyte, its distribution among untested patients is not recoverable, and a sentinel code for the undefined recorded value makes it carry all the entropy of the ordering indicator, fixing partial information decomposition (PID) for every redundancy measure satisfying the Williams-Beer axioms and invariant under injective re-encoding. Second, cross-site invariance of representation-conditional outcomes is a falsifiable implication of biological invariance taken jointly with exclusion and representation-sufficiency conditions, but not an identification strategy: we construct observationally equivalent models that disagree about biological invariance, whether the invariance test fails or passes. For binary physiology this happens exactly when case mix differs, the representation is lossy, and physiology affects the outcome. A worked example yields a 9.6-percentage-point cross-site gap under an unchanged biological mechanism. Tests performed for every patient remove the ordering gate, and measured tests can restore identification: biological invariance is identified when a test exactly summarizes outcome-relevant physiology, or, for binary physiology, when two tests with independent errors are available at sites with different case mix. Cross-site analyses should be reported as tests of a stated joint hypothesis.

</details>


### [321] [Spectral Rank and Step-Direction Dynamics in Language-Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11752)
*Khurram Khalil,Khaza Anuarul Hoque*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文调和了"更新近乎满秩"与"稳定秩变化小"两种看似矛盾的谱观测，证明在幂律奇异值谱下稳定秩相对固定能量秩随维度增长可忽略，并通过OLMo-2-7B的完整SVD、九个RLVR阶段131对相邻检查点的负余弦，以及OLMo-2-1B受控实验，区分了谱集中、能量覆盖与轨迹方向三个概念，并表明优势函数系统会影响方向对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于语言模型后训练更新谱性质的研究结论存在表面冲突：一方面报告更新近似满秩，另一方面报告稳定秩（stable rank）变化很小。作者希望说明这两者实际上相容，并进一步刻画训练过程中更新方向（方向对齐/轨迹方向）的性质，而非仅停留在奇异值谱层面。

Method: 1) 理论分析：假设奇异值满足 σ_i ∝ i^{-α}（0<α≤1/2），证明随维度增大稳定秩相对于固定能量秩可忽略；2) 实证SVD：对选定的OLMo-2-7B更新做完整奇异值分解，量化稳定秩与捕获80%能量所需奇异分量数的差异；3) 轨迹方向分析：用全模型内积计算连续检查点位移的余弦相似度，覆盖九个公开RLVR阶段的131对相邻检查点（含PPO、GRPO），并做四个模型家族的SFT对比；4) 受控实验：OLMo-2-1B、三组配对种子、2048 token响应上限，比较组相对优势与带GAE的学习评论家。

Result: 三个PPO注意力更新的稳定秩仅9.6–18.3，但需532–1,222个奇异分量才能捕获80%能量，验证了理论预测的分离。九个RLVR阶段全部131对相邻检查点在原生检查点网格上余弦均为负（含PPO与GRPO轨迹）。SFT在四个模型家族上呈配置依赖的对齐，Mistral与FP32 Llama出现训练后期负余弦。OLMo-2-1B受控实验在匹配60-rollout窗口下，训练后期平均余弦分别为+0.259（组相对）与+0.012（评论家+GAE），配对差值-0.247（种子级95%区间[-0.358,-0.137]）。

Conclusion: 论文区分了三个易混淆的概念——谱集中（稳定秩）、能量覆盖（满秩性）、轨迹方向（位移余弦），说明低稳定秩与近满秩更新可以共存。同时表明优势函数系统（组相对优势 vs. 学习评论家+GAE）在匹配训练条件下会显著影响更新的方向对齐，为理解后训练更新几何提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Language-model post-training modifies weights through cumulative updates and successive displacements, yet existing studies report seemingly conflicting spectral properties: nearly full-rank updates and low stable-rank changes. We show these observations are compatible and characterize update directions during training. For singular values following $σ_i \propto i^{-α}$ with $0 < α\leq 1/2$, we prove that stable rank becomes negligible relative to fixed-energy rank as dimension increases. Full singular value decompositions of selected OLMo-2-7B updates confirm this separation: three proximal policy optimization (PPO) attention updates have stable ranks of 9.6-18.3 but require 532-1,222 singular components to capture 80% of their energy. We next compute consecutive checkpoint-displacement cosines from full-model inner products. Across nine publicly released reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) stages, all 131 adjacent pairs have negative cosines on their native checkpoint grids, including PPO and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) trajectories. Supervised fine-tuning comparisons across four model families show configuration-dependent alignment, including negative late-training cosines for Mistral and FP32 Llama. Finally, a controlled OLMo-2-1B study with three paired seeds and a 2,048-token response cap compares group-relative advantages against a learned critic with generalized advantage estimation (GAE). At matched 60-rollout windows, late-training mean cosines are +0.259 and +0.012, respectively; the paired critic-minus-group difference is -0.247 (seed-level 95% interval [-0.358, -0.137]). These findings distinguish spectral concentration, energy coverage, and trajectory direction, and demonstrate that the advantage system influences directional alignment under matched training conditions.

</details>


### [322] [Payoff-Aware Prediction of Population Game Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11786)
*Kang Wang,Xiao Wang,Bo Li,Renzhe Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PA-RmD，一种在收益函数（payoff）改变时可预测种群博弈动力学轨迹的收益感知学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用中收益函数会变化，希望在缺乏新收益下轨迹数据时，预先预测由新收益设计诱导的种群演化轨迹。标准动力学学习只拟合训练收益下的运动，未将收益相关的激励与收益无关的响应结构分离，因而无法外推到新收益。

Method: 基于黎曼博弈动力学（Riemannian game dynamics）框架，提出 PA-RmD：将收益信息显式保留，并从数据中只学习与收益无关的响应结构（response structure），从而在新收益设计下直接组合生成预测。

Result: 理论上证明所学模型保持固定收益下的 Nash 均衡，并在"共享响应"与"收益覆盖"条件下给出有限时间单步预测误差界；实验在合成零和博弈与势博弈、以及 YouTube Trending 多国数据集上验证，在收益设计变化情形下表现良好。

Conclusion: 将收益依赖的激励与收益无关的响应结构显式分离，是实现新收益设计下种群博弈动力学可预测性的有效途径，PA-RmD 兼具理论保证与实证效果。

Abstract: Population game dynamics describe how aggregate behavior evolves in response to payoff signals. In many applications, the payoff function may change, and the goal is to predict the population trajectory induced by a new payoff design before trajectory data under that design are available. We study this problem as $\textbf{payoff-aware prediction of population game dynamics}$. Standard dynamics learning methods fit the motion observed under training payoffs, but do not separate the payoff-dependent incentive from the payoff-independent response structure needed for prediction under new payoffs. Building on the existing framework of Riemannian game dynamics, we propose PA-RmD, a payoff-aware learning method that keeps payoff information explicit and learns the payoff-independent response structure from data. Theoretically, we show that the learned model preserves fixed-payoff Nash equilibria and derive finite-time one-step prediction bounds under shared-response and payoff-coverage conditions. Empirically, we evaluate PA-RmD on synthetic zero-sum and potential games, as well as a YouTube Trending multi-country dataset, showing that our method performs well under changing payoff designs.

</details>


### [323] [APO2CS: Advantage-Aware Preference Optimization for Two-Level Constructive Search in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11792)
*Kaixi Yang,Linbo Qiao,Wei Shang,Yanqi Shi,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APO2CS，一种面向构造式神经求解器的偏好优化目标，在宏观层面用目标值差引入优势项、在微观层面比较部分轨迹，实现两级监督，提升偏好学习对组合优化问题的表达力与搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向神经组合优化的偏好优化方法依赖二值成对标签，一方面丢弃了解质量差异的幅度信息，另一方面仅在完整解层面提供监督，监督信号稀疏、表达力受限。

Method: 提出优势感知的两级构造式搜索偏好优化（APO2CS）：宏观层面在成对偏好中引入由目标值差导出的优势项，使偏好携带质量差异幅度；微观层面对比部分轨迹（partial trajectories），在序列构造过程中提供更密集的监督。该目标既可从零训练策略，也可微调预训练策略。

Result: 实验表明APO2CS提升了偏好学习在组合优化中的表达力与搜索能力，并在多种组合优化任务上展现出较强的有效性与适应性。

Conclusion: 通过融合宏观优势信息与微观轨迹级监督，APO2CS克服了二值成对偏好的信息损失与监督稀疏问题，是一种通用、可训练可微调的两级偏好优化框架。

Abstract: Preference optimization has emerged as a promising alternative to reward-based training for neural combinatorial optimization. However, existing methods typically rely on binary pairwise labels, which discard the magnitude of solution-quality differences and provide supervision only at the complete-solution level. We propose Advantage-aware Preference Optimization for Two-level Constructive Search (APO2CS), a preference-based training objective for constructive neural solvers that integrates both macro-level and micro-level supervision. At the macro level, APO2CS enriches pairwise preferences with an advantage term derived from objective-value differences. At the micro level, it compares partial trajectories, thereby providing denser supervision throughout the sequential construction process. The resulting objective can be used either to train a policy from scratch or to fine-tune a pretrained one. Experimental results show that APO2CS improves the expressiveness and search capability of preference learning for combinatorial optimization, while demonstrating strong effectiveness and adaptability across multiple combinatorial optimization tasks.

</details>


### [324] [Efficient Online Submodular Maximization with Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11808)
*Iasonas Nikolaou,Evimaria Terzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基数约束下带bandit反馈的在线单调子模最大化问题，提出首个对任意单调子模函数具有 $\sqrt{T}$ 依赖的多项式时间算法，期望 $(1-1/e)$-regret 为 $O(k\sqrt{nT\log(n/k)})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线子模最大化在bandit反馈（仅观测所选集合的值，无逐元素边际收益）下困难：此前缺乏对任意单调子模函数同时具备多项式时间与 $\sqrt{T}$ 型horizon依赖的算法，需要新的降维与反馈利用方式。

Method: 每轮从 $n$ 元素基集中选至多 $k$ 个元素，仅观测所选集合函数值。通过随机采样与精心构造的势函数（potential），将在线子模最大化归约为概率单纯形上的在线线性优化，从而调用在线线性优化工具（如OMD/FTRL）控制regret。

Result: 算法达到 $O\!\left(k\sqrt{nT\log(n/k)}\right)$ 期望 $(1-1/e)$-regret（相对最优固定 $k$-集合），是首个在bandit设定下对任意单调子模函数具有 $\sqrt{T}$ horizon依赖的多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 随机采样+势函数归约可有效将bandit反馈下的在线子模最大化转化为单纯形上的在线线性优化，获得 $\sqrt{T}$ 级regret；该框架为bandit在线组合优化提供了可推广的思路。

Abstract: We study online monotone submodular maximization under a cardinality constraint with bandit feedback. On each round, the learner selects at most $k$ elements from a ground set of size $n$ and observes only the value of the selected set. We develop an efficient algorithm achieving $O\!\left(k\sqrt{nT\log(n/k)}\right)$ expected $(1-1/e)$-regret over $T$ rounds. To our knowledge, this is the first polynomial-time algorithm with $\sqrt{T}$ dependence on the horizon for arbitrary monotone submodular functions in the bandit setting. Our approach uses randomized sampling and a carefully constructed potential to reduce online submodular maximization to online linear optimization over the probability simplex.

</details>


### [325] [KKT-Perturbation-Aware Communication for Fully Distributed Online Convex Optimization with Nonseparable Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11827)
*Zhaoye Pan,Tao Li,Yunjie Guo,Guanpu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带有不可分代价与耦合约束的分布式在线凸优化问题，提出一种基于KKT算子扰动感知的随机化通信触发机制，将坐标下降的自适应采样思想用于通信资源分配，在标准正则性假设下达到 O(T^{2/3}) 期望静态遗憾与 O(T^{2/3}) 期望累积约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性能与运行约束依赖所有智能体的联合配置、无法分解为个体贡献，导致非可分耦合；此时每个智能体只能借助邻居间交换的联合动作信念与共享拉格朗日乘子估计全局信息，而每轮传输全维原始-对偶信念的开销随联合决策维数与网络规模线性增长。现有事件触发方案依据状态变化或一致性误差触发通信，但在非可分耦合下，同等幅度的误差经过KKT算子后会放大为差异显著的扰动，因此触发准则与真实影响不匹配。

Method: 建立完全分布式原始-对偶更新框架，各智能体以局部信念估计全局信息并与邻居交换乘子；设计随机化KKT扰动感知通信机制，分别对原始与对偶通信信道判定是否触发，触发概率取决于该信道更新对KKT算子（即KKT残差）的影响大小；将坐标下降的自适应采样原理由"在决策块间分配计算"迁移为"在信息信道间分配通信"，优先刷新估计扰动较大的信道；在标准正则性假设下进行遗憾与约束违反的收敛分析。

Result: 理论上给出 O(T^{2/3}) 的期望静态遗憾与 O(T^{2/3}) 的期望累积约束违反界；数值实验中，相较于信念一致性基线，在单位传输比特上的学习性能与约束满足性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 在非可分代价与耦合约束场景下，按KKT算子影响而非状态变化幅度来决定通信触发，可显著提升通信效率；自适应采样的思想可有效跨域迁移至通信资源分配，实现精度与通信开销之间更优的权衡。

Abstract: Nonseparable costs and coupled constraints in distributed online convex optimization often arise when system performance and operational constraints depend on the joint configuration of all agents and cannot be decomposed into individual contributions. Such nonseparability leads to fully distributed decision-making with challenging partial local information, where each agent evaluates its local primal-dual update using estimated global information via a local belief of the joint action and the shared Lagrange multiplier to be exchanged with neighbors. While the belief consensus established by prior works ensures no-regret learning, exchanging beliefs comprising full-dimensional primal and dual states at every round imposes a communication overhead that scales with both the joint decision dimension and the network size. Existing event-triggered schemes typically activate communication according to state variation or consensus error. Under nonseparable coupling, however, errors of equal magnitude can produce markedly different perturbations after passing through the KKT operator. We therefore propose a randomized KKT-perturbation-aware communication mechanism, where the triggering of each agent's primal and dual communication channels depends on their impact on the KKT operator. The proposed mechanism transfers the adaptive-sampling principle of coordinate descent from allocating computation across decision blocks to allocating communication across information channels: channels with larger estimated perturbations receive greater priority for refresh. Under standard regularity assumptions, the resulting algorithm achieves $O({T}^{2/3})$ expected static regret and $O({T}^{2/3})$ expected cumulative constraint violation. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved learning and constraint-enforcement performance per transmitted bit over belief consensus baselines.

</details>


### [326] [MOOD: Benchmarking Post-Hoc OOD Detection for Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11839)
*Shirley X. Yu,Amey P. Pasarkar,Adji Bousso Dieng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOOD基准，系统评估15种事后OOD检测器在GNN材料属性预测中是否可迁移，发现现有检测器AUROC仅约0.5（接近随机），并将失效归因于"方法瓶颈"与"编码器瓶颈"两类机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后（post-hoc）OOD检测方法几乎只在图像分类任务上被验证，而科学应用中部署ML模型同样需要OOD检测；其在图神经网络与材料属性预测任务上的可迁移性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性基准。

Method: 构建MOOD（Materials Out-of-Distribution Detection）基准：评估15种事后OOD检测器与2种有监督诊断探针，覆盖4种GNN架构、5个MatBench任务、15种具有物理动机的成分与结构偏移，构成264个评估设置、3个编码器训练随机种子，累计超过13,000次AUROC测量。

Result: 三项关键发现：(1) 现有事后检测器表现接近随机，15种检测器AUROC均在0.5左右；(2) 检测失效可分解为方法瓶颈（有监督探针能区分ID/OOD但无监督检测器失败，说明嵌入中存在未被提取的OOD信号）与编码器瓶颈（连有监督探针都无法区分，说明偏移信息在冻结GNN表示中不可及）；(3) 当OOD预测误差超过ID误差时，基于距离与基于密度的检测器AUROC与该误差差距大小相关。

Conclusion: 科学领域可靠的OOD检测需要显式保留OOD相关信号的表示学习策略，尤其对于当前GNN架构根本无法编码可提取信号的任务；事后检测器的直接迁移不可依赖。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for deploying machine learning models in high stakes scientific applications, yet methods that flag OOD inputs without retraining the model have been validated almost exclusively on image classification. We introduce MOOD (Materials Out-of-Distribution Detection), a benchmark for evaluating whether these post-hoc OOD detectors transfer to graph neural networks (GNNs) on materials property prediction. MOOD evaluates 15 post-hoc detectors and 2 supervised diagnostic probes across 4 GNN architectures, 5 MatBench tasks, and 15 physically motivated compositional and structural shifts, yielding 264 evaluation settings and over 13,000 AUROC measurements across three encoder training seeds. Our evaluation reveals three key findings. First, current post-hoc detectors perform near chance; all 15 detectors achieve an AUROC around 0.5. Second, detector failures decompose into two regimes: method bottlenecks and encoder bottlenecks. Method bottlenecks arise when supervised probes can separate in-distribution (ID) from OOD samples but unsupervised detectors fail, meaning that the embeddings contain OOD signal that current detectors do not extract. Encoder bottlenecks arise when even supervised probes fail to separate ID from OOD samples, indicating that the relevant shift is not accessible in the frozen GNN representation. Third, when OOD prediction error exceeds ID error, the AUROC of distance-based and density-based detectors correlates with the size of that gap. Collectively, these findings indicate that reliable OOD detection in scientific domains requires representation learning strategies that explicitly preserve OOD relevant signal, particularly for tasks where current GNN architectures encode no extractable signal at all.

</details>


### [327] [Severity-Controlled Prediction Sets for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11847)
*Yu Gu,Zijun Yu,Chi-Kuang Yeh,Xinyu Wang,Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal Severity Control（CSC），一种基于ATC层级量化错配严重度的后处理框架，用于在无需重训练的前提下校准药物推荐预测集，控制患者级平均严重度与上分位严重度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物推荐（多标签预测）的预测集评估与校准主要依赖精确重叠指标，将所有漏预测药物视为同等严重，忽略了预测集与漏预测药物在药物层级（ATC）中的接近程度，导致评估与校准缺乏层级感知的细粒度严重度刻画。

Method: 利用ATC（Anatomical Therapeutic Chemical）层级结构度量错配严重度；构建后处理式（post-hoc）共形校准框架，在不重训练预测器的条件下控制患者级严重度，并同时支持两类控制目标：平均严重度（average severity）与针对最高严重度错配的上分位严重度（upper-quantile severity）。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上，CSC能够追踪用户指定的严重度容忍度，并使预测集由远距离错配向精确匹配或近邻匹配偏移。

Conclusion: 层级感知的严重度控制可作为精确重叠指标的有益补充，为药物推荐预测集提供可调、无需重训练且面向临床安全性的校准手段。

Abstract: Medication recommendation is a multi-label prediction task. However, existing prediction-set evaluation and calibration methods rely mainly on exact-overlap metrics and therefore treat all missed medications as equally severe, overlooking how closely the prediction set matches them within the medication hierarchy. To address this, we introduce Conformal Severity Control (CSC), a post-hoc framework for calibrating medication prediction sets under hierarchy-aware mismatch severity. CSC uses the Anatomical Therapeutic Chemical (ATC) hierarchy to quantify mismatch severity and controls patient-level severity without retraining the predictor, supporting both average severity and upper-quantile severity for the highest-severity mismatches. Experiments on MIMIC-IV show that CSC tracks user-specified severity tolerances and shifts prediction sets away from distant mismatches toward exact or close matches.

</details>


### [328] [Identifiable but Hard to Estimate: Rotation in Equally Spaced Overcomplete ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11852)
*Wenjun Xiao,Yinghao Fang,Hanxin Chen,Sihan Jia,Vince D Calhoun,Yuda BI*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在等间距混合列、已知源数、共同源分布与列范数的设定下，二维过完备ICA中共同旋转的估计所需样本量随源数呈指数增长；该样本量由相隔半个列间距的两个旋转间Hellinger距离平方的倒数刻画（相差绝对常数），并由一个径向加权的相位估计器一致达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维过完备独立成分分析中"估计共同旋转需要多少观测"这一基本样本复杂度问题，特别是当混合列等间距排列、源数较多时，旋转可辨识性与有限样本估计精度之间的关系。

Method: 固定非高斯四阶Gram-Charlier源分布，混合列等间距且列范数、源数、源分布已知；以相隔半个列间距的两个旋转之间的Hellinger距离平方的倒数刻画固定相对角精度所需的样本量；构造显式的径向加权相位估计器，利用观测半径避免角谐波均值中的相消；并分析单观测总变差的精确指数衰减率。

Result: 旋转在模列间距意义下可辨识，但达到固定相对角精度所需样本量随源数指数增长；所提样本量与Hellinger距离刻画在绝对常数范围内相匹配，且径向加权相位估计器在整个旋转族上一致达到该尺度；给出了单观测总变差的精确指数衰减率，从而给出样本量指数的上下界；另给出一个含噪二元源例子，说明观测特征函数的精确零线处法向导数可指数小。

Conclusion: 过完备ICA共同旋转的估计存在本质的指数级样本复杂度障碍，其根源可用Hellinger距离与总变差刻画；论文给出匹配上下界与最优阶估计器，但样本量指数的精确值仍未确定。

Abstract: How many observations are needed to estimate a common rotation in two-dimensional overcomplete independent component analysis? We study equally spaced mixing columns with known source count, common source law, and column norm. For a fixed non-Gaussian fourth-order Gram-Charlier source law, the rotation is identifiable modulo the column spacing. Yet the sample size for fixed relative angular accuracy grows exponentially with the source count. We characterize this sample size, up to absolute constants, by the reciprocal of the squared Hellinger distance between two rotations separated by half the column spacing. An explicit radially weighted phase estimator attains this scale uniformly over the rotation family, using the observation radius to prevent cancellation in the mean of an angular harmonic. We also determine the exact exponential decay rate of single-observation total variation for the same pair. This gives bounds on the sample size exponent; its exact value remains open. Finally, a noisy binary-source example shows how an exact zero line of the observed characteristic function can have an exponentially small normal derivative.

</details>


### [329] [DeltaForge: A Post-hoc Mixture-of-Experts VLA for Multi-Domain Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11856)
*Bingzhang Wang,Mo Poorsartep,Yasaman Esfandiari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DeltaForge，首个面向驾驶的后验混合专家（MoE）VLA 模型，仅用各领域专家权重与小型代理数据集，将 N 个独立微调模型合并为单一多领域模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶 VLA 模型按领域逐个开发：各运营方在私有数据上微调同一基座，形成分散的专家模型，合并代价高。权重合并在异构领域累积时精度下降，稠密 MoE 路由合并成本过高。

Method: 利用同一基座微调所得权重更新具有共享结构，对全部更新联合做 SVD 分解，拆分为共享部分与极小的领域特化部分；路由器仅作用于微小特化分量。设计两个协同的 clip 级盲路由器，分别负责 VLM 推理专家与 flow-matching 动力学专家，在代理数据集的软标签上训练。

Result: 在覆盖城市、越野、高速，及轿车、卡车、全地形车六类领域上，精度接近各领域专家，并在接近单模型参数与算力成本下超越所有合并、路由、集中式与联邦式基线。

Conclusion: 新增领域只需将权重更新投影至共享基，无需端到端重训；模型还能泛化到未显式训练的领域组合。

Abstract: Fleets of driving vision-language-action (VLA) models are developed one domain at a time: each operator finetunes a shared base model on private data, producing specialized experts that are costly to consolidate into a single model. Weight merging loses accuracy as heterogeneous domains accumulate, and dense mixture-of-experts routing is prohibitively expensive to combine them. We present DeltaForge, to our knowledge the first post-hoc mixture-of-experts VLA for driving, which consolidates N independently finetuned per-domain models into a single multi-domain model from their weights and a small proxy set alone. When different operators finetune the same base model, their weight updates end up sharing a lot of structure. We exploit this by decomposing all the updates together using singular value decomposition (SVD), splitting each one into a shared part (the same across all domains) and a tiny domain-specific part. The router then only needs to work with those tiny domain-specific pieces, not the full expert weights, which is what keeps the approach efficient. Then two cooperating clip-level blind routers, one over the VLM reasoning experts and one over the flow-matching dynamics experts, are trained from soft labels on the proxy dataset. Across six diverse domains spanning urban, off-road, and highway driving on cars, trucks, and all-terrain vehicles (ATVs), DeltaForge approaches the accuracy of the per-domain experts and surpasses every merging, routing, centralized, and federated baseline at close to single-model parameter and compute cost. Adding a new domain requires only projecting its weight update onto the shared basis with no end-to-end retraining needed. The resulting model also generalizes to domain combinations it was never explicitly trained on.

</details>
