### [330] [Are UFOs Real? Introducing Unified Factored Organizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11862)
*Thomas X. Sheppard,Alon Albalak,Joel Lehman,Kenneth O. Stanley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一因子组织器（UFOs）：训练一个网络去建模另一网络表示中的规律性，并以该建模结果作为正则项，使任务网络在学习低损失的同时对齐到更优表示；实验显示可学习到定性更优的表示，并在模运算grokking任务上大幅减少训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习研究普遍隐含假设"低损失即意味着学到了好的数据表示"。Kumar等（2025, arXiv:2505.11581）对此提出质疑，指出常规梯度下降训练的网络上虽损失很低，却学到碎裂纠缠表示（FER），使相关概念彼此断裂、无关概念不当纠缠；而通过开放式、人在回路过程演化出的网络则学到统一因子表示（UFR），对领域规律性的刻画远优于输出几乎相同的梯度下降对照网络。本文受UFR启发，试图将"好表示"的规律性显式建模并用作训练信号。

Method: 提出UFOs（unified factored organizers）：训练网络去建模其他网络表示中的规律性。任务网络同时被优化两项：任务目标上的低损失，以及将其表示与UFO所学模型对齐（UFO正则化）。在一表示易于解释的图像回归域中进行定性检验；在模算术问题上训练transformer，比较有无UFO正则（结合/不结合weight decay）达到grokking所需步数。

Result: 在图像回归域中，经UFO训练的网络学到定性上更优的表示。在模算术grokking实验中：结合weight decay时，UFOs可将grokking所需训练步数相对仅用weight decay减少10倍以上；不使用weight decay时，相对无正则基线可减少100倍以上。

Conclusion: UFOs能显著改善表示学习质量并加速grokking，提示一种新范式：损失函数本身可被训练以学习"好表示"的有效模型。作者以"若UFOs为真"的措辞表达其设想性立场。

Abstract: Implicit in much research in deep learning is the assumption that if a network achieves low loss, then it must have learned a good representation of its training data. Recent work by Kumar et al. (2025, arXiv:2505.11581) questions this assumption, arguing that networks trained with conventional gradient descent achieve low loss despite learning fractured entangled representations (FER), leaving related concepts disconnected and unrelated concepts entangled inappropriately. Kumar et al. further show that dramatically better representations are possible: a network evolved through an open-ended, human-in-the-loop process learned unified factored representations (UFR) that capture the regularities of the domain far better than a counterpart with nearly identical outputs trained with conventional gradient descent. Inspired by UFR, this paper introduces unified factored organizers (UFOs), networks trained to model regularities in the representations of other networks. A network can then be trained both to achieve low loss on its task objective and to align its representations with the model learned by the UFO. Networks trained with UFOs are shown to learn qualitatively superior representations in an image-regression domain where representations are easily interpretable. To provide quantitative evidence for the impact of UFOs on representation learning, a transformer is trained with and without UFO regularization to grok a modular arithmetic problem set. When combined with weight decay, UFOs can reduce the number of transformer training steps needed to grok by more than 10x relative to weight decay alone. Without weight decay, UFOs can reduce the number of transformer training steps needed to grok by more than 100x relative to an unregularized baseline. If UFOs are real, they suggest a new paradigm where loss functions themselves are trained to learn an effective model of good representation.

</details>


### [331] [Can Hindsight Critics Guide Delayed Actors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11863)
*Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hindsight Critics——一种面向观测延迟下部分可观测强化学习的非对称Soft Actor-Critic扩展：critic利用事后对齐的状态-动作转移训练，actor仅凭延迟观测学习特权价值估计，从而在不改变部署期观测的前提下部分补偿观测延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器延迟造成环境真实状态与智能体可用观测之间的时间错位，使原本的马尔可夫控制问题退化为部分可观测问题，导致状态-动作转移不对齐。作者希望探究能否在训练阶段利用"事后（hindsight）"信息恢复对齐的状态-动作转移，从而缓解这种错位，且无需在部署时修改观测。

Method: 在Soft Actor-Critic框架上引入非对称actor-critic结构：critic以事后对齐的转移（hindsight-aligned transitions）进行训练，actor则在仅有延迟观测的条件下，从critic给出的特权价值估计（privileged value estimates）中学习。在DelayBench基准上与延迟感知（delay-aware）和延迟无感知（delay-unaware）基线进行对比。

Result: 在DelayBench上的实验表明，Hindsight Critics能够部分补偿观测延迟，并且无需在部署阶段对智能体的观测做任何修改。

Conclusion: 事后对齐的critic训练可作为缓解观测延迟导致的时间错位的有效手段，为部分可观测强化学习提供了一种部署友好的非对称价值学习思路；但补偿仅为部分性的，延迟带来的性能损失未被完全消除。

Abstract: Sensory delays create temporal misalignments between the state of the environment and the observations available to an agent, turning Markovian control problems into partially observable ones. In this work, we inquire whether such misalignments can be mitigated during training by using hindsight to recover aligned state-action transitions. We introduce Hindsight Critics, an asymmetric extension of the Soft Actor-Critic for reinforcement learning under partial observability. The critics are trained on hindsight-aligned transitions whereas the actor learns from the resulting privileged value estimates despite only having access to delayed observations. Comparisons against delay-aware and unaware baselines on the DelayBench suite show that Hindsight Critics can partially compensate for observation delays without requiring modifications to the agent's observations during deployment.

</details>


### [332] [Tropical Gaussian Anticoncentration: Optimal Instance-Dependent Bounds for Online Learning in Extensive-Form Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11877)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Zhiyuan Fan,Lillian J. Ratliff,Patrick Jaillet,Gabriele Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明树丛（treeplex）策略空间中完全信息在线决策的极小极大遗憾为Ω(√(T log|V|))，与KOMWU/DilEnt的已知上界在常数因子内匹配，说明对约化标准形策略数|V|的对数依赖是不可回避的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家建议预测中，最优遗憾为Θ(√(T log K))，K为专家数（即标准形动作数）。对扩展式博弈的树丛策略空间，现有高效算法（KOMWU、权重为1的膨胀熵正则在线镜像下降）只能给出O(√(T log|V|))的上界，其中|V|是约化标准形策略数。自然的问题是：这种对数依赖是否在完全信息奖励模型的最坏情形下是极小极大最优的？难点在于树丛中的纯计划并非相互独立的专家，其奖励通过决策与观察的递归结构相关联。

Method: 为刻画纯计划间的相关性，引入“热带高斯公式”——由取最大值与归一化求和递归构造、保持方差的高斯表达式，其指标计数等于对应子树中约化标准形延续计划的数量。核心解析结果：任何指标计数为N的对数平衡热带高斯公式，其高斯宽度为Ω(√(log N))。证明不采用直接方法，而借助球面熵剖面度量高斯方向间随尺度变化的分离度，再结合Turán定理与Sudakov极小化得到宽度下界。将该结果应用于树丛，构造具有合法转移结果与Rademacher终端奖励的 oblivious 困难分布。

Result: 得到遗憾下界Ω(√(T log|V|))，与已知上界仅相差通用常数，说明DilEnt/KOMWU对约化标准形复杂度的依赖不可避免。

Conclusion: 树丛策略空间完全信息在线决策的极小极大最优遗憾为Θ(√(T log|V|))；对数依赖|V|是本质的，现有高效算法的复杂度刻画已最优（至常数因子）。

Abstract: We study the minimax regret of full-information online decision making in extensive-form games. For prediction with expert advice, the optimal regret is $Θ(\sqrt{T\log K})$, where $K$ is the number of experts, or equivalently, the number of normal-form actions. For treeplex strategy spaces, existing efficient algorithms, including KOMWU and online mirror descent with the weight-one dilated entropy regularizer, achieve regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T\log|\mathcal{V}|})$, where $\mathcal{V}$ is the set of reduced normal-form strategies. We prove that this logarithmic dependence is minimax optimal in the worst-case full-information reward model. The main difficulty is that pure plans in a treeplex are not independent experts: their rewards are correlated through the recursive structure of decisions and observations. To capture this correlation, we introduce tropical Gaussian formulae, recursive Gaussian expressions built from maxima and normalized sums which preserve the variance. The index count of such a formula matches the number of reduced normal-form continuation plans in the corresponding subtree. Our main analytic result shows that every log-balanced tropical Gaussian formula with index count $N$ has Gaussian width $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$. While a direct approach struggles to recover this lower bound, our proof uses a spherical entropy profile to measure scale-dependent separation among the Gaussian directions, and then applies Turán's theorem and Sudakov minoration to obtain the width lower bound. Applying this result to treeplexes gives an oblivious hard distribution with valid transition outcome and Rademacher terminal rewards, establishing a regret lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{T\log|\mathcal{V}|})$. This matches known upper bounds up to universal constants and shows that the DilEnt/KOMWU dependence on the reduced normal-form complexity is unavoidable.

</details>


### [333] [When Does Physics Beat Data? Data-Efficient Traffic State Estimation with Amortized PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11881)
*Anna Lazareva,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LWR守恒律作为物理先验可使稀疏传感器下的交通状态估计更具数据效率，数据驱动模型在传感器大量留出时崩溃，物理信息估计器则平缓退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动TSE模型在传感器密集时表现良好，但覆盖变稀疏时性能急剧下降，作者希望提升稀疏感知条件下的泛化性与数据效率。

Method: 以Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) 守恒律作为physics-informed prior构建估计器，在NGSIM、I-80、PeMS04、PeMS08四条真实走廊上与其他方法对比；并通过匹配的平滑先验、同一LWR模型上的扩展卡尔曼滤波，以及loss-conditional PINN进行消融与效率分析。

Result: 物理信息估计器可泛化到留出传感器，而经典与深度kriging在观测传感器上过拟合。两条激波密集走廊出现明显交叉：留出85-90%传感器时领先无物理网络约26-38%，留出90%时领先最强经典kriging约10-21%。匹配平滑先验无法复现该增益，EKF亦被超越。单个loss-conditional PINN以约5倍更低成本达到20点搜索的效果。

Conclusion: 优势来源于守恒律的施加方式而非单纯正则化或滤波框架；物理先验是提升稀疏TSE泛化性的有效且高效手段。

Abstract: Traffic state estimation (TSE) reconstructs traffic density at unmonitored locations from a sparse set of sensors. Data-driven models work well when sensors are dense but degrade sharply as coverage thins. We show that imposing the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards conservation law as a physics-informed prior makes TSE more data-efficient. On four real corridors (NGSIM, I-80, PeMS04, PeMS08), physics-informed estimators generalise to held-out sensors where data-driven models, including classical and deep kriging, overfit the observed ones. On the two shock-rich corridors the effect is a clear crossover: as more sensors are held out, data-driven models collapse while the physics-informed estimator degrades gracefully, leading the no-physics network by about 26-38% at 85-90% held out and the strongest classical kriging by about 10-21% at 90%. A matched smoothness prior does not reproduce the gain, and an extended Kalman filter on the same LWR model is outperformed on the shock corridors, so the advantage comes from how the conservation law is imposed. Finally, one loss-conditional PINN removes the per-corridor loss-weight search, matching a 20-point search at about 5x lower cost.

</details>


### [334] [How Many Candidates Does a One-Step World Model Need? A Random-Codebook Account of Nearest-of-m Training on Frozen DINO Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11887)
*Yong Hwi Song,Keon Myung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验了"小m的nearest-of-m（IMLE）一步随机世界模型足够用、应用严格proper的能量分数替代硬最小值"两项猜想，用冻结DINOv2/v3 token上的可枚举未来基准证明：m=16的nearest-of-m覆盖优于50步flow matching（约1/30延迟），并用码本失真视角给出三条预注册预测（记忆化、模式概率扁平化、欠离散）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步随机世界模型可避免确定性特征预测的模式平均，也避免diffusion/flow matching每步需数十次网络评估的开销。近期工作提出两项待验证猜想：(1) 小m即足以支撑特征空间世界模型；(2) 应以严格proper的能量分数取代硬最小（nearest-of-m）目标。作者希望在可枚举未来的基准上系统检验这两点，并搞清m的选择规律与方法的根本局限。

Method: 在冻结DINOv2/v3 patch token上构建一步世界模型，选取未来可枚举的基准（含模式式不确定性与随机Push-T变体）。对比对象包括：nearest-of-m（m=16）、50步flow matching、一步flow生成器、以及参数量匹配的能量分数生成器。核心理论视角：将nearest-of-m目标解释为m个生成样本构成随机码本的期望失真，据此事前记录三条预测再做实验验证。另采用自rollout上的逐步匹配，并通过消融分离流形与覆盖两方面收益。

Result: 在模式式不确定性下，m=16的nearest-of-m对枚举未来的覆盖优于50步flow matching（延迟约1/30）、一步flow生成器和同参数量的能量分数生成器；其rollout保持在token流形上，而flow rollout发生漂移。三条预注册预测均获验证：(i) 在不会重复的上下文中对已实现结果产生记忆化，其强度由码本的miss失真I(m)而非miss概率决定，且能量分数生成器提高m也无法摆脱；(ii) 模式概率随m扁平化，与已知最优值的偏差在0.01以内；(iii) 连续高维结果上出现欠离散，构成真实机器人视频上的性能上界。自rollout逐步匹配在随机Push-T变体上将长程误差降低三分之一。规划成功率未因风险估计准确而提升（阴性结果，已给出诊断）。

Conclusion: 小m的nearest-of-m一步随机世界模型是一种高效且覆盖良好的方案，码本失真视角能统一解释其m选择、记忆化与模式扁平化行为；但其固有欠离散性限制了在高维连续结果（如真实机器人视频）上的适用性，且准确的风险估计并不自动转化为规划收益。

Abstract: One-step stochastic world models avoid the mode averaging of deterministic feature predictors without the tens of network evaluations per step that diffusion and flow matching require. The simplest such objective is nearest-of-m matching (conditional implicit maximum likelihood estimation, IMLE): draw m candidate futures and pull only the one closest to the observed outcome. Recent work conjectures that a small m suffices for feature-space world models and that a strictly proper energy score should replace the hard minimum. We test both claims with one-step world models on frozen DINOv2/v3 patch tokens, using benchmarks whose futures can be enumerated. Under mode-like uncertainty, nearest-of-m with m=16 covers the enumerated futures better than 50-step flow matching (at about 1/30 of its latency), one-step flow generators and an energy-score generator at matched parameter counts, and its rollouts stay on the token manifold where flow rollouts drift. One quantity governs the choice of m: the nearest-of-m objective is the expected distortion of a random codebook of m draws from the generator. This view yields three predictions, recorded before the corresponding runs: (i) memorization of the realized outcome in contexts that never repeat, whose strength is governed by the codebook's miss distortion I(m) rather than its miss probability, and which an energy-score generator does not escape by raising m; (ii) a flattening of mode probabilities with m, matching a known optimum within 0.01; and (iii) under-dispersion on continuous high-dimensional outcomes, which bounds the method on real robot video. Per-step matching on self-rollouts cuts long-horizon error by a third on a stochastic Push-T variant; an ablation separates its manifold and coverage benefits. Planning success does not improve despite accurate risk estimates, a negative result we report with its diagnosis.

</details>


### [335] [When Answer Decodability Is Not a Decision: Separating Final-Answer and Imminent-Emission Signals in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11892)
*Hyojung Han,Jongmin Kim,Seung-Hun Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"发射受控双目标探测"（ECDP），在相同位置分别训练低秩读取器去解码"即将提及的候选"与"最终答案"，发现二者可解码性具有目标相对性和协议依赖性，强答案读出并不能证明模型已作出决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常把链式思维隐藏状态上的答案探针解码成功，解读为模型"已决定最终回答"；但这忽略了另一个竞争信号——模型即将在文本中提及的候选。需要区分"即将发射的文本"与"已定决策的证据"。

Method: 提出ECDP：对同一位置、同一低秩读取器家族分别拟合"即将提及"目标与"最终答案"目标，使用目标特异的零分布（null）与阈值，并在任何拟合前固定。在DD（DecisionDepth）正确滚动样本（干扰项先于最终答案出现）上比较两目标的可解码性；另在Qwen3-8B（开启思考）的MMLU留出题上做预注册评估，使用全前缀注意力读取器覆盖每个思考位置；并通过植入信号对照检验读取器灵敏度。

Result: Qwen3.5-4B在DD上解码下一提及准确率.966（null .234），而最终答案仅.221（null .186），发射差距.696 [.648,.744]；Llama-3.1-8B为.575 [.514,.632]。该模式并非普遍：Qwen3-8B在MMLU上两目标均可解码，最终答案读出更强，第22层提及减答案提升为-.398 [-.459,-.336]（467题）。植入信号对照显示读取器能以十分之一剂量恢复注入的最终答案信号。DD上匹配比较因提及探针训练不可靠而无有效统计。

Conclusion: 答案候选的可解码性是目标相对且协议依赖的：强答案读出本身不足以确定模型何时"承诺"某答案；探针训练失败也不能作为信息不存在的证据。ECDP可作为区分"即将发射文本"与"已定决策证据"的实用控制手段。

Abstract: Answer probes applied to chain-of-thought hidden states are often read as evidence that a language model has already decided its final response. This reading overlooks a competing signal, the candidate the model is about to mention. We introduce emission-controlled dual-target probing (ECDP), which fits the same low-rank reader family separately to the imminent mention and to the final answer at identical positions, with target-specific nulls and thresholds fixed before any fit. On correct DecisionDepth (DD) rollouts in which a distractor is mentioned before the final answer, Qwen3.5-4B decodes the next mention at .966 (null .234) but the final answer only at .221 (null .186), an emission gap of .696 [.648, .744]; Llama-3.1-8B gives .575 [.514, .632]. This pattern is not universal. In a pre-specified evaluation of Qwen3-8B with thinking enabled on held-out MMLU questions, using a full-prefix attention reader over every thinking position, both targets are decodable and the final answer is read far more strongly (mention-minus-answer lift -.398 [-.459, -.336] at layer 22 over 467 questions). Model, task, positions, and reader change together between these settings, and an attempted matched comparison on DD produced no valid statistics because the imminent-mention probe did not train reliably, so we do not identify what reverses the order. On DD, planted-signal controls show that the reader recovers an injected final-answer signal at one tenth of full dose. Answer-candidate decodability is therefore target-relative and protocol-dependent: a strong answer readout does not by itself establish when a model commits, and a probe that fails to train is not evidence that the information is absent. ECDP is a practical control for distinguishing imminent text emission from evidence of a settled decision.

</details>


### [336] [Spectral Unlearning: Transformer Structure-Preserving Updates for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11902)
*Sung Il Choi,Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱遗忘（spectral unlearning）：在标准遗忘目标之上引入矩阵结构优化，本质上是归一化输入输出下线性层Transformer的最速下降方向，可在提升遗忘效果（记忆去除、隐私）的同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要聚焦于遗忘目标函数的设计，却忽视了Transformer权重的内在矩阵结构；标准优化器在独立标量坐标上更新，无法利用这种结构信息。

Method: 提出一种更新框架，将标准遗忘目标与矩阵结构优化（谱更新）结合；理论上证明该谱更新对由线性层构成、输入输出归一化的Transformer等价于最速下降方向；方法可作为插件叠加在现有遗忘方法之上。

Result: 在记忆化、隐私、效用三个互补维度及其调和平均指标上评估，谱遗忘稳定优于广泛基线，并缩小了与保留数据重训练参考模型之间的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 利用权重矩阵结构进行优化是提升LLM遗忘效果的有效且通用的方向，可作为现有方法的通用增强模块。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in large language models (LLMs) aims to remove specific learned knowledge, capabilities, or behaviors while preserving model utility. Existing methods have mostly focused on the design of unlearning objectives, while largely overlooking the intrinsic matrix structure of transformer weights. In particular, standard optimizers operate on independent scalar coordinates, thereby ignoring this matrix structure. We propose spectral unlearning, an update framework that combines standard unlearning objectives with matrix-structured optimization. We show that the spectral update corresponds to the steepest-descent direction for transformers composed of linear layers with normalized inputs and outputs. Our approach is applied on top of existing unlearning methods and evaluated across complementary axes of memorization, privacy, and utility, summarized via their harmonic mean. Spectral unlearning consistently outperforms a broad range of baselines and closes most of the gap to a retain-trained reference. Code is available at https://github.com/unlearn-ml/spectral-unlearning/.

</details>


### [337] [On the Global Convergence of Stochastic Adam for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11905)
*Yihang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在NTK框架下为Adam优化器训练足够宽的两层神经网络建立了线性收敛保证，涵盖全批量与小批量梯度，并进一步推广到求解Poisson方程的PINNs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam在实践中常将训练损失几乎降为零，但已有NTK分析主要针对GD和SGD。分析Adam存在额外困难：一阶动量跨迭代耦合随机梯度，二阶自适应预条件依赖同一次更新所用的随机梯度。本文旨在弥合Adam经验成功与理论理解之间的差距。

Method: 对全批量Adam构造Lyapunov函数以控制历史动量的影响；对小批量Adam引入精确虚拟迭代表示、随机梯度与自适应预条件器之间依赖关系的条件分析，以及二阶矩估计的一致集中界；并将分析扩展到PINNs求解Poisson方程，处理微分算子及内部/边界点分别采样带来的挑战，在显式网络宽度与Adam参数条件下给出收敛保证。

Result: 证明全批量Adam经验损失的几何衰减；对小批量Adam在高概率事件上以受限期望意义证明几何衰减；对PINNs的确定性与随机Adam均建立全局收敛保证，并刻画小批量规模对收敛的影响，其保证比现有分析更紧。

Conclusion: 本文为Adam在宽两层网络训练中的快速收敛提供了理论刻画，并给出PINNs更锐利的收敛保证，加深了对Adam成功机理的理解。

Abstract: The Adam optimizer is widely used to train neural networks with highly nonlinear and nonconvex objective functions. Remarkably, Adam often drives the empirical training loss nearly to zero. This phenomenon has been partially explained by neural tangent kernel (NTK) analyses of gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD). However, analyzing Adam presents additional challenges. Its first-moment momentum couples stochastic gradients across iterations, while its adaptive second-moment preconditioner depends on the same stochastic gradient used in the update. In this paper, we establish linear convergence guarantees for Adam in training sufficiently wide two-layer neural networks with either full-batch or minibatch gradients. For full-batch Adam, we construct a Lyapunov function to control the effect of historical momentum. For minibatch Adam, our analysis builds on an exact virtual-iterate representation, a conditional analysis of the dependence between stochastic gradients and adaptive preconditioners, and uniform concentration bounds for the second-moment estimates. We prove geometric decay of the empirical loss for full-batch Adam and geometric decay in restricted expectation on a high-probability event for minibatch Adam. Beyond regression, we extend our convergence analysis to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving the Poisson equation, where differential operators and separate sampling of interior and boundary points introduce additional challenges. We establish analogous global convergence guarantees for both deterministic and stochastic Adam under explicit conditions on network width and Adam's parameters, characterizing the effects of minibatch size on convergence. These results help bridge the gap between Adam's empirical success and its theoretical understanding, providing sharper convergence guarantees for PINNs than existing analyses.

</details>


### [338] [Late but Valuable: Why Deadlines Change the Measured Value of LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11910)
*Sina Barazandeh,Benjamin E. Lundell,Jose Lugo-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出：在带硬截止时间的自动化实验室场景中，LLM 的答案若超时则被执行安全回退动作，因此应把"答案价值"与"到达时间"联合评估，并以实际执行的动作计分；传统"按时率×平均答案价值"的做法会系统性高估推理模式的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地在自动化实验室中规划并执行实验，实验在模型推理期间持续运行且有硬性时间表；LLM 答案迟到时系统改执行安全回退动作（如拒收样本）。这构成了标准服务指标无法刻画的新系统问题：与回退相同的答案无价值，而不同于回退的答案往往需要更长推理时间。若把答案质量与延迟分开汇报，会夸大推理的价值。

Method: 在合成样本决策任务上测试（最优选择已知，运行时强制截止时间），对比同一 LLM 的"快速模式"（直接作答）与"思考模式"（先推理）。预先注册的复现实验：1,200 个新决策，并以 300 个新决策重复验证。另做单 GPU 上 Qwen3-8B 的并发突发请求实验，将并发请求数从 8 提升到 56，观察 45 秒截止时间下的达标比例与总输出量。

Result: 1,200 决策复现中，思考模式中超时的答案价值约为按时答案的 5 倍；"按时率×平均答案价值"的估计值约为思考实际交付价值的 2 倍，对"思考相对快速模式的优势"高估 0.0241/决策，重复实验（300 决策）高估 0.0191/决策，两个 95% 置信区间均不含零。Qwen3-8B 突发实验中，并发请求由 8 增至 56 时，45 秒内达标的思考答案占比从 79.2% 降至 1.4%，而总输出量增至约 3 倍。

Conclusion: 面向实验室的 LLM 智能体评测应同时测量每个答案的价值与到达时间，并对"实际执行的动作者"打分，而非将质量与延迟分开报告；忽略截止时间与回退机制会高估推理收益，且高并发下推理模式的按时交付能力会急剧崩塌。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly plan and run experiments in automated laboratories. These experiments keep running while the model reasons and run on schedules with hard deadlines. If an LLM-generated answer arrives late, the system executes a safe fallback action instead, such as rejecting a sample. This creates a systems problem that standard serving metrics do not capture. An answer that matches the fallback adds no value, and answers that differ from the fallback may require more reasoning time. Reporting answer quality and latency separately can therefore overstate the value of reasoning. We test this on synthetic sample decisions, where the best choice is known exactly and a runtime enforces the deadline. We compare one LLM's fast mode, which answers directly, with its thinking mode, which reasons first. In a prespecified replay of 1,200 new decisions, thinking answers that missed a deadline were worth about five times as much as on-time ones. Multiplying the on-time rate by the mean answer value predicted about twice the value that thinking delivered. This prediction overstated thinking's advantage over fast mode by 0.0241 per decision, and by 0.0191 in a repeat on 300 new decisions. Both 95% confidence intervals excluded zero. In a single-GPU Qwen3-8B experiment using simultaneous request bursts, raising the number of requests from 8 to 56 reduced the share of thinking answers that met a 45 s deadline from 79.2% to 1.4%, although total output tripled. Evaluations of LLM agents for laboratories should therefore measure each answer's value and arrival time together and score the action that actually runs.

</details>


### [339] [CRAFT-GBM: Selecting Best Features for Gradient-Boosted Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11914)
*Basil Kuok,Yi Zhou,Yao Xuan,Shuping Li,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT-GBM 是 LightGBM 的原生扩展，利用训练中累积的分裂增益证据与联合时间一致置信界，自适应决定每轮搜索哪些、多少个特征，在保持原生特征捆绑表示的同时实现带统计保证的特征子采样，显著降低训练时间且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准特征子采样需预先固定特征数量并忽略训练中累积的分裂增益证据，缺乏统计层面的可靠性保障，因此希望用这些证据自适应地决定特征搜索范围与数量。

Method: 将根节点分裂增益转化为有界观测，在自适应、非平稳训练历史下构造联合时间一致置信界；默认内生模式在初始化后仅保留尚不能被排除为最优的特征，使特征集大小 K_t 由训练历史涌现；若指定特征比例 f，则按区间点估计排序特征并给出近似 top-K 保证；实现于 LightGBM 内并保留其原生特征捆绑表示。

Result: 在 14 个公开与合成数据集族及 4 种加噪设置上评估：完全内生设置下 18 个 RQ1 实验中每轮训练时间几何平均降低 39.74%，测试损失几何平均降低 1.04%；指定特征比例 f 时，外生 CRAFT-GBM 相比随机特征子采样的聚合训练时间降幅最多高出 13.52 个百分点，相对全特征 LightGBM 聚合测试损失不增加。初始化后以至少 1−δ 的概率联合保留所有最大期望增益特征。

Conclusion: 累积训练证据可支撑带统计保证的自适应特征搜索，同时保留原生梯度提升的工程与实用优势。

Abstract: Feature subsampling is used to accelerate gradient-boosted trees, yet standard approaches fix the feature count in advance and largely ignore the split-gain evidence accumulated during training. We introduce CRAFT-GBM (Confidence-Ranked Adaptive Feature Tracking), a native LightGBM extension that uses this evidence to adapt which---and, by default, how many---features are searched each boosting round. CRAFT-GBM transforms root-split gains into bounded observations and constructs joint time-uniform bounds valid under adaptive, non-stationary training histories. In its default endogenous mode, after initialization, CRAFT-GBM retains exactly the features that cannot yet be ruled out as best, letting the selected feature-set size $K_t$ emerge from training history rather than a pre-specified fraction. With probability at least $1-δ$, every feature with the largest expected gain is retained jointly across all rounds after initialization. When a feature fraction $f$ is specified instead, CRAFT-GBM ranks features by their interval point estimates and provides an approximate top-$K$ guarantee for feature budget $K_f$. We implement CRAFT-GBM within LightGBM while preserving its native feature-bundling representation, and evaluate it across 14 public and synthetic dataset families and four noise-augmented settings. In the fully endogenous setting, CRAFT-GBM reduces per-fit training time by 39.74\% with 1.04\% lower test loss in the geometric mean across all 18 RQ1 experiments. Under pre-specified feature fraction $f$, Exogenous CRAFT-GBM also yields aggregate training-time reductions up to 13.52 percentage points larger than random feature subsampling, with no increase in aggregate test loss relative to full-feature LightGBM. Accumulated training evidence can thus support adaptive feature search with statistical guarantees while retaining the practical advantages of native gradient boosting.

</details>


### [340] [LBSNet: Lightweight Brain State Modeling via Learnable Spectral Prototypes for EEG-Based Fatigue Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11916)
*Kun Jia,Zhenyu Cao,Hanlin Yu,Zeyuan Li,Yixuan Liu,Zhongye Xia,Sen Song,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量网络LBSNet，用基于原型的大脑状态建模将局部频谱结构与时间聚合关联，实现脑电疲劳检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的疲劳检测需建模跨频带频谱信息及其时间变化；现有方法虽结合多频带建模与自适应时间处理，但跨频带频谱结构应如何影响各时间区间贡献仍研究不足。

Method: LBSNet通过频带特定时间卷积、共享空间投影与跨频带重校准提取紧凑多频带特征；在每个窗口内将相对频谱活动编码为归一化描述子并与可学习频谱原型软匹配，原型分配与原型重要性共同决定功率聚合的时间权重。

Result: 在SEED-VIG与SADT上的被试独立实验表明LBSNet在保持低计算成本的同时优于七个基线；消融与可视化分析支持原型引导的时间加权，学习到的原型显示随疲劳增加相对跨频带频谱状态逐渐重分布。

Conclusion: 原型引导的时间加权机制有效且轻量，频谱原型的重分布可作为疲劳演化的可解释表征。

Abstract: EEG-based fatigue detection requires modeling spectral information across bands and its temporal variation. Recent methods combine multi-band modeling with adaptive temporal processing, yet how spectral structure across bands should influence the contribution of each temporal interval remains insufficiently explored. We propose LBSNet, a lightweight network that uses prototype-based brain-state modeling to link local spectral structure with temporal aggregation. LBSNet extracts compact multi-band features through band-specific temporal convolution, shared spatial projections, and cross-band recalibration. Within each window, relative spectral activity is encoded as a normalized descriptor and softly matched to learnable spectral prototypes. Prototype assignments and learned prototype importance jointly determine temporal weights for power aggregation, allowing the spectral pattern of each window to guide its contribution to the prediction. Subject-independent experiments on SEED-VIG and SADT show that LBSNet outperforms seven baselines while maintaining low computational cost. Ablation and visualization analyses support prototype-guided temporal weighting, while the learned prototypes reveal a gradual redistribution of relative cross-band spectral states with increasing fatigue. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/zy0929/LBSNet.

</details>


### [341] [An Evaluation of Tabular Foundation Models as Training-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11921)
*Xian Yeow Lee,Ryoichi Takase,Ahmed Farahat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练表格基础模型（TabFM）能否作为无需训练的动力学模型用于世界建模，发现其在数据稀缺且上下文充足时是有用的预测器，但推理慢约1000倍，尚不能替代训练好的世界模型用于规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是基于模型的强化学习与机器人规划的关键组件，但通常需要针对特定任务、依赖大量交互数据训练。作者希望探究预训练表格基础模型能否以零样本/免训练方式充当动力学模型，并明确其适用边界。

Method: 将下一状态预测表述为基于转移对（transition pairs）的上下文回归（in-context regression），直接调用预训练TabFM进行预测；在合成动力系统与机器人运动（locomotion）基准上，与从头训练的LSTM、MLP、高斯过程等回归器对比精度；并考察多步自回归滚动下的表现与推理开销。

Result: 几乎在所有设置下，TabFM在同量有限转移数据上优于从头训练的LSTM与MLP；在同样数据拟合的回归器中，其于locomotion任务上精度最高，而高斯过程在光滑低维系统上更优；但全量数据训练的模型在多数系统上仍显著更好。自回归滚动精度高度依赖上下文长度：短上下文下迅速退化至平凡基线水平，长上下文下明显改善，并在一个locomotion任务上超过训练好的LSTM。推理速度约比LSTM慢三个数量级。

Conclusion: 当前阶段，免训练先验在数据稀缺且上下文充足时可作为有效预测器，但尚不能替代训练好的世界模型用于规划。

Abstract: World models are widely considered to be a potential key component of model-based reinforcement learning and robot planning, but typically require task-specific training on substantial interaction data. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model (TabFM) can serve as a training-free dynamics model for world modeling by framing next-state prediction as in-context regression over transition pairs, and where such a model helps and where it breaks. Across synthetic dynamical systems and robot locomotion benchmarks, TabFM is more accurate than LSTM and MLP models trained from scratch on the same limited transitions in almost all settings, and among regressors fitted to the same data it is the most accurate on locomotion, while a Gaussian process is more accurate on smooth low-dimensional systems. However, models trained on the full dataset remain substantially better on most systems. When rolled out autoregressively over multiple steps, TabFM's accuracy depends strongly on how much context it is given. With a short context, its rollouts quickly degrade to the level of a trivial baseline, whereas with a long context they improve substantially and, on one locomotion task, exceed a trained LSTM. Inference is also about three orders of magnitude slower than an LSTM. Overall, in their current state, we find that training-free priors are useful as predictors when data are scarce and enough context is available, but they are not yet a substitute for trained world models in planning.

</details>


### [342] [Constrained Variational Policy Optimization for Hybrid Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11936)
*Shuchang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出 H-CVPO，将 CVPO 的“非参数改进 + 参数投影”变分框架从连续动作扩展到混合动作空间，通过指数型奖励-成本重加权得到闭式联合目标分布，并用因子化 M-step 分别拟合离散与条件连续分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合动作空间的约束强化学习需同时协调离散模式与模式条件下的连续控制，且要满足长时程成本约束；现有约束混合动作方法直接在参数空间优化因子化策略，缺少 CVPO 那样将非参数改进与参数投影分离的机制。

Method: 将 CVPO 变分框架推广到混合动作空间：(1) 在有界性与严格可行性下，证明约束 E-step 零对偶间隙，并在对偶最优于正温度取得时，通过奖励-成本指数重加权得到混合动作上的闭式联合目标分布；(2) 利用精确的混合 KL 分解，构造因子化 M-step，在离散与条件连续两个独立信赖域下拟合策略各分量；(3) 精确 E/M 更新下证明固定参考的代理改进与条件奖励-成本界（需充分可行性裕度条件）；近似更新下在紧域连续性条件下证明对偶极小点存在，并用残差型可接受性判定是否投影目标。

Result: 消融实验表明成本感知重加权是约束控制的重要贡献因素；在模拟混合控制环境的广泛实验中，H-CVPO 相较混合动作基线取得更优的奖励-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 变分式“非参数改进—参数投影”分离思想可有效迁移至混合动作约束优化，理论保证（零对偶间隙、代理改进、成本界、对偶极小点存在性）与实证结果共同支持 H-CVPO 作为混合动作约束 RL 的有效方案。

Abstract: Constrained reinforcement learning in hybrid action spaces must coordinate discrete modes and mode-conditioned continuous controls under long-horizon cost constraints. Existing constrained hybrid-action methods typically optimize factorized policies directly in parameter space, whereas Constrained Variational Policy Optimization (CVPO) separates nonparametric improvement from parametric projection in its original continuous-action formulation. We introduce Hybrid Constrained Variational Policy Optimization (H-CVPO), extending this variational framework to hybrid action spaces. Under boundedness and strict feasibility, its constrained E-step has zero duality gap and, when the dual optimum is attained at positive temperature, yields a closed-form joint target distribution over hybrid actions through exponential reward-cost reweighting. Using the exact hybrid KL decomposition, we formulate a factorized M-step that fits both policy components to this target under separate discrete and conditional-continuous trust regions. For exact E/M updates, we prove fixed-reference surrogate improvement and conditional reward-cost bounds with a sufficient feasibility-margin condition. For approximate updates, we establish the existence of a dual minimizer under continuity on a compact domain and use residual-based admissibility to determine whether to project the target. Ablations identify cost-aware reweighting as an important contributor to constraint control. Extensive experiments in simulated hybrid-control environments show that H-CVPO achieves favorable reward-cost trade-offs compared with hybrid-action baselines.

</details>


### [343] [Attention Is Only as Good as Its Witnesses: When Higher-Order Layers Help, and When They Do Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11937)
*Mostafa 'Neo' Mohsenvand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用"配分函数+温度读取"的视角统一解释Transformer在图搜索与SET卡牌任务上的学习失败，并由此导出深度律（L层两步游走恰好覆盖2^L−1步）与规模律（正确读出存在量词当且仅当逆温度×间隔≥候选数对数），据此设计的传播层与外积注意力在多项任务上以极小参数超越大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在图上无法随规模泛化地学会搜索、SET任务需大量样本才能学会，尽管注意力在表达力上足以表示这些计算；高阶注意力的收益也不稳定。作者希望从"每层对一族见证求和（即计算配分函数）并按温度读取"这一机制性视角，给这些失败与不均衡现象一个统一解释，并据此设计能补足缺陷的层结构。

Method: 将每层视为对见证族计算配分函数并在某一温度下读取，由此提出两条定律：(1) 深度律——L层、每层两步游走（two-step walks）最多覆盖2^L−1步，与L层嵌套"存在某顶点"量词的逻辑深度一致；给出达到该界的构造并用Lean形式化验证，且证明顶点无名字时无法超越。(2) 规模律——按温度读取存在量词的代价，最坏情况下正确读出当且仅当逆温度×margin ≥ log(候选数)。基于两条定律与两个经检验的原则构造新层，包括：传播层（propagator layer）；利用模3与指数Newton法精确计算2-单纯形注意力softmax（3^d·n次运算、d个时钟）；以及OR-join与power-mean通道组成的块。

Result: 1) 在49顶点图上训练的单个传播层，可在250顶点图的所有搜索深度上正确回答所有问题；2) 六个时钟的指数Newton实现使模型仅用与2-单纯形注意力相当的少量牌局即学会SET，并从8张牌迁移到20张牌；3) 在无提示的CLRS-30上，仅18.6万参数的OR-join+power-mean块在官方测试集12个图任务中的9个、以及新采样图上的8个任务上，与至少7300万参数模型持平（半分之差内）或更优。

Conclusion: 深度与规模两条定律为Transformer在搜索与推理任务上的失败提供了可解释的定量刻画，并直接指导层设计：该框架还解释了成对（pair）层在何处无益、以及并行层在何处必须引入循环（loop）。

Abstract: Transformers stop learning graph search as graphs grow, and learn the card game SET only after many distinct questions, although attention can represent both. We read these failures, and the uneven record of higher-order attention, from what a layer sums over: each layer we study computes the partition function of a family of witnesses and reads it at a temperature. Two laws follow. The depth law is a known result of logic, which we state for these layers: L layers over two-step walks reach exactly 2^L - 1 steps, just as a logical statement with L nested clauses of the form "there is a vertex such that" sees exactly that far. We build a construction that reaches this far, checked in Lean, and, when vertices carry no names, no such network reaches further. The size law is the cost of reading at a temperature, which logic does not have: existence is read correctly at every size, in the worst case, if and only if inverse temperature times margin is at least the logarithm of the number of candidates. Layers built on these laws, and on two principles that we test, learn where Transformers fail. One propagator layer trained on 49-vertex graphs answers every question at every search depth on 250-vertex graphs. Given the modulus 3, exponential Newton computes the softmax of 2-simplicial attention over pairs exactly in 3^d n operations for d clocks; with six clocks it learns SET from as few deals as 2-simplicial attention and transfers from 8- to 20-card tables. On CLRS-30 without hints, a 186,000-parameter block of OR-join and power-mean channels matches (within half a point) or beats a model of at least 73 million parameters on 9 of 12 graph tasks of the official test set, and on 8 of 12 with freshly drawn test graphs. The same account explains where pair layers did not help and where parallel layers must loop.

</details>


### [344] [Decision Aware Origin-Destination Demand Estimation with Structurally Constrained Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11946)
*Xianzhe Wang,Yueyue Fan,Johannes Royset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"决策感知"的OD需求估计框架：先用结构约束交通数据刻画可行需求区域，再在该区域内选取使下游决策后悔最小化的OD估计，从而同时实现数据一致性与决策相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OD估计仅以估计需求与观测交通数据、历史需求模式的吻合程度评判质量。作者认为仅"数据对齐"不足，还应考虑OD估计误差如何影响下游决策质量。

Method: 通过几何表示近似由物理网络约束诱导的可行需求区域，以此对可行OD估计的搜索进行正则化；在该正则化区域内，使用下游后悔的凸代理（convex surrogate），构造可求解的决策感知优化问题。

Result: 数值实验表明，相较传统估计基准方法，该框架能持续降低决策后悔，验证了将OD估计与下游决策质量对齐的价值。

Conclusion: OD估计应以决策质量为导向；所提决策感知框架在保持数据一致性的同时提升决策相关性，是优于纯数据对齐式估计的有效途径。

Abstract: Origin-destination (OD) demand plays an important role in transportation system design and management applications. Conventional OD estimation methods typically judge estimation quality based on how estimated demand aligns with observed traffic data and historical demand patterns. We argue that data alignment alone is insufficient; how errors in OD estimates influence downstream decision quality should also be considered. This paper develops a decision aware OD demand estimation framework that leverages structurally constrained traffic data to characterize an admissible demand region, within which we identify OD estimates that would lead to least regret in the downstream decision. Therefore, our OD estimates are both data-consistent and decision-relevant. Methodologically, we approximate the admissible demand region induced by physical network constraints through a geometric representation, which regularizes the search over feasible OD estimates. Within this regularized region, we use a convex surrogate of downstream regret to obtain a tractable decision aware optimization problem. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework consistently reduces decision regret relative to conventional estimation benchmarks, demonstrating the value of aligning OD demand estimation with downstream decision quality.

</details>


### [345] [Operation-aware Multimodal Clinical Decision Support System for ICU Discharge Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11960)
*Guoqing Zhang,Rafik Hadfi,Takayuki Ito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种“运营感知”的多模态临床决策支持系统(CDSS)用于ICU非计划30天再入ICU风险评估，融合病历文本(自进化RAG去噪)、科室工作负荷与入院流量等运营指标、连续生命体征与稀疏诊断编码，并针对类别不平衡与报警疲劳做归一化、类别重加权与阈值校准；在MIMIC-III 2万次ICU住院上20次独立实验中，端到端多模态微调(分层冻结+差分学习率)取得最佳AUROC 0.8288、PR-AUC 0.4373、F1 0.4757。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型普遍假设再入院风险仅由患者个体病理生理状态决定，忽略医院运营情境与资源容量；而实际出院决策是患者稳定性与床位周转压力之间的动态权衡。该文旨在弥补这一缺口，并把严重类别不平衡(约10.5%再入院)和报警疲劳纳入设计考量。

Method: 构建运营感知多模态CDSS：①多智能体协同抽取+自进化RAG消除病程记录模板噪声；②构造科室工作负荷指标与入院流量特征；③融合连续生命体征与稀疏医学编码(ICD/DRG)；④采用数据归一化、类别重加权与决策阈值校准；⑤对比策略包括仅生命体征、原始文本嵌入直接拼接(树模型)、以及端到端Transformer多模态微调(分层参数冻结+差分学习率)。

Result: MIMIC-III 2万次ICU住院、20次独立运行：仅生命体征在真实类别不平衡下预测力有限(PR-AUC 0.1387±0.0086)；原始文本嵌入直接拼接使树模型性能下降(XGBoost与LightGBM PR-AUC降至0.3893与0.4030)；端到端多模态微调(Transformer E2E FineTune)逆转文本负迁移，取得最高AUROC 0.8288±0.0110、PR-AUC 0.4373±0.0310、F1 0.4757±0.0196。运营指标提供独立风险信号。

Conclusion: 出院风险预测需显式建模医院运营情境；多模态融合(尤其是端到端分层微调)优于简单特征拼接，可逆转文本负迁移。运营指标带来独立风险信息，为高可用性临床决策支持工具奠定工程基础。

Abstract: Unplanned 30-day intensive care unit (ICU) readmission is a critical benchmark for evaluating healthcare quality. Most predictive models assume readmission risk depends solely on a patient's individual pathophysiological state, ignoring hospital operational context and resource capacity. In practice, discharge decisions are dynamic trade-offs between patient stability and bed turnover pressures. To address this gap, we develop an Operation-aware Multimodal Clinical Decision Support System (CDSS) for ICU discharge risk assessment. The system combines multi-agent collaborative extraction with self-evolving retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to eliminate template noise from progress notes, while constructing departmental workload indicators alongside admission flow features, continuous vital signs, and sparse medical codes (ICD/DRG). Addressing severe class imbalance (about 10.5% readmissions) and alarm fatigue, we implement data normalization, class reweighting, and decision-threshold calibration. In 20 independent runs across 20,000 ICU stays from MIMIC-III, empirical findings demonstrate that: vital signs alone have limited predictive capacity under real-world class imbalance (PR-AUC of 0.1387 +/- 0.0086); directly concatenating raw text embeddings degrades decision tree performance (XGBoost and LightGBM PR-AUC drop to 0.3893 and 0.4030); whereas an end-to-end multimodal fine-tuning architecture (Transformer E2E FineTune) utilizing layer-wise parameter freezing and differential learning rates reverses text negative transfer, achieving the highest AUROC of 0.8288 +/- 0.0110, PR-AUC of 0.4373 +/- 0.0310, and F1-Score of 0.4757 +/- 0.0196. Operational indicators supply independent risk signals, laying an engineering foundation for high-utility clinical decision support tools.

</details>


### [346] [Shaping Useful Noise: Energy Distributions Predict Visual Pretraining Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11962)
*Ching Lam Choi,Antonio Torralba,Phillip Isola,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用"能量谱"（数据在归一化真实图像密度模型下的对数概率分布）作为合成数据审计与筛选原则，并定义能量级分布距离 ELD 来比较真实与合成数据的能量分布，用于重采样与混合多源合成数据以提升预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可扩展视觉预训练，但现有筛选准则（如 FID 等）只能部分解释哪些数据集能训练出强表征，缺乏对数据分布覆盖性的刻画。真实图像兼具稀疏的高似然场景与稠密的低似然纹理，有用合成数据应覆盖该能量范围，而非塌缩到高概率、低多样性的样本。

Method: 以归一化真实图像密度模型定义数据的能量谱（对数概率水平分布）；据此提出能量级分布距离 ELD，度量真实与合成数据能量分布差异；并用 ELD 对合成数据重采样及多源混合，使合成能量直方图匹配真实图像能量直方图，同时保持多样性。

Result: 在多种合成数据集上，ELD 揭示了 FID 未能捕捉的分布失配；ELD 对预训练性能的预测能力超过 FID、LPIPS 变化度和特征空间召回率，并在 CNN 与 ViT 自监督设置下均成立；基于 ELD 的重采样与混合提升了多样性及下游迁移性能。

Conclusion: 数据能量谱可作为合成数据审计与策展的有效原则，ELD 是超越 FID 等指标的预训练性能预测与筛选工具，且可直接指导合成数据重采样与混合以改善下游迁移。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale visual pretraining, but current selection criteria only partially explain which datasets train strong representations. We propose using the energy profile of a dataset--the distribution of log-probability levels it occupies under a normalised real-image density model--as an audit and curation principle for synthetic data. To operationalise this view, we introduce Energy-Level Distribution Distance (ELD), which compares real and synthetic energy distributions. Real images span sparse high-likelihood scenes and dense low-likelihood textures, so useful synthetic data should cover this energy range rather than collapse to likely but low-variation samples. Across diverse synthetic datasets, ELD exposes mismatches missed by FID and predicts pretraining performance beyond FID, LPIPS variation, and feature-space recall, including across CNN and ViT self-supervised settings. We then use ELD to resample and mix synthetic sources, matching the real-image energy histogram while preserving diversity and improving downstream transfer.

</details>


### [347] [Pairwise distortion distribution for compression and quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11967)
*Carl Nuzman,Jeongmin Chae,Kursat Rasim Mestav,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出成对失真分布（PDD）及其渐近极限成对维度（PD），将相关积分推广到一般失真度量；证明PD可界定RID的下界，PDD为随机码本量化性能提供下界，从而为压缩、量化与降维中潜维度的选择提供理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据压缩中潜维度的选择至关重要，但传统超参数优化代价高且缺乏原则性（ad hoc）。内在维度提供了替代方案，可将Rényi信息维度（RID）等信息论概念与率失真维度及随机码本压缩联系起来，但缺乏对一般失真度量的推广。

Method: 引入成对失真分布（PDD），即数据点对间失真值的分布刻画，并定义其渐近极限——成对维度（PD）；该构造将经典的相关积分（correlation integral）从欧氏距离推广到任意一般失真度量。

Result: 理论结果表明PD构成RID的界（PD bounds RID），且PDD给出随机码本量化性能的下界，该下界可用于指导实际压缩与量化中的维度选择。

Conclusion: PDD/PD为内在维度的估计提供了统一框架，可作为免调参的潜维度选择准则，适用于压缩、量化与降维等应用。

Abstract: Choosing the latent dimension is a critical challenge in data compression, where conventional hyperparameter optimization is costly and ad hoc. Intrinsic dimension provides a good alternative, which connects information-theoretic notions such as Renyi information dimension (RID) to rate-distortion dimension and random codebook compression. In this work, we introduce the pairwise distortion distribution (PDD) and its asymptotic limit, pairwise dimension (PD), which generalize the correlation integral to general distortion measures. We show that PD bounds RID, and PDD provides a lower bound for random codebook quantization performance, which can guide a practical use for compression, quantization, and dimensionality reduction applications.

</details>


### [348] [ShiftCache: Fast and Exact Indel Effect Prediction by Reusing Shifted Reference Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11976)
*Sina Barazandeh,Arth Banka,Jose Lugo-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShiftCache：一个面向序列到功能模型（AlphaGenome、Borzoi、Enformer）的精确复用系统，通过"每个位移长度只编码一次参考序列并共享"来加速 indel 变异打分，在 A6000 上比去重密集执行快 3.5–6.5×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列到功能模型对变异的打分需分别前向计算参考与替代序列，成本高。indel 会使其后未改变的 DNA 落在不同的池化网格上，导致参考序列的编码器激活无法直接复用，因此此前（如 AlphaGenome Atlas）对 1 亿多个 indel 每个都做三次完整前向。然而 indel 之后的 DNA 实际上只是参考序列按 indel 长度平移后的结果，存在可复用的冗余计算。

Method: 提出 ShiftCache：对每个"平移量"只编码一次平移后的参考序列，并在同一窗口内共享给所有同位移的变异；将变异可复用的编码器输出全部拷贝，其余部分以批量分块（batched tiles）方式重算。对满足条件的编码器，在精确算术下与密集执行结果一致。

Result: 在含大量 indel 的留出 AlphaGenome 窗口上、单卡 A6000、严格 fp32：比去重密集执行快 3.5–6.5×，比逐层复用的适配版本快 1.6–2.7×；比两个已发表分析（Borzoi、AlphaGenome）的密集重实现快 3.5–4.1×，比 Enformer 密集执行快 2.6×。默认 AlphaGenome 与 Borzoi 运行的所有检查输出在绝对/相对容差 1e-5 内与密集执行一致。局限：若每个变异单独置于居中的 131 kb 窗口，仅比去重密集执行快 1.36×；插入长度高度多样或存在大量分散编辑时，可能慢于逐层复用或密集执行；更大分块批、Enformer 或 H100 上少量数值超出容差。

Conclusion: ShiftCache 通过刻画 indel 引起的网格位移冗余，实现了一种精确且通用的编码器激活复用机制，在典型 indel 密集场景下带来显著加速且数值可靠；其收益依赖同位移变异的共享程度与批量设置，在高度异构或分块过大时性能与精度会退化。

Abstract: Sequence-to-function models such as AlphaGenome and Borzoi read up to a megabase of DNA and score a genetic variant by comparing their predictions for the reference and alternate sequences. Insertions and deletions (indels) are costly to score, because an indel usually shifts the unchanged DNA after it onto a different pooling grid, which prevents direct reuse of the reference's encoder activations. The AlphaGenome Atlas scored more than 100 million indels with three full model passes each, although the DNA after an indel matches the reference shifted by the indel's length. We present ShiftCache, an exact reuse system that encodes each shifted reference once and shares it among all variants scored in the same window with the same shift. ShiftCache copies every encoder output a variant can reuse and recomputes the rest in batched tiles. On held-out AlphaGenome windows shared by many indels, in strict fp32 on one A6000, ShiftCache is 3.5 to 6.5$\times$ faster than deduplicated dense execution and 1.6 to 2.7$\times$ faster than our adaptation of layer-by-layer reuse to indels. It is 3.5 to 4.1$\times$ faster than dense reimplementations of two published analyses with Borzoi and AlphaGenome, and 2.6$\times$ faster than dense execution with Enformer. Scoring each variant in its own centered 131 kb window makes ShiftCache only 1.36$\times$ faster than deduplicated dense execution, and highly diverse insertion lengths or many dispersed edits can make it slower than layer-by-layer reuse or dense execution. For encoders that meet our conditions, ShiftCache computes the same predictions as dense execution in exact arithmetic. On an A6000, every checked output of the default AlphaGenome and Borzoi runs matches dense execution within an absolute and relative tolerance of $10^{-5}$. With larger tile batches, with Enformer or on an H100, a small fraction of values exceeds this tolerance.

</details>


### [349] [MixRoute: Rethinking Single-Distribution Training for Generalizable Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11977)
*Hang Yi,Ziwei Huang,Yining Ma,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出单分布训练在NCO跨分布泛化中的潜力被严重低估，并提出通过"混合归一化"这一简单架构归纳偏置来释放该潜力，仅用均匀分布训练即可实现强零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）在路由问题上表现有前景，但跨数据分布泛化是核心瓶颈。现有方法多依赖复杂多分布训练或元学习方案，隐含假设单分布训练只能得到较弱的零样本基线，本文对此提出质疑。

Method: 1）构建覆盖三类代表性路由问题、179个细粒度数据集的综合基准，涵盖节点坐标、客户需求、容量与时间窗的多类分布偏移；2）提出MixRoute框架，在不同粒度上自适应组合归一化统计量，以缓解不同形式的分布偏移；3）仅在均匀分布上训练，无需任何分布特定适配。

Result: 在涵盖三类路由问题、179个数据集的基准上，MixRoute仅用均匀分布训练，即在大量未见分布上取得一致的最先进零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 归一化可作为一种简约有效的零样本泛化手段，替代复杂的训练—自适应方案；单分布训练潜力被低估，架构归纳偏置（混合归一化）即可显著提升跨分布泛化。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) is a promising paradigm for solving routing problems, yet its generalization across diverse data distributions remains a critical bottleneck. Existing methods typically tackle cross-distribution generalization through complex multi-distribution training or intricate meta-learning schemes, implicitly presuming that single-distribution training yields a weak zero-shot baseline. In this paper, we demonstrate that the potential of single-distribution training has been substantially underestimated and can be unlocked by a simple architectural inductive bias: Mix Normalization. To approach this, we first construct a comprehensive benchmark spanning three representative routing problems and 179 fine-grained datasets, capturing diverse shifts in node coordinates, customer demands, capacity and time windows. Building on this benchmark, we propose MixRoute, a neural framework that adaptively combines normalization statistics at different granularities to mitigate varying distribution shifts. Trained exclusively on uniform distributions, MixRoute generalizes effectively to a wide range of unseen distributions without any distribution-specific adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MixRoute consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot generalization. Our work revisits normalization as a parsimonious alternative for zero-shot generalization against complex training-adaptation schemes.

</details>


### [350] [FireGate: Bounding Behaviour Change Before Training in Continual RL Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11979)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FireGate通过在模型冻结底层上加装门控分类器，预先（训练前）以PAC保证界定任务流训练中输出行为变化的上界——遗忘率不超过门控的误报率，代价是门控的检测功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的语言模型逐任务微调时会破坏本应固定的行为（早期技能、通用对话、拒答等）。现有遗忘只能在训练后、在人工选定的基准上事后发现，缺乏训练前的、针对指定提示分布的可证明界。

Method: FireGate为每个任务更新（如新LoRA）设置门控：门控是以模型冻结低层为特征、逐提示判定是否启用该更新的分类器；未激活处模型输出与旧模型完全一致。由此，在任意提示分布上，任何有界行为分数的变化量不超过整条任务流中门控在该分布的激活率。在"不可改变内容"的无标注提示上校准门控，可在训练前以PAC保证固定该激活率，且不依赖训练产生何种权重。理论上还给出该代价的下界：任何门控更新在保护输出上的改动越少，其改变新任务输出的频率不可能低于同水平最优检验器的表现。

Result: 在最长五任务的强化学习任务流上，门控训练保留早期任务，仅改变3.3%的留出对话回复，而无门控训练改变91.8%。跨七个模型，门控后保留的收益与其训练前在新任务提示上测得的激活率一致。

Conclusion: 遗忘被界定为门控误报率，并以门控功效作为代价。该保证针对部署形态的门控模型、在采样所得提示分布上成立，不针对刻意搜索触发门控提示的对抗者。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a deployed language model on one task after another can change behaviour that should stay fixed, such as earlier skills, general chat or refusals. Today, such forgetting is found only after training, on the benchmarks someone chose to run. This work bounds, before training, how often its outputs change on chosen prompt distributions. FireGate places each task's update, such as a new LoRA, behind a gate, which is a classifier on the model's frozen lower layers that decides for each prompt whether the update is used. Where no gate fires, the model computes exactly what the old one computes. As a result, on any prompt distribution, every bounded behaviour score changes by at most the gates' firing rate there over a whole task stream. Calibrating each gate on unlabeled prompts of what must not change fixes that rate before training, with a PAC guarantee, whatever weights training produces. Forgetting is thus bounded by the gate's false-alarm rate, and its price is the gate's power: no update, gated or not, that changes as few protected outputs can change new-task outputs more often than the best test of that level tells new-task prompts from protected ones. On reinforcement-learning streams of up to five tasks, gated training keeps the earlier tasks and changes 3.3% of held-out chat responses, whereas ungated training changes 91.8%. Across seven models, the gain kept behind the gate follows its firing rate on new-task prompts, measured before training. The certificate covers the gated model as deployed, on the prompt distributions sampled for calibration, not against an adversary who searches for prompts that fire the gate.

</details>


### [351] [HyInv Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11997)
*Haowei Lin,Cai Zhou,Xiangxin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyInv：1.96M参数的骨架条件图网络（编码器-解码器），由研究智能体Hyra 2.0训练（随机顺序自回归预训练+结构奖励强化学习），配合分组draft-and-rewrite采样器，在同等多样性下提升refolding scTM，推理速度约52倍快，并在7个数据集-预测器组合上超过10个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向折叠需要为给定骨架设计氨基酸序列，而筛选要求候选序列既多样又能良好折叠；现有方法在多样性与可折叠性（scTM）之间难以兼顾，且采样效率不足。

Method: 提出HyInv：轻量骨架条件图网络（编码器-解码器，1.96M参数）。训练流程为Hyra 2.0智能体驱动：先做随机顺序自回归序列建模预训练，再做结构奖励强化学习。推理时采用分组draft-and-rewrite采样器，对draft与rewrite使用不同温度以在匹配多样性下提升refolding TM-score。实现层面使用BF16数值并提供可精确复现参考序列的FP32路径。

Result: 单张NVIDIA H20上，402残基骨架生成128条序列耗时50.6 ms，约为参考采样器的52倍加速；精确FP32路径快8.7倍。在5个基准数据集、7个数据集-预测器组合上，HyInv平均scTM高于全部10个外部基线，且序列多样性相当或更高，相对ProteinMPNN与ProteinZero V2提升显著。默认设置即可支持de novo骨架、口袋约束库、保留催化位点的酶重设计及loop重设计。

Conclusion: HyInv以极小参数量在效率与质量上同时取得优势，证明智能体驱动的预训练+强化学习流程结合改进采样策略可有效提升反向折叠的可折叠性与多样性平衡，且具备多场景通用性；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Inverse folding designs amino-acid sequences for a given protein backbone, and screening needs many candidates that are both diverse and well folded. We present HyInv, a 1.96-million-parameter backbone-conditioned graph network with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained by Hyra 2.0, the research agent developed by our team: pretrained with random-order autoregressive sequence modeling, followed by structure-reward reinforcement learning. At inference, a grouped draft-and-rewrite sampler that uses separate draft and rewrite temperatures improves refolding TM-score at matched diversity. An optimized implementation with BF16 numerics designs 128 sequences for a 402-residue backbone in 50.6 ms on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU, about 52 times faster than the reference sampler, and an exact FP32 path that returns the reference sequences is 8.7 times faster. Across five benchmark datasets and seven dataset-predictor combinations, HyInv reaches a higher average scTM than all ten external baselines at comparable or higher sequence diversity, with large margins over ProteinMPNN and ProteinZero V2. The same default setting supports de novo backbones, pocket-constrained libraries, enzyme redesign with the catalytic site kept, and loop redesign. We have released our code and model at https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/Hyra-results/tree/main/AI4Science/hyinv.

</details>


### [352] [Reward-Estimated Hypergradient for Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Black-Box Follower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11998)
*Shigeki Kusaka,Mikoto Kudo,Takumi Tanabe,Akifumi Wachi,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RE-HG方法，在黑盒双层强化学习设定下用逆强化学习从跟随者轨迹中估计奖励，并理论证明线性奖励下势函数塑形偏差在多样动力学下相互抵消，同时用特征协方差小特征值截断处理有限样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层RL的超梯度（HG）方法普遍假设跟随者的奖励已知（白盒），而实际中领导者只能观测跟随者的轨迹、无法获知其奖励函数。

Method: 用IRL从轨迹中恢复跟随者奖励以构造估计超梯度。针对IRL只识别到势函数塑形等价类的问题：证明线性奖励下，当IRL估计在足够多样的动力学上聚合时该偏差抵消，而领导者自身的更新天然提供这种多样性；为应对有限样本，对聚合特征协方差的较小特征值做截断，恢复可辨识子空间并稳定估计。

Result: 实验显示RE-HG估计的梯度与白盒Oracle对齐，性能相当；截断还能抑制超梯度爆炸，使RE-HG在尖锐奖励地形下避开Oracle陷入的局部最优。

Conclusion: RE-HG为黑盒双层RL提供了可行且接近白盒Oracle的超梯度估计方案，并通过协方差特征值截断兼顾理论可辨识性与数值稳定性。

Abstract: Hypergradient (HG) methods for bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) typically assume a white-box follower whose reward is known. In practice, however, the leader rarely knows the follower's reward function and can only observe its trajectories. We address this black-box setting and propose Reward-Estimated Hypergradients (RE-HG), which recovers the follower's reward from these trajectories via inverse RL (IRL). Yet IRL identifies the reward only up to potential-based shaping, a transformation that leaves the follower's behavior unchanged but biases the HG. We prove that, for linear rewards, this bias cancels when IRL estimates are aggregated across sufficiently diverse dynamics, which the leader's own updates naturally supply. To handle finite samples, RE-HG truncates small eigenvalues of the aggregated feature covariance, restoring the identifiable subspace and stabilizing the estimation. Experiments show that RE-HG estimates gradients aligned with a white-box Oracle and achieves comparable performance; empirically, the truncation also suppresses HG explosions, letting RE-HG avoid local optima that trap the Oracle in sharp reward landscapes.

</details>


### [353] [HyPLM Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12000)
*Haowei Lin,Cai Zhou,Xiangxin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyPLM-Flash 是一个 6.5 亿参数的开源蛋白质掩码语言模型，用仅 726B token 训练，在保持与同量级 SOTA 模型相当精度的同时大幅降低训练与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLM 在变异效应预测、注释与设计中已成标准工具，但训练与推理算力需求持续膨胀，需要精度不降而成本更低的高效模型。

Method: 由研究智能体 Hyra 2.0 训练 650M 参数掩码语言模型，语料 726B token（仅为 ESM-2 650M 的 0.73 倍、E1-600M 与 ESM C 600M 的 1/5–1/8）；配套推理引擎采用融合算子与 INT8 矩阵乘法。

Result: 单张 NVIDIA H20 每秒嵌入约 13 万残基，较其他 600M 级编码器快 1.9–2.7 倍。与 28 个公开检查点在 32 项任务上对比：ProteinGym 零样本 Spearman 相关 0.409，与 ESM-2 650M、ESM C 600M、E1-600M 统计上持平；在自监督单序列模型中取得最高 organismal-fitness 分数；长程接触精度优于所评估的全部模型（含 ESM-2 15B、ESM C 6B）；在留出序列与新发布 PDB 链上掩码残基损失低于 ESM-2 650M 等模型。

Conclusion: HyPLM-Flash 以极小训练与推理成本达到 SOTA 精度，为 PLM 建立新的 Pareto 前沿；模型权重与推理引擎已开源（https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/hyra-results/tree/main）。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) are standard tools for variant-effect prediction, protein annotation and design, but the compute needed to train and serve them keeps growing. We present HyPLM-Flash, an efficient and open PLM trained by Hyra 2.0, the research agent developed by our team. HyPLM-Flash is a 650-million-parameter masked language model trained on 726 billion tokens, 0.73 times the data of ESM-2 650M and a fifth to an eighth of the data of E1-600M and ESM C 600M. It runs on an inference engine with fused kernels and INT8 matrix multiplication, embedding about 130,000 residues per second on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU, 1.9 to 2.7 times faster than other 600M-class encoders. In a matched evaluation against 28 public checkpoints on 32 tasks, HyPLM-Flash reaches a ProteinGym zero-shot Spearman correlation of 0.409, statistically tied with ESM-2 650M, ESM C 600M and E1-600M, and the highest organismal-fitness score among self-supervised single-sequence models. It gives the best long-range contact precision of all models evaluated, including ESM-2 15B and ESM C 6B, and a lower masked-residue loss than ESM-2 650M, ESM C 600M and E1-600M on held-out sequences and on recently released PDB chains. Matching state-of-the-art accuracy at a fraction of the training and inference cost, HyPLM-Flash sets a new Pareto frontier for protein language models. We have released the model weights and the inference engine at https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/hyra-results/tree/main.

</details>


### [354] [Scalable Bayesian Optimization for Efficient Exploration of Moire Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12010)
*Panagiotis Krokidas,Christos Lazaridis,Vassilis Sioros,Efi-Maria Papia,Vassilios Constantoudis,George Giannakopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FruBO贝叶斯优化框架，用CatBoost代理模型与委员会式不确定性估计，在60万构型的多层莫尔图案设计空间中高效找到全局最优，1,470次采样约1.15小时即达成，远优于随机采样与高斯过程BO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料设计空间庞大且目标分布高度偏斜，稀有高性能构型难以通过试错或纯代表性机器学习采样找到；而标准高斯过程贝叶斯优化在此类计算昂贵、样本规模大的问题上面临显存瓶颈， scalability不足。

Method: 以六个旋转六边形晶格叠加构成的多层莫尔图案为案例，目标为最大化孔径均匀性（用Jain公平指数量化），构建含600,000个唯一构型、目标分布高度偏斜的基准。提出FruBO框架：以轻量级CatBoost作为代理模型，结合基于委员会的（committee-based）不确定性估计开展贝叶斯优化，并与随机搜索、高斯过程BO进行对比。

Result: 随机采样需533,012次评估（约31小时）才命中全局最优；高斯过程BO在采集290个样本后因GPU显存耗尽而不可行；FruBO+CatBoost仅1,470次采集样本、约1.15小时即找到全局最优。

Conclusion: 可扩展的代理模型能显著提升贝叶斯优化在大型纳米材料设计空间中的可行性与效率，FruBO为计算代价高昂的材料构型发现提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Large materials design spaces often contain rare high-performing configurations that are difficult to identify through trial-and-error exploration or purely representative machine-learning (ML) sampling. In this work, we present FruBO, a flexible Bayesian Optimization framework for accelerating the discovery of optimal configurations in computationally demanding materials design problems. As a case study, we investigate multilayer Moiré patterns generated by the superposition of six rotated hexagonal lattices, where the objective is to identify the rotational conformation that maximizes pore-size uniformity, quantified using the Jain fairness index. The resulting benchmark contains 600,000 unique configurations and exhibits a highly skewed objective distribution, making the global optimum difficult to locate by random sampling. We compare random exploration, Gaussian Process-based Bayesian Optimization, and Bayesian Optimization using a lightweight CatBoost surrogate with committee-based uncertainty estimation. Random sampling identifies the global optimum only after 533,012 evaluations, requiring approximately 31 hours, while Gaussian Process-based BO becomes impractical due to GPU memory exhaustion after 290 acquired samples. In contrast, FruBO with CatBoost identifies the global optimum after 1,470 acquired samples and approximately 1.15 hours. These results demonstrate that scalable surrogate models can substantially improve the feasibility and efficiency of BO in large nanomaterials design spaces.

</details>


### [355] [Speculative Self-Distillation enables Efficient Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12016)
*Shayan Talaei,Agam Bhatia,Arshia Soltani Moakhar,Jonas Hübotter,Amin Saberi,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Speculative Self-Distillation（SSD），一种逐 token 混合策略的自蒸馏方法：学生默认自己生成 rollout，教师仅在其下一 token 分布与学生显著分歧的位置介入。在三个闭卷 QA 基准上，SSD 以少 38–69% 的监督 token 近似达到 on-policy 蒸馏效果，并优于 off-policy 蒸馏，同时在遗忘压力测试中取得最佳域内准确率与最强通用能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用常需语言模型内化预训练中缺失的知识、纳入持续更新的事实或习得新领域技能，自蒸馏是自然选择：以源材料为条件的教师模型指导无条件学生模型进行闭卷学习。但现有方案存在权衡难题——off-policy 蒸馏使用教师生成的前缀，训练分布偏离学生测试时行为；fully on-policy 蒸馏则易漂移至低信号状态，监督效率低。作者希望找到兼顾分布一致性与监督效率的折中方案。

Method: SSD 是一种高效的逐 token 混合策略自蒸馏方法。学生默认生成每个 rollout；教师仅在教师与学生的下一 token 分布显著分歧的位置进行干预。该设计使训练 rollout 保持接近学生测试时的行为，同时避免 fully on-policy rollout 漂移进入的低信号状态，从而让教师恰好在特权上下文改变预测之处对 rollout 施加引导。

Result: 在衡量事实回忆、知识更新与技能习得的三个闭卷 QA 基准上，SSD 几乎匹敌 on-policy 蒸馏，但使用的监督 token 减少 38–69%，并在留出测试集上稳定优于 off-policy 蒸馏。在遗忘压力测试中，SSD 在取得最佳域内准确率的同时，保留了最强的通用能力，且监督量少于 on-policy 基线。

Conclusion: SSD 在自蒸馏任务上建立了新的"准确率—效率—遗忘"帕累托前沿，表明稀疏、分歧触发的教师介入是一种兼顾性能、监督成本与通用能力保持的有效策略。

Abstract: Many real-world applications require language models to internalize knowledge that was absent from pretraining, to incorporate continually updated facts, or to learn new domain skills. Self-distillation has emerged as a natural approach for this task. A model conditioned on the source material acts as the teacher, and an unconditioned student is trained to reproduce the teacher's behavior closed-book. We propose Speculative Self-Distillation (SSD), an efficient per-token mixed-policy method for self-distillation. The student generates each rollout by default, while the teacher intervenes only at positions where its next-token distribution substantially diverges from the student's. Unlike off-policy distillation with teacher-generated prefixes, this design keeps training rollouts close to the student's test-time behavior, while avoiding the low-signal states that fully on-policy rollouts drift into. In effect, SSD lets the teacher steer the rollout exactly where the privileged context changes the prediction. On three closed-book QA benchmarks measuring fact recall, knowledge updates, and skill acquisition, SSD nearly matches on-policy distillation while using 38-69% fewer supervised tokens and consistently outperforms off-policy distillation on the held-out test set. On a forgetting stress test, SSD retains the most general capability while also achieving the best in-domain accuracy and using less supervision than the on-policy baseline. Overall, SSD establishes a new accuracy-efficiency-forgetting Pareto frontier for self-distillation.

</details>


### [356] [FocusBranch: Combinatorial Branch-and-Bound for $\ell_0$ Neural Network Robustness Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12024)
*Renwei Deng,Yang He,Linyi Li,Yuepeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FocusBranch 是一个针对 ℓ₀ 扰动下神经网络局部鲁棒性验证的组合式分支定界框架，通过引入由焦点像素集 S 与层级 l 构成的节点抽象，统一了具体节点（upper shadow 分支）与超节点（covering design 分支）两种分支机制，并结合自适应策略选择起始层级与分支方式，在多种架构与数据集上优于 SOTA 验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ₀ 扰动下局部鲁棒性验证需覆盖所有 ε 个被扰动像素的稀疏子集，本质上是组合爆炸问题，现有方法难以高效处理。作者希望将 ℓ₀ 球结构化为可分解、可分支的对象，从而在保持完备性的同时显著降低验证代价。

Method: 提出节点抽象（focus pixel set S 与 level l，允许 S 外至多 ε−l 个像素被扰动），使 ℓ₀ 球可分解可分支；具体节点通过 upper shadow 固定更多扰动位置，超节点通过 covering design 用更少子节点覆盖大量扰动情形；通过在节点约束的 ℓ₀ 球上具体化线性松弛证明节点安全性，并对未知节点递归分支；同时采样各层级节点估计安全率与代价，选择预测验证时间最小的起始层级与分支策略。

Result: 在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10 上的全连接与卷积分类器实验中，FocusBranch 能跨架构有效验证 ℓ₀ 鲁棒性，并优于当前最先进的验证器。

Conclusion: 将 ℓ₀ 球通过节点抽象转化为可组合分支结构，并配合具体化线性松弛与自适应策略选择，是一种有效且高效的 ℓ₀ 鲁棒性完备验证途径。

Abstract: Verifying the local robustness of neural networks under $\ell_0$ perturbations is inherently combinatorial, as it must reason over all sparse subsets of $ε$ perturbed pixels. This paper presents FocusBranch, a combinatorial branch-and-bound framework for $\ell_0$ robustness verification. The key insight is to make the $\ell_0$-ball decomposable and branchable using a node abstraction, which consists of a focus pixel set $\mathcal{S}$ and a level $l$, permitting at most $ε-l$ pixels outside $\mathcal{S}$. This abstraction unifies two branching mechanisms: concrete nodes branch through upper shadows to fix additional perturbed locations, while super nodes branch through covering designs to cover many perturbation cases with fewer child nodes. FocusBranch proves node safety by concretizing linear relaxations over node-constrained $\ell_0$-balls and recursively branches on unknown nodes. Rather than relying on a fixed verification plan, it samples nodes across levels to estimate safe rates and costs, then selects a starting level and branching strategy predicted to minimize verification time. Experiments on fully connected and convolutional classifiers for MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 show that FocusBranch verifies $\ell_0$ robustness effectively across architectures and outperforms state-of-the-art verifiers. The code is available at: https://github.com/simpl-group/FocusBranch.

</details>


### [357] [MambaPD: Bidirectional Selective State Space Model for Parkinson's Disease Detection from Resting-State EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12026)
*Ayesha Zulfiqar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MambaPD（双向选择性状态空间模型）用于静息态EEG的帕金森病检测，并在两个公开数据集上采用被试独立的5折交叉验证与四个基线比较；结果显示各模型准确率仅0.60–0.71，模型间差异小于折间差异，且MambaPD特异性最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病常在中脑多巴胺能神经元显著缺失后才被诊断，而alpha减慢、相干性降低等静息态EEG异常出现更早，具有早期筛查潜力；但已发表的EEG检测器多声称>95%准确率，往往使用epoch级划分导致被试身份泄漏，估计偏乐观。因此需要在被试独立协议下重新评估模型性能。

Method: 构建MambaPD：融合通道混合（channel mixing）、patch tokenization与双向Mamba编码器的选择性状态空间模型。与TCN、带注意力的BiLSTM、EEGNet、patch Transformer四个基线对比；在UNM与SanDiego两个公开数据集上采用被试独立5折交叉验证（检查点在留出折上选择，故绝对估计偏乐观），并报告完整流程、逐折结果及协议缺陷。

Result: UNM上准确率0.67–0.71，MambaPD 0.714与Transformer(0.715)、TCN(0.708)统计上不可区分，敏感性最高(0.82)但特异性最低(0.52)。SanDiego小队列准确率0.60–0.70，TCN最高(0.703)，EEGNet最低(0.601)，MambaPD为0.631且特异性最低(0.46)。UNM上模型间差异小于折间差异。

Conclusion: 在被试独立协议下，EEG-based PD检测性能远低于文献报告的>95%，说明此前高准确率主要源于数据泄漏；架构差异并非主要因素，折间方差占主导，MambaPD未显著优于Transformer/TCN且特异性偏弱。作者公开完整流程与逐折结果并指出协议不足，提示未来需更严谨的评估设计与特异性优化。

Abstract: Typical diagnosis of Parkinson's disease (PD) occurs only after significant dopaminergic loss, while resting-state EEG abnormalities like alpha slowing and reduced coherence occur earlier. While many published EEG-based PD detectors claim accuracy greater than 95%, many use epoch-level splits which leak subject identity. I introduce MambaPD, a bidirectional selective state space model for resting-state EEG-based PD detection, blending the channel mixing, patch tokenization and bidirectional Mamba encoder. It is compared with four baselines (TCN, BiLSTM with attention, EEGNet and a patch Transformer) in two public datasets (UNM, SanDiego) using subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation (checkpoints were selected on the held-out fold, so absolute estimates are optimistic). On UNM the accuracy is between 0.67 and 0.71, and MambaPD (0.714) is not statistically distinguishable from the Transformer (0.715), and TCN (0.708), with the highest sensitivity (0.82), while the lowest specificity (0.52). For the smaller SanDiego cohort, accuracy is in the range of 0.60 to 0.70, with the highest scores (0.703) from TCN and the lowest (0.601) from EEGNet, while MambaPD scores 0.631, with the lowest specificity (0.46) among the five models. The differences among the models on UNM are minor compared to the differences among folds. I report the whole pipeline, per-fold results, and the shortcomings of the protocol.

</details>


### [358] [Scale-Invariant Empirical-Bayes Laplace Approximation for ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12031)
*Shivam Pal,Piyush Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReLU网络的尺度对称性使经验贝叶斯(EB)拉普拉斯近似不良定义；作者提出γ-canonicalization，先对网络做权重均衡化规范化，再将EB拉普拉斯拟合到规范表示，从而使边际似然在ReLU缩放下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dinh等(2017)指出，通过重参数化可在不改变网络所表示函数的前提下任意改变损失landscape的几何（如曲率）。而EB拉普拉斯近似在计算边际似然时依赖Hessian，对ReLU尺度对称性不具不变性，导致对同一预测的网络可得到不同的先验精度、后验协方差与预测不确定性，因此该近似对ReLU网络不良定义。论文旨在给出一个良好定义、尺度不变的EB拉普拉斯框架。

Method: 1）形式化说明EB拉普拉斯近似因Hessian依赖而破坏ReLU尺度不变性；2）定义由γ索引的先验精度场族；3）提出γ-canonicalization：先将网络转换为规范表示（均衡化输入权重与输出权重之比），再对规范表示拟合EB拉普拉斯；4）该过程等价于把规范表示下的各向同性高斯先验传输回原始坐标，产生ReLU尺度兼容的先验精度场，使边际似然对ReLU缩放不变；5）γ通过最大化边际似然选择。

Result: 经γ-canonicalization后的EB拉普拉斯近似在ReLU尺度等价类上良好定义，跨功能等价的参数化得到一致的先验精度选择与预测不确定性；边际似然对ReLU缩放在参数化层面保持不变，同时γ仍可数据驱动地选取。

Conclusion: 对ReLU网络做贝叶斯推断时，必须谨慎处理尺度对称性带来的几何依赖；将网络先规范化（均衡化）再施加各向同性先验下的EB拉普拉斯，是获得尺度不变、可复现先验精度与不确定性估计的有效途径。

Abstract: ReLU networks are well-known to have continuous scale symmetries; infinitely many reparameterized weight vectors that represent the same function. Dinh et al. (2017) have shown that, by reparameterizing, the geometry of the loss landscape (e.g., curvature) of these networks can be changed arbitrarily without affecting the underlying function. Thus, any quantity that depends on the network's geometry should be defined with care. In this paper, we show that the Empirical Bayes (EB) Laplace's approximation is not well-defined for ReLU networks, mainly due to Hessian dependence in computing the marginal likelihood, which is not invariant under ReLU scale symmetries. Thus, we can get different prior precisions, posterior covariances, and predictive uncertainties for a network having identical predictions under ReLU scaling. We define family of prior precision fields indexed by $γ$ and introduce $γ$-canonicalization, which first converts the network to its canonical representation by balancing the ratio of incoming to outgoing weights, and then fits the Empirical Bayes (EB) Laplace to the canonical representation. This procedure is equivalent to transporting the isotropic Gaussian prior from the canonical representation to the original coordinates, by producing a ReLU-scale-compatible precision field. This makes the marginal likelihood invariant under ReLU scaling, while allowing $γ$ to be selected by maximizing the marginal likelihood. The resulting EB Laplace approximation is well-defined on ReLU scale-equivalence classes and yields consistent prior precision selection and predictive uncertainties across functionally identical parameterizations.

</details>


### [359] [BECON: Belief-Conditioned Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning under Drifting Preferences and Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12053)
*Bui Trong Duc,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BECON 将受限偏好条件 MORL 重构为部分可观测的任务规格问题，用信念条件框架从历史推断潜在偏好与安全预算，并与外部信号按不确定性门控融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受限偏好条件 MORL 假设任务完全可观测、偏好与安全预算为平稳信号；但实际部署中这些信号是不可靠代理：意图漂移、安全容忍度随情境变化、暴露描述符存在噪声、延迟或不完整，因而产生外部信号与潜在交互动态之间的根本冲突。

Method: 提出 BECON：从近期交互历史推断潜在任务上下文，估计偏好与安全预算；通过不确定性依赖的门控将历史推断与暴露任务信号融合（高认知不确定性时倾向观测，历史信息充分时用于纠正观测）；融合后的上下文同时用于偏好优化与上下文自适应约束执行。

Result: 在多种多目标控制任务上，BECON 一致优于 SOTA MORL 基线，在任务漂移下取得更强鲁棒性与更好的约束满足。

Conclusion: 将不可靠任务信号建模为部分可观测问题并以信念条件化+不确定性门控融合，是应对偏好/预算漂移的有效范式，可同时提升鲁棒性与约束满足。

Abstract: Constrained preference-conditioned MORL typically assumes full task observability, treating preferences and safety budgets as stationary task signals. In real-world deployment, however, these signals are often unreliable proxies: intent may drift, safety tolerances may shift with context, and exposed descriptors may be noisy, delayed, or incomplete. We therefore formulate constrained preference-conditioned MORL as a partially observed task-specification problem, where the agent must navigate a fundamental conflict between unreliable external signals and latent interaction dynamics. We propose \textbf{BECON}, a belief-conditioned framework for constrained MORL with drifting preferences and budgets, which infers latent task context from recent histories to estimate preferences and safety budgets. These estimates are fused with the exposed task signal via uncertainty-dependent gates, enabling the policy to defer to observations under high epistemic uncertainty while correcting them when history is informative. The fused context jointly conditions preference optimization and context-adaptive constraint enforcement. Experiments on diverse multi-objective control tasks demonstrate that BECON consistently improves over state-of-the-art MORL baselines, achieving stronger robustness and constraint satisfaction under task drift.

</details>


### [360] [Exact Natarajan Dimension of Homogeneous Multiclass Linear Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12058)
*Jiahua Liu,Benchong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文给出特征维数n、类别数k（n,k≥3）时，两两权重互异的齐次多类线性预测类的Natarajan维数精确值为n(k-1)-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Natarajan维数是多类假设类容量与泛化能力分析的核心度量，但齐次多类线性预测类的Natarajan维数精确值此前尚不明确。

Method: 通过构造并证明下界与上界，确定权重两两互异的齐次多类线性预测类的Natarajan维数。

Result: 齐次多类线性预测类（各类权重两两互异）的Natarajan维数为n(k-1)-1，适用于n,k≥3。

Conclusion: 该精确刻画为多类线性分类器的容量分析与泛化界推导提供了精确的复杂度度量基础。

Abstract: The Natarajan dimension is a fundamental measure of the capacity of multiclass hypothesis classes and plays an important role in analyzing their generalization ability. In this paper, by establishing lower and upper bounds, we show that the exact Natarajan dimension of homogeneous multiclass linear predictors with pairwise distinct class weights is \(n(k-1)-1\), where $n$ is the dimension of the feature space and $k$ is the number of classes, for \(n,k\ge3\), \(n,k\in\mathbb N\).

</details>


### [361] [Translating KV Caches Between Hybrid-Attention Language Models: MLA Latents, Linear-Attention States and Sparse-Attention Indexers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12062)
*Povilas Kvedaras,Tomas Rasymas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一的KV-cache翻译框架，使目标模型无需重读历史即可从源模型完整上下文继续，覆盖MLA、稀疏注意力indexer键与线性注意力循环状态，并在16–48B混合架构对上验证可达原生质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型接管另一模型的对话通常需重读全部历史，代价高；文本交接虽缩短重读但会丢失内容。已有KV-cache翻译（KV-Lingo）仅适用于GQA，而当前开源前沿模型缓存的是压缩潜变量（MLA）、稀疏注意力indexer键和固定大小线性注意力状态，缺乏对应方法。

Method: 先复现KV-Lingo于GQA模型对，再针对每类缓存对象给出捕获与注入位置及达原生质量的机制：MLA潜变量用逐token线性映射翻译；循环状态用闭式修正复制，或通过重放目标自身递归合成；从源模型第0层MLA潜变量解码token id以保留精确长程回忆。评估两个16–48B混合模型对的双向迁移及一个稀疏注意力目标模型。

Result: 从第0层MLA潜变量解码token id使needle recall由0.09提升至2–32K的0.99、64K的0.83–1.00（为最长训练提示的8倍）；全MLA方向在各项任务指标上处于原生质量的配对置信区间内；同形GDN混合对双向达原生水平（除继承源自源模型的6%拒答）；64K下TTFT较重读缩短18.7与46.8倍；分段多轮迁移保持重预填充水平，朴素重翻译则崩溃；稀疏注意力目标上预测indexer键的召回与目标自身键相当（单种子）。

Conclusion: KV-cache翻译可推广至MLA、线性注意力状态与稀疏indexer键等现代缓存对象，在多个混合架构对上实现近原生质量的上下文接管，并显著降低首token时延；作者以假设形式给出向300B+混合旗舰模型迁移的展望。

Abstract: A model that takes over another model's conversation must normally re-read the whole history; a text hand-off shortens the re-read only by dropping content. KV-cache translation instead maps the source's cache into the target's own format, so the target continues from the full context without re-reading it. It works for grouped-query attention (GQA), but open-weights frontier models now cache compressed latents (MLA), sparse-attention indexer keys and fixed-size linear-attention states. We replicate KV-Lingo on a GQA pair and then give, for each of these objects, where to capture and inject it and a mechanism that reaches native quality. We study two hybrid pairs at 16-48B scale in both directions, Kimi-VL-A3B - Kimi-Linear-48B (MLA - MLA + Kimi Delta Attention) and Qwen3.5-27B - Qwen3.8-27B (Gated DeltaNet hybrids), plus a sparse-attention target (Qwen3.8-27B - Qwen3.8-Flash-Next). MLA latents translate with token-wise linear maps; recurrent states are copied with a closed-form correction or synthesised by replaying the target's own recurrence. Exact long-range recall needs token identity: decoding token ids from the source's layer-0 MLA latent raises needle recall from 0.09 to 0.99 at 2-32K and to 0.83-1.00 at 64K, eight times the longest training prompt. The hybrid full-MLA direction is within the paired confidence interval of native quality on every task metric, and same-shape GDN hybrids reach the native level in both directions (apart from 6% refusals inherited from the source), with time to first token 18.7 and 46.8 times shorter than a re-read at 64K tokens. Segment-wise multi-turn transfer stays at the re-prefill level; naive re-translation collapses. On the sparse-attention target, predicted indexer keys recall the needle as well as the target's own keys (one seed). We state as a hypothesis how the recipes transfer to 300B+ hybrid flagships.

</details>


### [362] [XposedMIA: Exploiting Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) for One-Shot Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12067)
*Omar Hamdache,Serdar Kara,Kıvanç Kuzey Dikici,Abdullah Çağlar Öksüz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday,Sinem Sav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XposedMIA：一种基于解释输出的单次黑盒成员推理攻击框架，将LIME局部代理几何及表格/图像反事实解释转化为样本到决策边界的距离代理，经少量校准集一次性校准后，每个目标样本仅需一次查询即可高精度判断其是否属于训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习服务越来越普遍地在预测之外提供可解释性输出，本意是提升透明度，但解释可能泄露训练数据信息。已有成员推理攻击多依赖迭代查询或影子模型训练，成本高；而解释本身与决策边界距离可能存在关联，这一泄露信道尚未被系统研究，尤其是图像反事实解释此前未被利用。

Method: （1）发现LIME所采用的局部替代模型几何结构可被重新用作样本到决策边界距离的代理；（2）将该边界距离视角推广到表格反事实解释，并首次扩展到图像分类器的视觉反事实解释；（3）构建XposedMIA框架：先用少至30个带标签样本做一次性校准，把返回的解释映射为边界距离代理，之后对每个目标样本仅查询一次，仅依赖预测标签及其附带解释，无需迭代查询和影子训练。

Result: 在多种解释类型上评估显示XposedMIA效果显著，单次查询下成功率最高达0.95，与需多轮查询或影子训练的既有仅标签攻击相比具备明显效率与效果优势。

Conclusion: 公开解释输出为成员泄露开辟了额外信道，解释的"透明性"与隐私之间存在实质张力；服务提供方在发布LIME或反事实解释时需评估成员推理风险并考虑相应防护。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) services increasingly expose explainability outputs alongside model predictions. While such explanations are intended to improve transparency, they may also reveal information about the underlying training data. In this work, we show that the local surrogate geometry employed by Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations (LIME) can be repurposed as a proxy for a sample's distance to the model's decision boundary. We extend this boundary-distance view to tabular counterfactual explanations and, for the first time, to visual counterfactual explanations for image classifiers. We present XposedMIA, a one-shot, explanation-based black-box membership inference attack (MIA) framework that converts the returned explanation into a proxy for the queried sample's distance to the model decision boundary. After a one-time calibration step on a small labeled calibration set of as few as 30 samples, our attack requires only a single query for each target sample. Unlike prior label-only attacks requiring iterative queries or shadow training, our method relies solely on the predicted label and its accompanying explanation.
