Our evaluation indicates that XposedMIA achieves high effectiveness, with success rates of up to 0.95 using only a single query per target, revealing that releasing explanations introduces an additional channel for membership leakage.

</details>


### [363] [Correct First, Fast Second: What a Local 7B Code Model Can and Cannot Do for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12079)
*Jui-Chien Tsou,Po-Yi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在30个KernelBench任务上用固定本地7B代码模型比较四种交互策略，发现正确性（而非速度）是瓶颈，最佳策略随难度变化，且朴素的智能体脚手架反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在模型能力固定（Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct）的前提下，仅通过改变交互/脚手架策略能多大程度提升用自定义CUDA内核替换PyTorch算子的效果，即"交互策略"这一变量对代码生成任务的边际贡献。

Method: 在KernelBench level 1-3各前10个任务（共30个，V100评测）上比较四种结构化程度递增的regime：零样本提示、profile引导提示、基于执行反馈的迭代精修、以及由同一模型扮演分析/检索/生成/评估/反馈分诊四角色并配有非LLM评估器的多智能体循环。

Result: (1) 瓶颈在正确性：即使正确的内核也仅达eager PyTorch的0.97-0.99x速度，7B模型无法在稠密GEMM与卷积上超越cuBLAS/cuDNN，唯一真实增益来自融合的memory-bound epilogue；(2) 最佳策略依赖难度：profile引导提示总体最强（43%正确、比基线快27%），但智能体循环是唯一能解出任何level-2融合问题的方法（3/10 vs 其余0/10）；(3) 朴素智能体脚手架有害：初版agent仅3%正确，低于零样本30%，关键修复是让agent野心匹配模型能力，并把算力用于真实执行oracle下的best-of-n采样（n=1到n=8使正确率从3%升至33%），而非增加推理步数。

Conclusion: 在固定小模型条件下，交互策略的收益受限于模型自身代码能力；有效做法是按任务难度选择策略、以执行反馈为oracle做best-of-n采样，并避免过度设计的多智能体推理流程；单纯增加推理步数不如扩大采样与校准任务野心。

Abstract: We study how much the interaction strategy around a fixed, locally hosted 7B-parameter code model (Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct) affects its ability to replace PyTorch operators with custom CUDA kernels. On 30 KernelBench tasks (levels 1-3, first 10 each, evaluated on a V100), we compare four regimes of increasing structure: zero-shot prompting, profiling-guided prompting, iterative refinement on execution feedback, and a multi-agent loop (analysis, retrieval, generation, evaluation, feedback triage) in which the same model fills four roles around a non-LLM evaluator. Three findings stand out. (1) Correctness, not speed, is the binding constraint: even correct generated kernels average 0.97-0.99x the speed of eager PyTorch, because a 7B model cannot out-write cuBLAS/cuDNN on dense GEMM and convolution; the only real wins are fused memory-bound epilogues. (2) The best strategy depends on difficulty: profiling-guided prompting is strongest overall (43% correct, 27% faster than baseline), but the agent loop is the only method that solves any level-2 fusion problem (3/10 vs. 0/10 for all others). (3) Naive agentic scaffolding is actively harmful: our first agent scored 3% correct, below zero-shot's 30%, and the decisive repairs were matching the agent's ambition to the model's competence and spending compute on best-of-n sampling under a real execution oracle (3% to 33% correct from n=1 to n=8), not on more reasoning steps.

</details>


### [364] [Zero-Search Reinforcement Learning for Slay the Spire 2 with Failure-Driven Restarts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12088)
*Xiangqin Chen,Zhengxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练了一个6.6M参数的实体Transformer智能体，单次前向即可为《Slay the Spire 2》每一步做决策且无需前瞻搜索；其采用种子精确回放与失败驱动课程学习，在A10的12800局留出测试中五角色平均胜率8.05%，A0达80.2%与树搜索智能体持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《Slay the Spire 2》最高难度的成功局长达数百步决策，而失败多集中于学习者很少到达的后期Boss战，导致训练信号稀疏、探索困难；同时希望在没有前瞻搜索的轻量模型上达到可用的决策能力。

Method: 用6.6M参数实体Transformer在单次前向中对每个动作做选择，不做前瞻；训练与评估在无头.NET宿主中运行游戏自身编译程序集(v0.107.1)，宿主可按种子与动作精确重放整局，从而从已访问状态重启训练回合；采用失败驱动的课程学习，按完整对局失败位置比例分配这些重启；并做18次训练、6个随机初始化的受控消融。

Result: A10留出12800局：五角色平均胜率8.05%(Ironclad 15.6%、Regent 12.5%)；AlphaSpire已发布网络无搜索为0.55%；延长A10训练使Ironclad 12.4%→15.6%、Defect 3.0%→8.1%。A0为80.2%，与Spire Mentor报告的80.3%持平，但A4以上落后5-8个百分点。同一2000局A10中Mentor搜索8.8%对比本文8.2%，本文单CPU核每决策21-27 ms。GPT-6 Astra在最大推理下11局A10全败。消融显示失败驱动分配在三个Regent初始化上均优于均匀重启分配，平均+3.6个百分点，Silent效应小且符号不定。

Conclusion: 轻量无搜索策略在最高难度已具备可行但有限的胜率，低难度与树搜索相当；失败驱动课程学习在部分角色上带来稳定增益但非普遍有效，代码、策略、mod与全部数据已开源。

Abstract: Slay the Spire 2 (STS2) is a deck-building roguelike in which a winning run at the highest difficulty takes several hundred decisions, and most losses happen in late boss fights that a learner seldom reaches. We train agents that choose every action with one forward pass of a 6.6M-parameter entity transformer, without lookahead. Training and evaluation run the game's own compiled assembly (v0.107.1) in a headless .NET host. The host replays a run exactly from its seed and actions, so training episodes can restart from states the learner has visited, and a failure-driven curriculum allocates these restarts in proportion to where complete runs are lost. On 12,800 held-out games at Ascension 10 (A10), the released policies win 8.05% averaged over the five characters (Ironclad 15.6%, Regent 12.5%), and AlphaSpire's released networks used without search win 0.55% on a common panel. Longer A10 training raised Ironclad from 12.4% to 15.6% and Defect from 3.0% to 8.1% on these games. At Ascension 0 the released policies win 80.2%, level with the 80.3% that the tree-search agent Spire Mentor reports, while Mentor leads by 5 to 8 points from A4 upward. Run in our host on the same 2,000 A10 games, Mentor's search wins 8.8% against 8.2% for our policies, which need 21-27 ms per decision on one CPU core. GPT-6 Astra at maximum reasoning won none of 11 A10 games through our text interface. In a controlled study of 18 training runs from six random initializations, failure-driven allocation exceeds uniform restart allocation on each of three Regent initializations, by 3.6 percentage points on average, while the Silent effect is small and changes sign. Code, trained policies, an in-game mod and the records behind every table are at https://github.com/HalfVulpes/sts2ai.

</details>


### [365] [TSFFormer: A Frequency-Domain Spike Transformer for Green Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12090)
*Jiale Deng,Shuiying Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSFFormer——一种纯脉冲傅里叶Transformer，用于可穿戴IMU的人体活动识别，通过频域全局混合注意力与动态阈值双室LIF神经元，兼顾精度、训练速度与超低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴IMU虽支持普适人体活动识别（HAR），但传统DNN因稠密浮点MAC运算陷入"算术—延迟死锁"；现有SNN存在空间盲区、神经元刚性、梯度退化等问题。

Method: 两项创新：(1) MultiHeadFourier注意力，在频域做全局混合，复杂度由O(V²)降至O(V log V)，消除空间盲区并节省约50% GPU显存；(2) 双室时序脉冲LIF神经元（TSLIFNode），采用动态阈值适应非平稳节律、缓解梯度退化。

Result: 在8个公开IMU基准上，TSFFormer超越SOTA SNN乃至经典DeepConvLSTM，训练速度约提升3倍，能耗足迹0.1 pJ；消融实验验证各模块的协同效应。

Conclusion: 为可穿戴感知提供了一种高精度、超低功耗、事件驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Wearable Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) enable pervasive Human Activity Recognition (HAR), but traditional Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) suffer from an "arithmetic-latency deadlock" due to dense floating-point MAC operations. While Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer ultra-low power, existing ones face spatial blind spots, rigid neurons, and gradient degradation. To address these, we propose TSFFormer, a purely spiking Fourier Transformer for Green HAR. It introduces two key innovations: (1) a MultiHeadFourier attention that performs frequency-domain global mixing, reducing complexity from \(O(V^2)\) to \(O(V\log V)\), eliminating spatial blind spots, and saving \(\sim\)50% GPU memory; and (2) a dual-compartment Temporal Spiking LIF (TSLIFNode) that utilizes dynamic thresholds to adapt to non-stationary rhythms and mitigate gradient degradation. Extensive experiments on 8 public IMU benchmarks demonstrate that TSFFormer surpasses state-of-the-art SNNs and even the classic DeepConvLSTM, achieving a \(\sim\)3\(\times\) faster training speed with an energy footprint of 0.1 pJ. Ablation studies confirm the synergistic effect of our modules, providing a highly accurate, ultra-low-power event-driven paradigm for wearable sensing.

</details>


### [366] [Calibrating Document-Local Boundaries for Contextual Chunk Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12097)
*Yuanxiang Wang,Hui Jiang,Da Zhu,Zhihua Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出校准文档局部块边界的双组件框架：自适应判别尺度 + 块感知注意力路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Late Chunking 通过先编码整篇文档再在块边界池化来保留文档上下文，但共享上下文会使同一文档内语义相似的困难负样本难以区分，需要研究金标准块与其最难兄弟块之间的局部决策边界。

Method: 从 InSeNT 出发分析文档内梯度，证明在固定 logits 下条件负样本分配的熵随缩放因子单调递减；据此设计两部分：(1) 依据文档内 margin 自适应调整判别尺度；(2) 在文档编码时让块划分通过注意力路由暴露给模型（chunk-aware routing）。

Result: 在 ConTEB 与 NanoBEIR 上，模型在所评估的编码器设置下提升了上下文块检索效果，同时保持有竞争力的段落级检索质量；消融实验刻画了自适应缩放与块感知路由各自的不同作用。

Conclusion: 文档内负样本分布熵与判别尺度存在单调关系，据此分别校准尺度与引入块感知路由，可有效改善带上下文检索的块级决策边界。

Abstract: Late Chunking encodes an entire document before pooling at chunk boundaries, preserving document context in local retrieval units. Chunk-level post-training improves context-dependent retrieval, but shared context can make semantically similar same-document negatives difficult to distinguish. We study the local decision boundary between a gold chunk and its hardest sibling. Starting from InSeNT, we analyze InSeNT's within-document gradients and show that the entropy of the conditional negative allocation decreases monotonically with the scaling factor at fixed logits. We propose a two-part framework for calibrating document-local chunk boundaries. The first component adapts the discriminative scale from within-document margins, while the second exposes chunk partitions to attention during document encoding. Experiments on ConTEB and NanoBEIR show that the resulting model improves contextual chunk retrieval across the evaluated encoder settings while retaining competitive passage-level retrieval quality; ablations further characterize the distinct roles of adaptive scaling and chunk-aware routing.

</details>


### [367] [Forecast Value Add: Reward-Based Action Selection over Probabilistic Demand Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12116)
*Ankur Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将预测精化建模为奖励驱动的决策问题，智能体在强ML集成已产出的候选值（基础预测、无分布界、分位数、偏差校正估计）中做上下文匹配选择，并加季节性守卫与贡献审计，5个真实部署上WMAPE较集成降低6.3%（最高16.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强ML集成虽能捕获趋势、季节性与已知事件，但仍会低估峰值、漏掉移动型事件（如移动节假日）、把临时变化误判为需求水平漂移，因而仍需人工/规则"精化"环节，而该环节缺乏可审计、可量化的方法论。

Method: 把预测精化形式化为带奖励的决策问题：每个预测期，智能体仅可在集成既有输出（base forecast、无分布预测界、分位数、偏差校正值）中选一个动作；以日历位置、周期性与移动型事件、趋势、价格、促销等上下文匹配历史相似期来评估动作收益；再施加结构化季节守卫约束调整幅度；并对每个发布值记录模型、智能体、计划员三方贡献以实现可审计。

Result: 在零售、快消与DTC共5个部署、9个月上评估：相对stacked ML预测，所有客户平均WMAPE下降6.3%，最大降幅16.7%；45个客户-月中35个取得最低误差；相对统计预测平均误差下降18.7%。

Conclusion: 在强集成之上，上下文感知且可审计的动作选择仍能带来增量预测价值；精化不必生成新数值，只需在既有候选上做受约束决策，即可稳定降低误差并保持可追溯性。

Abstract: Strong machine-learning ensembles capture much of the trend, seasonality, and known events in demand, yet they can still understate peaks, miss moving events, or mistake temporary changes for shifts in demand level. We address this gap by framing forecast refinement as a reward-driven decision problem. For each forecast period, we let an agent choose among values already produced by the ensemble: its base forecast, distribution-free bounds, quantiles, and a bias-corrected estimate. We evaluate these choices against context-matched historical periods, accounting for calendar position, recurring and moving events, trend, price, and promotions. We then apply a structural seasonal guard to constrain adjustments and record the model, agent, and planner contributions to every published value. We evaluate our approach across five retail, consumer-goods, and direct-to-consumer deployments over nine months. Our refined forecasts reduce mean WMAPE by 6.3% compared with the stacked machine-learning forecast for every customer, with gains reaching 16.7%, and achieve the lowest error in 35 of 45 customer-months. Compared with statistical forecasts, our approach reduces mean error by 18.7%. These results demonstrate that context-aware, auditable action selection can add forecast value beyond a strong ensemble.

</details>


### [368] [CARE-PO: Controllable Tool-Call Efficiency for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12125)
*Xiaoyang Ye,Xiao Yao,Hairui Zhu,Yiying Yang,Bei Gan,Jinliang Lu,Tengjin Weng,Yuan Yao,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-PO通过"任务难度锚定的参考预算 + 成功轨迹注入 + 提示词努力标签"三重设计，缓解效率奖励训练中的锚点漂移、零调用坍缩与单操作点问题，在GAIA多尺寸上同时降低调用数并提升成功率，但跨基准泛化呈现尺寸依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部工具调用是LLM智能体的主要运行成本，而训练目标几乎只对齐任务完成度。作者对Qwen3.5在GAIA上的轨迹做步级诊断，发现多数轨迹含可移除调用，且该现象在两个模型尺寸上比例相近。已有"效率奖励"方法存在三个缺陷：(1) 定价锚点来自策略自身rollout（如组内最短正确轨迹），随策略漂移；(2) 训练易漂向"完全不调用工具"的坍缩策略（已见报道及本文27B基线）；(3) 一次训练只给出单一操作点，用户无法在效率与能力间权衡。

Method: 提出CARE-PO（Controllable Anchored Relative Efficiency Policy Optimization）：1) 效率奖励以任务自身难度确定的参考预算为锚，训练前固定，不随策略变化；2) 在每个在线GRPO rollout组中注入一条来自训练数据的成功轨迹，并对轨迹未采集到的证据计费，以抑制坍缩；3) 在提示词中加入effort tag以塑形成本曲线，使单次训练可获得效率型与能力型两个操作点。

Result: GAIA上，三个模型尺寸的六个操作点中有五个同时优于基座模型的成功率与调用数。在四个留出多跳基准上结果呈尺寸依赖：27B与4B在更低调用数下保持或提升成功率；9B的两个操作点则以成功率换取更少调用。三个尺寸在训练全程调用数均显著高于零。

Conclusion: 以离线固定的难度锚替代随策略漂移的组内锚，配合成功轨迹注入与未获证据计费，可稳定约束效率优化的退化行为；effort tag使单次训练覆盖帕累托前沿上的多个操作点。局限性在于跨基准收益不稳健、依赖模型规模，更大范围内的效率—能力权衡仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: External tool calls are a major running cost of an LLM agent, while its training objectives align almost exclusively with task completion. In a step-level diagnosis of Qwen3.5 trajectories at two model sizes on GAIA, a long-horizon tool-use benchmark, we find that most contain removable calls, at a similar rate at both sizes. Some methods add an efficiency reward to the objective to reduce the call count, but introduce problems of their own: the anchor they price calls against is computed from the policy's own rollouts (e.g., the group's shortest correct rollout) and so moves with the policy; training can drift toward a collapsed policy that issues no tool call at all, a degeneracy seen in published reports and in our 27B baseline; and a training run delivers a single operating point, so the user cannot trade efficiency against capability. We present CARE-PO (Controllable Anchored Relative Efficiency Policy Optimization) to mitigate these problems. The efficiency reward prices calls against a reference budget anchored to the task's own difficulty and fixed before training. Injecting a successful trajectory from the training data into each online GRPO rollout group and charging for evidence a trajectory never gathers ease the drift toward a collapsed call count. Shaping the cost curve by an effort tag in the prompt yields an efficiency and a capability operating point from a single training run. On GAIA, five of the six operating points across three model sizes improve on their base model in success rate and call count at once. On four held-out multi-hop benchmarks the result is size-dependent: at 27B and 4B success rate is held or raised at a lower call count, while at 9B both operating points trade success rate for fewer calls. The call count remains well above zero throughout training at all three sizes.

</details>


### [369] [One-Shot Federated Graph Learning via High-Fidelity Proxies and Transferability-Guided Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12131)
*Jia Jiang,Zihan Tan,Wenke Huang,Bin Yang,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedHFT，一个单轮个性化联邦图学习（OPFGL）框架，从“高保真知识外化”与“可迁移性感知的知识整合”两个角度缓解单传输瓶颈，并在同配与异配图上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮联邦图学习（OFGL）将协作压缩为一次传输，造成“单传输瓶颈”：一是将私有图蒸馏为可共享代理时的激进一次性压缩引发保真度危机，扭曲本地图拓扑与语义，导致外化知识表达不足；二是服务器端聚合代理的整合方案缺乏可迁移性感知，无法识别跨客户端协作潜力，导致整合知识利用不足。

Method: 提出 FedHFT 框架，包含两个组件：保真度驱动的代理精炼（Fidelity-Driven Proxy Refinement），用于保持客户端知识保真度；以及可迁移性引导的个性化协作（Transferability-Steered Personalized Collaboration），实现服务器端自适应协作与知识整合。

Result: 在同配图与异配图上开展的大量实验验证了 FedHFT 的优越性；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过同时提升知识外化保真度与整合时的可迁移性感知，FedHFT 有效缓解了 OFGL 的单传输瓶颈，为带宽受限下的个性化联邦图学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: One-shot Federated Graph Learning (OFGL) has emerged as a communication-efficient paradigm for federated graph learning under strict bandwidth constraints, compressing collaboration into a single transmission round. Nevertheless, this extreme setting suffers from a fundamental Single-Transmission Bottleneck, which limits both the quality and utility of exchanged knowledge. First, while existing methods attempt to distill private graphs into shareable proxies, their aggressive one-shot compression inherently triggers a fidelity crisis. This compression distorts local graph topology and semantics across clients, leading to externalized knowledge underexpression. Second, although the server aggregates these proxies to update global models, current integration schemes suffer from transferability agnosticism. The server cannot reliably identify cross-client collaboration potential, thus resulting in integrated knowledge underutilization. To tackle these dual challenges, we propose FedHFT, an effective One-Shot Personalized Federated Graph Learning (OPFGL) framework that addresses this bottleneck from two perspectives, namely High-Fidelity Knowledge Externalization and Transferability-Ware Knowledge Integration. We use Fidelity-Driven Proxy Refinement to preserve client-side knowledge fidelity, and Transferability-Steered Personalized Collaboration to enable adaptive server-side collaboration. The superiority of FedHFT is validated through extensive experiments on both homophilic and heterophilic graphs. The code is available at https://github.com/Jumpy-Juice/FedHFT.

</details>


### [370] [Good Views Are Not Enough: Global Counterexamples for Gaussian-Regularized JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12137)
*Anran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过全局认证的反例表明：在高斯正则化JEPA的全局最优解处，良好的视图（Good Views）并不能保证场景不变性或旋转意义下的唯一性；高斯边际匹配会奖励保留独立重采样的观测噪声，其收益可能超过预测代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（如Good Views）假设经过高斯扩展后的JEPA目标在全局最优处能实现场景唯一性与旋转不变性，但这只是一个未经检验的前提。作者希望检验该唯一性前提是否普遍成立，并厘清"视图共享什么、正则项偏好什么、预测任务能用什么"这三个决定表征学习的因素。

Method: 构造可全局认证（globally certified）的反例：(1) 四状态模型，与整个"仅场景"类别（scene-only class）对比，证明每个全局极小点都会拆分场景，并存在位于其旋转轨道之外的等值伙伴；(2) 512维模型，具备完整场景基、谱间隙与精确总体白化，证明 pooled Epps-Pulley 正则仍迫使全局极小点保留冗余变量（nuisance）。此外在匹配512标签读出预算、1%观测噪声下比较白化预测JEPA与下一词元预测，并提出基于保留信息的贝叶斯风险分解。

Result: 四状态模型中所有全局极小点均拆分场景且存在旋转轨道外的等值解，唯一性前提失效；512维模型中pooled Epps-Pulley正则下即使取最优旋转，在模态冗余独立时跨模态对齐误差仍为正。经验上，白化预测JEPA对场景的对齐优于下一词元预测，但线性读出测试NLL高出0.2872 nats/token——即更好的场景对齐可能伴随更差的预测。贝叶斯风险分解可区分观测信道造成的信息损失与读出拟合难度带来的差异。

Conclusion: 高斯边际匹配可奖励保留独立重采样的观测变化，且该收益可超过预测成本，因此Good Views的高斯扩展唯一性前提在指定软目标下不成立。表征质量由三者共同决定：视图间的共享信息、正则项所偏好的信息、以及预测任务实际可利用的信息；场景对齐与预测性能并不必然一致。

Abstract: Good views do not suffice to guarantee scene invariance or uniqueness up to rotation at the global optima of Gaussian-regularized JEPAs. We give globally certified counterexamples showing that the uniqueness premise of the Gaussian extension in Good Views fails for specified soft objectives. Gaussian marginal matching can reward retaining independently resampled observation variation, and this benefit can exceed its prediction cost. In a four-state model, comparison against the entire scene-only class proves that every global minimizer splits a scene and admits an equal-value partner outside its rotation orbit. In a 512-dimensional model with a complete scene basis, a spectral gap, and exact population whitening, pooled Epps-Pulley regularization still forces every global minimizer to retain nuisance and yields positive cross-modal alignment error even after the best rotation when modality nuisances are independent. Better scene alignment can also accompany worse prediction. With matched 512-label readout budgets and 1% observation noise, whitened prediction JEPA aligns better to the scene than next-token prediction but incurs 0.2872 nats/token higher linear-readout test NLL. A Bayes-risk decomposition distinguishes information lost through the observation channel from the fitted readout's difficulty using retained information. These results connect three determinants of learned representations: what views share, what regularization favors, and what a prediction task can use.

</details>


### [371] [Visible Tails: Characterizing Adaptation to Unknown Moments in Heavy-Tailed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12140)
*Tuan Ngoc Do,Hai Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画了在重尾、矩参数未知的bandit设定下，能够以常数因子代价适应未知矩的最优臂分布律，提出"方差核心可见性"条件，并给出分位数截断UCB的实现与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾bandit学习器在缺乏矩参数时面临障碍：携带某臂均值的奖励可能永远不被观测到（均值隐藏在稀有事件中）。本文针对COLT开放问题的剩余部分，研究哪些最优臂分布律允许在未知矩条件下实现常数因子代价的适应。

Method: 提出"方差核心可见性"(variance-core visibility)条件：单侧且平移不变，要求任何稀有事件中隐藏的均值至多是剩余奖励标准误的常数倍。基于此设计一种已知可见性常数的分位数截断UCB指数(quantile-trimmed UCB index)。

Result: 该指数在该类分布上达到中心矩基准(central-moment benchmark)；不存在单一策略能适应所有可见性水平。该指数在每个具有可见最优臂且均值有限的实例上取得对数期望遗憾。在一个无更高矩的显式分布族上，其对数为Δ和常数A的量级为Δ^{-1}e^{A/Δ}，与信息下界同阶，即在单一可见性类内随逆间隙指数增长。

Conclusion: 方差核心可见性精确刻画了可常数因子适应未知矩的最优臂分布类，分位数截断UCB在该类内达到基准与下界匹配，且不存在跨所有可见性水平的统一自适应策略。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed bandit learners without moment parameters face an obstruction: the rewards that carry an arm's mean can stay unseen. We address the remaining part of a COLT open problem by characterizing the optimal-arm laws that permit adaptation to unknown moments at constant-factor cost. The condition, variance-core visibility, is one-sided and translation-invariant: the mean hidden in any rare event is at most a constant multiple of the standard error of the rewards that remain. A quantile-trimmed UCB index that knows the visibility constants attains the central-moment benchmark on this class, and no single strategy adapts across all visibility levels. The same index achieves logarithmic expected regret on every instance with a visible optimal arm and finite-mean rewards. On an explicit family with no higher moment, gap $Δ$ and constant $A$, its logarithmic coefficient and the information lower bound are of order $Δ^{-1}e^{A/Δ}$, exponential in the inverse gap within one visibility class.

</details>


### [372] [Do Time-Series Foundation Models Forecast Index-Futures Returns? A Preregistered Evaluation of Models, Metrics, and Input Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12141)
*Rishabh Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文检验冻结的时间序列基础模型(TSFM)在日内指数期货收益上的"预测能力"是否为增量技能。在预注册评估中，5个零样本模型+Kronos在NQ与ES期货5-120分钟预测上全面劣于no-change基准(160格中0胜,Hansen SPA p=1.000;回顾性120格亦0胜),而若干常用指标(价格相关性、日内RankIC)产生的"技能"可由随机游走与均值回归琐碎规则解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFM在基准数据集上表现出色,易被假定其预测能力可迁移到其他领域。作者希望严格检验:这种"表面预测技能"在低信噪比的日内收益领域是否属于超越no-change基准的增量技能,并揭示某些常用评估指标为何会制造虚假技能。

Method: 1) 预注册评估:E-mini Nasdaq-100(NQ)与E-mini S&P 500(ES)期货,5-120分钟horizon;5个零样本模型共同面板(Chronos-Bolt、Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、Moirai 2.0、TiRex)+ Kronos独立实验;输入表示含原始价格、对数收益、波动率标准化收益、成交量时钟(volume clock)。2) 一次开启的盲测集,共160个"模型-表示-horizon-市场"格,配合Hansen SPA检验;另有回顾性hold-out(120格)作描述性检验。3) 消融:变换感知ridge回归。4) 指标诊断:价格水平相关性、日内RankIC、闭式推导随机游走下的期望日内协方差、符号随机化零分布。5) 波动率对照:HAR与EWMA。6) 强植入合成信号实验(5分钟、30分钟)。

Result: 盲测集:160格中0格优于no-change,SPA检验两市场p=1.000;回顾性hold-out 120格中0胜。输入表示只改变预测幅度不改准确度;变换感知ridge同样无增益。价格水平相关性>0.9996但no-change更高。日内RankIC奖励琐碎的均值回归规则(30分钟+0.35);闭式推导的随机游走期望协方差+符号随机化零分布可复现约95%的观测值。一个模型在5分钟波动率上胜过HAR,但不敌其输入中已含的EWMA。强植入合成信号下模型在5分钟能部分恢复信号,30分钟明显退化。

Conclusion: TSFM在基准层面的成功不应假定可迁移到低信号领域(如日内收益)。常用评估指标(价格相关性、日内RankIC)易被随机游走结构制造虚假技能;严格对照no-change与统计检验下,零样本TSFM在日内指数期货收益上无增量预测技能。

Abstract: We ask whether the apparent forecasting skill of frozen time-series foundation models (TSFMs) on intraday index-futures returns is incremental skill. In a pre-registered evaluation on E-mini Nasdaq-100 (NQ) and E-mini S&P 500 (ES) futures, a common panel of five zero-shot models (Chronos-Bolt, Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, Moirai 2.0 and TiRex), plus separate experiments with Kronos, is compared with a no-change forecast at 5-120 minutes, using native prices, log returns, volatility-standardised returns and a volume clock as inputs. On a blind set opened once, 0 of 160 model-representation-horizon-market cells beat no-change, and a Hansen SPA test gives p = 1.000 on both markets; a later retrospective hold-out, scored as a descriptive check, gives 0 of 120. Input representations change forecast magnitude but not accuracy, and a transformation-aware ridge regression does as well. Several standard measures nevertheless suggest skill. Price-level correlation exceeds 0.9996, but no-change scores higher. Within-day RankIC across forecast origins rewards a trivial revert-to-mean rule (+0.35 at 30 minutes); we derive the expected within-day covariance under a random walk in closed form, and a sign-randomised null reproduces about 95% of the observed value. One model beats HAR for 5-minute volatility, but not the EWMA its input already contains. On strongly planted synthetic signals the models recover part of the signal at 5 minutes, and recovery deteriorates at 30 minutes. Benchmark-level success of TSFMs should therefore not be assumed to carry over to low-signal domains such as intraday returns.

</details>


### [373] [Beyond Flat Walks: Compositional Abstraction for Autoregressive Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12160)
*Kaiwen Bian,Andrew H. Yang,Yusu Wang,Ali Parviz,Gal Mishne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出分层图抽象与由粗到细的生成流程，将图自回归生成拆分为粗化、分词与解码三阶段，系统分析了三个设计轴对生成质量与计算成本的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归图生成器通过扁平的节点-边游走将图线性化，会掩盖大而复杂图中的高阶结构信息，因此需要引入分层结构以保留图的层次性特征。

Method: 提出分层图抽象与由粗到细的生成流水线，分离粗化、分词与解码三个环节，并沿社区粗化、分层分词、顺序随机化三个设计轴进行系统性实验；在固定骨干网络与训练协议、匹配训练时间的条件下，于五个分子与五个非分子基准上进行评估。

Result: 基于遍历的分层方法在分布与结构保真度上一致提升；其V.U.N.成本强烈依赖粗化与随机化策略，当遍历顺序在整个层级上随机化时，社区内随机化下的保真度-V.U.N.权衡显著缓解；分层序列化在全部分子数据集上均取得一致的分布与结构增益，非分子数据集上的增益则取决于所提供层级与目标分布结构的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了分层序列化在何种条件下、以何种方式有利于自回归图生成：保真度收益与层级对齐度相关，而成本可通过全局层级顺序随机化有效降低。

Abstract: Autoregressive graph generators typically linearize graphs through flat node-edge walks, which can obscure higher-order structure in large and complex graphs. We introduce a hierarchical graph abstraction and coarse-to-fine generation pipeline that separates coarsening, tokenization, and decoding. This exposes three design axes: community coarsening, hierarchical tokenization, and order randomization. Across these axes, traversal-based hierarchy consistently improves distributional and structural fidelity, while its V.U.N.\ cost depends strongly on the coarsening and randomization policy. In particular, the fidelity--V.U.N.\ trade-off observed under within-community randomization is substantially reduced when traversal order is randomized across the full hierarchy. With a fixed backbone, training protocol, and matched training time across five molecular and five non-molecular benchmarks, hierarchical serialization yields consistent distributional and structural gains on all molecular datasets, while the non-molecular results further show that these gains depend on how well the supplied hierarchy aligns with structure in the target distribution. Together, these results characterize when and how hierarchical serialization benefits autoregressive graph generation.

</details>


### [374] [Replication of Thermal Comfort Cohort Models: No Support for Improved Thermal Preference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12178)
*Thomas Hirsch,Katja Ceplak,Veronika Windisch,Samina Kadkhoda Masoumali,Matias Quintana,Clayton Miller,Stefano Schiavon,Gerald Schweiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对"群体舒适模型(CCMs)优于个人舒适模型"的原始结论进行方法学审查与两次复现，发现CCMs并不优于简单基线：在F1-micro下与"永远预测多数类"的平凡基线持平，在F1-macro下所有CCMs的中位数得分均低于按热偏好分布随机预测的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人舒适模型(PCMs)需要大量个体数据，群体舒适模型(CCMs)被认为可通过人口学/心理测量特征分组（冷启动）或相似热偏好分组（热启动）来降低数据需求，并已有研究报告了性能增益。作者希望检验这些被报告的增益是否真实有效。

Method: 三部分工作：(i) 对原始研究进行方法学评估；(ii) 使用原始两个数据集之一(DORN：20名受试者、6个月)在修订实验协议下复现；(iii) 使用独立采集数据集(PersonAI：72名受试者、4周)复现。性能指标同时采用原始研究的F1-micro和可反映类别不平衡的F1-macro，并引入平凡的多数类基线与按热偏好估计分布随机预测的基线作对照。

Result: F1-micro下，始终输出多数类的平凡基线与各群体模型表现相当；F1-macro下，所有群体模型的中位数得分均低于按偏好分布随机预测的简单基线。方法学评估发现的关键问题包括：每位受试者及少数类样本量不足、依赖对类别不平衡不敏感的指标、缺少平凡基线、交叉验证策略不当，以及多处信息泄漏。

Conclusion: 结果对原始研究"CCMs能改善热偏好预测"的结论提出质疑：CCMs未能超越简单的类别分布基线，所报告的增益很可能源于实验设计与评估缺陷。后续研究应改进实验设计（如控制热环境条件），以获得覆盖各热偏好类别的充足观测。

Abstract: Cohort comfort models (CCMs) aim to overcome the extensive individual data requirements of personal comfort models (PCMs) by grouping occupants based on demographic and psychometric characteristics (cold start) or similar thermal preferences (warm start). We evaluated the validity of the reported CCM performance gains through (i) a methodological assessment of the original study, (ii) a replication using one of the two original datasets (DORN: 20 participants, 6 months) under a revised experimental protocol, and (iii) a replication using an independently collected dataset (PersonAI: 72 participants, 4 weeks). Classification performance was evaluated using F1-micro, as in the original study, and F1-macro to account for class imbalance. Under F1-micro, a trivial baseline that always outputs the majority class performed on par with cohort models. Under F1-macro, all cohort models achieved lower median scores than a simple baseline that makes random predictions based on the estimated distribution of thermal preferences. In our methodological assessment we identified the following key issues: Limited data per participant and per minority class, reliance on a metric insensitive to class imbalance, missing trivial baselines, inadequate cross validation strategy, and multiple occurrences of information leakage. Our findings challenge the original study's conclusion that CCMs improve thermal preference prediction. CCMs did not outperform simple class-distribution baselines. Future studies should improve experimental designs, including controlled thermal conditions, to obtain sufficient observations across thermal preference classes.

</details>


### [375] [NearFormer: Linear-Size Sparse Attention via Randomized Near Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12186)
*Ali Parviz,Alex Cloninger,Gal Mishne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NearFormer 将全局注意力限制在随机化的近邻(NN)图上，以线性规模 O(|E|+nk) 的注意力支撑实现长程建模，在准确率—可扩展性之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图 Transformer 能捕捉长程依赖，但二次注意力开销使其难以应用于大图。需要一种既保留长程建模能力、又具备线性可扩展性的注意力支撑结构。

Method: 先用高效的随机化构造，在由标签无关的特征或谱几何定义的分箱内进行近似最近邻搜索，为每个节点连接少量近邻，一次性构建 NN 图并固定不变；取固定预算 k，得到线性规模 O(|E|+nk) 的注意力支撑。以这些边作为稀疏全局注意力的支撑（可加可学习注意力偏置），同时保留原图用于局部消息传递。

Result: 在长程图基准与合成结构任务上，相较局部消息传递、稠密注意力与稀疏注意力，NearFormer 呈现出更有利的准确率—可扩展性权衡。

Conclusion: 将全局注意力约束在随机化近邻图上是一种简洁有效的方案：NN 图只需构建一次、规模线性、与标签无关，可在保持原图局部传递的同时提供长程注意力支撑。

Abstract: Graph transformers can capture long-range interactions, but their quadratic attention cost limits their use on large graphs. We introduce \emph{NearFormer}, a graph transformer whose global attention is restricted to a randomized near-neighbor (NN) graph. NearFormer uses an efficient randomized construction to connect each node to a small number of near neighbors from a broader neighborhood defined by a label-independent feature or spectral geometry. For a fixed retained budget \(k\), the resulting attention support has linear size \(\mathcal{O}(|E|+nk)\). The NN graph is built once by within-bin search and then held fixed. We use these edges as sparse global-attention supports, with optional learned attention biases, while preserving the original graph for local message passing. Experiments on long-range graph benchmarks and synthetic structural tasks show that NearFormer provides a favorable accuracy--scalability trade-off relative to local message passing, dense and sparse attention.

</details>


### [376] [Harnessing Vision-Language Models for Federated Unsupervised Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12187)
*Jian Liang,Kuangpu Guo,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedUR，首个利用 CLIP 零样本能力的联邦无监督视觉识别框架，通过类别感知数据选择的伪标签机制、负伪标签与类别权重以及个性化提示聚合，在标签级与特征级分布偏移下均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习现有无监督研究主要集中于聚类和自监督表示学习，而联邦场景下的无监督视觉识别（unsupervised visual recognition）这一关键问题尚未被探索；同时本地客户端数据无标签且存在数据异构（non-IID）问题。

Method: 1）FedUR 借助预训练视觉-语言模型（如 CLIP）的零样本能力实现识别；2）采用类别感知的数据选择策略的伪标签机制应对数据异构：先估计全局伪标签分布，再据此将伪标注数据分配给客户端；3）引入类别权重与负伪标签策略，缓解传统正伪标签的不确定性；4）提出个性化提示聚合协议：通用视觉提示在服务器端聚合，编码本地知识的个性化文本提示保留在客户端。

Result: 大量实验表明，在标签级（label-level）与特征级（feature-level）分布偏移两种设置下，FedUR 均稳定优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FedUR 为联邦无监督识别建立了首个框架，并认为该工作为该方向的进一步研究奠定了基础；代码将在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a promising research paradigm that enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without data sharing. To handle unlabeled data in local clients, existing unsupervised FL research has primarily focused on tasks like clustering and self-supervised representation learning, leaving the critical problem of unsupervised visual recognition largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce FedUR, the first framework designed for unsupervised recognition by leveraging the zero-shot capabilities of pre-trained vision-language models like CLIP. Specifically, FedUR addresses data heterogeneity through a pseudo-labeling mechanism with a class-aware data selection strategy. This strategy first estimates a global pseudo-label distribution, then allocates pseudo-labeled data to clients accordingly. To handle the uncertainty of conventional positive pseudo-labels, FedUR further incorporates class weights and a negative pseudo-labeling strategy. Furthermore, FedUR proposes a personalized prompt aggregation protocol where general visual prompts are aggregated on the server, and personalized text prompts encoding local knowledge are retained on clients. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedUR consistently outperforms baseline methods under both label-level and feature-level distribution shifts. We believe this work paves the way for further advances in federated unsupervised recognition. Code will be available at {github.com/tim-learn/FedUR/}}.

</details>


### [377] [A Geometric Invariant of Attention Predicts Head Specialization from Weights Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12206)
*Miguel P. Bento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个标量不变量 ρ，用于度量 Transformer 各注意力头与旋转位置编码（RoPE）的耦合能力，从而在无需前向传播的情况下分析注意力头；ρ 可预测归纳头，并可指导选择性位置插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 注意力机制内含连续对称性，但现有机制可解释性分析（如归纳头识别）普遍依赖前向传播与激活统计，代价高且依赖数据。作者希望建立一种数据无关、可从参数直接计算的注意力头分析框架。

Method: 从注意力机制的连续对称性出发推导标量不变量 ρ，量化每个头与 RoPE 的耦合容量；在四个无偏置 RoPE 模型（135M 至 7B）上计算 ρ，并与归纳头得分做 Spearman 相关性分析；进一步以 ρ 排序筛选位置头，仅对前 50% 位置头应用 YaRN 进行概念验证。

Result: ρ 与归纳头之间存在稳定负相关，Spearman 相关系数在 −0.55 至 −0.74 之间；仅对 ρ 排名前 50% 的位置头施加 YaRN，其困惑度退化小于对所有头均匀施加 YaRN。

Conclusion: ρ 提供了一种仅依赖参数、无需前向传播的注意力头分析工具，为现代 LLM 的数据无关机制分析开辟新方向，并在位置插值等实际场景中具备应用价值；当前 YaRN 实验为初步概念验证，尚需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Transformers are built with continuous symmetries in the attention mechanism. We derive $ρ$, a scalar invariant that measures each head's capacity to couple to rotary positional encodings. This framework allows us to analyze heads without forward passes, opening a new research direction into data-independent analysis of modern LLMs. Empirically, we find that $ρ$ predicts induction heads, the most-studied circuit type in mechanistic interpretability, with Spearman correlation between $-0.55$ and $-0.74$ across four bias-free RoPE models spanning $135$M to $7$B parameters. As a preliminary proof of concept, we use $ρ$ to apply YaRN to only the top-50% positional heads, which degrades perplexity less than uniform application. Code is available at https://github.com/MPedraBento/rho-invariant.

</details>


### [378] [An Empirical Evaluation of Quantum Kernel Stability in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12219)
*Sreejonty Chakraborty,Adrita Rahman Tory,Muhammad Aminur Rahaman,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控实验质疑"量子核方法性能受限源于硬件噪声"的通行假设：在数据预处理、PCA特征、数据划分与经典基线完全一致的条件下，对比Pegasos-QSVC与XGBoost，发现量子模型在无噪声后端同样出现分类器退化，其脆弱性源于量子表示本身而非硬件噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核方法已被探索用于网络入侵检测，但其性能不佳通常被归因于当前量子硬件的噪声。作者认为这一归因缺乏严格验证，需要区分"硬件噪声"与"模型/表示内在脆弱性"两种潜在原因。

Method: 在NSL-KDD与ToN-IoT数据集上对Pegasos-QSVC进行受控分析。固定预处理流程、PCA特征、数据划分与经典基线，执行56组量子实验，包括1个精确无噪声statevector后端与3个噪声模拟超导后端；同时以XGBoost作为经典对照，并通过核Gram矩阵分析评估有效秩与条件数。

Result: XGBoost在NSL-KDD与ToN-IoT上分别达98.94%与99.41%准确率，Pegasos-QSVC仅为91.80%与82.98%。量子模型种子间波动显著更大，8次运行退化为退化分类器（4例召回塌缩、4例平凡正类分类器），其中2例发生在无噪声后端。Gram矩阵分析显示即使无噪声，核矩阵仍呈低有效秩与严重病态条件。

Conclusion: 硬件噪声并非分类器退化的必要条件，Pegasos-QSVC的脆弱性内生于所学量子表示。仅报告平均准确率会掩盖操作上不可接受的行为，量子入侵检测研究应报告最差情况性能、种子级波动、类别特定指标，并执行显式退化检验。

Abstract: Quantum kernel methods have been proposed for network intrusion detection, yet their performance limitations are often attributed to noise in current quantum hardware. This study evaluates that assumption through a controlled analysis of Pegasos-QSVC on the NSL-KDD and ToN-IoT datasets. We perform 56 quantum experiments using one exact noiseless statevector backend and three noise-simulated superconducting backends while keeping preprocessing, PCA features, data splits, and classical baselines identical. XGBoost achieves accuracies of 98.94% on NSL-KDD and 99.41% on ToN-IoT, compared with 91.80% and 82.98% for Pegasos-QSVC. The quantum model also exhibits substantially greater seed-dependent variability. Eight runs collapse into degenerate classifiers, including four recall-collapse cases and four trivial-positive classifiers. Notably, two failures occur on the noiseless backend, demonstrating that hardware noise is not a necessary condition for classi-fier collapse. Kernel Gram matrix analysis further reveals low effective rank and severe ill-conditioning even in the absence of noise, indicating intrinsic fragility in the learned quantum representation. These results show that average accuracy alone can conceal operationally unacceptable behavior and highlight the importance of reporting worst-case performance, seed-level variability, class-specific metrics, and explicit degeneracy tests in quantum intrusion detection studies.

</details>


### [379] [Controlling Temporal Pseudo-Label Marginals for Stable Online Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12242)
*Rani Amar,Ofir Lindenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCALAR——一种即插即用模块，通过在短时窗口内用Sinkhorn算法平衡伪标签类别分布以抑制持续测试时适配（CTTA）中的漂移与崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTTA在在线更新中不稳定：早期预测偏差被强化，伪标签逐渐集中于少数类别，导致漂移甚至崩溃。作者将"伪标签类别使用范围收窄"识别为CTTA不稳定的指示器并加以量化。现有方法多通过样本筛选或损失重加权间接处理，未显式约束时间窗口内的类别边缘分布。

Method: SCALAR维护近期预测的缓冲区，用Sinkhorn算法将缓冲区内类别分布与指定目标分布对齐，得到平衡后的软目标用于适配；不改变模型架构，属于可插拔模块。

Result: 未改变每步运行时间；在ImageNet-C上仅增加约10 MB峰值显存。在偏斜、非平稳且分布偏移严重的场景下尤为有效，与基于熵或多样性的现有稳定化方法结合时显著缓解漂移与崩溃。

Conclusion: 显式约束短时窗口内的类别边缘分布是缓解CTTA不稳定的有效且低成本手段，SCALAR可即插即用地增强现有方法。

Abstract: Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) methods update models online using a stream of unlabeled data to overcome distribution shifts encountered at test time. However, CTTA is often unstable, as it can reinforce biases in early predictions and increasingly predict only a few classes, resulting in drift or even collapse. In this paper, we identify narrowing pseudo-label class usage as an indicator of CTTA instability and quantify this behavior. Most existing methods have attempted to address this issue by either selecting reliable adaptation samples or re-weighting the loss to encourage diverse pseudo-labels, but they do not explicitly enforce a class marginal over a temporal window. Instead, we propose Sinkhorn Class-Marginal Regularization (SCALAR), a simple plug-and-play module that controls the pseudo-label distribution over a short temporal window. SCALAR works by maintaining recent predictions in a buffer, balancing their class distribution via the Sinkhorn algorithm to match a specified target distribution, and using the balanced soft targets for adaptation. Without changing the model architecture, SCALAR maintains the per-step runtime and increases the peak allocated GPU memory by only 10 MB on ImageNet-C. Our method is particularly effective on skewed, non-stationary data with severe distribution shifts, where SCALAR significantly alleviates drift and collapse when combined with existing stabilization methods based on entropy or diversity.

</details>


### [380] [A method to automatically discover symbolic local learning rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12248)
*Andrea Perin,Fabio Anselmi,Stephane Deny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可自动发现可替代反向传播的符号化局部学习规则的通用方法，适用于任意深度架构与任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播存在能耗高、生物可信性差等问题，且现有替代方案多为手工设计；作者希望自动化地发现逐层的局部损失函数，使各层梯度的联合作用能够最小化目标全局损失。

Method: (1) 受Penrose图示法启发，构造局部张量变量的单项式字典；(2) 对这些单项式的系数进行回归，构建逐层多项式损失，使其梯度共同最小化全局损失。在小型多层网络的分类、重构与自监督任务上进行验证。

Result: 在全部任务中，发现的规则引起非平凡层间交互，显著降低对应全局损失；方法倾向于选择稀疏的单项式组合，所得局部规则比基于MLP的替代方案更具可解释性。

Conclusion: 该符号化局部规则发现方法可推广至节能深度学习与生物可信学习机制的探索。

Abstract: We present a general method for discovering symbolic local learning rules that can replace backpropagation, applicable, in principle, to any deep learning architecture and task. The method automatically discovers local loss functions specific to each layer by (1) constructing a dictionary of monomials of local tensor variables, inspired by Penrose diagram notation, (2) regressing over coefficients weighting these monomials to build layerwise polynomial losses whose gradients together minimize a target global loss. We test the method on classification, reconstruction, and self-supervised learning tasks with small multilayer networks and minimal datasets. Across all tasks, the discovered rules induce non-trivial interactions between layers, which lead to significant reductions in the corresponding global losses. Furthermore, the method selects sparse combinations of monomials, finding local rules that are more interpretable than those generated by MLP-based alternatives. The potential implications of our method range from energy-efficient deep learning to the discovery of biologically plausible learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [381] [RAIL: Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning for Student-Compatible Teacher Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12251)
*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出表示对齐模仿学习（RAIL），在教师训练阶段就用对比学习构建师生共享的隐表示空间，使教师策略只能在学生可见的表示上学习，从而降低不可消除的模仿差距，无需奖励改造或事后学生微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始感知输入（图像、车载传感器）做强化学习面临高维观测与稀疏奖励难题。常见做法是训练可访问特权状态信息的教师策略，再蒸馏为仅用原始输入的学生策略。但当教师依赖学生无法获取的信息时，精确模仿不可能实现，存在不可消除的模仿差距。现有方法多通过奖励塑形或教师训练后对学生再做强化学习来缓解，属于事后补救。

Method: RAIL 将师生观测的失配问题提前到教师训练阶段解决：使用对比学习学习一个教师观测与学生观测共享的隐表示空间，并直接在该空间中训练教师策略。由于教师策略被约束在共享表示上操作，其学到的行为天然可由学生观测复现，从而在保持任务性能的同时显著压缩模仿差距。

Result: 在多个环境中，RAIL 在无奖励修改、无事后学生微调的条件下优于强基线方法，并超过直接从原始观测进行强化学习的效果；所学表示还支持仅训练教师即可实现对新任务的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 通过在教师训练阶段对齐师生表示而非事后补偿，RAIL 提供了一种简洁有效的特权信息蒸馏范式，兼顾任务性能与可模仿性，并拓展出零样本任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from raw sensory inputs such as images or onboard sensors can be challenging due to high-dimensional observations and sparse rewards. A common strategy is to train a teacher policy with access to privileged state information and then distill it into a student policy that acts from raw inputs alone. However, when the teacher relies on information unavailable to the student, exact imitation may be impossible, creating an irreducible imitation gap. Existing approaches typically mitigate this mismatch through careful reward shaping or additional reinforcement learning on the student policy after the teacher has been trained. Instead, we introduce Representation-Aligned Imitation Learning (RAIL), which addresses the mismatch during teacher training itself. RAIL learns a latent representation shared across teacher and student observations using contrastive learning, and trains the teacher policy directly in this space. Because the teacher policy is constrained to operate on this shared space, it learns behaviors that are reproducible from student observations. This substantially reduces the imitation gap while preserving task performance. Across multiple environments, RAIL outperforms strong baselines without reward modification or post-hoc student fine-tuning, and surpasses direct reinforcement learning from raw observations. The learned representations further enable zero-shot transfer to new tasks through teacher-only training. Project site: https://merajmammadov.com/rail/.

</details>


### [382] [Odds-Structured Distributional Regression for Interpretable Time-to-Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12268)
*Jakob Versnjak,Manuel Welte,Titus Kuehne,Marcus Kelm,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ODDS-DR 范式，使分布回归的输出参数在对数几率（log-odds）上具有可加结构，从而在保持预测性能的同时获得可解释、可按时程归因的风险标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存/事件时间预测受部分可观测性与偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）困扰，例如临床中疾病进展或急性再入院的时间依赖高度动态且潜在的健康轨迹。分布回归可给出时间上的概率分布而非确定性点预测，但模型输出的原始分布参数是抽象数学量，难以与领域概念对齐，解释性差。

Method: 提出 ODDS-DR：将输出参数设计为在对数几率（log-odds，临床风险建模中常用度量）上可加，而非仅追求技术便利；由此获得与领域对齐的表示，并具备支持稳健、随时间变化的特征归因所需的逐层计算结构。

Result: 在两个真实临床队列上验证，预测性能与现有最优方法相当，同时可将分布输出在灵活设定的预后时程上分解为可解释的风险标记。

Conclusion: 通过 log-odds 可加参数化，分布式时间-事件预测可在不牺牲性能的前提下获得领域可解释性与逐层归因能力，为临床风险建模提供兼顾精度与透明性的方案。

Abstract: Time-to-event prediction is fundamentally challenged by partial observability and aleatoric uncertainty. In clinical medicine, for instance, the timing of disease progression or acute readmission is contingent on highly dynamic and largely latent health trajectories. Rather than forcing a deterministic point forecast, distributional regression offers a principled means to address this complexity by predicting a probability distribution over time. However, explaining these distributional predictions presents a key obstacle: raw distribution parameters output by the model are abstract mathematical quantities that do not align well with domain concepts. To gain interpretability, we propose a novel paradigm where output parameters are designed to be additive in log-odds---a widely established measure in clinical risk modeling. Our approach, called ODDS-DR, moves beyond technical convenience to deliver clear domain alignment as well as the layer-wise computational structure required for robust, time-dependent feature attribution. Validated across two real-world clinical cohorts, our framework matches state-of-the-art predictive performance while resolving distributional outputs into interpretable risk markers across flexible prognostic horizons.

</details>


### [383] [Learning Evidence-Grounded Reasoning for Context-Aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12285)
*Xingpeng Gao,Mingyue Cheng,Daoyu Wang,Huajian Zhang,Xiaoyu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastReasoner：一种证据驱动的推理模型，通过结构化CoT从上下文特征中提取预测证据并据此指导自适应预测决策；采用SFT+RL两阶段后训练与收敛引导的数据平衡策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的时间序列预测虽引入上下文特征来直接生成未来值或修正预测先验，但仅"输入特征"并不能保证模型识别哪些特征对预测有效、以及这些特征对未来动态意味着什么。核心挑战在于：如何从上下文特征中提取预测证据，以及何时、如何利用这些证据指导预测决策。

Method: 提出CastReasoner，用结构化思维链（CoT）抽取预测证据、推断证据所暗示的未来动态，并将其与预测先验（forecast prior）进行比对以指导自适应预测决策。训练上设计推理导向的两阶段后训练：(1) SFT阶段通过可控多教师指导合成推理轨迹，并用新颖的"学生感知难度度量"筛选高质量轨迹；(2) RL阶段设计细粒度多目标奖励以同时提升推理与预测。两阶段均引入收敛引导的数据平衡策略以应对跨域异质学习需求。

Result: 在多领域预测基准上进行了大量实验，CastReasoner取得更优的预测性能，同时两阶段训练提升了推理质量与预测精度。

Conclusion: 以"证据驱动推理"为核心、结合高质量轨迹合成与多目标RL后训练，可有效把上下文特征转化为可用的预测证据，验证了显式推理链条在上下文感知时间序列预测中的价值。

Abstract: LLM-driven time series forecasting has become increasingly promising by leveraging contextual features to generate future values directly or refine forecast priors. However, incorporating contextual features alone does not ensure that models can identify which features matter for forecasting and understand what they suggest about future dynamics. We argue that the fundamental challenge is how to extract predictive evidence from contextual features and when and how to leverage it to guide forecasting decisions. In this paper, we propose CastReasoner, an evidence-grounded reasoning model for context-aware time series forecasting. Specifically, CastReasoner uses a structured chain of thought (CoT) to extract predictive evidence from contextual features, infer the future dynamics suggested by this evidence, and assess these dynamics against the forecast prior to guide adaptive forecasting decisions. To enable this capability, we design a reasoning-centric two-stage post-training framework including supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). First, we synthesize reasoning trajectories through controllable multi-teacher guidance and select high-quality ones using a novel student-aware difficulty metric. Second, we design fine-grained multi-objective rewards to further improve reasoning and forecasting. To address heterogeneous learning needs across domains in both stages, we introduce a convergence-guided data balancing strategy. Through this two-stage training, CastReasoner improves both reasoning quality and predictive accuracy. Extensive experiments across multi-domain forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CastReasoner achieves superior forecasting performance.

</details>


### [384] [The Optimal Rate Constant of Heavy-Ball Momentum on Smooth Strongly Convex Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12310)
*Marcos Hernanz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了常步长、常动量系数的heavy-ball方法在光滑强凸函数类上的最优最坏情况收敛速率，证明其形式为 $1-8/\kappa$（维度≥2），即渐近上比梯度下降快四倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定步长与动量参数的heavy-ball方法在二次函数上能加速梯度下降，但在光滑强凸函数类上尚无任何参数选取能实现加速。作者希望确定该方法在此函数类上"究竟能比梯度下降快多少"，即求出所有调参下的最优最坏情况速率。

Method: 将最优最坏情况速率写为 $1-c(\kappa)/\kappa$。下界方面，构造双变量函数，证明任何在二次函数上快于 $1-8/\kappa$ 的调参必在该函数上呈缓慢衰减的螺旋轨迹，从而给出对所有调参成立的下界。上界方面，给出显式调参——动量接近Polyak调参、步长约小 $\sqrt{\kappa}/8$ 倍——并构造Lyapunov函数，用精确算术验证其正定性。

Result: 证明 $c(\kappa)\to 8$，且该结论在每个维度大于等于2时成立；下界适用于所有调参，上界由显式调参与Lyapunov函数给出，二者匹配。

Conclusion: 常参数heavy-ball方法在光滑强凸函数类上渐近比梯度下降快约四倍，但其加速能力存在 $8/\kappa$ 的硬性极限，无法像在二次函数上那样进一步提速。

Abstract: The heavy-ball method with a constant step size and a constant momentum parameter accelerates gradient descent on quadratics, but no choice of the two parameters does so on the class of smooth strongly convex functions. We determine how much faster than gradient descent it can be made on this class. Writing the best worst-case rate over all tunings as $1-c(κ)/κ$, where $κ$ is the condition number, we prove that $c(κ)$ tends to $8$ in every dimension of two or more. The lower bound on the rate holds for every tuning, because a tuning that is faster than $1-8/κ$ on quadratics follows a slowly decaying spiral on some function of two variables. The matching upper bound comes from an explicit tuning, with nearly the momentum of Polyak's tuning and a step size about $\sqrtκ/8$ times smaller, and from a Lyapunov function whose positivity is verified in exact arithmetic. Optimally tuned heavy-ball is therefore asymptotically four times faster than gradient descent on this class.

</details>


### [385] [A Modular Perspective for Modeling MARL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12314)
*Tiziano Maisonhaute,Jean-Christophe Soulié,Fabien Michel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出MARL文献碎片化，提出将MARL系统分解为八个功能模块的模块化视角，并以MARLKIT框架实例化，实现机制的透明建模、可控变化与组合复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习文献日益碎片化，多样实现掩盖了底层架构选择，导致难以在隔离条件下评估关键建模维度、难以识别各组件如何影响系统动态，也难以跨文献比较、复用或组合已有贡献。

Method: 提出模块化视角，将MARL系统分解为智能体、环境、学习、奖励建模、训练与执行模式、通信、他者智能体建模、评价准则八个功能模块；逐模块阐明其作用并综述代表性方法；在MARLKIT框架中实例化该分解，使各模块显式化且可替换。

Result: 实验表明MARLKIT支持机制的原型构建、比较、复用与组合，并使不同配置间的差异显式可见，验证了该视角对透明实验假设与受控实验的支持能力。

Conclusion: 该文为MARL建模与实现提供了结构化视角与共同语言，使建模选择显式化，有助于更清晰地描述MARL方法，并通过系统性隔离与组合机制支撑更受控的实验。

Abstract: The growing complexity of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has fragmented the literature, obscuring underlying architectural choices behind diverse implementations. This fragmentation makes it difficult to evaluate critical modeling dimensions in isolation, identify how components affect the resulting system dynamics, and compare, reuse, or combine contributions across the literature.
  This paper proposes a modular perspective for modeling, analyzing, and implementing MARL systems. We decompose them into eight functional modules: agents, environments, learning, reward modeling, training and execution modes, communication, modeling of other agents, and evaluation criteria. For each module, we clarify its role and review representative approaches.
  We instantiate this decomposition in MARLKIT, a framework whose modules are explicit and interchangeable. MARLKIT demonstrates how the proposed perspective supports transparent experimental assumptions and the controlled variation and combination of MARL mechanisms. Experiments illustrate its ability to prototype, compare, reuse and combine modules while making differences between configurations explicit.
  Overall, the paper provides a structured perspective on MARL modeling and implementation, offering a common language for making modeling choices explicit, supporting clearer descriptions of MARL approaches, and supporting more controlled experimentation through the systematic isolation and combination of MARL mechanisms.

</details>


### [386] [How Many Exposures Does a World Model Need? Learning Unseen Game Mechanics at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12324)
*Ku Onoda,Haruto Kawano,Shota Takashiro,Kohei Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MechEval 评估范式并构建 WARC（35M 参数视觉 Transformer 世界模型），研究世界模型预测新环境机制需要多少次"暴露"，在 ARC-AGI-3 上以极少交互实现高精度机制预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型能让智能体在想象经验中规划与学习，但在陌生环境中必须从有限交互中习得其"机制"（动作/交互改变环境的规则）。核心问题是：世界模型需要多少次机制暴露才能预测新环境的机制效果？现有缺乏可量化评估这一问题的基准与标注。

Method: 1）在 ARC-AGI-3（由程序定义的交互游戏基准）上提出 MechEval，依托游戏程序标注 20 款游戏中的 69 个反复出现的机制；2）每个预测按每次暴露中机制所改变的具体格子评分，而非整帧；3）构建 WARC：35M 参数视觉 Transformer，在其他游戏上预训练，每次状态转移后更新全部参数（测试时训练）。

Result: 留出游戏上，WARC 能对五分之四的机制在"其改变的格子"上达到≥80% 准确率，并保持至记录结束；其中一半机制在约 80 次暴露内（单局游玩）达标，而不做参数更新时仅七分之一能达到。闭环游玩公开游戏时，WARC-Agent（WARC + 固定图探索器）将官方效率分从 0.15 提升至 0.46，提升主要来自测试时训练；Kaggle 隐藏游戏榜得分 0.20。

Conclusion: 在陌生环境中，测试时参数更新（而非仅预训练先验）是少量暴露下快速习得新机制的关键；机制级、格子级评分能更精准刻画世界模型的样本效率，且该能力可直接转化为下游探索效率提升。

Abstract: World models enable agents to plan and learn from imagined experience, but in an unfamiliar environment they must learn its mechanics from limited interaction. A mechanic is a rule for how an action or an interaction changes part of the environment. Each occurrence of a mechanic that the model observes is one exposure. We ask how many exposures a world model needs to predict the effects of a new environment's mechanics. We address this question on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark of interactive games defined by their programs. We introduce MechEval, which labels 69 recurring mechanics across 20 games from these programs. It scores each prediction on the cells the mechanic changes at each exposure. As a test bed, we build WARC (World model for ARC-AGI-3), a 35M-parameter vision transformer that is pretrained on other games and updates all of its parameters after each transition. On held-out games, WARC learns to predict four in five mechanics with at least 80% accuracy on the cells they change and keeps that accuracy to the end of the recordings. Half of them reach that accuracy within about 80 exposures, in a single play of the game, and without updates only one in seven does at all. In closed-loop play on the public games, WARC-Agent, which pairs WARC with a fixed graph explorer, triples the explorer's official efficiency score from 0.15 to 0.46, mostly through test-time training. On the hidden games of the Kaggle leaderboard, our entry scores 0.20.

</details>


### [387] [Evidence or Marginal? What Moment-Vector Propagation Computes in Polynomial Density Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12339)
*Przemysław Spyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对神经元在输入丢失或以信念 q 已知时的输出问题，比较常分母近似 p_CD = E_q[N]/E_q[D] 与精确期望比 p_EU = E_q[N/D]，并给出事后解读、证明与（部分失败的）预注册检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hierarchical Correlation Reconstruction 用单价矩阵-向量乘积的 p_CD 近似 p_EU，但二者的语义差异与近似代价缺乏系统论证，需要一个解释框架来判定“神经元该输出什么”。

Method: 将 p_CD 解释为以信念为虚拟证据的贝叶斯更新、p_EU 解释为 Jeffrey 规则下安装新边缘，并给出事后证明，在最多 k=8 输入的非 pair 家族上验证；另做一次探索性真实数据试点，以及最多 32 输入、15 条假设的预注册合成研究，对比截断效应与 gap。

Result: 事后闭式解落在全部 112 个测量 gap 的 2 倍因子内，负密度约在输入相关性 1/k 附近出现；预注册合成研究中 15 条假设仅 5 条支持、2 条不确定、8 条不支持（含全部三条关于超越等相关高斯情形的迁移假设）；Beta 信念下 pair 神经元通常截断效应大于 gap，三重神经元在平坦与数据信念下例外。

Conclusion: p_CD 与 p_EU 分别精确对应不同问题，缺失输入时只有 p_CD 退化到模型条件分布；但该解读主要为事后性，预注册检验支持有限，泛化到更一般相关结构与高输入维度尚不成立。

Abstract: When a neuron's input is lost, or known through a belief $q$, what should it output? For a neuron storing a joint polynomial density $N$ with input density $D$, two formulas compete. $p_\mathrm{CD}=E_q[N]/E_q[D]$ costs one matrix-vector product; Hierarchical Correlation Reconstruction introduces it as a constant-denominator approximation to $p_\mathrm{EU}=E_q[N/D]$. For a density model both are exact answers to different questions: $p_\mathrm{CD}$ is Bayesian updating with the belief as virtual evidence; $p_\mathrm{EU}$ installs the belief as a new marginal (Jeffrey's rule). With a missing input $p_\mathrm{CD}$ returns the model's conditional; $p_\mathrm{EU}$ generally does not. This post hoc reading is proved, and illustrated outside the pair family up to $k=8$ inputs; it has no pre-registered test, only one exploratory real-data pilot. Post hoc, closed forms fall within a factor 2 of all 112 measured gaps, and negative density begins near input correlation $1/k$. In a pre-registered synthetic study (up to 32 inputs), 5 of 15 hypotheses are supported, 2 inconclusive and 8 not supported, including all three on transfer beyond equicorrelated Gaussians. Post hoc, where $p_\mathrm{EU}$ is defined, truncation usually outweighs the gap for Beta beliefs in pair neurons; triple neurons under flat and data beliefs are exceptions.

</details>


### [388] [Optimal Rates for Adaptive Private $k$-PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12419)
*Johanna Düngler,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在 d 和 k 上均达到最优样本复杂度的差分隐私随机 PCA 算法，同时保留噪声自适应与任意目标维数 k 的可扩展性，并去除样本量对特征间隙的指数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作 kdppca（Dungler 2025）虽首次同时实现 n=Õ(d) 样本复杂度、隐私噪声自适应数据内在随机性、并支持任意 k≤d，但其样本复杂度对 k 的依赖次优；此外既有效用保证要求最小样本量对特征间隙呈指数依赖。

Method: 放弃 kdppca 的紧缩（deflation）策略，改为对完整 d×k 子空间进行联合更新，而非逐个特征向量更新；采用非紧缩结构以简化算法、减少超参数。

Result: 样本复杂度在 d 与 k 上同时最优；消除最小样本量对特征间隙的指数依赖；谱参数依赖性得到改进，在 spiked covariance 模型下与已知下界匹配；计算效率提升（尤其利于块线性代数库）。

Conclusion: 联合更新整体子空间的非紧缩框架在保持 kdppca 优势的同时，实现了更优的理论保证与更简洁高效的算法，为差分隐私 PCA 提供了更紧的效用-隐私权衡。

Abstract: Given $n$ i.i.d. random matrices $A_i \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$ with common expectation $Σ$, the goal of \emph{Differentially Private Stochastic PCA} is to identify a $k$-dimensional subspace capturing the leading variance directions of $Σ$, while preserving differential privacy~(DP) for each individual sample $A_i$. \citet{dungler2025iterative} introduced \kdppca, the first algorithm to simultaneously (1) achieve sample complexity $n=\widetilde O(d)$ for sub-Gaussian data, (2) adapt its privacy noise to the intrinsic randomness of the data, and (3) extend seamlessly to any target dimension $k\le d$. However, its sample complexity has a suboptimal dependence on $k$. We propose the first algorithm that achieves optimal sample complexity in both $d$ and $k$, while retaining properties (2) and (3) of \kdppca. Additionally, prior utility guarantees require a minimum sample size with exponential dependence on the eigengap. Our method removes this dependence. It also improves the dependence on spectral parameters, matching known lower bounds for the spiked covariance model. Unlike deflation-based approaches like \kdppca, our algorithm updates the full $d\times k$ subspace jointly rather than one eigenvector at a time. This non-deflation structure simplifies the algorithm, reduces the number of hyper-parameters, and improves computational efficiency, particularly when using block linear algebra libraries.

</details>


### [389] [Two-Phase Rapid Simulation-Based Inference with Differentiable Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12422)
*Uros Zivanovic,Rahul Srinivasan,Roberto Trotta,Andre Scaffidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RSBI，一种基于扩散与薛定谔桥的免似然贝叶斯推断变分方法，用可微模拟器的距离度量构造替代似然，一次性给出高效提议分布，再由神经似然比校正的薛定谔桥采样器细化到真实后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大先验-后验体积比（prior-to-posterior volume）下，传统序贯式免似然推断（SBI）采样效率低，需多轮顺序提议，且易出现 prior leakage（先验泄漏）、多峰后验覆盖不足。

Method: 1) 利用可微模拟器定义距离度量，得到初始替代似然，构造一次性高效提议分布，替代多次序贯轮次；2) 基于薛定谔桥扩散采样器，从该提议热启动，处理多峰后验；3) 用仅在提议有质量区域学习的神经似然比修正采样器能量，将提议输运至由模拟器与原先验定义的真实后验；4) 可选引入 well-tempered meta-dynamics 以提升模态覆盖与先验空间探索；作为变分方法避免先验泄漏。

Result: 在标准 SBI 基准上表现强劲，尤其当先验体积相对真后验增大时；在 Two Moons 上、模拟预算严格受限条件下性能仅下降约 5%。并在受黑洞光谱学启发的引力波 ring-down 后验估计这一真实物理基准上进行了评估。

Conclusion: RSBI 通过“距离度量替代似然一次性提议 + 薛定谔桥细化 + 局部神经似然比校正”的组合，在大先验体积与紧模拟预算下实现高效、抗先验泄漏、模态覆盖良好的免似然后验推断，并可扩展到真实物理问题。

Abstract: We introduce Rapid Simulation Based Inference (RSBI), a diffusion-based variational approach to likelihood-free Bayesian inference that achieves high sampling efficiency under large prior-to-posterior volumes. RSBI builds on advances in Schrödinger Bridge (SB) diffusion sampling to handle multi-modal posteriors, with an initial likelihood supplied by a distance measure via a differentiable simulator. The key innovation of this initial surrogate likelihood is that it gives a highly efficient proposal distribution, replacing sequential rounds of other methods with an efficient one-shot proposal. Subsequent refinement then transports the proposal to the true posterior as specified by the simulator and the original prior. The SB sampler is warm-started from the proposal, and its energy is corrected by a neural likelihood ratio that is learned only where the proposal places mass. As a variational method, RSBI does not suffer from prior leakage commonly observed in standard sequential posterior estimation methods. To improve mode coverage we optionally utilize the well-tempered meta-dynamics framework, which also encourages exploration of the prior volume. We observe strong performance on standard SBI benchmarks, particularly when the prior volume is increased relative to the ground truth posterior, achieving a performance degradation of only $\sim5\%$ on Two Moons under a highly constrained simulation budget. Additionally, we evaluate RSBI's performance for gravitational wave ring-down posterior estimation, in a real-world physics benchmark inspired by black hole spectroscopy.

</details>


### [390] [UCB-min: Combining Bandits Under Variance Heterogeneity (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12427)
*Lilia Izri,Benoit Groz,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出UCB-min：一种逐臂自适应选择「方差无关」与「方差感知」探索奖励中较紧者的元算法，无需已知方差或奖励归一化，理论上界与两基算法之和/逐臂最小值同阶，实验中始终跟随最优基算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机广泛用于推荐、临床试验、教育题推荐、在线广告，其性能取决于探索策略。现有策略分为方差无关（UCB1，稳健但方差小时界过松）与方差感知（UCB-V，仅在方差估计与支撑上界可靠时紧）。实际中各臂奖励方差未知且差异大、奖励支撑常未明确给定，此时不存在一致最优的单一规则；多目标标量化成单一奖励等场景更会带来异质未知方差，故需要自适应机制。

Method: 提出UCB-min——逐臂（per-arm）自适应元算法：对每个臂分别计算方差无关与方差感知两类探索奖励，取其中更紧（更小）的一个作为该臂的实际探索奖励，从而在不需要先验方差信息、也不做奖励归一化的前提下自适应切换方差机制。理论分析以UCB-V作为方差感知基算法，分两种探索常数情形给出遗憾上界证明；实验在合成数据、教育辅导任务，以及MovieLens与Steam两个群体推荐基准上验证。

Result: 理论结果：在标准探索常数下，UCB-min的遗憾被两基算法界之和所界定；在较大探索常数下，被两者的逐臂最小值（至多相差一个常数）所界定。实验结果表明，UCB-min在多种方差机制与数据集上都能一致地逼近（track）对应场景下的最优基算法。

Conclusion: UCB-min以极小代价获得了「稳健性」与「紧致性」的兼得：无需先验方差知识与奖励归一化，即可在异质方差环境中自适应获得接近最优基算法的性能，为方差未知场景下的探索策略选择提供了统一的元算法框架。

Abstract: Multi-armed bandits are a natural framework for many real-world sequential decision problems, from recommender systems and clinical trials to educational test recommendation and online advertising. The performance of these algorithms depends on how they explore different arms, and exploration strategies range from variance-independent (e.g., UCB1) to variance-aware (e.g., UCB-V). In practice the reward variance of each arm is rarely known and varies widely across arms, and the reward support is often underspecified. In these cases no single rule is uniformly best: variance-independent methods are robust but loose when variances are small, while variance-aware methods are tight only when their variance estimates and the assumed support bound are reliable. Such heterogeneous, unknown variances arise in many settings, for instance when multiple objectives are scalarized into a single reward. We propose UCB-min, a per-arm adaptive meta-algorithm that selects, for each arm, the tighter of a variance-independent and a variance-aware exploration bonus, without prior knowledge of the variance regime and without reward normalization. We prove that, with UCB-V as variance-aware base, its regret is bounded by the sum of the two base bounds at the standard exploration constant, and by their per-arm minimum, up to a constant, for a larger one. We validate it on synthetic data, an educational tutoring task, and two group-recommendation benchmarks (MovieLens, Steam), showing that UCB-min consistently tracks the best base algorithm across diverse variance regimes and datasets.

</details>


### [391] [Patching Up Circuit Learning via Parameter Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12436)
*Charles Bricout,Gennaro Gala,Viacheslav Borovitskiy,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了概率电路(PC)中假设图像块独立性反而能取得更优似然的"可学习性问题"，并提出递归参数共享构造(RePA)及其推广(NoPA)，在不假设独立性的前提下大幅降低时间与内存开销并保持有竞争力的似然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率电路虽保证可处理的概率推断，但代价是模型规模远大于其他生成模型。为将PC扩展到图像等高维数据，以往工作采用参数共享以及在独立图像块上学习PC的快速评估协议。作者重新审视这一做法，发现一个反直觉的可学习性问题：假设独立性的模型反而能得到比不假设独立性模型更好的似然，说明现有做法存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出递归参数共享构造(RePA)，在共享参数、提升效率的同时不假设独立性，以突破上述瓶颈并进一步扩展PC规模；受此启发，将该方法推广到本身不具备块结构的数据模态(如表格数据)，即NoPA。

Result: 实验表明RePA能显著减少时间与内存消耗，同时取得有竞争力的似然；NoPA在七个表格数据集上带来类似的效率提升，似然与样本质量的下降仅为边际水平。

Conclusion: 假设独立性的快捷做法虽出人意料地提升似然，但构成扩展瓶颈；RePA/NoPA通过递归参数共享在不牺牲性能的情况下实现高效扩展，且可推广到非块结构数据。代码已开源。

Abstract: Probabilistic circuits (PCs) have emerged as generative models guaranteeing tractable probabilistic inference. However, tractability comes at the price of larger model sizes than in other generative models. As such, to scale PCs to high-dimensional data such as images, previous work has devised several parameter sharing techniques as well as fast evaluation protocols that consider learning PCs on independent image patches. In this work, we revisit this practice to highlight a surprising learnability issue: models that assume independence counterintuitively can yield better likelihoods than models that do not. To break this apparent bottleneck and to scale PCs further, we introduce a recursive parameter sharing construction (RePA) that fosters efficiency without assuming independence. We show empirically that RePA can greatly reduce time and memory consumption while yielding competitive likelihoods. Inspired by these results, we further generalize the approach to data modalities that do not inherently exhibit patch-structure (NoPA), such as tabular data, showing similar efficiency improvements, with marginal changes in likelihoods and sample quality on seven tabular datasets. Code available at https://github.com/april-tools/RePA.

</details>


### [392] [Learning to Select Sparse Linear Solvers with Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12475)
*Artemis Pados,Alan Edelman,Emmanuel Lujan,Daniel Pickard,Felipe Tome,Christopher Rackauckas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的稀疏线性系统自动求解器选择：以矩阵非零结构的spy图为输入，回归候选求解器间的对数时间比，在留出SuiteSparse矩阵与实际高性能仿真矩阵上均取得较高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏线性求解器的性能随矩阵稀疏结构差异极大，传统人工规则或特征工程难以准确刻画这种结构依赖，因此需要一种能直接从矩阵结构图像中学习选择策略的自动方法。

Method: 将矩阵非零结构可视化为spy图作为CNN输入；模型输出为候选求解器之间运行时间的对数比值（log timing ratio）回归目标，从而以相对性能而非绝对时间进行建模，避免绝对时长的尺度与噪声问题。

Result: 在留出的SuiteSparse矩阵集合以及来自实际高性能仿真代码的真实矩阵上，模型均表现出较强的预测准确度，验证了CNN可直接从结构图像中捕捉与求解器性能相关的稀疏模式。

Conclusion: spy图+CNN的端到端回归可作为稀疏求解器自动选择的有效手段，兼具实用性与跨数据源的泛化能力，为后续引入更多候选求解器与更大规模矩阵的扩展奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate convolutional-neural-network (CNN) based automatic solver selection for sparse linear systems, a problem of practical importance where solver performance varies dramatically with matrix sparsity structure. A CNN is trained to regress log timing ratios between candidate solvers using spy plot images of matrix nonzero structure as input, achieving strong prediction accuracy on both held-out SuiteSparse matrices and real-world matrices from high-performance simulation codes.

</details>


### [393] [Out-of-the-Box Tabular Foundation Models for Bayesian Optimization in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12491)
*Klemens Flöge,Simon Bing,Leo Grinsztajn,Eliott Kalfon,Lennart Purucker,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在材料科学主动学习的两个标准基准上，评估表格基础模型 TabPFN 与 TabICL 作为免调参替代代理模型，与高斯过程、随机森林、CatBoost 等大量基线比较，发现其竞争力很强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在材料科学主动学习中广泛使用，但事实标准的高斯过程代理模型需要依赖任务的核函数选择，且对复杂关系的建模能力有限；表格基础模型（TFM）在结构化数据预测上已是先进水平且无需定制核或超参数调优，因此有必要考察其作为代理模型的适用性。

Method: 将 TabPFN 和 TabICL 两个表格基础模型作为开箱即用的代理模型，在两个标准材料科学基准数据集上进行评估，并与覆盖高斯过程、随机森林和 CatBoost 的大规模基线集合进行对比；TFM 不针对主动学习任务做专门训练，也不进行 GP 核选择。

Result: TFM 在两个基准上均表现出高度竞争力的代理模型性能，且无需任务特定的主动学习训练或 GP 核选择，即可与上述基线相匹敌。

Conclusion: 结果支持将表格基础模型进一步发展为材料发现中主动学习的代理模型。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely used in active learning loops for materials science. Gaussian processes serve as the de-facto surrogate model family, but require task-dependent kernel choices and can lack the modeling capacity for complex relationships. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have recently emerged as the state-of-the-art for predictive tasks on structured data, without requiring bespoke kernels or hyperparameter tuning. We evaluate two TFMs, TabPFN and TabICL, as out-of-the-box surrogate models on two standard materials science benchmarks, comparing against a large field of baselines encompassing Gaussian processes, random forests, and CatBoost. Our findings indicate that TFMs are highly competitive surrogate models across the two benchmarks, without task-specific training for active learning or GP kernel selection. Our results motivate developing TFMs further as surrogates for active learning in materials discovery.

</details>


### [394] [UniHam: A Large-Scale SOC-Complete Dataset and Benchmark for Hamiltonian Learning in Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12509)
*Yuewen Huang,Pin Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了 UniHam——一个含 10 万+ 全 SOC 复数厄米哈密顿量的大规模数据集与基准套件，并提出三维度评估指标，揭示现有模型在 SOC 与元素相关场景下的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测电子哈密顿量可在规避 DFT 高计算成本的同时实现广泛性质推断，但现有哈密顿量数据集规模小、结构多样性不足，且常缺失自旋—轨道耦合（SOC）等关键相对论物理，构成通用材料基础模型发展的数据瓶颈。

Method: 构建 UniHam 数据集与基准：10 万+ 由 DFT 计算的复数厄米哈密顿量，包含完整 SOC，覆盖 72 种元素及多样晶格类型与空间群族。在标准化协议下评测两个代表性 SOTA 模型，并提出联合评估三维度的互补指标：(i) 哈密顿量重构精度；(ii) 成分/对称性分布偏移下的 OOD 泛化；(iii) 基于预测哈密顿量的下游性质预测能力。

Result: 实验表明该基准与指标能有效区分模型能力，揭示出内在的 SOC 依赖与元素依赖失效模式、成分 OOD 鲁棒性的显著差异，以及谱级评估对于判断哈密顿量预测是否可靠支撑下游电子结构性质的必要性。

Conclusion: UniHam 提供了可复现、SOC 完备的基准，有助于细化模型比较并加速下一代量子材料基础模型的研发。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of electronic Hamiltonians would enable broad property inference while avoiding the high computational cost of Density Functional Theory (DFT). However, progress toward general-purpose materials foundation models is limited by a data bottleneck: existing Hamiltonian datasets are typically small, lack structural diversity, and often omit essential relativistic physics such as spin--orbit coupling (SOC). We therefore construct UniHam, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset and benchmark suite comprising 100,000+ DFT-computed complex-valued Hermitian Hamiltonians with full SOC, covering 72 elements and a wide range of crystal geometries and symmetries (spanning diverse lattice types and space-group families). Building on UniHam, we benchmark two representative state-of-the-art models under a standardized protocol and introduce complementary evaluation metrics that jointly assess three dimensions: (i) Hamiltonian reconstruction accuracy, (ii) out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization across composition/symmetry shifts, and (iii) the ability to support downstream property prediction from the predicted Hamiltonians. Experiments on UniHam demonstrate that the proposed benchmark and metrics effectively differentiate model capabilities, revealing intrinsic SOC- and element-dependent failure modes, large variations in compositional OOD robustness, and the necessity of spectral-level evaluation to assess whether Hamiltonian predictions reliably support downstream electronic-structure properties. Overall, UniHam provides a reproducible, SOC-complete benchmark that can sharpen model comparisons and accelerate the development of next-generation foundation models for quantum materials.

</details>


### [395] [Can the Confidence and Decisions of System 1 Models Be Trusted? An Explainability and Perturbation Audit of Jev-Style Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12521)
*Julien Delaunay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放、确定性的审计基准，系统评测"System 1"决策模型（单次前向返回带类型概率分布）在标定、跨问题干扰与事后归因忠实性上的表现，并与生成式System 2基线对比，得到五项发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System 1决策模型已被用于低延迟路由与自动分诊，但其统计标定与事后可解释性尚缺乏刻画，需要公开、可复现的审计手段。

Method: 构建确定性审计基准，覆盖保标签扰动下的标定、跨问题干扰、事后归因忠实性三个维度；评测5个开源权重模型（0.8B–27B）与生成式System 2基线（单token logprob打分、结构化JSON/CoT解码）；任务含e-SNLI、HANS、合成临床分诊（N=900）、Tensor Trust提示注入防御（N=250）及86例逻辑门触发套件；并提出基于logit链接的S1-SHAP归因方法。

Result: 五项发现：(1) 模型置信度实为闭式离散度变换（<10B模型为1−H(p)/ln K，Clef-27B为归一化margin），并非经验准确率；(2) 单请求打包多问题引发有方向的跨问题干扰（Tev1-0.8B二分类翻转11.7%–22.4%，Tev1-4B仅0.1%–0.2%）；(3) ≥4B的System 1模型在句法偏移下更快且标定更优（ECE_w=0.030–0.087），优于logprob（0.144）与JSON（0.378）基线；(4) 提示注入防御中≥4B模型达0.988–0.998 AUROC，召回最高95.2%、零误报，耗时48–60 ms；(5) 单次推理使512联盟XAI可交互（1.3–3.2 s/输入），S1-SHAP缓解softmax饱和，OR门恢复率89.3%–100.0%（概率空间57.1%–82.1%，p_adj=0.004）。

Conclusion: System 1决策模型在≥4B规模上具备良好的标定鲁棒性、延迟优势与可交互归因能力，但其置信度需按离散度变换而非准确率解读；打包多问题会造成显著干扰，S1-SHAP等logit空间归因对析取结构更忠实。

Abstract: "System 1" decision models (pioneered by TypeSafe AI's Jev) replace autoregressive text generation with a single forward pass returning typed probability distributions over declared schemas (Choice, Score, Noul). While deployed for low-latency routing and automated triage, their statistical calibration and post-hoc explainability remain uncharacterized. We introduce an open, deterministic audit benchmark evaluating System 1 decision models across calibration under label-preserving perturbations, cross-question interference, and post-hoc attribution faithfulness. Evaluating five open-weight models (0.8B to 27B) alongside generative System 2 baselines -- both single-token logprob scoring and structured JSON/CoT decoding -- across NLI (e-SNLI), adversarial syntactic shortcuts (HANS), synthetic clinical triage (N=900), prompt injection defense (Tensor Trust, N=250), and an 86-case logic-gate trigger suite yields five findings: (1) provider confidence is an exact closed-form dispersion transform (1 - H(p)/ln K on sub-10B models; normalized margin on Clef-27B) rather than empirical accuracy; (2) bundling multiple questions in one request induces directional cross-question interference (11.7% to 22.4% binary flips on Tev1-0.8B, vs. 0.1% to 0.2% on Tev1-4B); (3) System 1 models with >= 4B parameters are faster and better calibrated under syntactic shifts (ECE_w = 0.030 to 0.087) than logprob (0.144) and JSON (0.378) baselines; (4) for prompt-injection defense (N=250), >= 4B models achieve 0.988 to 0.998 AUROC with up to 95.2% recall and 0.0% false alarms at 48 to 60 ms; and (5) single-pass inference makes 512-coalition XAI interactive (1.3 to 3.2 s/input), while KernelSHAP with a logit link (S1-SHAP) mitigates softmax saturation on disjunctive OR gates (89.3% to 100.0% gate recovery vs. 57.1% to 82.1% in prob space; p_adj = 0.004).

</details>


### [396] [K-prop: Deep Online Learning by Backpropagating Temporal Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12530)
*Bingkun Liu,Paul Haider,Federico Benitez,Mihai A. Petrovici,Walter Senn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K-prop，一种用于漏积分（leaky）循环神经网络的在线学习方法，通过传播“核增益”显著降低RTRL的计算与内存开销，性能优于现有RTRL近似并在多类任务上接近BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自循环/漏积分是神经网络时间处理的核心机制，但由于梯度中复杂的时序依赖，缺乏高效且有效的在线训练方法；RTRL虽可在线学习但代价过高。

Method: 提出K-prop在线学习算法：以传播kernel gain（核增益）的方式替代RTRL中完整雅可比矩阵的实时传播，从而大幅削减计算与内存复杂度；并对其唯一近似进行理论/实验分析，刻画其保持准确的适用条件。

Result: 在计算与内存开销上显著低于RTRL；性能优于已有RTRL近似方法；在序列学习、分类和强化学习任务上达到与BPTT相当的表现；并识别出K-prop保持梯度质量与有效性的条件。

Conclusion: K-prop为漏积分循环网络提供了一种高效、可在线运行的训练方案，在精度与代价之间取得良好折衷，其近似误差可控且适用范围明确。

Abstract: Self-recurrence represents an important component for temporal processing in neural networks, both artificial and biological, where it appears naturally in the form of leaky integration. However, due to the complex temporal dependencies of the gradient, an efficient and effective method of online training is still lacking. Here we propose K-prop, a novel online learning method for leaky neural networks. It dramatically reduces the computational and memory cost of real time recurrent learning (RTRL) by propagating kernel gains. K-prop also outperforms existing RTRL approximations and achieves performance comparable to backpropagation through time in sequence learning, classification, and reinforcement learning tasks. We further analyze how its sole approximation affects gradient quality and identify conditions under which K-prop remains accurate and effective.

</details>


### [397] [LawKAN: Group-Law Layers for Composition -- When Imposing a Known Law Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12567)
*Anna Mishchenko,Nikolai Gavrilin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"嵌入已知群结构、只学习残差"的层设计：节点处放置精确非交换群律，边学习局部增量，并用零初始化修正项ψ刻画硬件偏差，配合可预测"精确可表示性"的预训练判据。在SU(2)固定角复合任务上，54参数稠密边为最准确稠密模型，参数比最强固定矩阵基线少27倍、距贝叶斯下界仅0.05°；在真实机械臂上306参数ψ达到1.12–1.14 mm，优于Gauss-Newton的1.29–1.33 mm（N≥60），并跨三厂商两种协议迁移；当数据不服从所施群律时性能劣于经典拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多任务的变换由已知规则复合（如机器人与导航中的链式旋转）。纯学习模型需重新学得该规则，导致误差累积；经典估计器则把规则硬编码，无法吸收硬件偏差。作者希望同时利用已知结构（降低样本与参数需求、抑制误差累积）并保留对未知偏差的建模能力。

Method: 将精确的非交换群律作为"节点"置于一个自包含层内；层"边"只学习局部增量（如54参数稠密边）；引入零初始化修正项ψ学习群律的未知偏差（如306参数孤立ψ）。同时给出预训练判据，用于预测目标变换是否可被该结构精确表示。

Result: 固定角SU(2)复合：54参数稠密边为最准确稠密模型，参数比最强固定矩阵基线少27倍，距贝叶斯下界仅高0.05°。真实机械臂：孤立306参数ψ在N≥60时达1.12–1.14 mm，优于Gauss-Newton的1.29–1.33 mm；方法可跨三个厂商、两种协议迁移。在数据服从群律时匹配经典拟合，在任务不满足所施群律时性能下降。

Conclusion: 显式施加已知群结构、仅学习偏差残差，可在极小参数下达到接近贝叶斯下界的精度并优于经典估计器；其收益以"所施群律与任务一致"为前提，结构失配时不及经典拟合，因此预训练可表示性判据是必要的适用性检查。

Abstract: Many tasks compose transformations by a known rule: chained rotations in robotics and navigation. Learned models relearn it, compounding error; classical estimators hardcode it and miss hardware deviations. We impose the known structure and learn only the remainder: an exact non-commutative group law sits in the node of a self-contained layer, its edges learn local increments, and a zero-initialized correction psi learns unknown deviations of the law. A pre-training criterion predicts exact representability. On fixed-angle SU(2) composition, a 54-parameter dense edge is the most accurate dense model, at 27x fewer parameters than the strongest fixed-matrix baseline, and lies 0.05 deg above the Bayes floor. On real robot arms, an isolated 306-parameter psi reaches 1.12-1.14 mm versus 1.29-1.33 mm for Gauss-Newton at N >= 60, and the recipe transfers across three vendors and two protocols. The recipe matches classical fits where the data obey the law and loses where the imposed law is not the task's.

</details>


### [398] [From Likelihood Convergence to Parameter Convergence in POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12575)
*Jack Zhang,Saurabh Amin,Jiawei Zhang,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对POMDP参数恢复，给出参数恢复误差与似然间隙、观测/转移核的结构条件及行为策略之间的显式界，覆盖欠完备与过完备两种情形，从而为MLE及任意低似然间隙估计器提供有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于似然的方法广泛用于学习POMDP参数，但已有工作多关注策略次优性的有限样本保证，参数恢复的有限样本理论仍不充分；且无限时域POMDP的近最优学习现有方法依赖精确的转移与观测核估计。

Method: 建立参数恢复误差上界：将任意候选POMDP参数的恢复误差用其相对真实模型的似然间隙、观测核与转移核的结构条件数（conditioning）以及数据采集所用行为策略来表达；先分析欠完备（undercomplete）情形，过完备（overcomplete）情形由欠完备分析自然导出。

Result: 得到对任意足够好候选参数均成立的恢复误差界，故可直接推出MLE以及任何似然间隙受控过程的有限样本保证；该界将行为策略与模型结构的贡献分离，显示动作覆盖等数据采集选择及其混合（mixing）效应对加速参数学习的作

Conclusion: 似然间隙、核的结构条件与行为策略共同决定POMDP参数恢复精度；合理设计行为策略（含动作覆盖与混合性）可加速学习，并为无限时域POMDP的近最优学习提供理论基础。

Abstract: Likelihood-based methods are widely used to learn the parameters of partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs). While finite-sample guarantees for policy suboptimality exist for certain POMDP subclasses, analogous guarantees for parameter recovery remain less well understood. For any candidate POMDP parameter, we bound its parameter recovery error in terms of its likelihood gap to the ground truth, the structural conditioning of the observation and transition kernels, and the behavior policy used to collect data.
