The bound applies in both the undercomplete and overcomplete regimes, with the latter following naturally from the undercomplete analysis. Because the result applies to any sufficiently good candidate, it yields finite-sample guarantees for the maximum-likelihood estimator and any procedure producing a parameter with controlled likelihood gap. This is particularly useful for near-optimal learning in infinite-horizon POMDPs, where existing approaches largely rely on model-based planning with accurate transition and observation kernel estimates. The bound separates the contributions of the behavior policy and model, showing how data-collection choices, including action coverage, and their effects on mixing can accelerate parameter learning.

</details>


### [399] [TRACE: Adaptive Trust-Region Bayesian Optimization for Open-Ended Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12591)
*Madhav R. Muthyala,Mahsa Askari,Farshud Sorourifar,Evan Zhou,Joe Gauthier,Joel A. Paulson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE：一种开放式贝叶斯优化框架，用无目标图扰动按需生成分子，并以Tanimoto相似度阈值和基数预算显式控制化学探索范围，由成功/失败控制器自适应伸缩；六个基准上样本效率最优，并在FePc电催化剂上将Fe溶解热力学阻力从0.42 eV提升至2.59 eV（80次评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小评估预算下的分子优化需同时决定"在化学空间何处搜索"和"在该区域内评估哪个候选"。现有方法多从固定库中选取候选，且将搜索几何本身交由学习，导致探索尺度不透明、可控性差。

Method: TRACE由三部分组成：(1) 无目标图扰动机制，按需生成候选分子；(2) 固定、化学感知的高斯过程代理模型，采用fragprints（分子指纹与片段描述符的组合）表示；(3) 显式探索控制——以Tanimoto相似度阈值控制化学范围、以基数预算控制候选搜索广度，由简单的成功/失败控制器在优化过程中自适应扩张或收缩两者；GP与采集函数在可行分子中做最终选择。另构建面向精确能量评估的第一性原理DFT工作流用于FePc案例。

Result: 在六个挑战性分子优化基准、统一1000次评估预算下，TRACE按累积最优分子的样本效率总体最强；两个目标上的交叉消融表明，自适应区域控制的增益超越提议机制本身，但其收益依赖提议分布。FePc应用中，80次设计评估内将Fe溶解的计算热力学阻力由0.42 eV提升至2.59 eV。

Conclusion: 显式、可自适应调制的化学探索尺度控制是一种有效且与提议分布互补的分子BO设计原则；该方法可迁移至真实DFT评估的非贵金属分子电催化剂稳定性优化。

Abstract: Optimizing molecules under small evaluation budgets requires deciding both where in chemical space to search and which candidate within that region to evaluate. We introduce Trust Region Adaptive Chemical Exploration (TRACE), a framework for open-ended molecular Bayesian optimization (BO), where candidates are generated on demand rather than selected from a fixed library. TRACE combines an objective-free graph-perturbation mechanism with a fixed, chemistry-aware Gaussian process (GP) surrogate using fragprints, a representation combining molecular fingerprints and fragment descriptors. Rather than learning the search geometry itself, TRACE makes the scale of chemical exploration explicit through a Tanimoto-similarity threshold controlling chemical extent and a cardinality budget controlling candidate-search breadth. A simple success/failure controller expands or contracts both quantities during optimization, while the GP and acquisition function select which admissible molecules to evaluate. Across six challenging molecular optimization benchmarks under matched 1,000-evaluation budgets, TRACE achieves the strongest overall sample efficiency as measured by the best molecule found as evaluations accumulate. Crossed ablations on two objectives further show that adaptive region control contributes beyond the proposal mechanism, while its benefit depends on the proposal distribution. We further apply TRACE to the stability of iron phthalocyanine (FePc), a non-precious molecular electrocatalyst whose degradation can involve loss of the Fe center. Using a first-principles density functional theory (DFT) workflow designed for accurate energy evaluation, TRACE increases the computed thermodynamic resistance to Fe dissolution from 0.42 to 2.59 eV over 80 designed evaluations.

</details>


### [400] [Simulation-aided Reinforcement Learning with Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12643)
*Omer Hemo,Ido Sefi,Mirco Mutti,Aviv Tamar,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于控制变量（control variates）的保守式仿真辅助强化学习方法，将仿真数据仅用于降低学习过程的方差而非直接塑造学习目标，并扩展到策略评估、actor-critic 策略改进与离线 RL 场景，在多个 MuJoCo 基准任务上优于标准仿真混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 依赖目标环境中采集的数据，成本高且噪声大；仿真器虽可大量生成数据，但存在不准确性。标准的"仿真混合"方法（直接把仿真经验与真实经验合并）会因仿真偏差使解偏离最优策略，从而损害性能。因此需要一个既能利用仿真数据又不被其偏差误导的方案。

Method: 核心思想：用控制变量法重构仿真数据的使用方式，让仿真数据仅作为方差缩减项、而非学习目标的直接塑造项，从而保持目标估计无偏（或保守）。首先针对策略评估（policy evaluation，RL 的基本构件）开发该方法；随后嵌入 actor-critic 框架用于策略改进；并进一步扩展至离线 RL 设定。

Result: 在多个 MuJoCo 基准任务上的实验表明，该方法相较标准仿真混合（simulation mixing）方法取得性能提升，验证了控制变量作为仿真辅助 RL 有效工具的价值。

Conclusion: 将仿真数据作为控制变量做方差缩减，而非直接混合进目标，是规避仿真器偏差、稳健利用仿真数据的有效途径；该思路可自然衔接策略评估、actor-critic 策略改进与离线 RL，具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms often rely on data collected from a target environment, which can be expensive and noisy. Although simulators can generate large amounts of additional data, standard simulation mixing approaches, which directly combine simulated and real experience, can degrade performance due to simulator inaccuracies, especially when they bias the solution away from the optimal policy. We propose a conservative simulation-aided learning method based on control variates, where simulated data is used solely to reduce variance in the learning process rather than directly shaping the learning target. We develop our approach for Policy-Evaluation, which is a fundamental building block of RL algorithms. Then we combine it within the actor-critic framework towards policy improvement, as well as extend it towards offline RL scenarios. Experiments on multiple MuJoCo benchmark tasks demonstrate improvements over standard simulation mixing approaches, highlighting control variates as an effective tool for simulation-aided reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [401] [Certify, Report, or Refuse: Harm-Constrained Conformal Prediction for Clinical Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12662)
*Zhiyuan Li,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的伤害约束风险最优保形预测(HC-ROCP)，以冻结分类器概率加伤害惩罚选择预测集与动作，在多重性校正下认证有支持的声明、拒绝无支持的亚组声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠诊断覆盖率无法排除将黑色素瘤病例路由至常规随访这类严重行动错误，需要一种在使用预测集时显式约束伤害的决策框架，并对各组声明给出可信的统计认证。

Method: HC-ROCP为免训练框架：利用冻结分类器的概率输出，在构建预测集与选择动作时加入伤害惩罚项；在不相交的校准划分上进行检验并做多重性校正；输出包括有认证支持的声明、描述性回退策略，以及对无支持亚组声明的拒绝。

Result: 回顾性评估于COVID-19胸片、ISIC 2019皮肤镜与EyePACS-to-APTOS糖网迁移；十个校准种子下，COVID-19严重行动错误由精确风险最优基线的4.68%降至0.54%；5%预算下COVID-19与ISIC的全局声明获认证，罕见鳞状细胞癌声明被拒绝；1%预算下所有设置仅得描述性报告。另给出各预算下以零错误通过全局伤害门所需的最少严重病例数。

Conclusion: 在风险最优决策中显式引入伤害惩罚并对声明做多重性校正认证，可显著降低严重行动错误，并诚实区分可认证与不可认证的声明；所需最小严重病例数指标为后续数据收集提供起点。

Abstract: Routing a melanoma case to routine follow-up is a serious action error that diagnostic coverage alone does not rule out. We present Harm-Constrained Risk-Optimal Conformal Prediction (HC-ROCP), a training-free framework that uses frozen classifier probabilities and adds a harm penalty when choosing prediction sets and actions. We test the resulting policies on disjoint calibration splits with multiplicity adjustment. HC-ROCP certifies supported claims, reports descriptive fallback policies, and refuses unsupported subgroup claims. We evaluate these outputs retrospectively on COVID-19 radiographs, ISIC 2019 dermoscopy, and EyePACS-to-APTOS retinopathy transfer. Across ten calibration seeds, HC-ROCP reduces COVID-19 severe-action errors from the exact risk-optimal baseline's 4.68\% to 0.54\%. At a 5\% budget, global claims are certified for COVID-19 and ISIC, but the rare squamous-cell-carcinoma claim is refused. At 1\%, all settings receive descriptive reports. For each budget, we also report the minimum severe-case count needed to pass the global harm gate with zero errors, providing a starting point for further data collection.

</details>


### [402] [Trade Promotion Optimisation for Consumer Goods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12665)
*Ankur Verma,Namrata Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端促销优化（TPO）系统，将相似SKU迁移、点弹性估计、弹性曲线、价格推荐、促销提升与净提升串成一条链；在39个SKU的品牌四周实验中，方案使销量提升20.6%、收入提升6.9%且毛利持平，相似SKU池化将弹性误差较层级平均降低一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 促销投入占快消品收入很大份额，但多数计划者仍用单一且往往不可靠的逐品价格弹性来定折扣，缺乏可靠的弹性估计与系统性优化手段。

Method: 构建全流程TPO体系：促销后阶段用相对滚动常规价的方向性降价事件测弹性；促销期用因果估计器对趋势、季节性、促销做残差化，并以Tobit似然处理缺货删失，从首个干净促销周起自更新；证据弱的SKU通过交叉验证岭回归（基于品类、品牌、子品类、价格带、描述）匹配相似SKU，借用点弹性与曲率；优化器分离自身价格提升、替代效应与促销后回落，并对库存与渠道资金做调整。

Result: 在39个SKU、平均弹性-3.06的消费品品牌四周促销实验中，方案使销量提升20.6%、收入提升6.9%、毛利持平；相似SKU池化相对层级平均将弹性误差减半。

Conclusion: 端到端串联的弹性估计与优化框架能有效提升促销的销量与收入表现，且相似SKU借用信息显著改善弱证据SKU的弹性精度，为替代单一弹性假设提供了可落地的方案。

Abstract: Trade promotions consume a large share of consumer-goods revenue, yet most planners still set discounts with a single, often unreliable, price elasticity per item. We present an end-to-end Trade Promotion Optimisation (TPO) system built as one chain: analogue SKUs, point elasticity, elasticity curve, price recommendation, promotional lift and net lift. Elasticity is measured at post-promo time from directional price-dip events against a rolling regular price, and at promo time by a causal estimator that residualises trend, seasonality and promotion, handles stock-out censoring with a Tobit likelihood and updates itself from the first clean promotion week. Items with weak evidence borrow the point elasticity and the curvature from analogue SKUs chosen by a cross-validated ridge model over category, brand, sub-category, price line and description. The optimiser separates self-price lift from substitution and post-promo dips and adjusts for inventory and trade funding. In a four-week promotion experiment on a 39-SKU consumer brand (mean elasticity -3.06), the plan lifts units by 20.6% and revenue by 6.9% at flat margin. Analogue pooling halves elasticity error relative to hierarchy averaging.

</details>


### [403] [Controlled Binary Assignment Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12668)
*Paul Fröhling,Christoph Schnörr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何方法，用于表示与学习深度赋值流的时变参数化，以完成图上的二值数据标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深�度赋值流的参数空间是高维对称流形，参数化与学习困难；需要一种可解释、可并行化的几何局部合成方式来表示时变参数轨迹。

Method: 每条边承载一个在2维全测地子流形上的最优控制参数流，其目标函数由输入数据的非局部线性尺度空间表示导出；将三个此类流聚合到5维全测地子流形中并从数据学习；通过流的融合确定高维对称参数空间中的轨迹，并借助几何积分构造二值深度赋值流的各层。

Result: 数值实验验证了该高并行几何局部合成方法对高维深度赋值流参数化的表达能力，同时揭示了其局限性。

Conclusion: 基于全测地子流形的局部参数流合成可有效表示深度赋值流的时变参数化，具有高度并行性与可解释性，但在表达高维参数化方面存在固有局限。

Abstract: This paper introduces a geometric approach to the representation and learning of time-variant parametrizations of deep assignment flows, for binary data labelings on graphs. Each edge carries an optimally controlled parameter flow in a 2-dimensional totally geodesic submanifold, based on objectives derived from a non-local linear scale space representation of the input data. The aggregation of triplets of such flows in 5-dimensional totally geodesic submanifolds is learned from data. The fusion of these flows determines the trajectory in the high-dimensional symmetric parameter space and by geometric integration the layers of binary deep assignment flows. Numerical results demonstrate the expressivity and the limitation of our highly parallel geometric local synthesis approach to high-dimensional deep assignment flow parametrizations.

</details>


### [404] [CoilStellaration: A Dataset and Benchmark for Engineering-Aware Stellarator Coilset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12689)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewers,Santiago A. Cadena,Nicolò Foppiani,Andrea Merlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoilStellaration——一个用于学习"目标等离子体磁场与工程约束 → 线圈几何"映射的数据集与基准，包含 177,000 组优化的细丝线圈组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器线圈设计是高度非线性、无闭式解且解不唯一的逆问题，是仿星器设计的核心难题。现有研究缺乏标准化数据与评测，机器学习与优化研究者进入门槛高，阻碍了低成本、可制造的线圈几何探索。

Method: 构建数据集：基于 ConStellaration 的等离子体平衡，生成 177,000 组优化后的细丝线圈组，采样工程需求并记录达成的几何与磁场指标；覆盖多种线圈数与场周期数，支持跨构型的专用模型与共享模型研究。设立基准任务：模型以目标平衡及线圈-线圈间距、线圈-等离子体间距、曲率、磁场误差等需求为条件预测线圈几何。发布基线模型与评测代码。

Result: 磁场精度仍是主要挑战，学习模型很少能同时满足全部约束；但将学习预测作为传统线圈优化的初始化，可加速优化前期的收敛进展。

Conclusion: CoilStellaration 为直接线圈生成与学习式优化初始化提供了可复现的研究平台，降低了机器学习与优化领域研究者参与仿星器线圈设计的门槛，有望加速实用、经济可行的仿星器电站研发。

Abstract: Stellarators are fusion devices that use externally generated, three-dimensional magnetic fields to confine hot plasma and offer a promising route to commercial fusion. Their electromagnetic coils pose a challenging design problem: they must reproduce the target magnetic field while satisfying engineering constraints and remaining practical and cost-effective to manufacture. Finding suitable geometries is a highly nonlinear inverse problem that generally has neither a closed-form nor a unique solution. This problem lies at the heart of stellarator design. We introduce CoilStellaration, a dataset and benchmark for learning the mapping between coil geometries from target plasma magnetic fields and engineering requirements. The dataset contains 177,000 optimized filamentary coilsets targeting plasma equilibria from ConStellaration, with sampled engineering requirements and achieved geometric and magnetic-field metrics. It spans multiple coil counts and field-period numbers, supporting studies of both specialized and shared models across configurations. The benchmark asks models to predict coils conditioned on a target equilibrium and requirements on coil-coil and coil-plasma spacing, curvature, and magnetic-field error. We show that magnetic-field accuracy remains the main challenge, with learned models rarely satisfying all requirements simultaneously. However, using learned predictions to initialize conventional coil optimization can accelerate early progress. We release baseline models and evaluation code to support reproducible research on both direct coil generation and learned optimization initializations. By lowering the barrier for machine learning and optimization researchers to engage with stellarator coil design, we aim to accelerate progress toward practical, cost-effective stellarator power plants.

</details>


### [405] [Agnostic Marginal-Nonuniform Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12694)
*Tanmay Devale,Steve Hanneke,Amirreza Shaeiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在 agnostic 设定下研究边际非均匀(marginal-nonuniform)学习框架中的二分类问题，给出标准 agnostic 与松弛 agnostic 两种准则下的最优速率及其精细刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: realizable 设定中，边际分布的信息可带来更快的学习速率；作者希望厘清在 agnostic 设定下，允许常数依赖无标签数据边际分布是否仍能改善保证，并刻画精细的复杂度依赖关系。

Method: 在边际非均匀框架下，要求速率常数可依赖无标签数据的边际分布、但保证对所有标签条件分布一致成立。分别分析两类准则：(1) 标准 agnostic，学习器与类中最优概念竞争；(2) 松弛 agnostic，学习器与最优概念误差的 (1+λ) 倍竞争，λ∈(0,1]。

Result: (1) 标准 agnostic 下，对任意有限 VC(H) 的类可达到最优速率 1/√n，精细最优速率为 √(VC(H)/n)；VC(H) 无穷时任意慢速率不可避免，即依赖边际分布无益。(2) 松弛 agnostic 下出现三分律：类有限时指数速率 e^{-n}；类无穷但 VC(H) 有限时线性速率 1/n；VC(H) 无穷时任意慢速率。精细最优速率为 uVCE(H)/(λn)，其中 uVCE 为无中心 VC-eluder 维度。

Conclusion: agnostic 学习中的边际分布依赖在标准准则下不能改善速率，与 realizable 情形形成对比；但在松弛 agnostic 准则下 realizable 情形的图景重新出现，并由 uVCE(H) 给出精确的精细速率刻画。

Abstract: We study the problem of binary classification in the marginal-nonuniform learning framework in the agnostic setting. In this model, the constants in the learning rate may depend on the marginal distribution of the unlabeled data, but the guarantee must hold uniformly over all conditional distributions of the labels. We also study fine-grained rates.
  We consider two criteria.
  (1) The standard agnostic setting, where the learner competes with the best concept in the class. In this setting, an immediate consequence of standard agnostic PAC learning results gives us the optimal rate of $\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}$ for every class $\mathcal{H}$ with finite $\mathrm{VC}(\mathcal{H})$ and shows that arbitrarily slow rates are necessary otherwise. This also gives us the optimal fine-grained rate of $\sqrt{\tfrac{\mathrm{VC}(\mathcal{H})}{n}}$. Therefore, the dependence on the marginal distribution does not help, which is in contrast to the realizable setting.
  (2) In the relaxed agnostic setting, where the learner competes with $(1+λ)$ times the error of the best concept for a fixed $λ\in (0,1]$, the picture from the realizable setting reappears. We obtain a trichotomy of the achievable optimal rates: exponential rates of the form $e^{-n}$ arise precisely when the class is finite; linear rates of the form $\tfrac{1}{n}$ arise when the class is infinite but $\mathrm{VC}(\mathcal{H})$ is finite; and arbitrarily slow rates occur when $\mathrm{VC}(\mathcal{H})=\infty$. We also obtain the optimal fine-grained rate of $\tfrac{\mathrm{uVCE}(\mathcal{H})}{λn}$, where $\mathrm{uVCE}(\mathcal{H})$ is a recently introduced combinatorial complexity dimension called the uncentered $\mathrm{VC}$-eluder dimension.

</details>


### [406] [TACTUS: A Robotic Palpation System for Spatially Heterogeneous Hardness Mapping of Woody Breast Myopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12695)
*Chaitanya Pallerla,Yihong Feng,Avisekh Tripathy,Yang Tian,Pouya Sohrabipour,Amirreza Davar,Casey M. Owens,Cengiz Koparan,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTUS自动化触觉阵列系统，可在约30秒内于胸肌20个解剖位点生成厚度归一化的三维硬度图，实现对木质胸（WB）肌病空间异质性的定量表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 木质胸是胸大肌的空间异质性纤维退行性病变，现有机械参考方法依赖单点或顺序压缩测量，难以刻画硬度的空间变异性，且人工操作依赖性强、效率低。

Method: TACTUS集成20个独立电机驱动的触觉单元（力敏电阻+编码器直线执行器），配合LiDAR深度相机进行表面轮廓测量与自适应压缩，在20个解剖位点生成厚度归一化三维硬度图；用4个WB严重度等级共100块鸡胸肉样进行验证，并与纹理分析仪结果对比，采用方差分析、半变异函数、局部变化熵与异质性分析。

Result: 各严重度等级间力值差异显著（ANOVA，p<0.001），颅侧区域区分度最强，并检出纹理分析仪未检测到的尾侧效应；样品级力值与纹理分析仪中度但显著相关（r=0.441，p<0.001）；半变异函数、局部变化熵与异质性分析显示硬度变异性随严重度增加且呈解剖学结构化分布。

Conclusion: TACTUS是一种自动化、不依赖操作者的空间分辨力学表征平台，可作为真值生成工具，支撑基于高光谱与近红外光谱的WB分级系统。

Abstract: Woody breast (WB) myopathy is a spatially heterogeneous fibro-degenerative condition of the broiler Pectoralis major muscle. Existing mechanical reference methods rely on single-point or sequential compression measurements, limiting characterization of spatial hardness variability. This study presents TACTUS (Tactile Adaptive Conformal 3D Uniform Sensing), an automated tactile array that generates thickness-normalized, three-dimensional hardness maps at 20 anatomically distributed locations within approximately 30 s. TACTUS integrates 20 independently motorized tactuators with force-sensing resistors and encoder-driven linear actuators, alongside a LiDAR depth camera for surface profiling and adaptive compression. The system was evaluated using 100 broiler breast fillets across four WB severity classes (Normal, Mild, Moderate, Severe). TACTUS identified significant force differences among severity classes (ANOVA, p < 0.001), with the strongest differentiation in the cranial region and a significant caudal effect not detected by texture-analyzer measurements. Fillet-level TACTUS force showed moderate but significant correlation with texture-analyzer measurements (Pearson r = 0.441, p < 0.001). Semivariogram, local change entropy, and heterogeneity analyses revealed increasing and anatomically structured hardness variability with WB severity. These findings demonstrate TACTUS as an automated, operator-independent platform for spatially resolved mechanical characterization and ground-truth generation to support hyperspectral and near-infrared spectroscopy-based WB classification systems.

</details>


### [407] [Generalized Adaptive Boosting and the Geometry of Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12696)
*Marco Bressan,Nataly Brukhim,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Emmanuel Esposito,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Maximilian Thiessen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 $Z$-learner 概念，按 $(\mathrm{FP},\mathrm{FN})$ 联合保证刻画弱学习器，给出可提升集 $Z$ 的完整几何刻画，并给出无需预知 $Z$ 的自适应提升算法，推广 AdaBoost 对未知 margin 参数的处理，同时证明可实现置信度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 boosting 理论把假阳性（FP）与假阴性（FN）错误合并为单一错误率，但在医学诊断等场景中两类错误影响不同，不应聚合。因此需要一种更灵活的弱可学习性概念，用以刻画分布对可达成学习保证的影响。

Method: 引入 $Z$-learner：对给定集合 $Z\subseteq[0,1]^2$，弱假设同时满足 FP/FN 联合保证；该定义可推广 $\gamma$-弱学习（取 $Z=\{(z_1,z_2):z_1+z_2<1/2-\gamma\}$）。在此基础上对“哪些 $Z$ 可被提升”进行几何刻画，并设计不依赖 $Z$ 先验知识的自适应提升算法；理论分析借助离散与计算几何中的中心点定理（centerpoint theorem）完成。

Result: 给出可提升集合 $Z$ 的完整几何刻画，统一了经典 boosting 二分性与 Bressan 等（COLT 2025）多目标扩展作为特例；提出可自适应提升 $Z$-learner 的算法，无需预知 $Z$；证明 $Z$-learner 可实现置信度放大。

Conclusion: 通过区分 FP/FN 的视角重构经典 boosting 理论，$Z$-learner 框架在概念与算法层面统一并推广了已有结果；其分析显著复杂于经典 boosting，依赖几何中心点定理。

Abstract: Not all mistakes are alike. For example, in medical diagnosis, false positive and false negative mistakes have a different impact and should not be aggregated. By revisiting the classical theory of boosting through this lens, we introduce the notion of $Z$-learner, which returns weak hypotheses achieving joint false-positive/false-negative guarantees $(\mathrm{FP},\mathrm{FN})$ from some set $Z \subseteq [0,1]^2$. This formulation generalizes the standard notion of $γ$-weak learning--recovered by taking $Z=\{(z_1,z_2): z_1+z_2<1/2-γ\}$--and allows for more flexible notions of weak learnability that account for the impact of the distribution on the achievable learning guarantees. This raises a natural question: which sets $Z$ are boostable? We answer this by giving a complete geometric characterization that unifies, as special cases, both the classical boosting dichotomy and the recent multi-objective extension of Bressan et al. (COLT 2025). We also present an adaptive algorithm for boosting $Z$-learners without relying on the prior knowledge of $Z$, generalizing AdaBoost's ability to handle unknown margin parameters. Finally, we demonstrate that $Z$-learners can achieve confidence amplification. However, the analysis is significantly more involved than in classical boosting and relies on the centerpoint theorem from discrete and computational geometry.

</details>


### [408] [Fisher-Glass Learning: Classification Bounds and Subpopulation Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12702)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将 Fisher-glass 框架扩展到"去干扰信息"的度量，把局部分类难度、子群审计与信息量联系起来，并在 Waterbirds 上通过受控背景替换实验揭示了高达 52.6 个百分点的性能落差，说明仅凭预测精度无法刻画扣除干扰变量后的任务信息残留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的分类器在背景（干扰）线索变化而正确标签不变时可能失效，但单一预测性能指标无法量化在扣除此类干扰变异后还剩多少任务信息，因此需要一个能同时刻画信息量与局部分类难度的诊断框架。

Method: 扩展 Fisher-glass 框架，将去干扰调整后的信息与局部分类难度及子群审计相连接；在显式可区分性与决策分离条件下推导局部极小极大超额分类风险的下界，并给出考虑估计误差与正则化的统计审计方法；在 Waterbirds 上进行特征级任务-背景对齐诊断（选择规则在留出评估前冻结），并进行保留前景与标签的背景替换实验；另用任务标签与固定干扰方向搜索出独立评估子群。

Result: 在五个训练随机种子下，保留提取的前景与标签而保持不变背景类型时准确率为 93.2%，背景类型改变时降至 40.6%，受控差异为 52.6 个百分点；用任务标签和固定干扰方向搜索得到的独立评估子群，其成员加权准确率为 98.8%，但估计的投影预测 Fisher 信息较低；训练与数据获取实验显示信息增益与预测增益可能发生分离。

Conclusion: 诊断模型脆弱性与评估模型改进时，应联合考察预测性能、在声明干扰模型下的信息量以及受控敏感性，三者缺一不可。

Abstract: Accurate classifiers can fail when background cues change without altering the correct labels. Predictive performance alone does not quantify how much task information remains after accounting for such nuisance variation. Here we extend the Fisher-glass framework to connect nuisance-adjusted information with local classification difficulty and subpopulation auditing. We derive lower bounds on local minimax excess classification risk under explicit distinguishability and decision-separation conditions, alongside statistical audits accounting for estimation error and regularization. On Waterbirds, a feature-based diagnostic of task-background alignment selects an accurately classified group, with selection frozen before held-out evaluation. Across five training seeds, background replacements preserving the extracted bird foreground and labels yield 93.2% accuracy when background type is preserved and 40.6% when it changes, a controlled difference of 52.6 percentage points. Separately, search using task labels and a fixed nuisance direction identifies an independently evaluated population with 98.8% membership-weighted accuracy and low estimated projected predictive Fisher information. Training and acquisition experiments show that information gains and predictive gains can diverge. These findings support joint assessment of predictive performance, information under declared nuisance models and controlled sensitivity when diagnosing vulnerability and evaluating model improvements.

</details>


### [409] [Scaling Taxonomy-Free Image Labeling with Generative AI: A Multimodal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12725)
*Zhenjie Zhang,Sainan Chen,Kshiteesh Hegde,Mengze,Li,Pouya Rezazadeh Kalehbasti,Munachim Ezema,Karina Sobhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLOC（Generative Labeling On Clusters，基于聚类的生成式打标）利用优化后的图像嵌入对图像聚类，在簇级别用生成式模型总结洞察并泛化到簇内成员，从而为 Pinterest 混合搜索提供可扩展、低成本、低时延、高精度的在线图像洞察服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为图像提供实时多模态洞察需同时满足低时延、高描述精度、可扩展、低成本高效率。传统"人工标注金标集+训练模型+在线推理"路线受制于固定分类体系、标注成本高、样本空间覆盖有限、标注者偏差与专业能力不足，且迭代昂贵笨重；直接用生成式 AI 做在线洞察则时延高、成本过高，难以支撑高请求量或大规模离线打标。

Method: GLOC 流程：(1) 采用高度优化的图像嵌入；(2) 按洞察类型将语义相近的图像聚成簇；(3) 在簇级别生成并总结洞察，借由簇的语义一致性将结果高效泛化到簇内所有成员，避免逐图调用生成模型。落地于 Pinterest 的 image descriptors 与 Hybrid Search 的 pivot query suggestions。

Result: 离线实验与在线 A/B 测试表明，GLOC 在质量与效率两个维度均显著优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 簇级生成式打标是平衡生成模型成本/时延与标注质量的有效范式，可在工业级多模态搜索场景中规模化部署；摘要未给出具体量化指标，仅给出相对提升结论。

Abstract: Providing real-time multimodal insights into images is challenging because it requires low latency, high description accuracy, and scalable solutions that are both cost-effective and efficient. For example, given a new image of an outfit, we would like to generate insights that the image depicts "earth tones" and "linen pants" and allow users to explore similar ideas along these themes. Traditional strategies to generate such insights would typically involve building taxonomies producing a golden set labeled by humans, training models on these datasets, and deploying these models for online inference services. Such workflows are constrained by fixed taxonomy, high labeling cost, limited labeling coverage in sample space, and human errors and expertise gaps in labelers. In addition, these methods are expensive and cumbersome to iterate over time. Recent trends have started exploring generative AI models for online insights, but this method suffers from long latency and prohibitive costs to support a high volume of requests or large amounts of offline labeling needs.
  We present Generative Labeling On Clusters (GLOC) as a solution to provide highly scalable, cost-effective, low latency, and high accuracy online image insight services. GLOC leverages highly optimized image embeddings to form image clusters that are semantically close to each other for the purpose of the type of insights, and summarizes the insights at the cluster level, which effectively generalizes to members in the cluster. At Pinterest, GLOC powers image descriptors and pivot query suggestions for Hybrid Search, which is a multimodal search service enabling users to search with both an image and a text query. Our offline and online A/B tests demonstrate that GLOC significantly outperforms previous methods, delivering improvements in quality and efficiency.

</details>


### [410] [Safe Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Clinical Agents: When Dense Rewards Beat Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12728)
*Brendan P. Murphy,Agustina Saenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在多轮临床决策任务上，仅用可验证最终结果的强化学习（如GRPO）虽能提升总体奖励，却会诱发过早决策、只问一个问题等不良过程行为，并在罕见高风险病例上失败；只有把奖励设计扩展到"过程/信息收集"层面，才能训练出可靠的序贯决策智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）被广泛用于提升语言模型推理，但其对序贯决策过程的影响尚不清楚。作者关注临床多轮智能体：它必须决定问什么信息、何时停止收集证据、采取何种行动。核心问题是：仅校验最终结果的奖励是否足以训练可靠的多轮决策智能体。

Method: 构建三个受控环境：药物续方分诊、急性诊断检查、剂量滴定。每个环境均含隐藏临床状态、查询成本、精确自适应oracle和确定性结果校验器。以此对比：(1) 仅结果奖励的GRPO；(2) 对降低不确定性的提问给予稠密信用（过程奖励）；(3) 探索与利用分离（E/E分离）；(4) 监督热身后再接RL；(5) 基于有偏监督数据的初始化。并在多个训练种子与留出集上评估总体奖励、高风险病例支持度与过程指标。

Result: 结果奖励GRPO常提升聚合奖励但产生不良流程：过早承诺（premature commitment）、单问崩塌（one-question collapse）、罕见高风险病例失败。诊断任务中，对降不确定性提问给稠密信用，相较仅结果RL在三个种子下提升留出价值与中等病例支持度，且未出现仅结果训练时的高风险种子崩塌。E/E分离在最佳checkpoint进一步提升高风险支持度，但代价是低急重症病例上过度信息收集。过程信用的最优强度依任务而异；在已谨慎收集信息的监督热身后接RL，会通过削减提问数提升聚合奖励却损害高风险表现；RL也难以修复有偏监督数据带来的罕见行为缺口。

Conclusion: 可验证最终结果不足以训练可靠的多轮智能体：奖励设计必须纳入抵达答案的过程，评估须区分"自适应信息收集"与"奖励利用型捷径"。过程信用的强度需要按任务调节，且RL无法弥补监督数据中的罕见行为缺口。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is increasingly used to improve language model reasoning, yet its effects on sequential decision procedures remain poorly understood. We study this problem in clinical multi-turn agents that must decide what information to request, when to stop gathering evidence, and which action to take. We introduce three controlled environments: medication refill triage, acute diagnostic work-up, and dosing titration, each with hidden clinical states, query costs, exact adaptive oracles, and deterministic outcome verifiers. Across these tasks, outcome-only group relative policy optimization often improves aggregate reward while producing undesirable procedures, including premature commitment, one-question collapse, and failures on rare high-risk cases. In diagnostics, assigning dense credit to questions that reduce uncertainty improves held-out value and moderate-case support across three training seeds relative to outcome-only reinforcement learning, without the high-risk seed collapse observed under outcome-only training. Separating exploration from exploitation further increases high-risk support at its best checkpoint, but at the cost of excessive information gathering on low-acuity cases. Additional experiments show that the optimal strength of process credit is task-dependent; that after a supervised warmup that already gathers carefully, reinforcement learning raises aggregate reward by trimming questions while degrading high-risk performance; and that reinforcement learning does not reliably repair rare behavior gaps inherited from biased supervised data. Verifiable final outcomes are therefore insufficient for training reliable multi-turn agents: reward design must account for the procedure used to reach an answer, and evaluation must distinguish adaptive information gathering from reward-exploiting shortcuts.

</details>


### [411] [KiteNorm: Variance Regularisation for Stable and Scalable Post-LN Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12729)
*Leon A. Trochelmann,Sajad Movahedi,Shiwei Liu,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 KiteNorm——一种通过约束隐藏状态方差来稳定 Post-LayerNorm 训练的方法，结合残差缩放、方差正则化与标量双重归一化，在最多 1.3B 参数的 decoder-only Transformer 上稳定训练并优于强 Pre-LayerNorm 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-LayerNorm 在加深时难以扩展，现有工作主要通过改进初始化时的条件性来稳定它，但在大学习率下稳定性仍会恶化，需额外妥协；在 decoder-only 设定下，已有 Post-LN 方法均无法超过强 Pre-LN 基线。

Method: KiteNorm 以 Post-Norm 方式训练：由 LayerNorm 雅可比的上界分析可知方差增长会导致梯度消失，且经验上关联到秩塌缩，因此采用（1）残差缩放与（2）方差正则化维持全训练过程稳定；（3）标量双重归一化，为 skip 分支与残差分支提供可分别学习的尺度。设置消融验证各组件必要性。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数以内的 decoder-only Transformer 上，KiteNorm 提升训练稳定性，并在各自最优学习率下优于领先基线；缩放律在深度、宽度、批大小与训练预算维度上均偏好 KiteNorm。消融显示每个组件对完整稳定性与性能收益均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 方差受控是解决 Post-LN 深层训练不稳定的关键，KiteNorm 使 Post-Norm 在 decoder-only 大模型中具备与 Pre-Norm 竞争甚至超越的能力，并展现更好的缩放行为。

Abstract: Post-LayerNorm Transformers have seen limited practical adoption due to enduring difficulties in scaling them to large depths. While prior research has focused on stabilising Post-LayerNorm by improving conditioning at initialisation, stability often deteriorates when models are trained with large learning rates, forcing additional compromises. In the decoder-only settings we study, existing Post-LayerNorm methods fail to outperform strong Pre-LayerNorm baselines. We propose KiteNorm, a post-norm training method designed to break this trend by constraining hidden-state variance. This approach is motivated by a bound on the LayerNorm Jacobian suggesting that variance growth contributes to gradient vanishing, alongside empirical evidence linking it to rank collapse. KiteNorm combines residual scaling and variance regularisation to maintain stability throughout training, supported by scalarised double-normalisation that provides separately learnable scales for the skip and residual branches. Across decoder-only Transformers up to 1.3B parameters, KiteNorm improves training stability and outperforms leading baselines at their optimal learning rates, with scaling laws favouring KiteNorm across depth, width, batch size, and training budget. Ablations show that each component is necessary for the full stability and performance gains.

</details>


### [412] [$Σ$-LeWM: Learning World Models via S-Information for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12732)
*Xinrui Zu,Michael Przystupa,Zhao Yang,Shujian Yu,Kevin Sebastian Luck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在LeWorldModel中额外引入反向动力学预测器与逆动力学动作预测器两个隐空间损失，可进一步约束潜嵌入结构，在标准世界模型基准与两个模拟机器人操作任务上同时提升性能与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期世界模型依赖解码器防止模式坍缩并学习可预测潜空间；近期如JEPA、LeWorldModel倾向于无解码器接地，仅用潜前向动力学预测器和稳定化熵项学习嵌入。作者希望验证额外隐空间结构约束能否弥补无解码器方案的不足，从而提升实时化模型预测控制（MPC）在真实机器人上的可用性。

Method: 以LeWorldModel为基础架构，在原有潜前向动力学预测器与熵正则项之上，新增两个隐空间损失：一是反向动力学预测器（由当前状态与未来状态回归中间状态，即backward dynamics），二是逆动力学动作预测器（由状态转移推断执行的动作）。二者共同对潜嵌入施加更丰富的结构约束，且不引入解码器。

Result: 在标准世界模型基准（如控制/推演类评测）以及两个模拟机器人操作任务上，所提方法相对LeWorldModel基线在任务性能与规划效率两方面均取得提升；规划效率的提升意味着更少的滚动优化开销，有利于实时控制。

Conclusion: 无需解码器的隐空间世界模型可通过引入反向动力学与逆动力学辅助损失获得更结构化、更可预测的嵌入，从而在性能与规划效率上取得双重收益，为其应用于真实机器人的实时基于模型的控制提供了支撑。

Abstract: World models have increasingly been applied as a model-based control method for robotics tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. While early world model architectures relied on decoders to prevent mode collapse and learn a predictable latent space, recent works on world models, such as Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture or LeWorldModel, increasingly aim to learn embeddings without a grounding decoder, utilising only a latent forward dynamics predictor and a stabilising entropy term. In this paper, we set out to demonstrate that the further introduction of two additional latent losses to LeWorldModel, a backward dynamics predictor and an inverse dynamics action predictor, enforces further structure on the latent embedding, leading to further gains in both performance and planning efficiency. These are critical properties for improving the applicability of such methods to real-time model-based control of real-world robots. We demonstrate these gains on standard world model benchmarks and two simulated robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [413] [Clique Constraints for Exact Causal Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12747)
*Xiaoyu He,Pavel Rytíř,Petr Ryšavý,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在精确score-based因果结构学习中引入"骨架最大团规模上界"约束，通过惰性环约束与团约束实现分支切割，并系统评估割回调顺序、团割策略、可行解修复、分数分离与Big-M界等设计，指出团约束并非普遍加速，其收益依赖图密度、样本量与割管理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确score-based因果结构学习能提供显式结构约束与最优性证书，但其分支切割搜索计算开销大。作者希望借助对学习所得无向骨架最大团规模的上界约束（在整数候选解上通过惰性环约束和团约束施加）来改善求解效率，并厘清各实现选择带来的计算权衡。

Method: 在精确因果结构学习方法中加入骨架最大团规模上界，通过惰性环约束与团约束在整数候选解处强制执行。实验基于静态线性高斯Erdős-Rényi与无标度DAG，评估回调顺序、多种团割策略、可行解修复（incumbent repair）、分数分离（fractional separation）及Big-M界。重点比较两种变体：B3在每次回调时选择最违背的最小(k+1)-团不等式；C3通过删除违背边并用基于QR的最小二乘重拟合受影响回归列来修复不可行整数候选。

Result: 团约束并未一致地加速优化；其收益取决于图密度、样本量和割管理策略，大量相关约束会降低节点吞吐。更紧的有效Big-M界在多数设置下缩小了固定预算下的最优性间隙，但在固定时间限制下未必改变SHD或F1。报告同时涵盖因果恢复指标以及最优性间隙、原始/对偶界、节点吞吐、MIPSOL事件与回调开销。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了团约束精确因果学习的计算权衡：团约束的加速效果高度条件依赖，选择性分离（selective separation）与可行解修复（incumbent repair）是值得推进的方向。

Abstract: Exact score-based causal structure learning supports explicit structural constraints and optimisation certificates, but its branch-and-cut search can be computationally demanding. We augment exact causal structure learning methods with an upper bound on the maximum clique size of the learned undirected skeleton, enforced at integer candidates through lazy cycle and clique constraints. On static linear-Gaussian Erdős-Rényi and scale-free DAGs, we evaluate callback order, alternative clique-cut policies, incumbent repair, fractional separation, and Big-M bounds. We report causal-recovery metrics jointly with optimality gaps, primal and dual bounds, node throughput, MIPSOL events, and callback cost. Two variants receive particular attention: B3 selects the most violated minimal (k+1)-clique inequality at each callback, whereas C3 repairs an infeasible integer candidate by deleting violating edges and refitting affected regression columns using QR-based least squares. The results do not indicate that clique constraints uniformly accelerate optimisation. However, their benefit depends on graph density, sample size, and cut-management policy, while large collections of correlated constraints can reduce node throughput. Tighter valid Big-M bounds reduced the fixed-budget optimality gap in most tested settings, without necessarily changing SHD or F1 at a fixed time limit. These findings characterize the computational trade-offs of clique-constrained exact causal learning, and identify selective separation and incumbent repair as promising directions.

</details>


### [414] [Anchor Behavior, Not Parameters: Rehearsal-Free Continual Learning with a Single Shared Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12777)
*Duy-Cuong Bui,Trung-Anh Dang,Vincent Nguyen,Duc V. Le,Ngoc-Son Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEA-Prompt 提出在单提示（one-prompt）持续学习范式中保护"提示行为"而非参数：用输入条件化的 token 相关性评分做软性任务专化，并用基于高斯查询统计的注意力行为锚定损失约束旧类注意力输出，在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R、CUB-200 上以仅 0.38M 参数取得 SOTA 精度与显著更低遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提示的持续学习通常冻结预训练 ViT、只调少量 prompt token；为最大化参数效率，近期单提示方法让所有任务共享同一个 prompt。核心难题是：共享提示在为新任务更新时如何保留旧类知识。现有做法在参数空间保护旧知识（冻结任务特定提示、扩展新组件、稀疏门控梯度流），但这牺牲了可塑性，且共享 token 中仍被允许的漂移会破坏提示在旧类上的功能。作者认为真正需要保持的是"提示的行为"（即其注意力输出），而非参数本身。

Method: 方法含两个耦合组件：(1) Token 评估模块，为每个 prompt token 分配连续、输入条件化的相关性分数，注入注意力 logits，从而在不丢弃任何 token 的前提下软性实现跨任务专化；(2) 注意力行为锚定损失，利用紧凑的高斯查询统计（无需存储旧图像）将每个 token 的注意力输出约束在类特定查询区域附近，并以该 token 的过去相关性加权。由于锚定的是注意力值而非参数，参数可自由更新，只要其交互作用保持不变。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100、ImageNet-R、CUB-200 上达到 SOTA 精度，且遗忘显著降低，模型参数仅 0.38M。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 在单提示持续学习中，以注意力行为锚定替代参数空间保护，可在保持参数高效与可塑性的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘，为共享提示的知识保持提供了新思路。

Abstract: Prompt-based continual learning adapts a frozen pre-trained vision transformer by tuning a small set of prompt tokens. Recently, one-prompt methods which share one single prompt across all tasks make this paradigm maximally parameter efficient. The open question is how a shared prompt can keep the knowledge of old classes while being updated for new ones. Existing methods protect old knowledge in parameter space, where they freeze task-specific prompts, expand new components, or sparsely gate which tokens receive gradients. However, this method sacrifices plasticity, yet the drift it still permits in shared tokens can corrupt the prompt's function on past classes. What must be preserved is the prompt's behavior. We propose TEA-Prompt, which couples (i) a token evaluation module that assigns each prompt token a continuous, input-conditional relevance score injected into the attention logits, softly specializing tokens across tasks without discarding any of them, and (ii) an attention-behavior anchoring loss that constrains each token's attention output around class-specific query regions using only compact Gaussian query statistics without stored old images, weighted by the token's past relevance. Anchoring the attention value allows the parameters to continue updating freely, as long as their interaction remains unchanged. TEA-Prompt achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with markedly lower forgetting on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-R, and CUB-200 with only 0.38M parameters. Code is publicy available at https://github.com/cuonga1cvp/TEA-Prompt.git

</details>


### [415] [Smartphone-Enabled Depth-Aware Transillumination Imaging for Detection of Chicken Breast Fillet Myopathies in Chicken Fillets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12796)
*Chaitanya Pallerla,Ramesh Bahadur Bist,Yihong Feng,Yang Tian,Shiva Paudel,Casey M. Owens,Cengiz Koparan,Jeyamkondan Subbiah,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种基于智能手机透射成像与3D点云的无损框架，用于木质化鸡胸肉（WB）严重程度分级，多模态融合模型准确率约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 木质化鸡胸肉（WB）是肉鸡主要品质缺陷，表现为纤维化、组织硬度增加和结构改变；传统触诊主观且费时，现有无损方法多依赖专用设备或大量算力，故需低成本、便携、无损的WB分级手段。

Method: 采用iPhone 16 Pro采集267块鸡胸肉样本（正常NB 87、轻度MWB 85、重度SWB 95）的RAW透射图像与3D点云；提取91个特征（14个图像特征、76个几何特征、1个基于Beer-Lambert定律的物理先验衰减特征）；以厚度为控制变量做偏相关分析，并构建融合Fusion-DGCNN、XGBoost与物理引导逻辑回归（PGLR）的多模态集成模型。

Result: 控制厚度后的偏相关显示图像特征与WB严重程度显著相关（r = 0.413, p < 0.0001），说明光学信息独立于肉块几何；SWB组织单位厚度的光衰减比NB高约26%；仅用2D特征的模型测试准确率约82%，而多模态集成模型约达90%。

Conclusion: 光学、几何与物理先验特征具有互补价值，证明了基于智能手机的无损WB严重程度评估具有可行性。

Abstract: Woody Breast (WB) myopathy is a major quality defect in broiler chickens characterized by fibrosis, increased tissue hardness, and altered structural properties that affect product quality. Conventional palpation is subjective and labor-intensive, while many non-destructive methods require specialized equipment or substantial computational resources. This study developed a smartphone-based transillumination and 3D point-cloud framework for non-destructive WB severity classification. A total of 267 chicken breast fillets, including normal breast (NB, n = 87), mild WB (MWB, n = 85), and severe WB (SWB, n = 95), were evaluated using RAW transillumination images and 3D point clouds acquired with an iPhone 16 Pro. A total of 91 features, comprising 14 image-derived, 76 geometric, and one physics-informed Beer-Lambert attenuation feature, were extracted. Partial correlation controlling for thickness revealed a significant association between image characteristics and WB severity (r = 0.413, p < 0.0001), indicating optical information independent of fillet geometry. SWB tissue exhibited approximately 26% greater light attenuation per unit thickness than NB. The 2D-only models achieved approximately 82% test accuracy, while the multimodal ensemble combining Fusion-DGCNN, XGBoost, and physics-guided logistic regression (PGLR) achieved approximately 90% accuracy. These findings demonstrate the complementary value of optical, geometric, and physics-informed features and establish the feasibility of smartphone-based, non-destructive WB severity assessment.

</details>


### [416] [Agentic-OPD: Recovering Forgotten Agent Capabilities via On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12799)
*Tingcheng Bian,Kangye Gong,Jinchang Luo,MingQuan Cheng,Feiran Liu,Ni Li,Xiaoling Xia,Haiwei Wang,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic-OPD，将 GRPO 训练中被丢弃的中间 checkpoint 视为免费教师池，通过 HCN 动态选教师与 LAD 局部蒸馏，恢复 RL 训练中丢失的能力覆盖，实现 post-RL 能力回收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RL（GRPO）实践只保留最优验证 checkpoint 并丢弃其余，作者观察到这种做法的信息是有损的：最能通过验证的 checkpoint 会失败于同一次训练早期 checkpoint 已经解决的问题，且各 checkpoint 解决问题并集远超任一单点。因此被丢弃的 checkpoint 构成一个零额外训练成本的教师池，可用于弥补覆盖损失。

Method: 把 best-validation checkpoint 作为学生，所有保存的 checkpoint 作为冻结教师候选；用学生不断变化的能力缺口同时指导教师选择与监督过滤：HCN（Highest Coverage Next）策略按对"学生未解问题"的加权覆盖率动态选教师；LAD（Locality-Aware Distillation）模块仅在学生对某教师表现更差的问题上、针对失败 rollout 进行蒸馏，避免破坏已掌握行为。

Result: 在 5 个 agentic 任务领域、24 个 benchmark 与任务子集上，相对初始化 checkpoint 最高提升 7.4 分；4B 学生模型在 deep search 上超过 GPT-5.6 与 DeepSeek-V4.1 等前沿模型。

Conclusion: Agentic-OPD 将一次已完成的 RL 运行重构为可复用的策略池，并把 post-RL 能力回收确立为与 RL 算法改进本身正交的阶段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now the mainstream paradigm for training LLMs on agentic tasks, yet standard practice keeps only the best-validation checkpoint and discards the rest. We show that this is lossy: in GRPO training of LLM agents, the best-validation checkpoint fails on problems that earlier checkpoints from the same run had already solved, and the union of problems solved across checkpoints far exceeds any single one. The discarded checkpoints thus form a teacher pool obtained at no additional training cost, which on-policy distillation (OPD) can mine to recover the lost coverage. Mining this pool raises two challenges: i) which checkpoint to distill from, as no checkpoint dominates the others and complementarity to the student is problem-specific; ii) where to trust it, as even a well-chosen teacher is only locally superior, so indiscriminate distillation overwrites behavior the student has already mastered. We propose Agentic-OPD, which treats the best-validation checkpoint as the student and all saved checkpoints as frozen teacher candidates, using the student's evolving capability gaps to guide both teacher selection and supervision filtering: the HCN policy (Highest Coverage Next) dynamically selects teachers by weighted coverage of the student's unsolved problems, while the LAD module (Locality-Aware Distillation) confines transfer to failed student rollouts on problems where the teacher performs better. Across five agentic task domains and 24 benchmarks and task subsets, Agentic-OPD improves over its initialization checkpoint by up to 7.4 points, with a 4B student surpassing the latest frontier models GPT-5.6 and DeepSeek-V4.1 on deep search. Agentic-OPD recasts a finished RL run as a reusable pool of policies and establishes post-RL capability recovery as a stage orthogonal to improvements in the RL algorithm itself.

</details>


### [417] [Inversion-Free High-Dimensional Counterfactual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12815)
*Saketh Vishnubhatla,Pingchuan Ma,Shu Wan,Alimohammad Beigi,Andre Harrison,Adrienne Raglin,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将反事实推断从标量一维变量推广到高维节点（如图像），探讨了潜在空间反演步骤在高维反事实计算中的必要性，并评估了源自图像编辑领域的无反转方案，在CelebA和ADNI数据集上以有效性（effectiveness）与身份保持（identity preservation）两项指标进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中一维标量情形下的反事实推断已较清晰，但扩展到高维节点（如图像）仍具挑战。常规做法需先从事实图像反演潜在状态、再由图生成反事实属性、最后渲染；作者希望探究该反演步骤是否必要。

Method: 先讨论从标量一维情形到高维情形的推广；再分析反演步骤的相关性，并将图像编辑文献中的无反转（inversion-free）方法改造用于反事实推断；最后在CelebA与ADNI图像数据集上进行基准对比。

Result: 实验以有效性（反事实属性渲染的忠实程度）与身份保持（渲染对象是否为同一单位/图像）两个指标进行评估，给出反演式与无反转式方法的权衡（tradeoffs）结论。

Conclusion: 反演步骤并非高维反事实推断所必需；无反转方法可作为一种可行替代，但与反演式方法在有效性与身份保持之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference is the task of answering hypothetical "what-if" queries about a unit, given an observational dataset, modeling assumptions, and a causal graph. This enables a modeler to reason about hypothetical scenarios that cannot otherwise be answered without assumptions or a causal graph. The simpler case involves a causal graph with scalar 1-D variables that denote different attributes in the data-generation process. While the literature is clear in the 1-D case, extending this to settings with high-dimensional nodes (e.g., images) remains challenging. Conventionally, in the literature, methods recover the latent state from the factual image (inversion), generate counterfactual attributes from the graph, and render the counterfactual image from the latent state using the updated attributes. In our work, we investigate the necessity of the inversion step in computing high-dimensional counterfactuals. We first discuss moving from the scalar 1-D case to the higher-dimensional case. Secondly, we discuss the relevance of the inversion step and evaluate an inversion-free counterpart repurposed from the image-editing literature. Finally, on image datasets such as CelebA and ADNI, we benchmark our approach using two measures: namely, effectiveness and identity preservation. While effectiveness is a measure of how faithfully the counterfactual attribute is rendered, identity preservation measures whether the same unit (image) is being rendered, both of which are crucial for high-dimensional counterfactual inference. We present the tradeoffs of using inversion-based and inversion-free approaches in our work.

</details>


### [418] [What the Loss Curve Misses: FP4 Pretraining Slows the Acquisition of Rare Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12832)
*Chang Lu,Mengjiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"验证损失对齐即可认为低精度预训练等价"的惯例：在可控曝光次数的合成传记数据上，NVFP4训练需1.32×/1.52×曝光量才能达到BF16的稀有事实召回，而验证损失仅差0.5%/2%，建议将稀有事实习得作为低精度评测的独立指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度（如4-bit）预训练研究普遍以验证损失作为主要评价标准，隐含假设"损失匹配即学习效率等价"。作者要检验这一假设：损失对齐时，低精度模型是否同样高效地学会其训练中低频出现的稀有事实（知识记忆）。

Method: 从头训练Transformer，采用仿真4-bit矩阵乘法（NVFP4），在曝光次数可控的合成传记数据上训练，并进一步混入网页文本验证。进行受控噪声实验：对比零均值梯度噪声（仅量化反向传播）与仅注入Adam二阶矩估计的噪声、仅注入更新量的噪声，并在2×NVFP4误差量级下考察学习率补偿。最后用BF16检查点的梯度统计做成本排序（无需拟合并给出Spearman相关性）。

Result: NVFP4需1.32×/1.52×曝光量才达到BF16的召回，而验证损失仅相差0.5%与2%；若两者均以BF16矩阵乘法推理，则为1.26×/1.30×。主读出下匹配损失时曝光成本约1.2×。零均值梯度噪声（仅量化反向）可复现该成本，但几乎不改变损失。2×误差条件下，仅注入Adam二阶矩的噪声表现为降低学习率、成本逐渐消失；而更新量中的噪声降低信噪比，约承受2/3成本并持续到训练结束。提高学习率补偿平均步长变小并不能恢复成本（对上述噪声与NVFP4皆然）。BF16检查点的梯度统计可在无拟合情况下排序各格式、部分量化与噪声对照的成本（Spearman 0.96）。

Conclusion: 低精度训练在损失几乎不变的情况下显著损害稀有事实的习得效率，故仅以验证损失评估低精度方案不足；建议在评价低精度训练配方时同时测量稀有事实的获取速率，并可用BF16梯度统计低成本预测相对成本。

Abstract: Low-precision pretraining is usually judged by validation loss. We ask whether matching loss also means learning rare facts as efficiently. We train transformers from scratch with emulated 4-bit matrix multiplications on synthetic biographies with controlled exposure counts, on their own and mixed into web text. NVFP4 needs 1.32$\times$ and 1.52$\times$ as many exposures as BF16 to match its recall while validation loss differs by only 0.5% and 2%, and 1.26$\times$ and 1.30$\times$ when both models are evaluated with BF16 matrix multiplications. Under our primary readout, the exposure cost is about 1.2$\times$ at matched loss. Zero-mean gradient noise reproduces the cost of quantizing only the backward pass, which barely moves the loss. In a controlled experiment at twice the NVFP4 error, noise fed only to Adam's second-moment estimate acts like a lower learning rate and its cost fades, whereas noise in the update lowers its signal-to-noise ratio and carries about two thirds of the cost through the end of training. Raising the learning rate to offset the smaller average step does not recover the cost, either for this noise or for NVFP4. Gradient statistics of BF16 checkpoints rank the costs of formats, partial quantizations and noise controls without fitting (Spearman 0.96). We suggest measuring rare-fact acquisition alongside loss when evaluating low-precision recipes.

</details>


### [419] [Mitigating Agentic Deception via Evaluation-Side Partially Observable Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12835)
*Hanxiao Li,Shutong Liu,Yongjia Ma,Jiaming Ji,Juntao Dai,Shuning Zhang,Kejian Shi,Kai Chen,Pu Feng,Yuqing Ma,Qi Dou,Yaodong Yang,Xianglong Liu,Simin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将欺骗缓解建模为评估侧部分可观测扩展式博弈（e-PoG），在智能体最大化奖励、监控者最小化其收益、验证者裁决并处罚的三方结构下定义子博弈完美监督均衡（SPOE），并给出两时间尺度策略梯度算法与NLI/确定性检查增强；实验证明可显著缓解多种涌现欺骗模式并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型作为智能体时可能表现出欺骗行为（为高奖励而欺骗人类却显得乐于助人）。作者将这一失效归因于"评估侧部分可观测"：评估只看最终结果，不检查推理、工具使用与环境效应的忠实性。既有缓解手段依赖预定义的评估者隐信念空间或一次性监控，难以推广到自由形式语言空间，且易被更强的、规避简单监控的欺骗所突破。

Method: 提出e-PoG（Evaluation-Side Partially Observable Extensive Form Game）max-min框架：智能体最大化奖励、监控者基于完整交互轨迹揭露欺骗以最小化智能体收益、固定验证者核验监控者指控并转化为惩罚。定义智能体与监控者之间的子博弈完美监督均衡（SPOE）解概念并证明有限博弈下存在性；分析完美验证者下所有SPOE均为忠实，非完美验证者下若验证错误不反转监控者动作排序则保证仍成立。为达忠实SPOE推导两时间尺度策略梯度算法，并在流程中引入NLI验证与确定性检查。

Result: 在Gemma3-4B-IT与Qwen3-4B/8B上，于Agentic Role-Playing、SearchQA、WebShop任务中，e-PoG缓解了多种涌现欺骗模式；相比最强基线，欺骗缓解指标提升26.0%，任务成功率最高提升39.8%，且优势在弱到强监督下依然保持。

Conclusion: 欺骗问题的关键在评估侧部分可观测，而非仅在于智能体能力；通过博弈化的监控—验证—惩罚机制与均衡保证，可将评估从"看结果"转为"看轨迹忠实性"，在理论与实验上均实现可信的欺骗缓解，并具备向更强模型监督迁移的稳健性。

Abstract: As large language models become more capable agents, they can exhibit deceptive behaviors, cheating human for higher rewards while appearing helpful. We attribute this failure to evaluation-side partial observability, where models are evaluated mainly from final outcome, without checking the faithfulness of underlying reasoning, tool use, and environmental effects. Prior mitigations address this partial observability by predefined evaluator latent belief space or one-shot monitoring, which do not extend to free-form language space and vulnerable to stronger deceptions that evade simple monitoring. In this work, we formulate deception mitigation as an Evaluation-Side Partially Observable Extensive Form Game (e-PoG), a max-min framework in which an agent maximizes reward, a monitor minimizes the agent's payoff by exposing deception from the full interaction trajectory, and a fixed verifier validates the monitor's claims and converts them into penalties. We define the solution concept as a Subgame-Perfect Oversight Equilibrium (SPOE) between agent and monitor, and prove its existence in finite games. With a perfect verifier, every SPOE is faithful; with an imperfect verifier, the guarantee persists if verifier errors do not reverse the ranking of monitor actions. To achieve faithful SPOE, we derive a two-timescale policy gradient algorithm and enhance the pipeline empirically with NLI verification and deterministic checks. Experiments with Gemma3-4B-IT and Qwen3-4B/8B on Agentic Role-Playing, SearchQA, and WebShop show that e-PoG mitigates multiple emergent deception modes, outperforming the strongest baselines by 26.0% in deception mitigation and by up to 39.8% in task success. These gains persist under weak-to-strong supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/Toyoid/deceptive-agent.

</details>


### [420] [Where Does Alignment Emerge and What Does It Mean? A Closer Look at the Platonic Representation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12869)
*Julia Herbst,Simon Roschmann,Quentin Bouniot,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新检验"柏拉图表示假说"(PRH),定位单模态模型对齐在层与维度上的位置,确认在新模型上能力-对齐趋势仍成立(前提是caption足够干净),并引入单模态与多模态基线来正确解读跨模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作质疑PRH在更新语言模型上是否继续成立;且表示相似度指标会受模型深度与宽度混淆,缺乏合适基线难以解读。

Method: 分析单模态模型在层与表示维度上的对齐涌现位置;在更干净、信息量更足的caption条件下检验能力-对齐趋势;在多个数据集上构建单模态与多模态基线进行对照。

Result: 在caption干净充分时,PRH的能力-对齐趋势对更新模型依然成立;单模态模型的跨模态表示对齐可超过显式对齐的视觉-语言模型,但明显落后于同模态模型间的对齐。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐需以同模态与多模态基线为参照来解读;PRH趋势有其成立条件,同模态对齐仍是表示相似度的上界参照。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) suggests that neural networks are converging toward similar representation spaces. As language models become more capable, their representations are aligning with those of vision models. However, recent work has questioned whether this trend continues to hold for newer language models. Moreover, it has been shown that representation similarity metrics can be confounded by model depth and width, requiring appropriate baselines for interpretation. In this work, we first investigate where alignment emerges between unimodal models across model layers and representation dimensions. Next, we find that the PRH capability-alignment trend persists for newer models as long as the captions are sufficiently clean and informative. Finally, we contextualize cross-modal alignment, introducing unimodal and multimodal baselines on various datasets. Our results show that the cross-modal representational alignment of unimodal models can surpass that of explicitly aligned vision-language models, but remains substantially behind the alignment observed for models of the same modality. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/prh-closer-look.

</details>


### [421] [Vendi Anomaly Scores for Efficient and Accurate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12871)
*Amey Pasarkar,Adji Bousso Dieng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从"多样性"视角进行异常检测的新范式，给出Vendi异常分数（VAS）：以移除某样本后数据集Vendi分数的变化量作为异常度量，无需密度估计、无需调参，在148个基准嵌入数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法通常基于局部密度、孤立性启发式（如隔离树）或到拟合分布的距离，分别对邻域尺度、划分方式敏感，或依赖受限的分布假设，缺乏一种统一且鲁棒的度量。

Method: 将异常检测重构为多样性视角问题：定义VAS为样本被移除前后数据集Vendi分数的差值（leave-one-out 形式），从而同时刻画局部冗余性与全局数据结构；方法为非参数化，不依赖密度估计或划分启发式，无需超参数调优，可自动适应数据的谱结构，计算复杂度随数据集规模线性增长。

Result: 在由10个源数据集、4种嵌入架构生成的148个基准嵌入数据集上，VAS达到当前最优的异常检测性能；在大规模ImageNet实验中，随着污染率与数据规模上升仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多样性视角为异常检测提供了无需密度/划分假设、免调参且可扩展的统一框架，VAS兼具局部与全局信息并具备良好规模化表现，可作为通用嵌入空间异常检测评分。

Abstract: Anomaly detection (AD) is a critical for trustworthy machine learning and scientific discovery. Existing methods typically define anomalies through local density, isolation heuristics, or distance from a fitted distribution, making them sensitive to neighborhood scale, partitioning choices, or restrictive distributional assumptions. In this work, we introduce a new paradigm by formulating anomaly detection from a diversity perspective. We propose the $\textit{Vendi Anomaly Score}$ (VAS), which detects anomalies by quantifying how much a sample changes the diversity of the dataset, as measured by the Vendi score, when removed. VAS captures both local redundancy and global data structure without relying on density estimation or partitioning heuristics. Moreover, VAS requires no hyperparameter tuning, instead adapting automatically to the spectral structure of the data. VAS is non-parametric and scales linearly with dataset size. Across 148 benchmark embedding datasets derived from 10 source datasets and 4 embedding architectures, VAS achieves state-of-the-art AD performance. We further validate VAS on large-scale ImageNet experiments, where it remains robust as both contamination rate and dataset scale increase.

</details>


### [422] [Adaptive Quantization Preserves Fast Rates for TD Learning over Rate-Limited Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12878)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出批处理自适应量化的TD算法BAQ-TD，在有限容量信道上以O(d log T)比特总量达到无量化TD的阶最优收敛率Õ(1/T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自受通信约束的强化学习：智能体与服务器之间的信道容量有限，需要研究在速率受限信道上做线性函数逼近策略评估时，能否保留无限容量信道下的统计收敛速率。

Method: 设定为MDP环境下的策略评估：智能体将TD学习更新方向的量化估计经有限容量信道传给服务器。提出BAQ-TD算法，用批处理（batching）降低传输信息的方差，用自适应量化编码新息（innovations）；并对该算法证明高概率有限时间误差界。

Result: 证明了BAQ-TD的高概率有限时间误差界；表明在某一有限信道容量之上，BAQ-TD可保留未量化TD的收敛速率（至多相差对数因子）。对d维值函数参数、时长T，总传输O(d log T)比特即可达到阶最优速率Õ(1/T)，相对先前工作在通信复杂度上取得指数级改进。

Conclusion: 通信受限并非本质瓶颈：通过批处理降低方差与自适应量化编码新息，仅需对数级比特数即可匹配无量化TD的收敛率，通信复杂度较既有结果实现指数级下降。

Abstract: Motivated by reinforcement learning subject to communication constraints, we study a policy evaluation problem with linear function approximation over a rate-limited channel. In our setup, an agent interacting with an unknown environment (modeled as a Markov Decision Process) transmits quantized estimates of a temporal-difference (TD) learning update direction to a server over a finite-capacity channel. Our goal is to investigate to what extent one can preserve the statistical convergence rates achievable when the channel has infinite capacity. To that end, we propose a new algorithm called Batched Adaptively Quantized TD (BAQ-TD), which uses batching to reduce the variance of the transmitted information, and adaptive quantization to encode innovations. We prove a high-probability finite-time error bound for BAQ-TD, and show that, above a certain finite channel capacity, BAQ-TD preserves the convergence rate of unquantized TD up to logarithmic factors. Specifically, for a $d$-dimensional value function parameter, we establish that transmitting a total of $\mathcal{O}(d \log(T))$ bits over a horizon of length $T$ suffices to achieve the order-optimal rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$, marking an exponential improvement in communication-complexity over prior work.

</details>


### [423] [Kolmogorov-Chapman Maps: Trajectory-Free Learning for Simulating Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12880)
*Majdi Hassan,Marta Skreta,Dominique Beaini,Kirill Neklyudov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KC-Maps：仅用无边轨迹的marginal样本与已知漂移/扩散系数，学习SDE转移核，实现从任意初始(时刻,状态)到任意目标时刻分布的映射（弱解），并在GPU时耗上优于经典分子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDE单步积分代价低，但刻画有意义的转变常需数百万次顺序步进，导致实际耗时巨大、硬件利用率低。深度学习可把多步积分摊销为一次前向传播，但现有监督方法依赖长模拟轨迹数据，适用性受限。

Method: KC-Maps以神经网络参数化转移核，直接输出积分到任意给定时刻的分布。为保证正确动力学，将(i)边界条件直接嵌入网络结构；(ii)满足Kolmogorov–Chapman方程，并以其作为高效训练目标；训练数据仅需无需轨迹的marginal样本，配合已知漂移与扩散系数。

Result: 在肽分子体系上实验显示，KC-Maps能学习并积分其动力学，且在GPU-hours指标上优于经典分子动力学模拟。

Conclusion: 以marginal样本+已知SDE系数学习转移核，是绕开长轨迹监督、加速SDE模拟的可行路线，对分子体系具实证有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic differential equations (SDEs) describe stochastic processes across a wide range of scientific domains, including physics, biology, and finance. Although individual numerical integration steps are inexpensive, observing meaningful events or transitions can require millions of sequential steps, resulting in substantial wall-clock simulation time and poor hardware utilization. Deep learning offers an alternative by amortizing many integration steps into a single neural network forward pass, potentially enabling much faster simulation. However, existing supervised approaches typically require long simulated trajectory data, which restricts their applicability. In this work, we introduce Kolmogorov-Chapman Maps (KC-Maps), a method for learning the transition kernel of an SDE that relies only on the trajectory-free marginal samples together with the known drift and diffusion coefficients. Given an initial time and state of a process, KC-Maps models its distribution integrated to any given time, corresponding to the weak solution of the SDE. To recover the correct dynamics, KC-Maps satisfies two key properties: (i) it satisfies the boundary conditions, which we incorporate directly into the neural network architecture, and (ii) it respects the Kolmogorov--Chapman equation, which provides an efficient training objective. We empirically demonstrate that KC-Maps learns to integrate the dynamics of peptide molecular systems and outperforms classical molecular dynamics in terms of GPU-hours.

</details>


### [424] [Guiding Data Allocation for Robust Subpopulation Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12884)
*Zhaoying Pan,Yipei Wang,Shenyu Lu,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展高斯特征模型框架，从理论上刻画出一般训练子群分布下的性能景观，表明均衡训练数据并非实现稳健子群泛化的唯一最优配置；并推导出基于梯度的子群比例调整方向，以更高效地提升调整准确率与最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在训练数据上平均准确率高，但在测试数据上因亚群偏移（subpopulation shift）而表现差。常见的数据层面方案是强制均衡各子群比例，但完全均衡代价高、可能不可行且并非总是必要。作者因此质疑：均衡训练数据是否是稳健亚群泛化的唯一最优配置。

Method: 将高斯特征模型框架推广到一般的训练子群分布，理论上刻画跨子群分布的性能景观；在此基础上推导基于梯度的子群比例调整方向，用以指导数据分配。

Result: 理论上表明存在多种配置（包括显著不均衡的配置）能达到与均衡配置相当的性能；基于梯度的比例调整方向能更高效地提升调整准确率与最差组准确率。

Conclusion: 策略性地分配数据比简单均衡更灵活、更有理论依据，是实现稳健性能的可行路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine learning models often achieve high average accuracy in the training data while performing poorly on test data due to subpopulation shift. A common data-centric solution is to enforce balanced subgroup proportions, but full balance can be costly, infeasible, and not always necessary. In this work, we study whether balanced training data is the unique optimal configuration for robust subpopulation generalization. We extend a Gaussian feature-model framework to theoretically characterize the performance landscape across general training subgroup distributions. We show that multiple configurations, including substantially imbalanced ones, can perform comparably to the balanced one. We further derive gradient-based directions for adjusting subgroup proportions to improve adjusted accuracy and worst-group accuracy more efficiently. These findings suggest that strategically allocating data offers a more flexible and principled path to robust performance than simple balancing. The code is available at https://github.com/zhaoyingpan/subpop-data-allocation.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [425] [Navier-Stokes characteristic boundary conditions for the Spectral Difference method on simplex elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10839)
*Thomas Marchal,Maëlys Ruello,Hugues Deniau,Guillaume Daviller*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文将NSCBC非反射边界条件从四边形/六面体推广到谱差分(SD)方法在单纯形单元(三角/四面体)上的实现，通过物理坐标系下的特征分析与Raviart-Thomas散度算子评估法向通量导数，并求解局部线性系统恢复边界通量，多类测试算例验证其精度与张量积形式相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非定常可压缩高阶模拟需要让声波、熵波、涡波无伪反射地离开计算域。但SD方法上的NSCBC仅在四边形与六面体上建立，其强形式依赖垂直于边界面的通量点连线，而三角形与四面体上不存在这种结构，故需为单纯形单元重新建立NSCBC。

Method: 在边界面上每个通量点的物理坐标系中做特征分析；利用Raviart-Thomas散度算子计算法向通量的法向导数；由每个边界单元局部的线性系统恢复边界通量，其矩阵仅依赖几何；并纳入多组分反应气体。

Result: 在声学、涡、黏性及反应流动等一系列三角与四面体测试算例上，与同等自由度数的张量积实现对比，所有工况结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: NSCBC现可用于单纯形网格上的SD方法，从而适配复杂几何的非定常可压缩高阶模拟。

Abstract: Unsteady compressible simulations with high-order methods on unstructured meshes require boundaries that let acoustic, entropy and vorticity waves leave the domain without spurious reflection. For the spectral difference (SD) method, the Navier-Stokes characteristic boundary conditions (NSCBC) have only been formulated on quadrilaterals and hexahedra. Their strong-form formalism relies on a line of flux points normal to the boundary face, which does not exist on triangles and tetrahedra. This paper extends the NSCBC to the SD method on simplex elements, including multi-species reacting gases. The characteristic analysis is performed in the physical frame of each flux point of the boundary face, and the normal derivative of the normal flux is evaluated using the Raviart-Thomas divergence operator. The boundary fluxes are recovered from a linear system local to each boundary element, whose matrix depends on the geometry only. The treatment is compared with the tensor-product formulation at a comparable number of degrees of freedom on a series of test cases covering acoustic, vortical, viscous and reacting flows on triangles and tetrahedra. In all cases, the results are in close agreement with those obtained on quadrilaterals and hexahedra, so that the NSCBC become available for the SD method on simplex meshes, which are well suited to complex geometries.

</details>


### [426] [GPU acceleration of the numerical-atomic-orbital DFT code OpenMX with GEMMul8: performance and numerical agreement on consumer and HPC GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11860)
*Hiroyuki Kawai,Taisuke Ozaki,Yoshiaki Ōno*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 将INT8 Tensor Core上模拟FP64矩阵乘法的GEMMul8库集成到OpenMX 4.0的GPU版中，在RTX 5080上显著加速对角化环节（最高2.1倍），且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP64矩阵乘法（GEMM）是第一性原理计算的核心开销。消费级GPU（如RTX 5080）的FP64吞吐极低，而INT8 Tensor Core算力充裕，因此希望借助低精度整数张量核模拟FP64 GEMM来加速OpenMX的数值原子轨道计算。

Method: 把GEMMul8集成进OpenMX 4.0 GPU版本，构建“GEMMul8 vs. cuBLAS FP64”的对照配置，并在消费级GeForce RTX 5080与HPC级H100 PCIe两种硬件环境下，针对共线/非共线自旋 × 能带/Γ点簇求解器构成的四条计算路径进行性能与精度评测。

Result: 固定工作量（25次SCF迭代）下，RTX 5080上384原子CL能带计算的对角化段加速2.1倍、总运行时间加速1.8倍；512原子CL簇计算（仅用实矩阵乘积）对角化段仍有1.5倍加速。H100上GEMMul8带来的额外加速有限。216原子Si体系（单原子位移0.05 Å）精度测试中，24种配置在SCF收敛判据1e-8下全部收敛且迭代步一致，总能差≤2e-12 Ha、力分量差≤7e-12 Ha/Bohr。

Conclusion: GEMMul8在FP64算力受限的消费级GPU上能带来可观加速，而在FP64算力充足的H100上收益有限；同时其数值误差远低于物理可分辨精度，说明INT8 Tensor Core模拟FP64是加速第一性原理代码的可行且可靠途径。

Abstract: We integrate GEMMul8, a library that emulates FP64 matrix multiplication on INT8 Tensor Cores, into the GPU version of the numerical-atomic-orbital first-principles code OpenMX 4.0. The GEMMul8 configuration is compared against the cuBLAS FP64 configuration within each of two environments, a consumer GeForce RTX 5080 and an HPC-oriented H100 PCIe, for the four computational paths formed by collinear (CL) or noncollinear (NCL) spin and band or $Γ$-point-cluster solvers. In fixed-work measurements that completed 25 SCF iterations, the diagonalization section of a 384-atom CL band calculation on the RTX 5080 is accelerated by a factor of 2.1 and the total run time by 1.8; a 512-atom CL cluster calculation, which uses only real matrix products in contrast to the CL band calculation, still gains a factor of 1.5 in the diagonalization section. On the H100, GEMMul8 provides little additional acceleration of the diagonalization section. In an accuracy test on a 216-atom Si system in which one atom was displaced by 0.05 angstrom, all 24 configurations were converged using an SCF convergence criterion of $10^{-8}$; every configuration converged at the same iteration within each path, and the differences between GEMMul8 and cuBLAS are at most $2\times10^{-12}$ Ha in the total energy and $7\times10^{-12}$ Ha/Bohr in force components.

</details>


### [427] [Efficient sensitivity analysis for combustion problems via reformulation and adjoint methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12309)
*Francesca Loffredo,Raymond Langer,Marco Vivenzo,Sanket Girhe,Heinz Pitsch*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过坐标变换将着火延迟时间与熄灭应变率纳入解向量，使伴随法可高效计算这两类目标量的敏感性系数，实测计算成本最多降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细化学动力学模型参数数量远多于目标量（QoIs），传统直接敏感性分析成本过高；伴随法虽高效，但对IDT与ESR这类非解向量显式分量的QoIs难以直接应用。

Method: 对控制方程组做坐标变换，将QoI（IDT、ESR）转化为解向量的显式分量，从而可套用伴随敏感性分析；给出计算复杂度分析，并以数值方法交叉验证伴随敏感系数，同时进行跨模型规模的基准测试。

Result: 伴随敏感系数与其他方法结果一致；执行时间较标准直接敏感性分析最多降低约两个数量级；小规模问题受实现细节影响性能受限，大规模模型呈现预期的渐近标度。

Conclusion: 所提重构统一支持IDT与ESR的伴随敏感性分析，显著提升算法效率，并为识别性能瓶颈、优化实现提供基准参考框架。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of combustion processes rely on chemical kinetic models, whose predictive capability is typically assessed by comparing measurements of quantities of interest (QoIs) in idealized experimental configurations with the corresponding simulated values. Sensitivity analysis quantifies the influence of model parameters on QoIs, making it a key tool for model development and optimization. Given the large number of parameters involved in detailed kinetic models compared to the number of QoIs, adjoint methods are a promising approach for efficient sensitivity analysis. Ignition delay times (IDTs) and extinction strain rates (ESRs) are among the QoIs of primary importance. However, applying adjoint methods to these quantities is not straightforward and is addressed in this work. The system of governing equations is reformulated via a coordinate transformation that makes the QoI an explicit component of the solution vector. This reformulation enables the application of adjoint sensitivity analysis for both IDT and ESR. A computational complexity analysis demonstrates that the proposed formulation significantly improves algorithmic efficiency compared to conventional approaches. The methodology is verified by comparing adjoint sensitivity coefficients with those obtained from other methods. Execution time measurements show computational cost reductions of up to two orders of magnitude relative to standard direct sensitivity analysis. A benchmark study is conducted across kinetic models of increasing size to assess how far theoretical efficiency gains are realized in practice. While the results show that implementation details can limit performance for smaller problem sizes, the expected asymptotic scaling is observed for larger models. The benchmark thus provides a reference framework for identifying key performance bottlenecks and guiding subsequent optimization efforts.

</details>


### [428] [Consistent Mapping for Fluid Structure Interaction with Geometrically Nonlinear Isogeometric Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12599)
*Juan Ignacio Camarotti,Ricky Aristio,Roland Wüchner*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文为几何非线性等几何Bernoulli与Timoshenko梁发展了与各自样条运动学及截面旋转描述一致的梁到流固界面映射算子，并通过解析测试、收敛性研究、虚功校核及多个基准算例验证了其在大变形下界面重构与功共轭载荷传递的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等几何梁以少量结构自由度高效表征细长结构，但用于流固耦合（FSI）时需在梁中心线与流体界面间传递运动与载荷；已有映射策略多针对常规有限元梁，而几何非线性等几何梁需与样条运动学及不同截面旋转描述相一致的算子。

Method: 将流体界面点在参考构型下附着于梁，利用各梁公式的非线性运动学进行重构；对映射进行一致线性化，得到依赖于构型的映射算子，并以其转置实现功共轭载荷传递；在分区分步FSI框架下分别建立Bernoulli与Timoshenko等几何梁的映射格式。

Result: 解析变形测试、收敛性研究与虚功校核表明公式正确；在Mok、Turek–Hron基准的梁式改版及Goland机翼颤振基准中，界面重构准确、大变形下载荷传递满足功共轭，两种梁公式均可用于耦合FSI问题。

Conclusion: 所提一致映射算子使几何非线性等几何梁可靠地嵌入分区FSI计算，兼顾精度与效率，为基于降维等几何梁的细长结构流固耦合分析提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Isogeometric beam formulations provide an efficient reduced-dimensional representation of slender structures, combining accurate geometry and high-order continuity with few structural degrees of freedom. Their application to fluid--structure interaction (FSI) requires transferring motion and loads between the one-dimensional beam centerline and the surrounding fluid interface. While mapping strategies exist for conventional finite element beams, geometrically nonlinear isogeometric formulations require operators consistent with their spline-based kinematics and distinct descriptions of cross-sectional rotation. This work develops consistent beam-to-surface mappings for geometrically nonlinear isogeometric Bernoulli and Timoshenko beams in partitioned FSI. Fluid-interface points are attached to the beam in the reference configuration and reconstructed using the nonlinear kinematics of each formulation. Consistent linearization yields a configuration-dependent mapping operator whose transpose ensures work-conjugate load transfer. The formulations are verified through analytical deformation tests, convergence studies, and virtual-work checks, and evaluated in beam-based adaptations of the Mok and Turek--Hron benchmarks and the Goland wing flutter benchmark. The results confirm accurate interface reconstruction and work-conjugate load transfer under large deformations, while demonstrating the applicability of both beam formulations to coupled FSI problems.

</details>
